#CH 1
[ZH] 清晨的阳光努力透过窗帘的缝隙，一丝光芒洒落在一座高楼第二十二层屋内的卧室。
[EN] Morning sunlight pushed through the gap in the curtains, spilling a thin shaft of light across a twenty-second-floor bedroom in a high-rise apartment.
[ZH] 窗帘是紫色的，窗外的阳光衬得紫色更加鲜艳，使得卧室内的空气也像被染上淡淡的紫色。
[EN] The curtains were purple. Sunlight from outside sharpened the hue, tinting the bedroom air with a pale violet glow.
[ZH] 卧室内装饰得简单素雅，床上躺着千里寻，她睡得正鼾。凌乱的头发散落在枕头上，被子盖到脖子处，发出轻轻的鼾声。
[EN] The room was plain and neat. Qianli Xun lay fast asleep on the bed, messy hair splayed across the pillow, quilt pulled up to her neck, letting out soft snores.
[ZH] 放在床头柜上的手机铃声响起，显然是时下流行的一首歌曲。
[EN] A phone rang on the nightstand, playing a current pop hit.
[ZH] 千里寻并未睁开双眼，伸手摸向床头柜，摸了一会儿，终于抓住手机，依然没睁眼，用睡意浓浓的声音问道：“喂，什么事？”
[EN] Without opening her eyes, Qianli Xun reached toward the nightstand, groped around for a moment, and grabbed the phone. Still eyes shut, she asked in a thick, drowsy voice, "Hello? What is it?"
[ZH] 手机那头传来徒弟二杠焦急的声音：“师父！你怎么还睡着？赶紧打开手机，看我给你发的视频，网上都在骂你！”
[EN] Her apprentice Er'gang's frantic voice burst through the speaker: "Master! How are you still asleep? Open your phone right now and watch the video I sent you—everyone online is tearing you apart!"
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻完全清醒，她坐起来：“什么？网上都在骂我？为什么？”
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped wide awake and sat up. "What? Everyone online is tearing me apart? Why?"
[ZH] “你自己看吧！早都劝你别写网络小说，你偏不听，最近躲在家里别出来，挂了！”
[EN] "See for yourself! I told you ages ago to stop writing web novels, and you wouldn't listen. Stay indoors for a while and don't go out. Hanging up!"
[ZH] 千里寻坐起来，被子很自然地从上身溜下去，露出淡紫色的睡衣。她急忙打开手机上的聊天工具，找到二杠的头像，是财神爷，这家伙整日梦想着发财。点开后，果然看到有视频链接，她赶紧打开，视频里传出一个女人的声音：
[EN] As she sat up, the quilt slid off her shoulders, revealing pale purple pajamas. She opened her chat app and tapped Er'gang's profile picture—the God of Wealth, since the guy dreamed of getting rich day and night. Sure enough, a video link sat there. She tapped it, and a woman's voice rang out:
[ZH] “网络女作家千里寻被众多网友爆料，其新作品有抄袭他人，自我抄袭，水文字的现象，已失去早期作品的灵气，而且一味模式化，根本无法引起读者的兴趣……”
[EN] "Numerous netizens have exposed female web novelist Qianli Xun for plagiarizing others, recycling her own plots, and padding her chapters with fluff. She has lost the spark of her early work and churns out formulaic stories that fail to hold readers' interest..."
[ZH] 千里寻皱起眉头，关了视频，不想再听下去，大骂道：“老娘在网文界纵横十年，怎么写网文，要你在这里瞎掰掰！”
[EN] Qianli Xun frowned and shut off the video, refusing to listen to another word. "I've ruled the web-novel world for ten years! Who are you to spout nonsense about how I write?"
[ZH] 她把手机扔到一边，下床穿好拖鞋，走进厨房，从冰箱里拿出一瓶牛奶，给玻璃杯中倒入半杯。
[EN] She tossed the phone aside, climbed out of bed, slipped into her slippers, and walked to the kitchen. She pulled a bottle of milk from the fridge and poured half a glass.
[ZH] 牛奶刚从冰箱里拿出，很凉，喝下肚对身体不好，千里寻还是知道的。她懒得到厨房热牛奶，干脆端起一旁的热水瓶，添些热水到玻璃杯中，温度刚刚好，端起来，“咕咚，咕咚”喝下，因为生气，将玻璃杯重重放在桌上。
[EN] Cold milk straight from the fridge was bad for the stomach—she knew that much. Too lazy to heat it on the stove, she picked up the thermos next to her and topped the glass off with hot water until it felt just right. She raised the glass, gulped it down, and slammed it onto the table in anger.
[ZH] 她穿过客厅，走到阳台，向楼下看去，许多男男女女的记者堵在楼下，他们不断抬头向上张望。还好她住的是高档小区，每栋楼都有保安，那些记者根本上不来。
[EN] She crossed the living room to the balcony and looked down. Crowds of reporters, men and women alike, jammed the area below, craning their necks upward. Good thing she lived in an upscale complex with security guards stationed at every building; none of them could get up here.
[ZH] 千里寻的嘴角露出笑容：“没想到我还挺出名，来了这么多记者。也对，能够被网暴的人大小也是个名人儿。”
[EN] A smirk tugged at Qianli Xun's lips. "Didn't realize I was this famous—look at all those reporters. Well, makes sense. You've got to be somebody to get canceled online."
[ZH] 她离开阳台，又走进卧室，伸手从床上拿起手机，坐在床边，深吸一口气：“以放松的心态见证一下你有多出名。”
[EN] Leaving the balcony, she went back to the bedroom, grabbed her phone off the bed, sat on the edge, and took a deep breath. "Time to kick back and see just how famous you really are."
[ZH] 她点开一个短视频APP，第一个刷到的视频就在吐槽她。是位女主播，长得很一般，声音慢条斯理：
[EN] She opened a short-video app. The very first clip roasting her featured a plain-looking female streamer speaking in an unhurried drawl:
[ZH] “家人们，大家好，千里寻这个名字不陌生吧？对！她就是著名的网络小说女作家，写过好几部网络小说，估计家人们都看过，实不相瞒，我也看过，以前还是她的粉丝。注意，我说的是以前，为什么现在不是？因为我作为读者已成长，她作为网文写手竟然还原地踏步！依然写一些十八九岁青春少女爱看的，能不被粉丝吐槽吗？回头再翻翻曾经看过的那些小说，我最终对自己说一句：曾经的你真是又傻又纯又幼稚，要不怎么会喜欢那样的小说？……”
[EN] "Hey, chat! The name Qianli Xun rings a bell, right? Yep! She's that big-name web novelist who wrote a bunch of hit stories—bet most of you have read them. Truth be told, I read them too, and I used to be a fan. Notice I said used to be. Why not anymore? Because I grew up as a reader, while she's still stuck in place as a writer! She keeps churning out stories aimed at eighteen- and nineteen-year-old girls. How could fans not roast her? Looking back at the novels I once loved, all I can tell my younger self is: you were so naive, gullible, and immature—how else could you have liked stuff like that?..."
[ZH] 千里寻感慨自己内心够强大，竟然听了这么多，还是忍不住面对手机屏幕怼道：“你懂什么！老娘写小说定位的读者就是那个年龄段，你十几岁时爱看，不就证明老娘写得好？怪就怪你不再年轻！”
[EN] Qianli Xun marveled at her own thick skin for listening that long, yet she could not help snapping back at the screen: "What do you know? That age group is my target audience! If you loved my books as a teenager, doesn't that prove I did my job right? Blame yourself for getting old!"
[ZH] 正埋怨着，手机里蹦出一条信息。千里寻收起视频，将信息点开，竟然是提醒她有一大半儿粉丝已退群。
[EN] Mid-rant, a notification popped up. She minimized the video and tapped it—more than half the members of her fan group chat had quit.
[ZH] “有什么了不起！退就退！老娘是作家，又不是明星，当初网站让建粉丝群，老娘就十分不乐意，退了倒也干净，干脆全退群！”
[EN] "Big deal! Leave if you want! I'm a writer, not some pop idol. I hated setting up a fan group when the platform asked me to anyway. Good riddance—go ahead and clear out the whole chat!"
[ZH] 话音刚落，又收到一条信息，提示她，粉丝群人数已成个位。千里寻懒得理会，继续点开视频，将那女主播刷走，映入眼帘的大标题是：“文学死了”，这个标题引起她强烈的兴趣，心想：“这条肯定跟我没关系，不妨听听。”
[EN] No sooner had she spoken than another alert flashed: her fan group was down to single digits. Too annoyed to care, she went back to the video app and swiped past the streamer. A bold headline caught her eye: "Literature Is Dead." Intrigued, she thought, This one definitely has nothing to do with me. Might as well listen.
[ZH] 视频中出现一位老者，看上去很有学问，声音既洪亮又激进：
[EN] An elderly man appeared on screen, looking every inch the scholar, his voice booming and fiery:
[ZH] “文学死了！可悲！可叹！传统作家的文学作品没有人阅读！年轻人却喜欢用手机看那些没有营养的网络小说！文学要为现实服务！要揭露现实，批判现实！要文以载道！网络小说为了经济利益，一味迎合市场，迎合读者，娱乐至死！爽文，小白文满天飞！没有一部符合文学的要求！就拿千里寻的小说来讲……”
[EN] "Literature is dead! How tragic! How lamentable! Nobody reads the works of traditional authors anymore! Young people would rather scroll through mindless web fiction on their phones! Literature must serve reality—expose it, critique it, and convey the Way! Driven by profit, web novels pander to the market and amuse readers to death! Power fantasies and brainless reads flood every corner, and not a single one meets the standard of real literature! Take Qianli Xun's novels, for instance..."
[ZH] 铺垫这么多，还是为了批判千里寻。看来她真的一夜之间在网络上臭名昭著，那就听听这位激进的老者如何批判她。
[EN] All that buildup just to tear into her. She really had become notorious overnight. Fine—she would hear what this firebrand old man had to say.
[ZH] “全网都在批判，我便去翻了千里寻的小说，看了一段，实在看不下去，全是口水文，一点儿营养都没有！而传统文学语言文字有底蕴，内容丰富，值得人品味。网络小说根本无法与传统文学相比！我呼吁！趁全网批判千里寻的作品，掀起批判网络小说的浪潮，复兴传统文学！……”
[EN] "With the whole internet criticizing her, I went and flipped through Qianli Xun's novels. After one passage, I couldn't stomach another word—pure drivel with zero substance! Traditional literature has depth of language, richness of content, and layers worth savoring. Web fiction cannot hold a candle to it! I call on everyone: seize this wave of outrage against Qianli Xun's work, launch a campaign against web fiction, and revive traditional literature!..."
[ZH] 千里寻用手指在屏幕上点了一下，老者的声音立刻停止，她指着手机屏幕：
[EN] Qianli Xun tapped the screen, cutting the old man off mid-sentence, and pointed straight at her phone.
[ZH] “你讲得那么慷慨激昂，有本事写部网络小说试试，你肯定不会写！老娘告诉你，文学没死！在网络时代，文学换了一个样貌与读者见面。你懂不懂文学史？要是不懂就去看看，每个时代的文学都有其独一无二的特点，传统文学已不符合这个时代，必然会被淘汰，你想复兴根本就没用！说不定几百年之后，我千里寻的作品也会成为经典。老娘还想问你，我的小说让读者开心快乐有什么不对？曹雪芹曾经还说《红楼梦》不过是游戏笔墨，陶情适性而已，连四大名著之首都娱乐大众，网络小说不过是继承了文学的优良传统而已！……”
[EN] "Such a fiery speech—if you're so capable, try writing a web novel yourself! You wouldn't even know where to start! Let me tell you something: literature isn't dead! In the digital age, it just put on a new face to meet its readers. Do you even know literary history? Go read up on it if you don't! Every era has its own unique form of literature. Traditional writing doesn't fit this age, so it's bound to fade—reviving it won't work! Who knows, a few hundred years from now, my books might be classics too! And let me ask you this: what's wrong with my stories making readers happy? Even Cao Xueqin said Dream of the Red Chamber was just playful ink meant to lift the spirits. If the greatest of the Four Great Classical Novels existed to entertain people, web fiction is just carrying on literature's finest tradition!..."
[ZH] 千里寻还没发泄完，手机屏幕闪现出“悠悠编辑”四个字，是网站编辑打来的电话。
[EN] Before she could finish venting, "Editor Youyou" flashed across her screen—a call from her platform editor.
[ZH] 千里寻点了绿色的通话键，传来编辑的声音，是个温柔的女声：“千里寻，网站上层决定，鉴于网上喷你的人太多，为了维护网站形象，你的所有作品一律下架，包括你正在写的作品，因作品下架给你造成的损失，网站会酌情补偿。”
[EN] Qianli Xun tapped the green call button. A gentle female voice came through. "Qianli Xun, upper management has made a decision. Given the massive backlash against you online, to protect the site's image, all of your works are being taken down, including your ongoing serialization. The platform will offer fair compensation for your losses."
[ZH] “怎么能这样？再说能被人喷，说明我有名气，作品更不应该被下架……”
[EN] "How can you do that? Besides, getting roasted just proves I have clout—that's all the more reason not to pull my books..."
[ZH] 编辑根本不想继续听下去，迅速挂断电话，千里寻既无奈又生气，她想把电话扔到地上，发泄她的不满，还是忍住，得好好想想接下来该怎么办？难道创作生涯就此中断？这可是她最喜欢做的事。
[EN] The editor had no patience to listen and hung up at once. Helpless and fuming, Qianli Xun wanted to hurl the phone to the floor to blow off steam, yet she held back. She needed to think about her next move. Was her creative career really over just like that? Writing was the thing she loved most in the world.

#CH 2
[ZH] 还没想清楚该怎么办，肚子开始“咕咕”叫，一杯掺了水的牛奶根本不够。千里寻又走到餐厅，打开冰箱，除了牛奶，什么都没有。
[ZH] 她拨通徒弟的电话：“喂，二杠，你师父我被网暴，出不去，楼下还有好多记者围着，你给我买一盒肠粉，一杯豆浆，一碗豆腐脑送过来，记着豆腐脑多加辣子。”
[ZH] “这么多，能吃得下吗？”
[ZH] “全网都在骂我，还不得吃饱些对付他们？对了，来时给我带身古代女装，把帷帽也带上。”
[ZH] “要这些干嘛？”
[ZH] “我想回千里拳庄，楼下那么多记者，总得装扮一下，才不会被他们发现。”
[ZH] “还好大家只知道你是写网络小说的，不知道你跟千里拳庄的关系，那就回拳庄躲躲，你等着，我很快就来。”
[EN] Before she could work out her next move, her stomach let out a loud growl. Half a glass of watered-down milk did nothing to fill her up. Qianli Xun walked back into the dining area and pulled the refrigerator open. Nothing sat inside except milk.
[ZH] 大约半个小时后，二杠骑摩托车来到小区楼下，拎着早饭和一个大纸袋，看了记者们一眼，走进大楼，给保安悄悄说句话，拐到一旁的电梯，等了一会儿，电梯门自动打开，进入后按亮有数字“22”的按钮。
[EN] She dialed her apprentice. "Hey, Er'gang, your master is getting canceled online and stuck inside with reporters camping downstairs. Buy me a box of steamed rice rolls, a cup of soy milk, and a bowl of tofu pudding, and tell them to pile extra chili oil on the pudding."
[ZH] 记者们并未将二杠当回事，因为他身着黑色拳服，露出臂膀上的肌肉，怎么看都与千里寻扯不上关系。
[EN] "That much? Can you finish all of it?"
[ZH] 二杠上了二十二层，敲开师父的门，进入后，把大纸袋扔到沙发上，走进厨房将早餐放到餐桌上。
[EN] "The whole internet is gunning for me. Don't I need a full belly to fight back? Oh, and bring a set of ancient women's robes and a veiled hat when you come."
[ZH] 边走边埋怨：“师父不该写小说，千里拳第五百零八代传人的身份足以让你炫耀，这倒好，被全网骂。也好，从此以后老老实实待在拳庄，没事接接商演，挣的钱够你生活，然后再把自己嫁出去，人生也就圆满。”
[EN] "What do you want those for?"
[ZH] “我是师父，什么时候轮到徒弟教训师父？”千里寻跟在二杠身后，在餐桌旁坐下，打开塑料袋，拿出早餐，吃了起来。
[EN] "I'm heading back to Qianli Fist Manor. With all those reporters downstairs, I need a disguise so nobody spots me."
[ZH] 二杠说：“师父慢慢吃，我在小区外等你。”
[EN] "Good thing folks only know you as a web novelist and have no clue about Qianli Fist Manor. Lying low at the manor is smart. Sit tight, I'll be right there."
[ZH] 千里寻没说话，夹起一块肠粉吃起来，听着二杠出去的关门声，自语道：“我的这些徒弟越来越不把我当回事，等我回到拳庄，定要好好修理。”
[EN] About half an hour later, Er'gang pulled up outside the apartment complex on his motorcycle, carrying breakfast and a large paper bag. He shot one glance at the reporters, walked into the building, murmured a quick word to the security guard, and headed to the elevator. When the doors slid open, he stepped in and pressed the button for twenty-two. The reporters paid him no mind. Wearing a black martial arts uniform that showed off his muscled arms, he looked like the last person tied to Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 千里寻的胃口还真不小，把所有早餐吃完，用纸巾擦擦嘴巴，将废纸巾扔到垃圾桶中，伸了一个大大的懒腰，然后走到卫生间，又是刷牙，又是洗脸，又是整理飘逸的长发。
[EN] Up on the twenty-second floor, Er'gang knocked on his master's door. Once inside, he tossed the paper bag onto the sofa and carried breakfast to the kitchen table, grumbling as he walked. "You never should have written web novels, Master. Being the 508th-generation heir to Qianli Fist gives you plenty to be proud of, yet here you are getting torn apart online. Might be for the best, though. Stay put at the manor from now on, take commercial gigs when you feel like it, make a comfortable living, find a husband, and call it a happy life."
[ZH] 收拾完后，来到客厅，打开纸袋，取出衣服和帷帽，脱了睡衣，将衣服换上。还不错，像个古装女侠。她把手机等重要物件放进包里，斜挎在身上。再拿起帷帽戴在头上，轻纱拂下，确实不容易被认出。
[EN] "I'm the master here. Since when does an apprentice lecture his master?" Trailing behind him, Qianli Xun took a seat at the table, unpacked the plastic bags, and started eating.
[ZH] 到门口换上一双休闲鞋，打开门，上了电梯，来到一楼，心里还是有莫名的紧张感。经过保安身边时，他正在刷短视频，从传出的声音判断又是在喷千里寻。对于下楼外出的人，保安并不在意，继续津津有味地欣赏视频。
[EN] "Take your time, Master. I'll wait outside the complex gate."
[ZH] 千里寻走出楼门，透过轻纱看到记者们都瞧过来，她更加紧张。
[EN] Without replying, Qianli Xun picked up a steamed rice roll. Listening to the front door click shut behind him, she muttered to herself, "These apprentices have less respect for me every day. Wait till I get back to the manor and whip them into shape."
[ZH] 一位女记者主动打招呼：“你好，请问你住这栋楼第几层？”
[EN] Her appetite proved every bit as big as her order. She polished off every last bite, wiped her mouth with a tissue, tossed it into the trash, and gave a wide stretch before going to the bathroom to brush her teeth, wash her face, and comb out her long hair. Back in the living room, she pulled the outfit and veiled hat from the paper bag, shed her pajamas, and changed. Not bad at all; she looked like a classic swordswoman from a period drama. She tucked her phone and essentials into a crossbody bag, then settled the veiled hat onto her head. Light gauze drifted down around her face, hiding her features well.
[ZH] “二十二层。”
[EN] Slipping on casual sneakers by the door, she rode the elevator down to the lobby, an odd flicker of tension tightening her chest. As she walked past the security guard, he was watching short videos on his phone; judging by the voice blasting out, another clip was roasting Qianli Xun. Too absorbed in his screen to notice anyone walking out, he kept watching with relish.
[ZH] 听到二十二层，记者们一下子围过来：“你和千里寻住一层？可知道她的情况？”
[EN] Stepping through the main doors, Qianli Xun peered through the gauze and saw every reporter swing around to look her way. Her pulse sped up. A female reporter stepped forward first. "Hello, miss. Which floor of this building do you live on?"
[ZH] 千里寻后悔为何不说其他楼层，只怪自己太紧张，智商极度下降。
[EN] "Twenty-second."
[ZH] “我对她不熟，只知道她住在我家对面，一早听到对面的屋子里传出凄惨的哭声，估计是被网暴吓的。你们忙，我还有事。”
[EN] At the words "twenty-second," the entire pack surged around her. "You live on the same floor as Qianli Xun? Know how she's doing?"
[ZH] 千里寻慌忙从记者中逃离，来到小区门口，自语道：“还好没被认出。”
[EN] Qianli Xun kicked herself for not naming any other floor. Nerves had wrecked her wits. "I don't know her well. All I know is she lives across the hall, and this morning awful crying came out of her apartment, probably scared half to death by all the online hate. Excuse me, I have errands."
[ZH] “师父！这儿！”传来二杠的声音。
[EN] Breaking free from the reporters, she hurried out to the complex gate and breathed under her breath, "Thank goodness nobody recognized me."
[ZH] 千里寻赶紧走过去，跨上摩托的后座，抱住二杠。一阵摩托车启动的呜咽声后，他们已飞驰在马路上，朝千里拳庄而去。
[EN] "Master! Over here!" Er'gang called.
[ZH] 千里拳庄建在城市郊区，有一百多名徒弟，男女弟子均有。
[EN] She hurried over, swung onto the back of the motorcycle, and wrapped her arms around Er'gang's waist. With a sharp rev of the engine, they shot down the avenue toward Qianli Fist Manor.
[ZH] 当摩托车在千里拳庄外停下时，申简辰西装革履，带着申拳弟子又来千里拳庄大门外对战，看样子两边还未真正打起来。
[EN] Built out in the city suburbs, Qianli Fist Manor trained over a hundred apprentices, men and women alike. When the motorcycle rolled to a stop outside the gates, Shen Jianchen stood there in a crisp suit, leading his Shen Fist disciples to challenge the manor once more. Judging by the tense standoff, no blows had been exchanged yet.
[ZH] 千里寻卸下帷帽，扔到二杠的怀里，大声说：“申简辰！不是每月二十五号才来切磋吗？怎么提前来了？”
[EN] Pulling off her veiled hat and tossing it into Er'gang's chest, Qianli Xun shouted, "Shen Jianchen! Isn't our sparring match on the twenty-fifth of each month? Why show up early?"
[ZH] 申简辰转过身，见千里寻一身女侠装扮，甚是美艳，他露出深邃的目光，轻轻一笑：“今天一大早起来，看到你成为全网焦点，赶过来瞧瞧。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. Seeing Qianli Xun dressed like a striking swordswoman, he held her in a deep gaze and let a faint smile cross his lips. "Woke up early to find you trending across the whole internet, so I came over to check on you."
[ZH] 二杠把帷帽挂在摩托车上，走到申简辰身边，调侃道：“你可是大公司的CEO，放着大公司不管，每个月带你们申拳的弟子找我们比试，该不会是看上我师父？”
[EN] Hanging the veiled hat off his handlebars, Er'gang strolled over beside Shen Jianchen and teased, "You're CEO of a huge company. Ditching your office every month to drag your Shen Fist students out here for matches... sure you aren't after my master?"
[ZH] 申简辰盯着千里寻：“看上你师父不假，比试也是真，你们这些当徒弟的人，没事劝劝你师父，都三十岁的老姑娘，再不嫁，可就没人要喽。”
[EN] Eyes anchored on Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen answered straight, "After your master, yes; here to spar, also yes. You apprentices ought to talk sense into her. Already an old maid of thirty; wait any longer and nobody will want her."
[ZH] 一早被网暴，气没处撒，又听到说自己年龄大，千里寻更是气不打一出来：“老娘明天才过三十岁生日！……”
[EN] Already simmering from the morning's cyberbullying with nowhere to vent, hearing him poke at her age lit Qianli Xun's fuse on the spot. "I don't turn thirty until tomorrow!"
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“别整天老娘老娘地称呼自己，本来年纪都不小，会把自己叫得更老。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen chuckled. "Quit calling yourself 'this old lady' every other breath. You aren't twenty anymore; talking like that ages you faster."
[ZH] 千里寻更加生气：“申简辰，你听着，老娘今日要跟你决一死战，你要是输了，以后不准来千里拳庄！”
[EN] Fury spiked higher. "Listen up, Shen Jianchen! Today I'm challenging you to a real duel. Lose, and you never set foot near Qianli Fist Manor again!"
[ZH] “你要是输了呢？”
[EN] "And if you lose?"
[ZH] “输了就输了，你能把我怎样？再说老娘根本不会输！”
[EN] "Lose is lose; what could you do about it? Besides, no way I'm losing!"
[ZH] “你要是输了就嫁给我。”
[EN] "If you lose, marry me."
[ZH] 听了申简辰的话，千里拳弟子议论道：“这申CEO什么都好，就是不会追女孩子，这把师父给气的，能嫁给他吗？”
[EN] Hearing that, the Qianli Fist disciples exchanged whispers. "CEO Shen has everything going for him except zero game with girls. Winding Master up like this, how on earth would she ever marry him?"
[ZH] 千里寻说道：“想得美！除非猪能上树，老娘再考虑要不要嫁给你！今天晚上七点千年神柳处见，你一人前来，不准带其他人！”
[EN] "Keep dreaming!" Qianli Xun shot back. "Only when pigs climb trees will I consider marrying you! Tonight at seven under the Millennial Divine Willow. Come alone; bring nobody else!"
[ZH] “收到！”申简辰看上去很开心，对弟子们说，“撤！”
[EN] "Copy that!" Looking thrilled beyond words, Shen Jianchen waved to his disciples. "Move out!"
[ZH] 申拳的弟子们跟着师父向一辆劳斯莱斯走去，看到师父脸上一直挂着笑容，一位弟子好奇地问：“师父，你都要被打，怎么还这么开心？”
[EN] As the Shen Fist disciples followed their master toward a parked Rolls-Royce, spotting the broad grin plastered across his face, one apprentice asked in puzzlement, "Master, you're about to get beaten up. Why are you grinning like that?"
[ZH] “什么被打？明明是约会！晚上，明月升起，千年神柳旁，让我想起两句诗：月上柳梢头，人约黄昏后。”申简辰一脸畅想，丝毫都不担忧。
[EN] "Beaten up? That's a date! Nighttime, a bright moon rising beside the Millennial Divine Willow... brings to mind two classic lines: 'The moon rises above the willow tips; our tryst is set past dusk.'" Lost in a daydream, Shen Jianchen showed not a shred of worry.
[ZH] 弟子们看着他上了车，议论道：“师父这脑子也不知道是怎么当上CEO？又是怎么管理那么大的公司？还开上这么豪华的车？”
[EN] Watching him climb into the luxury car, the disciples traded baffled mutters. "How did Master ever become a CEO with a head like that? How does he run a giant company and drive a car this fancy?"
[ZH] 傍晚时分，郊外的千年神柳下，千里寻扎着高马尾，身着白色体恤，一条休闲牛仔裤，脚蹬一双跑鞋，看上去青春靓丽，根本不像一个明日就过三十岁生日的女人。
[ZH] 申简辰换一身休闲装，看着既随意又不失帅气，他盯着千里寻，眼中充满爱意。
[ZH] 旁边的池塘里传来一阵阵青蛙的鸣叫声，打破二人之间的寂静。
[ZH] 等到聒噪的青蛙声停下来，申简辰抬头望了望月亮，快要十五，天上那轮月亮即将圆满，再看看池塘岸边一棵粗壮的神柳，笑道：“喂！千里寻，想和我约会直说，何必借口与我对决？”
[ZH] “你哪只耳朵听到我想跟你约会？老娘就是来跟你对决！”
[ZH] 说着，千里寻伸出拳头，一个箭步冲上朝申简辰打去。
[EN] At dusk beneath the suburban Millennial Divine Willow, Qianli Xun stood with her hair tied in a high ponytail, dressed in a white T-shirt, casual jeans, and running shoes. Fresh and bright, she looked nothing like a woman turning thirty tomorrow. Shen Jianchen had changed into casual clothes, relaxed yet sharp, watching her with open affection in his eyes. Frogs croaked in bursts from the pond nearby, breaking the quiet between them.
[ZH] 申简辰向另一边躲开。
[EN] When the noisy frogs paused, Shen Jianchen gazed up at the moon. Near the fifteenth of the lunar month, it was almost full. He glanced at the thick trunk of the Divine Willow along the bank and laughed. "Hey, Qianli Xun, if you wanted a date with me, just say so. Why dress it up as a duel?"
[ZH] 千里寻不甘示弱，加大攻势力度，以迅猛的拳法组合，发起猛烈攻击，试图压制对方。
[EN] "Which ear of yours heard me say date? I came here to fight you!"
[ZH] 申简辰时而躲避，时而运用闪避和拆拳术，时而运用柔和的拳法化解攻势。他见千里寻使出快拳打来，立刻增加拳头的力度，迎上去，当他们的拳头撞到一起时，二人都被对方的力量击得向后退出两米多。
[EN] With that, Qianli Xun clenched her fists and lunged straight at him. Shen Jianchen sidestepped clean. Unwilling to give ground, she raised the pace, unleashing a fierce barrage of boxing combinations to pin him down. Shen Jianchen wove through her assault, using dodges, counter-grapple techniques, and supple deflections to drain her momentum. The instant she fired Quick Fist, he poured full power into his own punch and met hers head-on. The clash drove both of them more than two meters back.
[ZH] “千里寻，你一个女孩子家这么拼干嘛？不如嫁给我，我这么优秀，既是申拳第三百六十代传人，又是大公司CEO，你打着灯笼都难找，多少女孩子都想嫁给我，可我就对你感兴趣，你嫁给我后，不用再写什么网络小说，我养你。”
[EN] "Why push yourself this hard, Qianli Xun? You're a girl; why not marry me? Look at me: 360th-generation heir to Shen Fist, CEO of a major corporation, a catch hard to find even with a lantern! Crowds of women would line up to marry me, yet you're the only one I want. Marry me, quit writing web novels, and I'll support you."
[ZH] 也许是申简辰称她“女孩子”，千里寻心情好了许多，竟然还有些感动，嘴上依然不依不饶：“别妄想，我说过，除非猪能上树，老娘才会嫁给你，老娘说到做到。”
[ZH] 话音刚落，脚下的地突然颠簸起来，幅度甚大，两人受颠簸力度影响，根本站不稳，倒在地上，亲眼目睹旁边的建筑顷刻间化为乌有，他们才反应过来是发生了地震，也真正体会到人在大自然面前太过脆弱。
[ZH] 千年神柳因地震晃动得厉害，拔地而起，即将倾倒，千里寻正好在千年神柳下。
[ZH] ﻿申简辰注意到，努力从倾斜的地面爬起，向千里寻冲去，他刚扑到千里寻身上，神柳倒下，重重地压在二人身上，鲜血渗入颠簸的泥土中。
[EN] Maybe because he called her a "girl," her sour mood lifted a notch, stirring an unexpected warmth in her chest, though her tongue refused to soften. "Keep dreaming! I told you: only when pigs climb trees will I marry you, and I keep my word!"
[ZH] 大约十分钟后，一切安静下来，周围一片狼藉，千里寻感觉到自己从身体剥离出来，飘到空中，看到血肉模糊的尸身压在神柳之下，她痛苦极了。
[EN] No sooner had she spoken than the earth beneath their feet heaved in huge jolts. Knocked off balance by the brutal tremors, both hit the ground and watched nearby buildings collapse into rubble in seconds. Only then did realization strike: an earthquake! Humans stood fragile as dust before nature's fury.
[ZH] 接着看到申简辰也从尸身中剥离出来，她大声喊着：“申简辰！申简辰！”可对方根本听不到她的声音。
[EN] Rocking back and forth under the quake, the Millennial Divine Willow wrenched free from the soil and began to topple straight toward Qianli Xun standing underneath. Spotting the falling trunk, Shen Jianchen scrambled up off the tilting ground and sprinted toward her. The split second he threw his body over hers, the ancient willow crashed down atop them both, crushing them into the heaving dirt as blood soaked into the earth.
[ZH] 等到申简辰飘得足够高时，他们四目相对。千里寻能感受到，申简辰大声呼唤着她的名字，她努力回应着，却互相听不到对方的声音。
[EN] Ten minutes later, stillness fell over the wreckage. Qianli Xun felt herself peel loose from her body and drift into the air. Looking down at her mangled corpse pinned under the willow tore at her heart with searing grief. Then she saw Shen Jianchen separate from his body too. "Shen Jianchen! Shen Jianchen!" she shouted, yet he could not hear a single sound.
[ZH] 突然，从千年神柳粗壮的树干上涌出一股神力，将飘在空中的两人席卷而去。千里寻只觉眼前一片黑暗，她害怕极了……
[EN] When Shen Jianchen floated high enough, their eyes met. She could tell he was calling her name with everything he had, and she strained to answer, yet silence walled them off from each other. Then an immense, unearthly force surged from the Divine Willow's massive trunk and swept both spirits away. Pitch darkness swallowed Qianli Xun's sight, wrapping her in terror...

#CH 3
[ZH] 被神秘的力量牵引着，千里寻不敢睁开眼睛，速度之快，加上恐惧，她连用心体会的机会都没有，只觉脑中一片空白，直到那股力量狠狠地把她甩了出去……
[EN] Dragged along by a mysterious force, Qianli Xun dared not open her eyes. The sheer speed and terror left her no room to process a thing; her mind went blank until the force flung her out with brutal strength...
[ZH] 她听到一个声音：“小姐，您醒醒，快醒醒啊！呜呜，姑爷，你该不会是把我家小姐打死了？呜呜……！”
[EN] A voice reached her ears: "Miss, wake up, please wake up! Sob... Son-in-law, did you beat my Miss to death? Sob..."
[ZH] 千里寻在内心寻思着：“小姐？姑爷？该不会是做梦吧？不，我，千里寻，在地震中丧生，难道来到另一个世界？是死后的世界？会不会很可怕？”
[EN] Qianli Xun wondered in silence, Miss? Son-in-law? Am I dreaming? No—I, Qianli Xun, died in that earthquake. Did I end up in another world? The afterlife? Is it going to be terrifying?
[ZH] 她依然闭着眼，眼皮却能感受到周围光线不错，难道死后的世界也有光线？不如睁开眼瞧瞧。
[EN] Her eyes stayed shut, yet through her eyelids she sensed bright light around her. Did the afterlife have daylight too? She might as well take a look.
[ZH] 千里寻努力睁开眼睛，看到一位身着古装的丫鬟蹲在自己身边哭泣。
[EN] Straining to lift her eyelids, Qianli Xun saw a maid in period clothing kneeling beside her in tears.
[ZH] 丫鬟看到小姐睁开眼睛，立刻转悲为喜：“谢天谢地，小姐您还活着。”
[EN] The moment the maid saw her open her eyes, grief flipped to joy. "Thank goodness! Miss, you're still alive!"
[ZH] 千里寻问：“我是不是死了？这难道是死后的世界？你是谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, "Am I dead? Is this the afterlife? Who are you?"
[ZH] 丫鬟强调道：“小姐，您没死，您还活着，我是您的丫鬟苦荞呀！”
[EN] The maid insisted, "Miss, you aren't dead, you're alive! I'm your maid, Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “我的丫鬟？苦荞？”千里寻写过好几部穿越故事的网络小说，这桥段太像她在小说里写到的，“难道我穿越了？不是真的死亡？”
[EN] "My maid? Kuqiao?" Qianli Xun had written several transmigration web novels, and this setup felt straight out of her own chapters. "Did I transmigrate? I didn't really die?"
[ZH] 想到这里，她来了精神，才发现自己躺在地上。苦荞扶她站起来，只觉额头十分疼痛，用手摸一下，手指上竟然沾了些血迹。千里寻从小到大何时被人伤过？刚要发作，想起地震时尸身在血泊中的情景，又能活过来已是恩赐，气也就消去不少。
[EN] Her spirits lifted at the thought, and only then did she notice she was sprawled on the floor. Kuqiao helped her up. A sharp throb shot through her forehead; she touched it and found blood on her fingertips. Since childhood, nobody had ever laid a violent hand on Qianli Xun. Anger flared—until she remembered her body lying in a pool of blood during the earthquake. Coming back to life was already a gift. Most of her fury drained away.
[ZH] 她站稳后，瞧到面前站着一男一女。男的大约二十来岁，长相一般，但身材魁梧，一看就是个练家子，目光中写着“不满”二字。旁边那女的打扮得花枝招展，溜着头发，一身娇媚姿态，很不屑地瞧着自己。
[EN] Steady on her feet, she saw a man and a woman standing before her. The man looked to be in his twenties—average features, broad shoulders, clearly a martial artist—with irritation written all over his stare. Beside him stood a flashy woman with slicked hair and a coy posture, sizing Qianli Xun up with open disdain.
[ZH] 他们都一身古装，千里寻搞不清楚那古装叫什么，毕竟她曾经属于另一个时代。她瞧瞧周围，虽然里面的布置与现代化相去甚远，可看上去这户人家家境不错。
[EN] Both wore period clothes whose exact names Qianli Xun had no idea about, coming as she did from another era. She glanced around. Far removed from modern decor, sure, yet the furnishings spoke of a well-off household.
[ZH] 她瞅瞅自己身上，也是一身古装，再摸摸头上，有发髻、金钗，看来是真的穿越了。
[EN] She looked down at herself—period clothes too—and felt her head: coiled hair, gold hairpins. She really had transmigrated.
[ZH] 千里寻把苦荞拉到一边：“你刚说你是我的丫鬟，名字叫苦荞？”
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled Kuqiao aside. "You just said you're my maid, and your name is Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 苦荞瞪着眼睛点点头：“小姐，您该不会被姑爷打傻了？怎么会问我这个问题？”
[EN] Eyes wide, Kuqiao nodded. "Miss, did Son-in-law hit you so hard you lost your wits? Why are you asking me that?"
[ZH] “苦荞，你告诉我，我是谁？”
[EN] "Kuqiao, tell me—who am I?"
[ZH] “小姐，看来您真的被姑爷打傻了，连自己是谁都不知道。”
[EN] "Miss, he really did beat the sense out of you. You don't even know who you are!"
[ZH] 苦荞没有急着回答，走到男人面前：“姑爷，朱府老爷怎么着也是临安知府，官职品阶在你之上，你竟然敢出手打我家小姐，小心我回朱府告诉老爷治你的罪！”
[EN] Instead of answering right away, Kuqiao marched up to the man. "Son-in-law, Master Zhu is the Prefect of Lin'an—his rank is far above yours! How dare you lay a hand on my Miss! Just wait till I go back to Zhu Manor and tell Master to punish you!"
[ZH] 面对言语威胁，男人并不害怕：“她嫁给林家，就是林家的人，我打她是在教育她，嫁出去的女儿泼出去的水，临安知府也没有权利干涉。”
[EN] Unfazed by the threat, the man sneered, "Once she married into the Lin family, she became a Lin. I'm disciplining my wife. A married daughter is like water poured out—even the Prefect of Lin'an has no right to meddle."
[ZH] 千里寻越听越糊涂，越听越担心，什么嫁给林家？难道她穿越的这位已嫁给那男的？她顾不上其他，继续拉过苦荞：“快告诉我，我是谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun grew more bewildered and uneasy by the second. Married into the Lin family? Was the body she had landed in married to that guy? Setting everything else aside, she tugged Kuqiao back. "Hurry up and tell me who I am!"
[ZH] 苦荞看着可怜的小姐，告诉她：“您叫朱淑真，是朱府的千金。”
[EN] Looking at her poor mistress, Kuqiao answered, "Your name is Zhu Shuzhen, daughter of Zhu Manor."
[ZH] “朱淑真？难道是宋代女词人朱淑真？”千里寻读过不少朱淑真的诗词，很是喜欢，大部分背得滚瓜烂熟，“现在是什么年代？”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen? The Song-dynasty female ci poet Zhu Shuzhen?" Qianli Xun had read plenty of Zhu Shuzhen's poems, loved them, and knew most by heart. "What year is it?"
[ZH] 男人旁边的女人用娇滴滴的声音说：“练兵官，看来你真的把她打傻了，竟然不知道自己是谁，连什么年代都不知道，我来告诉你。”
[EN] The woman beside the man piped up in a sugary voice, "Training Officer, looks like you really knocked her silly. She doesn't know who she is or what era she's in. Let me tell her."
[ZH] 女人走到千里寻面前：“现在是大宋宣和四年。”
[EN] She sashayed over to Qianli Xun. "This is the fourth year of the Xuanhe reign of the Great Song."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“大宋宣和四年，果真是宋代，看来我真的穿越成女词人朱淑真，我作为千里寻生活的那个时代，朱淑真留下的资料太少，生卒年不祥，到底是北宋还是南宋也搞不清楚，既然是宣和四年，看来是北宋了……”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought to herself, The fourth year of Xuanhe of the Great Song—it really is the Song dynasty, and I really transmigrated into the poet Zhu Shuzhen. Back in my era, almost no records survived about her—unknown birth and death dates, people couldn't even agree whether she lived in the Northern or Southern Song. Since it's the fourth year of Xuanhe, Northern Song it is...
[ZH] 女人又拉着腔调说：“朱家姑娘，你既嫁给练兵官，就应该好好尽妻子的义务，不是我说你，他是个血气方刚的男人，把你这么一个如花似玉的女子放在家里，看着却不能碰，当然生气，才会对你拳打脚踢。”
[EN] The woman drawled on, "Miss Zhu, since you married the Training Officer, you ought to do your wifely duty. Not to lecture you, mind you, but he's a red-blooded man. Keeping a beauty like you at home where he can look but not touch—of course he loses his temper and beats you!"
[ZH] “什么？拳打脚踢？”
[EN] "What? Beats me?"
[ZH] 千里寻看着眼前这个男人，苦荞称他为姑爷，这个女人又说朱家姑娘嫁给练兵官，看来他就是朱淑真嫁的丈夫。按照千里寻了解的资料，朱淑真嫁人时正值二十岁，岂不是穿越后自己直接年轻了将近十岁？
[EN] Qianli Xun stared at the man. Kuqiao called him Son-in-law, and this woman said Miss Zhu had married the Training Officer—so he was Zhu Shuzhen's husband. From what Qianli Xun remembered reading, Zhu Shuzhen had married at twenty. Had transmigration just shaved almost ten years off her age?
[ZH] 刚有些高兴，又觉得不对。千里寻到三十岁都没谈过恋爱，穿越成朱淑真才二十岁就嫁人，努力保住的清白就这样付诸东流，而且还被眼前的男人拳打脚踢。
[EN] A spark of delight flashed, then soured. Qianli Xun had stayed single right up to thirty, only to land in a twenty-year-old married body—her hard-kept chastity gone down the drain, and stuck with a husband who beat her to boot!
[ZH] 想到这里，千里寻走到男人跟前：“你是朱淑真的丈夫？叫什么名字？干什么的？”
[EN] She marched straight up to the man. "You're Zhu Shuzhen's husband? What's your name? What do you do?"
[ZH] 男人只觉眼前的妻子被自己拳打脚踢，一把推到柱子跟前被撞晕后，醒来不仅看着怪怪的，说话的语气也比以前硬气许多。他充满怀疑，可亲眼目睹一切，并未发生异样，况且她的样子就是朱淑真，并非他人。为了保持大男子的形象，男人并未理朱淑真，头扭向一边。
[EN] The man stared back. After he had punched, kicked, and shoved his wife into a pillar until she blacked out, she had woken up looking odd and speaking with far more backbone than before. Suspicion gnawed at him, yet he had watched the whole thing happen with his own eyes—nothing strange had occurred, and her face was unmistakably Zhu Shuzhen's. Keeping up his macho front, he ignored her and turned his head away.
[ZH] 女人过去解释道：“他就是你的丈夫，叫林觉，是鼎鼎大名的练兵官，负责临安所有军队的训练和演练，你能嫁给林练兵官，就偷着乐吧！”
[EN] The woman stepped in to explain. "He's your husband, Lin Jue, the famous Training Officer in charge of drilling every soldier in Lin'an. You should count yourself lucky you married Officer Lin!"
[ZH] 练兵官？千里寻寻思，据她了解的资料上说朱淑真嫁了一位小吏，她对这段婚姻十分不满，整日郁郁寡欢。瞧眼前这位练兵官，趾高气扬，根本没有把自己的妻子放在眼里，她千里寻都瞧不上，何况词坛双璧之一——朱淑真。
[EN] Training Officer? Qianli Xun mulled it over. The historical notes she knew said Zhu Shuzhen had married a minor clerk, hated the match, and pined away in misery. Looking at this cocky Training Officer who treated his wife like dirt, even Qianli Xun despised him—let alone Zhu Shuzhen, one of the Twin Jewels of Ci Poetry.
[ZH] 千里寻决定要替朱淑真教训教训林觉，那就从眼前这个女人开始：“老娘既然嫁给他，就是他的正室，你又是从哪里冒出来的？难道是他娶的小妾？”
[EN] Qianli Xun decided to teach Lin Jue a lesson on Zhu Shuzhen's behalf, starting with the woman beside him. "Since I married him, I'm his primary wife. Where did you crawl out from? Are you a concubine he took?"
[ZH] 林觉和那女人更觉讶异，朱淑真本柔柔弱弱，怎么一下子说话既硬气又粗鲁？
[EN] Lin Jue and the woman gaped. Fragile, quiet Zhu Shuzhen—since when did she talk so tough and blunt?
[ZH] 苦荞赶紧解释：“小姐，她是姑爷从青楼带回来的，是春香楼的商妓，叫春金巧，姑爷经常把她带回家气您。今日就是因为又把她带回家，您依然懒得理姑爷，姑爷才对您拳打脚踢。”
[EN] Kuqiao rushed to explain. "Miss, she's a commercial courtesan Son-in-law brings home from Chunxiang Brothel—Chun Jinqiao. He drags her here all the time just to spite you. Today he brought her back again, and when you still ignored him, he started hitting and kicking you."
[ZH] 听到这里，千里寻气不打一处来，她最讨厌打女人的男人，而且还公然把青楼女子带到家里来。
[EN] Fury surged through Qianli Xun. She loathed men who hit women, and this bastard had the nerve to haul a courtesan right into the house.
[ZH] “林觉，你是不是男人？竟然因为青楼女子打老婆，有本事到院子里跟老娘打一架！”
[EN] "Lin Jue, call yourself a man? Beating your wife over a brothel girl? If you've got the guts, step into the courtyard and fight me!"
[ZH] 林觉心里犯嘀咕：“她怎么跟以前的朱淑真完全不同？”
[EN] Doubt churned in Lin Jue's gut. How is she nothing like the old Zhu Shuzhen?
[ZH] 林觉认为眼前的朱淑真晕倒后醒来风格大变，也不可能长什么新本事，不过是失忆，忘了他有多厉害，不如再教训她一顿，让她长长记性，知道嫁为人妇该尽的义务还是要尽的。
[EN] Even if her personality had flipped after fainting, he figured she could not have picked up new skills out of thin air. She must have lost her memory and forgotten how strong he was. Better give her another beating to jog her memory and remind her of a wife's duties.
[ZH] “既如此，为夫就陪你练练，这次为夫不会把你打晕，最多教训一下你。”
[EN] "Fine, then. Your husband will spar with you. This time I won't knock you cold—just teach you a little lesson."
[ZH] 大家来到院中，不一会儿林府的下人都围过来，他们很好奇，以前主君打夫人总是关了门在屋里，这次怎么闹到院子里来？
[EN] They moved out to the courtyard. Servants of Lin Manor soon gathered around, curious. Usually their master shut the doors before beating his wife; why had the fight spilled outside?
[ZH] 林觉很不屑地说：“朱淑真，为夫让着你，你先来。”
[EN] Lin Jue scoffed, "Zhu Shuzhen, I'll go easy on you. You strike first."
[ZH] 千里寻握紧拳头：“既然你让朱淑真先来，别怪老娘不客气。”说完，千里寻一拳打去。
[EN] Qianli Xun clenched her fists. "You offered Zhu Shuzhen the first move—don't blame me for not holding back!" With that, she threw a punch.
[ZH] 林觉没当回事，认为朱淑真本是娇弱之人，拳头能有什么力度，只要轻轻一推，她肯定倒下。
[EN] Lin Jue brushed it off. Delicate as Zhu Shuzhen was, what force could her punch hold? One light shove would drop her.
[ZH] 他伸出手掌去推袭击而来的拳头，当手掌挨到拳头时，感受到强大的威力，不由心里一惊：“她怎么突然变得如此厉害？”
[EN] He raised a palm to brush her fist aside. The instant skin met skin, crushing force slammed into his hand, jolting his heart. How did she get this strong out of nowhere?
[ZH] 还没反应过来，千里寻另一只拳头已打在林觉的眼睛上，立刻出现一片淤青。再上一脚，将林觉踢翻在地，顺势用一只腿压在林觉的身上，并将两只手固定住：“就这两下子还有脸当练兵官？苦荞！”
[EN] Before he could recover, Qianli Xun's other fist smashed into his eye, blooming into an instant bruise. A swift kick swept him off his feet. She pinned his torso under one knee and locked both his arms behind him. "With skills this pathetic, you dare call yourself a Training Officer? Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐变得厉害，诧异的同时，又觉甚好，以后就不怕被人欺负，干脆利落地答道：“在！”
[EN] Startled yet thrilled to see her mistress turn fierce—no more getting bullied!—Kuqiao snapped back crisp and clear, "Here!"
[ZH] “拿绳子来！”
[EN] "Bring me a rope!"
[ZH] 很快苦荞拿来粗绳，递给小姐。
[EN] Kuqiao dashed off and returned moments later with thick hemp rope.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，迅速把林觉绑起来，看到旁边站着两位下人：“你们两个过来，把他押到屋子里关起来，只许送水送饭，不准放他出来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun took it, trussed Lin Jue up in seconds, and pointed at two male servants standing nearby. "You two, get over here. Haul him into a room and lock him up. Give him food and water, nothing else—nobody lets him out!"
[ZH] 两个男仆很听话，把林觉扶起来。
[EN] Obediently, the two servants hauled Lin Jue to his feet.
[ZH] ﻿林觉虽被绑，嘴上依然不饶人：“小心我到官府告你欺压夫君！”
[EN] Tied up tight, Lin Jue still ran his mouth. "Watch out, or I'll report you to the local yamen for assaulting your husband!"
[ZH] “我还想告你不守夫道！”
[EN] "Go ahead—I'll report you for failing your duty as a husband!"
[ZH] “你不是朱淑真！”林觉终于说出心里犯嘀咕的话。
[EN] "You aren't Zhu Shuzhen!" Lin Jue burst out with the doubt eating at him.
[ZH] 千里寻走到林觉跟前，在他耳边轻声说：“你最好认为我就是朱淑真，否则她就是被你家暴而亡，毕竟是条人命，告到官府那里，你是要吃官司的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped close and murmured in his ear, "You'd better believe I'm Zhu Shuzhen. Otherwise that means you beat her to death. That's a human life—take it to the yamen and see who lands in a cell."
[ZH] 林觉只能闭口不言，被下人带进一个屋子关起来。
[EN] Lin Jue clamped his mouth shut as the servants dragged him off and locked him inside a room.
[ZH] 千里寻看一眼春金巧，走过去，狠狠在她脸上扇了一巴掌：“这是替朱淑真教训你的，还不赶紧滚回青楼去！”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned her gaze on Chun Jinqiao, strode over, and cracked a heavy slap across her face. "That one's for Zhu Shuzhen. Now get out of here and crawl back to your brothel!"
[ZH] 春金巧想要还回一掌，结果被千里寻挡住，她的力气很大，亏是吃定了，只能捂着被打疼的脸，边哭边扭着腰肢离开林府。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao swung back to return the slap, only for Qianli Xun to block her arm cold. Overmatched by sheer strength, she knew better than to stay; clutching her stinging cheek, she swayed out of Lin Manor in tears.

#CH 4
[ZH] 一切处理完毕，千里寻感到额头一阵刺痛：“苦荞，你家小姐住哪个屋子？赶紧带我进去，让我好好休息休息。”
[EN] With the mess settled, a sharp sting shot across Qianli Xun's forehead. "Kuqiao, which room is your Miss's? Take me there right now—I need proper rest."
[ZH] 苦荞上前扶住小姐：“小姐跟我来，您住的屋子就在后面。”
[EN] Kuqiao stepped forward to steady her. "Come with me, Miss. Your quarters are right in the back."
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝苦荞的搀扶，她太累。地震可怕的场景还历历在目，那股神力带来的恐惧感也未消失，穿越成朱淑真，又需要时间适应。帮朱淑真处理完那两个家伙，一轻松，疲惫感自然袭来。
[EN] Qianli Xun did not refuse the support; exhaustion weighed her down. The horrors of the earthquake still burned behind her eyes, dread from that uncanny force lingered, and waking up as Zhu Shuzhen took energy to process. Once those two nuisances were dealt with and her tension dropped, fatigue hit like a wave.
[ZH] 苦荞扶着小姐拐到后面。千里寻抬头看到一个园门，上写竹兰居。走进院中，发现到处种着竹子和兰花，倒也符合竹兰居的雅称。
[EN] Kuqiao guided her around to the rear. Qianli Xun looked up at a garden gate inscribed "Zhulan Residence." Inside the courtyard, bamboo and orchids filled every corner, living up to the refined name.
[ZH] 进入屋子，给人一种古朴而典雅的感觉，家具都是用红木打制而成，桌上放着精致的花瓶和香炉，增添了一丝清新。窗帘是用织物制作而成，使得房间充满通透感，上面的花鸟图案为房间添加了生气。墙壁上挂着字画，加上案上的书卷，只觉得书香之气扑面而来。
[EN] Stepping inside the rooms brought an understated, classic grace. Every piece of furniture was carved from rosewood; a delicate vase and incense burner sat on the table, lending a fresh charm. Sheer woven curtains let light breathe through the room, their bird-and-flower patterns adding life. Calligraphy scrolls hung along the walls, and volumes stacked on the desk filled the air with a scholar's quiet fragrance.
[ZH] “朱淑真住的屋子真不错！”千里寻边说边坐到床榻上。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen has quite a nice place!" Qianli Xun said, sinking onto the bed.
[ZH] “您说的哪里话？好像您不是我家小姐一样？”苦荞虽怀疑，却不愿承认，她找来一个小木盒，打开，用干净的巾帕从里面沾些药膏，涂在小姐额头的伤口上，“这药很管用，不出两日，小姐额头的伤就好了。”
[EN] "What are you saying, Miss, talking as if you aren't yourself?" Doubt lingered in Kuqiao's mind, yet she refused to accept it. She fetched a small wooden box, opened it, dabbed ointment onto a clean cloth, and applied it to her mistress's forehead. "This salve works wonders. In two days your cut will heal."
[ZH] 涂完药，苦荞把小木盒放好，乖巧地站在一边。
[EN] Once done, Kuqiao put the box away and stood obediently to the side.
[ZH] 千里寻瞅着苦荞：“你靠近些，我给你说件事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun eyed her. "Come closer. I have something to tell you."
[ZH] 苦荞小心靠近。
[EN] Kuqiao stepped in with caution.
[ZH] 千里寻压低声音：“其实我不是你家小姐，我本叫千里寻，在我们那时代遇到大地震，估计得有7.5级以上，我在地震中丧生，魂魄被一股可怕的力量卷走，醒来时，就跑到朱淑真身上，到现在我都不相信这会是真的。据我估计，你家小姐已被林觉家暴而亡。”
[EN] Lowering her voice, Qianli Xun said, "Truth is, I'm not your Miss. My real name is Qianli Xun. In my era, a massive earthquake struck—at least magnitude 7.5. I died in it, a terrifying force swept my soul away, and when I woke up, I was inside Zhu Shuzhen's body. I still can't believe it's real. My guess is Lin Jue beat your real Miss to death."
[ZH] 苦荞不愿承认，跪在地上：“您就是我家小姐，只是被姑爷打晕，失忆了而已。”
[EN] Refusing to accept it, Kuqiao dropped to her knees. "You are my Miss! Son-in-law just knocked you cold and made you lose your memory!"
[ZH] “你也看到，我会功夫，你家小姐可会？”
[EN] "You saw me fight. Did your Miss know martial arts?"
[ZH] “我家小姐只会写诗作词，一点儿功夫都不会，可这样貌、身段就是我家小姐，别人只会认为您是朱府千金。”
[EN] "My Miss only knew poetry and lyrics—not a scrap of martial arts. Still, that face and figure are hers. Everyone will take you for the daughter of Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 苦荞的话说得没错，千里寻在地震中已死亡，老天又给了一次新生的机会让她成为朱淑真，在这个时代别人只会认为她是朱淑真。
[EN] Kuqiao had a point. Qianli Xun had died in the quake, and Heaven had handed her a second life as Zhu Shuzhen. Nobody in this era would ever see her as anyone else.
[ZH] “起来吧，跪着干什么？唉，我既成为你家小姐，那就老老实实做朱淑真，只是她的记忆我一点儿都没有。我还会功夫，重要的是我说话的风格跟你家小姐肯定不一样，别人很容易认出来，你没听林觉都说我不是朱淑真吗？”
[EN] "Get up—why are you kneeling? Fine. Since I've stepped into her shoes, I'll live as Zhu Shuzhen. Problem is, I don't have a shred of her memories. Plus I know martial arts, and my way of talking is nothing like hers. People will spot it right away—didn't you hear Lin Jue already saying I'm not Zhu Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 苦荞想了想说：“小姐放心，我们乡下有时也会发生这样的怪事，一个人突然生一场病，病好后就像变了个人，乡下管这叫重生。到时别人问起，就说姑爷把您打晕，醒来后重生了，当然跟以前不一样。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought for a moment. "Don't worry, Miss. Back in our village, strange things like this happen sometimes—someone falls ill, recovers, and acts like a whole new person. Country folk call it 'rebirth.' If anyone asks, we'll say Son-in-law knocked you unconscious, and when you woke up you were reborn—of course you're different now!"
[ZH] “好！就这么说！再说事实如此。对了，我问你，为什么林觉打你家小姐？”
[EN] "Done! We'll go with that—it's pretty much the truth anyway. By the way, why did Lin Jue beat your Miss in the first place?"
[ZH] “怎么还你家小姐，就是小姐您！”
[EN] "There you go saying 'your Miss' again! You're my Miss!"
[ZH] “对，是我，我就是朱淑真。”说这话时，千里寻很不习惯，可她已经决定要老老老实实做朱淑真，以后还是把千里寻这个名字藏在心底。
[EN] "Right, right—me. I'm Zhu Shuzhen." The words still felt awkward on her tongue, yet having chosen to live as Zhu Shuzhen, she resolved to tuck the name Qianli Xun deep inside her heart.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“当初老爷把小姐您嫁给姑爷时，您老大不愿意，因为姑爷根本不是您喜欢的类型，他只会练兵，人又粗鲁，常常醉酒而归，而您喜欢的是能陪您花前月下吟诗作赋之人。所以，嫁给姑爷后您一直未与他圆房……”
[EN] Kuqiao explained, "When Master married you off to Son-in-law, you didn't want it one bit. He's nothing like the man you dreamed of—all he knows is drilling troops, he's rough, and he comes home drunk all the time. You wanted someone to recite poetry with beneath flowers and moonlight. So after the wedding, you never consummated the marriage..."
[ZH] 听到未圆房，千里寻忍不住高兴：“太好了！我的清白总算保住！”
[EN] Hearing that, Qianli Xun brightened at once. "Yes! My virtue is still intact!"
[ZH] “正因为您不愿与姑爷圆房，又以死相逼，不准姑爷靠近，他才把商妓领到家里，当着您的面做出那些见不得人的事，姑爷见您毫不介意，更加生气，才大打出手。”
[EN] "Because you refused to consummate the marriage and even threatened to kill yourself if he came near, he started dragging courtesans home and doing shameless things right in front of you. When you didn't even blink, he got madder and started hitting you."
[ZH] “原来如此，为何朱老爷，不，我爹要把我嫁给一个我不喜欢的人？”千里寻开始慢慢承认自己就是朱淑真。
[EN] "So that's why. Then why did Master Zhu—no, my father—marry me to someone I didn't love?" Qianli Xun began accepting her role as Zhu Shuzhen bit by bit.
[ZH] 苦荞对小姐的转变感到满意：“小姐本与徐家公子青梅竹马，两小无猜，怎奈后来徐家败落，不知迁往何处，徐公子临行前允诺小姐一旦考上功名，必然迎娶，可左等右等，等了整整五年，也不见徐公子前来。可您都二十岁，已是老姑娘，再不嫁人只怕没人要，正好姑爷上门提亲，老爷和夫人认为林家不错，便应下这门亲事。”
[EN] Pleased by the shift, Kuqiao went on, "You and Young Master Xu were innocently devoted childhood sweethearts. Sadly, the Xu family fell on hard times and moved away, nobody knows where. Before leaving, Young Master Xu promised he would come marry you once he passed the civil exams. You waited five whole years without a word from him. By then you were twenty—already an old maid—and Master and Madam feared nobody would want you if you waited longer. Right then Son-in-law came to propose. Master and Madam thought the Lin family was a decent match, so they agreed."
[ZH] 千里寻慨叹，无论在哪个时代她都是老姑娘。心里如此想，嘴上却不愿承认：“二十岁怎么能算老姑娘？在我来的那个时代，我都三十岁，还没结婚，周围还有好几个人追求我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed inwardly—an old maid in any era! Out loud, she refused to admit it. "How is twenty an old maid? Where I come from, I was thirty, still single, and still had plenty of guys chasing me!"
[ZH] 说到这里，她想起申简辰，他也被神秘的力量卷走，不知道是否也穿越到这个时代，希望他安然无恙。
[EN] At that, Shen Jianchen flashed across her mind. That mysterious force had swept him away too. Had he landed in this era as well? She hoped he was safe.
[ZH] 苦荞瞪大眼睛：“什么？三十岁还没嫁人？要知道在这个时代女子三十岁都是当奶奶的岁数。”
[EN] Kuqiao's eyes popped wide. "What? Still unmarried at thirty? In this day and age, women are grandmothers by thirty!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“三十岁就当奶奶？你骗我吧！”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "Grandmothers at thirty? You're pulling my leg!"
[ZH] “我怎么可能骗小姐您？再说我喜欢现在的小姐。”
[EN] "Why would I lie to you, Miss? Besides, I like you better now."
[ZH] “为什么？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “以前的小姐虽好，可话不多，整日写些我读不懂的诗词，还柔柔弱弱，被姑爷欺负。现在不一样，不仅跟我聊天儿，重要的是会功夫，以后跟小姐混，肯定不会被人欺负。”
[EN] "The old Miss was kind, sure, yet she kept quiet, spent all day writing poems I couldn't understand, and let Son-in-law push her around because she was frail. You're different—you chat with me, and best of all, you know martial arts! Sticking with you, nobody's going to bully us ever again!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“你家小姐我又渴又饿，赶紧弄点吃的喝的来，我吃饱喝足好美美睡一觉。”
[EN] Qianli Xun chuckled. "Your Miss is starving and parched. Go grab me some food and drink so I can fill up and get a good sleep."
[ZH] “好嘞，我这就给小姐去准备。”
[EN] "Right away! I'll go fetch it for you."
[ZH] 苦荞出去后，千里寻走到铜镜跟前，镜子模模糊糊，根本无法与玻璃镜子相比。她还是看到镜子中的脸与千里寻根本不是一人，她只是朱淑真。
[EN] After Kuqiao left, Qianli Xun walked over to the bronze mirror. The hazy surface couldn't compare to modern glass, yet it still showed clearly that the face staring back wasn't Qianli Xun—it belonged to Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] 她在镜前坐下，认真欣赏这张脸，既古典又精致：“没想到朱淑真也是个美人儿。”
[EN] Sitting down, she admired the refined, classic features. "Turns out Zhu Shuzhen's a real beauty."
[ZH] 她对镜中说：“朱淑真，你听着，以后你就是我，我就是你，我要借着你的名字和身份在属于你的时代生活下去。”说完，一一去掉头饰。
[EN] She spoke to her reflection. "Listen up, Zhu Shuzhen. From now on, you're me and I'm you. I'll borrow your name and your life to live on in your era." With that, she removed her hairpins one by one.
[ZH] 朱淑真的头发真是又多又长，漆黑如墨，这发质，放在千里寻那个时代绝对可以接洗发水广告。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen's hair was thick, long, and black as ink—good enough to land a shampoo commercial back in Qianli Xun's time.
[ZH] 正想着，苦荞用盘子端着饭食进来：“小姐，厨房正好做了您爱吃的小鸡双色莲子羹和野菜包子，我给您端些来，您先垫垫。”
[EN] Just then, Kuqiao returned carrying a tray. "Miss, the kitchen happened to make your favorites—two-color lotus seed chicken soup and wild vegetable steamed buns. I brought some to tide you over."
[ZH] 千里寻已忍不住，赶紧坐到桌前：“大宋的伙食真不错，虽然只有两样，却看着精致，尤其是这小鸡……”
[EN] Unable to wait, Qianli Xun hurried to the table. "Song food looks great! Only two dishes, yet so delicate—especially this chicken..."
[ZH] “小鸡双色莲子羹。”苦荞见小姐一时没记住，又说了一遍。
[EN] "Two-color lotus seed chicken soup," Kuqiao prompted when her mistress stumbled over the name.
[ZH] “对，小鸡双色莲子羹，听这名字就直到不好做，估计得好几道工序，而且熬制时间还不短。”
[EN] "Right, two-color lotus seed chicken soup. You can tell just from the name it takes a ton of steps and hours of simmering."
[ZH] “小姐说得没错，确实麻烦，您赶紧享用吧。”
[EN] "You're right, Miss, it's real work. Go ahead and eat."
[ZH] 千里寻已忍不住，大口吃了起来，确实美味。
[EN] Qianli Xun dug in with big bites. Delicious.
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁看着她的吃相，比以前的小姐豪放许多。想起被关的姑爷，为难道：“小姐，姑爷在屋子里大喊大叫，说您殴打夫君，要到衙门告您，依我看，不如把他放了，省得他胡乱喊叫。”
[EN] Watching her eat with far more gusto than her former mistress ever had, Kuqiao remembered the prisoner and frowned. "Miss, Son-in-law is hollering his head off in that room, saying you assaulted your husband and threatening to haul you before the yamen. Maybe we should let him out before he raises the roof?"
[ZH] 千里寻喝口羹，三下五除二吃完一个包子，边吃边说：“没关系，等我吃饱，把他的嘴堵住，就没人能听到他瞎掰掰。”
[EN] Qianli Xun took a sip of soup and polished off a bun in three bites. "No problem. Once I finish eating, I'll gag him so nobody hears his nonsense."
[ZH] “可姑爷毕竟是练兵官，您关押临安城的练兵官，恐怕官府会治罪。”
[EN] "He's still the Training Officer, though! Locking up Lin'an's Training Officer—won't the local yamen charge you?"
[ZH] “治什么罪？这是家事，官府管不着，再说官府真要来管，应该先管管他怎么家暴你家小姐。”
[EN] "Charge me with what? It's a domestic matter—none of their business! And if the yamen wants to poke its nose in, they can start by asking why he beats his wife!"
[ZH] 千里寻把羹和包子吃了个精光，起身说：“羹虽好喝，解渴差些，你再给我弄些茶水来，我先去看看林觉，回来好喝。”
[EN] Having cleaned the bowl and plate, Qianli Xun stood up. "Great soup, didn't quench my thirst though. Make me some tea—I'll go check on Lin Jue and drink it when I get back."
[ZH] “是。”苦荞说完，看着小姐出了屋门，走到放着做茶工具的茶桌前开始做茶。
[EN] "Yes, Miss." Watching her step out, Kuqiao walked to the tea table stocked with utensils and set to work.
[ZH] 千里寻来到关林觉的屋外，一名下人端着盘子和空碗出来，显然是刚刚伺候林觉用过饭，见到千里寻前来，赶紧行礼：“夫人。”
[EN] Outside the room holding Lin Jue, a servant emerged with a tray and an empty bowl—he had just fed the master. Seeing Qianli Xun, he bowed in haste. "Madam."
[ZH] 千里寻对这个称呼很不喜欢，她已是朱淑真，只能接受，没好气地问：“他在里面可老实？”
[EN] She hated the title, yet as Zhu Shuzhen she had to swallow it. "Is he behaving in there?" she asked in a flat voice.
[ZH] 下人怎能随便说主人家老实或不老实，随口回道：“主君被夫人绑着很不舒服。”
[EN] Hardly daring to judge his master's behavior, the servant hedged, "Master is uncomfortable tied up like that, Madam."
[ZH] “你下去吧。”
[EN] "Leave us."
[ZH] 下人离开后，千里寻推开门，穿过折叠的木质屏风，看到林觉被五花大绑躺在床上，这会儿倒老实，没有胡乱喊叫。
[EN] Once the servant left, Qianli Xun pushed the door open, stepped past the folding wooden screen, and spotted Lin Jue trussed up tight on the bed—quiet now, no longer yelling.
[ZH] 林觉听到有脚步声，立刻坐起来，到底是有些武功根基的人，不用手撑着，都能迅速起身。
[EN] At her footsteps, he sat straight up—proof of real martial training, rising without even using his hands.
[ZH] 见朱淑真向他走来，头上没有发饰，依然十分貌美，可给他的感觉既熟悉又陌生。这个女人以前只会写些他看不懂的诗词，还整日愁眉不展，幽怨多情，显得既酸臭又清高，而且从心里瞧不上他这位武夫。为何被他家暴打得晕死后醒来竟像变了个人？不仅眉头舒展许多，说话的声音也变大，关键是会功夫，拳头实在厉害。
[EN] Watching Zhu Shuzhen walk toward him, hair bare of ornaments yet still striking, Lin Jue felt an eerie mix of familiar and strange. Before, this woman only scribbled poems he could not fathom, wore a constant frown, acted aloof and priggish, and looked down her nose at a military man like him. How had one beating knocked her out and turned her into someone else—brow relaxed, voice loud, and packing punches like iron?
[ZH] 林觉决定先服软：“娘子，我们毕竟是夫妻，你把夫君捆起来传出去被人笑话，还是把我放开。”
[EN] Lin Jue chose to soften first. "Wife, we're still husband and wife. Word gets out you tied up your husband, people will laugh at us. Untie me."
[ZH] 千里寻手叉腰：“谁是你娘子？谁跟你是夫妻？告诉你，以前的朱淑真已被你打死，老娘是重生后的朱淑真，不仅没以前好欺负，还要让你老老实实休了我！”
[EN] Qianli Xun planted her hands on her hips. "Who's your wife? Who's married to you? Let me make this clear: the old Zhu Shuzhen died under your fists! I'm the reborn Zhu Shuzhen—not only am I done being your punching bag, you're going to sit right there and write me a bill of divorce!"

#CH 5
[ZH] 林觉之所以会到朱府提亲，只因偶然一次参加临安知府朱晞颜，正是他现任的岳父大人，举办的赏花钓鱼宴时，与朱淑真有过一面之缘。自此，她的美貌就像刻在他的心里，挥之不去。
[EN] Lin Jue had gone to Zhu Manor to propose for one simple reason: at a Flower-Viewing and Fishing Banquet hosted by Prefect of Lin'an Zhu Xiyan—now his father-in-law—he had caught a single glimpse of Zhu Shuzhen. From that day on, her beauty etched itself into his heart and refused to fade.
[ZH] 记得那是一年的春天，百花盛开。朱晞颜差人给族叔林子孝送来请帖，邀请他参加赏花钓鱼宴。林子孝的诗画虽谈不上震惊临安，但在临安城文人中也占有一席之地。而朱晞颜作为临安知府也是位好诗词歌赋的文人，要不怎么会把女儿培养成为女词人。
[EN] That spring, every flower in the city had burst into bloom. Zhu Xiyan sent an invitation to his clan uncle Lin Zixiao for the Flower-Viewing and Fishing Banquet. Though Lin Zixiao's poetry and painting did not shake all of Lin'an, he held a respected place among the city's literati. And Prefect Zhu himself loved verse—how else could he have raised his daughter into a renowned female ci poet?
[ZH] 林子孝接到请帖时，林觉正在一旁，便请族叔带他到赏花钓鱼宴上走一遭，体验一下文人风骚。林子孝说，像这种场合临安没有名头的文人根本没有机会参加，何况林觉只是个武夫。谁让林子孝有面子，能让知府大人瞧得上，带个把人去应该没问题。
[EN] Lin Jue happened to be beside his clan uncle when the invitation arrived and begged to tag along for a taste of scholarly elegance. Lin Zixiao told him lesser-known scholars could not even get through the gate, let alone a military man. Still, Lin Zixiao had enough face with the Prefect to bring one guest along.
[ZH] 就这样林觉参加了赏花钓鱼宴。这种宴会，除了赏花、钓鱼、品茶、听曲儿、宴饮外，最重要的就是赋诗作词，展示文人才华。
[EN] So Lin Jue attended the banquet. Aside from viewing blossoms, fishing, sipping tea, listening to music, and feasting, the main event was composing poetry and ci to showcase literary brilliance.
[ZH] 林觉玩了一会儿，当文人们以“春”为主题赋诗时，他毫无兴致。直到朱淑真出现，他眼前一亮，被深深吸引，内心爱意萌生，平生对附庸风雅的文词诗句最是愚笨的他，竟然把朱淑真做的那首词记在心间，现在依然能吟诵而出:
[EN] After amusing himself for a while, Lin Jue lost all interest once the scholars began writing poems on "spring"—until Zhu Shuzhen stepped forward. His eyes lit up, love struck on the spot, and this man who had zero head for affecting literary airs somehow memorized the entire ci she composed that day. Even now he could recite every word:
[ZH] 迟迟春日弄轻柔，花径暗香流。清明过了，不堪回首，云锁朱楼。
[EN] Slow spring daylight toys with gentle grace, along the flower path a secret fragrance drifts. Qingming has passed; I cannot bear to look back, where clouds lock in the crimson tower.
[ZH] 午窗睡起莺声巧，何处唤春愁？绿杨影里，海棠亭畔，红杏梢头。（《眼儿媚·迟迟春日弄轻柔》）
[EN] Waking by the noon window to clever oriole songs, where do they summon this spring sorrow? Amid green willow shadows, beside the crabapple pavilion, atop the red apricot boughs. ("Yan'er Mei: Slow Spring Daylight Toys with Gentle Grace") [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 恐怕这会成为他一生唯一记住的一首词，这就是爱情的力量。
[EN] It would likely remain the only ci he ever memorized in his life—such was the power of love.
[ZH] 只可惜娶了朱淑真后，曾经美好的想象在现实面前不堪一击。新婚之夜，她不愿圆房，甚至手拿剪刀做出不要命的样子。林觉爱着她，也就放弃了，想着刚成亲她不习惯，以后会慢慢接纳自己，毕竟两人已是夫妻。
[EN] What a pity that once he married her, every sweet daydream shattered against reality. On their wedding night, she refused to consummate the marriage and even held scissors to her own throat. Out of love, Lin Jue backed off, telling himself she only needed time to adjust—they were man and wife now.
[ZH] 事实却相反，他的理解并未换来朱淑真的改变，她不仅不愿与他同桌吃饭，甚至连句话都不愿与他讲，整日不是在屋里写诗作词，就是出去约好友郊游玩乐，甚至还有文人才子在其中。
[EN] Instead, his patience changed nothing. She refused to eat at the same table, wouldn't speak a word to him, and spent every day either shut inside writing verse or going on outings with friends—male scholars included.
[ZH] 林觉再也不想忍下去。有一日，冲进竹兰居，逼迫朱淑真与他圆房。这女人力气不大，却懂得如何保护自己，一脚踢在林觉的要害处，他疼了好一会儿，一气之下开始打她。
[EN] At last Lin Jue snapped. One day he stormed into Zhulan Residence and tried to force himself on her. Frail as she was, she knew where to strike—one kick to his groin doubled him over in agony, and fury drove him to hit her.
[ZH] ﻿被打后，她那个可怜样儿，让林觉既解气、兴奋，又找回丈夫的自尊，于是家暴朱淑真就成为林觉每隔一段时间要做的事情，尤其是想要与她圆房时。后来干脆将青楼女子带回家，当着她的面亲热，换来的依然是朱淑真的冷漠，这冷漠他看着更来气。
[EN] Seeing her battered and helpless gave Lin Jue a twisted rush of relief and restored his wounded pride as a husband. Beating Zhu Shuzhen became a regular habit, especially whenever she refused his bed. Later he went further, hauling brothel courtesans home to fondle right before her eyes—yet her icy indifference only stoked his rage higher.
[ZH] 今日就是在这种情况下又打了她。没成想用的力气大了些，打着打着，一把推去，她撞到柱子上晕死过去。林觉还担心好一阵儿，毕竟打死人要偿命的。谢天谢地，终于醒来，可却变了个人，样貌身段还是朱淑真，其他完全不一样。
[EN] Today had started the exact same way, only he had used too much strength and shoved her into a pillar until she collapsed. Panic had gripped him for a long stretch—killing someone meant paying with his own life. Thank heaven she had woken up, though she seemed a whole new person: same face, same figure, everything else turned upside down.
[ZH] 有一次朱淑真被打，仅仅提出和离，还被他一巴掌打回去，自此再也没提过。没想到眼前这个朱淑真竟然提出让休了她？林觉既诧异又满心不乐意。
[EN] Once before, after a beating, Zhu Shuzhen had mentioned heli—a divorce by mutual consent—only to get slapped down so hard she never breathed the word again. Now this woman demanded he unilaterally repudiate her? Shock and stubbornness knotted in Lin Jue's chest.
[ZH] 林觉又说出那句话：“你不是朱淑真。”
[EN] He repeated the same line: "You aren't Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“你老强调这句话干嘛？难道真想让所有人知道朱淑真因你家暴而亡？让官府把你抓起来？”
[EN] Qianli Xun rolled her eyes. "Why do you keep harping on that? Want everyone to know you beat Zhu Shuzhen to death so the yamen locks you up?"
[ZH] “官府不会抓我，因为所有人都能证明，你跟朱淑真长得一模一样。”
[EN] "The yamen won't arrest me. Everyone can swear you look identical to Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] “那不就结了，我就是朱淑真，你还怀疑什么？”
[EN] "Then that settles it! I am Zhu Shuzhen. What's left to doubt?"
[ZH] “她不会提出让我休了她。”
[EN] "She would never ask me to write a bill of divorce."
[ZH] 千里寻找椅子坐下：“那是以前，现在她的想法已改变。”
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled up a chair and sat. "That was then. She changed her mind."
[ZH] “难道你不知一个女人被丈夫休了是什么下场？”
[EN] "Don't you know what happens to a woman cast out by her husband?"
[ZH] “不管是什么下场，总比和你一起过日子强。”
[EN] "Whatever happens beats spending another day with you."
[ZH] “总之，我不同意！”
[EN] "Either way, I refuse!"
[ZH] 林觉态度强硬，又怎能难倒千里寻：“你不同意，没关系，我把你天天关在家里，不给你吃，不给你喝，也不让你上茅房，屋子里也不放夜壶，关你十天十夜，看你同不同意。”
[EN] His stubbornness didn't faze Qianli Xun one bit. "Refuse all you want. I'll just lock you in here day after day—no food, no water, no latrine, no chamber pot. Ten days and nights of that, let's see if you still refuse!"
[ZH] “你妄想！我是练兵官，天天要操练临安士兵，只要一天不去，就会有人来找我。”
[EN] "Keep dreaming! I'm the Training Officer—I drill Lin'an's troops every day. Miss one shift and people come looking!"
[ZH] “这个容易，大不了我让下人到你上班的地方……”
[EN] "Easy fix. I'll send a servant down to your workplace..."
[ZH] “上班？”
[EN] "Workplace?"
[ZH] 千里寻赶紧改口：“到你练兵的地方给你告个假，就说你病了，而且是很严重的病，需要在家待个十天半月，估计你的上司不会不同意。”
[EN] Qianli Xun corrected herself fast. "To your drill ground to call in sick for you—say you caught something nasty and need ten or fifteen days in bed. Your superior won't say no."
[ZH] “你这个狠毒的女人！即便如此，林府的下人也不会随便被你差遣！”
[EN] "Vicious woman! Even so, the servants of Lin Manor won't take orders from you!"
[ZH] “下人好说，使银子就行，有钱能使鬼推磨，再说刚才那两个下人可是听话得很，连银子都没使，乖乖把你关起来。”
[EN] "Servants are easy—money talks. Even ghosts turn the millstone for silver. Besides, those two guys just now didn't even need a bribe to haul you in here and lock the door!"
[ZH] 林觉气得不行：“这两个家伙！我迟早会把他们赶出去！”
[EN] Lin Jue seethed. "Those two fools! I'll throw them both out sooner or later!"
[ZH] 看这情况，眼前的朱淑真是铁了心要离开他。打又打不过，干脆服软：“跟你夫妻一场，也算缘分，我们好合好散，除被我休了这条路，还有第二条路，就是和离，到时你离开我传出去名声也好听些。”
[EN] Seeing she was dead set on leaving him and knowing he couldn't beat her in a fight, he gave ground. "We've been man and wife—that counts as fate. Let's part on clean terms. Besides a bill of divorce, there's a second way: mutual divorce. Leave that way, and your reputation won't suffer as much."
[ZH] 千里寻并不了解林觉，毕竟刚认识他不久，但仅从打女人这一条判断，不是什么好东西，他的建议还需认真考量：“你能这么好？”
[EN] She barely knew Lin Jue, yet any man who hit women was rotten to the core; his offer warranted suspicion. "Since when are you this nice?"
[ZH] “你晕死后醒来，变成母夜叉，把你放在家里，我会寝食难安，早些把你安顿走，我也好再娶一位娘子过舒坦日子。不过，和离之事我们两个说了不算，必须将你的父母以及有身份的长辈请来，当然林家也会叫来长辈，再把媒人请来，当着所有人的面，征得所有人同意，把财产分割清楚，在和离的文书上签字按下指印，再由媒婆呈送官府，你我的姻缘才能结束。”
[EN] "You wake up from a faint turned into a she-demon—keeping you under my roof will cost me my sleep and my appetite. Send you packing sooner, and I can marry a new wife and live in peace. Still, mutual divorce isn't up to just the two of us. We have to summon your parents and respected elders from both sides, plus the matchmaker. Everybody has to agree in person, divide the property, sign and affix our thumbprints to the divorce papers, and have the matchmaker file them at the yamen. Only then is our marriage over."
[ZH] 千里寻心想，古代离个婚怎么这么麻烦，还得长辈同意。既然已穿越成古人，还是入乡随俗吧。
[EN] Qianli Xun thought, Getting divorced in ancient times is such a hassle—even the elders have to sign off! Still, when in Rome...
[ZH] “我同意，那就和离。”
[EN] "Deal. Mutual divorce it is."
[ZH] “能不能看在我出了这么好的主意，帮我松开绳子？”
[EN] "Seeing as I gave you such a good idea, how about untying me?"
[ZH] 千里寻起身，拿起旁边的布子，走到床前，狠劲儿塞进林觉的嘴里。
[EN] Qianli Xun stood, grabbed a cloth nearby, stepped to the bed, and shoved it straight into Lin Jue's mouth.
[ZH] 林觉怎么反抗都没用。
[EN] No matter how he thrashed, he couldn't stop her.
[ZH] 千里寻指着他说：“本来我想关你好几日，看在你出了这么好一个主意，给你把时间缩短一下，只关你半天一晚上，明日一早再放你出去，这是对你殴打朱淑真的惩罚。”
[EN] She pointed a finger at him. "I was going to lock you up for days, so consider this your reward for the bright idea: just half a day and one night. I'll let you out tomorrow morning. Call it payback for beating Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 千里寻说完，转身离开。
[EN] With that, she turned and walked out.
[ZH] 林觉的嘴被堵着，不能大骂她，只能心里骂道：“走着瞧！你以为和离那么容易，到时长辈们的唾沫星子都能把你淹死，还有你爹娘，他们未必同意！哼！到时你就知道想离开林家没那么容易！”
[EN] Gagged and unable to curse her aloud, Lin Jue raged in his head: Just you wait! Think mutual divorce is that easy? The elders will drown you in lectures—and your parents won't necessarily agree either! Hmph! You'll soon learn walking out of the Lin family isn't that simple!
[ZH] 千里寻回到竹兰居，苦荞刚好将茶做好，还热着，见小姐回来，将茶盏端上：“小姐，喝茶。”
[EN] Back at Zhulan Residence, Kuqiao had just finished making tea, still steaming hot, and presented the tea bowl. "Tea, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻接过，看到茶汤色泽鲜白，还有一层细腻的泡沫，跟她喝过的茶很不一样：“这是茶吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun took it and blinked at the bright white tea liquor topped with fine foam—nothing like any tea she had ever drunk. "This is tea?"
[ZH] “是我刚刚为小姐点的茶。”
[EN] "Whisked tea I just prepared for you, Miss."
[ZH] “点茶？”千里寻听过点茶，第一次见，只知这茶做起来不简单，她端起茶盏，一口气喝下，口感绵密细腻，甘甜润滑，“不错！没想到你这丫头竟然会点茶。”
[EN] "Whisked tea?" She had heard of Song whisked tea, never seen it, and knew it took skill. Lifting the bowl, she drained it in one go—creamy, smooth, sweet, and silky. "Not bad! Didn't know you had this skill."
[ZH] 苦荞接过茶盏：　“是小姐您以前教我的，您喜欢喝点的茶，又不想总自己点，就教会我，要不像点茶这种功夫，我作为下人是没机会学的。”
[EN] Kuqiao took the empty bowl. "You taught me yourself, Miss! You love whisked tea, didn't feel like whisking it every time, so you trained me. Otherwise a servant like me would never get the chance to learn."
[ZH] “原来是我教你的？看来以后得倒过来，换你教我。”千里寻说完，到处找床，她太累了，想躺一会儿。
[EN] "I taught you? Guess we'll have to flip roles and let you teach me." Looking around for a bed, Qianli Xun felt exhaustion drag at her limbs. "Where's the bed? I need to lie down."
[ZH] 苦荞放下茶盏：“小姐，跟我来。”
[EN] Kuqiao set the bowl aside. "Right this way, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻跟着苦荞向里面拐去，一张闺床出现在眼前，她二话不说，躺了上去。
[EN] Following Kuqiao around an inner partition, Qianli Xun spotted a curtained bed and flopped straight onto it without a word.
[ZH] 苦荞走到床边：“小姐，宽了衣裳您再睡。”
[EN] Kuqiao hurried over. "Miss, take off your outer robes before you sleep."
[ZH] 千里寻坐起来，准备自己动手脱掉外衣，刚穿越过来，一切还不习惯。
[EN] Qianli Xun sat up to undress herself—new to this era, she wasn't used to being waited on.
[ZH] “小姐，您下床站着，我帮您脱。”
[EN] "Stand beside the bed, Miss, I'll help you."
[ZH] 千里寻照做。在苦荞帮她宽衣之际，问道：“为什么点茶这种功夫下人没机会学？”
[EN] Qianli Xun complied. As Kuqiao untied her robes, she asked, "Why wouldn't servants get a chance to learn whisked tea?"
[ZH] 苦荞边帮小姐宽衣边说：“点茶的功夫是像小姐这样的千金小姐，以及豪门贵族人士必备的技能之一，也只有像小姐这样的人才有闲情逸致在一盏茶上用功夫。市井小民，还有我们这些下人，既没闲情逸致，也没那机会，我是沾了小姐的光。”
[EN] "Whisking tea is a refined art expected of noble ladies like you and high-ranking families," Kuqiao explained as she folded the garments. "Only people of your standing have the leisure to fuss over a single bowl of tea. Common street folk and servants like us have neither the leisure nor the chance. I only learned because of your favor."
[ZH] 外衣脱掉后，千里寻一身轻松，穿着中衣躺入被中：“我太累了，天没黑就睡，等天黑了也不要叫我，我要一直睡到明天天亮。”
[EN] Down to her inner tunic, Qianli Xun slipped under the quilt with a sigh of relief. "I'm worn out. Going to bed before sundown—don't wake me after dark. I'm sleeping straight through till morning."
[ZH] 说完没多久，千里寻已进入梦乡。
[EN] Moments later, she was fast asleep.
[ZH] 苦荞放下床幔，轻轻走出去，把门带好。
[EN] Kuqiao let down the bed curtains, stepped out on light feet, and pulled the door shut behind her.

#CH 6
[ZH] 东京汴梁的郊区，有座仪王府，是当朝官家赵佶的十三子赵朴的府邸。赵朴的母亲是乔贵妃，只可惜乔贵妃刚生下儿子就撒手人寰。赵朴被奶娘带大，从小体弱多病，不堪重任，在众多皇子中最不受官家重视。
[EN] In the outskirts of Bianliang, the Eastern Capital, stood Prince Yi's Manor—home to Zhao Pu, thirteenth son of the reigning sovereign Zhao Ji. Zhao Pu's mother, Consort Qiao, had died right after giving birth to him. Raised by a wet nurse, sickly since infancy and unfit for state duties, he was the least regarded of all the imperial princes.
[ZH] 这日，赵朴再也坚持不下去，闭上眼睛，咽下最后一口气。
[EN] On this day, Zhao Pu's strength gave out at last. He closed his eyes and drew his final breath.
[ZH] 乔梦容和奶娘邹嬷嬷，以及赵朴的贴身丫鬟哭得甚是伤心。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong, wet nurse Matron Zou, and Zhao Pu's personal maids wept in bitter grief.
[ZH] 神柳的力量在申简辰隐约听到哭声时猛然消失。他飘在空中，惊魂未定，眼前身着古装之人令他迷惑：“我死了，怎么会来到拍戏的地方？千里寻呢？”
[EN] The moment Shen Jianchen caught faint echoes of weeping, the Divine Willow's force vanished. Floating in midair, still shaken, he stared down at the people in period dress. I'm dead—why am I on a film set? Where's Qianli Xun?
[ZH] 床上躺着的人向他发出莫名的引力，还好引力不大，他能稳住不被吸引而去。
[EN] A strange pull tugged at him from the body lying on the bed, weak enough that he held himself back from drifting into it.
[ZH] 接着看到有影子从那人的身体里剥离出来，散发着金光，飘到他面前：“你与我的肉身有缘，我离开，你可以住进去，记住，替我好好爱梦容。”
[EN] Then a glowing golden shade peeled free from the corpse and floated up to him. "You share a bond with my flesh. I am leaving—you may dwell in it. Remember: love Mengrong well for me."
[ZH] 无缘无故被不认识的人嘱托，申简辰很生气：“梦容是谁？我凭什么要替你好好爱她？我有喜欢的人，她是千里寻！”
[EN] Infuriated at being saddled with a stranger's dying wish, Shen Jianchen snapped, "Who's Mengrong? Why should I love her for you? I already love someone—Qianli Xun!"
[ZH] 对方根本听不到他的声音。
[EN] The shade couldn't hear a word he said.
[ZH] 更高的空中发出一道金光，美妙的仙乐响起，让人听了畅快无比，影子朝金光飘去。
[EN] A beam of golden light broke open higher overhead, accompanied by ethereal celestial music that filled the air with bliss. The shade drifted up toward the radiance.
[ZH] 金光和仙乐实在太美，申简辰努力向上飘，还未到跟前，被一束金光打落，不偏不倚掉落进躺在床上的尸身中，整个过程仿佛是掉进无穷无尽的深渊。
[EN] Drawn by the breathtaking glow and music, Shen Jianchen tried to float upward too—only for a shaft of golden light to strike him down, dropping him dead-center into the corpse on the bed, plunging him as if down a bottomless abyss.
[ZH] 不再往下掉时，申简辰又听到哭声，与刚才飘在空中时听到的区别甚大。在空中时，总觉得那些人看似很近，哭声却若隐若现。而此时真真切切，回荡在耳边，让人心烦，他大声说：“别哭了！”
[EN] When the falling stopped, the weeping returned—no longer distant and hazy as it had been in midair, but crisp and loud right beside his ears, grating on his nerves. He shouted, "Stop crying!"
[ZH] 声音果然停止，申简辰觉得自己不再有轻飘飘的感觉，莫名的踏实感充满全身，心想：“难道我真的到了那影子的身体里？不妨睁开眼睛试试。”
[EN] Silence fell at once. The weightless float was gone, replaced by a solid, grounded heaviness throughout his limbs. Did I really drop into that shade's body? Better open my eyes and see.
[ZH] 他试着睁开双眼，果然是一个明亮的世界，眼前还多出三个脑袋，都是女的。一个是古代丫鬟的装扮；一个是大约四十岁的女人，面容慈祥；一个长相俊俏，端庄典雅，一副大家闺秀的模样。
[EN] He forced his eyes open to a bright room—and three female heads hovering right over him. One wore a maid's garb; another, a woman around forty, had a kindly face; the third was pretty, poised, and refined, every inch a sheltered young lady.
[ZH] 乔梦容见仪王竟然奇迹般醒来，高兴地扑上去：“哥哥，我以为以后再也见不到你，没想到，你又活过来！呜呜……！”
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi wake up by some miracle, Qiao Mengrong threw herself onto him in joy. "Brother! I thought I'd never see you again! You came back to life! Sob...!"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷急切地说：“赶紧把王爷的药丸拿来，给他服下，别一会儿又出事！”
[EN] Matron Zou cried in haste, "Fetch His Highness's medicinal pills right now! Give him one before anything else goes wrong!"
[ZH] 丫鬟匆忙从一旁的桌上拿来一个锦盒，打开，用丝帕捏了一丸药，送到乔梦容跟前。
[EN] A maid dashed to the side table, grabbed a brocade box, popped it open, pinched a pill with a silk handkerchief, and handed it to Qiao Mengrong.
[ZH] 乔梦容不再哭泣，放开仪王，用水葱般的手指捏住药丸，递到仪王嘴边：“哥哥，快吃下这丸药，它对你的身体有益。”
[EN] Drying her tears, Qiao Mengrong let go of Prince Yi, held the pill between slender fingers, and brought it to his lips. "Brother, swallow this pill—it's good for your health."
[ZH] 申简辰还未搞明白是怎么回事，就被人喂药，他怎能随便接受？再说他平时最讨厌这些苦了吧唧的药，闻见味儿都想吐，何况吃下去？
[EN] Before he had even figured out what was going on, someone was shoving medicine down his throat! Worse, he hated bitter pills—just smelling them turned his stomach, let alone swallowing one.
[ZH] 药丸在嘴边，离鼻子很近，一股难闻的药味儿让他受不了，不由伸手将药丸打落，顺势坐起来：“我没病，吃那玩意儿干什么？”
[EN] The foul medicinal reek hit his nose so hard he slapped the pill away and sat straight up. "I'm not sick! Why would I eat that junk?"
[ZH] 哥哥第一次对她无礼，乔梦容揉着被打疼的手背，很是诧异。哥哥声如洪钟，坐起来的状态也不像以前病病怏怏。她顾不上疼痛和诧异：“邹嬷嬷，难道哥哥到鬼门关走一趟，疾病全消？”
[EN] Rubbing the stinging back of her hand, Qiao Mengrong stared in shock—her brother had never once been rough with her. His voice rang like a bronze bell, and he sat upright without a trace of his old frailty. Forgetting her sting and surprise, she gasped, "Matron Zou! Did Brother walk to the Gates of Hell and come back cured of every illness?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷懂些医术，抓起仪王的手腕，摸了摸脉，激动地说：“王爷好了！好了！真的好了！”
[EN] Knowing a bit of medicine, Matron Zou seized Prince Yi's wrist, checked his pulse, and burst out in excitement, "His Highness is cured! Cured! Really cured!"
[ZH] 旁边的丫鬟们听到也都高兴不已。
[EN] The maids standing around cheered in delight.
[ZH] 乔梦容更是开心，抱住仪王：“哥哥，肯定是上天被我的诚心感动，才让你死而复生，身体也恢复正常。”
[EN] Overjoyed, Qiao Mengrong threw her arms around him again. "Brother, Heaven must have been moved by my prayers—not only did you come back from the dead, your whole body is healthy!"
[ZH] 申简辰被眼前这位美女抱着，没有一丝开心，他清楚自己住进一个陌生的身体里，眼前的情景并不是拍戏，而是另一个时代，他此时最想搞清楚他是谁。
[EN] Held tight by a beauty, Shen Jianchen felt not a flicker of joy. He knew he had landed inside a stranger's body—this wasn't a movie set, it was another era. Right now, he needed answers.
[ZH] 他用手扶开乔梦容，问道：“你是谁？我又是谁？这是哪里？”
[EN] He eased Qiao Mengrong off him and asked, "Who are you? Who am I? Where is this?"
[ZH] 众人听到仪王的发问都愣住，尤其是乔梦容，哥哥的问题明显是不记得她。
[EN] Everyone froze at his questions—especially Qiao Mengrong, whose face fell at the realization he didn't know her.
[ZH] 还是邹嬷嬷有经验：“郡主放心，王爷从鬼门关走一趟回来，据我估计是喝过孟婆汤后才被送回来，这孟婆汤坏处是能让人忘记生前之事，好处便是可消百病。”
[EN] Seasoned Matron Zou stepped in at once. "Don't worry, Princess. Coming back from the Gates of Hell, His Highness must have drunk Mengpo's Soup before being sent back. The downside is forgetting one's past life; the upside is that it cures all illness!"
[ZH] 乔梦容终于松口气：“邹嬷嬷这样说，我便放心。哥哥身体健康才最重要，至于记忆，不急，我们帮他慢慢恢复，实在恢复不了，就让他的记忆从现在开始……”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong exhaled in relief. "Hearing you say that puts my heart at ease, Matron Zou. Brother's health matters most. As for his memories, there's no rush—we can help him remember bit by bit, and if not, his memories can start fresh from today..."
[ZH] 这些人不回答问题，只顾言其他，申简辰显得不耐烦：“你们还没回答我的问题。”
[EN] Tired of their chatter without answering him, Shen Jianchen cut in, "You still haven't answered my questions."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“哥哥别急，我一一告诉你。哥哥是仪王，大名赵朴，这里是仪王府……”
[EN] "Don't fret, Brother, I'll tell you everything," Qiao Mengrong said. "You are Prince Yi, Zhao Pu, and this is Prince Yi's Manor..."
[ZH] 没等乔梦容说完，申简辰急切地问道：“我是王爷？是皇帝的儿子？我爹叫什么？”
[EN] Before she could finish, Shen Jianchen blurted out, "I'm a prince? The emperor's son? What's my father's name?"
[ZH] “哥哥是王爷，是官家的十三子，官家的名讳不是我等能提的，我可以告诉哥哥现在是大宋宣和四年。”乔梦容边说，边观察仪王的表情，他看上去还是一脸懵。
[EN] "Yes, Brother, you are His Majesty's thirteenth son. People of our station dare not speak His Majesty's personal name aloud, though I can tell you it is the fourth year of the Xuanhe reign of the Great Song." Watching his expression as she spoke, she saw only blank bewilderment.
[ZH] 申简辰没想到自己死一回，再活过来，竟然成为皇帝的儿子，倒也身份尊贵：“你叫我哥哥，难道你是公主？”
[EN] Dying and waking up as an imperial prince wasn't a bad deal at all. "You call me Brother—are you a princess?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷解释道：“王爷，这位是郡主，是王爷八岁时从路边捡回来的妹妹，后来被官家封为佳和郡主，跟王爷从小一起长大，青梅竹马，而且王爷和郡主已被官家赐婚……”
[EN] Matron Zou explained, "Your Highness, she is Princess Jiahe—a sister you found abandoned by the roadside when you were eight. Later His Majesty granted her the title Princess Jiahe. You two grew up together as childhood sweethearts, and His Majesty even issued an imperial betrothal..."
[ZH] “什么？赐婚？”申简辰仔细瞅瞅眼前这位被捡回来的妹妹，与千里寻相比更显女子的温柔。他想起影子说的话，不由问道：“你叫梦容？”
[EN] "What? An imperial betrothal?" Shen Jianchen took a closer look at this adopted sister—softer and gentler than Qianli Xun. Remembering the shade's parting plea, he asked, "Your name is Mengrong?"
[ZH] 乔梦容兴奋道：“哥哥记着我的名字！”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong lit up. "Brother remembers my name!"
[ZH] 看到乔梦容兴奋的样子，申简辰提不起兴趣，指着邹嬷嬷问：“她是谁？”
[EN] Her excitement did nothing for him. Pointing at Matron Zou, he asked, "And who is she?"
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“这位是邹嬷嬷，是把哥哥带大的奶娘，我和邹嬷嬷是哥哥在这个世界上最亲的人。”
[EN] "That's Matron Zou, the wet nurse who raised you," Qiao Mengrong replied. "She and I are your closest family in this world."
[ZH] “奶娘？我亲娘呢？”申简辰觉得奶娘再亲怎能亲得过亲娘？还需问清楚。
[EN] "Wet nurse? What about my birth mother?" Surely a birth mother came first.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷叹口气：“哎，我本是乔贵妃在娘家时的丫鬟，贵妃进宫后，我就嫁了人。谁知贵妃二十多岁生下王爷后撒手人寰。我比贵妃早两个月生下孩子，那孩子才吃下我两个月的奶，后来生一场大病，死了，他爹因伤心过度也随那孩子而去。乔家就把我送到宫里替贵妃照顾王爷，有了王爷，我也渐渐走出失去孩子的痛苦。”
[EN] Matron Zou let out a sigh. "Ah, I served Consort Qiao as a maid in her maiden home and married after she entered the palace. Who knew she would pass away in her twenties right after giving birth to you? I had borne a child two months earlier—he only nursed from me for two months before a grave illness took him, and his father followed him out of grief. The Qiao family sent me into the palace to care for you in Consort Qiao's stead. Raising you helped heal the pain of losing my own son."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说得感人，申简辰不想再问下去，怕问出更多让人伤感的事情。目前他已清楚眼前这几位与正主的关系，其他的以后再慢慢了解。既然借用正主的身体活过来，还是想想新的生活该怎么过。
[EN] Moved by the sad tale, Shen Jianchen held back further questions lest he dig up more grief. He had the gist of who these people were; the rest could wait. Since he had borrowed the original owner's body to live again, he needed to figure out how to live this new life.
[ZH] 想到这里，他不愿继续坐在床上，掀开身上的锦被，刚把脚放在地上，两个丫鬟立刻过来，蹲下给他穿鞋。而那位唤他“哥哥”的梦容和邹嬷嬷都闪到一边，看上去十分恭顺。
[EN] Done sitting in bed, he threw off the brocade quilt and swung his feet to the floor. Two maids rushed forward at once, kneeling to put on his shoes, while Mengrong and Matron Zou stepped aside in quiet deference.
[ZH] 申简辰甚是享受这种感觉，自己虽曾是大公司CEO，可也从来没享受过这等待遇，在宋代当个王爷倒也不错。
[EN] Shen Jianchen relished the feeling. Even as a big-company CEO, he had never enjoyed royal treatment like this. Being a Song prince had its perks.
[ZH] 那位梦容说现在是宣和四年，申简辰不懂历史，也不愿在这些事情上下功夫，所以根本搞不清楚宣和四年是个什么玩意儿。
[EN] Mengrong had called it the fourth year of Xuanhe. Never big on history, Shen Jianchen hadn't a clue what Xuanhe meant.
[ZH] 他想起千里寻，如果她在身边，“宣和四年”定能被她品出意味来。他们一起在地震中丧生，一起被神柳的力量带走，他来到宋代，她是否也来到这个时代？
[EN] Thoughts of Qianli Xun surged back. If she were here, she'd unpack "the fourth year of Xuanhe" in a heartbeat. They had died together in the quake and been swept off together by the Divine Willow. He had landed in the Song dynasty—had she landed here too?
[ZH] 穿好鞋后，申简辰走出屋外，抬头望着仪王府的上空，在心中问道：“千里寻，你在哪里？”
[EN] Once his shoes were on, Shen Jianchen stepped outside, gazed up at the sky above Prince Yi's Manor, and asked in silence: Qianli Xun, where are you?
[ZH] 一名下人匆匆从外面回来，看到王爷出现在眼前，吓得腿脚发软：“鬼啊！……”
[EN] A servant hurried in from the front gate, spotted the prince standing right there, and went weak in the knees. "A ghost...!"
[ZH] 一个丫鬟批评道：“什么鬼！是仪王！”
[EN] A maid snapped, "What ghost? That's Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 下人跪在地上：“我刚到皇宫报了王爷离世的消息，一进王府大门，王爷又活过来，我岂不是犯了欺君之罪？”
[EN] The servant dropped to his knees. "I just went to the palace to report His Highness's death! Now I walk back through the gate and His Highness is alive again—haven't I committed the Crime of Deceiving the Sovereign?"
[ZH] 乔梦容和邹嬷嬷也走出来。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong and Matron Zou stepped out behind him.
[ZH] 乔梦容吩咐道：“你再去一趟，向宫里说明，王爷到鬼门关走了一趟，又回来，而且疾病全无。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong instructed, "Go back to the palace and explain that His Highness walked to the Gates of Hell and returned—cured of every illness."
[ZH] 下人先磕头：“恭喜王爷获得新生，小的这就再多跑一趟。”说完，起身跑了出去。
[EN] The servant kowtowed. "Congratulations on your new lease on life, Your Highness! I'll run back at once!" Jumping up, he bolted out the gate.
[ZH] 乔梦容走到仪王身边：“哥哥，你饿不饿？我让厨房给你做些吃的。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stepped beside Prince Yi. "Brother, are you hungry? I'll tell the kitchen to prepare food."
[ZH] 申简辰努力感受着新的身体，并没有饥饿的感觉：“我不饿，我想在王府走走，刚醒来，什么都不记得，这王府看着也陌生，以后要在这里生活，得熟悉熟悉。”
[EN] Checking in with his new body, Shen Jianchen felt no hunger. "Not yet. I want to walk around the manor. I woke up remembering nothing, and the place feels foreign. Since I'll be living here, I ought to learn my way around."
[ZH] “我陪着哥哥。”
[EN] "I'll walk with you, Brother."
[ZH] 申简辰并未拒绝，也未等乔梦容，一个人踏着王府的石板路，好奇地瞧着周围的一切。
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't refuse, nor did he wait for her; he set off along the stone paths alone, taking in everything around him with curiosity.
[ZH] 乔梦容带着丫鬟跟在仪王身后，用既兴奋又陌生的目光观察着哥哥的背影。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong trailed behind with her maids, watching his back with an eager yet unfamiliar gaze.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷给身边的丫鬟说：“去吩咐厨房给王爷准备好饭食，他逛累了正好将饭摆上。很久了，王爷一直躺在病榻上，都没在王府内走动过，现在真是可喜可贺，郡主总算是熬出头。”
[EN] Matron Zou turned to a maid beside her. "Tell the kitchen to get His Highness's meal ready so it's on the table once he's tired of walking. For so long His Highness stayed bedridden without ever setting foot in the grounds. What a blessing—Princess Jiahe has finally made it through the dark days."

#CH 7
[ZH] 仪王府挺大，像申简辰在城市里逛过的公园那么大。
[EN] Prince Yi's Manor was huge—about the size of a city park Shen Jianchen used to stroll through.
[ZH] 房屋建得不多，更多的是景观，赏心悦目；还有一半的土地并未开发，长满了天然的野花、野草和野树。逛到这里，申简辰停住脚步。
[EN] Buildings took up only a small part; most of the grounds showcased pleasing landscapes, while half the land remained untouched, overrun with wildflowers, tall grass, and wild trees. Reaching this edge, Shen Jianchen stopped.
[ZH] 乔梦容一直跟在仪王身后，显然新生的哥哥对她没有以前那么热情。要搁以前，他绝对会拉着她的手，处处照顾，看她的眼神也是含情脉脉。尽管那时的他拖着病体，很少在王府走动，可仅有的几次，已让她感受到浓浓的温情。
[EN] Trailing behind him, Qiao Mengrong felt the chill in her reborn brother's manner. Before, he would hold her hand, fuss over her every step, and gaze at her with deep affection. Even frail and rarely able to walk the grounds, those rare moments had wrapped her in warmth.
[ZH] 奇迹般醒来的哥哥什么都不记得，看样子连他们之间的感情都忘了。乔梦容在心里埋怨孟婆汤，转念一想，要不是孟婆汤，哥哥怎能身体康健？以前虽情浓，可哥哥拖着病体，即使嫁了迟早也要守寡。
[EN] Now he remembered nothing—not even their love. Resentment toward Mengpo's Soup flickered in her heart, until another thought checked it: without that soup, how could he be healthy? Intense love meant little if his frail body left her a young widow.
[ZH] 想到这里，乔梦容认为自己不能要求太多，现在的哥哥身形虽然还像以前一样消瘦，但看着精神饱满，更有股阳刚之气，就是那背影也仿佛充满力量，是乔梦容多次在梦中梦到的哥哥的样子。
[EN] She told herself not to ask for too much. Lean as his frame still was, he radiated vigor and masculine strength; even his back looked sturdy, just like the brother she had seen countless times in her dreams.
[ZH] 乔梦容不由向背影靠近，刚走近两步，背影转过来，乔梦容立刻止步，害羞地站在原地。
[EN] Drawn forward, she took two steps closer—only for him to turn around. Qiao Mengrong froze in place, cheeks flushing shy.
[ZH] “佳和郡主，为何王府有一半儿土地荒着？”
[EN] "Princess Jiahe, why is half the manor left wild?"
[ZH] “哥哥觉得建造一半儿已够居住，另一半便保持原貌，关键是哥哥喜欢天然的景色。”
[EN] "You felt half was plenty to live in, Brother, so you left the rest untouched. Above all, you love natural scenery."
[ZH] 乔梦容说完，提出要求：“哥哥以前从来不称呼我佳和郡主，只会唤我梦容，能不能还像以前一样称呼我？”
[EN] Then she ventured a plea. "You never called me Princess Jiahe before—only Mengrong. Could you still call me that?"
[ZH] 眼前的郡主一脸娇羞，申简辰不忍伤害，毕竟她与赵朴有情，又有婚约：“好吧，我就称呼你梦容，不过，很多事情我都不记得，那婚约……”
