[EN] Faced with her shy look, Shen Jianchen hated to hurt her; she and Zhao Pu had loved each other and shared a betrothal. "Alright, Mengrong it is. Still, I don't remember much, so about that betrothal..."
[ZH] 哥哥又像以前一样称呼她，乔梦容很开心：“至于婚约，哥哥不必着急，官家赐婚的诏书上写着明年中秋节时成亲。”
[EN] Delighted to hear her name on his lips again, Qiao Mengrong beamed. "No rush on the betrothal, Brother. His Majesty's marriage decree sets the wedding for next year's Mid-Autumn Festival."
[ZH] 申简辰再瞧瞧眼前的女孩儿，娇羞婉约，应是很多男子喜欢的类型：“我要是不醒来，岂不是婚约无效？”
[EN] Studying the gentle, blushing girl—surely the type most men fell for—he asked, "If I hadn't woken up, wouldn't the betrothal be void?"
[ZH] “怎么会无效？可是官家金口玉言所赐，即使哥哥不醒来，我都想好，替哥哥守一辈子仪王府。”
[EN] "How could an imperial decree spoken by His Majesty himself be void? Even if you hadn't woken up, I had made up my mind to stay and guard Prince Yi's Manor for you all my life."
[ZH] 申简辰替仪王感动，还是问道：“你看着也才十五六岁，正是玩耍的好年纪，为何要早早成亲？”
[EN] Moved on Zhao Pu's behalf, Shen Jianchen still asked, "You look barely fifteen or sixteen—right at the age to enjoy life. Why marry so young?"
[ZH] “我已经十六了，到了该嫁人的年纪，如果再拖下去，只怕嫁不出去。”
[EN] "I'm already sixteen—right at marrying age. Wait any longer and no one will want me."
[ZH] 申简辰才想起这是古代，女子和男子成婚的年纪都小，不由对仪王的年龄产生兴趣：“梦容，你告诉我，我到底多大？”
[EN] Remembering people wed young in this era, Shen Jianchen grew curious about Zhao Pu's age. "Mengrong, tell me—how old am I?"
[ZH] “哥哥刚过十八岁。”
[EN] "You just turned eighteen, Brother."
[ZH] 穿越后竟然比以前年轻了十二岁，申简辰忍不住说了声：“Yes!”
[EN] Twelve years younger in one leap! Shen Jianchen couldn't help cheering, "Yes!"
[ZH] 看到佳和郡主与丫鬟不解的表情，赶紧解释：“我是太高兴，我还这么年轻，实现了人生重来一次的愿望。”
[EN] Catching the puzzled stares of the princess and her maids, he covered fast. "Just thrilled to be this young—feels like getting my wish to start life over!"
[ZH] 乔梦容不甚理解，刚要继续追问哥哥为何要说人生重来一次？话还未出口，下人来报：“王爷，郡主，宫中派内侍前来探望王爷。”
[EN] Baffled, Qiao Mengrong opened her mouth to ask what he meant by starting life over, when a servant hurried up to report: "Your Highness, Princess, an Imperial Attendant from the palace has come to call on His Highness."
[ZH] 乔梦容说道：“哥哥，内侍来了，我们得去见见。”
[EN] "Brother, an Imperial Attendant is here. We have to go greet him," Qiao Mengrong said.
[ZH] “内侍？是干什么的？”申简辰不大明白古代的称谓。
[EN] "Imperial Attendant? What's that?" Shen Jianchen wasn't up on court titles.
[ZH] “内侍，是宫中伺候官家的人，肯定是官家派来的，你我必须前去接待。”
[EN] "Someone who serves His Majesty inside the palace. His Majesty must have sent him—we have to receive him."
[ZH] 听了这解释，申简辰明白对方身份尊贵，便与乔梦容匆匆往回走。
[EN] Catching the rank involved, Shen Jianchen hurried back with Qiao Mengrong.
[ZH] 来到厅堂，已有三人在此等候，中间一位年长许多，两边站在两个小跟班，衣着都一样。他们的打扮令申简辰想起影视剧中太监的形象，心想：“原来内侍就是太监。”
[EN] Three men waited in the main hall: an older man in the center flanked by two young attendants in matching robes. Their outfits brought TV eunuchs straight to mind. So Imperial Attendants are eunuchs, Shen Jianchen realized.
[ZH] 只见内侍迎上来：“真是神奇，殿下果然跟以前不同。官家开始听说殿下不幸病逝，正伤心，结果又传来喜讯，说殿下活过来，而且疾病全无，便派我来瞧瞧。从走路的姿态看，殿下的确已脱离病体。”
[EN] The senior eunuch stepped forward. "Truly a marvel! Your Highness looks nothing like before. His Majesty was grieving upon hearing you had passed away, only for glad news to follow that you had revived with every ailment gone, so he sent me to see for myself. Just by your stride, Your Highness has cast off illness for good!"
[ZH] 申简辰对仪王的生活不熟悉，但他也是见过世面之人，轻松应对：“感谢父皇关怀，辛苦内侍亲自跑一趟，只是我虽有幸活过来，身体的疾病也消除，可以前的记忆全无，要不是郡主和邹嬷嬷帮我解答疑惑，我都不知道自己是谁。”
[EN] Unfamiliar with Zhao Pu's past yet well versed in handling people, Shen Jianchen responded with ease. "My thanks for Imperial Father's concern, and thank you for making the trip in person. Though fortunate enough to revive and shed my illness, I have lost all memory of the past. If not for the Princess and Matron Zou, I wouldn't even know who I am."
[ZH] “竟有这等事？”内侍觉得新奇，目光瞟向一旁的佳和郡主。
[EN] "Such a thing happened?" Intrigued, the eunuch glanced over at Princess Jiahe.
[ZH] 乔梦容赶紧说：“哥哥所说非虚，他睁开眼睛时，我是谁都不记得，还请内侍禀报官家，如若哥哥以后哪里做得不对，定要海涵。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong spoke up at once. "Brother speaks the truth—when he opened his eyes, he didn't even recognize me. Please report this to His Majesty, and ask him to be magnanimous and forgive Brother if he makes any slip in the future."
[ZH] 内侍笑道：“记不得就记不得，重要的是人还在，疾病全消，真是可喜可贺，佳和郡主以后可是有福了。”
[EN] The eunuch chuckled. "Lost memory or not, what matters is that he lives and his ailments are gone—a joyous blessing indeed! Princess Jiahe has great fortune ahead of her."
[ZH] 乔梦容红着脸，从丫鬟手中拿过钱袋，走到内侍跟前：“辛苦内侍跑一趟，一点心意，不成敬意。”
[EN] Blushing, Qiao Mengrong took a money pouch from a maid and stepped forward. "Thank you for taking the trouble, Attendant. A small token of appreciation—nothing worthy of your rank."
[ZH] 内侍很自然地接过：“还是佳和郡主懂礼数，我回去定向官家把仪王殿下的情况禀明，告辞。”
[EN] The eunuch pocketed it with practiced ease. "Princess Jiahe knows proper etiquette as always. I will report Prince Yi's recovery to His Majesty in full. Farewell."
[ZH] 内侍行过礼后，带人离开。
[EN] After bowing, he led his attendants out.
[ZH] 申简辰瞅着内侍离去的背影，问道：“你刚才为什么要给他钱？”
[EN] Watching them leave, Shen Jianchen asked, "Why did you give him money?"
[ZH] 乔梦容叹口气：“唉，内侍是官家身边的人，在官家跟前说话大有作用，给他些银子，他会在官家面前替哥哥美言几句，即使不夸哥哥，也不会说哥哥坏话。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed. "Imperial Attendants stay at His Majesty's side, so their words carry weight. Slipping him some silver ensures he puts in a good word for you—or at least doesn't speak ill of you."
[ZH] 申简辰很不满：“我不是官家的儿子吗？怎么还需要内侍替我美言？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen frowned. "Aren't I the emperor's son? Why do I need a eunuch to put in a good word for me?"
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“看来哥哥确实什么都不记得。我来告诉哥哥，你虽是官家的儿子，可历来不被官家重视，你病着，官家从来都没看过你，也从不召见哥哥，每次只吩咐内侍来传话。所以哥哥的情况都是内侍说给官家听，他想怎么说都可以，使了银子起码保证他不说坏话。”
[EN] "You really don't remember a thing, Brother," Qiao Mengrong explained. "Though you are His Majesty's son, he has never favored you. Through all your years of illness, he never visited and never summoned you, only sending attendants to pass messages. Everything His Majesty hears about you comes through their mouths. A bit of silver at least buys peace."
[ZH] 申简辰这才知道，原来赵朴是位不受宠的王爷。他倒无所谓，不受宠有不受宠的好处，省去很多麻烦，自由自在。
[EN] So Zhao Pu was an unfavored prince. Fine by Shen Jianchen—less attention meant fewer headaches and more freedom.
[ZH] “以后内侍来，不用再给他银子，他想在官家面前说什么，随他。”
[EN] "Next time an attendant comes, don't give him a single coin. Let him tell His Majesty whatever he pleases."
[ZH] “绝对不行！”乔梦容立即否认，“银子还是要给，我不求哥哥被官家重视，只求哥哥平平安安。”
[EN] "Absolutely not!" Qiao Mengrong shook her head at once. "We have to give silver. I don't ask for His Majesty's favor—only for your safety, Brother."
[ZH] 申简辰被乔梦容多情的眼神触动：“你想怎样就怎样吧，我饿了，有没有吃的？”
[EN] Touched by the devotion in her eyes, Shen Jianchen relented. "Do as you like. I'm hungry—is there anything to eat?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷正好来到厅堂，听到仪王说饿了，急忙说：“饭食已为王爷摆好，就等王爷享用。”
[EN] Matron Zou walked into the hall right then and caught his words. "The meal is already set for you, Your Highness—just waiting for you to sit down."
[ZH] 来到用膳厅，申简辰看着一桌子菜，有的认识，有的根本没见过，慨叹一个不受宠的皇子也有这么好的伙食。
[EN] In the dining hall, Shen Jianchen eyed the spread—some dishes familiar, others total mysteries—amazed that even an unfavored prince ate this well.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷和乔梦容与仪王同桌吃饭，好久了，她们没有这么开心过。
[EN] Matron Zou and Qiao Mengrong sat down to dine with him, happier than they had been in years.
[ZH] 以前仪王只在病榻上用饭，吃的饭菜都是厨房单独做成，要蒸煮得又软又烂，才不会对他的身体产生不良影响。
[EN] In the past, Prince Yi ate only in bed, his meals cooked separate and stewed soft to mush so they wouldn't upset his weak frame.
[ZH] 而现在，他正活生生坐在她们面前，津津有味享用着美食，与正常人没有任何不同。
[EN] Now he sat right across from them, full of life, enjoying every bite like any healthy man.
[ZH] “王爷喜欢吃就多吃些，你病的时间太长，人太瘦，要胖些才好看。”邹嬷嬷夹了菜放入仪王碗中。
[EN] "Eat up if you like it, Your Highness," Matron Zou said, placing food in his bowl. "You've been sick so long you're skin and bones—putting on some weight will do you good."
[ZH] 乔梦容只顾欣赏哥哥吃饭的模样，就像从来没见过一般，她的确从来没见过。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong just watched him eat as if seeing him for the first time—and in truth, she was.
[ZH] 6岁那年她被亲生父母遗弃在路边，正好仪王的马车从旁边经过，听到她的哭声，停下车后，见她可怜，便把她带回仪王府当亲妹妹对待。
[EN] When she was six, her birth parents had abandoned her by the roadside. Prince Yi's carriage happened past; hearing her sobs, he halted, pitied her, and brought her back to Prince Yi's Manor to raise as his own sister.
[ZH] 从第一次见到仪王，他就病着，从不与她一处吃饭，哪怕到后来他们互相喜欢上对方，都未曾像现在这样在同一张桌子上吃过饭。她曾多次梦想过这样的场景，没想到在今天得以实现。
[EN] From the first day she met him, he had been ill and never ate at the same table—not even after their feelings grew into love. She had dreamed of this scene countless times, never expecting to live it today.
[ZH] 申简辰被两个女人盯着，甚是不习惯：“你们难道不吃吗？”他给两人分别夹了菜放进碗里。
[EN] Uncomfortable under two pairs of staring eyes, Shen Jianchen asked, "Aren't you two eating?" He picked up food with his chopsticks and served each of them.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“我有生之年能看到王爷恢复正常，就是即刻死了也是开心的。”
[EN] Matron Zou beamed. "Seeing Your Highness healthy in my lifetime, I could die this very instant and go happy."
[ZH] 乔梦容笑着批评道：“邹嬷嬷休说不吉利的话，您会长命百岁，以后还要享哥哥的福。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong chided her with a smile. "Don't talk such bad luck, Matron Zou! You'll live to a hundred and enjoy Brother's good fortune for years to come."
[ZH] 她们说完也高兴地吃了起来。
[EN] With that, both women picked up their chopsticks and ate in high spirits.
[ZH] 申简辰能看得出，眼前的二位对赵朴真是没得说，觉得自己有义务将实情告知。他向旁边的丫鬟吩咐：“你们都下去吧，不用在这里伺候。”
[EN] Seeing how wholehearted their devotion to Zhao Pu was, Shen Jianchen felt he owed them the truth. He waved to the maids standing by. "All of you step outside. No need to wait on us."
[ZH] 丫鬟们都一一往外走。
[EN] The maids filed out one by one.
[ZH] 乔梦容感到奇怪：“哥哥，你怎么能让丫鬟们走？她们得留这儿伺候哥哥。”
[EN] Puzzled, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Why send the maids away, Brother? They're here to serve you."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我有事跟你们说，外人在此很不方便。”
[EN] "I have something to tell you two, and I don't want outsiders listening."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷瞅着最后一个丫鬟出去把门带好后，说道：“人都走了，王爷放心说。”
[EN] Watching the last maid pull the door shut, Matron Zou said, "Everyone's gone, Your Highness. Speak freely."
[ZH] 申简辰放下筷子，显得郑重其事：“其实我不是赵朴，我叫申简辰，来自另一个时代，遇到地震，死了，就来到这里，我亲眼看到赵朴被一道金光和仙乐带走，然后我就进入到他的身体里……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen set his chopsticks down with grave focus. "Truth is, I'm not Zhao Pu. My name is Shen Jianchen, and I come from another era. I died in an earthquake and ended up here. I watched a beam of golden light and celestial music carry Zhao Pu away, and then I fell into his body..."
[ZH] 乔梦容笑道：“哥哥，没想到你这么会编故事。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong laughed. "Brother, I never knew you could spin such tall tales!"
[ZH] “我没有编故事，说的全是实情。”
[EN] "I'm not making it up. Every word is true."
[ZH] 乔梦容从仪王坚定的眼神中感受到他说的有可能是真的，她看向邹嬷嬷。
[EN] Sensing from his steady gaze that he might actually mean it, Qiao Mengrong glanced at Matron Zou.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷显得很镇定：“不管你是谁，既然你在仪王府借助仪王的身体醒来，你只能是仪王，不可能是其他人，请王爷以后不要再对任何人提起此事。”
[EN] Matron Zou stayed calm as stone. "Whoever you were, you woke up in Prince Yi's Manor inside Prince Yi's body. You can only be Prince Yi—no one else. Please never speak of this to another soul, Your Highness."
[ZH] 乔梦容根本不愿承认仪王是别人，否则她的感情该找谁依托？哥哥若今日永远醒不来倒也罢了，可他偏偏醒来，还恢复成她多次在梦中见到的状态，她又岂能让一切的美好被他一句“我不是赵朴”打破？
[EN] Qiao Mengrong refused to accept he was anyone else—if he weren't, where would all her love go? Had her brother never woken up, so be it. Yet he had awakened, transformed into the healthy man of her dreams—how could she let one sentence, "I'm not Zhao Pu," shatter everything?
[ZH] “不，你就是我哥哥！只是失忆了，很多事情想不起来而已。”
[EN] "No, you are my brother! You just lost your memory and can't recall the past."
[ZH] 申简辰很无奈，仔细想来，这个时代根本没有申简辰，他已成为赵朴，别人只会认为他是赵朴。
[EN] Helpless, Shen Jianchen had to admit the truth of it: Shen Jianchen didn't exist in this world. He was Zhao Pu now, and nobody would ever see him otherwise.
[ZH] “算了，说这么多有什么用，也没人相信，我就是赵朴，以后认真做个不被官家重视的仪王。”
[EN] "Forget it—no use talking when nobody believes me. Fine, I'm Zhao Pu, and from now on I'll be a proper unfavored Prince Yi."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷与乔梦容相视而笑，拿起筷子又高兴地吃起来。
[EN] Matron Zou and Qiao Mengrong exchanged a smile, picked up their chopsticks, and went back to eating in high spirits.

#CH 8
[ZH] 天放亮后，清晨的气息从窗户传入房中，纯净而甘甜，没有掺杂混凝土和汽车尾气的味道。千里寻把被子踢开，在梦中嗅着清新的空气，口中唤着：“申简辰！申简辰！……”
[EN] Day broke, and morning air drifted through the window—crisp and sweet, untainted by concrete dust or car exhaust. Kicking off her quilt, Qianli Xun breathed it in through her sleep and murmured, "Shen Jianchen! Shen Jianchen!..."
[ZH] 苦荞听到声音，掀开床幔，轻声问道：“小姐，您说什么？”
[EN] Hearing her, Kuqiao parted the bed curtains and asked in a hushed voice, "Miss, what did you say?"
[ZH] “申简辰！”千里寻并未睁眼，依然睡着。
[EN] "Shen Jianchen!" Eyes still closed, Qianli Xun slept on.
[ZH] 苦荞嘀咕：“申简辰是谁？我怎么从来没听小姐提起过？”
[EN] Kuqiao muttered under her breath, "Who's Shen Jianchen? Never heard Miss mention that name before."
[ZH] 她替小姐把被子盖好，继续放好床幔，坐在床边开始绣花，耐心等待小姐醒来。
[EN] She tucked the quilt back over her mistress, dropped the curtains, and sat by the bed working on embroidery while waiting for her to wake.
[ZH] 苦荞刚绣完一片叶子，千里寻睁开眼，坐起来，瞧着床铺和床幔，恍若隔世：“对，我穿越了，还以为自己依然是千里寻。”
[EN] Kuqiao had just finished stitching a single leaf when Qianli Xun opened her eyes, sat up, and stared at the bed frame and curtains in dazed disorientation. Right—I transmigrated. For a second I thought I was still Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 苦荞听到声音，停下绣花，把所有东西放入收纳的竹篮中，搁到一边，起身掀开床幔：“小姐，您醒了？”说着边把床幔挂好。
[EN] Hearing movement, Kuqiao put down her needlework, stowed everything in a woven basket, set it aside, and pulled back the curtains. "Awake, Miss?" She hooked the drapes up neatly.
[ZH] 千里寻看到苦荞，更清楚自己已成为朱淑真：“现在几点了？”
[EN] Seeing Kuqiao drove home the fact that she was Zhu Shuzhen now. "What time is it?"
[ZH] 苦荞想了一会儿，问道：“小姐是问现在是什么时辰吗？”
[EN] Kuqiao paused to think. "Are you asking what hour it is, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻苦笑了一下，时代不同，人们对同样事物的表达不同，还好，依然能交流：“是的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun gave a wry smile—different eras, different terms, at least they still understood each other. "Yes."
[ZH] “还未到巳时。”苦荞边说边给小姐拿来衣裳。
[EN] "Not yet the Hour of the Snake," Kuqiao replied, fetching her clothes.
[ZH] 千里寻写过关于古代的网络小说，研究过时辰，说明现在还不到九点。她走下床，任由苦荞为她穿好衣裳。
[EN] Having researched ancient timekeeping for her web novels, Qianli Xun knew that meant not yet nine in the morning. She climbed out of bed and let Kuqiao dress her.
[ZH] “小姐，昨晚上姑爷挺老实，没有听到任何声音。”
[EN] "Miss, Son-in-law stayed real quiet last night—didn't hear a peep out of him."
[ZH] “我把他的嘴堵住，当然老实。”
[EN] "I stuffed a gag in his mouth. Of course he stayed quiet."
[ZH] “啊！小姐怎么能把姑爷的嘴堵住？您就不怕姑爷休了您？”
[EN] "Ah! Miss, how could you gag Son-in-law? Aren't you afraid he'll cast you out?"
[ZH] “哼！我才不怕，昨天我还亲自让他把我休了，结果他不愿意，还给我出了一个好主意。”千里寻显得很得意。
[EN] "Hmph! Afraid of what? Yesterday I told him to his face to write me a bill of divorce! He wouldn't do it—and gave me a better idea instead," Qianli Xun bragged.
[ZH] 苦荞已为小姐穿好衣裳，立刻合掌：“阿弥陀佛！阿弥陀佛！多亏姑爷没答应，否则小姐可就惨了！”
[EN] Finished dressing her, Kuqiao pressed her palms together at once. "Amitabha! Amitabha! Thank goodness Son-in-law refused, or you'd be in terrible trouble, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞅着苦荞担忧的傻样，笑道：“看把你吓的，不就是被休吗？有什么了不起的？”
[EN] Amused by Kuqiao's panicked face, Qianli Xun laughed. "Look how scared you are. What's the big deal about being cast out?"
[ZH] 苦荞愁道：“这么看来，您确实不是以前的小姐，她绝对不会向姑爷提出这个要求。”
[EN] Kuqiao sighed in worry. "Hearing that, you really aren't the old Miss. She would never ask Son-in-law for that."
[ZH] “我本来就不是她。”
[EN] "I told you I'm not her."
[ZH] “可我只有您这一位小姐，就当苦荞求您，千万不要让姑爷休了您。”
[EN] "Still, you're the only Miss I have. Consider this Kuqiao begging you—never ask Son-in-law to cast you out!"
[ZH] 瞧着苦荞可怜紧张的样子，千里寻问道：“你倒是说说女子被休有什么不好？为何担心成这样儿？”
[EN] Seeing her genuine distress, Qianli Xun asked, "Tell me then—what's so terrible about a woman getting repudiated? Why are you this worried?"
[ZH] 苦荞立刻说：“被夫君休了的女子会被大家认为品行不端，以后再要嫁人都难，而且会让娘家蒙羞，如果娘家还有其他未出阁的姐妹，也会影响到她们的名声，只怕没有人敢再上门提亲，所以娘家为保住其他姑娘的清白，绝对不会让被夫家休了的女儿进门。”
[EN] Kuqiao rattled off at once, "Everyone assumes a cast-out wife committed some disgraceful misconduct. Remarrying becomes next to impossible, and it shames her maiden family! If she has unmarried sisters at home, their reputations get ruined too and nobody dares propose. To protect the other daughters' honor, maiden families won't even let a cast-out daughter back through the door!"
[ZH] 确实挺严重，千里寻不由问道：“我可否还有其他姐妹？”
[EN] Pretty harsh indeed. Qianli Xun asked, "Do I have any sisters?"
[ZH] “那倒没有，朱府只小姐一位千金，您上面有位哥哥。”
[EN] "No, you're the only daughter of Zhu Manor. You have an older brother."
[ZH] “那我就放心，即使被林觉休了，也不会担心连累他人。”
[EN] "Then I'm in the clear—even if Lin Jue casts me out, I won't drag anyone else down."
[ZH] “不行，小姐要是被姑爷休了，估计连朱府的门都进不了，老爷是位很看重清誉之人，他绝对不会允许子女做出有辱门楣之事。”
[EN] "No, Miss! If Son-in-law casts you out, you probably won't even be allowed inside Zhu Manor! Master values family honor above everything—he'd never tolerate his children bringing disgrace to the household!"
[ZH] 苦荞把小姐拉到铜镜前坐下，开始帮她整理妆容。
[EN] Kuqiao guided her to sit before the bronze mirror and began fixing her hair.
[ZH] 千里寻透过铜镜仔细观察苦荞帮她整理发髻：“你放心，林觉不会休我，他给我出了和离的主意。”
[EN] Watching Kuqiao coil her bun in the mirror, Qianli Xun said, "Relax. Lin Jue isn't casting me out—he suggested mutual divorce instead."
[ZH] “这么说来，姑爷还不错，和离是最好的选择，我们村有位姚婶婶当年跟以前的夫君和离，娘家不仅允许她进门，后来还找人嫁了，听说现在一家人日子过得和和美美。”
[EN] "Then Son-in-law isn't half bad! Mutual divorce is the best path. Back in our village, Aunt Yao got a mutual divorce from her first husband. Her maiden family welcomed her right back, she remarried later, and folks say her whole family lives happy as can be!"
[ZH] “可我总觉得林觉没那么好心，说不定有什么幺蛾子等着我。”
[EN] "Still, I doubt Lin Jue is acting out of kindness. He's probably cooking up some dirty trick."
[ZH] 苦荞继续给小姐整理头发，装点发饰：“姑爷以前对小姐确实不好，小姐和姑爷本是两条道上的人，偏偏给拉到一条道上，不仅自己难受，别人看着也难受。”
[EN] Pinning ornaments into her hair, Kuqiao sighed. "Son-in-law truly treated you poorly before. You and he belong on two different roads—forcing you onto the same path makes you miserable and hurts to watch."
[ZH] “所以，我一定要离开他，既对他好，也对我好。”
[EN] "Which is why I have to leave him. Better for him, better for me."
[ZH] 千里寻决定必须离开林觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun's mind was made up: she was divorcing Lin Jue.
[ZH] 从千里寻的角度来讲，她连正经男朋友都没有过，穿越到大宋，成为别人，先破天荒地进入到一段婚姻中，丈夫竟然还是打老婆的渣男，甚至公然将青楼女子带到家里，这种人不跟他离婚，天理难容！
[EN] From Qianli Xun's side, she had never even had a proper boyfriend—transmigrating into the Song dynasty only to wake up married to a wife-beating scumbag who hauled courtesans into the house? Not divorcing him would defy Heaven itself!
[ZH] 从朱淑真角度来讲，她跟林觉志不同道不合，没有共同爱好和情趣，就像一朵鲜花插在牛粪上，如此不协调，而且这朵鲜花迟早会被牛粪熏死。既然千里寻有幸成为朱淑真，有义务帮助她摆脱这段婚姻，再说帮她就是帮自己。
[EN] From Zhu Shuzhen's side, she and Lin Jue shared zero ideals or tastes—like a fresh flower stuck in cow dung, mismatched to the bone, bound to wither from the stench. Since Qianli Xun had inherited her life, she owed it to Zhu Shuzhen to break free—and helping her meant helping herself.
[ZH] 正想着，苦荞问道：“小姐，您说梦话时叫出一个人名，好像是申简辰，他是谁？”
[EN] Mid-thought, Kuqiao spoke up. "Miss, you called out a name in your sleep—sounded like Shen Jianchen. Who is he?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有必要瞒着苦荞：“他是跟我一起在地震中死亡的朋友，估计是睡着时梦到地震的场景，不由自主喊出他的名字。”
[EN] Seeing no reason to hide it, Qianli Xun said, "A friend who died in the earthquake with me. I must have dreamed of the quake and called his name."
[ZH] “他是不是小姐在另一个时代喜欢的人？”
[EN] "Was he someone you loved back in your era?"
[ZH] “呵呵！我要是喜欢他，估计猪都能上树，这个人每次见到我，不损我几句，就不舒服，还美其名曰追求我，真是搞笑！”
[EN] "Ha! Me love him? Only when pigs climb trees! Every time that guy saw me, he couldn't rest until he got a few digs in, and he had the nerve to call that pursuing me—hilarious!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完，又莫名悲伤：“现在想让他当面损我，都没有机会，我成了朱淑真，他不知道会怎样，也许永远也见不到他。”
[EN] A sudden ache tightened her chest as she added, "Now I don't even have the chance to hear him tease me to my face. I ended up as Zhu Shuzhen—no telling what happened to him. I might never see him again."
[ZH] 两人聊着，苦荞已为小姐装扮完毕：“小姐，您看，怎么样？满意吗？”
[EN] While they talked, Kuqiao finished her hair. "Look, Miss—how is it? Do you like it?"
[ZH] 千里寻再向镜中瞅一眼：“你这丫鬟心灵手巧，还知道你家小姐怎么装扮最美，不错，我很满意。”
[EN] Qianli Xun glanced in the mirror. "Clever hands—you know just how to make your Miss shine. Very nice, I love it."
[ZH] 千里寻梳洗完毕，用过早饭，决定去看看林觉，估计这一晚上他不怎么好过，还是赶紧把人给放了。
[EN] After washing up and eating breakfast, Qianli Xun went to check on Lin Jue. He must have had a rough night; time to turn him loose.
[ZH] 她推开关押林觉的房门，走进去。
[EN] She pushed open the door of his room and stepped inside.
[ZH] 林觉听到有声音，迅速坐起来，嘴里塞着布子，不能说话，那眼神，明显是命令朱淑真尽快放了他。
[EN] At the sound, Lin Jue sat straight up. Gagged and unable to speak, his glare practically ordered her to untie him on the spot.
[ZH] “你别拿眼睛瞪我，我过来就是给你松绑。”说着，千里寻过去，拔掉林觉嘴里的布子。
[EN] "Stop glaring—I came to let you go." Stepping up, Qianli Xun yanked the rag from his mouth.
[ZH] 林觉脱口而出：“你这恶毒的女人！”
[EN] "You wicked woman!" Lin Jue burst out.
[ZH] 千里寻边松绑边说：“我要是真恶毒，你都活不过今天，还是聪明点儿。你呢，打不过我，以后好好听我差遣，跟我把婚和和美美地离了，咱们以后井水不犯河水，你过你的日子，我过我的日子，以后见了面还能打个招呼，不也挺好？我爹在临安也是有头有脸的人物，官职也比你大，你对我好，他肯定不会亏待你。”
[EN] Untying the knots, Qianli Xun shot back, "If I were wicked, you wouldn't have lived to see sunrise. Smarten up. You can't beat me in a fight, so behave yourself, go through with a clean mutual divorce, and after that we stay out of each other's hair. You live your life, I live mine, and if we bump into each other later we can even say hello—isn't that nice? My father's a man of standing in Lin'an and outranks you. Treat me right and he won't shortchange you."
[ZH] 千里寻嘚啵嘚啵完，绳子也正好解完。想着绳子松开，说不定林觉会袭击而来，她已做好防备，却发现林觉并未如此做，而是盯着她看，眼神比刚才缓和许多。
[EN] Just as her lecture wrapped up, the last knot came free. Half expecting Lin Jue to swing at her the second he got loose, she braced herself—only to find him staring at her with a far softer look.
[ZH] “你看我干嘛？”
[EN] "What are you staring at?"
[ZH] “自从你嫁给我，跟我说过的话加起来，都没有昨天到今天说的多，如果你以前像这样，我也不会打你骂你。”
[EN] "Since the day you married me, every word you spoke to me combined doesn't match what you've said since yesterday. If you'd talked to me like this before, I never would've hit or cursed you."
[ZH] 千里寻突然觉得林觉也挺可怜，娶一位对她实施冷暴力的妻子：“所以我们不合适，在一起你难受，我也不好受。”
[EN] A pang of pity flickered in Qianli Xun—stuck with a wife who gave him nothing but cold violence. "All the more reason we're wrong for each other. Staying together makes both of us miserable."
[ZH] 林觉从床上下来，活动活动四肢：“给你三日，只要三日后能把你的父母和朱家长辈请来，我定会放你离开林家。”
[EN] Lin Jue climbed off the bed and stretched his stiff limbs. "I'll give you three days. Bring your parents and the Zhu elders here in three days, and I'll let you leave the Lin family."
[ZH] 千里寻兴奋道：“一言为定！不过，你得告诉我，你打算请几位长辈？我这边在数量上不能比你少，两边得势均力敌，互相谈判起来才不会你方压倒我方，或者我方压倒你方，这样才公平。”
[EN] Qianli Xun beamed. "It's a deal! Tell me one thing, though—how many elders are you bringing? My side can't be outnumbered. We need an even match so neither family bullies the other at the table. Only fair."
[ZH] “放心，你请几位，我就请几位，如若一位都请不来，还请娘子不要再想什么和离之事。”
[EN] "Don't worry—however many you bring, I'll match. And if you can't bring a single one, forget about mutual divorce."
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻警惕道：“你是不是有什么阴谋？”
[EN] Suspicion flared. "You planning some scheme?"
[ZH] 林觉盯着朱淑真，她虽不是以前那个她，但容貌还是。他只要看一眼，就不忍心把她送到其他男子的怀抱：“和离的主意是我出的，能有什么阴谋？”
[EN] Lin Jue gazed at her. Different soul or not, her face hadn't changed—one look and he couldn't bear handing her over to another man. "Mutual divorce was my idea. What scheme could I have?"
[ZH] “也对！”千里寻想着自己还得在林府待些时日，需跟眼前这位约法三章，瞅着林觉往外走的背影，大声道：“喂！”
[EN] "Fair point!" Remembering she still had to stay under this roof for a few days, she needed ground rules. Calling after his retreating back, she yelled, "Hey!"
[ZH] 林觉停下来，转过身：“还有什么事？”
[EN] Lin Jue paused and turned. "Something else?"
[ZH] “我需在林府住些时日，总得跟你提出些要求。”
[EN] "While I'm still staying in Lin Manor, I have three rules."
[ZH] “洗耳恭听。”
[EN] "I'm all ears."
[ZH] 林觉竟然喜欢听朱淑真说话，哪怕都是废话他也愿意，只怪以前想听她说句话都难。
[EN] Strange as it was, Lin Jue found himself enjoying the sound of her voice—even idle chatter—after starving for a single word from her for so long.
[ZH] “第一，不准进竹兰居，我的拳头你可是领教过。”
[EN] "First, stay out of Zhulan Residence. You've already tasted my fists."
[ZH] 林觉对竹兰居没有什么好印象，那里给他留下的只有冷漠和忽视，即使进过几次，除了打朱淑真，都想不起进去做过什么。不进也罢，他点点头。
[EN] Lin Jue held no fond memories of Zhulan Residence—only cold shoulders and humiliation; aside from hitting her, he couldn't recall why he ever went in. Fine by him. He nodded.
[ZH] “第二，不准把商妓带回林府，你要快活去青楼。”
[EN] "Second, no bringing courtesans into Lin Manor. Want fun, go to a brothel."
[ZH] “可以。”
[EN] "Agreed."
[ZH] “第三，没有和离前，你得给我银子使。”
[EN] "Third, until the divorce is final, you give me silver to spend."
[ZH] “你还是女主人，当然有使银子的权利。”
[EN] "You're still mistress of the house—spending silver is your right."
[ZH] “没想到你这人还挺痛快，我对你的好印象又增加一分。”
[EN] "Look at you, agreeing so fast. My opinion of you just went up a notch."
[ZH] 林觉离开后，千里寻准备着手和离之事，看来得到朱淑真的娘家走一趟。她对那个娘家一无所知，计划先向苦荞了解些情况，再做打算。
[EN] Once Lin Jue left, Qianli Xun set her mind to the mutual divorce. She needed to visit Zhu Shuzhen's maiden home—a place she knew nothing about. First, get the rundown from Kuqiao, then plan her move.
[ZH] 三日的时间太短，为了自己能在这个陌生的时代有新的生活，她决定拼一拼。
[EN] Three days was tight, yet for the sake of a fresh start in this unfamiliar era, she would give it everything she had.

#CH 9
[ZH] 千里寻回到竹兰居时，苦荞正坐在茶桌前做茶。她来了兴致，坐在一旁仔细欣赏，暂时把正事抛诸脑后。
[ZH] “怎么又做起茶来？”
[ZH] 苦荞用茶筅边在茶盏中击拂边说：“小姐每日吃完早饭，都要喝上一盏。”
[ZH] “你手里那玩意儿叫什么名字？”
[ZH] “茶筅。”
[ZH] 苦荞提起一旁的紫砂壶，沿着茶盏倒入些许热水，继续用茶筅击拂着，细腻的泡沫逐渐生起。
[ZH] 千里寻叹道：“好神奇！”
[ZH] 苦荞继续添些热水，用茶筅击拂，如此共添了七回水，直到茶汤被击拂得乳雾汹涌，溢盏而起，才作罢。她将茶筅放好，端起茶盏，递到小姐跟前：“小姐，您可以品尝了。”
[ZH] 千里寻接过，不再像第一次一样一饮而尽，她先品一小口，慢慢回味着茶的香味，再品第二口。顿时觉得高雅二字跟科技发达真没什么关系，在那个先进的时代她从未这么高雅过。
[ZH] 苦荞为小姐做完茶后，又到书桌前收拾，放好纸笔，开始研磨。
[ZH] 千里寻把剩下的最后一口茶品完，将茶盏放在桌上问道：“苦荞，你这是做什么？”
[ZH] “小姐每日喝完茶，要坐在这里写诗作词或读书，我先替小姐预备好。”苦荞继续研磨。
[ZH] 这些都是苦荞每日不断重复的工作程序，她不愿丢掉，更不愿征求千里寻的意见，去努力适应重生的小姐，只因她不想从心底承认以前的小姐已不在。
[ZH] 千里寻在书桌前坐下，她从未真正有过自己的书桌。
[ZH] 上学时，书桌是父母和学校准备的，她并不喜欢。
[ZH] 后来，她凭借自己的能力买了房，装修时，设计师问她要不要设计一个书桌。她想了想，觉得不需要，只要个电脑桌就行。再后来电脑桌她也很少用，写小说时总是坐在床上，背靠靠垫，怀里抱着一只大型的HelloKitty，拿着手机开始码字。
[ZH] 穿越后成为朱淑真，才真正拥并且需要一个书桌。
[ZH] 千里寻看看桌上放着的笔墨纸砚，她的记忆停留在用手指敲击电脑和手机键盘的阶段，而朱淑真这双手只会拿毛笔写字。
[ZH] ﻿还好千里寻上中学时学过软笔书法，一直练到大学，大约也有七八年的功底。
[ZH] 千里寻用余光发现地上有一个方型木桶，里面放着许多卷起来的字画。她从中拿起一副字，将纸展开，上面用娟秀的字迹写着一首词：
[ZH] 玉体金钗一样娇，背灯初解绣裙腰，衾寒枕冷夜香消。
[EN] When Qianli Xun returned to Zhulan Residence, Kuqiao sat at the tea table making tea. Her curiosity sparked, so she pulled up a chair to watch, pushing serious errands to the back of her mind for a moment.
[ZH] 深院重关春寂寂，落花和雨夜迢迢，恨情和梦更无聊。（《浣溪沙·春夜》）
[EN] "Making tea again?" she asked.
[ZH] 这首词千里寻读过，此时翻开，以朱淑真的身份再次体会，更能感受到身处一段并不理想的婚姻中朱淑真的幽怨、不满和孤寂，以及她对理想爱情的渴望。
[EN] Whisking brisk waves inside the tea bowl with a bamboo tool, Kuqiao answered, "You drink a bowl after breakfast every morning, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻提起笔，当笔尖吸饱墨汁后，照着纸上的笔迹将那词又抄了一遍。
[EN] "What do you call that gadget in your hand?"
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐撂下笔，凑过来欣赏了一会儿：“小姐的字和以前写得有七八分相似，再多练练，肯定会十分相似。”
[EN] "A tea whisk." Kuqiao lifted the purple clay teapot beside her, poured a splash of hot water along the rim of the bowl, and whisked again until fine froth began to rise.
[ZH] 千里寻想起和离之事：“苦荞，我得回趟朱府，你给我说说朱府的情况，我好提前有个准备。”
[EN] "That's magic!" Qianli Xun marveled.
[ZH] 苦荞摸着脑袋：“朱府的情况？小姐突然问，我一时不知从何说起。小姐能不能问具体点儿？”
[EN] Kuqiao added hot water and whisked again, seven infusions in all, until thick creamy foam surged to the very lip of the bowl. Setting the whisk down, she lifted the tea bowl and presented it. "Ready to taste, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻又觉口渴：“我口渴，再给我做盏茶，你边做我们边聊。”
[EN] This time Qianli Xun did not gulp it down in one swallow. She took a small sip, lingered over the fresh fragrance, then took a second. Refinement, she thought, had nothing to do with modern gadgets; back in her high-tech era, she had never lived half this refined.
[ZH] “好嘞。”苦荞又坐回茶桌前，打开盛茶粉的小瓷坛：“没有茶粉了，我现做些，小姐尽管问，我知无不言。”
[EN] With the tea served, Kuqiao moved to the study desk to straighten up, laying out brush and paper before grinding ink. Finishing her last mouthful, Qianli Xun set the bowl down and asked, "What are you up to now, Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻看着苦荞取了烤干的茶叶放入石臼中，用石杵轻轻捣着：“你先告诉我朱家都有哪些人？”
[EN] "Every morning after tea, you sit here to read or write poetry and ci, Miss. I'm getting everything ready," Kuqiao replied without pausing the inkstick. These daily rituals were habits she refused to drop, never once asking her reborn mistress what she preferred, because deep down she still could not bear admitting her old mistress was gone.
[ZH] 苦荞继续捣着茶叶：“老爷，夫人，大公子，以及少夫人，也就是小姐的嫂嫂，小姐嫁到林家时，少夫人已有八个月身孕，估计孩子已出生。”
[EN] Qianli Xun walked over and took a seat at the desk. She had never owned a real study desk before. During her school years, desks belonged to her parents or classrooms, and she had disliked them all. Later, when she bought her own apartment and the designer asked whether to include a study desk, she waved it off and asked for a simple computer desk instead. Even that gathered dust; whenever she wrote web novels, she propped herself against bed pillows, hugged a giant Hello Kitty plush, and tapped out chapters on her phone. Only after transmigrating into Zhu Shuzhen did she both own and need a study desk.
[ZH] “难道我嫁过来半年多从未回过一次朱家？”
[EN] She eyed the Four Treasures of the Study spread before her. Her fingers remembered keyboards and touchscreens, whereas Zhu Shuzhen's hands knew only the brush. Good thing Qianli Xun had practiced brush calligraphy from middle school through college, putting in seven or eight solid years.
[ZH] 苦荞将茶臼中捣碎的茶叶全部放入茶碾中，来回碾着：“当初是老爷答应下这门亲事，小姐开始根本不同意，可婚都定了，只能点头，被迫无奈才嫁过来，到第三日该回门时，小姐生气就没回。”
[EN] Out of the corner of her eye, she spotted a square wooden tub on the floor stuffed with rolled sheets of calligraphy. Pulling one out and smoothing it flat, she found a ci lyric penned in graceful strokes:
[ZH] “我和父母的关系闹得很僵吗？”千里寻担心朱淑真与父母关系闹得太僵，会给合离增加难度。
[EN] "Spring night, my jade body is soft as a gold hairpin as, back to the lamp, I unfasten my silk skirt. But the quilt and pillow are cold. The night's fragrance is gone.
[ZH] 苦荞继续碾着茶叶：“当时是挺僵，不过父母与子女之间哪有什么隔夜仇？再说夫人好几次在街上碰见我，都向我问起小姐的情况。”
[EN] Spring is a deep courtyard of many locked doors. Petals falling in falling rain make the night seem forever. Regret comes to me in dream. There is no escape." ("Huanxisha: Spring Night") [TN: Translated by Tony Barnstone and Chou Ping]
[ZH] “你说得对，父母跟子女之间确实没有隔夜仇……”
[EN] Qianli Xun had read the lyric before, yet opening it now in Zhu Shuzhen's own shoes, she felt the poet's ache far deeper: the quiet resentment, loneliness, and frustration of a loveless marriage, and the fierce hunger for true love. She lifted the brush, soaked the tip in ink, and copied the entire poem stroke for stroke beside the original hand.
[ZH] 千里寻想起自己的父母。小时候，她像男孩子一样调皮，经常被父母批评，有一次甚至离家出走，结果被父母找回来，她搂着母亲哭了起来，所有怨气瞬间消散。
[EN] Once she laid the brush down, Kuqiao leaned over to admire the sheet. "Your writing is already seven or eight parts like before, Miss! Keep practicing and nobody could tell the difference."
[ZH] 后来母亲在她十二岁时出车祸离世，父亲把千里拳尽数传给她后，嘱咐她要将千里拳发扬光大，然后剃度出家，云游四海。父亲出家时，她刚好从大学毕业，从此独自撑起千里拳庄。
[EN] That reminded Qianli Xun of her real business. "Kuqiao, I have to make a trip back to Zhu Manor. Give me the rundown on the household so I know what I'm walking into."
[ZH] 也不知地震后，千里拳庄还是否幸存？
[EN] Kuqiao scratched her head. "Zhu Manor? Asking out of the blue, I don't know where to start. Could you ask something specific, Miss?"
[ZH] 想到这里，千里寻十分感伤，不由叹了口气。
[EN] Thirst crept back into Qianli Xun's throat. "My mouth's dry. Make me another bowl of tea, and we'll talk while you work."
[ZH] 苦荞已将茶叶碾成细小的茶末，用小刷子把茶末刷进茶筛中，听到小姐叹气，开解道：“小姐如若想念老爷太太，我就随小姐回去看看他们，他们看到您指定高兴。”
[EN] "Right away!" Returning to the tea table, Kuqiao opened the small porcelain jar for tea powder. "Out of tea powder, so I'll grind some fresh. Ask whatever you like, Miss; I'll tell you all I know."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“我想不通，女儿不回门，当爹的官职不小，难道就不能向他挑的女婿问问情况？我不相信林觉敢驳了临安知府的面子。”
[EN] Watching Kuqiao drop roasted tea leaves into a stone mortar and pound them with a stone pestle, Qianli Xun asked, "First, who lives in Zhu Manor?"
[ZH] 苦荞边筛茶边说：“老爷和姑爷之间的事，我一个丫鬟不是很了解，但我知道老爷十分看重清誉。”
[EN] "Master, Madam, Eldest Young Master, and Young Madam, your sister-in-law," Kuqiao replied as she worked the pestle. "When you married into the Lin family, Young Madam was already eight months pregnant, so the baby must be born by now."
[ZH] 千里寻心想未谋面的亲爹看重清誉，不知面对和离之事，他能否替女儿出面？
[EN] "I've been married over half a year and never went back home once?"
[ZH] 千里寻认为没有必要再向苦荞打听下去，等见到朱淑真的爹娘，亲自了解一番，再做打算。
[EN] Kuqiao transferred the crushed leaves into a tea roller and ground them back and forth. "Master agreed to the match on his own. You hated the idea, yet the betrothal was sealed, so you had no choice but to nod and go through with the wedding. On the third day, when it was time for the return visit to your maiden home, you were too furious to go."
[ZH] “苦荞，做完这盏茶，就陪我回朱府。”
[EN] "Did I leave things real tense with my parents?" Qianli Xun worried a bitter fallout would ruin her chances at mutual divorce.
[ZH] “好的，小姐。”
[EN] "Tense at the time, sure," Kuqiao said, keeping the roller moving. "Still, what parent holds an overnight grudge against their own child? Besides, Madam ran into me on the street several times and asked after you every time."
[ZH] 苦荞已筛完茶，把筛好的绿色茶末在茶盏中放些，剩下的全部用刷子刷进小瓷坛中放着备用。接着，在茶盏中加入些许热水后，拿起茶筅开始击拂，如此七回后，一盏茶做好，起身端到小姐跟前。
[EN] "True enough. Parents don't stay mad at their kids forever..."
[ZH] “小姐，请用。”
[EN] Her own parents flashed across Qianli Xun's mind. Growing up wild as a boy, she had earned endless scoldings and once even ran away from home; the moment they found her, she threw her arms around her mother in tears and every bit of anger vanished on the spot. Then a car crash took her mother when Qianli Xun was twelve. Her father passed every technique of Qianli Fist on to her, charged her with keeping the school alive, and shaved his head to become a wandering monk right around her college graduation, leaving her to run Qianli Fist Manor alone. Had Qianli Fist Manor even survived the earthquake? Grief tightened her chest, and a heavy sigh escaped her.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，慨叹道：“看你做茶的过程，一步一步甚是用心，我都不忍心喝下它。”
[EN] Having ground the leaves into fine dust and brushed them into a tea sieve, Kuqiao heard the sigh and tried to lift her spirits. "If you miss Master and Madam, I'll go back with you to visit them, Miss. They'll be thrilled to see you."
[ZH] “小姐只有喝下这盏茶，才不枉费我这番功夫。”
[EN] "What I don't get is this," Qianli Xun said. "If a daughter skips her return visit, her father holds a high post. Couldn't he question the son-in-law he handpicked? Don't tell me Lin Jue dares snub the Prefect of Lin'an."
[ZH] “没想到你茶做得好，嘴也挺会说，从你这位丫鬟的身上就能看出你以前主子的风格，她一定是位事事都十分考究的人，哪像我大大咧咧……”
[EN] Sifting the tea powder, Kuqiao answered, "I'm only a maid, so I don't know what goes on between Master and Son-in-law. All I know is Master guards family honor above everything."
[ZH] 苦荞急了：“小姐说的什么话！我需要强调多少遍您才明白，我的主子只有您一位！”
[EN] A father obsessed with reputation. Would he step forward to back a mutual divorce? No sense pumping Kuqiao for guesses; better to meet Zhu Shuzhen's parents face to face and size them up herself.
[ZH] 千里寻没有理会苦荞，她急着回朱府，更确切地说，她想尽快结束这段对她来讲十分没有必要的婚姻，已没心情品茶，一口气喝下，放下茶盏后，站起来：“苦荞，回朱府！”
[EN] "Kuqiao, finish that bowl of tea, then come with me to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 苦荞跟着小姐走出林府，吩咐林府的车夫套好马车，二人先后登上马车坐好。
[EN] "Yes, Miss." Done sifting, Kuqiao spooned green powder into the tea bowl and swept the surplus into the porcelain jar for later. She added hot water and whisked through seven infusions, then carried the finished bowl to her mistress. "Please drink, Miss."
[ZH] 这是千里寻第一次乘坐马车，充满新奇，比她第一次乘坐申简辰的劳斯莱斯还要新奇。在她眼中劳斯莱斯只不过是申简辰炫富的筹码，她最讨厌像申简辰那种有几个臭钱就在女孩子面前炫来炫去的男人，显得极其无知。
[EN] Taking the bowl, Qianli Xun sighed in admiration. "Watching you pour so much care into every single step makes me almost hate to drink it."
[ZH] 可当马车启动后在颠簸中前进，千里寻才从心底承认还是劳斯莱斯坐着舒服，她努力让自己坐稳。
[EN] "Only if you drink it is all that work worthwhile, Miss."
[ZH] 马车行了一会儿，听到热闹喧嚣之声，掀开布帘，向外瞧去，临安城的街道上人来人往，甚是热闹，街道两旁依也是店铺林立。马车从人群中穿梭而过，能听到各种交谈和讨价还价的声音；还有茶馆酒肆，也是人满为患，时不时从茶馆里传出说书的声音。
[EN] "Good with tea and smooth with words too. Just looking at you tells me what your old mistress was like: meticulous about every detail, nothing like sloppy me..."
[ZH] 千里寻放下帘子：“没想到临安的街市如此热闹。”
[EN] Kuqiao flared up at once. "What talk is that, Miss?! How many times do I have to say it? You are my one and only mistress!"
[ZH] “这不算什么，东京汴梁才热闹。”
[EN] Ignoring the scolding, eager to reach Zhu Manor and cut loose from a marriage she had zero use for, Qianli Xun drained the tea in one gulp, set down the bowl, and stood up. "Kuqiao, let's go to Zhu Manor!"
[ZH] “你去过？”
[EN] They walked out of Lin Manor, had the Lin coachman hitch a carriage, and climbed aboard one after the other. Her very first ride in a horse-drawn carriage thrilled Qianli Xun even more than her first ride in Shen Jianchen's Rolls-Royce. To her, that Rolls-Royce had been nothing more than a prop for flashing wealth, and she loathed men who waved cash in front of girls like clueless fools. Yet the instant the carriage lurched forward over uneven cobblestones, she had to admit the Rolls-Royce won hands down for comfort, bracing both hands to stay steady.
[ZH] “没有，听小姐以前说的，东京汴梁有位魏夫人来信，邀请小姐去汴梁以诗会友，小姐还说什么时候带我去汴梁逛逛。”
[EN] Soon loud street clamor swelled outside. Lifting the cloth curtain, she looked out over Lin'an's crowded avenues, lined shoulder to shoulder with storefronts. As the carriage threaded through throngs of pedestrians, snippets of banter and haggling drifted in; teahouses and taverns overflowed with guests, snatches of storytelling carrying out onto the street.
[ZH] “魏夫人？可叫魏玩？”
[EN] Dropping the curtain, Qianli Xun remarked, "Lin'an's markets are livelier than I expected."
[ZH] “我不清楚，小姐从未对我说过魏夫人的名字。”
[EN] "This is nothing. Bianliang, the Eastern Capital, is way livelier!"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，应该就是魏玩，后世有资料记载，朱淑真与那魏玩有过来往，而且有诗为证。她此时没有心情搜寻那些诗词，还是想想怎么拿下朱淑真的爹娘。
[EN] "You've been there?"
[ZH] 一路思忖着，当周围安静下来时，马车缓缓停下。
[EN] "No, you told me before, Miss. A Lady Wei from Bianliang wrote inviting you up there to make friends through poetry, and you promised to take me sightseeing in the capital someday."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“小姐，到了。”
[EN] "Lady Wei? Is her name Wei Wan?"
[ZH] 千里寻多少有些紧张：“就这么到了？我还没想清楚见了爹娘说些什么，或者应该有什么样的表情。”
[EN] "No idea. You never told me her given name."
[ZH] “小姐别紧张，这可是您从小生活的地方。”
[EN] Must be Wei Wan, Qianli Xun thought. Later historical notes recorded Zhu Shuzhen corresponding with Wei Wan, with surviving poems to prove it. Right now she had no patience to dig through old verse; figuring out how to win over Zhu Shuzhen's parents came first.
[ZH] “有那么明显吗？都被你瞧出来。”
[EN] Lost in thought, she felt the clamor hush as the carriage rolled to a stop. "We're here, Miss," Kuqiao announced.
[ZH] 苦荞笑着没说话，先下了马车，站在车旁，等待伺候小姐。
[EN] Nerves fluttered in Qianli Xun's stomach. "Here already? I haven't even figured out what to say or what face to put on when I meet my parents!"
[ZH] 千里寻在车内犹豫了一会儿，还是鼓起勇气走下马车。
[EN] "Don't panic, Miss. You grew up right here."
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐出来，伸手去扶。
[EN] "Does it show that much? Even you noticed."
[ZH] 千里寻抬眼，看到朱红色的大门紧紧关闭，匾额上写着“朱府”二字，门前左右两边的石狮将朱府的大门衬得更加气派。
[EN] Kuqiao smiled without answering, hopped down first, and stood ready beside the step. After a brief hesitation inside, Qianli Xun braced herself and climbed down, taking Kuqiao's steadying hand. Looking up, she saw massive vermilion gates shut tight beneath a plaque reading "Zhu Manor," flanked by imposing stone lions that gave the entrance grand weight.
[ZH] “苦荞，你去敲门。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, go knock."
[ZH] “小姐，我们不从正门进入。”
[EN] "We don't enter through the main gate, Miss."
[ZH] “不从正门进，难道要走后门？”
[EN] "Not the main gate? The back gate, then?"
[ZH] “是侧门，小姐跟我来。”
[EN] "The side gate. Follow me, Miss."
[ZH] “真是奇怪，不让人走，建得那么气派干嘛？岂不浪费？”
[EN] "Weird. Why build such a grand gate if nobody walks through it? What a waste!"
[ZH] “小姐有所不知，正门平常不开，一旦府中来了贵重之人，才会打开正门迎接，小姐是自家人，适合走侧门，就连老爷平常也是走侧门。”
[EN] "You wouldn't know, Miss. The main gate stays shut unless honored guests visit. Family members use the side gate; even Master uses the side gate every day."
[ZH] 千里寻在苦荞的引领下向大门的左侧拐去，走了不多时，果然看到一个与正门比起来逊色许多的侧门，两边修竹成林，风景倒也雅致。
[EN] Following Kuqiao around the left wall, Qianli Xun soon reached a far more modest side gate framed by slender bamboo groves, quiet and tasteful.

#CH 10
[ZH] 苦荞上前敲了一会儿门。没过多久，当值的下人打开门，瞧见是苦荞，知道小姐回府，立即将门开大些：“小姐可回来了，夫人一直念叨您。”
[ZH] 苦荞问：“老爷和夫人在哪里？小姐要去拜见。”
[ZH] “老爷去了府衙，还未归来，夫人在园子里喂鱼。”下人说完，闪到一边，让出道来。
[ZH] 千里寻跨过门槛，苦荞紧随其后。
[ZH] 待她们走进后，下人说道：“小姐进屋等着，我去禀报夫人。”说完一路小跑朝园子方向而去。
[ZH] 千里寻问：“现在我们去哪里？”
[ZH] “去夫人房中，小姐只管跟着我。”
[ZH] 苦荞在前面带路，千里寻乖乖跟在后面。朱淑真从小生活过的地方对她来讲像进入迷宫，不过“迷宫”的景致实在不错，令人赏心悦目。
[ZH] 千里寻向来方向感极差，这么个拐法，如果没有人指引，她绝对会迷路。
[ZH] “苦荞，在朱府你必须时刻待在我身边，府邸太大，七拐八拐的，我怕自己找不着方向。”
[ZH] “小姐放心，我一刻也不离开您。”
[ZH] 也不知道拐了多少个弯，终于看到一个院落。
[ZH] “小姐，夫人就住这里，我带您进去。”
[ZH] 她们走进院子，迎面过来一名丫鬟。
[ZH] “小姐可回来了，快！里面坐，我这就差人去叫夫人。”
[ZH] “姐姐不必差人，门口的下人已去叫夫人。”
[ZH] 苦荞说完介绍道：“这位是夫人房中的丫鬟银杏。”
[ZH] 没等千里寻说话，银杏批评道：“小姐哪里不认得我？要你介绍？”说着拉起小姐向屋里走。
[EN] Kuqiao knocked on the side gate for a moment. Soon the servant on duty opened up, recognized Kuqiao, realized the young mistress was home, and swung the door wide. "Miss is back! Madam has been talking about you nonstop!"
[ZH] 边走边说：“小姐自从嫁到林家，就没回来过，要不是老爷挡着，夫人绝对会跑到林家找小姐。”
[EN] "Where are Master and Madam?" Kuqiao asked. "Miss came to pay her respects."
[ZH] 千里寻很少像此时一样沉默，她怕一开口，在丫鬟面前漏了馅儿。进入屋内，挑椅子坐下。银杏忙着吩咐别的丫鬟上茶。
[EN] "Master's still at the prefecture office. Madam is in the garden feeding the fish," the servant replied, stepping aside to clear the way. Qianli Xun crossed the threshold with Kuqiao right behind her. Once they were inside, the servant added, "Wait indoors, Miss; I'll run report to Madam." He jogged off toward the garden.
[ZH] 茶摆好后，银杏把苦荞拉到一边，悄悄问：“小姐怎么看着怪怪的？总觉得表情跟以前不太一样？”
[EN] "Where do we go now?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] “姐姐别瞎问，这里有我伺候，你尽管去忙。”苦荞说完，回小姐身边站好。
[EN] "Madam's quarters. Just stay right behind me, Miss."
[ZH] 银杏说：“小姐，您坐着，我到门口瞅瞅夫人有没有回来。”
[EN] Kuqiao led the way while Qianli Xun trailed after her. Zhu Shuzhen's childhood home felt like stepping into a labyrinth, though a scenic one that pleased the eye at every turn. With her hopeless sense of direction, Qianli Xun knew she would lose her bearings in seconds without a guide.
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“你去忙吧，不必在跟前伺候。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, stick beside me the whole time in Zhu Manor. This estate is massive and twists every which way; I'll get lost for sure."
[ZH] 银杏出去后，千里寻轻松许多。马上要见到朱淑真的母亲，她认为应该流出些眼泪才显得情真意切。可转念一想，怎么装也装不出朱淑真本人的状态，不如该怎样就怎样，然后再见机行事。
[EN] "Don't worry, Miss. I won't leave your side for a second."
[ZH] 刚打算好，一位四十多岁的妇人双眼微红，被四五个丫鬟簇拥着出现在面前。
[EN] After more twists than she could count, a courtyard gate appeared ahead. "Madam lives here, Miss. Come inside," Kuqiao said.
[ZH] 千里寻猜这位应该是朱淑真的母亲，她不由站起来。
[EN] Stepping into the yard, they met a maid hurrying toward them. "Miss is home! Quick, come sit inside; I'll send someone to fetch Madam!"
[ZH] 朱淑真的母亲向夫人正在园中喂鱼，听到小厮来报女儿回来了，已顾不上其他，带上丫鬟往回赶。
[EN] "No need, Sister. The gatekeeper already went," Kuqiao replied, turning to introduce her. "Miss, this is Yinxing, a maid from Madam's rooms."
[ZH] 一进门，看到半年多没见的女儿，向夫人鼻子一酸，眼睛也跟着变红，见女儿站起来，急忙上前抓住她的手：“淑真，娘以为你以后再也不会回来看我……”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could speak, Yinxing chided her, "Since when does Miss need you introducing me?" Taking Qianli Xun by the arm, she guided her indoors, chattering as they walked. "Miss, you haven't set foot home since marrying into the Lin house. If Master hadn't held her back, Madam would have marched right over to Lin Manor for you!"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“您就是朱淑真的亲娘？我醒来后，苦荞告诉我，我是朱淑真，是朱府的千金，所以今日就让她带我回来认亲。”
[EN] Rarely this quiet, Qianli Xun clamped her lips shut lest one slip give her away. Inside, she picked a chair and sat while Yinxing sent other maids to brew tea. Once tea was laid out, Yinxing drew Kuqiao aside and asked in a hushed voice, "What's gotten into Miss? Her expression looks odd, nothing like before."
[ZH] 千里寻一番话直接将向夫人与周围丫鬟给整蒙。
[EN] "Quit asking silly questions, Sister. I'm waiting on her here; go tend to your chores," Kuqiao brushed her off and resumed her post beside her mistress.
[ZH] 向夫人用禽满泪水的双眼再将女儿瞧一遍：“淑真，你说这话是什么意思？”
[EN] Yinxing turned back to Qianli Xun. "Sit tight, Miss. I'll step out to the doorway and watch for Madam."
[ZH] 只听“扑通”一声，苦荞跪在地上，还未说话，先哭起来。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "Go ahead, no need to hover."
[ZH] 千里寻心中赞叹：“苦荞这丫头真是配合！”
[EN] Once Yinxing left, Qianli Xun let out a quiet breath. About to face Zhu Shuzhen's birth mother, she wondered if she ought to squeeze out a few tears to look sincere, then thought better of it. Faking the original Zhu Shuzhen would look phony; better to stay natural and read the room as it came.
[ZH] 向夫人松开女儿的手，向后退一步，猜到有事情发生，她用丝帕沾了沾眼角的泪水，向其他丫鬟吩咐道：“你们都出去，把门关好。”
[EN] No sooner had she decided than a woman in her forties hurried in with reddened eyes, ringed by four or five maids. Guessing this was Zhu Shuzhen's mother, Qianli Xun rose from her seat.
[ZH] 除了苦荞，其他丫鬟都乖乖退出。向夫人见门已关好，在椅子上坐下：“你们两个这是唱得哪一出？”
[EN] Madam Xiang had been feeding the garden fish when a page boy ran up with word of her daughter's return; dropping everything, she rushed straight back with her maids. Stepping through the doorway and laying eyes on the daughter she had missed for over half a year, she felt her nose sting and tears well up. Seeing Shuzhen stand, she hurried over and seized both her hands. "Shuzhen, Mother thought you'd never come back to see me..."
[ZH] 苦荞会将一切告诉朱淑真的娘，千里寻不必操心，她继续坐好，仔细观察向夫人接下来会如何。
[EN] "You're Zhu Shuzhen's birth mother?" Qianli Xun asked straight out. "When I woke up, Kuqiao told me I'm Zhu Shuzhen, daughter of Zhu Manor, so I had her bring me home today to meet my family."
[ZH] 苦荞哭着说：“夫人，小姐自从嫁给姑爷，没过一天好日子，姑爷动不动打小姐，还把商妓带到家里。就昨天，又把商妓带到家里，还大打出手，结果把小姐猛一推，人撞到柱子上晕死过去，醒来后什么都不记得，像换了个人，呜呜……”
[EN] That single speech froze Madam Xiang and every maid in the room. Staring through eyes brimming with tears, Madam Xiang looked her daughter over from head to toe. "Shuzhen, what on earth are you saying?"
[ZH] 向夫人越听越气：“竟有这等事？为何不早回来告诉我和老爷？”
[EN] Thump! Kuqiao dropped straight to her knees and burst into sobs before getting a word out. Good teammate, Qianli Xun applauded in silence!
[ZH] 苦荞抹着眼泪：“小姐不让，说告诉了也没用，不如让姑爷打死算了。呜呜……”
[EN] Letting go of her daughter and stepping back, Madam Xiang sensed grave trouble. Dabbing the corners of her eyes with a silk handkerchief, she ordered the other maids, "All of you out, and shut the door." Once every maid except Kuqiao filed out and the door clicked shut, Madam Xiang sank into a chair. "What kind of scene are you two putting on?"
[ZH] 向夫人看向女儿，她额头上的确有伤，怪自己见到女儿太过激动，竟没留意到她有伤。向夫人起身走到女儿跟前，用丝帕在伤口上轻轻挨了一下：“疼吗？”
[EN] Knowing Kuqiao would lay out the whole tale, Qianli Xun sat back down and watched how Madam Xiang would take it.
[ZH] 自从母亲车祸去世后，千里寻对母爱更加渴望，向夫人的举动让她心里感到很温暖：“不疼，您真的是我娘？”
[EN] Weeping hard, Kuqiao poured it all out. "Madam, Miss hasn't had one peaceful day since marrying Son-in-law! He beats her over nothing and drags brothel courtesans right into the house! Just yesterday he brought one home again, beat Miss, and shoved her straight into a pillar! She fainted dead away, and when she woke up, she couldn't remember a thing, like a whole different person! Sob..."
[ZH] 向夫人点点头：“你就是我女儿淑真。”
[EN] Fury surged across Madam Xiang's face. "How dare he! Why didn't you come home and tell Master and me sooner?"
[ZH] “娘，我醒来后什么都不记得，如果我与您以前的女儿不一样，您千万不要不认我。”
[EN] Wiping her cheeks, Kuqiao sobbed, "Miss forbade me! Said telling you wouldn't help, might as well let Son-in-law beat her to death! Sob..."
[ZH] 向夫人又流下眼泪：“早知道你到林家是去受罪，娘绝对不会同意把你嫁给林觉。”
[EN] Madam Xiang looked close at her daughter and spotted the wound across her forehead; overwhelmed at seeing her, she hadn't even noticed. Rising, she stepped over and touched the cut light as a feather with her handkerchief. "Does it hurt?"
[ZH] 千里寻认为这个开场很不错，一下子把朱淑真的母亲拉到统一战线上来。
[EN] Starved for motherly warmth since losing her own mother in that crash, Qianli Xun felt heat bloom inside her chest. "Doesn't hurt. Are you really my mother?"
[ZH] 向夫人又说：“苦荞，起来回话。”
[EN] Madam Xiang nodded. "You are my daughter Shuzhen."
[ZH] 苦荞不再哭泣，乖乖站起来。
[EN] "Mother, I woke up remembering nothing. If I'm not like the daughter you knew, please don't disown me."
[ZH] 向夫人又坐回原位：“苦荞，你倒是给我说说，淑真跟以前有何不同？”
[EN] Fresh tears rolled down Madam Xiang's cheeks. "Had I known you'd suffer like this in the Lin house, I never would have agreed to marry you to Lin Jue!"
[ZH] 苦荞用目光征求小姐的意见，千里寻向她点点头，苦荞才大胆说：“除五官和身段是以前的小姐，其他都不一样，现在的小姐说话声音又大，话还多，连茶都不会做，关键是会功夫，姑爷都打不过她，还吵着让姑爷休了她。夫人，我觉得小姐一定是被姑爷打晕后重生了。”
[EN] What a clean start, Qianli Xun thought—already pulled Zhu Shuzhen's mother onto her side!
[ZH] “什么？重生？”向夫人惊得站起来，接着又坐下，拍着桌子大哭，“我那可怜的女儿啊！难道已被林觉打死了不成？呜呜……”
[EN] "Get up and speak, Kuqiao," Madam Xiang ordered. Drying her eyes, Kuqiao climbed to her feet. Sitting back down, Madam Xiang pressed on, "Tell me, Kuqiao: how is Shuzhen different from before?"
[ZH] 千里寻见向夫人哭得伤心，心有不忍：“娘，我活着，没死，只是跟以前有些不同。”
[EN] Glancing at her mistress for permission and catching a nod, Kuqiao spoke up. "Aside from her face and figure, everything's different! Now she speaks loud, talks nonstop, can't even whisk tea, and best of all, she knows martial arts! Even Son-in-law couldn't beat her, and she demanded he cast her out! Madam, I think Son-in-law knocked her cold and she was reborn!"
[ZH] 是啊，这声音还是女儿的声音，样貌也是女儿的样貌，她还活着。向夫人停止大哭：“对，我家淑真还活着，过来，让娘好好看看。”
[EN] "What? Reborn?" Madam Xiang shot up in shock, then dropped back down, slapping the table as she wailed, "My poor daughter! Did Lin Jue beat you to death?! Sob..."
[ZH] 千里寻起身走到向夫人面前，蹲下，头靠在母亲的膝头：“娘！”
[EN] Unable to bear seeing her weep like that, Qianli Xun spoke up. "Mother, I'm alive, not dead, just a bit different from before."
[ZH] 向夫人摸着女儿的头发：“娘差点儿见不到你，不行，我绝对不能再让你回林家。”
[EN] Yes, same voice, same face, living and breathing before her eyes. Madam Xiang choked back her wails. "Yes, my Shuzhen is alive! Come here, let Mother get a good look at you."
[ZH] 千里寻抬起头：“娘，我虽然什么都不记得，可我醒来后，林觉还打我，我亲眼看到商妓在家中，一气之下，让林觉休了我，后来才知女子被夫家所休，名声不好，就打算和离，林觉也同意，让我请朱家长辈出面，三日后商议和离之事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun walked over, crouched down, and rested her head against her mother's knees. "Mother!"
[ZH] 向夫人扶起女儿：“放心，娘绝不会让你继续待在火坑里，算林觉识相，愿意和离，主要是我闺女没有错处，他找不到任何休你的理由。”
[EN] Stroking her hair, Madam Xiang swore, "Mother almost lost you! No, I will never let you go back to that Lin house!"
[ZH] “娘，我想在家住两日，不想回林府。”
[EN] Lifting her head, Qianli Xun said, "Mother, I may not remember the past, yet even after I woke up Lin Jue tried hitting me, and I saw that courtesan right there in the house. Out of anger I told him to write a bill of divorce, till I learned a cast-out wife loses her good name. So I pushed for mutual divorce. Lin Jue agreed and told me to bring Zhu family elders in three days to settle it."
[ZH] “傻女儿，这是你的家，想住多久都可以。”
[EN] Madam Xiang helped her stand. "Rest easy; Mother won't leave you trapped in that fiery pit! Good thing Lin Jue has the sense to agree to mutual divorce. My daughter did nothing wrong; he has zero grounds to cast you out anyway!"
[ZH] 向夫人朝门外喊道：“银杏！”
[EN] "Mother, I want to stay home for a couple of days. I don't want to go back to Lin Manor."
[ZH] 银杏推门而入：“夫人，有何吩咐？”
[EN] "Silly girl, this is your home. Stay as long as you wish!" Madam Xiang called toward the door, "Yinxing!"
[ZH] “你亲自到林府一趟，告诉林家，小姐在朱府住下，至于什么时候回去，暂时还不知。”
[EN] Yinxing pushed the door open. "Your orders, Madam?"
[ZH] “是。”银杏领命而去。
[EN] "Go to Lin Manor in person and inform the Lin family that Miss is staying at Zhu Manor. When she returns, we don't know yet."
[ZH] 千里寻此刻感到很幸福，有家庭做后盾，让她在陌生的时代一下子充满踏实感。
[EN] "Yes, Madam." Yinxing bowed and went off on her errand.
[ZH] “等你爹回来，我会将此事与他说明，你再去拜见他，娘相信你爹也会同意和离之事。”
[EN] A rush of joy warmed Qianli Xun. Having real family backing her anchored her in this strange century.
[ZH] “我爹会不会很凶？”
[EN] "Once your father gets home, I'll explain everything before you pay your respects," Madam Xiang told her. "Mother believes he'll consent to mutual divorce too."
[ZH] 千里寻的问题令向夫人伤心，看来女儿真的什么都不记得：“你爹不凶，但他很严肃，有时候也很严厉，你不必担忧。以前的院子还给你留着，让苦荞带你过去，既然什么都记不得，就让苦荞再带你各处走走，熟悉熟悉，也许慢慢会想起来。”
[EN] "Is Father fierce?"
[ZH] 千里寻拜别向夫人，在苦荞的带领下继续拐着弯儿，向朱府深处走去。很难想象朱府到底有多大，反正此刻她觉得怎么走都走不到尽头。
[EN] The question pierced Madam Xiang's heart, proving her daughter truly recalled nothing. "Not fierce, just stern, and strict at times. Nothing to fear. Your old courtyard is still kept for you. Have Kuqiao take you over and walk you around the estate so you can get your bearings; maybe your memories will come back bit by bit."
[ZH] 走着走着，听到唱曲儿的声音。千里寻停下脚步，向声音传来的方向望去，果然有个院落，院墙挡着，根本看不到里面的房屋。
[EN] Bidding Madam Xiang farewell, Qianli Xun followed Kuqiao through winding lane after winding lane deeper into Zhu Manor. Impossible to guess how sprawling the grounds were; it felt as if they could walk forever without hitting a wall.
[ZH] “苦荞，那里住着什么人，怎么有人在唱曲儿？”
[EN] Along the way, singing drifted across the air. Qianli Xun halted and looked toward the sound, spotting another walled courtyard whose high walls hid the halls within. "Kuqiao, who lives in there? Why is someone singing?"
[ZH] “那里住着老爷养的歌伎和舞伎。”
[EN] "Those are the singing girls and dancing girls Master keeps."
[ZH] “什么？歌伎和舞伎？我爹养她们做什么？”
[EN] "What? Singing and dancing girls? Why does my father keep entertainers?"
[ZH] “达官贵人家里基本都会养歌伎和舞伎，主要用来招待有身份的客人。老爷每年会举办诗会，邀请全临安有头有脸的文人才子参加，诗会也需歌伎和舞伎烘场面。”
[EN] "Most high officials' estates keep musicians and dancers to entertain honored guests. Every year Master hosts a poetry banquet for Lin'an's noted scholars, and entertainers help liven up the occasion."
[ZH] “我可参加过诗会？”
[EN] "Did I attend those banquets?"
[ZH] “小姐从十岁开始年年参加，您的好多诗词正是在诗会上做出，被文人才子争相传诵，才有了江南女词人的美称。”
[EN] "Every year since you were ten, Miss! Many of your finest poems and lyrics were composed right at those banquets and passed from hand to hand by scholars. That's how you earned the title Poetess of Jiangnan."
[ZH] “江南女词人。”千里寻低首沉吟，小说她倒会写，诗词她可是门外汉，顶着朱淑真的身份，肯定要维护她在文坛的地位，千里寻倍感压力。想到已将朱淑真的诗词记在脑中，才松口气，“苦荞，朱府难道没有一条直路吗？曲里拐弯的？”
[EN] "The Poetess of Jiangnan," Qianli Xun murmured. Web novels she could spin in her sleep, yet classical verse was not her craft. Wearing Zhu Shuzhen's name meant defending her place in the literary world, and pressure pricked at her chest until she remembered she knew Zhu Shuzhen's poems by heart. Exhaling, she asked, "Kuqiao, isn't there a single straight path in Zhu Manor? Everything twists like a maze!"
[ZH] “还真没有，我听说老爷建这座宅子时，取得就是曲径通幽，是不是很有诗意？”
[EN] "Not one! Heard Master built this manor around the poetic ideal of winding paths leading to secluded spots. Isn't it poetic?"
[ZH] “是有诗意，可把我搞得晕头转向。我住的地方到底在哪里？”
[EN] "Poetic, sure, and it's making me dizzy! Where on earth is my courtyard?"
[ZH] “小姐别急，再拐两个弯就到了。”
[EN] "Patience, Miss. Two more bends and we're there."
[ZH] 千里寻跟着苦荞拐了两个弯，眼前出现一片湖水，并不大，荷叶将湖水占去一大半儿，零零散散竖着几个花骨朵。湖上横着一座木质小桥，穿过这座桥，才真正到达朱淑真住过的园子。
[EN] Two turns later, a small lake opened before them, over half its surface blanketed with lotus leaves dotted with upright lotus buds. A little wooden bridge spanned the water, leading at last into Zhu Shuzhen's own secluded garden.

#CH 11
[ZH] 从园子里迎面走出一位丫鬟，见千里寻先行礼：“小姐，我与几个负责洒扫的丫鬟已把院子和屋子收拾干净，您回来，正好住。”
[EN] A maid stepped out of the garden and bowed the instant she saw Qianli Xun. "Miss, the sweeping maids and I have already cleaned the courtyard and rooms—all ready for you to move right in."
[ZH] 苦荞小声对小姐说：“她是绿桃。”
[EN] Kuqiao whispered in her ear, "That's Lutao."
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“原来是绿桃啊，没想到我刚回来，你们就把屋子收拾干净，真是神速！”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled. "Ah, Lutao! I just walked in the gate and you already got the place spotless—fast work!"
[ZH] 绿桃觉得小姐跟以前很不一样，作为下人，不能当面问：“小姐忘了，您出嫁时吩咐，不管您回不回朱府，都让我每日差人把院子和屋子收拾干净。”
[EN] Sensing her mistress sounded off yet knowing her place as a servant, Lutao kept her surprise to herself. "Did you forget, Miss? When you married, you instructed me to have the courtyard and rooms cleaned every single day, whether you came back or not."
[ZH] “哦，原来是我吩咐的，可我已出嫁，嫁出去的女儿泼出去的水，我爹为何还把这园子给我留着？难道他舍不得我，专门给我留的？”千里寻想通过这个问题，从绿桃口中了解还未谋面的父亲对出嫁后的女儿到底是什么态度。
[EN] "Oh, right—my orders! Still, I'm married now, and a married daughter is like water poured out. Why did Father keep this garden for me? Did he miss me and save it on purpose?" Qianli Xun fished for clues about her unseen father's feelings toward his married daughter.
[ZH] 绿桃本想摸摸小姐的额头，看她是不是发烧，要不怎么会问出这样的问题。见小姐额头有伤，没敢去摸，直接说：“小姐，您该不会因为额头有伤，影响到记忆力？这园子又远又偏，没人能瞧得上，老爷更不会把它当回事，只有小姐这样喜欢安静的人才把这里当成宝。”
[EN] Lutao almost reached up to check her mistress's forehead for fever—why ask such a question? Spotting the wound there, she held back and answered plain, "Miss, did that cut on your forehead rattle your memory? This garden is so tucked away nobody wants it, and Master pays it no mind. Only someone who loves peace and quiet like you treats it like treasure."
[ZH] 千里寻感到尴尬，努力掩饰：“我当然知道是这样，只是希望我爹能关心一下我，才故意问问。苦荞，随我进园子！”
[EN] Embarrassed, Qianli Xun covered fast. "Of course I know that! I just hoped Father cared about me a little, so I asked. Kuqiao, let's go in!"
[ZH] 苦荞对绿桃说：“快中午，姐姐去安排饭吧，有我陪小姐，你不用操心。”
[EN] Kuqiao told Lutao, "Almost noon, Sister—go see to lunch. I'm here with Miss, don't worry about us."
[ZH] 绿桃拜别而去。
[EN] Lutao bowed and took her leave.
[ZH] 千里寻踏入园子，她觉得像是进入乡间小院儿，又比乡间小院儿更加别致。有三处用低矮的栅栏围起：一处种着紫苏，紫色的叶子一片挨着一片，长得很茂盛；一处种着向日葵，高高的花盘迎着阳光；一处种着菊花，不只一个品种，有的正开放，有的还未到开放的季节。
[EN] Stepping inside, Qianli Xun felt as if she had entered a rustic cottage yard, only far more refined. Three plots sat behind low fences: one brimming with lush purple perilla leaves; one holding tall sunflowers angled toward the sun; and one filled with several varieties of chrysanthemums, some already in bloom, others still waiting for their season.
[ZH] 围着菊花的栅栏旁，放置一张精致的圆形木桌，旁边有把藤椅。
[EN] Beside the chrysanthemum fence stood a fine round wooden table paired with a rattan chair.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“在朱府时，小姐总喜欢烹上一壶茶，坐在藤椅上赏菊花。”
[EN] "Back when you lived here, Miss, you loved brewing a pot of tea and sitting in that rattan chair to admire the chrysanthemums," Kuqiao said.
[ZH] 千里寻想象着朱淑真坐在这里手捧书卷，品茶赏菊的情景。她也想体验一番，走到藤椅跟前坐下，欣赏着已开放的菊花，不由诗兴大发，吟诵起朱淑真写过的一首诗:
[EN] Picturing Zhu Shuzhen seated there with a book, sipping tea among the blossoms, Qianli Xun wanted to try it herself. She settled into the rattan chair, gazed at the open chrysanthemums, and felt a poetic spark stir—reciting a poem by Zhu Shuzhen:
[ZH] 土花能白又能红，晚节犹能爱此工。宁可抱香枝上老，不随黄叶舞秋风。（《菊花》）
[EN] "Wild blooms can show both white and red; in late-season virtue I prize such craft. Sooner grow old on the branch hugging my scent than dance with yellow leaves on the autumn wind." ("Chrysanthemum") [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 苦荞激动地问：“小姐，您恢复记忆了？”
[EN] Kuqiao gasped in excitement. "Miss, your memory came back!"
[ZH] 原来这丫头虽找了重生的理由，可从心里还是愿意接受小姐只是失忆，听到千里寻吟诵朱淑真写过的诗，以为从前的小姐又回来。
[EN] Even after inventing the "rebirth" excuse, the girl still clung to the hope her mistress only had amnesia; hearing her recite her own verse made Kuqiao think the old Zhu Shuzhen had returned.
[ZH] 千里寻没有回答她，而是问道：“刚才你让绿桃去安排饭食，难道我要在这里用饭？”
[EN] Instead of answering, Qianli Xun asked, "You just told Lutao to arrange lunch—am I eating here?"
[ZH] 苦荞的希望落空，眼前的小姐对朱府极其陌生，什么都不懂，她失落地回道：“是。”
[EN] Hope draining from her face—this mistress still didn't know basic house rules—Kuqiao replied in a flat tone, "Yes."
[ZH] “我刚回朱府，为何不与我娘一起用饭？”
[EN] "I just got back to Zhu Manor. Why aren't I eating with Mother?"
[ZH] “朱府很大，平常各园子各自独立，只有特殊日子或是老爷提出要求，大家才在一起用饭。今日小姐刚回来，老爷还未归，夫人不能与小姐一起用饭，要等老爷回来，征得老爷同意方可。”
[EN] "Zhu Manor is huge, Miss. Each courtyard dines on its own unless it's a special occasion or Master gives the word. You just arrived and Master isn't home yet, so Madam can't dine with you until Master returns and approves."
[ZH] “什么？”千里寻站起来，“我跟我娘一起吃个饭，还得我爹同意？”
[EN] "What?" Qianli Xun jumped up. "I need my father's permission just to eat lunch with my own mother?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“这是朱府历来的规矩。”
[EN] "That's always been the rule in Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 千里寻很担心：“我娘刚才跟我说的那些同意我和离的话，难道也需征求我爹同意？”
[EN] Dread crept in. "Wait—everything Mother just said about backing my mutual divorce... does that need Father's nod too?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“这个家历来都是老爷做主，夫人说的那些话出自真情，也是心里话，但都需老爷点头方可。”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded. "Master makes every decision in this house. Madam meant every word from the heart, yet none of it counts unless Master agrees."
[ZH] 千里寻本以为请长辈出面之事，已搞定一半儿，现在才知分毫未搞定。
[EN] Just when she thought half the battle was won, she hadn't even left the starting line.
[ZH] “你为什么不早告诉我家里是我爹做主？”
[EN] "Why didn't you tell me earlier Father runs the whole show?"
[ZH] 苦荞很委屈：“小姐没问。”
[EN] "You didn't ask, Miss," Kuqiao mumbled, feeling wronged.
[ZH] “算了，不能怪你。我记得你说过我爹很重视清誉，只要和离不影响朱家清誉，我估计他应该不会阻拦。”
[EN] "Forget it, not your fault. You said Father guards family honor—long as mutual divorce doesn't stain the Zhu name, he shouldn't block it."
[ZH] “小姐，和离虽比被夫家所休传出去好听，可也不是什么好事，只怕老爷不会同意。”
[EN] "Miss, mutual divorce sounds better than being cast out, sure, yet it's still no honor. I'm afraid Master won't agree."
[ZH] 苦荞的话给了千里寻当头一棒，她镇定了一会儿，安慰自己：“我相信，但凡是当爹的，肯定会把子女的幸福看得更重要。”
[EN] Kuqiao's words hit like a cold slap. Steadying herself, Qianli Xun tried optimism. "Any real father puts his daughter's happiness first."
[ZH] 接着又问出在向夫人面前提到的问题：“我爹凶不凶？”
[EN] Then she asked the same question she had asked Madam Xiang: "Is Father fierce?"
[ZH] “老爷毕竟是知府，有学问，有修养，他很少发怒，不过很严肃，让人望而却步，下人见到老爷大气都不敢出。”
[EN] "Master is a Prefect—learned and cultured. He rarely loses his temper, yet he's so stern he keeps everyone at arm's length. Servants barely dare breathe around him."
[ZH] “有这么可怕吗？”
[EN] "That scary?"
[ZH] “有，小姐您从小到大跟老爷都不亲近，就是因为他太过严肃，您跟老爷除了礼节性的交流，从来没在一起谈过心，聊过天儿。”
[EN] "Yes! You were never close to Master growing up because he's so stiff. Aside from formal greetings, you two never once sat down for a heart-to-heart or a casual chat."
[ZH] 穿越前的时代，千里寻从小与父亲的关系最好，父亲总是宠着她，没事给她讲故事，跟她聊天儿，天南地北地瞎扯。她一直认为所有的父亲对待女儿都是如此。后来父亲出家，云游四海，依然会在每年中秋节时回来陪她一起度过。
[EN] Back in her old life, Qianli Xun had been closest to her dad—he spoiled her, spun stories, and bantered about everything under the sun. She had assumed all fathers loved their daughters like that. Even after shaving his head to wander the land, he still came home every Mid-Autumn Festival to spend the holiday with her.
[ZH] 她很难想象，朱淑真与她的父亲将父女关系处理成上下级关系是怎样一种感觉。
[EN] Treating a father-daughter bond like boss and subordinate sounded surreal.
[ZH] “我爹这个样子怎么搞诗会？还不得把那些文人给吓跑了！”
[EN] "How does he even host poetry banquets acting like that? Doesn't he scare all the scholars off?"
[ZH] “老爷只在家里如此，他对待文人和同僚还是有笑脸的。”
[EN] "Master's only like that at home. Around scholars and colleagues, he smiles plenty."
[ZH] “真是个奇怪的人。”
[EN] "What a weird guy."
[ZH] 两人正聊着，绿桃带领五个丫鬟和一个婆子进来。
[EN] As they talked, Lutao arrived leading five maids and an older servant woman.
[ZH] “小姐，该用饭了，夫人吩咐多给您加两道菜。”
[EN] "Time for lunch, Miss. Madam ordered two extra dishes added for you."
[ZH] 只见每个丫鬟手里端着一盘菜，婆子提着食盒。她们走进后，先向小姐行礼，然后进屋把饭菜摆好。
[EN] Each maid carried a dish while the older woman carried a food carrier. They bowed, stepped inside, and laid the table.
[ZH] 绿桃又带众人行礼离开：“小姐慢用，有苦荞伺候，奴婢就不在此打扰。”
[EN] Lutao led them in a farewell bow. "Enjoy your meal, Miss. With Kuqiao attending you, we won't disturb you."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，心想绿桃还真懂事，猜到她只想让苦荞留下。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded—tactful girl, sensing she only wanted Kuqiao around.
[ZH] 她走进屋内，里面的布置与林家的竹兰居并无两样，看来朱淑真就喜欢这种雅而不俗的风格。
[EN] Stepping inside, she found the interior matched Lin Manor's Zhulan Residence—clearly Zhu Shuzhen favored this refined, uncluttered style.
[ZH] 瞧到一桌子精美的食物，食欲大增，她快速坐在桌前享用起来。吃了两口，见苦荞在一旁只管伺候她，命令道：“你也坐下一起吃。”
[EN] Appetite roused by the spread of delicacies, she sat down and dug right in. Two bites in, spotting Kuqiao standing by ready to serve, she ordered, "Sit down and eat with me."
[ZH] “这怎么行？哪有下人与主子同桌用饭？朱家没这个规矩。”
[EN] "I couldn't! Servants never eat at the same table as masters—Zhu Manor has no such custom!"
[ZH] “什么规矩不规矩的，这么多菜，我一个人吃不完，剩下多浪费，赶紧的，别废话。”
[EN] "Forget custom! I can't finish all this food alone, and wasting it is a sin. Sit down, quit arguing."
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐态度坚决，不再推辞，也坐在桌旁：“没想到我有生之年竟然能和小姐一桌吃饭。”
[EN] Seeing her mistress wouldn't budge, Kuqiao gave in and took a seat. "Never dreamed I'd live to share a table with Miss."
[ZH] “快吃吧，允许你以后天天跟我一桌吃饭。”
[EN] "Eat up. From now on, you eat at my table every day."
[ZH] 苦荞很感动：“小姐，我喜欢您现在的样子，虽然还很怀念以前的小姐，甚至希望小姐回到以前的样子，但我从心里更喜欢现在的小姐。”
[EN] Moved, Kuqiao said, "Miss, I really like you now. Even though I still miss the old Miss and sometimes wish she'd come back, deep down I like you even better."
[ZH] “什么以前的现在的？我就是你家小姐。”
[EN] "Old me, new me—I'm your Miss either way."
[ZH] “您说得对！”
[EN] "You're right!"
[ZH] 主仆二人动起筷子，有了苦荞加入，饭菜很快被吃个精光。用过饭后，千里寻在书桌前翻阅朱淑真曾经读过的书，感到有些困倦时，才宽衣躺到床上。
[EN] With Kuqiao pitching in, the two of them polished off every plate. Afterward, Qianli Xun browsed through Zhu Shuzhen's books at the desk until drowsiness crept in, then shed her outer robes for a nap.
[ZH] 大约半个时辰后她醒来，刚爬起穿好衣裳时，银杏前来拜见。
[EN] She woke about an hour later and had just pulled on her clothes when Yinxing arrived to call.
[ZH] 苦荞把银杏领进屋时，千里寻看到她手里拿着一个锦盒。
[EN] Kuqiao showed Yinxing in; Qianli Xun spotted a brocade box in her hands.
[ZH] “小姐，夫人让我把这个送来，说是替您预备的，本来是要在您的大侄子满月时送上，可小姐那时并未回朱府，既然现在回来，就由您亲自或派人送给您的大侄子。”
[EN] "Miss, Madam sent this over—she prepared it on your behalf for your nephew's full-month celebration. Since you weren't home then, now that you're back, you can deliver it yourself or send someone with it."
[ZH] 苦荞接过锦盒，送到小姐手中。
[EN] Kuqiao took the box and handed it to her mistress.
[ZH] 千里寻打开一看，原来是只长命百岁的金锁，纯金打造，价值不菲。她感慨这位母亲实在是贴心，什么都替女儿操心到。合上盖子递给苦荞：“收好，一会儿随我去看看我的大侄子。”
[EN] Opening the lid, Qianli Xun saw a solid-gold Longevity Gold Lock—worth a small fortune. What a thoughtful mother, looking out for every detail! Closing the box, she handed it back to Kuqiao. "Keep it safe. Come with me to see my little nephew in a bit."
[ZH] 银杏完成任务，行完礼就要离开，被千里寻拦住：“留步，我还有事要问你。”
[EN] Task done, Yinxing bowed to leave, only for Qianli Xun to stop her. "Hold on—I have a question."
[ZH] 银杏只好站住：“小姐有什么事，尽管吩咐。”
[EN] Yinxing paused. "Yes, Miss?"
[ZH] “你去过林家？”
[EN] "Did you go to Lin Manor?"
[ZH] “奴婢去了，林家大爷说小姐想在朱府住多久都行，等姑爷回来，他定向姑爷转达。”
[EN] "I did. Eldest Master Lin said you may stay at Zhu Manor as long as you wish, and he'll pass word to Son-in-law once he returns."
[ZH] “林家大爷？”
[EN] "Eldest Master Lin?"
[ZH] 银杏疑惑小姐怎么连林家大爷都不知道，又不好过问，于是回道：“就是姑爷的大哥？”
[EN] Puzzled that her mistress didn't know who that was yet too polite to pry, Yinxing answered, "Son-in-law's eldest brother."
[ZH] 千里寻怕再问下去，引起银杏怀疑，把已到喉咙的问题咽进肚内，只说道：“辛苦了，你去忙吧。”
[EN] Afraid more questions would arouse suspicion, Qianli Xun swallowed the rest and said, "Good work. Go tend to your duties."
[ZH] 银杏刚一走，千里寻急切地问：“苦荞，我在林家也待了半天一晚，再加一早上，也没发现林觉有个大哥，怎么突然蹦出个大哥来？”
[EN] The second Yinxing stepped out, Qianli Xun pounced on Kuqiao. "I spent half a day, a night, and a morning at Lin Manor and never saw any big brother—where did an eldest brother pop out from?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“林府虽比不上朱府大，可也不小，林家大爷住北边，林家二爷住东边，姑爷排行老三，住西面，离得都不近，平常大家很少照面。”
[EN] "Lin Manor isn't as vast as Zhu Manor, yet it's still big," Kuqiao explained. "Eldest Master Lin lives in the north wing, Second Master Lin in the east, and Son-in-law is the third son over in the west. They live far apart and rarely cross paths."
[ZH] 千里寻听到还有个二爷，更是担忧：“林觉家里的人倒挺多，两个哥哥再加上他父母，到时和离岂不是人多势众？”
[EN] Hearing there was a second brother too made Qianli Xun's stomach sink. "He's got a whole crowd—two brothers plus his parents! Won't they outnumber me at the divorce meeting?"
[ZH] “小姐有所不知，姑爷的父母早已离世。”
[EN] "You don't know, Miss—Son-in-law's parents passed away years ago."
[ZH] 顿时减少两人，千里寻又感到庆幸：“那我就放心了。”
[EN] Two down already—Qianli Xun exhaled in relief. "Well, that's a break."
[ZH] 苦荞又说：“可林家是大家族，姑爷的族叔我知道的就有七位，而且还有我不知道的。”
[EN] "Except the Lins are a huge clan," Kuqiao added. "Just the clan uncles I know of number seven—and there are more I don't know."
[ZH] “什么？这么多？”千里寻顿时站起来，在屋子里走来走去，“我一个都没搞定，他那里就有那么多长辈，如此对比下去，到时我岂不是处于弱势？只怕想和离都难。苦荞，你说到时林家的长辈会不会一个都不会来？”
[EN] "What? That many?" Qianli Xun sprang up and paced the floor. "I haven't nailed down a single elder yet, and he has an army! With those odds, they'll steamroll me—I'll never get divorced! Kuqiao, any chance none of the Lin elders show up?"
[ZH] “我看难。”苦荞也替小姐担忧。
[EN] "Not likely," Kuqiao said, sharing her worry.
[ZH] 千里寻瞅一眼苦荞手中的锦盒：“不如去拜见一下哥哥，先把他搞定再说。”
[EN] Eyeing the brocade box in Kuqiao's hands, Qianli Xun decided, "Let's go see my brother first and get him locked in."
[ZH] 千里寻打定主意，就往出走，苦荞只能跟上。走到园子门外，千里寻停住脚步，她根本不知道朝哪个方向走：“苦荞，还是你前面带路。”
[EN] Mind made up, she headed straight out, Kuqiao hurrying after her. Right past the garden gate, Qianli Xun froze—she had zero clue which way to go. "Kuqiao, take the lead again."
[ZH] 苦荞又当起引路人，她们朝朱府的东边走去。
[EN] Resuming her role as guide, Kuqiao led her toward the eastern side of Zhu Manor.

#CH 12
[ZH] 走了一阵，依然是曲径通幽，不同的是朱淑真住的园子像是幽中之幽，她哥嫂住的地方则是开阔之地。到达时，千里寻只觉眼前一亮，视野也随之变得开阔起来。
[EN] They walked on through winding paths—only where Zhu Shuzhen's courtyard was tucked in the deepest seclusion, her brother and sister-in-law's quarters opened into wide, airy grounds. Arriving there, Qianli Xun felt her eyes brighten as the view expanded.
[ZH] 朱淑真的哥哥叫朱景元，文才不及妹妹，倒也能写些文章。毕竟是家中男丁，父亲朱晞颜通过关系给他在临安谋了个文职，也娶了临安首富陈家的女儿陈文心为妻，夫妻挺和睦。朱淑真成婚时，陈文心已有八个月身孕，后诞下一儿子，有六个月有余。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen's older brother, Zhu Jingyuan, lacked his sister's brilliance yet could turn out decent essays. Being the sole son, his father Zhu Xiyan had pulled strings to land him a civil post in Lin'an and married him to Chen Wenxin, daughter of the Chen family, the wealthiest clan in Lin'an. The couple got along well. When Zhu Shuzhen married, Chen Wenxin had been eight months pregnant; their son was now over six months old.
[ZH] 千里寻到达朱景元住的园子外，停住脚步：“苦荞，哥哥和嫂嫂人可好？”
[EN] Outside Zhu Jingyuan's courtyard gate, Qianli Xun halted. "Kuqiao, what are my brother and sister-in-law like?"
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“大公子才华虽比不上小姐您，人却老实，对小姐也好。至于少夫人，我听说嘴是刻薄些，人吧，还行。”
[EN] "Eldest Young Master may not match your talent, Miss, yet he's honest and treats you well," Kuqiao replied. "As for Young Madam—I've heard she has a sharp tongue, though she's decent at heart."
[ZH] “听说？你没见过她？”
[EN] "Heard? You haven't met her?"
[ZH] “跟着小姐见过几次，小姐不甚理她，她也没机会跟小姐说话，所以我没领教过。”
[EN] "Only a few times when following you, Miss. You barely spoke to her, so she never got the chance to say much—haven't seen her sharp tongue myself."
[ZH] “我以前不理人家，这次可是要求到人家门上来，你去敲门吧。”
[EN] "Used to ignore her, huh? Well, today I'm the one asking a favor. Go knock."
[ZH] 苦荞上前敲了敲园门。
[EN] Kuqiao stepped up and rapped on the garden gate.
[ZH] 门从里面打开，探出一个丫鬟的脑袋：“是苦荞啊，听说你跟小姐回来了，本来不信，现在果真见到。”
[EN] The gate cracked open and a maid poked her head out. "Oh, Kuqiao! Heard you came back with Miss—didn't believe it till I saw you!"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“姐姐好，小姐好不容易回来一趟，过来拜见公子和少夫人。”
[EN] "Good day, Sister," Kuqiao said. "Miss finally made it home and came to visit Young Master and Young Madam."
[ZH] 丫鬟给里面的小厮说：“快去禀报大公子，小姐来访。”
[EN] The maid called over her shoulder to a page boy inside, "Run tell Eldest Young Master that Miss is here!"
[ZH] 丫鬟把门开大，行礼道：“小姐来得正好，大公子刚忙完公务回来，您跟苦荞进来，我带您过去。”
[EN] Swinging the gate wide, she curtsied. "Perfect timing, Miss—Eldest Young Master just got back from office duties. Come on in, I'll take you right to him."
[ZH] 千里寻用眼神向苦荞询问，苦荞领会，在她耳边悄悄说：“她叫月桂，是少夫人从陈家带来的丫鬟。”
[EN] Catching her mistress's questioning glance, Kuqiao leaned in and whispered, "Her name is Yuegui, the maid Young Madam brought from the Chen house."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，二人跟着月桂朝里走。院子甚大，里面的房屋不少，看上去像一座府宅。千里寻感慨，同一个朱府，每个园子的风格甚不相同。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded, and they followed Yuegui inside. The yard was vast and lined with pavilions, almost a manor of its own—every courtyard in Zhu Manor had its own distinct style.
[ZH] 走着走着，迎面来了一位公子，个头不低，长相周正不失帅气，笑起来的样子跟向夫人有五分相似，千里寻猜出他定是朱淑真的哥哥。
[EN] Walking along, they met a tall young gentleman approaching—clean-cut, handsome, his smile carrying half of Madam Xiang's warmth. Qianli Xun knew at once this was Zhu Shuzhen's brother.
[ZH] 朱景元见到妹妹，甚是喜悦：“我回府已见过娘，她告诉我你回到家中，正想换完衣裳，逗逗莱宝，再去看妹妹，没成想妹妹竟亲自登门，也省得我跑一趟。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan beamed at the sight of his sister. "Just saw Mother when I got back and heard you were home. I was about to change clothes, play with Laibao a bit, and come visit you—never expected you to walk right over and save me the trip!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有兄弟姐妹，穿越而来，平白无故多一位哥哥，她从心底感到满意，学着下人行礼的样子：“我半年多没回来，好不容易回来一趟，当然要看看哥哥，还得看我大侄子，也得和嫂嫂聊两句。”
[EN] Having grown up an only child, Qianli Xun loved gaining an older brother out of the blue. Copying the servants' bow, she said, "Been away over half a year—now that I'm home, of course I had to visit my brother, see my nephew, and chat with my sister-in-law."
[ZH] 朱景元已从母亲口中得知淑真重生失忆之事，亲眼见到，才知妹妹有多严重。她以前见他从不行礼，最多称呼声“哥哥”，再不会多说一个字。朱景元从小已习惯，他深知这位妹妹少言寡语，喜欢安静，心思全用在诗词上，从不愿跟人说些没用的废话，更别说与人聊天儿。今日，她一见面竟说要和嫂嫂聊两句，实在不一样得厉害。
[EN] Already briefed by Madam Xiang on Shuzhen's "rebirth" and amnesia, Zhu Jingyuan still gaped at seeing how drastic the change was. Before, she never bowed to him, barely uttered "Brother," and never wasted a syllable on small talk—quiet, buried in poetry, immune to chitchat. Today she walked up wanting to chat with her sister-in-law! Night and day.
[ZH] 朱景元还是上前扶住妹妹：“文心正在屋里逗莱宝，我带你去见她。”
[EN] Still, he reached out to steady her. "Wenxin is inside playing with Laibao. Come meet her."
[ZH] 千里寻对哥哥有说不出的喜欢，也许是因为她曾渴望有位哥哥，终于实现，一高兴，忘乎所以，直接用手搀住朱景元的手臂：“有个哥哥真好，以后我要在外面被人欺负，就有哥哥保护了。”
[EN] Warmth swelled in Qianli Xun—maybe she had always wished for an older brother. Carried away with delight, she linked her arm right through Zhu Jingyuan's. "Having a brother feels awesome! Now if anybody bullies me outside, my brother's got my back!"
[ZH] 向来只有千里寻欺负别人的份儿，但她毕竟是女孩子，从心里渴望被人保护，尤其是有亲哥哥的保护。
[EN] Usually Qianli Xun was the one knocking people flat, yet deep down she was still a woman who liked the idea of protection—especially from a big brother.
[ZH] 朱景元被很不一样的妹妹整懵，瞧见她额头的伤，应该是这伤搞得她重生后失忆：“没想到妹妹嫁人后变得跟以前大不相同。头上的伤还疼吗？”
[EN] Thrown off by this unrecognizable sister, Zhu Jingyuan spotted the wound on her forehead—that injury must have caused the rebirth and amnesia. "Marriage sure changed you, sister. Does your forehead still hurt?"
[ZH] 千里寻掺着朱景元，一边往里走一边说：“苦荞给我上过药，伤口不是很疼，可疼在心里，林觉家暴成性，我跟他实在过不下去，哥哥要替我做主。”
[EN] Clinging to his arm as they walked inside, Qianli Xun said, "Kuqiao put salve on it, so the cut barely hurts—what hurts is my heart! Lin Jue beats me nonstop. I can't live with him another day—Brother, you have to stand up for me!"
[ZH] “娘已把你的情况告诉我，哥也很气愤，我这么个如花似玉的妹妹嫁给他，他还有什么不满意的？竟然出手打人！天理难容，哥肯定会为你出面，我们进去说。”
[EN] "Mother told me everything, and I'm furious too! Marrying a gem like my sister, what more could he want? Raising a fist to her defies Heaven! Count on me—I'll stand up for you. Let's talk inside."
[ZH] 二人说着走进屋子。
[EN] They stepped into the room together.
[ZH] 莱宝坐在用檀木做成的小床上，咿咿呀呀笑着，陈文心拿着拨浪鼓逗孩子，旁边站着伺候的丫鬟。
[EN] Little Laibao sat babbling and laughing on a small sandalwood crib while Chen Wenxin shook a pellet drum to amuse him, maids standing at attention beside her.
[ZH] 陈文心看到淑真进来，把拨浪鼓递给丫鬟，站起来迎上去：“妹妹真是稀客，怎么想着来我们这里？”
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen enter, Chen Wenxin handed the drum to a maid and rose to greet her. "Look what rare guest blew in—what brings you over to our side, Sister?"
[ZH] 千里寻放开朱景元，伸出手，苦荞赶紧把锦盒放到小姐手中。千里寻接过锦盒递给陈文心：“嫂嫂，这是给大侄子的见面礼，满月时跟爹赌气，没回来，今日既然回来就补上。”
[EN] Letting go of Zhu Jingyuan, Qianli Xun held out her hand; Kuqiao slipped the brocade box into it. Offering it to Chen Wenxin, Qianli Xun said, "Sister-in-law, a first-meeting gift for my nephew. I was sulking with Father during his full-month feast and missed it, so let me make it up today."
[ZH] 朱淑真第一次跟陈文心说这么多话，说话时还笑脸相迎，陈文心感到意外。她接过锦盒，打开一瞧，是枚金灿灿的锁子：“没想到妹妹成亲后，倒像变了个人，礼物不错，这就让你大侄子戴上。”
[EN] Never before had Zhu Shuzhen spoken this many words to her—let alone with a smile. Surprised, Chen Wenxin accepted the box and lifted the lid to see the gleaming gold lock. "Marriage really turned you into a new person! Lovely gift—let's put it right on your nephew."
[ZH] 月桂从陈文心手中接过锦盒，把金锁拿出来，给莱宝戴在脖子上。
[EN] Yuegui took the box from Chen Wenxin, lifted out the gold lock, and slipped it around Laibao's neck.
[ZH] 陈文心说：“妹妹坐吧。”
[EN] "Take a seat, Sister," Chen Wenxin said.
[ZH] 接着对旁边的丫鬟大声说：“杵着干什么？来客人了，还不赶紧上茶！”
[EN] Then she barked at a nearby maid: "What are you standing there like a post for? We have guests—go fetch tea!"
[ZH] 被训斥的丫鬟不敢说一句，忙去准备茶水。
[EN] Too scared to speak, the maid scurried off to prepare tea.
[ZH] 朱景元上前拉起陈文心：“别跟下人置气，来，坐下，妹妹好不容易来一趟，我们跟她好好聊聊。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan took Chen Wenxin's arm. "Don't snap at the servants. Come sit down—Shuzhen rarely visits, let's have a nice talk."
[ZH] 千里寻坐下，观察眼前这二位，看样子哥哥对嫂嫂不错，的确是个好男人。
[EN] Taking a seat, Qianli Xun observed the couple. Her brother treated his wife with genuine affection—a solid man.
[ZH] 陈文心与朱景元坐好后，瞅了一眼淑真：“妹妹平常都不大理我，今日突然过来，让我好生意外，我想除看你大侄子外，应该还有其他事吧？”
[EN] Once seated beside her husband, Chen Wenxin eyed Shuzhen. "You hardly ever look my way, Sister—dropping by out of the blue really surprises me. Aside from seeing Laibao, you must have something else on your mind?"
[ZH] 丫鬟进来把茶摆好，手拿盘子站在一旁伺候。
[EN] The maid returned, set the tea down, and stayed standing beside them with her tray.
[ZH] 陈文心气又不打一处来：“主人家说话，你个下人杵在那里干什么，还不赶紧出去！”
[EN] Chen Wenxin flared up again. "Masters are talking—why are you still loitering here? Get out!"
[ZH] 丫鬟心里着实委屈，屋里明明还有苦荞和月桂，以及另一名负责逗莱宝的丫鬟，为何她就不能在？再委屈也不能与主人顶嘴，只能灰溜溜地出去。
[EN] Stung—Kuqiao, Yuegui, and the maid tending Laibao were all still in the room, so why single her out?—the maid dared not talk back and slunk out.
[ZH] 千里寻这才说：“难道我就不能来看望哥哥和嫂嫂？以前是淑真不懂事，怠慢了嫂嫂。”
[EN] Qianli Xun spoke up smoothly. "Can't I just come visit my brother and sister-in-law? Shuzhen was immature before and neglected you."
[ZH] 为迅速切入正题，千里寻接着故意装道：“苦荞，伤口有些疼，再帮我上些药。”
[EN] To steer straight to the point, she put on an act: "Kuqiao, my cut is stinging—dab on some more ointment."
[ZH] 苦荞从怀里掏出小木盒，打开，当着众人的面用丝帕沾些，给小姐涂到伤口处。
[EN] Kuqiao pulled out the wooden box, opened it, and dabbed ointment onto her mistress's forehead in plain view.
[ZH] 陈文心自然注意到伤口：“妹妹是碰到哪里？伤得严重不严重？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin noticed at once. "Where did you bang your head, Sister? Is it bad?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有回答，她知道哥哥定会帮她说明。
[EN] Qianli Xun kept silent, letting her brother carry the ball.
[ZH] 果不其然，朱景元说：“我还没来得及告诉你，淑真自从嫁给林觉，经常被家暴，那伤正是林觉打的，也因为那伤，她失去以前的记忆，整个人变得很不一样，娘说是重生。”
[EN] Right on cue, Zhu Jingyuan spoke up. "Haven't had a chance to tell you—Lin Jue has been beating Shuzhen ever since she married him. He gave her that cut, and the blow wiped her memory and changed her whole personality. Mother says she was reborn."
[ZH] “什么？重生？”陈文心感到诧异，很快镇定下来，“我倒觉得重生失忆后的妹妹比以前更接地气儿，看着让人舒服。重生就重生呗，失忆也没什么大不了，重要的是人还活着。不过，林觉竟然打朱府的小姐，实在过分，应该让公公好好教训那小子。”
[EN] "What? Reborn?" Surprise flickered across Chen Wenxin's face before she recovered fast. "If you ask me, reborn and memory-less Shuzhen is way more down-to-earth and pleasant to be around! Reborn is reborn, amnesia is no big deal—what matters is she's alive. Still, Lin Jue laying a hand on a daughter of Zhu Manor? Outrageous! Father-in-law ought to teach that punk a real lesson!"
[ZH] 千里寻听后，喜上心头，看来哥哥嫂嫂都站在她这边，趁机说：“嫂嫂，你我都是女子，知道女子嫁个好男人有多重要。我没嫂嫂幸运，能嫁给像哥哥这样的好男人，却嫁给林觉那样打女人的渣男，我与他实在过不下去，打算和离，和离之时，还请哥哥嫂嫂前去为我撑腰。”
[EN] Delighted that both brother and sister-in-law backed her, Qianli Xun seized the moment. "Sister-in-law, woman to woman, you know how much marrying a good man matters. I didn't have your luck landing a gem like my brother—I got stuck with a wife-beating scumbag like Lin Jue. I can't bear another day with him, so I'm pushing for mutual divorce. When the day comes, please stand behind me!"
[ZH] 千里寻说话间，陈文心看向丈夫，他虽没有多少本事，却胜在对她好，听她话，把她看得很重，比起很多男子不知强多少倍。她的脸上不由露出幸福的表情。
[EN] As she spoke, Chen Wenxin glanced at her husband. No towering genius, maybe, yet he cherished her, listened to her, and put her first—better than ten times the men out there. A warm glow lit her face.
[ZH] “像林觉这种男人，的确不能再跟他过下去，和离倒是不错的选择，离开他妹妹依然能找到好婆家。”
[EN] "You really can't stay with a man like Lin Jue. Mutual divorce is the smart move—leave him, and you can still find a fine husband."
[ZH] 千里寻继续说：“嫂嫂和哥哥是否同意在我与林觉和离时帮我出面？”
[EN] Qianli Xun pressed on, "So you and Brother agree to appear on my behalf at the divorce meeting?"
[ZH] 陈文心回道：“那还用说？我同意。”
[EN] "Need you even ask? I agree," Chen Wenxin answered.
[ZH] 朱景元跟着说：“你嫂嫂同意，哥哥肯定同意。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan chimed right in: "If your sister-in-law agrees, I agree!"
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，上前抱住陈文心：“嫂嫂对我真好，有你这样的嫂嫂真是幸运。”
[EN] Jumping up, Qianli Xun threw her arms around Chen Wenxin. "You're the best, Sister-in-law! I'm so lucky to have you!"
[ZH] 陈文心说：“我还得感谢林觉。”
[EN] "Actually, I ought to thank Lin Jue," Chen Wenxin remarked.
[ZH] 千里寻放开手：“什么意思？”
[EN] Qianli Xun let go. "What do you mean?"
