[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“他家暴妹妹是可恶，可要没有他打妹妹，妹妹也不会变成我喜欢的样子。以前的你清高冷漠，话不多，让人看着很有距离感，反而现在像个正常人。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin laughed. "Beating you is despicable, sure, yet without his beating, you never would've turned into someone I actually like! Before, you were frosty and aloof, barely spoke two words, kept everyone miles away—now you act like a real person!"
[ZH] 千里寻怨道：“嫂嫂这话说的，好像林觉打我还有功劳？”
[EN] Qianli Xun pouted. "Sister-in-law, you make it sound like Lin Jue deserves credit for hitting me!"
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“好了，我不开玩笑，林觉就是你说的那种……”
[EN] "Alright, alright, just teasing! Lin Jue really is that word you used..."
[ZH] “渣男。”
[EN] "Scumbag."
[ZH] “对！渣男！”
[EN] "Right! A scumbag!"
[ZH] 两人正说着，门外传来银杏的声音：“公子，少夫人，老爷回来了，听说小姐在这里，让我过来请她过去，晚饭与老爷、夫人一起用。”
[EN] Mid-laugh, Yinxing's voice floated from outside the door: "Young Master, Young Madam—Master is home! Hearing Miss was here, he sent me to summon her to dine with Master and Madam."
[ZH] 朱景元说：“爹回来了，让你过去，你把林觉打你之事再好好给爹说说，哥相信，爹肯定会替你做主。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan encouraged her, "Father's back and calling for you. Tell him everything about Lin Jue beating you—I'm sure Father will back you up."
[ZH] 朱景元的话令千里寻感到踏实：“哥哥嫂嫂难道不一起过去用饭？”
[EN] Reassured by his words, Qianli Xun asked, "Aren't you and Sister-in-law coming to dinner too?"
[ZH] 朱景元看着失忆的妹妹，很是心疼，解释道：“爹只让你过去，没有叫我跟文心，我们不能随意过去打扰。”
[EN] Pitying his memory-less sister, Zhu Jingyuan explained, "Father only summoned you, not Wenxin and me. We can't intrude uninvited."
[ZH] 千里寻只能拜别哥嫂。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no choice but to take her leave.
[ZH] 走出屋外时，天已蒙蒙黑。一日即将过去，还有两日，只要再搞定朱淑真的父亲，一切万事大吉。千里寻带着希望，在银杏的带领下，借着她手中灯笼散发出的微弱光芒，在朱府继续穿梭。
[EN] Dusk had fallen by the time she stepped outside. One day gone, two left. Win over Zhu Shuzhen's father, and she was home free. Buoyed by hope, she followed Yinxing's dim lantern as they wound once more through the paths of Zhu Manor.

#CH 13
[ZH] 到达朱府正院，天变得更黑。
[ZH] 正院里每隔一段距离挂有一个灯笼，黑夜根本无法遮住灯笼里散发出的亮光。
[ZH] 银杏将手中提着的灯笼抬起，吹灭里面的火光，带千里寻和苦荞穿过一个长廊，来到正屋的庭院。院子很大，铺着用石头打制的地砖。因灯光的原因能够清晰地看到两边分别栽种着三棵玉兰树，花开得正艳，有白色，紫色和黄色三种。
[ZH] 千里寻被眼前的花树吸引，驻足欣赏一会儿。
[ZH] 银杏催道：“小姐若喜欢，明儿天亮时再来瞧，太阳底下，花更美。现在还是尽快去跟老爷太太用饭，晚了老爷会怪罪。”
[ZH] 千里寻只好启步，她边走边寻思，一会儿见到朱淑真的父亲，是该学朱淑真一样对待他，还是按照千里寻的模式？最终决定，先按照朱淑真的模式观察一会儿，再看情况。她在心里叮嘱自己，朱淑真的模式是先行礼，然后保持沉默。
[ZH] 正想着，已来到朱府正院用饭之地，好几个丫鬟端着盘子往屋里走。
[ZH] 银杏站住，向屋内禀报：“老爷，夫人，小姐已到。”
[ZH] 传出一个男人的声音，冷静中带着冷漠：“让她进来。”
[ZH] 银杏说：“小姐，老爷允许您进去。”
[ZH] 千里寻走出两步，见苦荞未跟上，回头问：“怎么不跟着？”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“奴婢没资格进，我在外面等小姐。”
[ZH] 银杏说：“小姐放心，我会带苦荞到丫鬟房用饭。”
[ZH] 千里寻叹道：“好吧，看来我只能单枪匹马会会我爹。”
[ZH] 走进正屋，迎面被一个大大的屏风挡住，里面什么情况，根本无法看到。她停住脚步，在心里提醒自己：“先稳住，要有礼貌，保持沉默。”
[ZH] 一个丫鬟过来，小声提醒：“小姐，这边。”
[ZH] 千里寻跟着丫鬟拐过屏风，屋内的情景才尽收眼底。
[ZH] 迎面是坐榻，上面放着方形的木桌，一边坐着向夫人，看到女儿进来，她并不说话，仅以微笑示意。另一边坐着的应该是朱淑真的爹，他手中拿着一本书，全神贯注，根本没有抬眼看女儿。
[ZH] 千里寻仔细瞧这位爹，他大约四十多岁，留着胡须，浓眉大眼，脸型甚好，皮肤也不错，年轻时应该是个美男子。
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“原来朱淑真长得像她爹。”
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“爹，娘。”
[ZH] 向夫人点点头，依然不说话。
[ZH] 朱晞颜未抬眼，吩咐道：“坐下吧。”
[ZH] 千里寻找位置坐下，在心里不断叮嘱自己：“保持沉默，保持沉默。”
[ZH] 坚持了一会儿，甚觉无聊，看向进进出出摆饭的丫鬟，心想：“三人吃饭，摆这么多菜，太奢侈了吧！”
[ZH] 向夫人还未找到机会把女儿的情况告知老爷，只因她在老爷面前不敢随意说话。女儿走进来时，她用心观察，淑真走路温婉可人，安安静静坐在那里，跟从前并无两样。向夫人的脸上不由露出笑容。
[ZH] 紧接着听到女儿说：“爹，娘，饭已摆好，赶紧过去吃吧，吃得太晚，不容易消化，还容易发胖……”
[EN] By the time they reached Zhu Manor's Main Courtyard, night had fallen thick. Lanterns hung at even intervals all around the yard, their bright pools of glow pushing back the dark. Yinxing raised her handheld lantern, blew out the flame, and led Qianli Xun and Kuqiao down a long corridor into the main hall's paved stone courtyard. By the lantern light, three magnolia trees stood clear on each side in full bloom, white, purple, and yellow.
[ZH] 向夫人流下一滴泪，眼前的女儿已经不是从前的淑真。她扭过头，悄悄用丝帕擦掉泪水。
[EN] Drawn by the blossoms, Qianli Xun paused to take them in. Yinxing nudged her along: "If you like them, Miss, come look in daylight tomorrow; they shine even brighter under the sun. Better hurry to dine with Master and Madam before Master grows displeased."
[ZH] 朱晞颜甚觉诧异，抬起眼，瞥向一旁的女儿。
[EN] Resuming her pace, Qianli Xun weighed her plan: act like the old Zhu Shuzhen before her father, or stay Qianli Xun? Better start in Zhu Shuzhen's mode, watch the room first, and adapt. She coached herself in silence: bow first, then stay quiet.
[ZH] 千里寻接触到这位父亲的目光，心里不由打了个寒颤，如此严厉，不敢再说下去，赶紧低下头，继续保持沉默。
[EN] Soon they reached the main dining hall, where maids bustled indoors bearing platters. Halting at the threshold, Yinxing announced, "Master, Madam, Miss has arrived."
[ZH] 丫鬟过来，行礼道：“老爷，夫人，小姐，饭已摆好。”
[EN] A man's voice answered from inside, cool and remote: "Let her in."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“终于好了！”第一个起身，步子还没迈出，看到向夫人摇头，用手示意她坐下。千里寻只能继续坐下，偷偷瞧朱晞颜。只见他放下书卷，从榻上下来，经过她面前。
[EN] "Master says you may enter, Miss," Yinxing murmured.
[ZH] 向夫人也从榻上下来，走到女儿身边，拉起她，跟在朱晞颜身后，来到饭桌旁，把女儿安排在合适的位置。
[EN] Two steps in, Qianli Xun saw Kuqiao staying back and glanced over her shoulder. "Aren't you coming?"
[ZH] 千里寻坐下，长这么大第一次吃饭遇到如此不自在的氛围，她很不习惯，决定不再沉默。
[EN] "I'm not permitted inside, Miss. I'll wait right out here."
[ZH] 听到朱晞颜说了句：“可以开动。”终于忍不住，夹一块鸭肉给向夫人放进碗里：“娘，吃这个。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Miss," Yinxing added. "I'll take Kuqiao to eat in the maids' quarters."
[ZH] 向夫人又向她摇摇头。
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed. "Fine. Looks like I'm walking in solo to meet my dad."
[ZH] 千里寻不想再理会，继续夹一块鸭肉，递到朱晞颜跟前，刚叫了声“爹”，见爹一脸阴沉，看上去很不高兴，便抽回手：“爹不吃，我吃。”很快塞进自己嘴里，大嚼起来。
[EN] Stepping past the doorway, she met a massive standing screen blocking the interior. Pausing a beat, she reminded herself: Keep calm, mind manners, hold your tongue. A maid stepped out and murmured, "This way, Miss." Rounding the screen behind her, Qianli Xun took in the hall at a glance.
[ZH] 旁边的丫鬟过来，在耳边叮嘱道：“小姐，食不言，还要注意姿态。”
[EN] Straight ahead stood a wide couch with a low square table set across it. On one side sat Madam Xiang, greeting her daughter with a quiet smile and no words; across from her sat Zhu Shuzhen's father, absorbed in a volume without lifting his eyes once. Qianli Xun studied him: forties, neatly kept beard, bold brows, clear eyes, clean jawline, good complexion. He must have been quite handsome in his youth. So Zhu Shuzhen got her looks from her father, she mused.
[ZH] 千里寻很不满，心想：“在自己家里吃个饭，注意这注意那，一点儿都不自在，算了，谁让我有事求这位爹。看他那个样子，一个大男人吃个饭细嚼慢咽，连个吧唧声都没有，实在不像男人。”
[EN] Stepping up, she bowed. "Father. Mother."
[ZH] 再观察向夫人，她夹菜的动作十分优雅，吃菜时微启朱唇，也是细嚼慢咽。千里寻只能学着照做。学了一遍，自己都觉得好笑，与她的风格相去甚远。她从小练习千里拳，跟练武之人混在一起，吃饭从来是豪放派，何时这么婉约过？她决定不能让自己难受，还是恢复正常，于是完全放开，大嚼大咽起来。
[EN] Madam Xiang nodded without speaking. Without raising his gaze from the book, Zhu Xiyan ordered, "Sit."
[ZH] 向夫人看到，停下筷子，一脸惊讶。这哪像自己的女儿淑真！那个吃相，如同山野村妇。她又流下眼泪，拿丝帕去擦。旁边伺候的丫鬟互相交换眼色，脸上露出讶异的笑容。
[EN] Qianli Xun took a seat, chanting inside her head: Stay quiet, stay quiet. Minutes dragged in stiff silence while maids shuttled in and out laying dishes. All this spread for three people? Talk about wasteful!
[ZH] 只听“啪”的一声，原来是朱晞颜将筷子摔在桌上，周围的人都被吓坏。
[EN] Madam Xiang had not yet found an opening to brief her husband, never daring to speak out of turn before him. Watching Shuzhen walk in with poised grace and sit still without a peep, a relieved smile lit her face.
[ZH] 千里寻刚要去拽鸡腿，被这一声吓得抽回手，摸着额头，开始装疼：“哎呦！我额头上的伤口好疼呀！”
[EN] Then her daughter opened her mouth: "Father, Mother, food's all set! Let's go eat. Eating too late messes up your digestion and puts on weight..."
[ZH] 向夫人担心道：“淑真，你没事吧？来人，快拿创伤药来，给小姐涂上。”
[EN] A single tear slid down Madam Xiang's cheek: this was no longer her old Shuzhen. She turned her face aside to blot it with her handkerchief. Startled, Zhu Xiyan raised his eyes and shot a sharp look at his daughter. A chill ran down Qianli Xun's spine under that stern glare; biting off the rest, she ducked her head and went mute again.
[ZH] 丫鬟迅速拿来创伤药，给小姐的额头涂了些。
[EN] A maid stepped forward with a bow. "Master, Madam, Miss, dinner is served."
[ZH] 向夫人又急切地问：“还疼吗？”
[EN] At last! Qianli Xun started up first, only to catch Madam Xiang shaking her head and waving her back down. Sitting again, she sneaked a look as Zhu Xiyan laid down his book, stepped off the couch, and walked past her. Madam Xiang rose too, took her daughter's hand, trailed behind Zhu Xiyan to the dining table, and guided Shuzhen into her proper seat.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“好多了。”她希望这么一装，能引起父亲关怀，或是尽快引出她需要的话题。
[EN] Never in her life had Qianli Xun endured such a suffocating meal. Fed up with the chill, she ditched silence. The instant Zhu Xiyan said, "You may begin," she picked up a slice of duck and dropped it into Madam Xiang's bowl. "Mother, try this."
[ZH] 还别说，这招真灵，朱晞颜终于说话，表情依然严肃：“听说你想跟林觉和离，可有此事？”
[EN] Madam Xiang shook her head again. Ignoring the hint, Qianli Xun lifted a second piece of duck toward Zhu Xiyan's bowl, starting "Father..." before pulling back at his thunderous scowl. "Don't want it? I'll eat it!" She popped it straight into her own mouth and chewed away.
[ZH] 既然话匣子打开，千里寻岂能放过这么好的机会？
[EN] A maid leaned down and whispered in her ear, "Miss, no talking at the table, and watch your posture."
[ZH] “爹，您也看到，我额头的伤就是林觉打的，您肯定也发现，我跟您以前的女儿很不一样，就是因为林觉把您的女儿打得晕死过去，醒来后，就成我这样，而且以前的事情都不记得。他还把商妓往家里带，您说过分不过分？让这样的人继续当您的女婿，岂不是把女儿往火坑里推？女儿求爹出面促成和离之事。”
[EN] Irritation simmered in Qianli Xun: Can't even eat in peace in my own home, rules for this and rules for that! Fine, I need a favor from him. Look at him though, a grown man nibbling without a sound, zero manly appetite! Glancing at Madam Xiang, she saw dainty chopsticks, tiny morsels, quiet chewing. Qianli Xun tried copying her, then almost laughed at herself; this wasn't her at all. Raised practicing Qianli Fist among martial artists, she had always eaten bold and hearty. Refusing to torture herself, she dropped the act and dug in with big bites.
[ZH] “难道你心里还装着徐家公子？今日也不怕告诉你，徐家搬到北边，徐家公子早已娶妻，现在已儿女双全。”朱晞颜面无表情，连女儿看都不看一眼。
[EN] Madam Xiang froze mid-bite in shock. This was not her refined Shuzhen; she ate like a country bumpkin! Tears welled again as she reached for her handkerchief, while serving maids traded stunned smirks.
[ZH] 千里寻想起，苦荞提到过与朱淑真青梅竹马的徐家公子，徐家公子对她来讲不过是个陌生人而已。
[EN] Smack! Zhu Xiyan slammed his chopsticks down on the table, making everyone jump.
[ZH] “什么徐家公子？我被林觉打得失忆，什么都不记得。”
[EN] Mid-reach for a drumstick, Qianli Xun jerked her hand back, clapped a palm to her forehead, and put on a show. "Ow! My forehead wound hurts so bad!"
[ZH] “不记得最好，那就老老实实过日子，不要想什么和理之事。”
[EN] Alarmed, Madam Xiang cried, "Shuzhen, are you alright? Somebody fetch wound salve at once!" A maid rushed over and dabbed ointment onto her forehead. "Does it still hurt?" Madam Xiang asked in anxiety.
[ZH] 朱晞颜的话令千里寻感到意外，女儿被打成这样，亲爹竟然一点儿都不心疼，这算什么事！
[EN] "Much better," Qianli Xun said, hoping the stunt might stir a father's pity or at least crack open the subject she needed.
[ZH] “爹，他打我，还找商妓，您觉得我还能跟他过下去吗？”
[EN] To her surprise, it worked. Zhu Xiyan spoke at last, face still carved in stone. "I heard you want a mutual divorce from Lin Jue. Is that true?"
[ZH] “哼！你以为我什么都不清楚？今日林觉来府衙找过我，将事情的缘由说清楚，也给我跪下承认错误。他把你打伤，你也把他打得不轻，他带商妓到家里，难道你就没错？要早些与他圆房，也没后面的事。”
[EN] With the door cracked open, Qianli Xun pushed straight through. "Father, look at the cut on my forehead; Lin Jue gave me that! And you can tell I'm nothing like your old daughter because he beat her unconscious, and when she woke up she became me, with zero memory of the past! On top of that, he brings brothel courtesans right into the house! Isn't that outrageous? Keeping a man like that as your son-in-law shoves your daughter into a fiery pit! Father, please step forward and arrange a mutual divorce!"
[ZH] 林觉竟然恶人先告状，千里寻气不打一处来，她站起来：“爹，您不能听他一面之词！”
[EN] "Are you still clinging to Young Master Xu?" Without even glancing at her, Zhu Xiyan spoke flat and cold. "I may as well tell you today: the Xu family moved north, and Young Master Xu married long ago. He already has a son and a daughter."
[ZH] “难道我要听你一面之词不成？”
[EN] Recalling Kuqiao's story about that childhood sweetheart, a total stranger to Qianli Xun, she brushed it off. "Young Master who? Lin Jue beat the memory out of me; I don't remember anyone!"
[ZH] 千里寻更来气，认为这位父亲的关注点实在奇葩，继续强调道：“爹，林觉已将您以前的女儿打死，我是借助您女儿身体重生后的朱淑真，不是您真正的女儿，她的记忆我一点儿都没有，难道您没有发觉吗？仅凭这一点，您也该治林觉一个杀人之罪，把他关起来。”
[EN] "Better that you don't. Then settle down and live your married life, and forget this nonsense about mutual divorce."
[ZH] 朱晞颜抬眼仔细看这个女儿，确实与以往不同，可样貌还是，他又垂下眼帘：“重生之说，只不过是乡野之人胡乱编造，哄骗人而已，岂可当真？你不是淑真，又能是谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun gaped. His own daughter came home battered, and he didn't even blink? What kind of father was this? "Father, he beats me and fools around with courtesans! How can I keep living with him?"
[ZH] 千里寻举起握紧的拳头：“我会拳法，以前的朱淑真可会？我打了林觉，以前的朱淑真只有被林觉打的份儿，您难道还认为您以前的女儿没被林觉打死？”
[EN] "Hmph! Think I'm in the dark? Lin Jue came to the prefecture office today, laid out the whole affair, and knelt to admit his fault. He injured you, yet you beat him just as hard! As for bringing a courtesan home, are you blameless? Had you consummated the marriage when you should have, none of this would have happened."
[ZH] 向夫人听了在一旁哭出声来：“呜呜……，淑真！你死得好惨啊！”
[EN] So Lin Jue had beaten her to the punch with his own twisted story! Fury boiled over as Qianli Xun shot to her feet. "Father, you can't listen only to his side of the story!"
[ZH] 丫鬟们听了这番对话，甚是诧异，因主人在此，都不敢交头接耳。
[EN] "And I'm supposed to listen only to yours?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜用平静而有力的声音说：“哭什么？事情没搞清楚，跟着瞎起哄！”
[EN] Stung by his warped logic, Qianli Xun pressed harder. "Father, Lin Jue beat your original daughter to death! I'm a reborn Zhu Shuzhen borrowing her body, not your real daughter! I don't hold a single shred of her memories; haven't you noticed? On that alone, you should charge Lin Jue with murder and lock him up!"
[ZH] 向夫人立刻停止哭声，继续抹眼泪。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan raised his eyes to study her: different in manner, yes, yet identical in face and frame. Dropping his gaze again, he scoffed, "Tales of rebirth are rustic superstition made up to fool simpletons. How could anyone take that as truth? If you aren't Shuzhen, who else could you be?"
[ZH] 接着朱晞颜说出了千里寻怎么都想不到的话：“既然你不是我女儿淑真，我更没有必要为你跟林觉和离而出面。你走吧，有你在，连顿饭都吃不好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun raised a clenched fist. "I know martial arts; did the old Zhu Shuzhen? I beat Lin Jue flat; the old Zhu Shuzhen could only take his fists! Do you still refuse to believe he killed her?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞪大眼睛，没想到朱淑真的父亲如此无情。
[EN] Beside them, Madam Xiang burst into loud sobs. "Sob... Shuzhen! What a tragic death!" Stunned by the clash, the maids kept their heads down, not daring to whisper.
[ZH] “如果我是您的女儿呢？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan spoke with calm, iron weight: "What are you crying for? Joining the uproar without knowing the facts!" Madam Xiang choked back her wails at once and dabbed her tears in silence.
[ZH] “那就更不应该和离，好好听从父母安排，安心与林觉过日子。当初把你嫁给他，就是想让你明白生活没有那么多花前月下。”
[EN] Then Zhu Xiyan delivered the last words Qianli Xun ever expected: "Since you claim you are not my daughter Shuzhen, I have even less reason to step forward for your divorce. Leave; your presence spoils my meal."
[ZH] 千里寻被着实气到，她很想用拳头逼朱晞颜替自己出面主持和离之事，可谁让他在别人眼里是她的长辈。气已将力量集聚到拳头上，必须找个途径发泄出去。
[EN] Eyes wide, Qianli Xun could not fathom how heartless Zhu Shuzhen's father was. "And if I am your daughter?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜若无其事继续享用美食，千里寻气不打一处来，忍不住一拳砸在饭桌上，桌子顺间变成两半，桌上的碗碟和饭菜纷纷掉在地上。向夫人和朱晞颜朝一旁躲去，丫鬟们也被重生的小姐吓坏。
[EN] "Then even more reason not to divorce! Obey your parents' arrangement and live peaceably with Lin Jue. I married you to him so you would learn life isn't all flowers and moonlight."
[ZH] 发泄完，千里寻收起拳头，对朱晞颜说：“不管怎样，我还叫你一声爹，你既不愿替我出面，我自己想办法，就不信，还离不了这个婚！”说完，千里寻大踏步走了出去。
[EN] Rage flooded straight into Qianli Xun's fists. Part of her longed to make him agree at fistpoint, yet in everyone's eyes he stood as her elder. All that banked fury demanded an outlet. Watching Zhu Xiyan pick up his chopsticks and keep eating without a flicker of feeling, Qianli Xun snapped and drove her fist straight down onto the dining table. The solid wood cracked clean in two, sending bowls, platters, and food crashing to the floor. Zhu Xiyan and Madam Xiang scrambled back while the maids gasped in terror at the reborn young mistress.
[ZH] 屋里留下抹眼泪的向夫人和面不改色的朱晞颜，此时的他在心里不得不承认刚才的女儿已不是以前的淑真。
[EN] Pulling back her fist, Qianli Xun glared at Zhu Xiyan. "Like it or not, I still call you Father. If you won't stand up for me, I'll find my own way. I refuse to believe I can't get out of this marriage!" Turning on her heel, she strode out of the hall, leaving behind a weeping Madam Xiang and an outwardly composed Zhu Xiyan, who deep inside could no longer deny that this daughter was no longer the Shuzhen he had known.

#CH 14
[ZH] 千里寻走出后，苦荞已在外面等候，她手里提着灯笼。见小姐出来，迎上去：“我寻思着还需等一会儿，没想到小姐这么快出来。”
[EN] Outside waited Kuqiao, lantern in hand. Seeing her mistress emerge, she hurried over. "I figured it'd be a while longer—didn't expect you out so soon, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻没有搭话，径直往外走。走出正院后，不明去向，只好站住。
[EN] Silent, Qianli Xun marched straight out of the Main Courtyard—only to stop dead the moment she cleared the gate, clueless which way led back.
[ZH] 苦荞看出小姐不高兴，她不敢多问，只顾跟着。见小姐停下，知道她找不到回去的路，赶紧走到前面：“小姐只管跟我走。”
[EN] Sensing her dark mood, Kuqiao held back questions and trailed quiet behind. Seeing her halt, she stepped ahead. "Just follow me, Miss."
[ZH] 又是一阵穿梭。
[EN] Another maze of winding paths.
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情关注朱府夜间幽密的小径，一心盘算着接下来该怎么办？父亲不愿出面，说明朱府没有人能替她出面，和离之事岂不是要以失败告终？难道自己穿越成朱淑真必须要跟林觉过日子不成？
[EN] Paying no mind to Zhu Manor's shadowed walkways, Qianli Xun racked her brain for a plan. With her father refusing to show, nobody in Zhu Manor would back her—did that mean the mutual divorce was dead on arrival? Was she really doomed to spend her transmigrated life stuck with Lin Jue?
[ZH] 朱淑真留给后世的信息太少，依据仅存的信息，朱淑真与林觉并未离婚，而是常年分居；后来遇到相爱之人，婚内出轨，因战乱又与相爱之人分开；最后郁郁而终，离开人世时不过四十来岁。
[EN] Sparse as historical records on Zhu Shuzhen were, Qianli Xun knew the outline: Zhu Shuzhen never divorced Lin Jue, lived estranged from him for years, later fell in love with another man outside marriage, lost him to war, and died brokenhearted in her forties.
[ZH] 难道她千里寻也要如此度过此生？坚决不！她一定要改变！要从结束这段婚姻开始。
[EN] Live out that script? Never! She was rewriting this destiny—starting by ending this marriage!
[ZH] 想着想着，已到朱淑真居住的园子。朱府的路真是奇怪，用心关注时，只觉怎么都走不到目的地，不去关注，反而快了许多。
[EN] Before she knew it, they stood back at Zhu Shuzhen's garden. Strange place, Zhu Manor—watch every turn and it felt endless; tune out and you arrived in half the time.
[ZH] 苦荞推开园门，提着灯笼在前面照亮。院子里没有挂一个灯笼，黑漆漆的。想到朱晞颜居住的正院灯火通明，千里寻说：“院子太黑，应该多挂些灯笼。”
[EN] Kuqiao pushed the garden gate open, lighting the dark yard with her lantern. Not a single lantern hung outside—pitch black compared to Zhu Xiyan's blazing Main Courtyard. "Too dark in here," Qianli Xun said. "We need more lanterns."
[ZH] 绿桃在屋内听到声音，出来迎接：“小姐回来了，奴婢已把床铺好，您随时可以休息。”
[EN] Hearing them, Lutao stepped out to greet her. "Welcome back, Miss. Your bed is all made whenever you're ready to rest."
[ZH] 千里寻问：“绿桃，院子一直是你管理？”
[EN] "Lutao, you're in charge of this courtyard?"
[ZH] “是的，小姐。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss."
[ZH] “明儿找几个丫鬟照着我爹的正院给院子多挂些灯笼，到了晚上点着，也好亮堂些。”
[EN] "Tomorrow grab some maids and hang lanterns all around this yard just like Father's Main Courtyard—light them up at night so it isn't so gloomy."
[ZH] 绿桃感到纳闷：“以前院子里挂过灯笼，小姐不喜欢，才让我吩咐下人把灯笼都撤了，您……确定要把灯笼再挂上去？”
[EN] Lutao blinked in confusion. "We used to hang lanterns here, Miss, until you said you disliked them and ordered them all taken down. Are you... sure you want them back up?"
[ZH] “确定！而且十分肯定！”千里寻说着，走进屋子。
[EN] "Sure—one hundred percent sure!" Qianli Xun stepped inside.
[ZH] 苦荞把灯笼递给绿桃：“姐姐按照小姐的吩咐去安排吧。”
[EN] Kuqiao passed the lantern to Lutao. "Just do as Miss says, Sister."
[ZH] 绿桃接过灯笼，边往出走边小声嘀咕：“听丫鬟们议论，小姐跟以前很不一样，还真是。不管了，主子说怎样就怎样，改天吩咐人把灯笼挂上就是。”
[EN] Taking the lantern, Lutao walked off muttering under her breath, "Heard the other maids whispering Miss isn't the same—guess they weren't kidding. Oh well, mistress's orders are orders. I'll have them hung soon."
[ZH] 千里寻在苦荞的伺候下，躺到床上，继续盘算着。就目前情形来看，朱家人十有八九靠不住，只怕哥哥嫂嫂也得听爹的。这一天充满希望而来，瞬间所有希望被那个面无表情的爹给击得粉碎。
[EN] With Kuqiao's help, Qianli Xun climbed into bed, still turning options over. The Zhu household looked like a bust—even her brother and sister-in-law probably dared not defy her father. A whole day of high hopes shattered flat by one stone-faced dad.
[ZH] 尽管心里搁着事儿，可千里寻从小是那种头一挨枕头呼呼大睡的人，因此一晚上睡眠质量挺高。
[EN] Still, worries or not, Qianli Xun had always been the type to knock out the second her head hit the pillow, and she slept solid through the night.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 当清晨第一声鸡叫响起时，她睁开眼睛，这是她平生第一次被鸡叫醒，甚是新奇。
[EN] At the first rooster crow of dawn, her eyes blinked open—first time in her life an actual rooster had been her alarm clock. Kind of novel.
[ZH] 千里寻起身掀开床幔，溜下床，简单披上外衣，打开窗户，一阵清新的空气扑面而来，她深深吸口气：“真好闻，有植物的香味儿。”
[EN] Pulling back the bed curtains, she slid out of bed, threw on an outer robe, and pushed open the window. Fresh morning air rushed in; she drew a deep breath. "Smells so clean—like fresh greens!"
[ZH] 她看到一只公鸡站在院墙上，红冠鲜艳夺目，脖子粗壮有力，全身的羽毛黑中略带红色，它伸长脖子，又发出一串清脆的啼叫：“喔喔喔……”
[EN] Perched atop the courtyard wall stood a rooster with a bright red comb, sturdy neck, and glossy black feathers glinting red. Stretching its neck, it let out another crisp crow: "Cock-a-doodle-doo!"
[ZH] 苦荞也被吵醒，走出丫鬟房里，从地上捡起一个石子，朝公鸡打去：“让你在这里瞎吵！”公鸡被吓坏，扑棱棱地飞到院墙之外。
[EN] Woken too, Kuqiao stepped out of the maids' room, snatched a pebble from the ground, and tossed it at the bird. "Quit making a racket here!" Startled, the rooster flapped over the wall and vanished.
[ZH] “小姐，我把它赶走了，您再睡会儿。”
[EN] "Chased it off, Miss. Go back to sleep for a bit."
[ZH] “我睡不着，不如起来。”
[EN] "Wide awake already. Might as well get up."
[ZH] 苦荞进到屋里，开始为小姐穿衣打扮。
[EN] Kuqiao came inside to help her dress and fix her hair.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“朱府怎么会有公鸡？”
[EN] "Why does Zhu Manor have a rooster?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] “有可能是哪个下人养的，也可能是佛堂养的。”
[EN] "Could belong to one of the servants—or maybe the Buddhist Shrine."
[ZH] “佛堂？”千里寻不解。
[EN] "Buddhist Shrine?"
[ZH] 苦荞给小姐边整理衣裳边说：“朱府有个佛堂，听说里面住着一位老祖宗，都一百多岁，可我跟小姐从来没见过，主要是那位老祖宗待在佛堂里，根本不出来。”
[EN] Straightening her mistress's collar, Kuqiao explained, "Zhu Manor has a Buddhist Shrine where an Old Ancestor lives—over a hundred years old! Neither you nor I have ever seen her, though, because she never steps out of that shrine."
[ZH] “我爹也没见过？”
[EN] "Not even my father?"
[ZH] “不知道，反正我从没见老爷去过佛堂。”
[EN] "No idea—never once saw Master go near the shrine."
[ZH] “朱府真是一个深不可测的府邸。不过，你凭什么判断公鸡可能是佛堂养的？”
[EN] "This manor is full of mysteries. Wait—why guess the rooster belongs to the Buddhist Shrine?"
[ZH] “在朱府大家形成一个习惯，对于少见的东西，总会说一句可能是佛堂的，我习惯了，顺口说的。”
[EN] "Habit around here! Whenever something odd turns up in Zhu Manor, folks shrug and say it's probably from the Buddhist Shrine. Slipped right out."
[ZH] “原来如此。”
[EN] "Got it."
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情纠结佛堂之事，在苦荞为她整理妆容后，吩咐道：“苦荞，一会儿不必为我做茶，随便弄点儿喝的，我要早些去见哥哥和嫂嫂，问问他们还能不能为我出面。”
[EN] Too busy to dwell on shrines, Qianli Xun waited until Kuqiao finished her hair and ordered, "Skip the fancy whisked tea this morning—grab me something simple to drink. I want to go see my brother and sister-in-law early and check if they'll still back me."
[ZH] 苦荞担心道：“难道老爷昨晚拒绝在您和姑爷和离时出面？”
[EN] Worry flickered across Kuqiao's face. "Did Master refuse to appear at your divorce meeting last night?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded.
[ZH] “小姐还是不要去问，大公子和少奶奶肯定不会出面。”
[EN] "Don't bother asking, Miss—Eldest Young Master and Young Madam won't dare step forward."
[ZH] “跟我爹有关？”尽管千里寻知道是这个原因，还是不由问道。
[EN] "Because of Father?" She already knew the answer, yet asked anyway.
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“朱府历来如此，只要老爷不答应，其他人都不敢违拗，只有干着急的份儿。”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded. "Always been that way in Zhu Manor. Once Master says no, nobody dares defy him—all they can do is fret."
[ZH] “为什么？”千里寻很想知道原因。
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “朱家家法甚严，可以说老爷就是家法。”
[EN] "Zhu family discipline is ruthless—Master's word is the law."
[ZH] “哼！什么家法！我看他就是法西斯！”
[EN] "Hmph! Family discipline my foot—he's a straight-up Fascist!"
[ZH] 苦荞听不懂什么是法西斯，不一样的小姐总会说些她听不懂的名词儿。
[EN] Kuqiao had no idea what a "Fascist" was; her changed mistress tossed out strange words all the time.
[ZH] 千里寻又问：“不知道朱家有没有几个像林家那样的族人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought of another angle. "Does the Zhu clan have outside relatives in town like the Lin family?"
[ZH] “有，临安城里住着五六个，都是小姐的叔叔和伯伯。”
[EN] "Yes! Five or six of your uncles live right here in Lin'an."
[ZH] 千里寻立刻高兴起来：“真是柳暗花明又一村，今日我前去拜见这些叔叔和伯伯，不相信说不动一位。”
[EN] Hope flared bright again. "Just when the road looks blocked, another path opens! Today I'll visit every single uncle—no way I can't talk at least one into coming!"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得空手去显得不诚心，要说动别人替自己出面，需带些贵重的礼物，最好直接塞银子。她摸摸身上，空空如也，刚穿越而来，还没遇到需花钱之事，出林府时，也忘记要些银子带上。
[EN] Going empty-handed felt weak; if she wanted elders on her side, lavish gifts—or cold silver—would grease the wheels. Patting her sleeves, she came up empty; fresh off transmigrating, she hadn't needed cash yet and forgot to grab any before leaving Lin Manor.
[ZH] “苦荞，要不你回林府一趟，问林觉要些银子，他答应给我银子使。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, run back to Lin Manor and get some silver from Lin Jue—he promised me spending money."
[ZH] “小姐要用银子，我可以回林府取，可也没必要问姑爷要。”
[EN] "If you need silver, Miss, I can fetch it from Lin Manor, no need to ask Son-in-law."
[ZH] “什么意思？不问林觉要，问谁要？”
[EN] "Wait—if not from Lin Jue, then who?"
[ZH] “小姐嫁给姑爷时，带过去很多嫁妆，其中不乏田产和铺子，每年收的租子小姐都花不完，姑爷可没小姐有钱。”
[EN] "You brought a huge dowry when you married, Miss—landed estates and shops galore! You can't even spend half the annual rent they bring in. Son-in-law isn't nearly as rich as you!"
[ZH] “什么！”千里寻站起来，“我这么有钱！那我还怕什么！俗话讲嫁汉穿衣吃饭，我穿衣吃饭又不靠他林觉，凭什么还要跟他过日子？这婚必须得离，离的时候还要把我所有嫁妆带走！我真傻，在林府跟他约法三章时，让他给我银子使，他竟然笑着答应，真是个阴险小人！苦荞，用完早饭，你就去林府取银子，多多益善。”
[EN] "What?!" Qianli Xun shot to her feet. "I'm loaded?! Then what am I scared of?! People say women marry for clothes and a roof—I don't need Lin Jue for either! Why on earth would I stay married to him? This divorce is happening, and I'm taking every last bit of my dowry with me! What a fool I was making him promise me spending money—no wonder the sly bastard smiled and agreed! Kuqiao, right after breakfast, go fetch silver from Lin Manor—the more the better!"
[ZH] “好勒。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss!"
[ZH] 二人正说着，绿桃带人来将早饭摆上。苦荞陪小姐吃完早饭，整理好书桌上的笔墨纸砚，正要回林府，听到外面传来银杏的声音：“夫人来看小姐。”
[EN] Right then Lutao led servants in with breakfast. After eating and watching Kuqiao tidy the study desk, just as Kuqiao prepared to leave for Lin Manor, Yinxing's voice rang from outside: "Madam is here to see Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻对苦荞说：“你先别着急回林府，看我娘前来说些什么，再做定夺。”
[EN] "Hold off on Lin Manor," Qianli Xun told Kuqiao. "Let's hear what Mother has to say first."
[ZH] 向夫人走进来，见女儿站在屋里迎接她，上前抓住女儿的手：“淑真，娘一晚上没睡好，你虽跟以前不一样，可还是娘的亲女儿，娘希望你不要怪你爹。”
[EN] Madam Xiang walked in, saw her daughter waiting, and clasped her hands. "Shuzhen, Mother barely slept a wink last night. Even changed as you are, you're still my flesh and blood. Please don't hold a grudge against your father."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“如果和离时，娘和哥哥嫂嫂能为我出面，我就不怪爹。”
[EN] "If you, Brother, and Sister-in-law back me at the divorce meeting, I won't hold a grudge."
[ZH] 向夫人放开女儿的手，显得十分为难：“你爹只要不同意，一家人没人敢替你出面。”
[EN] Letting go of her hands, Madam Xiang sighed in distress. "Once your father says no, nobody in this house dares step forward."
[ZH] “既如此，娘别嫌我怪他。我爹就是个冷血动物，一点儿不关心女儿的人生幸福，只看重他所谓的清誉，真不明白，我与林觉和离，能影响他多少清誉？”
[EN] "Then don't ask me not to resent him! My father is cold-blooded—doesn't care a bit about his daughter's happiness, only his precious reputation! How much reputation can one mutual divorce even cost him?"
[ZH] 向夫人在椅子上坐下：“你坐下，听娘慢慢给你讲。”
[EN] Madam Xiang sank into a chair. "Sit down and hear Mother out."
[ZH] 千里寻乖乖坐下：“娘要给我讲什么？”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat. "What do you want to tell me?"
[ZH] 向夫人先吩咐苦荞：“去，给我们娘俩做两盏茶来。”
[EN] Madam Xiang turned to Kuqiao first. "Go whisk two bowls of tea for my daughter and me."
[ZH] 苦荞答应着，去茶桌前做茶。银杏对做茶很感兴趣，也跟着去茶桌旁凑热闹。
[EN] Kuqiao went to the tea table; fascinated by whisked tea, Yinxing wandered over to watch.
[ZH] 向夫人叹口气：“唉，你从小十分聪慧，又喜读书，而且无师自通，十岁便能写诗作词，你爹说是遗传他的才华，便让你频频在诗会上抛头露面，女词人的名头渐渐传遍整个江南。当年我们家与徐家交好，你与徐家公子青梅竹马，两小无猜，也暗生情愫，大人看在眼里，喜在心里，本想结秦晋之好……”
[EN] Madam Xiang let out a long sigh. "You were bright from the cradle, loved books, and taught yourself to write poetry and ci by age ten. Proud that you inherited his gifts, your father paraded you at banquet after banquet until your name as a female ci poet spread across all of Jiangnan. Back then we were close to the Xu family. You and Young Master Xu grew up childhood sweethearts, fond of each other, and both families hoped to seal a marital alliance..."
[ZH] 千里寻对朱淑真曾经的感情经历不感兴趣，不想听下去：“娘给我讲这些做什么？跟和离又没有什么关系。”
[EN] Uninterested in Zhu Shuzhen's old crush, Qianli Xun cut in, "Why tell me all this, Mother? What does it have to do with my divorce?"
[ZH] “你要是还承认自己是淑真，就听娘讲下去。”
[EN] "If you still call yourself Shuzhen, listen to the end."
[ZH] 千里寻只好耐着性子听下去。
[EN] Qianli Xun bit her tongue and listened.
[ZH] 向夫人继续说：“突然有一天，徐家要搬到北边，理由是南边生意不好做，家族所有生意都要迁往北边，至于你与徐公子的姻缘，徐家没有提一个字。徐家搬走后，我与你爹骗你说徐家败落，不得已举家搬迁。再后来，娘参加临安官眷的聚会，偶然听到有人议论，说徐家之所以搬迁，是因为不想让儿子娶你为妻，据说有风水先生给徐家看过风水，告诉徐家若让儿子娶才女，必然家道中落，什么生意都做不成……”
[EN] "Out of nowhere, the Xu family announced they were moving north—claiming southern trade had soured and relocating every shop, without breathing a word about your betrothal. After they left, your father and I told you they had fallen on hard times so you wouldn't break down. Later, at a gathering of Lin'an officials' wives, I overheard gossip: the Xus moved because they didn't want their son marrying you! Some geomancer told them marrying a talented woman would ruin their family fortune and sink every venture..."
[ZH] “一派胡言！”千里寻忍不住为朱淑真鸣不平。
[EN] "Complete garbage!" Qianli Xun snapped on Zhu Shuzhen's behalf.
[ZH] 苦荞已将茶做好，与银杏一起端着茶盏给夫人和小姐放在桌上。
[EN] Finished with the tea, Kuqiao and Yinxing brought the two bowls over and set them down.
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情品茶，端起茶盏一口气喝完，然后放下茶盏：“娘，您与爹要感到庆幸，多亏没把女儿嫁给徐家。依我看，徐家人见识浅薄，根本不值得结亲家。”
[EN] Too fired up to savor it, Qianli Xun drained her bowl in one gulp and slammed it down. "Mother, you and Father should thank your lucky stars you didn't marry me into that house! Narrow-minded fools like the Xus aren't worth being in-laws with!"
[ZH] 向夫人也端起茶盏，品了一小口：“你爹是临安知府，官职不小，徐家得罪不起，才找了搬迁的理由，其实徐家在临安依然有生意，只是全家搬到北边居住而已，后来徐家公子也娶了妻。可怜我女儿一等就是五六年。”向夫人又开始抹眼泪。
[EN] Madam Xiang took a small sip. "Your father is Prefect of Lin'an—the Xus dared not offend him openly, so they made up an excuse to leave. They still run businesses here, just live up north now, and their son married someone else. My poor girl waited five or six years for nothing!" Madam Xiang dabbed at fresh tears.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“娘与爹为什么不将实情告诉女儿？”
[EN] "Why didn't you and Father tell me the truth?"
[ZH] 向夫人稳定情绪后继续说：“还不是怕你听到受伤？你爹看似对你冷淡，其实也关心你。他后悔让你接触诗词，让你参加诗会，可也阻挡不了，你的名声已传遍江南，身边有志同道合的朋友，其中不乏青年才俊。你爹时常会听到各种风言风语，甚至有污秽之语，他才体会到什么是女子无才便是德。正好林家上门提亲，你爹经过考量，认为林觉与你很不相同，嫁给他才能让你真正明白身为女子应该相夫教子才不负满腹才华。再说，你年纪也不小，不嫁人只怕耽搁了你。”
[EN] Composing herself, Madam Xiang went on, "We didn't want to crush your heart! Your father acts cold, yet he cares in his own way. He regretted pushing you into poetry and banquets, yet by then your fame stretched across Jiangnan and you kept company with scholars and young gentlemen. Ugly rumors—even crude slander—reached his ears, making him believe 'a woman's virtue is having no talent.' Right then the Lin family proposed. Your father weighed it and figured someone different like Lin Jue would bring you down to earth to raise a family instead of chasing dreams. Besides, you weren't getting any younger—he feared waiting longer would ruin your chances."
[ZH] 千里寻忍不住说：“谬论！谬论！简直就是谬论！娘现在也看到，朱淑真嫁给林觉只有痛苦，就是我也想跟他离婚。”
[EN] "Ridiculous! Utter nonsense!" Qianli Xun burst out. "You can see for yourself, Mother—marrying Lin Jue brought Zhu Shuzhen nothing but misery! Even I want out of this marriage!"
[ZH] 千里寻的话又引得向夫人伤心：“你……你不认为自己是淑真？”
[EN] Her phrasing stung Madam Xiang again. "You... still don't consider yourself Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 千里寻立刻意识到自己太过激动，忘记她就是朱淑真：“娘，我当然是您的女儿。”
[EN] Catching her slip at once, Qianli Xun backpedaled. "Of course I'm your daughter, Mother!"
[ZH] 向夫人起身走到女儿面前：“让娘好好看看，对，你就是淑真，我家淑真还活着。”
[EN] Rising, Madam Xiang stepped close and cupped her face. "Let Mother look at you... yes, you are Shuzhen. My Shuzhen is still alive."
[ZH] 千里寻被感动，也站起来：“不管我变成什么样，您就是我娘。”
[EN] Touched, Qianli Xun stood too. "No matter how much I change, you're still my mother."
[ZH] “娘知道。”向夫人抬手捋了捋女儿鬓角的头发，“娘说这么多，想让你明白，你爹也是用心良苦，不要怪他。娘给你出个主意，你爹既然不愿出面，可以找朱家的族人，族中有六位你的叔叔伯伯，都住在临安城，我派人给你哥哥说了，让他今日陪你上门拜访，你哥哥已在府外等候。”
[EN] "I know." Tucking a stray lock behind her daughter's ear, Madam Xiang said, "I told you all this so you understand your father meant well—don't hate him. Now here's an idea: since your father won't appear, go to the Zhu clan elders. Six of your uncles live right here in Lin'an. I already sent word to your brother to take you visiting today—he's waiting outside the gate right now."
[ZH] 千里寻高兴不已，抱住向夫人：“娘，您是天底下最好的娘！”
[EN] Overjoyed, Qianli Xun hugged Madam Xiang tight. "Mother, you're the best mother in the whole world!"
[ZH] 向夫人在女儿背上拍了拍：“都要和离，还这么高兴。”
[EN] Patting her daughter's back, Madam Xiang laughed through her tears. "Getting divorced and grinning ear to ear!"
[ZH] 千里寻放开向夫人：“娘，要不要给叔叔伯伯送钱或者礼物？”
[EN] Pulling back, Qianli Xun asked, "Should I bring silver or gifts for the uncles?"
[ZH] 向夫人笑着摇摇头：“都是自家人，送钱和礼物太见外。”
[EN] Madam Xiang shook her head with a smile. "We're all one family—gifts or money would feel stiff."
[ZH] 千里寻已等不及：“娘，我现在就去找哥哥。苦荞，愣着干什么？还不赶紧跟你家小姐我出发！”
[EN] Eager to move, Qianli Xun grabbed her skirt. "I'm going right now, Mother! Kuqiao, what are you staring at? Let's roll!"
[ZH] 向夫人瞧着女儿的背影，既熟悉又陌生，叹道：“唉，希望你的叔叔伯伯们不要把你爹的想法当回事。”
[EN] Watching her daughter dash out—both familiar and foreign—Madam Xiang murmured a sigh. "Heaven grant those uncles don't care what your father thinks..."

#CH 15
[ZH] 到了朱府的侧门外，朱景元正在那里等着，马车也已套好。
[EN] Outside Zhu Manor's side gate waited Zhu Jingyuan beside a hitched carriage.
[ZH] 千里寻一踏出门，兴奋地抱住朱景元：“有哥哥真好！”
[EN] The second Qianli Xun stepped out, she threw her arms around him in glee. "Having a brother is the best!"
[ZH] 朱景元虽然还不习惯不一样的妹妹，可打心底喜欢她：“哥今日告了假，专程陪你到各位长辈处走一趟，我们车上聊。”
[EN] Still adjusting to his bold new sister, Zhu Jingyuan couldn't help grinning anyway. "Took leave from office today just to make the rounds of the clan elders with you. Let's talk inside the carriage."
[ZH] 上了马车，苦荞坐在对面，见小姐挨着大公子亲密的样子，心里直嘀咕：“以前的小姐从未如此对待过大公子，现在的小姐对哥哥实在是兄妹情深。”
[EN] Climbing aboard, Kuqiao took the seat opposite and watched her mistress cozy up to Eldest Young Master, thinking to herself: The old Miss never acted this close with Eldest Young Master—now they're thick as thieves!
[ZH] 马车穿过安静的街道，来到临安城街市，外面不断传来嘈杂的人声。
[EN] The carriage rolled down quiet lanes into bustling Lin'an streets, lively chatter spilling past the curtains.
[ZH] 朱景元被妹妹搀胳膊坐着，很是享受：“娘一早给我把昨晚的情形说了，只要爹不同意，全家人都不敢为你出面，希望你不要怪娘，也不要怪哥哥和你嫂嫂。”
[EN] With his sister tucked against his arm, Zhu Jingyuan looked right content. "Mother told me what happened last night. Once Father says no, nobody under his roof dares step out for you. Don't blame Mother, or Wenxin and me."
[ZH] “我怎么会怪你们？要怪只怪爹冷酷无情。”千里寻把头靠在哥哥的肩膀上，“听说，你们要是违背爹的意愿，他会动用家法，不知道家法是什么，哥哥不妨说来听听？”
[EN] "Why would I blame you? Father's the heartless one!" Leaning her head against his shoulder, Qianli Xun asked, "Heard if you cross him, he brings out family discipline—what does that even look like?"
[ZH] 朱景元说：“家中男子杖责二十，女子被打手板，然后都要罚跪祠堂，两日不给吃饭，只能喝水，还要罚一年内不准走出朱府。”
[EN] "Twenty blows of the cane for the men, palm strikes for the women, kneeling in the ancestral hall for two days on water alone—and confined inside Zhu Manor for a full year."
[ZH] 千里寻放开哥哥，气愤道：“真是法西斯！”
[EN] Sitting bolt upright, Qianli Xun fumed, "Total Fascist!"
[ZH] “法西斯？什么意思？”朱景元不解。
[EN] "Fascist? What's that?" Zhu Jingyuan blinked.
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“是形容爹残暴无情。”
[EN] "Means Father is a ruthless tyrant!"
[ZH] 朱景元想起母亲说昨晚妹妹用拳头砸坏饭桌一事，以前的淑真绝对做不出，也没有能力去做，看来身边的妹妹真的不再是从前的淑真。
[EN] Recalling Madam Xiang's tale of how Shuzhen had smashed a dining table clean in half with one fist—something the old Shuzhen lacked both nerve and muscle to do—Zhu Jingyuan knew his sister had changed for good.
[ZH] “淑真，你重生后，力气突然变大，我知道你已不是从前的你，但在哥哥心里你永远是我妹妹。”
[EN] "Shuzhen, gaining all that strength after your rebirth... I know you aren't the same as before, yet in my heart you'll always be my little sister."
[ZH] 千里寻很感动，又靠在哥哥肩头：“你也永远是我哥哥。”
[EN] Warmed to the core, Qianli Xun leaned back on his shoulder. "And you'll always be my big brother."
[ZH] 朱景元接着说：“你变得厉害，哥不用担心以后你被人欺负。你肯定还不知道，现在的官家重视文人，搞得全天下把文人捧得甚高，你在临安也是有名的女词人，不知有没有忘记写诗作词的才华？”
[EN] "At least with you this strong, I won't worry about anyone pushing you around," Zhu Jingyuan went on. "You probably don't remember, yet His Majesty prizes scholars above all, lifting literary figures to the skies across the empire. You're a celebrated poet here in Lin'an—tell me you didn't lose your gift for poetry and ci?"
[ZH] 千里寻不愿哥哥伤心，反正朱淑真的诗词她都背过，足以应对。坐正后，面对朱景元，认真说：“哥哥放心，你妹妹我写诗作词的才华尽在，一点儿没丢。”
[EN] Unwilling to disappoint him—with Zhu Shuzhen's works locked in her head anyway—Qianli Xun sat straight and met his eyes. "Relax, Brother! Your sister's poetic genius is right where it belongs—didn't lose a scrap!"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“大公子放心，我可以作证。”苦荞想起小姐吟诵《菊花》诗的情景。关于小姐作这首诗，苦荞还是有很深的印象。平日小姐不善饮酒，可那日多喝了几杯，醉意袭来，然后写下这首诗。现在的小姐既然能吟诵，说明写诗作词的本领还在，真是不幸中的万幸。
[EN] "I can vouch for that, Young Master!" Kuqiao chimed in, remembering how her mistress had recited "Chrysanthemum" just yesterday—a poem the old Miss had written one tipsy evening after rare cups of wine. If she remembered her own verse, her talent lived on—a blessing amid disaster.
[ZH] 朱景元说：“那便好，只要才华还在，江南女词人的美名就不会丢。”
[EN] "Good, good! As long as your talent remains, the title Poetess of Jiangnan won't slip away."
[ZH] 马车来到一位族叔家的大门外，朱景元让妹妹和苦荞留在马车里，由他出面即可。
[EN] The carriage halted outside the first clan uncle's gate. Zhu Jingyuan left his sister and Kuqiao inside while he went in alone.
[ZH] 千里寻和苦荞在车内等了一会儿，哥哥很快回来，一进马车，摇摇头，看那表情便知未成功。
[EN] Minutes later he stepped back into the carriage and shook his head—no luck.
[ZH] 一连见了五位，均是如此。
[EN] Five houses in a row, exact same result.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“哥哥，叔叔伯伯们为何不愿替我出面？”
[EN] "Why won't any of our uncles back me, Brother?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 朱景元叹口气：“唉，只怪我们爹是临安知府，他们得罪不起，每个人一见面就问，知府大人可同意？一听爹不同意，一个个都成缩头乌龟。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan sighed. "Because our father is Prefect of Lin'an—none of them dare cross him! First question out of every mouth was, 'Does the Prefect approve?' The instant they heard Father said no, every last one pulled his head into his shell like a turtle."
[ZH] 千里寻怨道：“哥，你可真老实，不会骗他们，就说爹同意，让你专程来请他们跟着一起出面。”
[EN] "Brother, you're too honest! Why didn't you lie and tell them Father sent you to invite them?"
[ZH] “骗不得，和离是你的人生大事，把他们骗去，到了现场发现爹不在，他们再离开，你面子上挂不住，还会影响你的名声。”
[EN] "Can't lie about something this big! Mutual divorce decides your whole future. Drag them there under false pretenses, they spot Father missing and walk out—you'd lose face and ruin your reputation!"
[ZH] “还是哥想得周到，还有最后一位，我与哥哥一起去请，才显得诚心。”
[EN] "Good thinking, Brother. One uncle left—let me go in with you so he sees I'm sincere."
[ZH] 最后一位是位族中的伯父，比朱晞颜年长，接待了兄妹二人，一听说眼前的侄女要和离，也先问知府大人的意思，然后只是拒绝，还一再劝侄女好好过日子，女子名声最重要，和离虽比被夫家休掉名声好些，依然会损毁女子的声誉，以后再想找婆家没那么容易。
[EN] The last elder, an older cousin-uncle senior to Zhu Xiyan, received the pair politely—only to ask the Prefect's stance first, refuse outright, and launch into a lecture about preserving a woman's good name, warning that even mutual divorce scarred a lady's reputation and made remarriage hard.
[ZH] 千里寻努力挤出眼泪，哭道：“伯伯，要是您家女儿嫁给一个又是打她，还把商妓往家带的夫君，您还会让自己家女儿在火坑里待着吗？呜呜……！”
[EN] Squeezing out tears on command, Qianli Xun wailed, "Uncle! If your own daughter married a husband who beat her and dragged courtesans home, would you leave her trapped in that fiery pit? Sob...!"
[ZH] 伯伯见侄女说得在理，又哭得梨花带雨，甚是可怜，安慰道：“你要是我亲闺女，我肯定会出面，可你爹是临安知府，谁敢得罪？不如我给你出个注意，在临安城内，只有一人出面你爹不敢说一个不字。”
[EN] Moved by her logic and tear-streaked beauty, the elder softened. "Were you my own daughter, I'd step forward in a heartbeat. Yet your father is Prefect of Lin'an—who dares cross him? Still, I'll give you a hint: there is one person in all of Lin'an whom your father dares not say a single word against."
[ZH] 千里寻立刻来了精神，含着半真半假的泪水问：“此人是谁？”
[EN] Perking up through half-fake tears, Qianli Xun asked, "Who?!"
[ZH] 伯伯还未回答，朱景元立刻领会，站起来拱手道：“感谢伯伯指点，我这就带妹妹去找老祖宗。”
[EN] Before the uncle could answer, light dawned on Zhu Jingyuan. Jumping up with cupped hands, he bowed. "Thank you for pointing the way, Uncle! I'll take Shuzhen to see the Old Ancestor right now!"
[ZH] 伯伯笑着说：“去吧，只要你和淑真能请动她，她一人可顶朱家所有族人。”
[EN] The elder chuckled. "Go on! If you two can move her, she alone outweighs every elder in the Zhu clan."
[ZH] 千里寻虽未搞明白这位老祖宗是谁，可既然又有破解之法，她自然高兴。拜别伯伯，跟在哥哥身后上了马车。
[EN] Still fuzzy on who this Old Ancestor was, Qianli Xun beamed at having a way out, bade the uncle farewell, and climbed back into the carriage behind her brother.
[ZH] 到了马车上，已完全忍不住，急切地问：“哥哥，老祖宗是何方神圣？连临安知府都不怕？”
[EN] Inside, bursting with curiosity, she asked, "Brother, who on earth is this Old Ancestor that even the Prefect of Lin'an can't defy?"
[ZH] 朱景元吩咐车夫“打道回府”，才对妹妹说：“老祖宗住在朱府佛堂内，已经一百二十岁，是我们祖父的祖父的娘。祖母祖父，曾祖母曾祖父，以及祖父的祖母祖父均已仙逝，可这位老祖宗还健在，你说神奇不神奇？以她的年龄和辈分，当然不会把爹放在眼里。”
[EN] Calling out "Home!" to the driver, Zhu Jingyuan explained, "She lives inside Zhu Manor's Buddhist Shrine—a hundred and twenty years old! She's our grandfather's grandfather's mother. Grandparents, great-grandparents, even great-great-grandparents have all passed on, yet she's still alive and well—amazing, right? With her age and seniority, she doesn't give Father a second glance."
[ZH] 一长串像绕口令一样的称谓，直接把千里寻搞晕，她根本理不清楚其中的关系，反正只要知道这位老祖宗连朱淑真的爹都管不着就行。
[EN] The tongue-twister family tree made Qianli Xun's head spin; forget tracking generations, all that mattered was she outranked Zhu Xiyan!
[ZH] “哥哥，你和娘要是早想到老祖宗，我们就不用白白在临安城跑来跑去。”
[EN] "Brother, if you and Mother had thought of her sooner, we wouldn't have wasted all day running around Lin'an!"
[ZH] “也不能怪我和娘没想起她老人家，主要是她常年待在佛堂根本不出来走动，也不允许别人打扰，连爹也很难见到，渐渐地大家习惯性地把她忘记。”
[EN] "Can't blame Mother or me—she stays shut inside the Buddhist Shrine year after year, forbids visitors, even Father rarely lays eyes on her. Over time everybody forgets she's there."
[ZH] “老祖宗年纪这么大，会不会连路都走不动？”
[EN] "A hundred and twenty years old... can she even walk?"
[ZH] “我也不清楚，从小到大只是听说她，从来没见过。”
[EN] "No idea—heard of her all my life, never met her once."
[ZH] 千里寻又开始担忧，一百二十岁的老人恐怕行动都成问题，而且连朱府的佛堂都没走出过，如何会愿意为她走出朱府？目前没有其他更好的办法，只能死马当活马医。
[EN] Fresh worry crept in. At one hundred and twenty, could she even leave the shrine, let alone trek out of Zhu Manor for a divorce? Still, dead horse or not, Qianli Xun had to try doctoring it—no other play left.

#CH 16
[ZH] 以朱府的布局来看，佛堂所在之处，比朱淑真住的园子还要偏僻。
[EN] By the layout of Zhu Manor, the Buddhist Shrine sat tucked even deeper than Zhu Shuzhen's garden.
[ZH] 在朱景元的带领下，千里寻跟着往朱府深处走去，直到眼前出现一座小山丘，千里寻才看到，在那山丘底下有座园子，外形像是一座寺庙，园墙不高，能清楚地看到里面的房屋。
[EN] Following Zhu Jingyuan into the farthest reaches of the estate, Qianli Xun spotted a small hillock ahead—and nestled at its base, a walled compound shaped like a temple, low enough that the rooftops showed clear above.
[ZH] “哥哥，朱府怎么把山都给搬了进来？”
[EN] "Brother, did Zhu Manor haul an actual hill inside the walls?"
[ZH] “那不是山，只是个大土堆而已，当年建造朱府时，为了搞这么一个像小山一样的大土堆，可是从临安城外拉了不少土进来。”
[EN] "Not a real hill—just a massive earth mound. When they built the manor, they hauled tons of soil in from outside Lin'an just to raise that mound."
[ZH] 千里寻又看到一条清澈见底的小河流，河面很窄，水并不深，水里放着几块石头，需要穿过这条小河才能到达佛堂。
[EN] Next came a clear, shallow stream spanned by stepping-stones leading straight toward the shrine.
[ZH] 三人踩着石头穿过小河，是一段石子甬路，走上去脚底硌硌的，像是在做足底按摩。当石子甬路走尽后，穿过野草地，才算真正到达佛堂跟前。门紧闭着，上面有一个小小的匾额，写着“佛堂”二字，里面连续传出敲击木鱼的声音。
[EN] Hopping across the stones, the three stepped onto a pebble path that poked through their soles like a foot massage, then crossed a patch of wild grass before reaching the shrine gate. Closed tight beneath a modest plaque reading "Buddhist Shrine," it vibrated with the steady clack of a wooden fish inside.
[ZH] 朱景元说：“淑真，你在这里等着，我前去敲门。”
[EN] "Wait here, Shuzhen—I'll knock," Zhu Jingyuan said.
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，看着哥哥走到跟前，将那木门敲了三下，等了一会儿，并没有人来开门。又敲了三下，又等了一会儿，还是没有人开门。继续敲了三下，终于听到里面有脚步声传来，三人一阵高兴。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded as her brother stepped up and rapped three times. Silence. Three more knocks—still nothing. On the third round of knocks, footsteps sounded inside, lifting all three spirits.
[ZH] 里面有拔门栓的声音，接着“吱扭”一声门被打开，一位头发花白的老妇人出现在眼前，问道：“你们是？”
[EN] A bolt slid back and the wooden door creaked open to reveal a silver-haired elderly woman. "Who are you?"
[ZH] 苦荞赶紧上前有礼道：“嬷嬷好，这位是朱府大公子，这位是小姐，前来拜见老祖宗。”
[EN] Kuqiao stepped forward with a polite bow. "Good day, Matron. This is Eldest Young Master and Miss from the manor, come to pay respects to the Old Ancestor."
[ZH] 嬷嬷说：“原来是府中的公子和小姐，你们这些小辈可能还不知道，老祖宗一心向佛，从不见俗世之人，还是走吧。”说完就要关门。
[EN] "Ah, the young master and miss," the matron said. "Being young, you may not know—the Old Ancestor devotes herself solely to the Buddha and never sees worldly guests. Please go back." She started shutting the door.
[ZH] 朱景元阻拦道：“嬷嬷，这些晚辈都知道，本不该来打扰老祖宗清净，只因舍妹遇到人生难题，需要面见老祖宗，还请行个方便。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan wedged a hand up to stop her. "We know well, Matron, and wouldn't dare disturb her peace lightly—yet my sister faces a crisis of a lifetime and begs an audience. Please grant us this favor."
[ZH] 嬷嬷说：“老祖宗脱离世俗，六根清净，岂可涉足红尘之事，你们听话，还是离去吧。”
[EN] "The Old Ancestor has severed all worldly ties and purified her senses—how could she meddle in mortal affairs? Be good and leave."
[ZH] 这是唯一的机会，如果请不到一位长辈出面，和离只能失败。哪怕请一位，成功的几率也会多一些，只要有一线希望，千里寻都会牢牢抓住。于是大声说：“老祖宗修习佛法，难道不明白佛菩萨普度众生的夙愿吗？现在众生之一，您的不知道第几代的孙女有难，需要您这位菩萨出来救赎，可您却躲在佛堂里只顾一个人清修，这算哪门子修习佛法？”
[EN] This was her last shot—no elder, no divorce. Even one ally improved her odds, and Qianli Xun refused to let go of a single thread of hope. Raising her voice, she called straight over the matron's shoulder: "The Old Ancestor cultivates the Buddha's Dharma—doesn't she know the bodhisattvas vow to deliver all sentient beings from suffering? Right now one of those beings—her own descendant however many generations down—is in dire trouble and needs a bodhisattva to step out and save her! Hiding inside a shrine chasing private peace—what kind of Buddhist practice is that?!"
[ZH] 嬷嬷批评道：“你这小辈，太不知礼，怎能随意批评老祖宗？”
[EN] The matron bristled. "Impertinent child! How dare you lecture the Old Ancestor!"
[ZH] 里面的木鱼声戛然而止，传出一个苍老而有力的声音：“秀珍，让她进来吧。”
[EN] Inside, the wooden fish stopped dead. An aged yet resonant voice rang out: "Xiuzhen, let her in."
[ZH] 三人一阵欣喜，正要一起进入，被秀珍嬷嬷挡住：“没听老祖宗说只让她一人进去？你们两个在外面侯着。”
[EN] Delighted, all three moved forward, only for Matron Xiuzhen to bar the doorway. "Didn't you hear the Old Ancestor say her alone? You two wait outside."
[ZH] 朱景元和苦荞只好收住脚步，在外面等候。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan and Kuqiao halted outside the gate.
[ZH] 千里寻兴奋之余跟着秀珍嬷嬷进入。
[EN] Heart racing, Qianli Xun followed Matron Xiuzhen in.
[ZH] 佛堂的小院不大，简单而整洁，最吸引人的是三株腊梅，早已过花季，枝叶倒是繁茂。
[EN] The tiny courtyard lay spare and spotless, its centerpiece three wintersweet trees—long past bloom, yet thick with lush green leaves.
[ZH] 走进佛堂的正屋，迎面最显眼的地方摆着一尊高大的佛像，看了不由让人肃然起敬。佛像前面的供桌上有个香炉，里面插着三炷香，正在燃烧，供桌前放着一个蒲团。
[EN] Inside the main shrine hall towered a grand statue of the Buddha that inspired instant awe. Three incense sticks smoldered in a censer on the altar table before it, with a woven prayer cushion laid on the floor.
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷说：“姑娘既然进来，需先拜拜。”
[EN] "Since you've entered, young lady, pay respects first," Matron Xiuzhen instructed.
[ZH] 千里寻乖乖照做，谁让她有求而来。她走上前，双手合十，跪在蒲团上，拜了三拜，心想：“老祖宗还没见到，倒先拜起佛像来。”
[EN] With a favor to beg, Qianli Xun obeyed without fuss—palms pressed together, kneeling on the cushion for three bows, thinking: Haven't even met the Old Ancestor yet, already bowing to Buddha!
[ZH] 拜完后起身，还没来得及瞧四周，听到秀珍嬷嬷的声音：“老祖宗，朱家的小辈进来了，就由您老教训教训她。”
[EN] Rising, before she could take in the rest of the hall, she heard Matron Xiuzhen announce: "Old Ancestor, the young Zhu descendant is here—please give her a good scolding."
[ZH] 千里寻扭头看去，在佛像的左边放着一张几案，上面放着木鱼和犍稚，几案旁坐着一位头发全白的老人，眼窝深陷，鼻梁高挺，嘴巴周围充满褶皱，可以说整张脸都充满褶皱。褶皱以及零散的老年斑也无法遮住皮肤散发出的白光，一看便知年轻时绝对是位皮肤白皙的美人。千里寻觉得这张脸虽苍老，却很慈祥，只要看那么一眼，心里便会产生奇妙的温暖的感觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun turned. Left of the altar sat a low table holding a wooden fish and mallet, and behind it sat a completely white-haired elder—deep-set eyes, high nose bridge, face etched top to bottom with wrinkles. Yet neither wrinkles nor scattered age spots dimmed the luminous pallor of her skin; she must have been a radiant beauty in her youth. Aged as that face was, kindness radiated from it, wrapping Qianli Xun in an unexpected glow of warmth at first sight.
[ZH] 老祖宗微闭双目，双腿盘坐，手里拿着佛珠用心盘着，嘴里念念有词。听到秀珍的话后，张嘴说：“让她过来。”
[EN] Eyes half-closed, legs crossed, the Old Ancestor rolled prayer beads through her fingers while murmuring sutras. Hearing Xiuzhen, she spoke: "Have her come over."
[ZH] 千里寻知道叫自己，走上前。
[EN] Knowing she was summoned, Qianli Xun stepped forward.
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷向她示意坐在蒲团上，她虽不习惯，还是听话地坐下。
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen gestured toward a prayer cushion; awkward as sitting cross-legged felt, Qianli Xun lowered herself obediently.
[ZH] 老祖宗依然盘着佛珠，双眼微闭：“听你刚才说话有些见识，所以请你进来，不知你找我所为何事？”
[EN] Still rolling her beads with eyes nearly shut, the Old Ancestor said, "Your speech outside showed some spirit, which is why I invited you in. Tell me why you came."
[ZH] 千里寻迅速在心里组织开场白，终于找到合适的：“老祖宗是女人，应该最能体会女人的不易，孙儿因父母之命媒妁之言嫁给才德无法与孙儿相匹配的丈夫，这些倒没什么，如果他人好，日子还可以过下去，可他打孙儿，还把商妓往家里带，孙儿实在无法跟他过下去，便想到和离。可父亲不同意替我出面，只要父亲不同意，一家的长辈，包括族中的长辈都不会替我出面，孙儿无路可走，只能来求老祖宗。求老祖宗可怜我，替我出面主持和离之事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun assembled her pitch fast and plunged in: "As a woman yourself, Old Ancestor, you know best how hard women's lives are. Obeying parents' orders and matchmakers' words, I married a husband unmatched in mind or character. I could have endured even that if he had a decent heart—instead, he beats me and brings brothel courtesans into the house! Unable to bear another day, I sought mutual divorce. Yet Father refuses to step in, and without his nod, not a single elder in the family or clan dares back me. With nowhere left to turn, I came to beg your mercy—please step forward and preside over my mutual divorce."
[ZH] 老祖宗终于抬眼，那双眼睛周围的皮肤虽有很多褶皱，但目光炯炯有神。千里寻被深深吸引。
[EN] At last the Old Ancestor opened her eyes. Surrounded by folds of age, her gaze shone clear and sharp—holding Qianli Xun transfixed.
[ZH] “女人活着确实不易，但我为何要凭你一己之言出面为你主持和离？你父亲不同意，说明只是你这孩子瞎胡闹而已。”
[EN] "A woman's life is indeed hard. Yet why should I take your word alone and preside over a divorce? If your father opposes it, perhaps you're simply acting rash."
[ZH] “老祖宗此言差矣，我身为女子，深知婚姻对女子的重要，岂能拿自己的婚姻大事胡闹？在一段不符合心意的婚姻中，就像深陷泥潭，无力自拔，即便如此，孙儿也要努力挣扎，哪怕死在泥潭之中，也比活着忍受婚姻的不幸要舒坦许多。”
[EN] "With respect, Old Ancestor, I know full well how grave marriage is for a woman—how could I act rash with my own future? Living in a loveless marriage feels like sinking in a swamp with no footing. Even so, I will struggle with all I have; dying in the fight beats living trapped in misery."
[ZH] “看来，你和离的决心倒是很坚定。那你说说你的父亲为何不同意替你出面？”
[EN] "Your resolve runs deep, I see. Tell me then—why does your father refuse to back you?"
[ZH] “他认为女子无才便是德，后悔将我培养成女词人，认为我嫁给现在的丈夫，才能明白身为女子重在相夫教子，而不是写诗作词，整日沉迷在不切实际的幻想中。他还十分看重朱家的脸面，认为即使我和离也会损害到家族清誉。”
[EN] "He believes a woman's virtue lies in having no talent. He regrets raising me as a ci poet and thinks marrying my current husband will force me to serve my spouse and raise children instead of writing verse and chasing airy dreams. Above all, he guards the Zhu family's face and fears even mutual divorce will tarnish its honor."
[ZH] “你父亲没有什么不对，只是与你的想法不同而已。你刚说丈夫打你，还把商妓领到家里，我听着也认为女子不应该与这样的丈夫过下去，难道你父亲不知？还要因此反对和离？”
[EN] "Your father isn't wrong—he simply sees things differently from you. Still, if your husband beats you and brings courtesans home, even I agree no woman should endure that. Doesn't your father know? Why would he still oppose the divorce?"
[ZH] 千里寻不想隐瞒老祖宗：“我爹当然知道，他之所以还反对和离，只因他认为错在孙儿。”
[EN] Unwilling to hold anything back, Qianli Xun spoke plain: "Father knows everything. He still opposes it because he thinks I'm at fault."
[ZH] “哦，说来听听。”
[EN] "Oh? Tell me how."
[ZH] “孙儿在新婚之夜，不愿与丈夫圆房，还以死相逼，平日对丈夫也是不闻不问，冷漠至极。所以，父亲认为错在孙儿，才不同意和离，劝我回去好好过日子。”
[EN] "On our wedding night, I refused to consummate the marriage and threatened suicide. Day after day I ignored my husband and treated him like ice. So Father blames me, refuses divorce, and orders me back to behave."
[ZH] 老祖宗又微闭双目，沉默着。千里寻甚是担心，她会不会也像其他人一样拒绝自己？周围的空气就像凝固了一般，只能听到老祖宗盘佛珠的声音。
[EN] The Old Ancestor let her eyelids droop once more and fell silent. Anxiety gripped Qianli Xun—would she turn her down too? The air seemed frozen solid, broken only by the dry click of prayer beads.
[ZH] 过了一会儿，老祖宗终于又开口：“你既是女词人，当着我的面写下一首词，若打动我，我必然帮你出面。秀珍，纸笔伺候。”
[EN] At length the Old Ancestor spoke again: "Since you are a ci poet, compose a lyric before me right now. Move my heart, and I will stand up for you. Xiuzhen—brush and paper."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷在佛像右侧的书桌上铺好纸张，将墨研好：“姑娘，纸笔已准备好，过来写吧。”
[EN] At a desk to the right of the Buddha, Matron Xiuzhen laid out paper and ground fresh ink. "Everything is ready, young lady. Come write."
[ZH] 从老祖宗让她写下一首词时，千里寻就开始搜刮脑细胞，回顾朱淑真写过的诗词，到底哪一首更加合适？
[EN] The instant the Old Ancestor asked for a lyric, Qianli Xun began ransacking her memory of Zhu Shuzhen's poems—which one would strike home?
[ZH] 当秀珍嬷嬷请她过去时，还毫无头绪，只能硬着头皮站起来，放慢脚步走向书桌。朱淑真的诗词虽然每一首都写得不错，可要选出一首能打动老祖宗，让她出面主持和离，还是有一定难度。
[EN] Even as Matron Xiuzhen beckoned, she drew a blank and stalled, walking slow toward the desk. Every poem of Zhu Shuzhen's carried power, yet picking the exact one to move a century-old Buddhist elder into backing a divorce wasn't easy.
[ZH] 千里寻已坐在书桌前，她提起笔，轻蘸墨汁，无意中抬头看到老祖宗身后的墙壁上挂着一个“谜”字，不由问道：“嬷嬷，一般修佛之人给墙上要么挂一‘禅’字，要么挂‘清净’二字，为何老祖宗要挂一个‘谜’字？”
[EN] Seated at the desk, dipping her brush into ink, she happened to glance up—and saw a single massive calligraphy character hanging behind the Old Ancestor: "Riddle" (谜). Curiosity struck. "Matron, most Buddhists hang 'Zen' or 'Serenity' on their wall—why does the Old Ancestor hang 'Riddle'?"
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷回道：“老祖宗认为人之所以无知，是因为身在谜中，她要用这个字时刻提醒自己突破谜境，才可明了人生真相，只有明了人生真相，方可放下贪嗔痴慢，达到内心清净。”
[EN] "The Old Ancestor holds that mortals stay ignorant because they dwell inside a riddle," Matron Xiuzhen replied. "She keeps that character there as a constant reminder to break past illusion and grasp life's truth—only then can one cast off greed, anger, delusion, and pride to reach inner peace."
[ZH] 佛理太过深奥，千里寻听了也是一知半解，但这个“谜”字却让一首词出现她的头脑中，迅速提笔写下：
[EN] Half the Buddhist philosophy sailed right over Qianli Xun's head—yet that single word "riddle" unlocked the perfect lyric in her mind! Her brush flew across the paper:
[ZH] 下楼来，金钱卜落；问苍天，人在何方？恨王孙，一直去了；詈冤家，言去难留。
[EN] Coming downstairs, golden coins drop for divination; I ask High Heaven, where has he gone? Resenting the noble lord who walked straight away; cursing the heartless one—speaking of parting, hard to hold back.
[ZH] ﻿悔当初，吾错失口，有上交无下交。皂白何须问？分开不用刀，从今莫把仇人靠，千种相思一撇销。（《断肠谜》）
[EN] Regretting how I first misspoke; there is upper union, no lower tie. Why ask right from wrong? Parting needs no knife; from this day on lean no more on a foe—a thousand kinds of longing wiped away with one stroke. ("The Heartbreak Riddle") [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 写完后，千里寻放下笔，拿起纸，用嘴巴吹了吹未干的墨迹，送到秀珍嬷嬷手中。秀珍嬷嬷接过，走过去，给老祖宗在桌上铺开。
[EN] Setting down the brush, Qianli Xun lifted the sheet, blew across the wet ink, and handed it to Matron Xiuzhen, who spread it on the table before the Old Ancestor.
[ZH] ﻿只见老祖宗停下盘佛珠的手，抬眼向纸上瞧去，用心仔细鉴赏了一会儿，说道：“你回去吧，我答应为你出面。”
[EN] The Old Ancestor halted her beads, raised her eyes to the page, and read every character with deep attention before declaring: "Go back. I agree to step forward for you."
[ZH] 千里寻兴奋不已：“晚辈谢过老祖宗！”
[EN] Joy surged through Qianli Xun. "Your descendant thanks you, Old Ancestor!"
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷将千里寻送出屋外，嘱咐道：“老祖宗既然同意出面，请姑娘明日把和离的时间和地点差人写在纸条上，从门缝塞进来即可。”
[EN] Walking her to the door, Matron Xiuzhen instructed, "Since the Old Ancestor has consented, send someone tomorrow with a note stating the time and place of the divorce meeting and slip it under the gate."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“嬷嬷放心，我定会办妥。不过，我怕老祖宗不认得路，要不我派人来接她老人家？”
[EN] "Count on it, Matron! Only—I'm worried the Old Ancestor might not know the way. Should I send a carriage to fetch her?"
[ZH] “不必，老祖宗活了一百二十岁，什么没经历过？你只管等着就是，我自会与她过去。姑娘，请！”
[EN] "No need. At a hundred and twenty, what hasn't the Old Ancestor seen? Wait where you are—I will bring her myself. Farewell, young lady."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷做出送客的手势，千里寻拜别离开。出了佛堂小院，门又被紧紧关上。
[EN] Gesturing her out, Matron Xiuzhen shut the shrine gate firm behind her.
[ZH] 朱景元与苦荞迎上来。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan and Kuqiao hurried up.
[ZH] “妹妹，老祖宗可同意？”
[EN] "Well, Sister? Did the Old Ancestor agree?"
[ZH] 千里寻轻松地点点头：“同意了！”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded with a breezy smile. "She agreed!"
[ZH] 苦荞也替小姐感到高兴：“小姐，接下来咱们该怎么做？”
[EN] Thrilled for her mistress, Kuqiao asked, "What's our next move, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻干脆地说：“回林府！”
[EN] Qianli Xun answered crisp and clear: "Back to Lin Manor!"

#CH 17
[ZH] 千里寻天生急性子，说回林府必须立刻回去。并不是她想念林府，而是想回去探听林觉的虚实，好为和离之事做好万全准备。费了老鼻子劲儿请了一尊大神，还不得回去好好筹谋筹谋。
[EN] Impatient by nature, once Qianli Xun decided to return to Lin Manor, she moved at once—not because she missed the place, but to feel out Lin Jue's hand and lock down every detail for the divorce. After going through hoops to summon a heavy hitter like the Old Ancestor, she wasn't leaving anything to chance.
[ZH] 她吩咐苦荞找车夫把马车套上。林府的车夫早已回去，只能辛苦朱府的车夫再跑一趟。苦荞安排好后，搀扶小姐上了马车，行走在回林府的路上。
[EN] She had Kuqiao order a carriage. The Lin coachman had long since gone back, so Zhu Manor's driver had to make the run. Soon they were rolling back toward Lin Manor.
[ZH] 千里寻忽觉额头的伤口有些发痒，估计是快好了。这一痒，让她想起打林觉的那一拳，他眼睛周围的淤青估计一时半会儿好不了，他因此在朱晞颜面前告了她一状，如果和离时再以此说事，岂不麻烦？
[EN] Halfway there, Qianli Xun felt her forehead cut itch—nearly healed. That reminded her of the punch she had landed on Lin Jue; his black eye couldn't have faded yet, and he had already tattled to Zhu Xiyan about it. What if he waved that bruise around at the divorce meeting to muddy the waters?
[ZH] “苦荞，你身上可带银子？”
[EN] "Kuqiao, got silver on you?"
[ZH] “多的没有，只替小姐装了五两。”
[EN] "Not much—just five taels I packed for you, Miss."
[ZH] “买药应该够吧？”
[EN] "Enough for medicine?"
[ZH] “那得看小姐买什么药？”
[EN] "Depends what kind."
[ZH] “林觉的眼睛周围被我打得淤青，我想买药帮他治治。”
[EN] "Lin Jue's eye is still bruised from my punch. I want to buy ointment to clear it up."
[ZH] “这种药花不了多少银子，尽够。不过，小姐怎么突然对姑爷这么好？”
[EN] "Plenty for that! Wait—why are you being nice to Son-in-law all of a sudden?"
[ZH] “苦荞，你记着，有时看似对别人好，其实是另有所图。”
[EN] "Remember this, Kuqiao: sometimes being nice to someone is just a way to get what you want."
[ZH] 千里寻掀开帘子，向马车外瞧，希望能找到一家药房。
[EN] Lifting the window curtain, Qianli Xun scanned the street for a pharmacy.
[ZH] 苦荞猜出小姐的心思：“小姐不用找，马车再往前走一会儿，有家吉祥药房，他们家跌打损伤药是全临安城最好的，我给小姐涂的药就是从吉祥药房买的。”
[EN] Reading her mind, Kuqiao said, "No need to look, Miss—just up ahead is Jixiang Pharmacy. Best trauma salves in all of Lin'an. The ointment I put on your forehead came from there."
[ZH] 到达吉祥药房门口时，苦荞让车夫把车停下，她扶着小姐下了车。
[EN] At Jixiang Pharmacy, Kuqiao called a halt and helped her mistress step down.
[ZH] 走进吉祥药房，一股草药之气扑面而来。再仔细观察，药房四周靠墙的地方全部放着木制货架，一层一层的架子上摆着各式各样的瓶瓶罐罐。
[EN] Stepping inside, a rich scent of dried herbs washed over them. Wooden shelving lined every wall, row after row stacked with jars and vials of every shape.
[ZH] 药房的伙计见有顾客光临，从柜台后面走出来，笑脸相迎：“姑娘，想买什么药？”
[EN] An apothecary clerk stepped out from behind the counter with a welcoming grin. "What medicine can I fetch for you today, young lady?"
[ZH] 千里寻向药房四周瞅瞅：“有人的眼睛周围被打得淤青，有没有一涂就好的药？”
[EN] Glancing around the shop, Qianli Xun asked, "Someone took a punch to the eye and has a nasty bruise—got anything that clears it up fast?"
[ZH] 伙计笑道：“姑娘可是找对地方，吉祥药房别的药不敢说，这类药还真有，只要头一天往那淤青处一涂，保准第二天淤青全消。”
[EN] The clerk beamed. "Came to the right place! Can't brag about everything, yet for bruises like that, dab this on tonight and by tomorrow it's gone without a trace!"
[ZH] 伙计说完从货架上取下一个小瓷瓶：“就是这瓶药。”
[EN] He reached up and pulled down a small porcelain vial. "Right here."
[ZH] 千里寻接过药瓶：“就它了。苦荞，拿着。”苦荞上前接过药瓶。
[EN] Qianli Xun took it. "We'll take it. Hold this, Kuqiao." Kuqiao stepped up to tuck it away.
[ZH] 伙计说：“这瓶药，一百个铜钱。”
[EN] "One hundred wen for this vial," the clerk said.
[ZH] “你别急，钱不会少你的，我还得买一种药，你看我额头的伤……”
[EN] "Hold on, you'll get your money. I need a second medicine—see this cut on my forehead..."
[ZH] 千里寻话还未说完，药房的伙计插嘴道：“姑娘额头的伤快好了，不用再浪费钱买药，我们是良心商家，不能欺骗姑娘花冤枉钱。”
[EN] Before she could finish, the clerk cut in, "Your cut is practically healed, miss! Don't waste coin on more salve—we run an honest shop and never trick customers into buying what they don't need."
[ZH] 千里寻急了：“谁说要买药治伤！我想让这伤口变得更厉害，但不能毁我的容貌，你们药房可有此药？”
[EN] Qianli Xun raised her voice. "Who said anything about healing it?! I want to make this wound look worse—without leaving a scar! Got anything like that?"
[ZH] 伙计第一次碰到买药伤害自己的人，为难道：“我们药房卖药只为救死扶伤，从不卖伤人伤己的药，姑娘这不是为难我吗？”
[EN] Never having met a buyer asking to harm herself, the clerk frowned. "We sell medicine to heal and save lives, never potions to hurt people! Don't put me in a bind, miss."
[ZH] 千里寻伸出手：“苦荞，把银子拿来！”
[EN] Qianli Xun held out her hand. "Kuqiao—the silver!"
[ZH] 苦荞从怀里掏出钱袋，放到小姐手里。千里寻接过，取出一两银子放在柜台上：“有没有？”
[EN] Kuqiao pulled out the money pouch. Qianli Xun fished out one tael of silver and set it on the counter. "Got any?"
[ZH] 伙计看看银子，坚持道：“没有。”
[EN] Eyeballing the silver, the clerk stood firm. "No."
[ZH] 千里寻又拿出一两，继续放在柜台上：“有没有？”
[EN] Qianli Xun laid down a second tael. "Got any?"
[ZH] 伙计依然毫不动容：“没有！”
[EN] Unmoved: "No!"
[ZH] 千里寻又拿出一两：“有没有？”
[EN] A third tael hit the wood. "Got any?"
[ZH] 伙计刚要说没有，掌柜掀开帘子从里面出来，笑嘻嘻地拿过三两银子：“有！当然有！”
[EN] Before the clerk could refuse again, the shopkeeper brushed aside the back curtain, grinned ear to ear, and scooped up all three taels. "Yes! Of course we do!"
[ZH] 收好银子后，掌柜从柜台下面拿出一个小瓷瓶，递给千里寻：“姑娘，这里装的是腐肌膏，只要在您额头的伤口上涂一点点，那伤口会变得严重，如果想让伤口尽快好，再涂上您刚买的那瓶药即可。”
[EN] Pocketing the silver, the shopkeeper pulled a tiny porcelain vial from beneath the counter and handed it over. "Miss, this is Corrosive Paste. Just a tiny dab on your cut will make it flare up looking fierce. Whenever you want it healed fast, just dab on the first salve you bought."
[ZH] “多谢掌柜。”千里寻高兴道，然后批评伙计，“你啊，以后要多向掌柜学习，做生意需头脑灵活。”
[EN] "Thanks, boss!" Turning to the clerk, Qianli Xun teased, "Take notes from your boss—gotta stay flexible in business!"
[ZH] 千里寻把药和剩下的银子给了苦荞，两人走出吉祥药房，继续乘坐马车回林府。
[EN] Handing the vials and remaining change to Kuqiao, she stepped back out to the carriage for Lin Manor.
[ZH] 苦荞端详着两瓶药：“花了三两银子，实在亏。小姐就不该把银子一两一两地往出拿，这两瓶药最多一吊钱。”
[EN] Kuqiao frowned down at the two vials. "Three whole taels—highway robbery! You shouldn't have dropped silver one tael at a time, Miss—both vials together aren't worth a single string of cash!"
[ZH] “我要不这么做，药房的掌柜能拿出腐肌膏？”
[EN] "If I hadn't, would the boss have pulled out Corrosive Paste?"
[ZH] “真想不通小姐是怎么想的，花这么多钱就是为买瓶伤害自己的药膏。”
[EN] "Still don't get what you're thinking—spending all that silver just to buy ointment to hurt yourself!"
[ZH] “有时候看似伤害自己，其实全是为自己好，到时你就知道。”
[EN] "Sometimes hurting yourself a little is the smartest move you can make. You'll see soon enough."
[ZH] 回到林府，千里寻不由向四处瞧瞧，既然里面也住着林觉的两位哥哥，说不定能碰到。可瞧了半天，只有下人来回忙碌走动的身影。
[EN] Back inside Lin Manor, Qianli Xun glanced around hoping to catch sight of Lin Jue's two brothers, yet only busy servants crossed the paths.
[ZH] 千里寻只好向西拐去，回到她与林觉居住的院子，问过下人，才知道林觉还未归来，便直奔竹兰居。想起嫁妆，马上要离婚，必须把属于她的财产了解清楚，否则到时被林觉算计都不自知。
[EN] Heading west back to her shared courtyard, she learned from servants that Lin Jue wasn't back yet and went straight to Zhulan Residence. With divorce looming, she needed a full accounting of her own assets before Lin Jue could pull a fast one.
[ZH] 千里寻刚坐下，连茶都没来得及喝，急切地问：“苦荞，我的嫁妆平时都是怎么管的？”
[EN] Barely seated—not even waiting for tea—she asked, "Kuqiao, who manages my dowry?"
[ZH] 苦荞拿出一个放药的圆形竹筐，把新买的两瓶药放进去：“小姐一心扑在诗词上，根本不管这些事，都是韦嬷嬷帮小姐管着。”
[EN] Setting the two new vials into a round bamboo medicine basket, Kuqiao answered, "You poured your whole heart into poetry and never bothered with accounts, Miss—Matron Wei handles all of it for you."
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷？”
[EN] "Matron Wei?"
[ZH] “她是您的奶娘，夫人刚生下小姐，奶水不够，找了韦嬷嬷喂养小姐，后来她就留在小姐身边照顾您，深得夫人信任，您嫁人时，也跟着过来，负责管理嫁妆之事。小姐用钱只需问韦嬷嬷要，不用理会姑爷。”
[EN] "Your wet nurse! When Madam gave birth to you, she lacked enough milk and hired Matron Wei to nurse you. Afterward Matron Wei stayed to raise you, trusted completely by Madam, and came with you when you married to manage your dowry. Whenever you need cash, just ask Matron Wei—never mind Son-in-law."
[ZH] “我怎么没见到她？”
[EN] "Why haven't I seen her around?"
[ZH] “这几日她带下人到乡下收租子，收完租便回来，小姐到时自然见到。”
[EN] "She took servants out to the countryside these past few days to collect rent. Once she's done, she'll be back and you'll meet her."
[ZH] 原来是自家人管理嫁妆，千里寻不用担心林觉算计：“我要睡一会儿，今儿跑来跑去有些累，林觉回来，告诉我一声。”
[EN] One of her own people holding the purse strings—zero worry of Lin Jue skimming her assets. "I'm beat from running around all day—going to grab a nap. Tell me the second Lin Jue gets back."
[ZH] 苦荞伺候小姐躺好，关了房门出去打探姑爷的消息。
[EN] After helping her mistress lie down, Kuqiao closed the door and went to check on Son-in-law.
[ZH] 姑爷身边的小厮说，主君一旦回来，第一个告诉苦荞。她放心回到竹兰居，给院子里的兰花和竹子浇水。这些事本应该是洒扫丫鬟的职责，她闲来无事，用此打发时间。
[EN] Lin Jue's page boy promised to notify her the moment his master returned. Reassured, Kuqiao returned to Zhulan Residence and watered the bamboo and orchids—normally sweeping maids' work, just something to pass the time.
[ZH] 刚给院子的植物浇完水，姑爷身边的小厮跑来报告：“苦荞姐姐，主君回来了。”
[EN] She had just finished watering when the page boy dashed over. "Sister Kuqiao, Master is back!"
[ZH] 苦荞谢过后，进屋走到床边，轻声说：“小姐，姑爷回来了。”
[EN] Thanking him, Kuqiao slipped inside to the bedside and whispered, "Miss, Son-in-law's back."
[ZH] 因老祖宗答应在和离时出面，千里寻太过兴奋，没有一挨枕头就睡着，一直躺着闭目养神。听到苦荞的声音，立刻爬起来，掀开床幔：“等的就是他，把治淤青的药给我拿来。”
[EN] Too wired over the Old Ancestor agreeing to appear, Qianli Xun hadn't fallen asleep, only resting with shut eyes. Hearing Kuqiao, she sat right up and parted the drapes. "Right on time! Hand me the bruise ointment."
[ZH] 千里寻穿好衣裳，简单整理好头发，并未簪发饰，拿好苦荞取出的药瓶，急急匆匆出了竹兰居，来到林觉居住的屋外，一把推开门。
[EN] Slipping on her clothes, smoothing her hair without pinning ornaments, and pocketing the vial Kuqiao handed her, she strode straight out of Zhulan Residence to Lin Jue's quarters and shoved the door open.
[ZH] 林觉刚在下人的伺候下换完衣裳，听到门响，习惯性地转过身。
[EN] Having just changed clothes with a servant's help, Lin Jue spun around at the noise.
[ZH] 千里寻看到他左眼周围的淤青还在，心想，多亏自己想得周到，及时买好药，否则和离时，她打的这一拳说不定会带来麻烦。
[EN] Spotting the bruise still circling his left eye, Qianli Xun smiled inwardly—good thing she had bought this ointment so he couldn't weaponize that punch at the divorce meeting.
[ZH] “娘子这么快就从朱府回来？难道一个长辈都没请到？如果没请到，安心待在林府，我不会亏待你。”
[EN] "Back from Zhu Manor so soon, Wife? Couldn't get a single elder to show up, huh? If not, settle down in Lin Manor—I won't mistreat you."
[ZH] “呵呵，让你失望，要是没请到，我也不会这么早回来。你恶人先告状，我不跟你计较，我这个人呢，善良，跟你不一样，想着我们毕竟夫妻一场，也算缘分，把你打得这么重，于心不忍，经过吉祥药房时，买了一瓶好药。”
[EN] "Ha, hate to burst your bubble—if I hadn't lined someone up, why would I come back early? You tattled to my dad first, fine, I won't hold it against you. I'm a softhearted person, unlike you. Figuring we were husband and wife once and I clocked you pretty hard, I felt bad—so I picked up some top-grade ointment passing Jixiang Pharmacy."
[ZH] 千里寻把药瓶扔到林觉怀里：“赶紧涂上，省得你去练兵，被下属知道是我打的，搞得你面子上挂不住。”
[EN] She tossed the vial straight into his chest. "Put it on right away, before your troops at the drill ground find out your wife gave you a shiner and cost you your face."
[ZH] 林觉本就爱着朱淑真，现在她变得跟以前完全不同，话又多，还给自己买药，他不禁浮想联翩。
[EN] Still smitten with Zhu Shuzhen, seeing her talk nonstop and even buy him medicine sent Lin Jue's imagination racing.
[ZH] “怎么还不涂？”千里寻希望他尽快涂上，想让那淤青快些消散，可林觉摸着怀里的药，愣在那里。
[EN] "Well? Put it on!" Eager to make that bruise vanish fast, Qianli Xun frowned as he just clutched the vial in a daze.
[ZH] 千里寻干脆过去又从他怀里拿了药瓶，打开，给手指上倒些粘稠的药膏，帮林觉涂在左眼周围的淤青之处。
[EN] Impatient, she stepped up, snatched the vial back, popped the lid, scooped thick salve onto her finger, and smeared it right over the bruise around his left eye.
[ZH] 一旁的下人见二人如此，偷乐着，悄悄走出屋外。
[EN] Snickering at the sight, the servants slipped out the door on quiet feet.
[ZH] 当千里寻涂完药膏，要收回手时，被林觉抓住手腕：“你为什么突然对我这么好？”
[EN] As Qianli Xun finished dabbing and pulled her hand back, Lin Jue caught her wrist. "Why are you suddenly so nice to me?"
[ZH] 千里寻抽回手：“我刚才不都说过？我这个人善良，你可别瞎想。”
[EN] Yanking her wrist free, she snorted. "Didn't I just tell you? Soft heart. Don't read into it."
[ZH] 她把药瓶放在桌上：“记得明日继续涂，别辜负我一番心意。”
[EN] Setting the vial on the table, she added, "Keep applying it tomorrow—don't waste my goodwill."
[ZH] 刚要走，想起重要的事，转过身，见林觉依然呆呆地看着她：“喂！和离的具体时间和地点不如现在定下来？”
[EN] Turning to go, she remembered the real reason she came and spun back to find him still staring dumbstruck. "Hey! Let's nail down the time and place for the mutual divorce right now."
[ZH] 听到“和离”二字，林觉清醒过来：“后日巳时在林府北院的会客厅。”
[EN] "Mutual divorce" snapped him cold awake. "Day after tomorrow, the Hour of the Snake, North Courtyard reception hall."
[ZH] “你也知道，我不是以前的朱淑真，什么都记不得，也不知道媒人是谁，和离时媒人由你负责请。”
[EN] "You know I'm not the old Zhu Shuzhen—remember nothing, don't even know who our matchmaker was. You're in charge of summoning her."
[ZH] “没问题。”
[EN] "No problem."
[ZH] “你那里共有几位长辈出面？”
[EN] "How many elders are coming on your side?"
[ZH] “八位，本来还有更多长辈要来，都被我拦住。”
[EN] "Eight. More wanted to come, had to hold them back."
[ZH] “什么？八位？”千里寻很担心，自己只请了一位，从数量上已败下阵来，“我才请一位，你请八位，不公平，能不能你也只请一位？”
[EN] "What? Eight?!" Qianli Xun's heart dipped—one elder against eight! "I've only got one, you're bringing eight—how's that fair? Can't you bring just one too?"
[ZH] “没办法，林家长辈实在太多，也太热情。”林觉走近，看着朱淑真，“要不别跟我和离？你虽然不是以前的朱淑真，看着却比以前更好，不如跟我好好过日子？”
[EN] "Can't help it—Lin clan elders are huge in number and eager to help." Stepping closer to study her face, Lin Jue murmured, "Why bother divorcing me? Even if you aren't the old Zhu Shuzhen, I like you even better now. Why not stay and build a life with me?"
[ZH] 千里寻也瞅着林觉，他并不是她喜欢的类型，其实她也不知道自己到底喜欢什么样的类型，反正不是林觉这种。再说她刚穿越而来，对方没给她留下多少好印象。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked him up and down. Definitely not her type—even if she wasn't sure what her type was, it sure wasn't Lin Jue, especially after their rocky start.
[ZH] “你别妄想，这婚我跟你是离定了。虽然我这里长辈只有一位，但我也会为之努力。”
[EN] "Save your breath—this divorce is locked in. Only one elder on my side or not, I'm fighting this through!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完，扬长而去。
[EN] With that, she marched out.
[ZH] 林觉冷笑道：“是你不要妄想才对！”
[EN] Lin Jue let out a cold laugh. "You're the one who should save your breath!"

#CH 18
[ZH] 第二天，千里寻写好纸条，派苦荞回朱府将纸条从佛堂的门缝塞进去。
[EN] The next day, Qianli Xun penned a note and sent Kuqiao back to Zhu Manor to slip it beneath the Buddhist Shrine's gate.
[ZH] 苦荞完成任务后，赶回林府，在门口遇到东方尘泽的小厮化雨。
[EN] Mission complete, Kuqiao hurried back to Lin Manor and ran right into Huayu, Dongfang Chenze's page boy, outside the front gate.
[ZH] 东方尘泽乃临安才子之一，长相俊朗，家境优渥，最喜欢组织文人才子与才女的聚会活动，几乎每个月要组织一次，朱淑真则是他的座上宾。
[EN] One of Lin'an's premier young scholars—handsome, wealthy, and fond of hosting literary gatherings—Dongfang Chenze threw a salon almost every month, with Zhu Shuzhen always an honored guest.
[ZH] 化雨看到苦荞，立刻送上请帖：“苦荞姐姐，我家公子邀请你家姑娘今日参加诗会，地点在西湖边的永泰茶楼。”
[EN] Spotting Kuqiao, Huayu presented an invitation card at once. "Sister Kuqiao, my young master invites your mistress to a poetry salon today at Yongtai Teahouse by West Lake."
[ZH] 苦荞接过请帖：“你家公子每月搞一次诗会，都不嫌烦，我家小姐近日忙得很，不一定能参加。”
[EN] Taking the card, Kuqiao remarked, "Your master throws salons every single month—doesn't he ever get tired? My Miss is swamped these days, might not make it."
[ZH] “姐姐只需把帖子送到，参不参加是你家姑娘的事，我的任务已完成，告辞。”
[EN] "My job's just delivering the card; whether she comes is up to her. Task done—farewell!"
[ZH] 化雨离开后，苦荞走进林府，来到竹兰居，告诉小姐纸条已顺利送到，然后把帖子给了。
[EN] After Huayu left, Kuqiao returned to Zhulan Residence, confirmed the note was delivered, and handed over the invitation.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，打开浏览一遍，问道：“东方尘泽是干什么的？”
[EN] Opening it for a quick glance, Qianli Xun asked, "Who's Dongfang Chenze?"
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“他是临安城有名的才子，最喜欢把文人聚集到一起，老爷每次举行诗会也邀请他，他就是这样认识小姐您，后来他有事没事也搞诗会，都会邀请小姐。”
[EN] "A famed young scholar in Lin'an who loves gathering poets," Kuqiao explained. "Master always invites him to our annual banquets—that's how he met you. Whenever he hosts salons of his own, he always invites you too."
[ZH] “我可愿意参加？”
[EN] "And I usually like going?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“甚是乐意。不过明日就要和离，您可否有心情？”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded. "Love it! Still, the divorce meeting is tomorrow—are you even in the mood?"
[ZH] 这几日为请长辈出面只在朱府和林府待着，千里寻觉得自己快要生锈，不如出去透透气，多认识些朋友也是好事。
[EN] Cooped up between Zhu Manor and Lin Manor for days chasing elders, Qianli Xun felt rusty. Getting fresh air and meeting people sounded great.
[ZH] “心情大大的有，既然每次我都乐意去，这次也去。快帮我收拾收拾，毕竟要去见人。”
[EN] "Plenty in the mood! If I always go, I'm going today. Help me fix up—can't show up looking messy."
[ZH] 苦荞伺候小姐打扮一番后，坐上马车，往西湖而去。
[EN] Once dressed and groomed by Kuqiao, she boarded a carriage bound for West Lake.
[ZH] 千里寻曾计划过要去西湖旅游，一直未抽出时间，没想到穿越后反而实现，而且还是一千多年前的西湖，她定要好好欣赏一番。
[EN] Qianli Xun had always meant to vacation at West Lake back in her own era and never found the time—now transmigration handed her West Lake a thousand years in the past! She intended to soak in every view.
[ZH] 到达西湖后，眼前的美景宛如一幅水墨画卷，湖水清澈而宁静，倒映着周围的山峦和树木，峰峦起伏，山清水秀，真是小桥流水人家，古柳清风野鸭。
[EN] Stepping out by the shore, she found an ink-wash painting come to life: calm, crystal waters mirroring rolling hills and lush trees, little bridges over clear streams, ancient willows swaying in a cool breeze, wild ducks gliding across ripples.
[ZH] 千里寻被美景吸引，驻足不前。正全神贯注欣赏时，传来一位女子的声音。
[EN] Transfixed, Qianli Xun stopped in her tracks—until a woman's voice broke her reverie.
[ZH] “幽栖居士又不是第一次来西湖，怎么今日会对这里的景色如此感兴趣？”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi, this is hardly your first trip to West Lake—why so captivated by the scenery today?"
[ZH] 千里寻并未有任何反应，只因她对幽栖居士的称谓并不熟悉，根本不知道这个声音是与她打招呼，继续沉浸在美景中。
[EN] Unfamiliar with the pen name Youqi Jushi, Qianli Xun didn't realize she was being addressed and kept gazing at the water.
[ZH] 直到苦荞悄悄在耳边说：“小姐，幽栖居士就是您，说话的是苗知瑜，她爹是临安通判。”
[EN] Not until Kuqiao whispered in her ear—"Miss, Youqi Jushi is you! That's Miao Zhiyu, daughter of the Vice-Prefect of Lin'an"—did it click.
[ZH] 千里寻才反应过来，朱淑真，号幽栖居士。她面带笑容，转过身，看到一位聘聘袅袅的古典美人，身上散发着书卷之气，就连她身后的丫鬟在这股书卷之气的影响下，也让人觉得气质独特。
[EN] Right: Zhu Shuzhen, styled Youqi Jushi. Turning with a smile, Qianli Xun beheld a slender classical beauty radiating scholarly poise—even the maid trailing behind her looked refined by association.
[ZH] 千里寻已知苗知瑜的名讳，不知如何称呼她，还是尽量避免：“我虽常来这里，但美景百看不厌，今儿景色更是宜人，忍不住欣赏一会儿。”
[EN] Knowing Miao Zhiyu's name yet unsure how Shuzhen addressed her, Qianli Xun played it safe and skipped titles. "I come here often, yet scenery this fine never grows old. Today's view is extra lovely—couldn't help pausing a while."
[ZH] “幽栖居士的额头怎么有伤？”
[EN] "What happened to your forehead, Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] “没什么，不小心碰的。”
[EN] "Nothing much—just a clumsy bump."
[ZH] 苗知瑜在内心笑道：“练兵官经常打你，以为我不知？又何必掩饰？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu smirked inwardly. Everybody knows the Training Officer beats you—why bother covering it up?
[ZH] “幽栖居士可是来参加东方公子的诗会？”
[EN] "Heading to Young Master Dongfang's poetry salon?"
[ZH] “没错。”千里寻尽量让自己的话语简洁些，对方才不容易发现她的不同。
[EN] "Yes." Keeping answers short lowered the odds of giving herself away.
[ZH] “不如你我一起过去？”
[EN] "Shall we walk together?"
[ZH] 千里寻点头同意，与苗知瑜并排走着，忍着不与对方说话。苗知瑜倒也安静。她们走了一会儿，面前出现一大片荷叶在水面铺开，许多花骨朵迎着微风轻轻摆动。千里寻心想，要是有手机，她绝对会在这里多拍几张美照。
[EN] Nodding, Qianli Xun fell into step beside her, holding her tongue. Miao Zhiyu kept quiet too. Soon a vast spread of lotus leaves carpeted the water ahead, dotted with lotus buds nodding in the light breeze. If only I had a phone, Qianli Xun thought—I'd snap fifty photos right here!
[ZH] 她瞧苗知瑜面对美景毫无感觉，慨叹再美的事物，只要人长期欣赏，也会逐渐失去美感。为了体现自己也是生活在美景边儿的人，她收回眼神，继续走着。
[EN] Noticing Miao Zhiyu didn't spare the view a glance, she mused how living next to beauty dulled the eye. To look like a local, she tore her eyes away and walked on.
[ZH] 她们拐过一条小径，进入西湖岸边的林子，再往里走一会儿，果然出现一座茶楼，有两层高。茶楼的正门前没有树木，只种着花花草草，视线越过花花草草，正是碧波静好的湖水。
[EN] Turning down a narrow path into a lakeside grove, they soon came upon a two-story teahouse. No trees blocked its front door—only flowerbeds opening straight onto tranquil blue ripples.
[ZH] “幽栖居士，请。”苗知瑜做出请的姿态，展现出大家闺秀的风范。
[EN] "After you, Youqi Jushi," Miao Zhiyu said with a graceful gesture worthy of a noble daughter.
[ZH] 千里寻岂能让朱淑真落下？要知道朱淑真也是真正的大家闺秀：“您请。”
[EN] Refusing to let Zhu Shuzhen lose on manners, Qianli Xun mirrored the bow. "After you."
[ZH] 二人相让着，并排进入茶楼。
[EN] Yielding back and forth, they stepped inside side by side.
[ZH] 茶楼的布置极有品位，每个茶桌上放有插花，周围挂着名人字画，一些茶客端着茶盏边品茶边欣赏字画。有的茶桌前女茶师正在展示茶艺；有的茶桌前，围坐着茶客，欣赏两位女茶师斗茶。
[EN] The teahouse dripped with taste: flower arrangements on every table, famous calligraphy and paintings lining the walls while guests sipped tea and studied the brushwork. At some tables female tea performers displayed whisking art; at others crowds gathered to watch two tea performers face off in a tea competition.
[ZH] 茶楼的伙计迎上来，显然认识她们：“两位姑娘可到了，东方公子正在楼上等二位。”
[EN] A waiter hurried up, recognizing them at once. "Welcome, young ladies! Young Master Dongfang is waiting upstairs."
[ZH] 苗知瑜问：“可有新挂的字画？”
[EN] "Any new calligraphy or paintings hung?" Miao Zhiyu asked.
[ZH] 伙计回道：“都是以前的，下个月会有新的挂上。”
[EN] "Same as before, miss—new pieces go up next month."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“幽栖居士，这些字画，你我都已鉴赏过，我们直接到楼上，听说东方公子得了易安居士的新诗，才邀请我们前来品鉴学习。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu turned to Qianli Xun. "We've seen all of these already, Youqi Jushi. Let's head right upstairs—I heard Young Master Dongfang obtained a new poem by Yi'an Jushi and invited us to study it."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“易安居士？难道是李清照？不知得了哪首新诗？不过这些诗对我来讲都是旧诗而已，嘿嘿！”
[EN] Yi'an Jushi? Li Qingzhao?! Qianli Xun grinned inside. Wonder which "new" poem he got—all classics to me, hehe!
[ZH] “既是易安居士的诗，是该好好品鉴一下。”
[EN] "If it's Yi'an Jushi's poem, we definitely must examine it."
[ZH] 在伙计的带领下，她们上了二楼。二楼是精致的雅间，每个雅间均用一句诗命名，有“桂华秋皎洁”，“燕草如碧丝”，“润物细无声”，“明月松间照”等等。
[EN] Following the waiter up to the second floor, they passed private booths each named after famous lines of verse: "Autumn Moonlight Bright on Cassia," "Yan Grass Like Green Silk," "Moistening Things Without a Sound," "Bright Moon Shines Between Pines," and more.
[ZH] 茶楼的伙计直接推开写着“露浓花瘦”四个字的包间。
[EN] The waiter pushed open a booth labeled "Thick Dew, Slender Blossoms."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“这四个字应出自李清照一首词。”却一时想不起到底来自哪首。
[EN] That phrase comes from a Li Qingzhao lyric, Qianli Xun thought, though the exact title slipped her mind.
[ZH] 二人进入包间，看到三位气度不凡，样貌不俗的公子分别坐在各自的桌前品茶。他们见朱淑真和苗知瑜进来，都站起来，笑脸相迎，其中一位说：“幽栖居士，苗姑娘，你们可来了，让我们好等。”
[EN] Inside sat three poised, handsome young gentlemen sipping tea at separate low tables. Rising with warm smiles as Zhu Shuzhen and Miao Zhiyu entered, one called out, "Youqi Jushi, Lady Miao—at last! You kept us waiting!"
[ZH] 千里寻终于知道大家只称苗知瑜为“苗姑娘”，而这位说话的公子，她猜应是东道主东方尘泽，其他两位叫不上名字，还是等会儿悄悄问问苦荞。
[EN] Aha—everyone called her "Lady Miao," and this host must be Dongfang Chenze. She'd ask Kuqiao about the other two in a second.
[ZH] 千里寻行礼道：“辛苦三位公子久等。”
[EN] Qianli Xun bowed. "Apologies for keeping you three gentlemen waiting."
[ZH] 苗知瑜也跟着行礼。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu bowed beside her.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“两位不必多礼，尽快落坐。”
[EN] "No need for ceremony, ladies—please sit," Dongfang Chenze urged.
[ZH] 三位公子看到朱淑真额头的伤，尽管伤口快要愈合，却依然清晰可见。三人交换眼色，也都纷纷落座。
[EN] All three gentlemen spotted the healing cut on Zhu Shuzhen's forehead, traded silent glances, and resumed their seats.
[ZH] 这时茶楼的一位女茶师带着丫鬟进来，走到茶桌跟前，开始做茶。在场所有人保持沉默，大家仔细欣赏茶师做茶的过程。
[EN] Just then a female tea performer entered with an assistant, took her place at the tea table, and began whisking tea. Everyone fell silent to watch.
[ZH] 千里寻见过苦荞做茶，比起茶师来，苦荞做茶的手法显得粗笨，只见这位茶师动作不紧不慢，优美自然，令人赏心悦目。
[EN] Compared to Kuqiao's workmanlike moves, this performer's hands flowed with unhurried elegance—a feast for the eyes.
[ZH] 千里寻欣赏了一会儿，向苦荞勾勾手指。苦荞领会，凑到小姐跟前。千里寻小声问：“中间那位公子应是东方尘泽，另外两位是谁？你可知道？”
[EN] After watching a moment, Qianli Xun crooked a finger at Kuqiao. Kuqiao leaned in close as her mistress whispered, "Center one's Dongfang Chenze—who are the other two?"
[ZH] 苦荞经常陪朱淑真参加此等聚会，自然清楚，压低声音说：“左边那位是经略使的儿子曹班，右边那位是提举的儿子安明轩。”
[EN] A veteran of these salons, Kuqiao whispered back, "Left is Cao Ban, son of Military Commissioner Cao; right is An Mingxuan, son of Intendant An."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，迅速把名字记在心间，又悄悄问：“以前我如何称呼他们？”
[EN] Locking both names into memory, Qianli Xun murmured, "How do I usually address them?"
[ZH] “小姐一直对他们以公子相称。”苦荞答完，继续站好。
[EN] "Always as Young Master." Answer given, Kuqiao stepped back into place.
[ZH] 千里寻总算安下心来，继续欣赏茶艺表演，只见茶师用手中的细长的竹板在茶盏中画着，待她停手后，身后的丫鬟将茶盏端来给千里寻放在桌上：“幽栖居士，请用。”
