[EN] Relaxed now, Qianli Xun watched the performer draw delicate lines across the foam with a slender bamboo slip. Once done, her assistant lifted the bowl and set it before Qianli Xun. "Please enjoy, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，心想：“连茶楼的丫鬟都知道朱淑真，看来她是此处的常客。”
[EN] Even the teahouse assistant knows her—Shuzhen really is a regular here, Qianli Xun mused, nodding thanks.
[ZH] 她低头瞧着这盏茶，只见茶沫上画着柳枝和一轮明月，简单的几笔，使得茶汤充满意境之美，千里寻不忍品尝，慨叹一千多年前人们的生活比科技发达的时代更有品位。柳枝与明月让她想起地震的场景，也想起申简辰，他到底被神柳的力量带去了哪里？
[EN] Glancing down, she caught her breath: delicate strokes on the tea foam formed willow branches curving around a bright moon. Too lovely to drink! People a thousand years ago lived with far more elegance than modern folks. Willows and moon brought back the earthquake—and Shen Jianchen. Where had the Divine Willow carried him?
[ZH] 正想着，丫鬟又端着茶盏放在苗知瑜的桌上：“苗姑娘，请用茶。”
[EN] The assistant set the next bowl before Miao Zhiyu. "Your tea, Lady Miao."
[ZH] 女茶师起身，向大家行完礼，带着丫鬟离开。
[EN] Bowing to the company, the tea performer withdrew with her assistant.
[ZH] 东方尘泽开口说：“两位，请用茶。”
[EN] "Please drink, ladies," Dongfang Chenze invited.
[ZH] 苗知瑜端起茶盏品了一口，缓缓放下后说：“听说东方公子得了易安居士的新诗，还请尽快拿出来，让我等鉴赏。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu took a dainty sip, set her bowl down, and spoke: "I hear Young Master Dongfang obtained a new poem by Yi'an Jushi—please bring it out for us to admire!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“等幽栖居士品口茶，我们再品诗不迟。”
[EN] "Let Youqi Jushi taste her tea first before we turn to poetry," Dongfang Chenze suggested.
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻只能端起茶盏，目睹着充满意境的画面被她一口一口啜入，然后咽入腹内。
[EN] On cue, Qianli Xun lifted the bowl and gulped that poetic masterpiece straight down sip after big sip.
[ZH] 当她放下茶盏，发现所有人不解地盯着她。千里寻立即明白过来，原来她喝猛了，赶快解释道：“一路过来，有些口渴。”
[EN] Setting down the empty bowl, she met four bewildered stares. Realizing she had drunk way too fast, she covered with a sheepish smile. "Parched from the ride over."
[ZH] 东方尘泽笑道：“我让茶师给幽栖居士再做一盏。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze chuckled. "I'll have the performer whisk you another bowl."
[ZH] “不了不了，尽够尽够。”千里寻面露尴尬地阻止道，她不想因为自己耽误大家品诗。
[EN] "No, no, plenty already!" Flushing slightly, she waved him off—no holding up the poetry session on her account.
[ZH] 苗知瑜催道：“既如此，还是尽快鉴赏易安居士的诗吧？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu urged again, "Then let's see Yi'an Jushi's poem right away!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽吩咐一旁的小厮化雨：“把易安居士的诗挂在墙上。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze waved to his page boy Huayu. "Hang Yi'an Jushi's poem on the wall."
[ZH] 化雨将手中的两个卷轴展开，并排在墙上挂好。
[EN] Huayu unrolled two scrolls and hung them side by side.
[ZH] 所有人都向那诗瞧去。
[EN] Every pair of eyes turned to the wall.
[ZH] 千里寻在心里乐道：“原来是李清照的感怀诗，右边卷轴上是这首诗的序文。我对此诗倒也熟悉，应该不会在他们面前出丑。”
[EN] Qianli Xun cheered inwardly: Li Qingzhao's poem "Reflections"! With the prose preface on the right scroll! I know this one—no chance of embarrassing myself!
[ZH] 东方尘泽介绍道：“诸位，这首诗是易安居士去年所做，不算新诗，但得来不易，多亏乔文仙乔兄认识和易安居士交好之人，才得此诗，以书信的方式转达给我，我才有机会请大家品评鉴赏。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze introduced it: "Friends, Yi'an Jushi wrote this last year—not brand-new, yet hard to come by. Thanks to Brother Qiao Wenxian knowing a close friend of hers, he secured a copy and sent it to me by letter so we could all study it together."
[ZH] 苗知瑜听到乔文仙的名讳，脸上出现一片红晕：“乔公子人在临安，还是在汴梁？”
[EN] At the name Qiao Wenxian, rosy color bloomed across Miao Zhiyu's cheeks. "Is Young Master Qiao in Lin'an, or still in Bianliang?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“乔兄要是在临安，我定会请他一起前来鉴赏。自从幽栖居士嫁给练兵官后，乔兄就去了汴梁，一直未回临安，听说住在他外甥仪王的府中。”
[EN] "Were Brother Qiao in Lin'an, I'd certainly have invited him today," Dongfang Chenze replied. "Ever since Youqi Jushi married the Training Officer, Brother Qiao left for Bianliang and hasn't come back—word is he's staying at his nephew Prince Yi's Manor."
[ZH] 三位公子看向朱淑真，研究着她的表情。
[EN] All three young gentlemen turned straight toward Zhu Shuzhen to gauge her reaction.
[ZH] 被三位公子盯着，搞得千里寻很不好意思，她回以微笑。想不通东方尘泽为何专门强调朱淑真嫁人后那位叫乔文仙的公子去了汴梁，难道这二人之间有什么关系？反正三位公子看她的眼神很是奇怪。
[EN] Flustered under three probing stares, Qianli Xun offered an awkward smile. Why did Dongfang Chenze go out of his way to link Qiao Wenxian leaving town with Zhu Shuzhen's wedding? What was between those two? Those looks felt awfully loaded.

#CH 19
[ZH] 曹班见朱淑真很不自在，看来他们三人如此瞧着她实在不妥，便用话语打破尴尬。
[EN] Noticing Zhu Shuzhen's discomfort and realizing their staring crossed a line, Cao Ban broke the awkward hush.
[ZH] “幽栖居士带伤而来，看来练兵官又对你动手，实在不行到知府大人面前告他一状，看他还敢再有下次！”
[EN] "Arriving with an injury again, Youqi Jushi—looks like the Training Officer laid hands on you once more! If it comes to it, report him straight to the Prefect—see if he dares pull that again!"
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻立即明白，朱淑真被家暴之事并对在坐诸位隐瞒，倒令她轻松许多：“诸位放心，不会再有下次，明日我会与林觉和离。”
[EN] Realizing her friends already knew about the domestic abuse took a load off Qianli Xun's mind. "Don't worry, everyone—there won't be a next time. Tomorrow I'm getting a mutual divorce from Lin Jue."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“早该如此，当日你嫁给练兵官时，我等都替你惋惜。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu nodded. "High time! We all grieved for you the day you married that Training Officer."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“幽栖居士若能离开练兵官，我等都会替你高兴。”
[EN] An Mingxuan agreed, "If you break free of him, Youqi Jushi, we'll all celebrate for you!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽神秘地笑道：“有一人要是听说此事，肯定比在座诸位都高兴。”
[EN] A sly grin curved Dongfang Chenze's lips. "And one person out there will celebrate harder than all of us combined."
[ZH] 三位公子又互换眼神，露出意味深长的笑容。
[EN] The three men traded knowing smiles.
[ZH] 千里寻搞不明白笑容背后的深意，毕竟朱淑真的记忆她一丝一毫都没有，也懒得琢磨，提醒道：“易安居士的诗在此，大家还是尽快品评。”
[EN] Clueless about the inside joke—having zero of Shuzhen's memories—Qianli Xun couldn't care less and steered them back: "Yi'an Jushi's poem is right in front of us—let's get to discussing it."
[ZH] 易安居士的诗是乔文仙想方设法得来，苗知瑜一心在诗上，仿佛字里行间也有乔文仙的气息一般。至于三位公子的笑容，她并未关注。
[EN] Because Qiao Wenxian had gone out of his way to obtain this poem, Miao Zhiyu had eyes for the verse alone, half imagining his presence between the lines, oblivious to the gentlemen's knowing looks.
[ZH] 听到朱淑真的提醒，苗知瑜第一个发表意见：“从诗的序文能够看出易安居士人在莱州，与德甫先生夫妻团聚，本应高兴才对，可字里行间让我感受到孤独和落寞，难道他们夫妻之间的感情出现问题？如果真是这样，岂不更令我等女子伤感？”
[EN] Prompted by Zhu Shuzhen, Miao Zhiyu spoke first: "From the preface we see Yi'an Jushi is in Laizhou, reunited with Master Defu—which ought to bring joy. Yet every line radiates solitude and chill. Has strain crept into their marriage? If even they grow estranged, how heartbreaking for women like us!"
[ZH] 安明轩好奇地问：“为何会令女子伤感？”
[EN] Curious, An Mingxuan asked, "Why heartbreaking for women?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜回道：“身为女子最是羡慕易安居士，既嫁给喜欢之人，又与丈夫志趣相投，如果这样还会孤独落寞，像我等待字闺中的女子真不知道以后该嫁什么样的人。相比较这首诗，我还是喜欢‘莫道不销魂，帘卷西风，人比黄花瘦。’起码这几句还能让我感受到易安居士甜蜜的思念之情。”说完苗知瑜一脸怅然。
[EN] "Every woman envies Yi'an Jushi—marrying the man she loves and sharing his mind and soul," Miao Zhiyu replied. "If even she feels lonely, unmarried daughters like me have no idea what sort of husband to hope for! Compared to this, I still prefer: 'Don't say she's not heartbroken, / The west wind lifts the blinds, / She's more wasted than the yellow flowers.' [TN: Translated by Ronald Egan] At least those lines carry the sweetness of longing." A wistful shadow lingered on her face.
[ZH] 曹班接着说：“我最喜诗中用典，只有如此，方可展现我的才华。易安居士的诗，正中我意，请允许我在诸位面前献丑，解解其中典故。”
[EN] Cao Ban spoke next: "Nothing delights me more than historical allusions—where else can I show off my reading? Yi'an Jushi's poem suits me down to the ground! Allow me to embarrass myself by unpacking her allusions."
[ZH] 东方尘泽笑道：“你啊，就怕别人不知道你读的书多，好吧，我们大度，机会让给你。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze laughed. "Always itching to prove how many volumes you've read! Fine—we'll be generous and yield the floor."
[ZH] 曹班开始侃侃而谈：“此诗有三处用典，第一处‘公路可怜合至此’，袁术字公路，此句来源于《三国志·袁术传》注引《吴书》：袁术兵败后，饥渴交至，叹曰：‘袁术至于此乎！’；第二处‘青州从事孔方兄’，‘青州从事’指美酒，《世说新语·术解》有云：‘桓公有主簿，善别酒，辄令先尝。是谓青州从事，恶者谓平原督邮。’‘孔方兄’指钱财，鲁褒《钱神论》中说道：‘亲之如兄，字曰孔方’；第三处‘乌有先生子虚子’，则是司马相如《子虚赋》中虚拟的人物。”
[EN] Cao Ban launched right in: "Three allusions anchor this poem. First, 'Pity Gonglu brought to such a pass'—Yuan Shu's courtesy name was Gonglu, drawn from the Book of Wu cited in the Records of the Three Kingdoms: after defeat, racked by hunger and thirst, Yuan Shu sighed, 'Has Yuan Shu truly come to this?' Second, 'Qingzhou Inspector and Brother Kong Fang'—'Qingzhou Inspector' means fine wine; A New Account of the Tales of the World records that Duke Huan had a clerk skilled at judging wine who called fine vintages 'Qingzhou Inspector' and poor ones 'Pingyuan Postmaster.' 'Brother Kong Fang' means money, from Lu Bao's Discourse on the God of Money: 'Cherished as a brother, styled Kong Fang.' Third, 'Sir No-such and Sir Fantasy' are fictional figures from Sima Xiangru's Rhapsody on Sir Fantasy."
[ZH] 曹班讲完，众人无不佩服。
[EN] Everyone listened in open admiration.
[ZH] 千里寻更佩服得五体投地，她觉得自己此刻像一个无知的小学生，慨叹这家伙怎么懂得这么多，更慨叹写这首诗的人学富五车。还是悠着点，听听他们说些什么，再做定论。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt blown away—like an ignorant schoolkid next to a walking encyclopedia, marveling both at Cao Ban's memory and Li Qingzhao's vast erudition. Better play it smart, hear everyone out first, then speak.
[ZH] 曹班道了句：“献丑了！”
[EN] "I've embarrassed myself!" Cao Ban bowed in mock modesty.
[ZH] 安明轩说：“曹兄果然厉害，引经据典的能力无人能及。”
[EN] An Mingxuan praised him: "Nobody matches Brother Cao at unpacking classical allusions!"
[ZH] 安明轩注意到苗知瑜虽被曹班的解析折服，却依然沉浸在感伤之中，安慰道：“苗姑娘不必伤感，德甫先生任莱州知州，公务繁忙，多多少少会冷落易安居士。我以为，如果仅把这首诗看作书写闺中女子的孤独和落寞，则是小觑了易安居士的才华。我读此诗，品味到她表面写孤独和落寞，以及对德甫先生的不满与失落，实则暗藏对朝廷官员的不满，集中在这两句‘青州从事孔方兄，终日纷纷喜生事’，不就是朝廷官员日常生活的写照吗？”
[EN] Seeing Miao Zhiyu still steeped in gloom despite Cao Ban's erudition, An Mingxuan offered comfort: "Don't be downcast, Lady Miao. Master Defu serves as Prefect of Laizhou—busy with official duties, he's bound to neglect Yi'an Jushi at times. In my view, reading this solely as a wife's lonely lament underestimates her scope. Beneath the surface loneliness and frustration with her husband lies a veiled jab at court officials—look at 'Qingzhou Inspector and Brother Kong Fang, bustling all day stirring trouble.' Isn't that the spitting image of officialdom's daily life?"
[ZH] 安明轩的解释令苗知瑜释然许多。
[EN] An Mingxuan's reading lifted much of the cloud from Miao Zhiyu's brow.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“安兄慎言，在座五位的父亲虽是地方官员，也都是朝廷所封，你如此解析，被父辈们知道，可是要挨板子的，呵呵，再说如此解析易安居士的诗，会将她置于众矢之的，慎言！慎言！”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze warned with a laugh, "Watch your tongue, Brother An! Every father of the five of us holds an imperial post—let them hear that reading and you'll catch a beating! Worse, spreading such an interpretation would turn Yi'an Jushi into a target. Careful, careful!"
[ZH] 安明轩谦虚道：“东方兄所言甚是，我的理解仅代表个人，也仅限于我们五人知道，恳请大家不要传扬出去。”
[EN] An Mingxuan bowed in quick humility. "Well spoken, Brother Dongfang. Just a personal fancy—strictly between the five of us. Please keep it inside these walls!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽批评道：“亏我们五人常在一起论诗作词，竟如此不信任大家，尤其不信任两位才女，该罚，罚你为幽栖居士做盏茶来。”
[EN] "We debate poetry together all the time, and you don't trust us—least of all our two talented ladies?" Dongfang Chenze teased. "Penalty! Whisk a bowl of tea for Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] 安明轩站起来，向大家拱手致歉：“该罚，该罚，我这就为幽栖居士做茶。”说完走到做茶桌前，开始忙碌。
[EN] An Mingxuan stood and cupped his hands. "Guilty as charged! I'll whisk tea for Youqi Jushi at once." Stepping to the tea table, he set to work.
[ZH] 曹班赶紧把自己的茶喝完，要求道：“罚你也给我做一盏。”
[EN] Cao Ban downed his own tea fast and chimed in, "Penalty includes a bowl for me too!"
[ZH] “放心，我给你们一人做一盏，我做的茶口感比不上茶楼的茶师，你们可别嫌弃。”安明轩开始认真做茶，边做边用心听其他人如何品鉴此诗。
[EN] "Relax, one bowl each! Don't complain if my whisking falls short of the teahouse master's." Focusing on his utensils, An Mingxuan kept listening closely to the discussion.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“易安居士这首诗的妙处在于她将两种生活对比着来写，一种是‘青州从事孔方兄，终日纷纷喜生事’，另一种则是‘作诗谢绝聊闭门，燕寝凝香有佳思’。前者带来的结果只能是‘寒窗败几无书史’，后者则是‘静中吾乃得至交，乌有先生子虚子’，两相对比，警示世人应该选择什么样的生活。不管别人如何，易安居士只会选择‘作诗谢绝聊闭门，燕寝凝香有佳思’，所以在这首诗中我们能感受到易安居士的生活志趣。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze gave his view: "The genius of Yi'an Jushi's poem lies in contrasting two worlds: 'Qingzhou Inspector and Brother Kong Fang, bustling all day stirring trouble,' set against 'Declining guests with verse behind closed doors, fragrant quiet breeds fine thoughts.' The former leaves only 'cold window, broken desk, stripped of histories and books'; the latter yields 'in stillness I gain true friends—Sir No-such and Sir Fantasy.' Placed side by side, they challenge the world to choose what life is worth living. Whatever others chase, Yi'an Jushi chooses quiet study behind closed doors—revealing her true spirit."
[ZH] 三位公子与苗知瑜都已谈完，只剩下千里寻。别人已从诗中品出各种滋味，她听了无不赞叹，也在心中组织着语言，还不断问自己，该怎么说才能不辜负朱淑真的才华？
[EN] All three men and Miao Zhiyu had spoken; every gaze now turned toward Qianli Xun. Impressed by their insights, she scrambled to assemble her own take—how to speak without shaming Zhu Shuzhen's name?
[ZH] 这时听到东方尘泽的声音：“幽栖居士有何见解？你可是易安居士忠实的追随者。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze prompted, "What are your thoughts, Youqi Jushi? You're Yi'an Jushi's most devoted admirer."
[ZH] 朱淑真竟然是李清照忠实的追随者？更不能给朱淑真丢脸。千里寻越如此想，心里越紧张。她努力稳住，使得自己看上去平淡自如：“各位见解独到，才华横溢，令淑真佩服。大家都品到诗中的精华和韵味所在，以至于我都不知该说些什么……”
[EN] A devoted Li Qingzhao fangirl, huh? Even more reason not to drop the ball! Heart pounding, she forced a calm, poised smile. "Every one of your insights is sharp and brilliant—Shuzhen stands in awe. You've already uncovered every subtle layer of the poem, leaving me hard-pressed to add a word..."
[ZH] 夸赞谦虚式的开场白效果不错，四位友人面露笑容，浑身轻松下来。那么接下来她千里寻无论说什么，哪怕稍有不到位之处，他们应该不会与她计较。
[EN] Flattery and modesty worked like magic; all four beamed and relaxed. Even if her commentary fell slightly short now, nobody would nitpick.
[ZH] “既然受邀参加诗会，总得发表发表意见，我在大家的基础之上说说个人的浅薄之见。”
[EN] "Still, invited to a salon, I owe a few humble thoughts built upon yours."
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，缓步走到卷轴跟前，她这么做无非是想给自己更多思考的时间。她在卷轴跟前稍立片刻，转身面向大家。
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun strolled over to the hanging scrolls—buying herself precious seconds to think—stood before them a beat, and turned back to face the room.
[ZH] “别人写诗，诗眼均在诗中，易安居士这首诗诗眼却在序中。‘独坐一室，平生所见，皆不在目前。’一个‘独’字道尽所有，既有女子独处一室没有丈夫陪伴的孤寂，亦有摩诘居士‘独坐幽篁里’品性高洁的孤独。‘平生所见’到底是什么？可以是易安居士以前幸福的婚姻生活，亦可是美好的人物或事物，令人回味无穷，无比向往，只可惜‘皆不在目前’，让人失落惆怅……”
[EN] "Most poets hide the poetic core inside the verse itself; Yi'an Jushi placed hers in the preface: 'Sitting alone in one room, all I have known in life lies absent before my eyes.' That single word 'alone' holds everything—both a woman's quiet ache without her husband beside her, and the lofty solitude of Wang Wei's 'Alone I sit in dark bamboo.' [TN: Translated by Burton Watson] What is 'all I have known in life'? Past marital bliss, cherished people, finer days—all longed for, all 'absent before my eyes,' breeding sorrow and loss..."
[ZH] 千里寻还未说完，传来苗知瑜轻声叹息。
[EN] Before she finished, a soft sigh escaped Miao Zhiyu.
[ZH] 千里寻心中喜悦：“竟然讲到她心里，不愁不被她认可。”
[EN] Score! Qianli Xun celebrated inside. Hit her right in the heart—she's hooked!
[ZH] 千里寻放松许多，越放松，话越多。
[EN] Her nerves melted away, words flowing free.
[ZH] “理解完诗眼，再来看诗句，便能够把握其中意味。‘寒窗败几无书史，公路可怜合至此’是写居住环境，一个‘寒’字既让人感受到秋风萧瑟，也写出环境并不符合诗人的志趣，‘可怜’二字，到底是说什么可怜？接着往下看，‘至此’引出下面两句，我才恍然大悟，原来‘可怜’说的并不是缺少物资，而是精神的贫乏。与‘平生所见，皆不在目前’遥相呼应，印证了诗眼中的‘独’字定然是品性高洁的孤独。易安居士的诗，让我想起了佛教中有言‘如露亦如电，应作如是观’，世间男女情爱，以及名利如同露水，再美好，太阳一出来会消失不见，只有精神的志趣爱好，以及高洁的人格品德才是永恒。”
[EN] "Grasp that core, and the lines unlock themselves. 'Cold window, broken desk, stripped of histories and books; pity Gonglu brought to such a pass' paints her surroundings—'cold' evokes autumn chill and a setting mismatched to her spirit. What does 'pity' mourn? Read the next lines introduced by 'such a pass' and it clicks: she isn't mourning material poverty, she's mourning spiritual emptiness! That echoes 'all I have known in life lies absent before my eyes,' proving the 'alone' in her preface is the solitude of a noble mind. Her poem brings to mind the Buddhist saying: 'like dew, or again like lightning—in this way should you view them.' [TN: Translated by Burton Watson] Worldly love, fame, and wealth vanish like morning dew the moment the sun rises; only spiritual pursuits and unblemished character endure."
[ZH] 千里寻终于讲完，深深吐口气，总算没有白白顶着朱淑真的名头，她从容地走回桌前坐下。
[EN] Finished at last, Qianli Xun exhaled in quiet relief—she hadn't disgraced Zhu Shuzhen's crown—and strolled back to her seat.
[ZH] 东方尘泽第一个赞道：“幽栖居士的品鉴令人佩服，自愧不如，自愧不如。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze spoke first: "Brilliant critique, Youqi Jushi! I fall far short—far short!"
[ZH] 曹班也说：“自愧不如！”
[EN] Cao Ban echoed, "Far short indeed!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜只顾沉浸在个人的伤感中，一言不发。
[EN] Lost in her own wistful thoughts, Miao Zhiyu sat silent.
[ZH] 安明轩已将茶做好，先将第一盏端给朱淑真：“用这盏茶表示我的赞赏之情，幽栖居士，请用。”然后又把茶盏给其他人一一端了。
[EN] An Mingxuan finished whisking and set the first bowl before Zhu Shuzhen. "This bowl carries my admiration—please drink, Youqi Jushi." Then he served the others one by one.
[ZH] 大家都拿起茶盏刚品了一口新茶，还没来得及放下，听到从楼下传来的声音：“本公子今儿偏让她来陪我！看你们能把我怎样！”接着听到楼下乱成一片。
[EN] Everyone lifted their bowls for a single sip—when a rough shout erupted from downstairs: "Today I say she's keeping me company! Let's see what any of you can do about it!" Chaos crashed out below.

#CH 20
[ZH] 安明轩做完茶已回到他的位置，放下茶盏：“陈家的纨绔子弟隔一段时间不出来闹一回，就不舒服，今儿竟然闹到永泰茶楼，真是稀奇。”
[EN] Back at his seat, An Mingxuan set down his bowl. "That Chen family dandy can't go two weeks without causing a scene—odd he dragged it into Yongtai Teahouse today."
[ZH] 曹班接着说：“也是，他可是勾栏瓦肆，赌场妓馆的常客，没想到今儿竟然有雅兴来茶楼。”
[EN] Cao Ban chimed in, "Right—he practically lives in Goulan Wasi, gambling dens, and brothels. Since when does he have the taste for a teahouse?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜十分不屑：“哼！这等纨绔子弟，就应该被官府抓起来在狱中关些时日，也就老实了。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sneered in disgust. "Hmph! A dandy like that belongs locked up in the yamen jail for a spell to teach him manners."
[ZH] 千里寻很是不解：“你们说的纨绔子弟到底是谁？”
[EN] Confused, Qianli Xun asked, "Who is this dandy you're talking about?"
[ZH] 四人惊讶地瞧着朱淑真。
[EN] All four stared at her in astonishment.
[ZH] 千里寻立刻意识到这个问题出卖了自己，朱淑真定然认识这位纨绔子弟。
[EN] Oops—she'd just given herself away! Zhu Shuzhen clearly knew this guy.
[ZH] 东方尘泽问：“幽栖居士不是认得他吗？”
[EN] "Don't you recognize him, Youqi Jushi?" Dongfang Chenze asked.
[ZH] 千里寻努力掩饰：“这个世道，纨绔子弟太多，我怎么知道你们说的是哪位？”刚说完，又意识到这话只不过是此地无银三百两而已。
[EN] Qianli Xun tried to bluff: "Plenty of dandies roam the streets—how should I know which one you mean?" The words barely left her lips before she realized how hollow the excuse sounded.
[ZH] 苦荞见情势不对，赶紧替小姐解释：“三位公子，苗姑娘，你们也看到我家小姐额头上有伤，因为这伤，有些人和事她记不得，还请见谅。”
[EN] Sensing danger, Kuqiao jumped in fast: "Young masters, Lady Miao—you saw the cut on Miss's forehead. That blow left her forgetting quite a few faces and events. Please forgive her."
[ZH] 四位又露出惊讶的表情。
[EN] Fresh astonishment rippled across all four faces.
[ZH] 东方尘泽帮大家询问：“幽栖居士可还记得我们？”
[EN] Speaking for the group, Dongfang Chenze asked, "Do you still remember us, Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“记得一些，记得一些，要不怎么跑来参加诗会？”
[EN] "Bits and pieces—why else would I show up for the salon?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽继续说：“那便好，那便好。既然幽栖居士记不得陈家公子，我给幽栖居士说说。陈家是临安首富，陈公子名叫陈浩宇，不学无术，整日不是流连青楼就是赌坊，仗着家里有钱，还经常带着一群小弟到处欺负人，今儿又欺负到茶楼里来。”
[EN] "Good, good! Since you don't recall Young Master Chen, let me fill you in. The Chens are the richest family in Lin'an. Their son Chen Haoyu is an idle good-for-nothing who haunts brothels and gambling houses, throws his wealth around, and drags thugs along to bully folks—now bringing his mess into a teahouse."
[ZH] 千里寻平日最讨厌这种人：“待我去教训教训他！”
[EN] People like that made Qianli Xun's blood boil. "Let me go teach him a lesson!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽阻止道：“陈家与朱家联姻，陈浩宇的姐姐正是幽栖居士的嫂嫂，你去教训他，小心他在你哥嫂面前告状，再说陈浩宇有些拳脚功夫，带的那帮人也是，你是弱女子，去了只有吃亏的份儿。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze stopped her at once. "The Chens are your in-laws—Chen Haoyu's older sister is your sister-in-law! Cross him and he'll tattle to your brother and sister-in-law. Worse, he and his cronies know rough brawling—a delicate lady like you would only get hurt!"
[ZH] 说话间，茶楼的伙计进来，满面愁容，求道：“东方公子，曹公子，安公子，你们都是临安城有名的公子，麻烦你们帮帮茶楼，陈公子见女茶师长得美貌，非要调 戏，调 戏不成，在楼下大闹起来。”
[EN] Right then the teahouse waiter rushed in, wringing his hands. "Young Master Dongfang, Young Master Cao, Young Master An—you're respected gentlemen of Lin'an, please save our teahouse! Young Master Chen took a fancy to our female tea performer, tried forcing himself on her, and when she resisted, started smashing the place downstairs!"
[ZH] 没等三位公子说话，千里寻站起来：“我去！”
[EN] Before the three men could open their mouths, Qianli Xun shot to her feet. "I'm going!"
[ZH] 三位公子与苗知瑜只能跟着出了雅间，他们从二楼向下瞧去。
[EN] The three scholars and Miao Zhiyu had no choice but to trail out of the booth and peer down over the second-floor railing.
[ZH] 只见楼下的茶客已不见踪影，到处一片狼藉。桌上的插花被扔在地上；墙上的字画有些被撕烂，有些扔在地上，被陈家公子带的人踩在脚下；做茶的桌子被掀翻，茶粉和茶具也都撒落一地。
[EN] Every customer downstairs had fled, leaving utter wreckage behind. Flower arrangements lay smashed across the floor; wall scrolls hung ripped or trampled under thugs' boots; the tea table lay overturned, tea powder and utensils scattered everywhere.
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐在一个椅子上，将一位女茶师搂坐在腿上，紧紧箍住。
[EN] Seated on a chair, Chen Haoyu had pinned a female tea performer across his lap, gripping her tight.
[ZH] 千里寻仔细瞧陈浩宇，长得倒人模狗样，做下的事与那长相实在不匹配。
[EN] Qianli Xun sized him up—handsome enough on the outside, rotten to the core inside.
[ZH] 只听陈浩宇调戏道：“因为你整个茶楼都遭殃，你就从了我，只要陪我一晚上，本公子不会亏待你。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu leered at the struggling woman. "The whole teahouse is paying for your stubbornness! Give in already—spend one night with me and I'll treat you right."
[ZH] 女茶师说：“你休想！谁不知道被你玩弄，只有被卖到青楼的下场。”
[EN] "In your dreams!" the tea performer spat. "Everyone knows girls you toy with end up sold to brothels!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇大声命令道：“继续给我砸！”话音刚落，只见他带的七八个小弟又在茶楼内破坏起来。
[EN] "Keep smashing!" Chen Haoyu barked. Seven or eight cronies resumed wrecking tables and chairs.
[ZH] 千里寻实在看不下去，大喊道：“放开她！”
[EN] Unable to stomach another second, Qianli Xun shouted from above: "Let her go!"
[ZH] 所有人向楼上瞧去。
[EN] Every head snapped upstairs.
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“我当是谁，原来是亲家姑娘，叫什么来着，对朱……朱淑真，快回去，好好做你的诗，别瞎胡闹，看在朱陈两家是亲家，我不跟你计较。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu broke into a mocking grin. "Well, well—who do we have here? My dear in-law! What was your name again... oh right, Zhu—Zhu Shuzhen! Go back upstairs and write your little poems, don't meddle! Out of respect for our families being in-laws, I'll let you off."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“可我想跟你计较！”
[EN] "Too bad I'm not letting you off!" Qianli Xun snapped back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇冷笑道：“你用什么和我计较？难道想和本公子比做诗？只可惜本公子最瞧不上那玩意儿，也只有你身边那三位公子把诗词当个宝，他们呀，没别的本事，不得已才附庸风雅，吟诗作词，实在不像男人！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu laughed cold. "And how do you plan to take me on? A poetry duel? Spare me—I have zero use for that junk! Only those three softies beside you treat verse like treasure because they can't do anything else—affecting literary airs and reciting lyrics like a bunch of sissies!"
[ZH] 曹班被气到，不由骂道：“陈浩宇！你整日欺负良家妇女，连猪狗都不如！”
[EN] Stung, Cao Ban cursed back: "Chen Haoyu! Preying on respectable women every day—you're lower than pigs and dogs!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“还号称临安有名的公子，张口就说猪狗，实在不文雅，有损你的美名。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smirked. "Calls himself a famed scholar of Lin'an and talks of pigs and dogs—not very refined! Mind your precious reputation!"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“陈浩宇，这是茶楼，不是你常去的烟花柳巷之地，女茶师身怀茶艺绝技，不是青楼女子，你还是放了她，否则我告到官府，有你好果子吃。”
[EN] An Mingxuan stepped forward. "Chen Haoyu, this is a teahouse, not your usual brothel haunt! This tea performer is a skilled artisan, not a courtesan. Release her now, or I report this to the yamen and watch you pay!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇冷笑道：“哼！你爹不过是个提举，什么时候轮到你在这里大呼小喝，有本事你去告，本公子根本不怕！”
[EN] "Hmph! Your dad's just an Intendant—who gave you leave to bark at me?" Chen Haoyu sneered. "Go ahead and report me! Think I'm scared?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽问：“你不怕官府，可怕官家？”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze spoke cool and steady: "Not scared of the local yamen—what about His Majesty?"
[ZH] “官家远在汴梁，哪有时间管我喜欢个把女人？东方公子的问题实在可笑。”陈浩宇将嘴巴靠近女茶师。
[EN] "His Majesty sits way off in Bianliang—think he has time to care if I fancy a girl or two? Don't make me laugh, Dongfang Chenze!" Chen Haoyu leaned his face toward the tea performer.
[ZH] 女茶师厌恶地躲闪到一边。
[EN] She twisted away in revulsion.
[ZH] 东方尘泽继续说：“你刚说我们吟诗作词不像男人，此话已得罪官家，当今的官家不仅字写得好，诗词歌赋无一不通，如若我给汴梁的朋友写封信，让他送到仪王手中，再由仪王呈给官家，到时你们陈家会因你口出狂言而遭殃。”
[EN] "You just sneered that men who compose poetry aren't real men," Dongfang Chenze pressed on. "That insults His Majesty himself—our sovereign excels at calligraphy, poetry, and lyrics alike! One letter from me to my friend in Bianliang, passed to Prince Yi and straight to His Majesty, and your entire Chen clan pays for your loose tongue!"
[ZH] “东方尘泽，你说的朋友该不会是乔文仙吧？他那外甥仪王是个病秧子，在官家面前根本说不上话，你以为我不知？少在这里吓唬我。”陈浩宇的样子十分嚣张。
[EN] "Talking about Qiao Wenxian? His nephew Prince Yi is a sickly prince who has zero pull before His Majesty! Think I don't know? Quit trying to bluff me!" Chen Haoyu swaggered even harder.
[ZH] 千里寻已看不下去：“陈浩宇！有本事吃老娘一拳！”她用轻功从二楼跳到一楼，一拳打在陈浩宇的脸上。
[EN] Done listening, Qianli Xun snapped: "Chen Haoyu! Eat my fist!" Using light-foot skill, she vaulted clean over the second-floor railing, landed below, and drove a punch square into Chen Haoyu's face!
[ZH] 陈浩宇被打疼，手一松，女茶师迅速逃离。陈浩宇用手捂着半边脸：“吆喝！你什么时候这么厉害？”
[EN] Yelping in pain, he loosened his grip; the tea performer tore free and bolted. Clutching half his face, Chen Haoyu stared. "Whoa! Since when can you hit like that?!"
[ZH] 楼上认识朱淑真的人也都张大嘴巴，怎么都无法相信眼前的人是幽栖居士。
[EN] Upstairs, every friend who knew Zhu Shuzhen stood with jaws on the floor—was that really Youqi Jushi?!
[ZH] 陈浩宇吩咐身边的小弟：“给我上！立刻把她给我打趴下！”
[EN] "Get her!" Chen Haoyu barked at his thugs. "Beat her flat right now!"
[ZH] 七八个小弟上来，围成一圈。千里寻站在中间，警惕地看着他们。当他们伸出拳头一起打过来时，千里寻身手敏捷，巧妙躲开，来到圈外，将一人打倒在地，趁其他人还没反应过来，迅速用连环拳和脚上功夫，将那些小弟全部打倒在地。他们一个个倒在地上直喊：“疼死我了！”
[EN] Seven or eight toughs closed in around Qianli Xun. Calm and sharp, she tracked every stance. The instant fists flew from every angle, she slipped outside their ring with agile footwork, dropped one man flat, and before the rest could pivot, unleashed a blur of rapid punches and kicks that put every last thug on the floor groaning, "Ow! It hurts!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状，亲自上阵，没打两下，被千里寻扭转臂膀：“以后老娘再见你欺负人，见一次打一次！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu jumped in himself—two moves later, Qianli Xun twisted his arm behind his back. "Catch you bullying anyone again, and I beat you every single time!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇嘴硬道：“你欺负我，小心我告诉朱景元和我姐，让他们替我收拾你。”
[EN] Still talking tough, Chen Haoyu hissed, "Dare touch me? Watch me tell Zhu Jingyuan and my sister to deal with you!"
[ZH] “哼！我哥哥和嫂嫂最通情达理，只怕他们知道后会帮我收拾你！”千里寻又将陈浩宇的手臂拧紧。
[EN] "Hmph! My brother and sister-in-law are fair people—they'll probably help me thrash you!" She wrenched his arm higher.
[ZH] 陈浩宇疼得不行，直喊：“哎吆！姑奶奶饶命！”
[EN] Agony shot up his shoulder. "Ow! Mercy, Granny, mercy!"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“想让我饶命容易，先给女茶师道歉，再给茶楼赔钱，还有，往后不准来永泰茶楼！”
[EN] "Easy! First, apologize to the tea performer. Second, pay for the wrecked teahouse. Third, never set foot in Yongtai Teahouse again!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇没想到朱淑真的力气如此大，他难以反抗，好汉不吃眼前亏，只能答应：“好，我答应你。”
[EN] Outmatched by her iron grip and knowing better than to fight a losing battle, Chen Haoyu folded. "Fine, fine—I agree!"
[ZH] 千里寻松开手，一把将陈浩宇推到女茶师面前：“赶紧道歉！否则老娘的拳头打得就是你！”
[EN] Letting go, Qianli Xun shoved him straight toward the tea performer. "Apologize now—or my fist talks next!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇害怕，只能对女茶师说：“对不起，以后我不会再调戏你。”
[EN] Cowed, Chen Haoyu muttered, "I'm sorry. I won't harass you again."
[ZH] 女茶师根本不想理会陈浩宇，厌恶地扭过头。
[EN] Disgusted, the tea performer turned her head away without a glance.
[ZH] 陈浩宇从身上掏出一袋银子放在一旁没有被踢翻的茶桌上：“朱淑真，你看清楚，本公子把赔茶楼的银子放在这里了。”说完就要走。
[EN] Pulling a pouch of silver from his belt, Chen Haoyu tossed it onto an upright table. "See that, Zhu Shuzhen? Payment for the teahouse right there." He turned to leave.
[ZH] “慢着！”千里寻阻止道，“银子够不够我还不知道，等验完再走。”
[EN] "Hold it!" Qianli Xun halted him. "Haven't checked if it's enough yet—stay put till they count!"
[ZH] 茶楼的伙计过去把钱袋打开，大概数数：“够，还多了。”
[EN] A waiter opened the pouch and did a quick count. "Enough—more than enough!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“陈家是临安首富，最不缺的就是银子，你如此不信任我，就是对我最大的不敬。看在你功夫了得，本公子不跟你计较。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu forced a cocky laugh. "The Chens are the richest clan in Lin'an—silver's the one thing we're never short on! Doubting my word is an insult. Seeing how tough your fists are, I'll let it slide."
[ZH] 然后对所有小弟说：“还愣着干什么！走！这破地方以后本公子不会再来！”
[EN] Glaring at his bruised thugs, he barked, "What are you lying around for? Move! Never coming back to this dump anyway!"
[ZH] 刚走到门口，陈浩宇又回过头：“朱淑真，以后可别栽到我手里。”说完扬长而去。
[EN] At the doorway he looked back over his shoulder. "Watch yourself, Zhu Shuzhen—don't ever fall into my hands!" With that, he swaggered off.
[ZH] 女茶师、茶楼的掌柜、伙计纷纷前来感谢。
[EN] The tea performer, shopkeeper, and waiters crowded around pouring out gratitude.
[ZH] 楼上的公子和苗知瑜也都从楼梯走下来，用陌生的目光盯着朱淑真。
[EN] Upstairs, the three scholars and Miao Zhiyu came down the steps, staring at Zhu Shuzhen as if meeting a stranger.
[ZH] 苗知瑜先问：“幽栖居士什么时候悄悄学了功夫？以前从未见过你这样。你既如此厉害，为何多次带伤而来，难道练兵官比你还厉害？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu asked first, "Youqi Jushi, when on earth did you learn martial arts? Never saw anything like that before! If you're this skilled, why did you always come to salons covered in bruises—is the Training Officer even stronger?"
[ZH] 千里寻不知如何回答，只能保持沉默，尴尬地笑着。
[EN] At a loss for an excuse, Qianli Xun kept silent behind an awkward grin.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“幽栖居士深藏不露，不仅诗词写得好，功夫也了得，令我等男儿刮目相看，难怪乔兄他……”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze stepped in with a smile. "Youqi Jushi hides her depths well—gifted in verse and deadly in combat, putting us men to shame! No wonder Brother Qiao..."
[ZH] 东方尘泽话中有话，千里寻无法猜出，倒是苗知瑜关心不已：“乔公子怎么了？”
[EN] His trailing words puzzled Qianli Xun, yet Miao Zhiyu caught fire at once: "What about Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽不便明说：“没什么，今日诗会也算尽兴，茶楼遭此一劫也需修整，我们还是走吧。”
[EN] Choosing discretion, Dongfang Chenze waved it off. "Nothing. Today's salon was thrilling enough, and the teahouse needs time to tidy up—let's call it a day."
[ZH] 大家带着丫鬟和小厮离开茶楼，沿途又欣赏一会儿西湖美景，在西湖外道别后各自归家。
[EN] Leading their maids and pages out of the teahouse, they strolled a bit longer along scenic West Lake before parting ways outside the lakeside path.

#CH 21
[ZH] 回去的路上，千里寻为今日的表现感到满意。看得出，朱淑真的四位朋友虽觉得她与以往不同，可并未认为她不是以前的朱淑真。肯定是品鉴李清照的诗时，她展现出能够与朱淑真相媲美的才华。想到这些，千里寻的脸上浮现出笑容。
[EN] Riding home, Qianli Xun felt pleased with how today went. Zhu Shuzhen's four friends saw differences, sure, yet none suspected she wasn't the real Zhu Shuzhen—credit to her Li Qingzhao critique living up to the poet's fame. A satisfied smile tugged at her lips.
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐开心，说道：“小姐看上去心情不错。”
[EN] Noticing her bright mood, Kuqiao remarked, "You look real happy, Miss."
[ZH] “那是当然。”千里寻突然想知道朱淑真与朋友在一起时到底如何表现，“苦荞，我以前见到三位公子和苗姑娘时爱不爱说话？该不会还是高冷的样子？”
[EN] "Course I am!" Curious how the real Zhu Shuzhen behaved among peers, Qianli Xun asked, "Kuqiao, did I talk much around those three gentlemen and Lady Miao before? Or did I play the ice queen there too?"
[ZH] 苦荞笑道：“您对不懂诗词的人才会表现出高冷，在家人面前话也不多，只有见到东方公子他们才会春风满面，侃侃而谈。”
[EN] Kuqiao chuckled. "You only acted frosty around folks who didn't understand poetry—and stayed quiet around family. Around Young Master Dongfang and the others, you beamed and talked nonstop!"
[ZH] “哦，原来我是这样的人，难怪今日他们不觉得我有什么不同。”
[EN] "Aha—so that's how I was! No wonder they didn't bat an eye."
[ZH] “怎么不觉得？您从二楼跳到一楼打陈公子，东方公子他们惊得个个眼睛瞪得老大。不过还好，他们只觉得小姐您深藏不露而已。”
[EN] "Didn't bat an eye? When you jumped off the second floor and beat up Young Master Chen, their eyes popped out of their skulls! Lucky they just figured you were hiding your talents."
[ZH] “今日见见朋友，我突然明白一个道理，我就是我，没有必要想着还像以前一样，该怎样就怎样，把我自己最真实的一面展现在大家面前。”
[EN] "Meeting friends today taught me one thing: I am who I am. Done bending over backward pretending to be the old Zhu Shuzhen—from now on I show the world the real me!"
[ZH] 千里寻决定，以后再也不去想如何将自己伪装成以前的朱淑真，她要让以前的朱淑真成为现在的自己。
[EN] She made up her mind: stop squeezing herself into Zhu Shuzhen's mold, and let Zhu Shuzhen become Qianli Xun instead.
[ZH] 马车到达林府门口，千里寻想起腐肌膏，是该涂抹的时候，还是尽快回竹兰居。
[EN] Pulling up outside Lin Manor, Qianli Xun remembered the Corrosive Paste—time to apply it back at Zhulan Residence.
[ZH] 刚走进西院，被林觉堵住。
[EN] The moment she stepped into the West Courtyard, Lin Jue blocked her path.
[ZH] “娘子这是去了哪里？”
[EN] "Where have you been, Wife?"
[ZH] 林觉左眼周围的淤青已消失，千里寻心里暗喜：“从吉祥药房买的药真不错。”
[EN] The bruise around his left eye had vanished clean. Qianli Xun smiled inside: That Jixiang Pharmacy salve really delivers!
[ZH] 林觉继续追问：“怎么不说话？”
[EN] "Why aren't you answering?" Lin Jue pressed.
[ZH] 千里寻轻松地答道：“我去参加诗会，与几位朋友聚聚。”
[EN] "Went to a poetry salon to catch up with friends," she replied breezy as air.
[ZH] “哼！恐怕是以参加诗会为由，与所谓的才子行苟且之事吧？”林觉不满中带着些许恨意。
[EN] "Hmph! Probably using 'poetry salon' as a cover to carry on illicit affairs with those fancy scholars!" Bitter resentment edged his voice.
[ZH] “林觉！你狗嘴里吐不出象牙，尽想些龌龊之事，老娘懒得理你！”千里寻被气到，打算回竹兰居，向一旁拐去。
[EN] "Lin Jue! Nothing decent ever comes out of your filthy mouth—your head is full of gutter trash! Not wasting breath on you!" Fuming, she sidestepped toward Zhulan Residence.
[ZH] 林觉快速冲上前，挡住她的去路：“我都警告过你，不要去参加诗会，为何不听？”
[EN] Lin Jue darted ahead to bar her way. "I already warned you to stay away from poetry salons—why didn't you listen?"
[ZH] 千里寻很不耐烦：“林觉，你个大男人，婆婆妈妈的！我们都要和离，管那么多干嘛？再说即使没离，你也管不着我，我想干什么就干什么！”
[EN] Exasperation flared. "Lin Jue, you're a grown man—quit nagging like an old busybody! We're getting divorced anyway, why do you care? Even if we weren't, you don't own me—I go where I please!"
[ZH] 林觉很没面子，伸出手向朱淑真打去。
[EN] Stung in his pride, Lin Jue swung a hand to strike her.
[ZH] 被千里寻一拳挡住：“怎么？还想家暴我？也不掂量掂量你有几斤几两，就想在我面前动手？”
[EN] Qianli Xun caught his blow cold with one fist. "What, trying domestic violence again? Didn't even weigh yourself before swinging at me?"
[ZH] 林觉被一股力量挡着，深知自己根本打不过眼前这个不一样的娘子，只好放下手，语气尽量放缓和。
[EN] Stopped dead by her force, knowing full well he stood no chance against this reborn wife, Lin Jue lowered his hand and softened his tone.
[ZH] “有一次我练兵，士兵休息时，无意中听到他们悄悄议论你，说你跟东方尘泽不清不楚，还跟乔文仙眉来眼去，还说你们……，反正很难听，我是你夫君，是个男人，听到外面人如此议论你，能不生气吗？后来警告你不要去参加东方尘泽组织的诗会，你倒是老实一回，今日又跑去，难道想让整个临安城的人都看我笑话？”
[EN] "Once during a drill break, I overheard my soldiers whispering about you—saying you and Dongfang Chenze had something going on, that you and Qiao Wenxian exchanged flirtatious glances, saying all kinds of ugly things! I'm your husband, a man—how could I not lose my temper hearing folks talk like that? After I warned you off Dongfang Chenze's salons, you stayed home once, and now you go right back! Trying to make me the laughingstock of all Lin'an?"
[ZH] 千里寻见林觉态度缓和下来，也不再生气，她不想把关系闹得太僵，省得影响明日和离之事，语重心长地说：“你这人啊，最大的问题就是对自己的娘子缺乏最基本的信任，以后娶了妻，可不能再这样。为证明我以前的清白，等下次他们再邀请我时带你一起去，让你亲眼目睹我们都做些什么。不过，估计那时你我已经和离，没关系，婚离了，还是朋友，我以朋友的名义带你去。”
[EN] Seeing him cool down, Qianli Xun let her anger ebb too—no point souring things right before tomorrow's divorce. Speaking earnest and plain, she said, "Your biggest problem is having zero basic trust in your own wife. When you remarry, drop that habit. Just to clear my old name, next time they invite me I'll bring you along so you see with your own eyes what we actually do. Granted, by then we'll be divorced—no big deal, we can still be friends, and I'll bring you along as a friend."
[ZH] 林觉立刻傻眼。以前遇到此类事情，朱淑真从不做任何解释，更不愿意带他去参加诗会，更确切地说，她从心里瞧不起他，只因他是个武夫，不懂诗词歌赋，更不懂花前月下。而眼前的朱淑真却主动要求带他去诗会，如果她早如此，也许他们之间的关系会有所不同。
[EN] Lin Jue stood stunned. Whenever this came up before, Zhu Shuzhen never offered one word of explanation, never invited him along—down deep she despised him as an uncultured brute who knew nothing of verse or romance. Now she offered to take him herself! Had she acted like this from day one, might everything have turned out different?
[ZH] “我才不稀罕去那种场合，乔文仙那帮人又酸又臭，跟我实在合不来。”
[EN] "Who wants to sit through that boring stuff? Qiao Wenxian and his crowd are pompous snobs—we'd never mix."
[ZH] 千里寻将林觉上下瞧瞧：“从我第一眼见你，就觉得你根骨不错，不如和离后，你拜我为师，我教你千里拳。”
[EN] Sizing him up from head to toe, Qianli Xun remarked, "First time I saw you, I noticed you've got solid bones for martial arts. Tell you what—after we divorce, become my apprentice and I'll teach you Qianli Fist!"
[ZH] 这话更让林觉傻眼，也让他更加确定以前的朱淑真已被家暴而亡。林觉突然感到害怕，不由后退两步，转过身，迈着沉重的步伐离开。
[EN] That froze Lin Jue colder still—clinching his dread that the old Zhu Shuzhen really had died by his hand. A chill crawled up his spine; taking two steps back, he spun around and stalked off with heavy strides.
[ZH] 千里寻看着林觉的背影，感到奇怪：“喂！怎么走了？难道不愿意当我的徒弟？”
[EN] Watching him leave, Qianli Xun blinked. "Hey! Where you going? Don't want to be my apprentice?"
[ZH] 林觉没有回答，出了院子，消失在千里寻面前。
[EN] Without answering, Lin Jue cleared the yard and vanished.
[ZH] 千里寻自语道：“这人真奇怪。”
[EN] "Weird guy," Qianli Xun muttered.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“是小姐奇怪才对，要收姑爷为徒弟，我听后都觉得诧异，更别说姑爷。”
[EN] "You're the weird one, Miss," Kuqiao piped up. "Offering to take Son-in-law as your apprentice—even my jaw dropped, let alone his!"
[ZH] 这一日，听到最多的名字就是乔文仙，他到底与朱淑真什么关系？千里寻决定搞搞清楚。
[EN] One name had echoed all day: Qiao Wenxian. What exactly was his tie to Zhu Shuzhen? Time to find out.
[ZH] “苦荞，随我回竹兰居，你家小姐我有事要问你。”
[EN] "Back to Zhulan Residence, Kuqiao—got questions for you."
[ZH] 苦荞很听话，跟着小姐往回走。
[EN] Kuqiao trailed obedient behind her.
[ZH] 到了竹兰居，千里寻换了居家的衣裳后，开始打听：“老听人提到乔文仙，他到底是谁？”
[EN] Inside, after changing into lounge clothes, Qianli Xun asked straight out, "Everyone keeps mentioning Qiao Wenxian. Who is he?"
[ZH] 苦荞把小姐换下的衣裳放好后说：“他是乔家公子，是乔老爷纳的妾室所生，因是独苗，未来要继承乔家产业，所以养在夫人处，上面四个姐姐，大姐进了宫，成为乔贵妃，可惜命不好，生下儿子后就殁了。”
[EN] Folding the outer robes neatly, Kuqiao answered, "Young Master of the Qiao family—born to Master Qiao's concubine, yet as the sole male heir slated to carry on the Qiao estate, he was raised by Madam Qiao. He has four older sisters; the eldest entered the palace and became Consort Qiao—pity her fate was short, she passed away right after giving birth to a son."
[ZH] “你怎么知道得这么清楚？”千里寻好奇，苦荞一个丫鬟怎么对乔家如此了解。
[EN] "How do you know all that?" Qianli Xun wondered how a maid had the full scoop.
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“乔家的事在临安已不是秘密，很多人都知道，只因他家出了一位乔贵妃，大家关注得多，议论得多而已，我也是在朱府跟其他丫鬟聊天儿时才知道。”
[EN] "Nothing secret about Qiao family business in Lin'an—everyone knows because they produced Consort Qiao, so folks talk about them nonstop. I heard it chatting with other maids back at Zhu Manor."
[ZH] “乔文仙是不是经常被东方公子邀请参加诗会？”
[EN] "Does Young Master Dongfang invite Qiao Wenxian to salons often?"
[ZH] “是这样，不过他从来不做诗写词，只喜欢品鉴小姐您的诗词，而且是品鉴小姐诗词的绝佳之人，所以小姐视他为知己。”
[EN] "All the time! Though he never writes poems himself—just loves critiquing yours, and nobody reads your verse deeper than him, so you considered him a true confidant."
[ZH] “知己？难道我喜欢他？”
[EN] "Confidant? Did I love him?"
[ZH] “小姐那时心里装着徐公子，容不下他人。”
[EN] "Back then your heart belonged to Young Master Xu—nobody else could fit."
[ZH] 千里寻叹道：“都怪爹娘不早将实情说明，让你家小姐深陷其中，要搁现在，徐公子倒贴，我都不一定能看得上。真怀疑你家小姐以前的眼光，怎么能看上徐公子那样的人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed. "Blame Mother and Father for hiding the truth and letting you pine for nothing! Put Young Master Xu in front of me today, I wouldn't take him for free! Wonder what your old self saw in him."
[ZH] “其实，徐公子人不错，既长得风流倜傥，又才华横溢，与小姐有共同语言，加之青梅竹马，小姐自然喜欢，只是他太听从父母的话，往好了来讲是孝顺，就是对小姐太不公。”
[EN] "Young Master Xu wasn't bad, really—handsome, brilliant, spoke your language, plus childhood sweethearts. Only flaw was obeying his parents blindly—filial to them, cruel to you."
[ZH] “不谈他了，他已经是过去式。还是想想明日和离之事，给我把腐肌膏拿来。”
[EN] "Enough about him—ancient history! Time to focus on tomorrow's divorce. Hand me the Corrosive Paste."
[ZH] 苦荞取来腐肌膏：“真不知道小姐是怎么想的，非要伤害自己。”
[EN] Fetching the vial, Kuqiao frowned. "Still can't fathom why you're hurting yourself on purpose, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻接过，走到铜镜前坐下，倒出一些药膏涂在正在愈合的伤口上：“明天你就知道，去找林觉把治淤青的药拿回来。”
[EN] Taking a seat at the bronze mirror, Qianli Xun tipped salve onto her healing cut. "You'll see tomorrow. Now go fetch the bruise ointment back from Lin Jue."
[ZH] “是，小姐。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss."
[ZH] 苦荞出了竹兰居，来到林觉住的屋外，小心向里面说：“姑爷，小姐让我来拿治淤青的药。”
[EN] Outside Lin Jue's quarters, Kuqiao called in politely: "Son-in-law, Miss sent me to retrieve the bruise medicine."
[ZH] 过了一会儿，出来一名下人：“主君让你进去。”
[EN] Moments later a servant stepped out. "Master says come inside."
[ZH] 苦荞走进去，发现姑爷坐在桌前，拿着一本书看，她纳闷：“姑爷怎么看起书来？”
[EN] Stepping in, Kuqiao saw Lin Jue sitting at the table reading a book—Wait, Son-in-law reading?!
[ZH] 林觉见苦荞进来，把书扣在桌上。苦荞清晰地看到“王右丞诗集”五个字，她从小跟着小姐，不仅认得几个字，而且知道王右丞是唐代著名诗人，他的诗小姐甚是喜爱，没想到姑爷竟然读他的诗。
[EN] Spotting her, Lin Jue flipped the book facedown. Growing up beside Zhu Shuzhen, Kuqiao recognized characters well enough to read "Collected Poems of Wang Youcheng" on the cover—her mistress's beloved Tang poet! Never dreamed Son-in-law would crack it open!
[ZH] “药瓶在桌上，自己拿。”
[EN] "Vial's on the side table. Grab it."
[ZH] 苦荞到一旁的桌上拿了药瓶，行完礼，要离开，被姑爷用话语拦住：“你等会儿，我有事问你。”
[EN] Retrieving the bottle, Kuqiao bowed to leave, only for Lin Jue to halt her: "Hold on—a question."
[ZH] 苦荞站住：“姑爷请问。”
[EN] She paused. "Yes, Son-in-law?"
[ZH] “你家小姐跟以前不一样，难道朱家人不怨我？”
[EN] "With your Miss acting nothing like before, doesn't the Zhu family resent me?"
[ZH] 苦荞心想，姑爷还有脸问！看我怎么怼你：“怎能不怨？只不过重生之说太过离奇，让人难以置信，再说现在的小姐性情虽大变，可长得跟以前一模一样，即使到官府告姑爷打死我家小姐，也找不到证据，朱家也只能吃哑巴亏。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought, The nerve of him asking! Watch me jab back: "Of course they resent you! Only tales of rebirth sound too wild for folks to believe, and since Miss still wears the exact same face despite changing inside, even if they dragged you to the yamen for beating her to death, there's zero proof—so the Zhus have to swallow it in silence."
[ZH] 林觉心里还是有些害怕，毕竟岳父大人是临安知府。还好他已提前做好功课，在朱淑真之前拜访了岳父大人，跪着哭诉婚后的经历，说自己打朱淑真是因为她不顾丈夫颜面，在外与临安才子勾勾搭搭，因多次听到他人议论，才不得已出手打她。至于找商妓，是因为朱淑真不愿与他圆房所致。总之，岳父大人因他哭诉，又说得在理，并未怪罪。
[EN] Unease pricked Lin Jue—his father-in-law was Prefect of Lin'an, after all. Lucky he had moved first, visiting Zhu Xiyan ahead of Shuzhen to kneel and weep out his side: claiming he only hit her because she flirted with Lin'an scholars and dragged his name through barracks gossip, and only saw courtesans because she refused his bed. Convinced by the tears and excuses, Prefect Zhu hadn't blamed him.
[ZH] “你喜欢你家小姐现在的样子，还是从前？”
[EN] "Do you like your Miss better now, or how she was before?"
[ZH] 苦荞根本不愿在姑爷面前承认：“只要是我家小姐，不管过去还是现在我都喜欢。”
[EN] Refusing to hand him an inch, Kuqiao snapped, "Past or present, she's my Miss—I love her either way."
[ZH] “可我更喜欢现在的她。又会功夫，跟我算是同道中人，关键是还跟我讲话，不像以前那么冷漠。现在的她可喜欢那些附庸风雅的玩意儿？”
[EN] "Well, I like her better now. Knows martial arts like one of my own kind, and actually talks to me instead of freezing me out. Does she still care for those fancy word games?"
[ZH] 苦荞用不满的语气说：“令姑爷失望，小姐的诗词才华依然还在，今日在诗会上品评易安居士的诗，得到所有人赞赏。”
[EN] Kuqiao answered with sharp relish: "Hate to disappoint you, Son-in-law—Miss's poetic gift hasn't faded a bit! At today's salon, her reading of Yi'an Jushi's poem won raves from everyone!"
[ZH] 林觉露出失落的表情，瞧了瞧放在桌上的诗集，他研究半天，研究不出所以然，也提不起一点儿兴致：“真想不通，喜欢这玩意儿有什么用？既不能吃又不能喝。你走吧，告诉你家小姐，和离没那么容易。”
[EN] Disappointment crossed Lin Jue's face as he glanced down at the poetry book on his desk; staring at it half the night hadn't made heads or tails of it or sparked an ounce of interest. "Don't see the point of that stuff—can't eat it, can't drink it. Go on, and tell your Miss: mutual divorce won't come easy."
[ZH] 苦荞退出，在门外小声对着屋内怼道：“我家小姐现在厉害着呢！不好欺负！”
[EN] Stepping outside, Kuqiao muttered back toward the closed door: "My Miss is tough now—nobody pushes her around!"
[ZH] 回到竹兰居，苦荞把林觉说的话原原本本告诉小姐。
[EN] Back at Zhulan Residence, she relayed every word to Qianli Xun.
[ZH] ﻿千里寻只关注林觉说的“和离没那么容易”，心想，自己该做的都做了，明日的结果只能交给老天。
[EN] Qianli Xun zeroed in on one line—"mutual divorce won't come easy." She had done everything she could; tomorrow was up to Heaven.

#CH 22
[ZH] 一晚上，千里寻被额头的伤口搞得甚是难受，没想到腐肌膏如此厉害。为了睡个好觉，只能将另一瓶药涂些，才渐渐入睡。
[EN] All night long, the wound on Qianli Xun's forehead burned like fire—Corrosive Paste packed a real punch. Just to catch some sleep, she had to dab on a bit of the healing salve before dozing off.
[ZH] 一大早醒来，照镜子一瞧，伤口好许多。千里寻又拿出腐肌膏，给伤口再涂些。用过早饭，挑了一件漂亮的衣裳，收拾好，带上苦荞往北院而去。
[EN] First thing in the morning, checking the mirror showed the cut much calmer—so she dabbed fresh Corrosive Paste right back on! After breakfast she picked out a fine dress, tidied up, and set off with Kuqiao toward the North Courtyard.
[ZH] 走到半路，苦荞见小姐站住，知道她又找不着方向，只好走到前面引路。
[EN] Halfway there she halted again, lost as ever; Kuqiao stepped ahead to guide.
[ZH] 林府与朱府比起来，景色差很多，看上去都没怎么认真设计修整过，只因下人来来往往，才在原来的土地上踩出一条路而已，要是一下雨，肯定泥泞难走。
[EN] Lin Manor paled beside Zhu Manor's landscaped beauty—barely designed at all, with dirt paths worn down purely by servants' feet, bound to turn to mud in the first rain.
[ZH] 当路越来越宽敞而且好走时，千里寻看到许多房屋，应该是北院。
[EN] Where the lane widened into neat stone paving, rows of buildings rose ahead—the North Courtyard.
[ZH] 快要走到院门口，一个下人迎上来：“少夫人，林家人已到齐，都在里面等着。”
[EN] Near the gate, a servant hurried up. "Young Madam, the Lin family has all arrived and awaits inside."
[ZH] 只因“少夫人”的称谓很不喜欢，千里寻未搭理那个下人，径直走进去，迎面应该是会客厅，她已看到里边坐着一些人。
[EN] Detesting the title "Young Madam," Qianli Xun brushed right past him into the yard, heading straight toward the reception hall where figures already sat visible inside.
[ZH] 千里寻踩着院子里的石砖一步一步向里走去，既激动又担忧。激动的是，今日之后她很有可能摆脱掉对她来讲毫无意义的婚姻。担忧的是如果不成功，她该怎么办？
[EN] Stepping across the courtyard flagstones, excitement and nerves churned in her gut—excitement at winning freedom from a meaningless marriage today, nerves over what she'd do if it fell through.
[ZH] 到达会客厅的门口，她抬脚踏进去，只觉里面不小。迎面的墙上挂着一幅牡丹图，画上的牡丹一朵一朵簇拥着。前面放着长形的桌案，并不宽，桌案上摆放着瓷器和香炉，香炉空着，应该只是装饰屋子而已。
[EN] Crossing the threshold, she took in the spacious hall. A painting of clustered peonies dominated the facing wall above a narrow long table holding porcelain vases and an empty incense burner kept purely for show.
[ZH] 长桌案的前面摆着方形桌子，两边放着椅子，左边坐着一位长辈，用手捋着胡须，人看上去温文尔雅。
[EN] Before it stood a square table flanked by seats of honor. On the left sat an elder stroking his beard with refined poise.
[ZH] 千里寻的目光离开正位，向左边的偏位瞧去，前面坐着四人，后面坐着四人。林觉正坐在前一排离正位最近的椅子上。
[EN] Shifting her gaze to the left side-seats, she counted two rows of four—eight seats in all, with Lin Jue sitting front row closest to the head seat.
[ZH] 林觉见朱淑真额头的伤口变得比昨日严重许多，猜到她定是动了手脚。她为离开他，竟然连伤害自己的事情都做得出来，林觉的内心有说不出的滋味。
[EN] Spotting how much worse Zhu Shuzhen's forehead cut looked than yesterday, Lin Jue knew at once she had tampered with it. Willing to hurt herself just to get away from him—a bitter twist tightened his chest.
[ZH] 千里寻看向右边，老祖宗还未到，四把椅子，三个空着，只有一个上坐着一位妇人。她以为是朱家哪位长辈知道自己的事主动前来帮忙，激动地上前拜见：“感谢长辈帮我出面，不知怎么称呼？”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked to the right side: the Old Ancestor hadn't arrived yet. Four chairs—three empty, one occupied by a middle-aged woman. Thinking some sympathetic Zhu elder had shown up on her own, Qianli Xun hurried over with a grateful bow. "Thank you for coming to support me, Elder! How should I address you?"
[ZH] 妇人笑道：“少夫人不认得我了？我可是您跟练兵官的媒人，您称呼我闫媒婆。朱家长辈的位置都空着，我借坐一下。”
[EN] The woman chuckled. "Don't recognize me, Young Madam? I'm the matchmaker who arranged your marriage to the Training Officer—Matchmaker Yan! All the Zhu seats were empty, so I borrowed one."
[ZH] 千里寻一阵无语，尴尬地坐在一把空椅上，只觉额头一阵阵刺痛。
[EN] Speechless, Qianli Xun sank awkwardly into an empty chair as sharp stings pulsed across her forehead.
[ZH] “幽栖居士，你的额头怎么有伤？”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi, what happened to your forehead?"
[ZH] 林家长辈中竟然有人称她“幽栖居士”，千里寻认为此人一定是爱好诗词之人。
[EN] Someone among the Lin elders called her Youqi Jushi—must be a poetry lover!
[ZH] 她用丝帕沾了沾伤口周围，露出疼痛的表情，看向正位处，用柔弱可怜的语气说：“这位长辈，我额头的伤是拜林觉所赐，你们不知道，他经常打我，也因这伤，我什么都不记得，也不知如何称呼您。”
[EN] Dabbing around the cut with her silk handkerchief and putting on a fragile, pained look, she turned toward the head seat. "Elder, this wound is Lin Jue's handiwork! You don't know—he beats me all the time, and this blow wiped my memory clean. Forgive me for not knowing your name."
[ZH] 只听那位长辈说：“林觉侄儿，你能娶到幽栖居士，不知是几世修来的福分，却不懂珍惜，怎么能动手打她？还把她打得失忆？难怪幽栖居士要跟你和离！”
[EN] The elder flared up at once: "Nephew Lin Jue! Marrying Youqi Jushi took blessings built across lifetimes, and instead of cherishing her you raise your hand and beat the memory out of her? No wonder she wants a mutual divorce!"
[ZH] 林觉急道：“二叔，很多事情您不清楚，不能只听她一面之词。”
[EN] "Second Uncle, you don't know the whole story—don't listen only to her side!" Lin Jue protested fast.
[ZH] 林觉的二叔正是林子孝，他经常应邀参加朱晞颜组织的诗会，十分佩服朱淑真的才华，侄儿能娶到才女，在他看来是三生有幸。
[EN] This Second Uncle was Lin Zixiao, frequent guest at Zhu Xiyan's poetry banquets and ardent admirer of Zhu Shuzhen's talent—to him, his nephew landing such a poetess was sheer fortune.
[ZH] 现在闹到和离，他是一百个不同意，命令道：“林觉侄儿，现在就当着你的这些族叔和两位哥哥的面给幽栖居士跪下，承认你的错误，求她原谅！然后发誓以后再也不对幽栖居士动手！”
[EN] Dead set against letting the marriage crumble, he barked, "Lin Jue! Kneel right now before your clan uncles and two brothers, admit your fault to Youqi Jushi, beg her forgiveness, and swear never to lay a finger on her again!"
[ZH] 林觉不想和离，也不想当众给朱淑真跪下。在他看来，能闹到今日地步，错不完全在他。
[EN] Neither willing to divorce nor willing to kneel in public—he felt blame ran both ways—Lin Jue rose instead.
[ZH] 林觉站起来： “二叔息怒，该承认错误时我定会承认，现在还不是时候。今日和离，林家来了这么多人，可朱家一位都没到，各位叔叔，还有大哥二哥，你们认为说明什么？”
[EN] "Calm your anger, Second Uncle—I'll admit my faults when the time is right, not now. Look around: every Lin elder turned out today, yet not a single soul from the Zhu family showed up! Uncles, eldest brother, second brother—what does that tell you?"
[ZH] 其中一位说：“难道是知府大人根本不同意和离之事？”
[EN] One man spoke up: "Does the Prefect oppose the divorce?"
[ZH] 林觉认可道：“大哥说得很对，是这样。”
[EN] "Hit the nail on the head, Eldest Brother—that's it!" Lin Jue confirmed.
[ZH] 一位族叔劝道：“侄媳妇儿，既然你爹不同意，朱家又没长辈出面，还是别再胡闹，回西院好好过日子吧。”
[EN] A clan uncle chimed in: "Nephew's wife, since your own father disagrees and no Zhu elder came, quit making a scene and head back to the West Courtyard to live in peace."
[ZH] 千里寻再不说话，岂不是要被人强压一头？她忍着腐肌膏带来的疼痛：“我爹娘是不来，可不代表朱家其他长辈不会来，时辰还未到，你们急什么？就不能再等等。”
[EN] Refusing to get steamrolled, Qianli Xun braced through the burn of Corrosive Paste. "My parents aren't coming—doesn't mean other Zhu elders aren't! It's not even the appointed hour yet—what's the rush? Can't you wait?"
[ZH] 林子孝说：“我们就给幽栖居士些时间，耐心等等。”
[EN] "Let's grant Youqi Jushi some time and wait in patience," Lin Zixiao agreed.
[ZH] 林家长辈们都表示同意，林觉只能坐回原位，不满道：“如果巳时人还未到，和离就算失败，而且你以后永远别想再提。”
[EN] The other elders nodded. Lin Jue sank back into his seat, grumbling, "If nobody shows by the Hour of the Snake, the divorce is dead—and you never bring it up again."
[ZH] 千里寻并未理会林觉，想着怎么更大地发挥伤口的作用，要不白白买了药，白白忍受痛苦。
[EN] Ignoring him, Qianli Xun played up her wound—she hadn't paid three taels and endured this stinging for nothing.
[ZH] “各位长辈，林觉把我伤成这样，我要是再跟她过下去，估计连小命都得搭进去，所以这婚我必须离，希望长辈们能支持我。”
[EN] "Elders, look what Lin Jue did to me! Stay with him and I'll lose my life! I have to divorce him—please back me up!"
[ZH] 一位长辈说：“侄媳妇，林觉侄儿打你是不对，可男人打自家女人，肯定女人也有问题，还请侄媳妇想想自己错在哪里。”
[EN] Another elder spoke up: "Nephew's wife, sure Lin Jue shouldn't hit you—yet when a man strikes his wife, the wife shares blame too. Look inside yourself and ask what you did wrong."
[ZH] 千里寻火冒三丈，蹭地站起来，想要大骂说话的长辈，却听到外面传来声音。
[EN] Fury exploded in Qianli Xun; she shot to her feet ready to rip into him—when a clear voice floated in from outside.
[ZH] “此言差矣，不管女人如何，男人只要动手打女人，便是男人的不对，何况我朱家的姑娘知书达礼，有何错处？”
[EN] "Nonsense! Whatever a woman's flaws, the instant a man raises a hand to hit a woman, the fault is his! Doubly so when a daughter of our Zhu house is educated and refined—what fault could she possibly have?"
[ZH] 这话令千里寻的火气全消，顿时感受到有人撑腰的重要性。她与众人向外看去，原来是老祖宗，她手拿佛珠，缓缓走来。秀珍嬷嬷跟在身后，连搀扶的动作都没有，没想到一位一百二十岁的老人走起路来腿脚如此稳健。
[EN] Every ounce of anger melted into pure relief—nothing beat having heavy backup! Every head turned toward the doorway as the Old Ancestor strolled in, rolling prayer beads, Matron Xiuzhen walking behind without even needing to steady her—a hundred and twenty years old and striding firm as iron!
[ZH] 千里寻迎上前：“感谢老祖宗替孙儿出面。”
[EN] Qianli Xun rushed forward. "Thank you for coming on my behalf, Old Ancestor!"
[ZH] 林家人一听是老祖宗，都站起来迎接。
[EN] Hearing "Old Ancestor," every Lin in the room scrambled to their feet.
[ZH] 林子孝带头说：“恭迎老祖宗，今日有幸见到老祖宗真容，是我等晚辈的荣幸。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao led the bow: "Greetings to the Old Ancestor! Beholding your esteemed presence today honors all of us juniors!"
[ZH] 一旁的闫媒婆也站起来。朱家有位长寿的老祖宗，临安城无人不知，今日竟然有幸见到，闫媒婆自然也是无比恭敬。
[EN] Matchmaker Yan jumped up too; everybody in Lin'an knew of the Zhu clan's century-old matriarch, and meeting her face to face commanded deep awe.
[ZH] 老祖宗走进会客厅，扫视一遍林家的人：“你们这些小辈聚众欺负我孙女，我代表朱家出面替她撑腰，顺便讨个说法。”
[EN] Stepping into the hall, the Old Ancestor swept a cool gaze over the Lin men. "Ganging up on my granddaughter? I'm here representing the Zhu family to back her—and demand answers."
[ZH] 林觉虽跟着长辈们站起来恭迎，心里却很不屑：“林家众多长辈前来，朱家却只来您一位，足以看出岳父大人不同意和离之事。老祖宗既然来了，不如帮晚辈劝劝娘子，让她别闹着与我和离。”
[EN] Rising with the rest yet scoffing inside, Lin Jue pushed back: "Crowds of Lin elders came today, while the Zhu house sent only you—proof enough Father-in-law opposes this divorce! Since you're here, Old Ancestor, please help persuade my wife to drop this divorce talk."
[ZH] 老祖宗盯了林觉一会儿：“你就是我孙女的夫君？”
[EN] The Old Ancestor fixed her eyes on him for a beat. "You're my granddaughter's husband?"
[ZH] 林觉回道：“正是。”
[EN] "I am."
[ZH] 老祖宗回过头面向孙女儿，她额头的伤比上次见到时严重许多，又回看林觉：“你把我孙女打伤，还将商妓带到家里，我朱家岂能让她跟你继续过下去？朱家确实只来我一位，难道我一人不可以顶朱家所有长辈？”
[EN] Turning to her granddaughter—whose cut looked far nastier than when they last met—then back to Lin Jue, the Old Ancestor said cool and crisp, "You batter my granddaughter and haul brothel courtesans into the house, and you expect the Zhu family to let her stay? True, I came alone—do I not outweigh every elder in the Zhu house?"
[ZH] 林子孝说：“可以，当然可以，老祖宗别跟小辈一般见识，快，快上座。”
[EN] "You do, of course you do!" Lin Zixiao hastened to smooth things over. "Pay no mind to the lad's rudeness, Old Ancestor—please take the seat of honor!"
[ZH] 其他林家长辈也无不认同。
[EN] The other Lin elders echoed their agreement.
[ZH] 千里寻此时此刻更加感受到有长辈撑腰的重要性。她上前扶住老祖宗，把她扶到右边的正位坐下后，老实回到自己的位置旁，安心享受老祖宗为她撑腰的感觉。
[EN] Glowing with pride at having real backing, Qianli Xun guided the Old Ancestor to the right-hand seat of honor, then resumed her own chair ready to let the matriarch work.
[ZH] 老祖宗往右边一坐，林子孝不敢在左边坐下去，吩咐一旁的下人给他在偏位安排一把椅子。
[EN] Once the Old Ancestor sat right, Lin Zixiao dared not stay seated beside her on the left and signaled a servant to fetch him a side chair.
[ZH] 老祖宗说：“你是林家长辈中有身份的人，就坐我旁边。”
[EN] "You hold standing among the Lin elders—sit beside me," the Old Ancestor instructed.
[ZH] 林子孝谦虚道：“既然老祖宗让晚辈坐下，晚辈无不听从。只是没想到老祖宗还知道我。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao bowed humbly. "Since the Old Ancestor commands, I obey—never dreamed you even knew of me."
[ZH] “我并不知道你，但我这双眼睛会观察，大家都坐下吧，心平气和商量这俩孩子的和离之事。”
[EN] "I don't, yet these eyes know how to read people. Everyone sit—let's discuss these two children's mutual divorce calmly."
[ZH] 老祖宗吩咐，所有人都乖乖坐下。
[EN] At her word, the entire hall sat.
[ZH] 林子孝带头说：“老祖宗既然来了，我就把林家晚辈，以及林觉侄儿的意思说明，我们一致认为林觉侄儿能把幽栖居士娶进门，是林家祖上烧了高香，和离之事，林家小辈们都觉不妥，希望他们小两口能继续把日子过下去。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao opened: "Now that the Old Ancestor is here, allow me to state the consensus of our Lin clan and Nephew Lin Jue: marrying Youqi Jushi into our home is our ancestors' greatest blessing. None of us approve a divorce—we hope the young couple stays together."
[ZH] 老祖宗面向孙女：“你是何态度？不妨向各位长辈说明。”
[EN] Turning to her granddaughter, the Old Ancestor asked, "Where do you stand? State it plain before the elders."
[ZH] 千里寻虽已将态度表明，但老祖宗又给她机会，她不妨趁此进一步说明。
[EN] Even having made herself clear already, Qianli Xun seized the floor.
[ZH] “老祖宗，各位长辈，我虽失忆，记不起林觉以前如何对待我，可疼痛还在，被他打晕后醒来，第一眼看到他与商妓十分亲密的样子，我怎能忍受此等屈辱？提出让他休了我，也许是他良心发现，给我出了和离的主意，才有今日之事。我连他休了我都不怕，更何况和离？”
[EN] "Old Ancestor, esteemed elders—though amnesia erased how Lin Jue treated me before, the pain lingers! Waking up after he knocked me cold, the very first thing I saw was him cozying up to a brothel courtesan! How could any woman swallow that humiliation? I demanded a bill of divorce; maybe conscience pricked him and he suggested mutual divorce instead, bringing us here today. If I didn't even fear repudiation, why would I hesitate over mutual divorce?"
[ZH] 老祖宗盘着佛珠，眼帘垂下，认真听孙女说话。等孙女说完，接着说：“常言道宁拆十座庙，不破一门婚，可朱家孙女过得实在辛苦，总不能让她在火坑里待着不出来？今日我要帮她破了这门婚事，希望林家不要阻拦，让这两个孩子和和美美地散了。”
[EN] Eyelids lowered, rolling her beads, the Old Ancestor listened to every word before speaking: "Folks say tear down ten temples sooner than break one marriage—yet my Zhu granddaughter suffers so bitterly, how can we leave her trapped in a fiery pit? Today I intend to break this match for her. I hope the Lin clan steps aside and lets these two part in peace."
[ZH] 还未等林家长辈说话，林觉急忙道：“老祖宗有所不知，如若她新婚之夜跟我顺利圆房，我也不会在外找商妓，如若她平日不漠视我，不去跟文人才子勾勾搭搭，我也不会打她。大不了我当着老祖宗和林家长辈还有两位哥哥的面保证，只要淑真不离开我，我以后再也不打她，只要她同意让我娶一房妾室，我也保证不会再找商妓，也允许她自由参加各种诗会，只要她记着自己已婚，不随便跟文人才子勾勾搭搭就行。”
[EN] Before any elder could answer, Lin Jue jumped in: "Old Ancestor, you don't know the truth! Had she consummated our marriage on our wedding night, I never would've sought courtesans! Had she not treated me like air and carried on with young scholars outside, I never would've hit her! Tell you what—right here before you, my clan uncles, and both my brothers, I swear: if Shuzhen stays, I'll never strike her again! Let me take one concubine and I'll never touch another courtesan, and she can attend all the poetry salons she likes—long as she remembers she's married and doesn't flirt with scholars!"
[ZH] 林家的长辈听完无不点头。
[EN] Heads nodded all down the Lin row.
[ZH] 林子孝说：“老祖宗，看得出，林觉侄儿很舍不得幽栖居士，他既有悔改之心，不如成全，再说女子和离，多多少少会影响名声。幽栖居士有江南女词人的美称，老朽不希望这美称出现任何瑕疵。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao chimed in: "Old Ancestor, you can see how much Lin Jue hates to lose Youqi Jushi. Since he vows to mend his ways, why not grant him a chance? Besides, mutual divorce still smudges a woman's name—I would hate to see any blemish touch the Poetess of Jiangnan."
[ZH] 千里寻开始佩服林觉，他虽是个武夫，关键时候说话挺能笼络人心，看来他是做了充分准备，是想当着长辈的面灭了她和离之心。岂能如他所愿？这婚必须离！涂腐肌膏这一招不灵，既如此又何必再忍受痛苦。
[EN] Qianli Xun had to hand it to Lin Jue—brute or not, the guy knew how to play a room! Clearly he had rehearsed this speech to kill her divorce cold. No way was she letting him win! And since Corrosive Paste wasn't moving the needle, why keep suffering?
[ZH] “苦荞，给我涂上治疗伤口的药。”
[EN] "Kuqiao—put the healing salve on my cut."
[ZH] 苦荞掏出药瓶，倒一些药膏，涂在小姐的伤口上。冰冰凉凉的，灼热的痛感很快消失，头脑顿觉清晰许多。
[EN] Kuqiao pulled out the vial and dabbed cooling ointment over the wound; the burn faded at once, clearing her head.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，行了礼，说道：“老祖宗，各位长辈，淑真和离之心坚不可摧，我与林觉才不相匹配，志趣不相投，在一起过日子如同嚼蜡，不管林觉说再多，做再多保证，我都不会改变和离之心。林觉说我参加诗会，与文人才子勾勾搭搭，完全是污蔑！足以说明他不信任我，也无法理解我！跟这样的人继续过日子有何意义？”
[EN] Rising with a bow, Qianli Xun spoke loud and clear: "Old Ancestor, elders—nothing can shake my resolve to divorce! Lin Jue and I share neither mind nor taste; living together tastes like chewing wax. No matter how many vows he makes, my mind is set. Calling my poetry salons 'flirting with scholars' is pure slander—proof he neither trusts nor understands me! What point is there staying married to a man like that?"
[ZH] 老祖宗停下盘佛珠的手：“秀珍，把它拿出来，给姑爷瞧瞧，他若猜出其中的谜底，我便说服孙女不要再想和离之事，如若他猜不出，还请还我孙女自由。”
[EN] The Old Ancestor's hand stopped on her beads. "Xiuzhen, bring it out and show Son-in-law. If he solves the riddle inside, I will talk my granddaughter out of divorcing. If he cannot, grant my granddaughter her freedom."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷从怀里掏出叠好的纸，小心展开，上面正是千里寻凭记忆写下的《断肠谜》。
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen drew a folded sheet from her sleeve and opened it with care—Qianli Xun's handwritten "The Heartbreak Riddle."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷走到林觉跟前，递给他：“请姑爷品品，品完当着众人的面说出谜底。”
[EN] Stepping before Lin Jue, she handed it over. "Examine this, Son-in-law, and announce the riddle's answer before everyone."
[ZH] 林觉接过，上面的字他都认识，每一句都能读下去，可要说谜底，实在猜不出所以然。
[EN] Lin Jue took the sheet. He knew every character and could read every line—yet as for any riddle, he hadn't the foggiest clue!

#CH 23
[ZH] 林觉在心里将《断肠谜》来来回回念了不下十遍，既品不出其中滋味，更不知谜底，他完全陷入一团乱如麻的文字谜中。
[EN] Lin Jue read "The Heartbreak Riddle" silently ten times over—grasping neither its depth nor its hidden answer, tangled up in a knot of words.
[ZH] 千里寻见林觉急得满头是汗，甚觉好笑，更认为朱淑真嫁给他实在是亏大发了。
[EN] Watching sweat bead on his forehead, Qianli Xun almost laughed out loud—what a rotten deal Zhu Shuzhen had gotten marrying this guy!
[ZH] “林觉，既看不懂我写的词，就不要为难自己，乖乖放我离开林家，我将感激不尽。”
[EN] "If you can't make sense of my lyric, Lin Jue, stop torturing yourself—let me leave the Lin family in peace, and I'll owe you one."
[ZH] 林觉将写有《断肠谜》的纸张向地上扔去，正好扔在林子孝的脚边，他顺手捡起，仔细品味起来。
[EN] Lin Jue flung the sheet to the floor—right beside Lin Zixiao's feet, who scooped it up and began reading with delight.
[ZH] 林觉则说：“哼！我平日最讨厌这些附庸风雅的玩意儿，老祖宗该不会知道我不懂，故意拿来为难我？想以此为由让我同意和离，实在不公平！”
[EN] "Hmph!" Lin Jue snapped. "Everyone knows I despise fancy word games! Did the Old Ancestor pick this on purpose knowing I couldn't solve it just to force me into divorce? Hardly fair!"
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷批评道：“你这后辈着实无礼，老祖宗长年居住在朱家佛堂，大门不出二门不迈，对你这位小辈一无所知，要不是朱家姑娘无路可走，求到老祖宗面前，她今日怎会来此？……”
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen rebuked him sharply: "Watch your insolence, young man! The Old Ancestor stays shut in the Zhu Buddhist Shrine year round and knew nothing about you until Miss Zhu came begging with nowhere else to turn..."
[ZH] 老祖宗抬手阻止秀珍嬷嬷，边盘佛珠边说：“我本不愿为孙女出面，是她的词打动我，既让我看到她的才华，也让我明白她想要摆脱这段婚姻并不是一时冲动，而是经过认真思量后做出的决定。姑爷既猜不出谜底，看来的确不适合我孙女，还请放手，才能各自安好。”
[EN] Raising a hand to check Xiuzhen, the Old Ancestor resumed rolling her beads. "At first I refused to step in. Her lyric moved me—showing both her talent and that her wish to end this marriage is no passing whim, but a weighed decision. Since you cannot solve her riddle, you two are clearly mismatched. Let go so both of you can find peace."
[ZH] 未等林觉及其他族人说话，林子孝不由脱口而出：“妙！幽栖居士这首词写得实在是妙！”
[EN] Before Lin Jue or any elder could reply, Lin Zixiao burst out: "Brilliant! Utterly brilliant lyric, Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] 林子孝解诗的兴致大发，不由站起来：“我给诸位解解……”
[EN] Fired up to analyze verse, he sprang to his feet: "Allow me to unpack this for everyone!"
[ZH] 有人说：“二哥，林觉侄儿这婚都要离了，你竟然还有心情解诗？还是赶紧想办法怎么帮他护住这个小家吧！”
[EN] Someone groaned, "Second Brother, Nephew Lin Jue's marriage is falling apart and you're lecturing on poetry?! Help save his home!"
[ZH] 林觉很想知道朱淑真在词中到底写些什么，既能打动老祖宗出面，还让二叔赞不绝口，于是说道：“就让二叔给大家解解，我就不信，附庸风雅的玩意儿能破坏我的婚姻？”
[EN] Curious what on earth Zhu Shuzhen had written to summon the Old Ancestor and dazzle his uncle, Lin Jue waved a hand. "Let Second Uncle explain—I don't believe some fancy word game can wreck my marriage!"
[ZH] 林子孝暂时把和离之事放到一边，侃侃而谈：“这首词名为《断肠谜》，不仅抒发了断肠的主旨，还藏着十个数字之谜……”
[EN] Setting the divorce aside, Lin Zixiao beamed and launched in: "Titled 'The Heartbreak Riddle,' this lyric not only conveys heartbreak—it conceals ten number riddles!"
[ZH] 林觉心想：“十个数字之谜？我怎么看不出？”他努力回想词中的句子，还是不明所以，不由脱口而出：“二叔先说说谜底。”
[EN] Ten number riddles? Where? Lin Jue racked his brain over the lines and blurted out, "Tell us the answers first, Second Uncle!"
[ZH] 林子孝继续讲道：“听我慢慢给大家解。‘下楼来，金钱卜落’，‘下’字‘卜落’为一；‘问苍天，人在何方’，‘天’字‘人’去为二；‘恨王孙，一直去了’， ‘王’字去‘一直’为三；‘詈冤家，言去难留’， ‘詈’字‘言’去为四；‘悔当初，吾错失口’，‘吾’字失‘口’为五；‘有上交，无下交’，‘交’字‘有上’‘无下’为六；‘皂白何须问’，‘皂’字去‘白’为七；‘分开不用刀’， ‘分’字‘不用刀’为八；‘从今莫把仇人靠’，‘仇’字无‘人靠’为九；‘千里相思一撇消’，‘千’字‘一撇消’则为十。大家说说，妙不妙？”
[EN] "Listen close," Lin Zixiao declared. "'Coming downstairs, golden coins drop for divination'—drop the 'bu' (卜) from 'xia' (下), leaving 'one' (一); 'I ask High Heaven, where has he gone?'—remove 'ren' (人) from 'tian' (天), leaving 'two' (二); 'Resenting the noble lord who walked straight away'—remove the vertical stroke from 'wang' (王), leaving 'three' (三); 'Cursing the heartless one—speaking of parting, hard to hold back'—remove 'yan' (言) from 'li' (詈), leaving 'four' (四); 'Regretting how I first misspoke'—remove 'kou' (口) from 'wu' (吾), leaving 'five' (五); 'There is upper union, no lower tie'—keep the top of 'jiao' (交) and drop the bottom, leaving 'six' (六); 'Why ask right from wrong?'—drop 'bai' (白) from 'zao' (皂), leaving 'seven' (七); 'Parting needs no knife'—drop the knife radical (刀) from 'fen' (分), leaving 'eight' (八); 'From this day on lean no more on a foe'—drop the person radical (亻) from 'chou' (仇), leaving 'nine' (九); 'A thousand miles of longing wiped away with one stroke'—drop the top flick from 'qian' (千), leaving 'ten' (十)! Tell me, everyone—isn't that sheer genius?!"
[ZH] 林子孝解完谜底，林觉恍然大悟，他确实不了解眼前的妻子，可“断肠”的主旨依然品不出来，冷笑道：“又能如何？不就是写了十个数字吗？有什么了不起？”
[EN] Understanding dawned on Lin Jue—he really didn't know his wife at all—yet the deeper heartbreak still sailed over his head. He sneered, "So what? Just ten numbers—big deal!"
[ZH] 林家其他族人有的被折服，有的与林觉一样，认为没什么了不起。
[EN] Around the hall, half the Lin elders marveled while the rest shrugged alongside Lin Jue.
[ZH] 林子孝接着讲：“林觉侄儿此言差矣，写这类从头到尾是谜底的词，首先要选适合的字，选字当需费一番功夫；其次，字选好了，选韵入韵亦需费时费力，还要想方设法将每一个字缀入词句；最后，也是最关键的，不能背离主旨，要将情感融入其中。所以，写诗作词好比建造楼阁，一砖一瓦都需谨慎，不比你练兵容易。幽栖居士心思巧妙，才华横溢，能写出这等词，令老夫佩服。唉！词中句句充斥着不满，处处诉说分道扬镳，尤其是这句‘从今莫把仇人靠’，林觉侄儿，幽栖居士已将你视为仇人，如若二叔再不同意和离，只怕你们以后的日子不好过啊，还是放幽栖居士自由吧！”
[EN] "You're wrong, Nephew," Lin Zixiao countered. "Composing an entire riddle lyric requires selecting exact characters, fitting strict rhyme schemes, weaving every glyph seamlessly into natural phrases, and never drifting from the emotional core! Writing verse is like raising a tower brick by brick—no easier than drilling armies! Youqi Jushi's wit and craft leave this old man in awe. Alas, every line brims with resentment and finality—above all 'from this day on lean no more on a foe.' She already regards you as an enemy, Lin Jue! If I still block this divorce, your future together will be poison. Give Youqi Jushi her freedom!"
[ZH] 听到这话，林觉坐在椅子上一脸落寞的表情，尽管他什么都明白，可就是不想轻易放朱淑真离开林家，谁让他喜欢她，因喜欢不愿看到她将来扑进其他男人的怀抱。什么东方尘泽，什么乔文仙，觊觎他的娘子不是一天两天，决不能让他们得逞！
[EN] Lin Jue slumped in his chair, gloom darkening his face. He understood every word—yet couldn't bear letting her walk out into another man's arms! Dongfang Chenze, Qiao Wenxian—those vultures had eyed his wife for ages; no way was he handing her over!
[ZH] 千里寻一阵欣喜，没想到朱淑真的一首词这么快拿下林子孝。她起身谢道：“二叔的解析实在到位，感谢二叔理解和成全。”
[EN] Thrilled that one poem had flipped Lin Zixiao so fast, Qianli Xun rose and bowed. "Spot-on reading, Second Uncle! Thank you for understanding and granting your blessing."
[ZH] “幽栖居士不必客气，能不能把这首词送给我？我想回去再仔细研究研究。”
[EN] "Don't mention it, Youqi Jushi. Might I keep this copy to study more at home?"
[ZH] “二叔尽管拿去。”
[EN] "Yours to keep, Second Uncle!"
[ZH] 林子孝把纸张叠好，揣进怀里，面向老祖宗行礼：“老祖宗，我是林家族人，既已同意和离，不适合在此待下去，晚辈先告退。”
[EN] Folding the sheet carefully into his sleeve, Lin Zixiao bowed to the Old Ancestor. "As a Lin elder who now consents to the divorce, it's improper for me to stay. I take my leave."
[ZH] 老祖宗点点头，表示同意。
[EN] The Old Ancestor gave a slight nod.
[ZH] 林子孝又面向族人：“各位兄弟，三位侄儿，我走了，你们继续。”
[EN] Turning to his kin, Lin Zixiao announced, "Brothers, nephews—I'm off. Carry on."
[ZH] 一位族人站起来阻止道：“二哥，你走了，谁来主持？”
[EN] A clan brother sprang up. "Second Brother, you leave—who presides?!"
[ZH] 林子孝说：“三弟还在，让他主持。”说完，林子孝拂袖而去。
[EN] "Third Brother's right here—let him preside!" Flicking his sleeve, Lin Zixiao walked out.
[ZH] 老祖宗开口说：“姑爷，我刚才说过，你若猜不出，就请还我孙女自由。你既然没有猜出谜底，请在和离书上签字吧。”
[EN] The Old Ancestor spoke up: "Son-in-law, I said earlier—fail the riddle, grant my granddaughter her freedom. You failed. Sign the mutual divorce papers."
[ZH] 千里寻充满期待地看着林觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked at Lin Jue full of hope.
[ZH] 林觉满面愁容，用失落的语气说：“仅凭一首词就想让我同意和离，实在没有道理，晚辈念在老祖宗年长，不想跟您计较，除非您把这里的长辈和我两位哥哥一一说服，让他们也像二叔一样甩袖离去，我才会同意在和离书上签字。”
[EN] Wrinkling his brow in a glum look, Lin Jue pushed back: "Forcing me to divorce over one poem makes zero sense! Out of respect for your age I won't argue with you, Old Ancestor—yet unless you convince every elder here and both my brothers to walk out like Second Uncle, I'm not signing a thing!"
[ZH] 老祖宗无言，继续微闭双目，盘着佛珠。
[EN] Without a word, the Old Ancestor closed her eyes and rolled her beads.
[ZH] “既然让我主持，我还是坐在正位上。”林觉旁边的三叔站起来，向老祖宗行礼，走到正位跟前坐下。
[EN] "Since I'm presiding, I'll take the main seat!" Third Uncle Lin rose from beside Lin Jue, bowed to the Old Ancestor, and settled into the head chair.
[ZH] 千里寻瞅着这位三叔，身形偏胖，留着胡须，眼睛迷成一条缝，五官离得较近，看上去很滑稽。本想与这位三叔说两句，仔细一想，还是先听听他如何说。
[EN] Qianli Xun eyed Third Uncle: plump frame, beard, eyes squinted to slits, features bunched close together—looked downright comical. Holding her tongue, she waited to hear his pitch.
[ZH] 三叔坐定后，咳了两声：“二哥能轻易被你们改变，只怪他平日喜欢附庸风雅的玩意儿。我可不喜欢，侄媳妇写多少词，对我来讲，毫无用处。我劝侄媳妇还是省省吧，年纪也不小，和离后谁还会要你？别整天想着什么诗啊词啊的，那玩意又不能当饭吃，过日子跟那玩意儿是两码事，跟了谁，到晚上还不是灯一熄，盖上被子，被窝里那点事儿……”
[EN] Clearing his throat twice, Third Uncle began: "Second Brother caves easy because he loves fancy word games. Not me! Write a hundred lyrics, means zilch to me. Give it a rest, nephew's wife—you're not young anymore, who'd take a divorced woman? Quit dreaming of poems and songs—verse won't put rice on the table! Marriage is one thing, poetry's another. Whoever you marry, come nightfall you blow out the lamp, pull up the quilt, and it's all the same under the covers..."
[ZH] 老祖宗觉得林家三叔说得露骨，咳了一声，以示提醒。
[EN] Finding the remark crude, the Old Ancestor gave a warning cough.
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷跟着说：“林家三叔，注意说话内容。”
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen snapped, "Third Uncle Lin, mind your language!"
[ZH] 三叔立刻收住：“常言说得好，女子无才便是德。女子有才华，思想容易变得复杂，想得多，要求的也多，搞得家庭不稳。林觉侄儿是临安城的练兵官，训练的士兵负责守卫整个临安，贡献极大，多少女子想嫁给他，可他却偏偏看上侄媳妇，也说明他对侄媳妇是真心。三叔劝你，别整日想着那些临安才子，他们不过是银枪蜡头，中看不中用，哪像我林觉侄儿，练武出身，身强体壮，到了晚上……”
[EN] Checking himself at once, Third Uncle pivoted: "As the saying goes, a woman's virtue is having no talent! Too much cleverness fills a woman's head with lofty demands and wrecks a home. Lin Jue is Lin'an's Training Officer guarding the whole city—plenty of girls would kill to marry him, yet he picked you out of true devotion! Stop pining for those Lin'an scholars—all flash and no steel, pretty to look at and useless in a fight! Not like my nephew Lin Jue, martial-trained, strong as an ox, come nightfall..."
[ZH] 林家三叔说着说着还是没管住嘴，又扯到被窝里那些事。千里寻觉得好笑，林家真是什么人都有。
[EN] There went his mouth slipping back under the covers again! Qianli Xun bit back a laugh—what a circus this Lin clan was.
[ZH] 又听到老祖宗的咳嗽声，三叔才没敢往下说，终于开始收尾：“总之，三叔是为侄媳妇好，希望你们小两口能把日子好好过下去，家和万事兴嘛，我说完了。”
[EN] Another cough from the Old Ancestor cut him short, forcing a wrap-up: "Point is, Third Uncle wants what's best for you. Stay together and make it work—harmony brings prosperity. Done!"
[ZH] 林觉听完三叔所说，《断肠谜》造成的失落感顿时消散，又恢复先前的自信。走一个二叔，还有好几位族叔在此为他撑腰，好戏还在后头。
[EN] Third Uncle's speech swept away every bit of Lin Jue's gloom, puffing his confidence right back up. Lose one Second Uncle? Plenty more clan uncles stood behind him—the real show was just starting!
[ZH] 千里寻不能再沉默下去：“三叔讲得甚是实在，您的好意我心领，但我离开林觉之心坚不可摧，无论林家人怎么劝说，都不会改变想法，如果诸位长辈不同意和离，就请林觉当场写下休书，我不怕被休之后名声不好，也不怕娘家不让我进门！”
[EN] Done staying quiet, Qianli Xun spoke up: "Very practical advice, Third Uncle—appreciated! Yet my resolve to leave Lin Jue is ironclad; no lecture from the Lin clan will change my mind. If you elders refuse mutual divorce, then let Lin Jue write a bill of repudiation right here! I don't fear a ruined name, and I don't fear being barred from my maiden door!"
[ZH] 千里寻的话令在场大部分人感到震惊，尽管她说过主动要求丈夫休她之事，可大家亲耳听到，还是难以置信，竟然有女子敢于让丈夫休自己，真是闻所未闻。只有老祖宗依然盘着佛珠，双目微闭。
[EN] Shock rippled across the room. Rumors of her asking to be cast out were one thing—hearing a noble lady demand repudiation to their faces sounded unheard of! Only the Old Ancestor rolled her beads undisturbed.
[ZH] 闫媒婆听后，急忙站起来：“少夫人，您是女子，千万不能被婆家休了，再说您又没触犯‘七出’，林家也没有理由休您。”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan sprang up at once. "Young Madam, never talk of being cast out! Besides, you haven't violated any of the Seven Conditions for Repudiating a Wife—the Lin family has no grounds to cast you out!"
[ZH] “七出”？千里寻第一次听说，根本不明白，不由问道：“‘七出’是什么？”
[EN] "Seven Conditions?" First time Qianli Xun had heard that phrase. "What are the Seven Conditions?"
[ZH] 闫媒婆解释道：“‘七出’是休妻的七大条件，分别是不孝公婆、无子、淫、善妒、身有恶疾、口多言、盗窃。练兵官父母早亡，无公婆在身边，谈不上不孝公婆；少夫人成婚不到一年，又年轻，只要想生孩子，再容易不过，所以无子也不符合；至于淫和善妒更与少夫人不相符；少夫人身体健康，我们都看在眼里，身有恶疾也不符合；至于口多言，凭少夫人的修养和才华，绝不是那种多言又爱搬弄是非之人；盗窃更谈不上。您一项都不符合，练兵官的休书岂能写得下去？”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan ticked them off: "Disobedience to parents-in-law, failure to bear a son, adultery, jealousy, severe illness, gossip, and theft! The Training Officer's parents passed away early, so disobedience doesn't apply. You've been wed less than a year and are young—bearing children is easy, so no failure there. Adultery and jealousy certainly don't fit. You're clearly healthy—no severe illness. With your poise and refinement, gossip is unthinkable—and theft is absurd! You don't fit a single ground; how could he even write a bill of repudiation?"
[ZH] 闫媒婆说完，又坐回原位。
[EN] Finished, Matchmaker Yan sat back down.
[ZH] 有“七出”挡在面前，休妻这一招也进行不下去，千里寻被难住。
[EN] Blocked cold by the Seven Conditions, even repudiation hit a wall! Qianli Xun felt boxed in.
[ZH] 林觉倒开心，尤其看到朱淑真为难的样子，心想：“哼！想离开我，没那么容易！”
[EN] Delighted at her stumped face, Lin Jue gloated inside: Hmph! Walking out on me won't be that easy!
[ZH] 林家又有人说：“弟妹呀，你想离开三弟，无非是因为他打你，又找商妓，只要你跟他好好过日子，二哥在此向你保证，他打你时，你就来找我，我定打断他的腿；他要是找商妓，你也来找我，我会追到青楼当着众人的面揍他一顿，让他颜面全无，要敢把商妓带到家里，我就让他跪在林府的大门外，跪个三天三夜，不相信治不了他！你看可否？”
[EN] Another Lin spoke up: "Sister-in-law, you only want to leave Third Brother because he hit you and saw courtesans. Stay with him, and Second Brother swears right here: if he hits you, come to me and I'll break his legs! If he visits courtesans, I'll haul him out of the brothel and thrash him in public! If he dares bring one home, I'll make him kneel outside Lin Manor's main gate for three days and nights! Fair enough?"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，林觉与他的两个哥哥同住在一个宅子，虽在不同的方位，但她不相信以前的事儿林觉的两位哥哥会不知？以前都不管，现在却来保证，实在不可信。反正她只有一个念头，就是离婚，不如在“七出”里硬拉一条，只要沾些边儿就行。
[EN] Living under the same roof—different courtyards or not—Qianli Xun didn't buy for one second his brothers knew nothing before. Now they suddenly play tough? Worthless promises! Only one thing mattered: getting divorced—even if she had to claim one of those Seven Conditions herself!
[ZH] 千里寻打算豁出去，她没有回答林家二哥的问题，说出令所有人更加震惊的话：“‘七出’是吧，倒有一条符合，林觉不是说我在外面跟临安才子勾勾搭搭，难道不符合‘淫’这一条吗？”
[EN] Going all-in, ignoring Second Brother, she dropped a bombshell: "Seven Conditions, right? One fits! Didn't Lin Jue accuse me of carrying on with Lin'an scholars outside? Doesn't that fit 'adultery'?!"
[ZH] 只见林家长辈瞪大眼睛，面面相觑；林觉的嘴巴微微张开，这个不一样的娘子真的很不一样；老祖宗正在盘佛珠的手立刻停下，秀珍嬷嬷搓着手不敢言语；闫媒婆转头盯着这位少夫人，像是头一次见到她；苦荞担心得头上直冒冷汗，重生后的小姐真是胆大，什么话都敢说，有损女子清誉之事别人躲都躲不及，这位冤家怎么还大包大揽起来？
[EN] Eyes bulged all down the Lin row; Lin Jue's jaw dropped—this reborn wife stopped at nothing! Even the Old Ancestor's hand froze on her beads while Matron Xiuzhen wrung her hands in stunned silence. Matchmaker Yan stared as if seeing a stranger, and cold sweat drenched Kuqiao's brow: Reborn Miss is fearless—people run a mile from anything touching a woman's honor, and she's volunteering for it!

#CH 24
[ZH] 大家的表现，让千里寻意识到她的话的确是说猛了，只因她太想离开林觉。她连恋爱的滋味都没品尝过，凭什么穿越而来就要与一个她根本不感兴趣的人过婚姻生活？
[EN] Judging by every horrified face in the room, Qianli Xun knew she had swung way too hard—she just wanted out of Lin Jue's clutches so bad! She hadn't even tasted romance once in thirty years—why spend her transmigration chained to a man she couldn't stand?
[ZH] 伤害自己的事从昨晚就做了，一大早过来该表达的已表达，老祖宗连《断肠谜》都搬出来，她甚至要求林觉写下休书，结果均不奏效。不如采取置之死地而后生的招数，此刻的她只想用毁坏名声的方式获取自由。
[EN] She had burned her own forehead, argued herself hoarse, brought out the Old Ancestor and "The Heartbreak Riddle," even begged for repudiation—nothing budged them. Fight to survive from the brink of ruin: if trashing her reputation bought freedom, so be it!
[ZH] 最先说话的是闫媒婆：“少夫人，怎能如此说自己？女子名声最重要，即使练兵官说过，他也只是猜测，或是听人瞎议论，又没有捉奸在床，我也相信在坐的每位长辈绝不会把练兵官的话当回事。如果以此为理由让练兵官休你，我闫媒婆第一个不同意！”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan jumped in first: "Young Madam, how can you slander yourself?! A woman's good name is everything! Even if the Training Officer made accusations, that's just idle guesswork and gossip—nobody caught you in bed with anyone! Not a single elder here takes his words as truth. Using that as grounds to cast you out—I, Matchmaker Yan, will be the first to block it!"
[ZH] 千里寻清楚闫媒婆是好心，只想为她正名，可她根本感激不起来，因为闫媒婆的话又一次为她堵死通向自由的大门。
[EN] Qianli Xun knew the matchmaker meant well trying to protect her honor—yet she couldn't feel an ounce of thanks when the woman had just slammed her last exit shut.
[ZH] 老祖宗又开始盘佛珠，边盘边说：“我朱家孙女的话都说到这个份儿上，连个人声誉都不顾及，硬要认下一个‘淫’字，足以见她不想与姑爷过下去，为何林家长辈与姑爷还要强行维持这段婚姻？”
[EN] Resuming her beads, the Old Ancestor said, "My Zhu granddaughter has gone so far as to throw away her own reputation and claim 'adultery' just to break free. Plain as day she cannot bear living with Son-in-law. Why do you Lin elders insist on forcing this marriage?"
[ZH] 林家有位长辈劝道：“林觉侄儿，不如和离吧，看样子侄媳妇是铁了心要离开你，你与她继续过下去还有什么意思？”
[EN] One Lin uncle softened: "Nephew Lin Jue, grant the mutual divorce. Clearly her heart is set on leaving—what joy is there dragging it out?"
[ZH] 林觉依然不想轻易放弃，他站起来，向各位长辈行礼：“老祖宗，各位族叔，大哥二哥，我既不同意和离，也不会休了淑真。原因有二：其一，我心里还有她，想跟她过下去；其二，淑真她不顾及朱家颜面，我得顾及，只因岳父大人看重朱家声誉，我不能令他失望。”
[EN] Refusing to fold, Lin Jue stood and bowed to the room. "Old Ancestor, uncles, brothers—I will neither agree to mutual divorce nor cast Shuzhen out! Two reasons: first, I still love her and want a life with her; second, if Shuzhen won't guard Zhu family honor, I will! Father-in-law prizes the Zhu name above all—I cannot let him down."
[ZH] 林觉又面向老祖宗：“老祖宗，您是朱家人，也应顾及朱家颜面，帮晚辈劝劝淑真。”
[EN] Turning directly to the Old Ancestor, he added, "Old Ancestor, you are of the Zhu clan—guard your family's face and persuade Shuzhen to stay."
[ZH] 老祖宗叹口气，没有说话，继续盘着佛珠。
[EN] The Old Ancestor let out a quiet sigh and rolled her beads in silence.
[ZH] 千里寻从老祖宗的叹气声中感受到老人家的无奈。她更加紧张，难道这婚离不成？
[EN] Hearing helplessness in that sigh, Qianli Xun's stomach tightened—was the divorce really slipping away?
[ZH] 林家又有长辈说：“侄媳妇，你既主动认领‘七出’之一，明显是承认自己有失妇德，既如此，别嫌林觉侄儿打你，我看打得极其对！你有错在先，不管是和离还是休你，都应该由林觉侄儿说了算。”
[EN] Another Lin elder spoke sharp: "Nephew's wife, claiming one of the Seven Conditions admits you failed wifely virtue! No wonder Lin Jue hit you—sounds like you earned it! With you at fault, whether to divorce or keep you is Lin Jue's call alone!"
[ZH] 林家长辈中有人接着说：“七弟说的不对，不管侄媳妇有错没错，我朝历来男女离婚都是男方说了算，既然林觉侄儿不嫌弃侄媳妇，又能为朱家考虑，依我看和离到此为止吧。”
[EN] Another added, "Seventh Brother misses the point—fault or no fault, in our dynasty the husband always decides divorce! Since Lin Jue doesn't hold it against her and protects Zhu family face, this discussion is over!"
[ZH] 千里寻开始后悔冒失认领“七出”之一，只怪她不了解这个时代。从目前来看，整个局势对她很不利。
[EN] Regret stung Qianli Xun—claiming one of the Seven Conditions had backfired hard! She didn't understand this era's rules, and now the room had flipped completely against her.
[ZH] 她算是琢磨出味儿来，林觉出了和离的主意并非好心，而是想让她被众人当面说教，用事实给她当头一棒。难道他记恨打他那一拳？以及将他关了半天一晚上？此人真是心胸狭窄，睚眦必报。
[EN] It dawned on her at last: Lin Jue hadn't suggested mutual divorce out of mercy—he had set a trap to lecture her in public and crush her hopes! Still sore over that black eye and spending the night tied up? Petty, vengeful jerk!
[ZH] 千里寻越想越生气，站起来，走到林觉面前，双眼瞪着他一言不发。
[EN] Boiling over, Qianli Xun stood, marched right up to Lin Jue, and glared straight into his eyes without a word.
[ZH] 林觉被娘子如此近距离盯着看，还是头一遭，不一样的娘子，让他觉得他们之间还有希望，怎能轻易用和离放她离去？他面对朱淑真笑了笑，坐回位置。
[EN] Never had his wife stood this close staring straight at him—this fiery new wife gave him fresh hope, making it even harder to let her go. He met her glare with a calm smile and sat back down.
[ZH] 千里寻收回双目，不管今日和离与否，还是先自证清白。
[EN] Pulling her gaze off him, Qianli Xun figured divorce or not, better clear her name first.
[ZH] “老祖宗，各位林家长辈，刚才我说的话请大家不要当真，我只是太想和离，胡乱说的，昨日我受邀参加诗会，回来时，林觉怀疑我，我告诉他，下次再参加诗会带他一起去，让他亲眼见识我如何与才子们相处，也好证明我的清白。诸位也可以问我的丫鬟苦荞，她每次都跟着我，我在诗会中做什么，她最清楚，至于外面人瞎胡议论，纯属诽谤！”
[EN] "Old Ancestor, Lin elders—forget what I just said. I only blurted it out desperate for a divorce! When I got back from yesterday's poetry salon and Lin Jue accused me, I offered to bring him along next time so he could watch with his own eyes and clear my name! Ask my maid Kuqiao—she attends every single salon with me and knows every word spoken. Every rumor outside is pure slander!"
[ZH] 苦荞主动替小姐正名：“我可以作证，我家小姐每次参加诗会，不是吟诗作词，就是鉴赏别人的诗词，从无逾越之举。”
[EN] Kuqiao stepped forward at once: "I swear it! Every salon Miss attends, all they do is write verse and discuss poetry—never once an improper act!"
[ZH] 三叔又开口说话：“侄媳妇呀，女人最重要的是在家里把自己男人照顾好，三叔劝你以后别再参加什么诗会，跟那些文人才子聚在一起，时间长了，什么风言风语都有，影响你声誉；也是林觉侄儿大度，要搁我，直接把你锁在家里，看你还敢出去！”
[EN] Third Uncle piped up again: "Nephew's wife, a woman's duty is tending her husband at home! Quit attending salons and mingling with scholars—keep that up and gossip's bound to follow and ruin your name! Lucky Lin Jue's patient—were it me, I'd lock you inside the house and see you try stepping out!"
[ZH] 听到这话，老祖宗不愿再沉默下去：“如若朱家姑娘在林家被如此虐 待，我看这日子也没有必要过下去，还是离了吧。”
[EN] At that, the Old Ancestor broke her silence: "If a daughter of the Zhu house faces mistreatment like that under a Lin roof, this marriage cannot stand. Grant the divorce!"
[ZH] 千里寻趁机说：“林觉虽没把我锁在家里，可他做下那些事，难道不是虐 待我？三叔，各位林家长辈，还是听从老祖宗的话，让林觉跟我离了吧？”
[EN] Seizing the opening, Qianli Xun cried, "Lin Jue didn't lock me in, yet weren't his beatings and courtesans mistreatment?! Third Uncle, Lin elders—heed the Old Ancestor and let him divorce me!"
[ZH] 林觉怎愿让良好的局面再度被扭转，立刻说：“说我虐 待你，实在是夸大其词，应该是你虐 待我在先，新婚之夜不愿与我圆房，难道不是虐 待？三天前不仅打我，还把我五花大绑关起来，难道不是虐 待？”
[EN] Not about to let the tide turn back, Lin Jue shot back: "Calling that mistreatment stretches the truth! She mistreated me first!
