Refusing to consummate our marriage on our wedding night—isn't that mistreatment? Beating me three days ago and trussing me up in a locked room—isn't that mistreatment?!"
[ZH] 此事被林觉拿出来说，千里寻万万没有想到，已到这等境地，和离之仗必输无疑。
[EN] Qianli Xun froze—never dreamed he'd air that dirty laundry in front of the whole clan! Game over—the divorce battle was lost.
[ZH] 三叔很生气：“侄媳妇，不是三叔说你，都是你不对，不圆房也罢了，大不了把你摆在家里，他再纳个妾。而你呢？怎能打他？还把他关起来？真是污了才女的美名！不过，林觉侄儿，你是练兵官，力气不小，怎能被女人打？”
[EN] Third Uncle bristled: "Nephew's wife, plain talk—every bit of this is on you! Refuse his bed? Fine, leave you as primary wife and let him take a concubine. Yet raising fists to your husband and locking him up?! Disgracing the name of a talented lady! Wait—Lin Jue, you're a Training Officer, strong as a bull—how'd you let a woman beat you?"
[ZH] 林觉不想提及朱淑真与以往不同之事，更不想丢了男人的面子：“是我让着她，不想跟她一般见识。”
[EN] Loath to bring up her strange transformation or lose face as a man, Lin Jue shrugged: "I held back—refused to stoop to fighting my wife."
[ZH] 三叔立刻向老祖宗请示：“老祖宗，您说这婚还能离吗？”
[EN] Third Uncle turned right to the Old Ancestor: "Old Ancestor—can this marriage still be dissolved?"
[ZH] 老祖宗停住盘佛珠的手：“我还是走吧。”
[EN] The Old Ancestor stopped her beads. "I should take my leave."
[ZH] 千里寻阻止道：“老祖宗等等，听我把话说完。”
[EN] "Wait, Old Ancestor—hear me out!" Qianli Xun cried.
[ZH] 老祖宗并未起身，孙女的面子还是要给的。
[EN] Granting her granddaughter's plea, the Old Ancestor stayed seated.
[ZH] 尽管好的局面已不在千里寻这边，她还想再搏一次，决定抖出一切：“其实我不是真正的朱淑真，以前的朱淑真已被林觉打死，我来自另一个更先进的时代，名叫千里寻，会千里拳法。如若不信，我在此为各位表演一番。”
[EN] Odds stacked against her, Qianli Xun risked one final Hail Mary—spilling everything: "Truth is, I'm not the real Zhu Shuzhen! Lin Jue beat the old Zhu Shuzhen to death! I'm Qianli Xun from a far more advanced era, master of Qianli Fist! Don't believe me? Watch!"
[ZH] 说完，千里寻打出一套拳法，动作标准流畅，然后一拳打向闫媒婆旁边的椅子，只见那椅子一分为二倒在地上，再一次惊讶了周围的人。
[EN] Stepping into the center, Qianli Xun launched into a crisp, seamless martial form—ending with a thunderous punch into the empty chair beside Matchmaker Yan! Wood splintered clean in two and crashed to the floor, shocking every soul in the room.
[ZH] 苦荞见怪不怪，心里只为小姐担忧。
[EN] Unfazed by now, Kuqiao just worried sick for her mistress.
[ZH] 老祖宗依然盘着佛珠，岿然不动。
[EN] The Old Ancestor rolled her beads without flinching.
[ZH] 千里寻打到最后一拳时，直接向林觉袭击而去，拳头并未真正打在林觉的脸上，而是在他面前停下。
[EN] On the final beat of her form, Qianli Xun drove a punch straight at Lin Jue's face—stopping a hair's breadth from his nose!
[ZH] 袭击而来的威力，令林觉无法睁眼，他很快闭上眼睛，头朝后躲去。
[EN] The sheer gust slammed his eyes shut and snapped his head back.
[ZH] “怎么样？怕了吧？如果怕了，告诉大家，朱淑真是被你打死的！”
[EN] "Well? Scared now? If you're scared, confess to everyone you beat Zhu Shuzhen to death!"
[ZH] 拳头之力带动气流，滑过林觉的面颊及五官，一股肌肤的醇香扑面而来。林觉深深吸一口气，沉浸了一会儿，直到内心的气焰被浇灭，才睁开双眼，看到朱淑真玉拳紧握，皮肤细腻白皙，散发着香气，他竟然看呆。
[EN] The breeze from her fist brushed his cheeks, carrying a subtle, clean fragrance of skin. Drawing an involuntary breath, Lin Jue felt his anger melt away; opening his eyes to behold her slender white fist poised inches from his face, he went completely starstruck.
[ZH] 千里寻见林觉的表情和目光怪怪的，让她很不舒服，迅速抽回拳头：“你愣着干什么？赶紧当众交代你的罪行！”
[EN] Creeped out by his weird, dreamy stare, Qianli Xun yanked her fist back. "What are you gawking at? Confess your crime in front of everyone!"
[ZH] 朱淑真抽回拳头时，林觉瞬间清醒，半真半假地编道：“娘子偷偷练习拳法，我也是最近才知，一直以为你只会写诗作词，没想到跟我也是同道中人，相信以后你我之间共同语言会多些。别再瞎胡闹，也别再说你不是淑真之类的话。”
[EN] Snapping back to earth, Lin Jue spun a half-truth on the spot: "I only learned recently my wife practiced martial arts in secret! Always thought she only wrote poems—turns out we share martial pursuits too! Surely we'll have more in common from now on. Quit acting wild and stop saying you aren't Shuzhen."
[ZH] 林觉的话让周围人松口气。
[EN] A collective sigh of relief swept the Lin elders.
[ZH] 林觉的二哥说：“弟妹，你为了和离什么话都敢说，不仅毁自己名声，又来毁我三弟名声，从你进林家门，就长这样，一点儿没变，说是别人，谁敢信。你既偷偷练了厉害的拳法，三弟不是你的对手，他能打你我真不信。”
[EN] Lin Jue's second brother shook his head: "Sister-in-law, you'll say anything for a divorce—trashing your own name and slandering Third Brother too! You look identical to the day you stepped through our doors—who'd swallow tales of another soul? And with fists like yours, Third Brother isn't even your match—no way he ever beat you!"
[ZH] 其他林家长辈也都跟着议论：“是啊！我们都不信！”
[EN] A chorus of elders chimed in: "Exactly! Nobody buys it!"
[ZH] 千里寻只好喊声：“苦荞！”
[EN] Desperate, Qianli Xun called out: "Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞走上前对大家说：“我家小姐原本不会任何拳法，只有姑爷打她的份儿。三天前，姑爷又打小姐，一把将小姐推到柱子跟前，额头撞伤，伤还在，大家都能看到。因为这伤，小姐晕死过去，怎么唤都不醒，后来终于醒来，人就变成现在这样，还会拳法，你们说奇怪不奇怪？我们乡下管这叫重生。”
[EN] Stepping forward, Kuqiao pleaded, "My Miss never knew a lick of martial arts before—only took Son-in-law's beatings! Three days ago he hit her again and shoved her into a pillar—look at the cut still on her forehead! She fainted dead away, wouldn't wake up for anything, and when she finally opened her eyes she turned into this and knew martial arts! Country folk call it rebirth!"
[ZH] 林觉大笑道：“哈哈……！苦荞是你的丫鬟，你想让她怎么说，她就会怎么说。娘子，消停些吧，老祖宗和林家长辈都很忙，还是赶紧让他们回去吧！”
[EN] Lin Jue barked a loud laugh. "Haha! Kuqiao's your own maid—she'll recite whatever script you feed her! Give it a rest, Wife. The Old Ancestor and our elders are busy folks—let's not hold them up!"
[ZH] 千里寻第一次遇到如此吃瘪之事，她很想用拳头打得林觉服服帖帖同意与她和离，就目前的情形看，拳头根本解决不了问题，该怎么办？她只能向老祖宗求救：“老祖宗。”
[EN] For the first time in her life, Qianli Xun hit a brick wall. Part of her longed to beat Lin Jue until he signed—yet fists couldn't unlock this mess. Helpless, she turned to her sole ally: "Old Ancestor..."
[ZH] 老祖宗站起来，目光停留在自己的鼻尖上：“世界之大，无奇不有，而人大多愚昧，重生之说不被认可倒也正常。以前的朱家姑娘我没见过，只见过眼前这位，无法替你和诸位分辨，今日和离我也无能为力，谁让这世道女人不能替自己做主。”
[EN] Rising to her feet, gaze resting serene upon the floor ahead, the Old Ancestor murmured: "The world is vast and holds every marvel, yet most mortals stay blind—no surprise nobody accepts rebirth. Never having met the old Zhu daughter, knowing only you before me, I cannot tell one from the other for these elders. Today I am powerless—such is a world where women hold no say over their own fate."
[ZH] 老祖宗带着秀珍嬷嬷走出会客厅。
[EN] With Matron Xiuzhen beside her, the Old Ancestor walked out of the reception hall.
[ZH] 林家的长辈和闫媒婆都站起来：“恭送老祖宗！”
[EN] Every Lin elder and Matchmaker Yan rose to bow: "Farewell, Old Ancestor!"
[ZH] 千里寻望着老祖宗的背影怅然若失，难道她必须跟林觉过下去？难道她的人生就此终结？
[EN] Watching her retreating silhouette, hollow defeat washed over Qianli Xun. Was she really trapped with Lin Jue forever? Was her life over before it began?
[ZH] 此时申简辰的身影出现在她的脑海，早知会发生这些事，不如答应他，与他来一场轰轰烈烈的恋爱，也许此刻不会如此失落难受。
[EN] Shen Jianchen's image flashed in her mind. Had she known this awaited her, she should have said yes to him and lived out a whirlwind romance—maybe she wouldn't feel this bleak now.
[ZH] 闫媒婆见老祖宗已走远，劝道：“少夫人，我得说你两句，你打练兵官，又绑又关的，他依然愿意跟你过下去，知足吧！既然和离不了，我还是赶紧走，有好几桩婚事等我出面，忙着呢！剩下的你们自家人关起门来处理吧。”
[EN] Once the Old Ancestor was gone, Matchmaker Yan offered parting advice: "Young Madam, word to the wise—you beat the Training Officer, tied him up, locked him away, and he still wants to stay married! Count your blessings! Since there's no divorce, I'm off—got several matches waiting on me! Sort the rest behind closed doors."
[ZH] 闫媒婆离开后，林家长辈们也没什么好说的，三叔一句：“大家都散了吧！”
[EN] Once the matchmaker left, Third Uncle waved a hand: "Everyone disperse!"
[ZH] 剩下的人也都离开，会客厅留下千里寻和林觉，还有苦荞。
[EN] One by one the clan filed out, leaving only Qianli Xun, Lin Jue, and Kuqiao in the hall.
[ZH] 千里寻面向林觉厉声道：“林觉！别以为你赢了，我会好好跟过日子！今日我把话撂这儿，以后不准踏进竹兰居半步！我想做什么就做什么，也不准干涉。你我住在林府西院，你这井水不要犯了我的河水，否则让你吃不了兜着走！还有，朱家给的嫁妆够老娘用的，老娘不需要你养活，以后见老娘最好远远避开！”
[EN] Rounding on Lin Jue with blazing eyes, Qianli Xun declared: "Don't think you won and I'm playing obedient wife, Lin Jue! Mark my words right here: never set half a foot inside Zhulan Residence! I go where I want, do what I want—don't dare meddle! We share the West Courtyard, so you stay in your lane and stay out of mine, or you'll regret it! My Zhu dowry keeps me rich—I don't need a coin from you! See me coming, walk the other way!"
[ZH] 林觉被气坏，站起身怒道：“朱淑真！你实在过分！……”
[EN] Furious, Lin Jue sprang up. "Zhu Shuzhen, you go too far...!"
[ZH] 林觉生气，千里寻更开心：“谁让你不愿跟我和离？这就是报应！别以为你不放我走，我会小鸟依人，任你摆布，哼！记住，我，千里寻，不是以前任你欺负的朱淑真！”
[EN] His rage lit a wicked grin on her face. "You refused the divorce—reap what you sow! Think holding me here turns me into a timid little bird obeying your every whim? Hmph! Remember this: I, Qianli Xun, am NOT the old Zhu Shuzhen you used to push around!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完，扬长而去；苦荞瞪了一眼姑爷，迅速跟上自家小姐。
[EN] Spinning on her heel, she strode out; Kuqiao shot Son-in-law a fierce glare and hurried after her mistress.
[ZH] 留下林觉一人享受胜利后的喜悦，好像不对，他此刻根本高兴不起来。
[EN] Left alone to savor his "victory," Lin Jue felt a strange emptiness—funny, he didn't feel victorious at all.

#CH 25
[ZH] 千里寻坚持涂抹吉祥药房买来的治伤之药，仅仅两日，额头的伤已好。她坐在铜镜前瞧着，担心留下疤痕，瞧来瞧去没发现一丝疤痕，以后不用再顶着伤到处招摇。
[EN] Applying Jixiang Pharmacy's healing ointment faithfully, Qianli Xun saw her forehead cut vanish clean in just two days. Examining herself in the bronze mirror, she found not a trace of a scar—no more walking around advertising a wound.
[ZH] 额头的皮肤白净细腻，本应开心，千里寻却怎么也高兴不起来。和离失败后，她还真有了朱淑真的多愁善感。
[EN] Smooth, clear skin should have cheered her up, yet gloom clung tight. After the failed divorce, she seemed to have caught Zhu Shuzhen's melancholy.
[ZH] 昨晚，做了一个梦，梦见申简辰，他在梦中说：“千里寻，做我女朋友吧！”她破天荒地点头答应。申简辰把她紧紧搂在怀里，她则用心体会着爱情到来时的甜蜜。申简辰的怀抱很温暖，她醒来时，觉得身上还有被他拥抱的感觉和温度。
[EN] Last night she had dreamed of Shen Jianchen. In the dream he asked, "Qianli Xun, be my girlfriend!"—and for once she nodded yes. He pulled her tight against his chest, and she tasted the sweet warmth of love for the very first time. His embrace felt so warm she woke convinced its heat still lingered across her shoulders.
[ZH] 经历和离之战，败下阵来，越是有失败的感觉，对爱情的渴望越强烈，千里寻萌生想要谈一场恋爱的念头，这是她在另一个时代从来没有过的想法。婚姻却像一堵厚厚的围墙，把她紧紧包围着，让她的感情不得自由。
[EN] Defeat in the divorce battle sharpened her hunger for real love—sparking a longing to fall in love that she had never felt back in her old life. Yet this unwanted marriage rose like a thick wall around her heart, barring every path to freedom.
[ZH] 千里寻不由叹一口气：“唉！”
[EN] A heavy sigh escaped her lips: "Ah..."
[ZH] 叹息过后，想起朱淑真的一首词，与此时此刻她的心境十分吻合：“苦荞，准备纸笔，我要作词。”
[EN] Right behind the sigh rose a lyric of Zhu Shuzhen's that fit her mood to the bone. "Kuqiao, lay out brush and paper—I want to write a ci."
[ZH] 苦荞端着刚做好的茶过来：“已准备好，小姐先喝茶再写不迟。”
[EN] Carrying fresh whisked tea over, Kuqiao smiled. "Already set up, Miss. Drink your tea first before writing."
[ZH] 千里寻坐在铜镜前将茶喝完，放下茶盏，起身走到书桌前坐下，提笔在砚台里蘸满墨汁，开始书写：
[EN] Drinking the tea by the mirror, Qianli Xun set the bowl aside, moved to the study desk, dipped her brush full in the inkstone, and wrote:
[ZH] 斜风细雨作春寒，对尊前，忆前欢。曾把梨花，寂寞泪阑干。芳草断烟南浦路，和别泪，看青山。
[EN] Slanting wind and fine rain chill the spring; before my wine cup, I recall old joys. Once before pear blossoms, lonely tears fell in streams. Fragrant grass fades in mist along the southern parting road; through farewell tears, I gaze at green hills.
[ZH] 昨宵结得梦夤缘，水云间，悄无言。争奈醒来，愁恨又依然。展转衾裯空懊恼，天易见，见伊难。《江城子·赏春》
[EN] Last night in dreams a tender tie was forged; amid water and clouds, we stood without a word. Yet waking finds sorrow and resentment waiting just as before. Tossing beneath the quilt in vain regret—Heaven is easy to behold, yet seeing you is hard. ("Jiangchengzi: Admiring the Spring") [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 写完后，一心沉浸在词句中，笔依然捏在指间，用心品着：“展转衾裯空懊恼，天易见，见伊难。”
[EN] Brush still poised in her fingers, she stayed lost in the closing line: "Tossing beneath the quilt in vain regret—Heaven is easy to behold, yet seeing you is hard."
[ZH] 申简辰到底有没有跟她一样穿越而来？如果有，人又在哪里？正想着，笔尖的墨汁不断向下聚集，“吧嗒”，不偏不倚掉在“伊”字上，辛苦写下这些字，白白浪费功夫。
[EN] Had Shen Jianchen transmigrated here too? And if he had, where in this vast world was he? As her thoughts wandered, excess ink gathered at the brush tip and—plop—dropped dead-center onto the character "you" (伊), ruining all her careful work.
[ZH] 千里寻搁下笔，把纸卷起：“扔了吧？”
[EN] Setting the brush down, she rolled up the sheet. "Throw it away."
[ZH] 苦荞接过，放入旁边的木桶中，端起木桶往外走。
[EN] Kuqiao took the scroll, tucked it into the wooden tub beside the desk, and hoisted the tub toward the door.
[ZH] 木桶里放满卷起来的纸张，千里寻好奇，问道：“里面怎么放了那么多？”
[EN] Noticing the tub brimmed with rolled sheets, Qianli Xun asked, "Why are there so many in there?"
[ZH] 苦荞回过头：“都是小姐平日写废的，让我积攒多了，拿到大门口放着。”
[EN] Kuqiao glanced back over her shoulder. "All your discarded practice drafts, Miss! You always have me save them up and leave the tub outside the front gate."
[ZH] 千里寻疑惑地问：“大门口放着？”
[EN] "Outside the front gate?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“小姐把它们当垃圾，有人却把它们当成宝贝，每隔一段时日会有人守在林府门外等着捡垃圾呢！以前在朱府也是。”
[EN] "What you call trash, outsiders treat like gold!" Kuqiao explained. "Every few days folks camp outside Lin Manor waiting to snatch up your scraps—same back at Zhu Manor!"
[ZH] 千里寻甚觉好笑，又懒得去理会，此时此刻她的内心充满莫名的忧伤。
[EN] Amused yet too low-spirited to care, Qianli Xun let it go as quiet sadness filled her chest.
[ZH] 苦荞端着木桶来到林府门外，把木桶放在地上，从里面抽出小姐刚刚写下的词，拿在手中，对周围喊道：“你们都出来吧！尽快拿走，我好去忙正事。”
[EN] Carrying the tub outside Lin Manor's main gate, Kuqiao set it down, pulled out the fresh lyric her mistress had just written, kept it in her hand, and called out: "Come on out, all of you! Grab what you want fast so I can get back to chores!"
[ZH] 话音刚落，不知从哪里窜出五六个小厮和书童，一拥而上，将木桶里的字画抢个精光，临走前不忘说句：“感谢苦荞姐姐！”
[EN] On cue, five or six page boys and study attendants popped out from corners and swarmed the tub, clearing every scrap in seconds while calling out, "Thanks, Sister Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞叫住一位小厮：“南双！你等会儿！”
[EN] Kuqiao stopped one page boy: "Nan Shuang! Hold up!"
[ZH] 南双回到苦荞身边：“苦荞姐姐有什么吩咐？”
[EN] Nan Shuang trotted back. "What can I do for you, Sister Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 苦荞问：“听说你家公子人在汴梁，你怎么没跟着去？”
[EN] "Heard your young master's up in Bianliang—why didn't you go with him?"
[ZH] “以前苦荞姐姐见我从不问我家公子的事，怎么今日突然关心起来？”
[EN] "You never asked about my master before, Sister Kuqiao—what's got you curious today?"
[ZH] “以前没想起来，现在想起了呗！问那么多干嘛？”
[EN] "Didn't think of it before, thought of it today! Quit asking questions!"
[ZH] “姐姐也不想想，我要是去了，谁帮我家公子搜集幽栖居士的诗词？”南双展开拿到的纸张，“只可惜今日手气不好，纸上只有两句。”
[EN] "Think about it, Sister—if I left, who'd collect Youqi Jushi's poems for my master?" Unrolling the scrap he had grabbed, Nan Shuang sighed: "Rotten luck today—only two lines on this sheet."
[ZH] 苦荞把手中的纸递给南双：“我家小姐刚写的，很完整，送给你家公子。”
[EN] Kuqiao handed him the intact scroll from her hand. "Miss just wrote this—complete from start to finish. Give it to your master."
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐为何对我家公子这么好？”
[EN] "Why so generous to my master today, Sister Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “还用问？乔公子最会解我家小姐的诗词，他们视对方为知己。”
[EN] "Need you ask? Young Master Qiao reads my Miss's poetry deeper than anyone—they're true confidants."
[ZH] “既如此，不如让你家姑娘直接把写好诗词送给我家公子得了，也省得我整日在林府门外蹲守。”
[EN] "Then why doesn't your mistress just send her finished poems straight to my master instead of making me camp outside Lin Manor every day?"
[ZH] “那怎么行？本来外面就有我家小姐跟你家公子的风言风语，把没有的事都说出事来，所以更需谨慎，你以后还是在门口蹲守吧，有好的，我自然会给你。”
[EN] "Not a chance! Enough rumors already swirl outside twisting nothing into gossip about Miss and your master—better stay cautious! Keep waiting by the gate; whenever something good turns up, I'll slip it to you."
[ZH] 南双谢过后离开，苦荞端起木桶往回走，半路上碰到姑爷。
[EN] Thanking her, Nan Shuang darted off. Lifting the empty tub, Kuqiao headed back inside—and ran straight into Lin Jue halfway down the lane.
[ZH] 林觉没好气地质问：“又有人捡拾没用的垃圾？”
[EN] "More people scavenging useless trash?" Lin Jue snapped.
[ZH] 苦荞没好气地说：“回姑爷，在您看来是垃圾，外面的人却当成宝。”
[EN] "Trash to you, Son-in-law—pure treasure to everyone outside," Kuqiao shot back.
[ZH] 林觉气道：“我今日就派士兵守在府外，看谁敢来，来一个抓一个，来两个抓一双。”
[EN] Lin Jue bristled. "Watch me station soldiers outside the gate today—arrest every scavenger who shows up, one by one or two by two!"
[ZH] “我劝姑爷不要招惹与我家小姐相关的任何事，只怕她知道，再用拳头打你。”苦荞说完，继续往里走。
[EN] "Better leave anything touching my Miss alone, Son-in-law—she hears about it, she'll give you another taste of her fists!" With that, Kuqiao walked straight past him.
[ZH] 林觉盯着苦荞的背影，甚是气愤：“这丫头以前不这样说话，越来越放肆！真是有其主必有其仆！”
[EN] Glaring at her retreating back, Lin Jue fumed: That maid never used to mouth off like this—getting bolder by the day! Like mistress, like servant!
[ZH] 这两日林觉也思考，不放走朱淑真到底是对还是错？从理性角度讲是错，从感性讲，他舍不得放她离开，哪怕把她搁在林府，一根毫毛不碰，只要还挂着林觉娘子的名号，他都会觉得受用。尤其是临安才子们对朱淑真只能看在眼里，却吃不到嘴里，林觉想想都觉得面上有光。当然，最重要的是他对她依然有情。
[EN] Over the past two days, Lin Jue had wondered himself whether refusing the divorce was right or wrong. Reason said wrong; his heart said he couldn't let her go. Even keeping her untouched inside Zhulan Residence, as long as she bore the name of Lin Jue's wife, it satisfied his pride—especially knowing all those Lin'an scholars could only gaze from afar and never have her! Above all, he still loved her.
[ZH] 苦荞回到竹兰居，见小姐坐在书桌前发呆，没敢过去打扰，放下木桶，开始收拾屋子。
[EN] Back inside Zhulan Residence, seeing her mistress staring blankly at the desk, Kuqiao set the tub down and tidied the room without disturbing her.
[ZH] 千里寻穿越成朱淑真，竟然开始像她一样多愁善感。她猛然想起朱淑真四十五岁离开人世，她千里寻可不想英年早逝。既然不想，就不该学朱淑真，要记住自己是千里寻。
[EN] Transmigrating into Zhu Shuzhen had actually infected Qianli Xun with her gloom! Wait—Zhu Shuzhen had died around forty-five! Qianli Xun had zero intention of dying young and heartbroken! Time to stop acting like Zhu Shuzhen and remember who she was.
[ZH] 她拍拍脑门，心里想：“对啊！我是千里寻，千里寻可从来不伤春悲秋，振作起来，仔细想想，接下来的人生该怎么过，我可不愿像朱淑真四十多岁郁郁而终。”
[EN] Slapping her own forehead, she pep-talked herself: Wake up! You're Qianli Xun—since when did Qianli Xun mope over spring winds and autumn rain?! Pull yourself together and plan your next move! No way am I fading away in my forties!
[ZH] 千里寻立即振作精神，她要付诸行动改变命运，到底该怎么做？暂时想不到更好的办法，不如迷信一回。
[EN] Energy surged right back. Time to take action and reshape her fate—and lacking a concrete plan yet, why not try a bit of superstition?
[ZH] “苦荞，你认不认识看相算命特别准的人？”
[EN] "Kuqiao, know any fortune-teller around here who's crazy accurate?"
[ZH] 苦荞停下手中的活儿：“小姐可是问对人，我虽不认识，却知道有这么一位。临安城外有座山，山上有个尼姑庵，里面的女住持人称净光法师，能知过去未来，老爷就曾找她看过。”
[EN] Pausing her chores, Kuqiao brightened. "You asked the right person, Miss! Don't know her personally, yet everybody knows her name: on a hill outside Lin'an stands a nunnery whose abbess, Master Jingguang, sees past and future! Even Master visited her once."
[ZH] “我爹？”
[EN] "My dad?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“我听说，老爷那会儿年轻，与净光法师聊过后，一路青云直上，才成为今日的临安知府。”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded. "Word is back when Master was young, after one talk with Master Jingguang his career shot straight up till he became Prefect of Lin'an!"
[ZH] “这么厉害！”
[EN] "That legit?!"
[ZH] 苦荞接着说：“不过，据说净光法师不随便给人看，她有条件，只有达到她的条件才能与她见上一面。”
[EN] "Only catch is Master Jingguang doesn't see just anyone—she sets conditions, and you only get an audience if you meet them."
[ZH] “什么条件？”
[EN] "What conditions?"
[ZH] 苦荞为难道：“嗯，我不大清楚。”
[EN] Kuqiao hesitated. "Not quite sure..."
[ZH] 千里寻来了兴致，站起来：“我要去会会净光法师，多带些银子。”
[EN] Fired up, Qianli Xun sprang to her feet. "Let's go meet Master Jingguang! Pack plenty of silver!"
[ZH] “这就要去吗？”
[EN] "Right now?"
[ZH] “现在就去！”
[EN] "Right this second!"
[ZH] 苦荞带好银子，跟在小姐身后，来到林府门外，吩咐车夫套了马车，往临安城外而去。
[EN] Packing a pouch of silver, Kuqiao trailed her mistress out of Lin Manor, ordered the coachman to hitch the carriage, and rode out of Lin'an.
[ZH] 千里寻无心欣赏街头闹市的繁华，她只想为自己的人生找到一线曙光，更想知道还有没有机会摆脱与林觉的婚姻，能不能在属于朱淑真的时代有属于千里寻的人生。
[EN] Ignoring the lively market streets along the way, Qianli Xun focused on one wish: finding a ray of hope for her future, learning whether she'd ever break free from Lin Jue and carve out Qianli Xun's own destiny in Zhu Shuzhen's era.
[ZH] 不知马车走了多久，终于出了临安城；也不知在临安城外又走了多久，终于到达山脚下。
[EN] After a long ride out through the city gates and further along country roads, the carriage rolled to a stop at the foot of a mountain.
[ZH] 车夫提醒道：“夫人，到了。”
[EN] "We're here, Madam," the coachman called.
[ZH] 千里寻第一个跳下马车，一座像刷满绿色彩釉的高山矗立在眼前。
[EN] Hopping down first, Qianli Xun looked up at a towering hill glazed lush green from base to peak.
[ZH] 苦荞从马车上下来后，指着大山说：“小姐，尼姑庵就在半山腰，剩下的路马车上不去，我们得步行。”
[EN] Climbing down behind her, Kuqiao pointed upward. "The nunnery sits halfway up the mountain, Miss—carriages can't climb the trail, so we walk from here."
[ZH] 千里寻没有说话，朝大山的入口处走去。她从小练习千里拳，爬山对她来讲并不费体力。尽管现在的身体不是千里寻的，但她的潜意识依然能够操纵朱淑真的身体，让它和千里寻的身体同样敏捷。能顺利使出千里拳，力度丝毫不减，就是最好的证明。
[EN] Without a word, Qianli Xun strode straight onto the trailhead. Years of Qianli Fist made hill climbing effortless; though this body belonged to Zhu Shuzhen, Qianli Xun's instincts steered its reflexes just as nimbly—her full-power punches proved as much.
[ZH] 山路并不宽敞，又窄又弯曲，从山路的泥土被踩得结实程度判断，尼姑庵的香火应该不错。
[EN] The winding mountain track ran narrow, its dirt packed hard by countless feet—clearly the nunnery drew heavy traffic.
[ZH] 千里寻看到有五六个香客顺着山路往下走。当第一个香客走近时，千里寻和苦荞侧身让路，顺便问道：“大娘，尼姑庵还有多远？”
[EN] Five or six pilgrims descended toward them. Stepping aside to let the first pass, Qianli Xun asked, "How much further to the nunnery, Auntie?"
[ZH] “不远不远，再走一阵就到。”大娘继续往下走。
[EN] "Not far at all—just a bit further up!" the woman called back as she continued down.
[ZH] 千里寻和苦荞则继续向上爬，碰到其他香客也是侧身让路，只怪山路太窄，仅能容纳一人行走。
[EN] Climbing on, they stepped aside several more times along the single-file path.
[ZH] 山路周围的景致不错，千里寻边走边欣赏，不知不觉来到半山腰，才有平坦之地，视野变得开阔起来。一座尼姑庵出现在眼前，门口是进进出出的香客，男男女女均有，还有卖香火的摊位。
[EN] Taking in the lush hill views as they went, Qianli Xun reached a broad mid-mountain clearing before she knew it. Ahead stood a nunnery bustling with men and women coming and going, incense stalls lining the entrance.
[ZH] 千里寻抬头看到门楣上的匾额写着“承佛慈力”四字：“苦荞，买些香火，随我进去拜拜。”
[EN] Glancing up at the plaque reading "By the Compassionate Power of the Buddha," Qianli Xun said, "Buy some incense, Kuqiao—let's go pay respects."
[ZH] 苦荞买来三柱高香，跟着小姐走进尼姑庵。
[EN] Buying three tall sticks of incense, Kuqiao followed her mistress through the gate.
[ZH] 迎面是正殿，很多人在殿外先把香点燃，再一一插入大香炉中。
[EN] Outside the main hall stood a huge bronze censer where pilgrims lit incense and planted their sticks.
[ZH] 苦荞照做，完成后，又跟随小姐走进正殿内。
[EN] Kuqiao did the same before following her mistress into the hall.
[ZH] 刚把脚踏进去，两边传来念佛的声音：“南无阿弥陀佛，南无阿弥陀佛……”声音很好听，像是在吟唱一首歌曲，让人听后不由心境舒畅。
[EN] The instant they stepped across the threshold, chanting rose from both sides: "Namo Amitabha, Namo Amitabha..." Flowing like calm song, the cadence soothed the heart on contact.
[ZH] 千里寻向两边瞧去，各盘腿坐着六名女尼，神态自然安详，口中念念有词，吟唱的声音正是从她们口中发出。
[EN] Glancing left and right, Qianli Xun saw six nuns seated cross-legged along each side, serene and composed, chanting in unison.
[ZH] 高大的神像前面放着三个蒲团，千里寻合掌跪拜；苦荞也学着小姐的样子拜起来。她们每拜一下，会响起一声敲击木鱼的声音“梆！”伴着佛号，甚是和谐。
[EN] Before the towering Buddha statue lay three prayer cushions. Qianli Xun pressed her palms together and knelt to bow; Kuqiao followed suit. Each bow drew a resonant clack from a wooden fish—"tok!"—blending seamless with the chant.
[ZH] 三拜之后，两人起身，朝木鱼声传来的方向瞧去，佛像旁边坐着一位女尼，她面前的桌上放着木鱼和犍稚。
[EN] Rising after three bows, they looked toward the sound and saw a young nun seated beside the statue with a wooden fish and mallet before her.
[ZH] 千里寻走上前有礼道：“小师傅，我想找住持净光法师，不知她今日可在？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped over politely. "Little Master, I'd like to see Abbess Master Jingguang—is she available today?"
[ZH] 女尼起身说：“跟我来。”
[EN] The young nun rose at once. "Follow me."
[ZH] 千里寻一阵庆幸，没想到如此容易，心想苦荞得到的信息应该有误，不由盯了一眼苦荞。
[EN] Thrilled at how easy that was—guess Kuqiao's rumors were overblown—Qianli Xun shot Kuqiao a teasing glance.
[ZH] ﻿苦荞接触到小姐的目光，有些不好意思，更为能顺利见到住持感到高兴。
[EN] Flushing slightly at being proved wrong, Kuqiao grinned anyway, glad her mistress got her wish so fast.
[ZH] ﻿她们跟上女尼向正殿后面走去。
[EN] Together they followed the young nun behind the main hall.

#CH 26
[ZH] 正殿后面除供香客烧香跪拜的殿阁外，两边是寮房。
[EN] Behind the main hall stood smaller worship halls flanked on both sides by monastic living quarters.
[ZH] 女尼掀开一个房间的布帘：“两位请。”
[EN] The young nun lifted the cloth curtain of one room. "Please step inside."
[ZH] 千里寻认为主持应该就在里面，可走进去后，除了桌椅茶具，空无一人。
[EN] Expecting the abbess to be waiting, Qianli Xun entered—only to find an empty room with a table, chairs, and tea set.
[ZH] 女尼说：“二位在此稍侯，我去请慈玄法师。”
[EN] "Wait a moment here," the young nun said. "I'll fetch Master Cixuan."
[ZH] 千里寻强调道：“小师傅，我找净光法师，不找慈玄法师。”
[EN] "Little Master, I asked for Master Jingguang, not Master Cixuan!" Qianli Xun corrected her.
[ZH] 女尼没有说话，掀开帘子走出去。
[EN] Without answering, the young nun ducked back past the curtain.
[ZH] 千里寻只得找椅子坐下，耐心等候。
[EN] Nothing to do but pull up a chair and wait.
[ZH] 大约过了一刻钟，布帘被掀开，进来一名年纪较大的女尼，手拿佛珠：“阿弥陀佛，让施主久等。”
[EN] About a quarter hour later, the curtain lifted and an older nun entered holding prayer beads. "Amitabha. Forgive the wait, benefactor."
[ZH] 千里寻起身问：“您是慈玄法师？”
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun asked, "You're Master Cixuan?"
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] "I am."
[ZH] “我想拜见净光法师，请她为我指点迷津。”
[EN] "I came to beg an audience with Master Jingguang—hoping she'll point the way through my troubles."
[ZH] “住持她老人家不随便接见外人……”
[EN] "The Abbess does not receive outside visitors lightly..."
[ZH] 千里寻急了：“不随便接见外人？为何刚才的小师傅要带我进来？”
[EN] Qianli Xun flared in alarm. "Not lightly? Then why did that young nun bring me back here?!"
[ZH] “她刚刚剃度出家，在正殿负责敲击木鱼，有些规矩不清楚，我已批评过她。”
[EN] "She only recently took vows and tends the wooden fish in the main hall; she didn't know the rules. I have already reprimanded her."
[ZH] 千里寻直接傻眼，本以为很顺利，原来是这么回事。进都进来，总不能白跑一趟。
[EN] Stumped—so much for smooth sailing! Having climbed all the way up here, she wasn't leaving empty-handed.
[ZH] “求法师通融，要不是遇到人生难题，我也不敢随便打扰住持。”
[EN] "Please make an exception, Master! I wouldn't dare trouble the Abbess if I weren't facing a crisis of a lifetime."
[ZH] “请施主下山去吧。”
[EN] "Please head back down the mountain, benefactor."
[ZH] 千里寻给苦荞使使眼色，苦荞拿出一两银子塞给慈玄法师：“请法师笑纳。”
[EN] Qianli Xun signaled Kuqiao with her eyes. Kuqiao slipped out one tael of silver and pressed it toward Master Cixuan. "A small offering, Master—please accept."
[ZH] “出家人不贪图世间财物，施主还是收回去吧。”
[EN] "Monastics do not covet worldly wealth. Take it back, benefactor."
[ZH] 慈玄面无表情，给人难以接近之感，苦荞只得把银子收回，用目光向小姐请示。
[EN] Master Cixuan's face stayed cold as stone; pulling the silver back, Kuqiao glanced at her mistress for orders.
[ZH] 千里寻以为对方嫌银子给得少，又给苦荞使眼色。
[EN] Thinking one tael looked stingy, Qianli Xun gave another nod.
[ZH] 苦荞从怀里拿出一袋银子，把那一两也放进去，再把钱袋递到慈玄面前：“法师还是笑纳吧。”
[EN] Kuqiao drew the entire silver pouch, dropped the single tael back inside, and offered the whole bag. "Please accept our sincere offering, Master."
[ZH] 慈玄依旧面无表情，苦荞只好又拿回银子。
[EN] Not a muscle twitched on Master Cixuan's face; Kuqiao tucked the pouch away again.
[ZH] 千里寻见钱无用，立刻跪在地上，苦荞也跟着跪下。
[EN] Seeing silver fail, Qianli Xun dropped straight to her knees, Kuqiao dropping right beside her.
[ZH] “今日不见到净光法师，我绝不下山，恳请慈玄法师通融。”
[EN] "I'm not leaving this mountain until I see Master Jingguang! Please grant this favor, Master Cixuan!"
[ZH] 千里寻下跪倒是让慈玄感到意外，好言劝道：“施主起来下山去吧，住持她年事已高，不愿再为世俗之人预知未来。昨日一位临安高官前来想见住持，也被我请下山去。”
[EN] Surprised by the abrupt kneel, Master Cixuan spoke milder: "Rise and go home, benefactor. The Abbess is advanced in years and no longer reads fortunes for laypeople. Just yesterday a senior Lin'an official came seeking her, and I sent him down the mountain too."
[ZH] “我不起来，也不下山，净光法师不见我，我就永远跪在这里，反正回到现在的家里还不如待在寺庙，再不济我也剃度出家，不怕见不到住持。”
[EN] "I'm not getting up and I'm not going down! If Master Jingguang won't see me, I'll kneel here forever—going back to that house is worse than staying in a nunnery anyway! Push me and I'll shave my head right here; then you can't keep me from seeing the Abbess!"
[ZH] 千里寻十分坚决，搞得慈玄法师为难起来。
[EN] Her iron stubbornness put Master Cixuan in a real bind.
[ZH] 正犹豫间，进来一名女尼，年纪看上去与慈玄相仿，在慈玄耳边悄悄说句话，很快又出去。
[EN] Just as she hesitated, another nun of about the same age slipped in, whispered a single line in Cixuan's ear, and slipped straight back out.
[ZH] 慈玄面色缓和许多：“起来吧，看在你与住持有缘，请跟我来。”
[EN] Master Cixuan's stony face softened at once. "Rise. Fate links you to the Abbess—follow me."
[ZH] 终于有进展，千里寻很开心，起身跟着走出寮房，心想这回肯定能见到住持尊容，刚才慈玄都说了，她与住持有缘。
[EN] Breakthrough! Beaming, Qianli Xun climbed up and followed her out of the quarters—sure she was about to meet the Abbess face to face now that fate had spoken.
[ZH] 千里寻跟着慈玄穿过一个石门，向东边拐去，走了没多久，来到两个山洞跟前。
[EN] Passing through a stone archway and turning east, they soon came upon two mountain caves side by side.
[ZH] 慈玄掀开左边山洞的布帘：“施主，请。”
[EN] Master Cixuan lifted the curtain over the left cave. "Please enter, benefactor."
[ZH] 千里寻带着苦荞走进后，并未看到任何人影。
[EN] Leading Kuqiao inside, Qianli Xun found not a single soul.
[ZH] 迎面是尊佛像，前面放着一个很大的功德箱；旁边有个茶桌，上面摆着做茶的器具和材料；左边有一个书桌，笔墨纸砚放置得整整齐齐；再瞧两面墙壁，挂满唐诗，与佛像显得很不相称，不过字倒写得圆润饱满，不失风骨。
[EN] Straight ahead stood a Buddha statue fronted by a large donation box; beside it sat a tea table stocked with whisking gear and supplies; to the left stood a study desk with the Four Treasures of the Study neatly lined up; and along both walls hung Tang poems—odd decor next to a Buddha, though written in rich, vigorous calligraphy.
[ZH] 千里寻无心欣赏书法与诗，问道：“住持呢？”
[EN] Paying no mind to art or verse, Qianli Xun asked, "Where's the Abbess?"
[ZH] “住持在右边的山洞，施主想见住持，缘是第一步，还需完成三件事，得到住持允许，方可与她相见。”
[EN] "In the right-hand cave. Fate is only step one—to meet the Abbess, you must pass three tests and win her approval."
[ZH] 慈玄法师的话让千里寻感到前路障碍重重，好不容易走到这一步，还需接着往下走。
[EN] Hoop after hoop! Having come this far, she wasn't turning back now.
[ZH] “请法师说说是哪三件事？”
[EN] "Name the three tests, Master."
[ZH] “第一件事很简单，只需将施主身上的钱财以及所有值钱之物统统放入功德箱。”
[EN] "First is simple: place every coin and every valuable item on your person into the donation box."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“确实简单，她刚才不为钱财所动，原来是为将我洗劫一空。算了，不跟她计较！”想到这里，千里寻叫声：“苦荞。”
[EN] Simple indeed! Qianli Xun thought wryly: Refused my silver earlier just to clean me out completely here! Fine, whatever! "Kuqiao!" she called.
[ZH] 苦荞赶紧把身上带的银子全部拿出，放入功德箱中。
[EN] Kuqiao emptied every last coin they had brought straight into the donation box.
[ZH] 千里寻又将玉镯，耳坠，以及头上的金钗统统取下，放入功德箱内。
[EN] Qianli Xun stripped off her jade bangle, earrings, and gold hairpins and dropped them all inside.
[ZH] “法师，第一件事已做完，快说第二件吧。”
[EN] "Test one done, Master—what's number two?"
[ZH] 慈玄法师说：“你那丫鬟身上还有贵重之物。”
[EN] "Your maid still wears something of value," Master Cixuan pointed out.
[ZH] 苦荞为难道：“是小姐要见住持，又不是我，为何还要我身上唯一值钱的银镯子？”
[EN] Kuqiao balked. "Miss is the one seeing the Abbess, not me—why take my one and only silver bracelet?!"
[ZH] 千里寻走到苦荞身边，抓起她的右手，掀开袖子，果然手腕上有个银镯子，强行取下：“回头我送你好几个。”
[EN] Stepping over, Qianli Xun grabbed Kuqiao's right wrist, pushed back her sleeve, and slipped the silver bracelet off. "I'll buy you a whole stack later!"
[ZH] 苦荞不再为难：“小姐说话可要算话。”
[EN] Relieved, Kuqiao grumbled, "You'd better keep your word, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻把银镯放进功德箱：“放心，你家小姐我一诺千金。”
[EN] Dropping the bracelet into the box, Qianli Xun promised, "A promise worth a thousand gold!"
[ZH] 慈玄满意道：“这第二件需施主在墙上挑一首诗，将解诗的内容写在纸上，由我呈给住持，她老人家满意，方可进行第三件，如若她老人家不满意，也请即刻下山。”
[EN] Satisfied, Master Cixuan moved on: "Second: pick any poem on the wall, write out your interpretation on paper, and I will present it to the Abbess. If she approves, you advance to test three; if not, leave the mountain at once."
[ZH] 解诗对千里寻来讲并非难事，她再次环顾两面墙上的唐诗。
[EN] Analyzing poetry? Easy! She scanned the Tang poems lining both walls.
[ZH] 以前的千里寻对王右丞的诗并不感兴趣，不知为何此刻的她被那首《竹里馆》吸引住。她靠近墙边，细心琢磨诗句，内心文思如泉涌，转过身，走到书桌前坐下，提笔写下所思所想。
[EN] Before transmigrating, Qianli Xun hadn't cared much for Wang Wei—yet right now his "The Bamboo Pavilion" pulled her eyes like a magnet. Stepping close to study the lines, ideas surged into her head; turning to the desk, she sat down and poured every thought onto paper.
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐写得非常专注，心中暗喜：“现在的小姐看上去跟从前没有任何区别。”
[EN] Watching her mistress write with deep focus, Kuqiao smiled inside: Right now she looks identical to the old Miss!
[ZH] 千里寻用小楷写了整整一大张纸，终于写完，搁下笔：“法师，辛苦您拿给住持阅览。”
[EN] Filling a full sheet in crisp small regular script, Qianli Xun set down her brush. "Please deliver this to the Abbess, Master."
[ZH] 苦荞过去把纸张拿了，送到慈玄法师手中。慈玄接过，简单折起后，掀开布帘离开。
[EN] Kuqiao carried the sheet over to Master Cixuan, who folded it neat and stepped past the curtain.
[ZH] 没多久，慈玄空手而归：“恭喜施主，住持对您解的诗很满意，可以进行第三件。”
[EN] Minutes later Cixuan returned empty-handed. "Congratulations, benefactor—the Abbess approved your essay. Ready for test three."
[ZH] 千里寻早已预料到结果，别的她不敢说，解个把诗倒不在话下：“第三件是什么？”
[EN] No surprise there—literary essays were her bread and butter. "What's test three?"
[ZH] “第三件是为住持做盏茶，住持品后若满意，便可与她老人家见面，若不满意还请施主下山。”
[EN] "Whisk a bowl of tea for the Abbess. If she approves upon tasting, you may meet her; if not, descend the mountain."
[ZH] 做茶是苦荞的强项，千里寻还未学会：“可否让我的丫鬟来做？”
[EN] Whisking tea was Kuqiao's specialty—Qianli Xun hadn't learned a lick yet! "Can my maid make it?"
[ZH] “不行，是你求事，又不是你的丫鬟，必须施主亲自动手。”
[EN] "No. You seek the audience, not your maid. You must prepare it with your own hands."
[ZH] “能不能让我的丫鬟在一旁指点。”
[EN] "Can my maid stand beside me and talk me through it?"
[ZH] “也不行，在一旁指点跟她做没什么区别。”
[EN] "No—that is no different from her making it herself."
[ZH] 千里寻只能离开书桌，来到茶桌跟前，看到一桌子的茶具，不知该如何下手。努力回忆苦荞做茶的过程，好像需先温盏。千里寻拿起一个茶盏，放到面前，从旁边壶里倒些热水，再将盏双手捧着晃几下，倒还有些意思。晃完，四处瞧瞧，不知道该把盏中的水倒在何处。
[EN] Leaving the desk for the tea table, Qianli Xun stared blankly at the array of tools. Straining to recall Kuqiao's moves—first, warm the tea bowl! Picking up a bowl, she poured in hot water from the kettle and swirled it in both hands—kind of fun! Done swirling, she looked around clueless where to dump the rinse water.
[ZH] 苦荞急得在心里提醒：“废水盂就在旁边，倒里面就行!”
[EN] Kuqiao screamed inside her head: The waste water bowl is RIGHT THERE! Dump it in there!
[ZH] 结果看到小姐盯着地面瞧了一会儿，直接把水倒在地上，苦荞一脸无奈。
[EN] Instead, her mistress glanced at the floor and splashed the water straight onto the ground, leaving Kuqiao staring in despair.
[ZH] 千里寻把盏放好，接下来该怎么做？对，放茶粉！她拿过茶粉盒，用小木勺给盏中放一勺茶粉，感觉有些少，又一连放好几勺。
[EN] Setting the bowl down—next step: tea powder! Opening the caddy, she scooped one wooden spoonful in, thought it looked skimpy, and dumped several more big scoops on top.
[ZH] 苦荞又在心里提醒：“多了！放多了！”
[EN] Too much! Way too much! Kuqiao wailed silently.
[ZH] 接下来该注入热水，千里寻记得苦荞做茶时，分好几次将水注入，到底多少次，她不记得。
[EN] Next, hot water! She remembered Kuqiao adding water in rounds—how many rounds though?
[ZH] 常言道事不过三，那就三次吧。既然三次要把盏倒满，第一次不能多也不能少。千里寻按照自己的判断，将水加入，她认为不多不少，再倒两次，则刚刚好。
[EN] Things come in threes—so three rounds it is! If three pours had to fill the bowl, round one needed a hefty splash. Going by gut, she poured in a third of a bowl.
[ZH] 可急坏一旁的苦荞，继续在心里提醒：“水倒多了！倒多了！”
[EN] Too much water! You flooded it! Kuqiao nearly fainted inside.
[ZH] 千里寻拿起一旁的茶筅，在盏中开始搅动。
[EN] Grabbing the bamboo whisk, Qianli Xun started stirring in circles.
[ZH] “击拂，击拂，不是胡乱搅！”苦荞不忍心看下去，她捂住眼睛。做茶的方法曾是小姐教她的，现在的小姐竟然一点儿都不会。捂了一会儿，还是担心小姐，又把手放下，见小姐仅倒三次水，再胡乱搅几下，茶算是做好。
[EN] Whisk back and forth—not stir like soup! Unable to watch another second, Kuqiao clapped both hands over her eyes. Miss herself taught me how to whisk tea—how did she forget every single step?! Peeking through her fingers, she saw her mistress finish in three pours and a few clumsy stirs.
[ZH] “七次注汤，在小姐这里变成三次注汤，做出的能是茶吗？”苦荞继续着心里的话语，瞧一眼旁边的慈玄法师，她一脸严肃，眼睛虽盯着鼻端，可也让苦荞不敢妄动。
[EN] Seven infusions turned into three splashes—can you even call that tea?! Kuqiao lamented silently, glancing at stone-faced Master Cixuan, whose strict posture kept the maid rooted in place.
[ZH] 千里寻看着盏中的茶，跟苦荞做的无法相比，简直一个天上一个地下，茶汤中零散地躺着些许细沫，让人一看，产生不了一点儿品茶的欲望。
[EN] Qianli Xun peered down at her creation: worlds apart from Kuqiao's creamy froth, just murky liquid dotted with pathetic specks of foam nobody in their right mind would drink.
[ZH] 能做到这种程度，千里寻算是尽力。她已想好，如果住持因此不愿见她，就冲进隔壁的山洞，跪在住持面前哭诉，不相信打动不了她。打定主意后，吩咐道：“苦荞，把茶盏端给法师。”
[EN] Oh well—she'd given it her best shot. Plan B was already set: if the Abbess turned her away over bad tea, she'd storm straight into the next cave, drop to her knees, and cry until the Abbess caved! Mind made up, she ordered, "Kuqiao, hand the bowl to Master."
[ZH] 苦荞上前端起茶盏，瞧那茶汤，心想：“这哪是给人喝的？”
[EN] Lifting the bowl and peeking inside, Kuqiao thought: Who on earth could drink this?!
[ZH] 慈玄接过，也瞧了瞧茶汤，眉头不由皱起，没说一句话，出了山洞。
[EN] Master Cixuan took it, glanced at the brew, knitted her brows without a word, and walked out.
[ZH] 千里寻起身对苦荞说：“我做的茶住持肯定瞧不上，一会儿我要冲进隔壁的山洞。”
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun whispered to Kuqiao, "No way the Abbess likes my tea—in a minute I'm rushing straight into the next cave!"
[ZH] 苦荞很担忧：“能行吗？”
[EN] "Will that even work?" Kuqiao worried.
[ZH] “怎么不行？世间之事，有时太遵守规矩反而办不成，必须出其不意，才能取得意想不到的效果。待会儿慈玄法师回来，只要说让我们下山，你我假装同意乖乖走出山洞，趁她不注意，你帮我拽住她，我瞅准机会冲进去。”
[EN] "Sure it will! Playing by the rules gets you nowhere sometimes—you've got to catch folks off guard! The second Master Cixuan tells us to leave, we play nice and step outside; the instant she looks away, you grab her arm and I bolt straight inside!"
[ZH] 苦荞点头同意。
[EN] Kuqiao nodded.
[ZH] 很快慈玄法师掀开布帘进入，还未张口，千里寻立刻说：“法师不用说，肯定因为我做的茶不好，住持不愿见我，我这就带丫鬟下山。”说完顺便向苦荞使了个眼色。
[EN] Moments later Master Cixuan parted the curtain. Before she could open her mouth, Qianli Xun jumped in: "Don't bother saying it, Master—my tea was awful, the Abbess won't see me, we'll pack up and go!" She shot Kuqiao the signal glance.
[ZH] 慈玄法师笑道：“住持允许施主进山洞与她见上一面。”
[EN] A faint smile broke across Master Cixuan's face. "The Abbess grants you leave to enter her cave for an audience."
[ZH] 真是喜出望外，那么差的一盏茶住持竟然不嫌弃？看来她老人家在茶方面要求不高，既如此又何必搞这一出？反正能见面是好事，既免去千里寻采取莽撞的方式达到目的，也保住朱淑真大家闺秀的风范。
[EN] Jackpot! The Abbess accepted that swamp water?! Guess she wasn't picky about tea—then why make her whisk it in the first place? Never mind! Getting in without causing a scene saved both the effort and Zhu Shuzhen's ladylike image.

#CH 27
[ZH] 从外面看，两个山洞没有任何差异，唯一不同的是，净光法师的山洞窗户半开着。千里寻试图通过窗户瞧见里面的情景，可里面光线很暗，什么也瞧不到。
[EN] From outside, both caves looked identical—except Master Jingguang's cave had its wooden window propped half open. Peering inside, Qianli Xun saw only pitch darkness.
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷，刚才的山洞没有开窗户，里面挺亮堂，为何住持的山洞光线如此暗？仔细一想，明白过来，刚才的山洞内点着几盏油灯，怪她忙着应付三件事，没有在其他事情上用心思。
[EN] Weird—why was the first cave bright with shut windows while the Abbess's cave was dark with an open one? Then she remembered: oil lamps had been burning inside the first cave; too busy with her three trials, she hadn't even noticed.
[ZH] 正想着，一只蝴蝶从窗户里飞出来。千里寻从未见过这么大这么漂亮的蝴蝶，它的翅膀是蓝色的，翅膀的边缘呈黑色，扑闪着来到她面前，绕着她飞了一圈儿。她伸出手想要去抓，结果蝴蝶落在她的手背上，搞得她不忍心再去捉它，只用爱怜的目光欣赏着蝴蝶之美。
[EN] Just then a butterfly drifted straight out of the half-open window—bigger and lovelier than any butterfly Qianli Xun had ever seen, electric blue wings edged in velvet black. Fluttering in a circle around her, it landed right on the back of her outstretched hand instead of fleeing, softening her impulse to catch it into quiet awe.
[ZH] 慈玄法师向洞内说：“住持，女施主已在洞外等候。”
[EN] Master Cixuan called toward the cave: "Abbess, the lady benefactor awaits outside."
[ZH] 慈玄话音刚落，窗户被人从里面紧紧关上。洞门被打开，对慈玄说悄悄话的女尼出现在面前：“让她进来吧。”
[EN] The instant she spoke, the window pulled shut from within. The cave door opened to reveal the same nun who had whispered to Cixuan earlier: "Let her enter."
[ZH] 蝴蝶仿佛能听懂人话，立刻飞走。
[EN] As if understanding human speech, the blue butterfly lifted off and vanished.
[ZH] 千里寻向蝴蝶飞去的方向望了一眼，才向山洞内走去。
[EN] Watching it flutter away, Qianli Xun stepped toward the cave entrance.
[ZH] 苦荞也跟着往里走，被慈玄法师拦住：“你不必进去，在外面候着。”
[EN] Kuqiao started in behind her, only for Master Cixuan to block her: "Stay outside and wait."
[ZH] 苦荞只好站住，等慈玄离开后，她才有心情欣赏山洞外的院落。
[EN] Halting, Kuqiao waited until Cixuan walked off before taking in the quiet yard outside the caves.
[ZH] 院子打扫得很干净，没有过多布置，仅有一棵她叫不上名字的树，开着玫红色的花朵，实在美观。树下有石桌和石凳，上面落满花瓣。
[EN] Swept spotless and spare, it held only one unfamiliar tree blooming with vibrant rose-red flowers, petals scattered across a stone table and stools beneath its branches.
[ZH] 苦荞过去将石凳上的花瓣吹落，坐下耐心等待。
[EN] Blowing petals off a stone stool, Kuqiao sat down to wait.
[ZH] 千里寻走进山洞后，女尼将洞门关上。
[EN] The second Qianli Xun stepped inside, the nun shut the cave door behind her.
[ZH] 眼前立刻漆黑一片，无法往前走，她站在原地，适应一会儿，隐约看到女尼站在身边：“师傅，住持在哪里？”
[EN] Pitch darkness swallowed everything. Stopping dead until her eyes adjusted, she made out the nun's dim outline beside her. "Master, where is the Abbess?"
[ZH] 女尼没说话，走到一旁，用火折子将油灯点着，洞内立刻亮堂起来。
[EN] Without speaking, the nun moved aside and struck a tinder tube to light an oil lamp, bathing the chamber in warm glow.
[ZH] 千里寻发现这个山洞比另一个深邃许多，她站的地方只是个外洞，除桌子、两盏油灯、一个木盒外，基本没什么布置。心想，住持该不会是位不喜见光之人？
[EN] This cave ran much deeper than the other; she stood only in an outer chamber holding a table, two oil lamps, and a wooden box. Does the Abbess hate sunlight? Qianli Xun wondered.
[ZH] 女尼说：“女施主，住持正在苦修，本不接见外客，只因有只蝴蝶不畏黑暗飞入洞中，落在住持的身上，让她老人家领悟到有人来此，甚觉有缘，才愿与你见上一面。”
[EN] The nun explained: "Benefactor, the Abbess is in ascetic retreat and sees no guests. Only because a butterfly braved the dark to fly inside and alight upon her did she sense a fated visitor and grant you this meeting."
[ZH] 千里寻想起刚才的蝴蝶，内心生起感激之情。常言道：飞蛾扑火，它能来到光线暗淡之处，实属难得，住持如此想，倒也正常。无论如何，蝴蝶帮了她，以后有机会遇见……
[EN] Remembering the blue butterfly, gratitude warmed Qianli Xun's heart. Moths seek flame, yet that butterfly flew straight into darkness—no wonder the Abbess saw an omen. Either way, that butterfly had done her a massive favor; if she ever crossed paths with it again...
[ZH] 千里寻想不出如果再次遇见那只蝴蝶，她需要怎么做，才能表达她的心意。
[EN] Not that she could think what favor you even do for a butterfly.
[ZH] 千里寻向里面的山洞瞧瞧，黑漆漆的，什么也看不到，如果没有女尼在一旁，她绝对会感到害怕。
[EN] Peering toward the inner cave, she saw solid blackness—without the nun beside her, she'd have gotten chills.
[ZH] 女尼伸出手说：“女施主，住持在里面，牵着我的手，跟我来。”
[EN] Extending a hand, the nun said, "The Abbess sits within. Take my hand and follow me."
[ZH] 千里寻伸手拉住女尼，跟着朝洞内深处走去，边走边说：“住持法号净光，我以为她老人家喜欢亮光，没想到她喜欢在这么暗的地方待着。”
[EN] Gripping the nun's hand, Qianli Xun walked deeper into the dark. "With a name like Master Jingguang—Pure Light—I figured she'd love bright rooms, not sit in the dark."
[ZH] 女尼解释道：“女施主是世俗之人，当然不明白苦修之人也要修炼无光之苦。”
[EN] "As a layperson, benefactor, you wouldn't know that ascetics also train in enduring the hardship of darkness."
[ZH] 千里寻不明白苦修为何意，也不愿在此事上多费脑筋，毕竟与自己关心之事没有多大关系，她只觉在黑暗中走一会儿，女尼站住，她也跟着站住。
[EN] Too deep for Qianli Xun—and off topic anyway. After walking several paces through ink-black air, the nun stopped, and she stopped too.
[ZH] 女尼说：“住持，女施主到了。”
[EN] "Abbess, the lady benefactor is here," the nun announced.
[ZH] 传来住持的声音：“慈空，让她坐下吧。”显然是位老妇人的声音。
[EN] An aged woman's voice floated out of the dark: "Cikong, have her sit."
[ZH] 慈空拉着千里寻：“女施主再往前一步。”千里寻按照要求向前迈进一步。
[EN] Master Cikong guided her hand. "One step forward, benefactor." Qianli Xun stepped forward.
[ZH] 慈空又说：“可以坐下。”千里寻摸着黑坐下，一分一毫不差坐在一个软软的蒲团之上。
[EN] "Sit now." Feeling her way down in the dark, she landed square on a soft prayer cushion.
[ZH] 坐好后，她听到慈空走向一旁，不由从心里慨叹，这位师傅在黑暗中行走的本事实在厉害。
[EN] Listening to Cikong step aside without a stumble, Qianli Xun marveled at her dark-room navigation skills.
[ZH] 好不容易见到净光法师，千里寻不愿错过机会。尽管看不到住持的尊容，却能感觉到她就坐在对面。
[EN] At last face to face with Master Jingguang—unseen yet unmistakably seated right across from her—Qianli Xun wasted no time.
[ZH] “住持，我遇到人生难题，无路可走，不得已才上山向您老人家请教，希望您为我指一条明路。”
[EN] "Abbess, I'm trapped in a life crisis with nowhere left to turn, which is why I climbed this mountain to beg your guidance—please show me a clear path forward."
[ZH] “你可是对婚姻不满，想摆脱现在的夫君？”
[EN] "Unhappy in your marriage and desperate to break free of your husband?"
[ZH] 千里寻在心里叹道：“住持果然厉害，一下子猜中我心中所想。”赶紧回道：“是的。”
[EN] Spot on! Qianli Xun gasped inside—the Abbess really sees everything! "Yes!" she blurted out.
[ZH] “历来女子嫁鸡随鸡，嫁狗随狗，难有机会摆脱不如意的婚姻，即使摆脱，名声多少会受到影响，你有没有想过一旦离开现在的夫君，以后该如何是好？”
[EN] "Since ancient times, women have followed whatever husband fate dealt them, rarely escaping an unhappy match—and even when they do, gossip clings to their name. Have you thought what lies ahead once you leave him?"
[ZH] “我没想那么多，只想离开这段婚姻，在我看来，只有离开，我的人生才能重新开始。”
[EN] "I haven't thought that far—I just want out! To me, leaving this marriage is the only way my real life can begin."
[ZH] 住持沉默着，黑暗中的空气仿佛凝固一般。
[EN] Silence descended, heavy and still in the dark.
[ZH] 千里寻慢慢适应洞内的黑暗，朦胧中见到一个黑影坐在对面，旁边站着的应该是带她进来的慈空。住持不说话，千里寻却不想这么沉默下去。
[EN] As her eyes adjusted further, Qianli Xun made out a shadowy silhouette seated across from her, with Cikong standing beside. Tired of the hush, she spoke again:
[ZH] “您刚才猜中我来此的目的，说明您的确有知过去和未来的能力，您能不能告诉我，我到底还有没有机会摆脱这段婚姻？还有，我未来的命运如何？”
[EN] "You guessed why I came on the first try—proving you truly see past and future! Tell me: do I actually get out of this marriage? And what does my future hold?"
[ZH] 住持终于又开口说话，她并未直接回答千里寻的问题：“从你刚才进来，我已感受到你体内的神识不属于这个时代，先给我说说是怎么回事？”
[EN] At length the Abbess spoke, sidestepping her questions: "The instant you entered, I sensed your spirit does not belong to this era. Tell me first how you came here."
[ZH] 千里寻更觉得这位她看不清容貌的住持实在厉害，便将自己遇到地震，成为朱淑真的过程毫无保留详细说明。最后问道：“我还想知道，我到底是朱淑真还是千里寻？我在这个时代会像朱淑真一样？还是会有千里寻的命运？”
[EN] Stunned by the Abbess's insight, Qianli Xun held nothing back—telling of the earthquake, her death, and waking up inside Zhu Shuzhen's body. Finishing, she asked, "Above all, I want to know: am I Zhu Shuzhen or Qianli Xun? Will I end up living Zhu Shuzhen's tragic fate, or carve out Qianli Xun's own destiny?"
[ZH] 住持反问道：“你认为自己是谁？”
[EN] The Abbess countered, "Who do you believe yourself to be?"
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫一会儿，坚定地回道：“在别人眼中我是朱淑真，可我认为我依然是千里寻？”
[EN] Pausing a beat, Qianli Xun answered firm: "Everyone else sees Zhu Shuzhen—yet I still believe I am Qianli Xun."
[ZH] “我朝有位能人写下一篇文章，名为《太上感应篇》，首句便是‘福祸无门，惟人自召’，意思是福与祸不会随意找任何人，都是人自己的行为招感而来。佛门也讲五戒十善，均在告诫世人，想要改变命运最好的办法是改变自己的行为，而不是拜佛求神，以及星象占卜之类……”
[EN] "A sage of our dynasty wrote the Treatise on Response and Retribution, whose opening line states: 'Good fortune and calamity have no fixed gate; people summon them upon themselves.' Likewise Buddhism teaches the Five Precepts and Ten Virtues—all reminding mortals that the surest way to change one's fate is changing one's own conduct, not bowing to idols or chasing divination..."
[ZH] 千里寻对《太上感应篇》，以及什么五戒十善根本不感兴趣，认为住持只是对她进行说教而已，直到住持说出“改变命运”四个字，她才提起兴趣，认真往下听。
[EN] Qianli Xun tuned out the preaching on treatises and precepts—until "change one's fate" perked her ears right up.
[ZH] “你成为朱淑真还是千里寻，是由你的行为决定，行为又由观念操纵，你要从改变观念做起，在这个时代才能过上千里寻的生活。”
[EN] "Whether you live as Zhu Shuzhen or Qianli Xun depends on your actions—and actions follow mindset. Transform your mindset first, and you will live Qianli Xun's life in this era."
[ZH] 千里寻感到庆幸，她虽有着朱淑真的外表，观念倒与她截然不同，看来她正走在正确的道路上。
[EN] Relief warmed her—wearing Zhu Shuzhen's body or not, her mindset couldn't be more different! Clearly she was already on the right track.
[ZH] “不过，这身体并不是千里寻的，千里寻的命运多多少少会受到朱淑真命运的牵制，你更需努力才对，婚姻便是牵制之一。据我观察，朱淑真的命格中情路颇为坎坷，你借着她重生，自然情路不会一帆风顺，可又与她不同，只要你经历过几段情路，人生自然顺畅。”
[EN] "Still, this body belongs to Zhu Shuzhen, so her destiny will pull at yours—meaning you must strive harder. This marriage is one such tether. From what I perceive, Zhu Shuzhen's chart carries a rocky road in love; reborn through her, your path won't run smooth either, yet it differs from hers. Once you pass through a few romantic trials, your road opens clear."
[ZH] 千里寻在心里问道：“这么暗的山洞，也不知净光法师是如何观察的？该不会她能看到我，而我看不到她？难道修佛之人都会有这种能力？”
[EN] Wait—how is she reading my face in pitch darkness?! Qianli Xun wondered. Can she see in the dark while I'm blind? Do Buddhist masters unlock night vision?!
[ZH] 又听住持说她需经历几段情路，高兴道：“住持的意思是我会摆脱现在的婚姻？”
[EN] Catching the hint about future romantic trials, she lit up: "Does that mean I'll get out of this marriage?!"
[ZH] “我虽帮你看命，但天机不可泄露，有些事情不能明说。你只需记着，无论遇到任何事，要坚持正思正念，便可逢凶化吉。”
[EN] "Even reading your fate, Heaven's secrets must not be revealed—some things cannot be spoken plain. Remember only this: whatever strikes, hold to Right Thought and Right Mindfulness, and peril turns to blessing."
[ZH] “住持的话真是醍醐灌顶，我定会按照您所说坚持正思正念。”
[EN] "Your words wake me like cold water, Abbess! I'll hold to Right Thought and Right Mindfulness without fail!"
[ZH] “既然你我有缘，我再帮帮你。”住持停了一会儿，继续说道，“你选了王右丞的《竹里馆》，解诗的内容我详细阅览，发现你的解析很独到，你说这首诗不是赞美隐居之人品性高洁，而是书写诗人的无奈以及对时局的批判。我认为有一定道理，像王右丞这等有才华之人，应该被重用，为百姓谋福，却独自隐居山林，实在是人才浪费，也的确与当时的时局密不可分。你能有如此见识，说明你是位有主见的女子，既有主见，便有了掌控自己命运的第一步。我还发现你的字里行间并未提到诗中非常关键的一个字：‘竹’字，于是敏锐地感觉出竹能给你带来好运，回去后找一处有竹林的地方住上一个月，自然能体会到妙处。”
[EN] "Since fate brought us together, let me offer one more boost." Pausing a breath, the Abbess went on: "You chose Wang Wei's 'The Bamboo Pavilion,' and I read your essay word for word. Original view—arguing the poem doesn't praise a hermit's loftiness so much as voice helplessness and critique the times. There's truth to that: talent like Wang Wei belonged in office serving the people, not hiding alone in mountain woods—a waste shaped by his era's politics. Thinking that independently proves you have a mind of your own, which is step one in mastering your destiny. Yet I noticed you never once mentioned the poem's key word: 'bamboo.' From that I sensed bamboo brings you good luck. Go home, find a place surrounded by a bamboo grove, and stay there one full month—you'll see its gift."
[ZH] 千里寻终于明白，住持让人解诗并非只为解诗，而是要从解诗的内容中了解解诗之人。
[EN] So the poem test wasn't an exam—it was a reading of her mind!
[ZH] “好，我定照做。”
[EN] "Done! I'll do exactly that!"
[ZH] “你的茶做得实在粗糙，我一口都没喝，知道我为何还愿见你吗？”
[EN] "As for your tea—utterly dreadful, didn't touch a drop! Care to know why I still agreed to see you?"
[ZH] “晚辈不知，还请住持明示。”
[EN] "I have no idea—please enlighten me, Abbess."
[ZH] “只因你做的茶和我年轻时第一次做得一模一样。慈玄又将你做茶时的动作向我详细描绘，尤其是你把温盏的水倒在地上，呵呵，我第一次也是如此，让我甚觉亲切。”
[EN] "Because your awful tea looked identical to the very first bowl I ever made in my youth! Cixuan described every move—especially dumping the warming water straight onto the floor! Haha—I did the exact same thing my first time! Made me feel close to you right away!"
[ZH] 千里心想，也是她运气好，瞎猫撞上死耗子，否则今日哪有机缘与净光法师聊天儿。
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned in the dark—dumb luck at its finest!
[ZH] “你离开时，慈空会给你一条红绸，记得将自己的名字写在上面，至于写哪个名字由你决定。后山有棵千年神柳，你把红绸绑在神柳西边的树枝上，必然会对你产生助益。”
[EN] "On your way out, Cikong will hand you a red silk ribbon. Write your name on it—which name is your choice. Behind the mountain grows a Millennial Divine Willow; tie your ribbon to a western branch, and it will aid your path."
[ZH] 听到千年神柳，千里寻感到命运实在神奇，她来的那个时代有棵千年神柳，来到的这个时代又碰到一棵，看来她的命运与神柳有着密切的关系。
[EN] A Millennial Divine Willow?! Chills raced up her spine—a Divine Willow back in her old era, and another right here! Her destiny really was tangled up with that tree.
[ZH] 住持不再说话，传来慈空的声音：“女施主，可以走了。”
[EN] The Abbess fell silent, and Cikong's voice stepped in: "You may leave now, benefactor."
[ZH] 慈空走近，伸出手：“牵着我的手。”
[EN] Stepping near, Cikong extended a hand. "Take my hand."
[ZH] 千里寻起身，摸到慈空的手，跟着往出走。
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun took her fingers and followed her out.
[ZH] 从黑暗中来到有灯光之处，光线刺眼，千里寻不由闭上双眼。奇怪的是，慈空竟然一点儿感觉都没有。
[EN] Stepping from pitch darkness back into lamplight seared her eyes shut—yet Cikong didn't even blink!
[ZH] 千里寻闭一会儿眼睛慢慢缓过来，问道：“师傅，从里面出来，您怎么一点儿都不怕光？”
[EN] Slowly squinting open, Qianli Xun asked, "Master, how come the light didn't blind you coming out of there?"
[ZH] “习惯就好。”慈空简单回道，然后从桌上的木盒里取出一条红绸递给千里寻，“拿着，到正殿找敲木鱼的小师傅，她会帮你指明神柳所在之处。”
[EN] "You get used to it," Cikong replied brief, lifting a red silk ribbon from the wooden box on the table. "Take this. Ask the young nun at the wooden fish in the main hall—she'll point you toward the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 千里寻接过红绸，谢过后，推门离开山洞。
[EN] Accepting the ribbon with thanks, Qianli Xun pushed the door open and left.
[ZH] 等千里寻离开后，慈空把门关上，将另一盏油灯点着，端着进入内洞，里面一下子亮堂起来。
[EN] Once she was gone, Cikong shut the door, lit the second oil lamp, and carried it into the inner cave, flooding the chamber with light.
[ZH] 洞内有一个石炕，石炕上铺着柔软的褥子，上面盘腿坐着的正是住持净光法师，她微闭双目，双手放置膝头。
[EN] On a stone platform spread with soft cushions sat Abbess Jingguang cross-legged, eyes closed, hands resting on her knees.
[ZH] 慈空把油灯放好后说道：“师父每次都不点灯，搞得信众都不知道您长什么样。”
[EN] Setting the lamp down, Cikong teased, "Master never lights a lamp, so none of your devotees even know what you look like."
[ZH] 净光法师说：“这样才好，省得我下山一趟都把我围起来，不得自由。”
[EN] "All the better," Master Jingguang replied calm. "Saves me from getting mobbed every time I go down the mountain."
[ZH] “师父，您给那位姑娘说的方法真管用吗？”
[EN] "Master, will the advice you gave that girl actually work?"
[ZH] “管不管用，不在于我，关键在她。世人愚昧，才会相信占卜之类，如果明心见性，才知道这命啊都是自己造作而成。”
[EN] "Whether it works rests with her, not me. Blind mortals cling to fortune-telling; awaken to your true mind, and you see fate is crafted by your own hands."
[ZH] “师父就是明心见性之人，所以才能看得透，说得透。”
[EN] "Master sees right through everything—that's why your words cut straight to the bone."

#CH 28
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐拿着红色的绸带从山洞出来，立刻站起来迎上去：“小姐，可有收获？”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“随我去正殿。”
[ZH] 她们原路返回，来到尼姑庵的正殿，又传来连续不断的念佛声。有人正在跪拜，女尼忙着敲木鱼。千里寻不便打扰，与苦荞站在一旁耐心等待。
[ZH] 拜佛人起身从正殿后门离开后，女尼抬眼看到千里寻手中的红绸，主动走上前，双手合十：“阿弥陀佛，施主请跟我来。”
[ZH] 千里寻带苦荞跟随女尼拐到正殿后面。
[ZH] 女尼说：“这里有笔墨，施主先写下名字，我再带您去找神柳。”
[ZH] 正殿后面有张桌子，放着笔墨。千里寻走到跟前，将红绸铺好，拿起旁边的笔，要写下名字时犹豫起来。
[ZH] 尽管她认为自己是千里寻，可周围的人认为她是朱淑真，也只会用这个名字称呼她。到底该写哪一个？犹豫半晌，最终决定两个名字都写。她用小楷先写“千里寻”，再写下“朱淑真”。
[ZH] 想起读书时，偶尔看到一本书编者很多，其中有编者已去世，会在此人的名字外加一个方框。千里寻学着，在“朱淑真”三个字的外面画了一个长形的方框。完成后，搁下笔，拿起红绸看了看，十分满意。
[ZH] “小师傅，我已写好，麻烦前面带路。”
[ZH] “请跟我来。”
[ZH] 出了正殿，从西边的门向尼姑庵后面拐去。尼姑庵并不大，很快来到后门，跨出后门，便是后山。
[ZH] “我送施主到此，神柳在后山之下，您顺着这条路一直往下走，会看到一个池塘，神柳在池塘边上，善男信女喜欢把红绸挂在神柳上，找起来十分容易。”
[ZH] 说完，女尼从后门进了尼姑庵，顺手把门关上。
[ZH] 千里寻急道：“门怎么关了？一会儿我们还要回去！”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“小姐不用担心，我们待会儿下山，直接从山脚下绕到上山的入口处，不就行了。”
[ZH] 千里寻恍然大悟：“说的有道理，赶紧下山。”
[ZH] 苦荞从小姐手中接过红绸：“还是我替小姐装着。”
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝，松手给了苦荞，看着她把红绸卷好，塞进袖子里。
[ZH] 主仆二人顺着山路一直向下走。
[ZH] 后山的景色相当宜人，千里寻却无心欣赏，她一直挂念神柳，关键是这棵神柳也生长在池塘边，会不会让她产生一种故地重游之感？
[ZH] 下山的路很好走，看样子从尼姑庵去神柳处挂红绸的人不少，她猜测定以年轻人居多，因为山路被踩得相当结实。
[ZH] 下山比上山要容易许多，不知不觉已到山脚下，周围都是石头和疯长的野草，有的野草甚至与千里寻一样高。还好，野草中也被踏出一条路，苦荞走在前面探路，千里寻一边拨动身旁的野草，一边向外走。
[ZH] 苦荞先走了出去：“小姐，果然有个池塘。”
[ZH] 千里寻用手拨开最后挡住视线的野草，将步子迈出去，眼前出现一片池塘，不是很大，里面生长着荷花，一只青蛙蹲在荷叶上，没有发出叫声，瞪着两只鼓鼓的眼睛观察周围，没多时，纵身跃起，跳入水中，水中绿色的浮萍立刻向四周散去。
[ZH] 千里寻只觉一切似曾相识。她快走几步，来到池塘边寻找神柳。
[ZH] 的确很好找，池塘边只有一棵柳树，关键是树上挂着许多红绸，映着绿叶，使整棵树成熟中更显娇艳。
[ZH] 千里寻绕着池塘向神柳走去，她的心跳加快，到底是梦？还是她又回到穿越之前？眼前的神柳与地震时倾倒的那棵一模一样，唯一不同的是它的枝条上挂着许多红绸。
[EN] Seeing her mistress step out of the cave holding a red silk ribbon, Kuqiao jumped up at once. "Did it go well, Miss?"
[ZH] 来到树下，千里寻抑制不住激动的心情，泪水从眼中滚落下来，她忍不住上前抱住神柳粗壮的树干，像抱住亲人一般，轻声说：“带我回去吧，我不喜欢这个时代，尤其不喜欢莫名其妙的婚姻。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "Back to the main hall."
[ZH] 她闭上眼睛，用心感受一会儿，希望神柳能给予她肯定的答复。
[EN] Retracing their steps into the nunnery's main hall, they heard unbroken Buddhist chanting. Pilgrims knelt in prayer while the young nun tapped her wooden fish, so Qianli Xun and Kuqiao waited to the side rather than intrude. Once the worshipers rose and left through the rear exit, the young nun spotted the red silk ribbon in Qianli Xun's hand, pressed her palms together, and stepped over. "Amitabha. Come with me, benefactor."
[ZH] 可神柳没有任何反应，冷冰冰的树干也没有一丝温度。千里寻睁开双眼，一脸失落。
[EN] She led Qianli Xun and Kuqiao behind the main hall, where brush and ink waited on a small table. "Write your name first, benefactor, then I'll show you the way to the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 她慢慢放开神柳，向后退去，抬头望着繁茂的枝叶和随风轻轻摆动的红绸，更加确信它就是地震后带她来到这个时代的神柳。
[EN] Spreading the red silk flat and lifting the brush, Qianli Xun paused. Deep down she was Qianli Xun, yet everyone around her knew her only as Zhu Shuzhen. Which name belonged on the silk? After a long beat, she chose both, writing "Qianli Xun" in crisp small regular script and "Zhu Shuzhen" beside it. Remembering how modern books drew a neat box around the name of a deceased co-author, she traced a slim rectangle around "Zhu Shuzhen." Setting down the brush, she lifted the ribbon and nodded in satisfaction.
[ZH] 她转过身，想要寻找她与申简辰决斗的场地。周围全是杂草和石头，不远处只有山，除了池塘和神柳，其他完全不一样。千里寻清醒许多，这是一千多年前神柳所在之地，又如何能找到一千多年后的痕迹？
[EN] "All done, Little Master. Lead the way."
[ZH] 更奇怪的是，穿越前的时代神柳在另一个城市，穿越之后它却在临安地界，一切到底到底是做梦还是现实？
[EN] "Right this way." Exiting the west side of the main hall, they crossed the compact nunnery to its back gate, which opened straight onto the rear slope of the hill. "This is as far as I escort you, benefactor. The Divine Willow stands at the foot of the rear mountain. Follow this trail straight down until you reach a pond; the willow grows right on its bank, easy to find from all the red ribbons devotees hang on its boughs."
[ZH] 苦荞从袖中取出红绸：“小姐，我帮您把红绸挂上去吧？”苦荞找到一条柳枝，准备将红绸绑上去。
[EN] With that, the young nun stepped back through the gate and pulled it shut.
[ZH] 千里寻从迷惑中清醒过来，阻止道：“净光法师说要挂在西面的枝条上。”
[EN] "Hey, why'd she shut the door?! How do we get back?" Qianli Xun yelped.
[ZH] 苦荞只好住手，抬头望望太阳，刚刚偏西，她走到太阳的方向：“小姐，这里是西面，我找一条柳枝挂上去。”
[EN] "No worries, Miss," Kuqiao reassured her. "Once we're at the bottom, we just skirt the foot of the hill back around to the front trailhead."
[ZH] 千里寻瞧着神柳，发现凡是人能够到的枝条一大半已挂上红绸，她不想与众人的红绸挤在一起。再说这么低，很容易被人伸手拽走。
[EN] "Makes sense! Let's head down."
[ZH] 她向神柳高处望去：“我打算挂在那里。”
[EN] "Let me hold the ribbon for you, Miss." Handing it over, Qianli Xun watched Kuqiao roll the silk neat and tuck it inside her sleeve before they started down the rear slope.
[ZH] 苦荞顺着小姐的目光望去：“啊！小姐，这么高，怎么上去？您不是为难我吗？”
[EN] Lovely as the hillside looked, Qianli Xun had eyes for one thing alone: this Divine Willow also stood beside a pond! Would seeing it feel like stepping back into her old life? The downhill trail ran smooth and packed solid—clearly crowds of young people trekked down from the nunnery to tie ribbons. Heading down took half the effort of climbing; before long they reached the base amid boulders and wild grass growing tall as Qianli Xun's shoulders. Good thing a trodden path cut through the weeds; Kuqiao parted the brush in front while Qianli Xun pushed stalks aside behind her.
[ZH] 千里寻伸出手：“拿来！”
[EN] Stepping into the clear first, Kuqiao called out, "Miss, there really is a pond!"
[ZH] 苦荞乖乖地把红绸放到小姐手中：“小姐，这么高，您能上去吗？”
[EN] Brushing past the last tangle of tall grass, Qianli Xun stepped out before a modest pond filled with lotuses. A frog squatted mute on a lotus leaf, bulging eyes surveying the bank before springing into the water and scattering green duckweed across the ripples. Déjà vu hit like a wave. Hurrying along the bank, she scanned for the willow.
[ZH] 千里寻没说话，攥紧红绸，启动轻功，“嗖”地一声蹿到神柳的高处，踩在柔软的柳枝上，如同步履平地。
[EN] Impossible to miss: a lone ancient willow stood by the water, draped top to bottom in red silk ribbons glowing bright against emerald leaves, lending the grand tree a striking charm. Rounding the pond toward the trunk, Qianli Xun felt her heart hammer. Was this a dream, or had she slipped back before the quake? Trunk for trunk, bough for bough, this willow mirrored the exact tree that had toppled during the earthquake, save for the ribbons fluttering across its branches.
[ZH] 苦荞不由赞道：“我家小姐真厉害！”
[EN] Reaching the trunk, emotion broke loose; tears rolled down her cheeks as she threw both arms around the rough bark like hugging long-lost kin, whispering, "Take me home... I hate this era, especially this ridiculous marriage!"
[ZH] 千里寻在高处找到一条柳枝，将红绸绑好，刚要跳下时，发现离池塘不远处的密林中藏着一座府宅，不像是普通人家的住的宅子。
[EN] Shutting her eyes, she waited in tense silence, willing the willow to answer. Nothing stirred—only cold, lifeless bark under her palms. Opening her eyes, hollow letdown flooded her chest. Stepping back to gaze up at lush fronds and ribbons swaying in the breeze, she knew beyond doubt this was the same willow that had hurled her across time.
[ZH] 她跳下后指着府宅的方向：“那里竟然有座宅子，你知不知道是谁家的？”
[EN] Turning around, she looked for the open ground where she and Shen Jianchen had fought. Nothing stood there but weeds, stones, and green hills; aside from pond and willow, not one landmark matched. Reality settled back in: this was the willow a thousand years earlier; how could modern traces exist here? Stranger still, in her own era the willow had grown in another city altogether, yet here it sat right outside Lin'an. Was any of this real, or one long dream?
[ZH] 苦荞朝小姐指的方向望去，只看到一片茂密的树林，根本看不到宅子。
[EN] Drawing the ribbon from her sleeve, Kuqiao stepped toward a low willow limb. "Let me tie it up for you, Miss."
[ZH] “看来你也不知道，算了，跟我们又没什么关系，还是赶紧回去吧。”
[EN] Snapping out of her daze, Qianli Xun stopped her. "Master Jingguang said tie it on a western branch!"
[ZH] 千里寻又看了一眼神柳，她打算以后常来此处坐坐，在陌生的时代神柳令她倍感亲切。
[EN] Checking the afternoon sun slanting west, Kuqiao walked around to the western side. "West side right here, Miss. Let me find a good branch."
[ZH] 她与苦荞环绕山脚走着，一路上也是杂草和顽石相伴。想起净光法师的建议，千里寻问道：“苦荞，你可知哪里有竹林？”
[EN] Looking up, Qianli Xun saw almost every limb within reach crowded thick with red silk. No way was she wedging hers down low where anyone could yank it off! Pointing high toward the upper canopy, she said, "I'm tying mine up there."
[ZH] 苦荞在小姐前面边走边说：“我们村的人以种竹子为生，周围有一大片竹林。小姐问这个干吗？”
[EN] Following her finger, Kuqiao gasped, "Whoa, Miss! How do we climb way up there? You're messing with me!"
[ZH] “还不是净光法师给我出的主意，说竹子对我好，让我找个有竹林的地方住上一个月。”
[EN] "Hand it here."
[ZH] “太好了！小姐就去我家，我也正好回家陪爹娘住一段时日。”苦荞很开心，很快又担忧道，“只怕姑爷不会同意。”
[EN] Placing the ribbon in her palm, Kuqiao blinked. "You can really jump that high, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻根本没将林觉放在眼里：“林觉算老几？老娘的事老娘做主！”
[EN] Without a word, Qianli Xun clenched the silk, kicked off with light-foot skill, and shot straight up into the high willow boughs, balancing on supple branches light as air.
[ZH] 主仆二人聊着天儿，不知不觉来到大山入口处。林家的车夫正坐在石头上，拿着饼子大嚼大咽，见夫人回来，收起半个饼子，走到马车前，做好回城的准备。
[EN] "My Miss is awesome!" Kuqiao cheered from the bank.
[ZH] 到达林府时，天刚黑。
[EN] Fastening the ribbon tight to a high western limb, Qianli Xun paused just before dropping down—spotting an estate hidden deep inside dense woods not far past the pond, far too grand for ordinary folk. Dropping light beside Kuqiao, she pointed toward the trees. "There's a manor tucked over there. Know whose it is?"
[ZH] 林觉早已从下人口中得知朱淑真出去一整天，待她回到竹兰居后，派人把车夫叫来，问道：“她今日去了哪里？”
[EN] Squinting where she pointed, Kuqiao saw only solid forest canopy and no rooftop at all. "Guess you don't know either," Qianli Xun shrugged. "Forget it, none of our business. Let's head back."
[ZH] “回主君，夫人去了尼姑庵。”
[EN] Casting one last look back at the willow—she meant to visit often, drawn by its familiar comfort in a foreign age—she skirted the mountain base with Kuqiao amid weeds and boulders. Remembering Master Jingguang's parting counsel, she asked, "Kuqiao, know anywhere with a bamboo grove?"
[ZH] “尼姑庵？去那里做什么？”
[EN] Walking ahead, Kuqiao brightened. "My home village grows bamboo for a living! We're surrounded by a massive bamboo grove. Why ask, Miss?"
[ZH] “小的只负责赶车，没跟着上去，夫人到底做些什么，我不大清楚。据我猜测应该是拜拜佛，求神佛保佑。”
[EN] "Master Jingguang's advice. Says bamboo brings me luck and told me to live near a bamboo grove for one month."
[ZH] “谁不知道！要你说？下去吧！”林觉是想知道娘子有没有借着拜佛跟哪位才子私会。
[EN] "Awesome! Come stay at my home, Miss—I get to spend time with my parents too!" Joy flashed, then dimmed with worry. "Only, Son-in-law probably won't agree."
[ZH] 千里寻回到竹兰居，看到里面栽种的竹子，又想起净光法师的建议，心想，这里也有竹子，难道住这里不行吗？非要找个竹林？很快她想通，肯定是竹兰居的竹子太少，对人的影响不够大。
[EN] Qianli Xun scoffed. "Who cares what Lin Jue thinks? My life, my call!"
[ZH] 千里寻换完衣裳，感到腹中饥饿：“苦荞，我饿了，弄些吃的来。”
[EN] Chatting along, they rounded the hill back to the front trailhead, where the Lin coachman sat on a boulder munching flatbread; spotting Madam, he stowed the rest and climbed onto the box ready for town.
[ZH] 苦荞去厨房安排，不一会儿将吃的端来，两道菜，一碗鸡汤，倒没有什么特别之处。
[EN] Dusk had just fallen when they rolled back to Lin Manor. Already told by servants that Zhu Shuzhen had been gone all day, Lin Jue summoned the coachman the moment she returned to Zhulan Residence. "Where did she go today?"
[ZH] 当苦荞将一碗饭放下后，千里寻被吸引，只见饭上放着些桃花的花瓣：“这是什么饭？如此别致？”
[EN] "To the nunnery, Master."
[ZH] “桃花饭。”
[EN] "The nunnery? Doing what?"
[ZH] “桃花饭？”千里寻嘀咕着，她想起陆游的两句诗，“软炊香甑桃花饭,浅酌清尊竹叶醅。”果然陆放翁不欺后人，仔细想来，现在是宣和四年，陆放翁还未出生，嘿嘿，还是不要把他的诗句念出来为好，“早已过了桃花开放的季节，哪里来的桃花？”
[EN] "I stayed by the carriage at the foot of the hill, Master, so I couldn't say for sure. Guessing she went to burn incense and pray for blessings."
[ZH] “小姐难道没听过地桃花？”
[EN] "Anyone could guess that! Get out!" All Lin Jue wanted to know was whether she had used prayer as cover to meet some young scholar.
[ZH] 千里寻摇摇头：“没听过。”
[EN] Back in Zhulan Residence, eyeing the clumps of bamboo planted in her courtyard, Qianli Xun wondered why staying right here didn't count, then figured a handful of garden stalks couldn't match a real forest. Changing into lounge clothes, hunger hit. "Starving, Kuqiao. Fetch me dinner!"
[ZH] “林府的路边种的到处都是，小姐肯定见过，只是不知道它们是地桃花而已。”
[EN] Kuqiao hurried to the kitchen and returned soon with two dishes and a bowl of chicken soup, nothing unusual until she set down a bowl of rice dotted with pink petals. "What's this fancy rice?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 的确在林府见过，地桃花大约一米来高，花朵外形很像桃花。自己真是孤陋寡闻，什么都不懂。千里寻拿起筷子夹一口桃花饭放入口中，香甜软糯：“不错，还有红枣的香味。”
[EN] "Peach Blossom Rice."
[ZH] “蒸桃花饭时，必须放红枣，小姐再吃几口就能看到红枣。”
[EN] "Peach Blossom Rice?" Qianli Xun murmured, recalling Lu You's famous couplet: "Soft-steamed fragrant rice with peach blossoms, light sips from clear cups of bamboo-leaf brew." [TN: Translator's rendering] Lu You hadn't lied to later ages—good thing she didn't speak it aloud, since in the fourth year of Xuanhe Lu You wasn't even born yet! "Peach season passed ages ago. Where'd peach blossoms come from?"
[ZH] 千里寻用完晚饭，安排苦荞今晚收拾好东西，明日一早去有竹林的地方居住。想到林觉，不如知会他一声，毕竟她住在林府，名誉上还是他的妻子。
[EN] "Haven't you heard of Caesarweed, ground peach blossom, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻独自一人往林觉住的屋子而来，刚走到门外，听到里面传出女子娇滴滴的笑声，猜到林觉又把商妓带到家里。
[EN] Qianli Xun shook her head. "Never."
[ZH] 本打算转身回去，转念一想，她本不在乎林觉，又何必忌讳面对此事，不如让他看到自己无所谓的态度，也许会更加后悔没有同意和离。再说院子里不时有下人经过，见她来了又走，岂不是会偷偷笑她太软弱。
[EN] "Grows all along the paths in Lin Manor! You've seen plenty, just didn't know its name."
[ZH] 千里寻叫住一名下人：“你，去敲门，告诉林觉，我有事与他说。”
[EN] Right, those waist-high bushes with blooms shaped just like peach blossoms! So much to learn here. Lifting her chopsticks, she tasted a mouthful—sweet, soft, and sticky. "Delicious! Smells like red dates too!"
[ZH] 下人走到门前，向里面报告：“主君，夫人来了，有事与您说。”
[EN] "You always steam Peach Blossom Rice with red dates, Miss. Dig deeper and you'll hit them."
[ZH] 里面的笑声立刻停止，过了一会儿，门被打开。下人瞅准机会赶紧离开，恐怕主君与夫人要干一架，还是躲远些，别伤到自己。
[EN] After supper, Qianli Xun told Kuqiao to pack tonight so they could leave for the bamboo grove first thing tomorrow. Thinking of Lin Jue—well, she lived under his roof and still wore his wife's title; might as well drop him a courtesy notice.
[ZH] 林觉站在门口：“夫人不是说井水不犯河水吗？怎么前来找我？难道是想通，来找为夫圆房？”
[EN] Walking alone to Lin Jue's quarters, she heard a woman's coy giggle drifting through the closed door—another courtesan! Half tempted to turn back, she checked herself. She didn't care two cents about Lin Jue anyway; why act shy? Showing him she couldn't care less might make him regret refusing divorce sooner, and with servants passing through the yard, backing off would make them smirk at her for being timid.
[ZH] 本来心情挺好，被这家伙两句调侃的话搞得炸毛，千里寻生气道：“圆你个头！老娘过来有事通知你，还是进去说。”
[EN] Flagging down a passing servant, she ordered, "Go knock and tell Lin Jue I have something to tell him."
[ZH] 千里寻快步向屋里走去，到门口用肩膀将林觉撞到一边。她虽不介意，可在好奇心的驱使下想要看看里面的女子是何等货色，结果一个人影儿都没有。
[EN] Stepping to the door, the servant announced, "Master, Madam is here to speak with you." The giggling cut off dead; moments later the door cracked open, and the servant bolted fast, sure a brawl was about to break out.
[ZH] 千里寻转过身笑道：“呵呵，林觉，何必把人藏起来？老娘有那么可怕吗？再说老娘根本不介意你带商妓回家，现在就收回约法三章的第二条，以后你想带多少商妓回林府都可以，只要你喜欢。”
[EN] Standing in the doorway, Lin Jue sneered, "Didn't you swear we stay out of each other's hair? What brings you to my door? Changed your mind and came to consummate our marriage?"
[ZH] 春金巧从里面出来：“练兵官，我说没事，你偏让我躲起来。”
[EN] That jab lit her fuse on the spot. "Consummate my foot! I'm here to give you notice. Let's talk inside!" Shouldering Lin Jue aside, she marched in, half curious to inspect his guest, only to find the front chamber empty. Spinning back with a mocking laugh, she teased, "Ha! Why hide her, Lin Jue? Am I that scary? Relax—I don't care one bit who you drag home! Consider Rule Two revoked: bring as many courtesans into Lin Manor as your heart desires!"
[ZH] 千里寻盯着浓妆艳抹的春金巧，继续笑道：“呵呵，林觉，没想到你找商妓倒挺专一。”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao sauntered out from the inner room. "Told you it was fine, Officer Lin; you're the one who made me hide!"
[ZH] 林觉没好气地问：“你到底找我什么事？”
[EN] Eyeing heavily rouged Chun Jinqiao, Qianli Xun laughed again. "Well, well—loyal to one courtesan at least!"
[ZH] “我是来通知你，从明天起，我要到乡下住一段时日，至于什么时候回来，看心情。”
[EN] Irritated, Lin Jue snapped, "What do you actually want?"
[ZH] “是你一个人还是有别人？”
[EN] "Just giving notice: starting tomorrow I'm moving out to the countryside for a spell. When I come back depends on my mood."
[ZH] “当然不是我一个人，还有苦荞。”
[EN] "Alone, or with somebody else?"
[ZH] 林觉松口气：“只要不是跟野男人在一起就行。”
[EN] "Not alone. Kuqiao's coming."
[ZH] “野男人”三个字直接伤到千里寻的自尊：“你都把野女人带到家里，我即使跟野男人在一起，你也管不着，还是先管好你自己。”
[EN] Relief relaxed his jaw. "Long as you aren't off with some wild man."
[ZH] 说完，千里寻摔门而出。
[EN] "Wild man" pricked her pride. "You haul wild women straight into the house! Even if I went off with a wild man, none of your business! Tend your own mess first!" Slamming the door hard behind her, she marched out.
[ZH] 春金巧把门关上，走到林觉跟前，夹着嗓子，劝道：“练兵官，你这过得什么日子！不如同意和离，放她走吧？我不指望你为我赎身把我留在身边，只要能娶一位知你疼你的夫人，我也就心满意足。”
[EN] Latching the door, Chun Jinqiao sidled up and purred in a sugary voice, "Officer Lin, what kind of life is this? Just agree to mutual divorce and let her go! I don't expect you to buy my freedom—just want you wed to a sweet wife who loves and pampers you!"
[ZH] 林觉无法抵抗春金巧撒娇的声音，把她抱起来：“我暂时还不想放她走，等想通的时候再说。”然后抱着春金巧进了里屋。
[EN] Helpless against her cooing, Lin Jue scooped her right off her feet. "Not letting her go just yet. Wait till I'm ready." Carrying Chun Jinqiao, he strode into the inner chamber.

#CH 29
[ZH] 一大早用过早饭，苦荞安排好马车，叫来三四个丫鬟，把昨晚收拾好的包袱和一个木箱搬到马车上。
[EN] Early next morning right after breakfast, Kuqiao had the carriage hitched and rounded up three or four maids to load last night's cloth bundles and a wooden chest aboard.
[ZH] 包袱里装的无非是小姐的衣裳、胭脂水粉、笔墨纸砚之类；木箱里装着做茶的器具，乡下没这些玩意儿，小姐每日要喝茶，必须带齐全。
[EN] The bundles held clothes, cosmetics, and the Four Treasures of the Study; the chest held full whisked-tea gear—country villages lacked such tools, and her mistress drank tea every day.
[ZH] 一切收拾好之后，苦荞回到竹兰居：“小姐，可以出发。”
[EN] Everything loaded, Kuqiao returned to Zhulan Residence. "All set to go, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻问：“银子可带够？”
[EN] "Packed enough silver?"
[ZH] “小姐放心，银子尽够。”
[EN] "Plenty, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻才出了竹兰居，又走出西院。因为昨晚品尝过桃花饭，一早想起来依然口齿留香，走在林府的路上，不由自主多用目光照顾地桃花几眼，步子自然放慢。
[EN] Stepping out of Zhulan Residence and leaving the West Courtyard, Qianli Xun lingered a beat over the Caesarweed bushes along the paths, last night's fragrant Peach Blossom Rice still fresh in her mind.
[ZH] 正欣赏着，身后传来声音：“哎吆！少夫人真是说走就走，这一大早的，走得比我还早。”
[EN] Right then a familiar voice piped up behind her: "My, Young Madam really means business—leaving even earlier than me this morning!"
[ZH] 千里寻停下脚步，回过头，原来是春金巧：“怎么？要回春香楼？”
[EN] Stopping to glance back, Qianli Xun saw Chun Jinqiao. "Heading back to Chunxiang Brothel?"
[ZH] “可不是吗？我这样的女人哪比得上少夫人您，想去哪里就去哪里。”
[EN] "Where else? A woman like me can't compare to Young Madam, free to come and go as you please."
[ZH] 一只蚊子打算袭击春金巧，千里寻看到，抬手去赶。
[EN] Spotting a mosquito dive toward Chun Jinqiao, Qianli Xun raised a hand to swat it.
[ZH] 春金巧赶紧用巾帕捂住右脸：“少夫人又想打我？”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao clapped a handkerchief over her cheek in terror. "Going to hit me again, Young Madam?!"
[ZH] 千里寻放下手：“我是打蚊子，不过它已经飞走。”
[EN] Dropping her hand, Qianli Xun shrugged. "Swatting a mosquito—flew off already."
[ZH] “吓死我了，还以为你又要打我。”春金巧不再捂脸，扭着腰肢走到千里寻前面。
[EN] "Scared me half to death—thought you were slapping me again!" Lowering her cloth, Chun Jinqiao swayed past her down the path.
[ZH] 千里寻瞅着春金巧扭动的腰肢，心想原来林觉喜欢这样的，也许她能帮到自己：“喂！你等会儿。”
[EN] Watching her swaying hips—so that's Lin Jue's type—an idea struck Qianli Xun. "Hey! Hold up a second!"
[ZH] 春金巧站住，转过身：“少夫人有什么事？”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao halted and turned. "Yes, Young Madam?"
[ZH] 千里寻靠近春金巧：“林觉每次带到家里的商妓都是你吧？”
[EN] Stepping close, Qianli Xun asked, "Every time Lin Jue brings a courtesan home, it's always you, right?"
[ZH] 提到这一点，春金巧倒是挺骄傲：“那是自然，在整个春香楼，练兵官只对我好。”
[EN] Pride flashed on Chun Jinqiao's face. "Of course! Out of all Chunxiang Brothel, Officer Lin only cares for me."
[ZH] “看在他对你好的份上，你帮帮他，劝他跟我和离。”
[EN] "Then do him a favor and talk him into granting me a mutual divorce."
[ZH] 春金巧盯着林家的少夫人，露出研究的眼神：“少夫人真是个特别的女子，只可惜心里没有练兵官。放心，为了练兵官，我会劝他，只是他目前还放不下你。”
[EN] Studying Lin Manor's primary wife with sharp curiosity, Chun Jinqiao murmured, "You really are one of a kind, Young Madam—pity your heart isn't with Officer Lin. Don't worry, for his sake I'll try talking him round, though right now he still can't let you go."
[ZH] 春金巧又扭着腰肢走开，在大门口碰到林觉的大哥和二哥。两位衣装整洁，正要出门。春金巧并未与他们打招呼，直接上了春香楼的马车。
[EN] Swaying on toward the front gate, Chun Jinqiao crossed paths with Lin Jue's two older brothers, neatly dressed on their way out. Ignoring them completely, she climbed straight into Chunxiang Brothel's carriage.
[ZH] 大哥气道：“这叫什么事儿！三弟怎么又把商妓带到家里？有辱门风！”
[EN] Eldest Brother fumed: "What a disgrace! Third Brother hauling brothel women home again—shaming the family name!"
[ZH] 二哥劝道：“大哥，你要理解三弟，他那娘子不跟他圆房，心也没在他身上，他毕竟是个血气方刚的男子。”
[EN] Second Brother sighed: "Cut him some slack, Eldest Brother—his wife refuses his bed and has zero heart for him, and he's a healthy young man."
[ZH] 大哥说：“改日我劝劝三弟，让他赶紧纳一房妾室。”
[EN] "Next chance I get, I'm telling Third Brother to take a proper concubine!"
[ZH] 千里寻在身后听到，十分赞同：“大哥二哥早，你们的建议甚好，赶紧给林觉纳房妾室，我看刚出去的春金巧挺合适，不如就她了？”
[EN] Overhearing right behind them, Qianli Xun chimed in cheerful: "Morning, Eldest Brother, Second Brother! Brilliant idea—hurry up and get Lin Jue a concubine! Chun Jinqiao who just walked out looks like a great fit—why not her?"
[ZH] 大哥二哥面面相觑。
[EN] Both brothers exchanged awkward glances.
[ZH] 大哥回道：“纳妾也要选好人家的姑娘，怎么能选商妓？不行不行！”
[EN] Eldest Brother balked: "Even a concubine has to come from a respectable family—how could we pick a courtesan? Never!"
[ZH] 二哥不接这茬，直接问：“一大早弟妹要去哪里？”
[EN] Dodging the topic, Second Brother asked, "Where are you headed so early, Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] 千里寻毫不避讳，谁让他们阻止和离：“去乡下住段时日，呼吸呼吸新鲜空气，一直待在林府快要把我憋坏。”
[EN] Making no secret of it—they were the ones who had blocked her divorce—Qianli Xun shot back, "Staying in the countryside for a spell to breathe fresh air! Stuck inside Lin Manor was suffocating me!"
[ZH] 大哥想要说出批评的话，被二哥拦住，只能眼睁睁看着千里寻带苦荞走出大门，登上马车。
[EN] Second Brother held back Eldest Brother before he could scold, leaving both watching helpless as Qianli Xun and Kuqiao stepped through the gate and boarded their carriage.
[ZH] 大哥只能慨叹道：“这叫什么事儿！谁家的娘子会像她一样？”
[EN] Eldest Brother shook his head: "What on earth is this?! Ever seen a wife act like that?!"
[ZH] 二哥劝道：“三弟都奈何不了她，我们又能如何？不如劝三弟同意和离，放她走吧。”
[EN] "If even Third Brother can't handle her, what can we do?" Second Brother sighed. "Might as well talk Third Brother into agreeing to mutual divorce and letting her go."
[ZH] 大哥点点头：“今晚我们三兄弟约在一起喝点儿，顺便劝劝他。”
[EN] Eldest Brother nodded. "Let's invite him for drinks tonight—just us three brothers—and talk sense into him."
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 马车行走到闹市，透过窗户，千里寻看到一家首饰店，叫车夫把车停下，她要进店瞧瞧。
[EN] Rolling through bustling city streets, Qianli Xun spotted a jewelry shop out the window and ordered the driver to stop.
[ZH] 首饰店以金银首饰为主，一进店门，能看到柜台上摆着各式各样的款式。
[EN] Inside, counters gleamed with gold and silver pieces in every style.
[ZH] 千里寻看到一个银镯子，拿起来欣赏一番，觉得很不错：“苦荞，手伸出来。”
[EN] Spotting a handsome silver bracelet, Qianli Xun picked it up and smiled. "Hold out your wrist, Kuqiao."
[ZH] 小姐打算赔给她手镯，苦荞并不客气，伸出手腕：“谢谢小姐。”
[EN] Knowing her mistress meant to replace the bangle donated at the nunnery, Kuqiao didn't hesitate. "Thanks, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻抓住苦荞的手腕，给她把银镯子戴上。苦荞收回手，爱溺地欣赏着，比原来的还好看。
[EN] Slipping the bracelet onto her wrist, Qianli Xun watched Kuqiao admire it—even prettier than the old one!
[ZH] 掌柜说：“一两银子。”
[EN] "One tael of silver," the shopkeeper said.
[ZH] 千里寻毫不吝啬地说：“同样的，再拿一个包上。”
[EN] Without blinking, Qianli Xun ordered, "Wrap up a matching one!"
[ZH] 苦荞阻止道：“小姐，一个够了，不用送我那么多。”
[EN] "One's plenty, Miss—no need to buy me two!" Kuqiao protested.
[ZH] “不是送你，是送你娘。我去你家需住一个月，得麻烦你的家人，带些礼物以表心意。”
[EN] "Not for you—for your mother! I'm crashing at your family's place for a whole month; can't show up empty-handed."
[ZH] “小姐不要见外，您能去我家住，我爹娘高兴还来不及，怎能要礼物？”
[EN] "No need to be polite, Miss—my parents will be over the moon just hosting you! They'd never ask for gifts."
[ZH] “他们要不要是他们的事，送礼物是我的心意。”
[EN] "Whether they ask is their business; bringing gifts is mine."
[ZH] 掌柜的已将礼物包好：“二两银子。”
[EN] Wrapping the package, the shopkeeper said, "Two taels of silver."
[ZH] 苦荞只能接过，从钱袋里掏出二两银子给了。
[EN] Kuqiao accepted the bundle and counted out two taels from her pouch.
[ZH] 千里寻又要求再买些礼物，苦荞拗不过，建议小姐买些临安城的特色糕点带上即可。
[EN] When Qianli Xun insisted on buying more, Kuqiao talked her down to picking up a box of Lin'an specialty pastries.
[ZH] 当马车出了临安城，越来越靠近村子时，周围的竹子变得多起来，比竹兰居的竹子长得更茂盛，更粗壮，很快眼前出现一大片竹林。
[EN] Once clear of the city gates, bamboo thickened along the roadside—taller and sturdier than the stalks in Zhulan Residence—until a vast emerald bamboo sea opened ahead.
[ZH] 穿过茂密的竹林，一直向深处行进。千里寻难以想象，在竹林深处还会有人居住，甚至藏着一个村子。直到竹子不再成片成片挤在一起，一个个竹屋出现在眼前时，她才相信竹林深处真的可以藏着一个村庄。
[EN] Rolling deep into the dense grove, Qianli Xun marveled that anyone could live hidden in here—until the stalks thinned into a clearing dotted with bamboo houses!
[ZH] 竹屋都很简陋，但够结实。千里寻惊讶人的创造力实在强大，把一根根竹子想方设法连接在一起可以造出一座座房屋来。
[EN] Rough yet sturdy—human ingenuity never ceased to amaze her, lashing stalks into entire dwellings.
[ZH] 马车走着走着，已无路可走，只能停下来。
[EN] Where the track ended, the carriage rolled to a stop.
[ZH] 千里寻往外看去，有许多村民都看向马车。
[EN] Peeking out, Qianli Xun saw villagers turning heads from every direction.
[ZH] 一年到头很难有马车进来，村民们很是好奇。有的从竹屋里探出脑袋；有的停下手中的活儿；有的本要出村，又拐回来看热闹；一群小孩子，本来正在玩耍，也被吸引而来。
[EN] Carriages barely entered the village once a year, drawing instant crowds: faces poked out of doorways, hands paused mid-chore, folks headed out turned back for a look, and playing children ran right up.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“小姐，我下去叫些人帮忙拿东西。”
[EN] "I'll hop down and grab some hands to haul our luggage, Miss," Kuqiao said.
[ZH] 苦荞跳下马车后，有人说：“原来是苦荞啊，到城里大户人家当丫鬟，回来就是不一样，坐这么气派的马车，我还以为是城里收租的来了。”
[EN] The moment Kuqiao jumped out, a villager called, "Hey, it's Kuqiao! Working for rich folks in the city really upgrades you—rolling in on a fancy carriage! Thought the rent collector came early!"
[ZH] 小孩子中有认识苦荞的，都围过来：“苦荞姐姐，你从城里回来有没有带好吃的？”
[EN] Kids who knew Kuqiao swarmed around: "Sister Kuqiao, bring any treats from town?!"
[ZH] 苦荞提前有准备，从怀里掏出五六个糖果分给小孩子，然后对围观的村民说：“来几个人，帮忙把东西拿到我家。”
[EN] Prepared, Kuqiao pulled five or six candies from her sleeve to hand out, then called to the crowd: "Couple of strong arms to carry luggage to my house!"
[ZH] 一下子过来三四个年轻男子：“我来！我来！”
[EN] Three or four young men jumped forward at once: "I'll grab it! Me too!"
[ZH] 苦荞又走上马车，把包袱和木箱一个一个推出来，年轻男子们抢着拿了。
[EN] Climbing back up, Kuqiao passed down the bundles and wooden chest while the lads eagerly shouldered them.
[ZH] 苦荞先下车，千里寻才跟着下来。
[EN] Kuqiao stepped down first, followed by Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 苦荞向车夫说：“大叔，您先回林府，下个月同一天来接我和小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao instructed the driver: "Uncle, head on back to Lin Manor—come pick Miss and me up on this exact date next month!"
[ZH] 车夫答应着，赶马车出了村。
[EN] Nodding, the coachman turned the carriage back out of the village.
[ZH] 苦荞目送马车离开后，回过头，看到大家都直愣愣地盯着自家小姐。也难怪，村里人哪里见过像小姐这样气质典雅又高贵的大家闺秀。
[EN] Turning around, Kuqiao caught every villager staring wide-eyed at her mistress—no surprise, country folk had never seen a refined noble lady like her up close.
[ZH] 苦荞骄傲地介绍道：“这位是朱府大小姐，要在我家住一段时日，她可是竹林的主人，你们可得恭敬些。”
[EN] Swelling with pride, Kuqiao announced: "This is Eldest Miss of Zhu Manor! She's staying at my home for a spell—and she's the owner of this bamboo grove, so mind your manners!"
[ZH] “竹林的主人？”千里寻感到惊讶，苦荞这小丫头有多少事瞒着自己，她用询问的目光盯着苦荞。
[EN] "Owner of the bamboo grove?!" Qianli Xun blinked in surprise, shooting Kuqiao a sharp look—how many more secrets did this girl have?
[ZH] 苦荞越来越适应现在的小姐，竟然无意中忘记小姐重生失忆之事，赶紧解释：“我不是故意瞒着小姐，是忘了您不记得此事。”
[EN] So used to her lively mistress by now she'd clean forgotten her amnesia, Kuqiao scrambled to explain: "Didn't mean to hide it, Miss—forgot you don't remember!"
[ZH] 千里寻不便在众目睽睽之下向苦荞打听太多，对众人说：“各位父老乡亲，我在此打扰几日，还望大家不要嫌弃。”
[EN] Holding further questions back in front of the crowd, Qianli Xun smiled at the villagers. "Good folks, apologies for imposing for a bit—hope you don't mind my stay."
[ZH] 两人说话时，村长已出现，既是竹林的东家，他必须上前拜见：“东家来此，有失远迎，您应该提前派人说一声，我好准备东家住的地儿。”
[EN] Right then the village head hurried up; with the estate proprietor arriving in person, he had to pay respects. "Welcome, Proprietor! Forgive us for not greeting you sooner—had you sent word ahead, I would've readied proper quarters!"
[ZH] “您是？”千里寻不认识村长，只觉这位主动前来打招呼的中年汉子身份应该不一般。
[EN] "And you are?" Unfamiliar with him yet sensing status in his manner, Qianli Xun asked polite.
