[ZH] “是村长。”苦荞赶紧介绍，“李叔，小姐住我家，您不用操心。”
[EN] "Our village head!" Kuqiao introduced him fast. "Uncle Li, Miss is staying at my house—no need to trouble yourself."
[ZH] 千里寻也说道：“村长不必客气，我住苦荞家就行。”
[EN] "Don't stand on ceremony, Village Head," Qianli Xun added. "Kuqiao's place is fine."
[ZH] 村长只好说：“既如此，东家请。”
[EN] "In that case, right this way, Proprietor."
[ZH] 村长在前面带路，千里寻跟着刚走出两步，有村民喊道：“东家！能不能少收些租子？”
[EN] Leading them forward—two steps in, a bold villager yelled: "Proprietor! Any chance you'll cut our rent?!"
[ZH] 千里寻还未见过韦嬷嬷，对收租一事不明了，并未理会。
[EN] Not having met Matron Wei yet and clueless about rent rates, Qianli Xun kept silent.
[ZH] 村长朝那村民吼道：“乱喊什么？滚一边去！”
[EN] The village head barked at the man: "Shut your trap and get lost!"
[ZH] 村子的路狭窄弯曲，均是绕着各家的竹屋而设，竹子是路边唯一的植物。绕了大约十来个竹屋，终于到达苦荞家门口，竹屋倒是建得挺大，只是日久失修，看上去有些破烂，勉强还能住人。
[EN] Narrow lanes twisted past bamboo huts lined on every side by bamboo stalks. Rounding about ten homes, they arrived at Kuqiao's family house—decent-sized yet worn by years of weather, barely holding together.
[ZH] 村长在竹屋外大喊：“老苦！你家来贵客了！”
[EN] Outside, the village head hollered: "Old Ku! Big guest at your door!"
[ZH] 从屋里走出两男一女。两个男的，一老一少，老的满脸沧桑，鬓角和额头已有白发；年轻的大约十四五岁，皮肤黝黑，因常年劳作臂膀上露出结实的肌肉。女的也是两鬓斑白，额头上挂着几道皱纹，面色有些脏，并不是没洗脸，而是洗也洗不掉的劳动痕迹。
[EN] Two men and a woman emerged: an older weathered man with frost at his temples; a sturdy lad around fourteen or fifteen, sun-darkened and muscled from toil; and a silver-templed woman with creased brow and skin weathered by years of work.
[ZH] 三人先看到苦荞，女人激动地说：“荞，你可回来，想死娘了。”她快步走下竹梯，上前拉住苦荞。
[EN] Spotting Kuqiao first, the woman rushed down the bamboo steps in joy. "Qiao, you're home! Mother missed you to pieces!"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“娘，我给您介绍，这位是小姐，她要在我们家住段时日。”
[EN] "Mother, this is my Miss—she's staying with us for a while!" Kuqiao introduced her.
[ZH] 女人才看向千里寻：“这位像从画里出来的姑娘就是朱府小姐？看着面善，你跟着她娘就放心了。”然后拉起千里寻，“快！屋里坐。”
[EN] Turning to Qianli Xun, the mother beamed: "This beauty straight out of a painting is Miss Zhu? Such a kind face—knowing you serve her puts my heart at ease!" Taking Qianli Xun's hand, she urged, "Quick, come sit inside!"
[ZH] 千里寻被拉着走进竹屋。
[EN] Qianli Xun let herself be drawn into the bamboo house.
[ZH] 几位帮忙搬东西的村民进屋把东西放好后，走出门，不愿走远，伸长脖子朝屋内瞧去。
[EN] Having set the luggage inside, the young helpers lingered outside craning their necks through the doorway.
[ZH] 村长吼道：“有什么好看的？还不赶紧忙自家的事去！”
[EN] The village head bellowed: "What's there to gawk at? Get back to your own work!"
[ZH] 几位村民最怕村长，只能嘻哈着离开。
[EN] Scared of the village head, the lads scattered grinning.
[ZH] 老苦和儿子上前招呼村长家里坐，村长说：“东家来住，需要什么尽管说，不要跟我客气。”
[EN] Old Ku and his son invited the village head inside. "Anything the Proprietor needs, just say the word—don't be shy," the village head told them.
[ZH] 老苦笑得合不拢嘴：“有村长这话，我还能不找你？有什么需要，我让苦蕨去说。”
[EN] Grinning ear to ear, Old Ku replied, "With your backing, Village Head, you bet! Need anything, I'll send Kujue right over."
[ZH] 村长说出心中疑虑：“往年都是韦嬷嬷前来收租，今年东家怎么亲自前来？该不会有什么变动？”
[EN] Lowering his voice, the village head voiced his worry: "Every year Matron Wei comes to collect rent—why'd the Proprietor show up in person this year? Something changing?"
[ZH] 老苦解释道：“村长就是疑心重，我家苦荞不是说了吗？东家是来我家住段时日，应该不是为收租而来。”
[EN] "Always suspicious, Village Head!" Old Ku waved it off. "Didn't Kuqiao say she's just staying a spell? Doubt it's about rent."
[ZH] “不管了，我进去跟东家说两句就走。”
[EN] "Either way, let me step in and greet the Proprietor before heading off."
[ZH] 村长往屋里走去，老苦与苦蕨也都跟上。
[EN] They walked in together, Old Ku and Kujue trailing behind.
[ZH] 竹屋内一切家具均用竹子打制而成，虽不典雅倒也古朴。千里寻坐在低矮的竹椅上，身边的桌子上放有用竹叶泡好的茶水，正冒着热气。
[EN] Every piece of furniture inside was crafted of bamboo—plain yet charmingly rustic. Seated on a low bamboo chair, Qianli Xun found steaming bamboo leaf tea waiting on the side table.
[ZH] 村长进来客套道：“东家先住老苦家，有什么需要让老苦来我找。”
[EN] The village head bowed politely: "Settle in at Old Ku's first, Proprietor—need anything at all, send Old Ku for me."
[ZH] 千里寻客气道：“感谢村长照顾，您去忙吧，一旦有任何需要，定会找您。”
[EN] "Thank you for looking out for me, Village Head. Go tend your duties—I'll call if anything comes up."
[ZH] “东家忙，村里还有事需要我去处理，就不在此打扰。”村长说完辞别而去。
[EN] "Got village business waiting, so I won't intrude." With a bow, the village head departed.
[ZH] 苦荞向小姐一一介绍家人后，千里寻让她把银镯子和特色糕点拿出来，苦家人无不高兴。
[EN] Once Kuqiao introduced her family, Qianli Xun had her bring out the silver bracelet and pastries, delighting the entire Ku household.
[ZH] 老苦问：“不知东家要在这里住多久？”
[EN] Old Ku asked, "How long will you be staying, Proprietor?"
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“一个月。”
[EN] "One month."
[ZH] 老苦又说：“东家是贵人，总不能跟我们一样住在破竹屋里。正好，去年刚给苦蕨建了新竹屋，还没人住过，屋子靠近东面的竹林，景色也好，东家若不嫌弃，暂时住那里。”
[EN] "A noble lady like you can't stay in a rundown shack like ours," Old Ku said. "Lucky we built a brand-new bamboo house for Kujue last year—nobody's lived in it yet! Sits right beside the eastern bamboo grove with a fine view. If you don't mind, stay there!"
[ZH] 此屋正合千里寻心意，她无不同意。
[EN] Sounded like heaven to Qianli Xun—she agreed on the spot.
[ZH] 苦荞问：“爹，娘，苦蕨该不会要成亲？怎么这么早给他建竹屋？”
[EN] Kuqiao blinked: "Father, Mother—is Kujue getting married? Why build his house so early?"
[ZH] 老苦回道：“是该了，婚事已定下，新媳妇明年过门。”
[EN] "About time! Match is already sealed—his bride comes home next year," Old Ku beamed.
[ZH] 听到谈论自己的婚事，苦蕨坐在一旁的凳子上一直红着脸。
[EN] Seated on a stool nearby, Kujue turned bright red at mention of his wedding.
[ZH] 苦荞娘说：“东家，苦荞在您身边，给您添麻烦了，她以后的婚事，还得东家您多操心。”
[EN] Kuqiao's mother turned to Qianli Xun: "Thank you for looking after Kuqiao, Proprietor—when it comes to her marriage someday, we'll have to trouble you too."
[ZH] 苦荞插嘴道：“娘，您说什么呢？我不嫁人，永远陪着小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao cut in fast: "Mother, quit talking nonsense! I'm never marrying—I'm staying by Miss's side forever!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“伯母放心，我定会帮她找户好人家。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed: "Don't worry, Auntie—I'll make sure she lands a wonderful husband!"
[ZH] 大家又说一会儿话，苦荞才去帮娘蒸竹筒饭，又把去年晒的竹笋干拿出来用水泡开，炒了一道竹笋菜。因东家来，苦蕨到村后的河里捞来一条鱼，烧了鱼汤，一家人围坐一桌，款待东家。
[EN] After chatting a while longer, Kuqiao went to help her mother steam bamboo tube rice and stir-fry rehydrated dried bamboo shoots from last year; in honor of the Proprietor, Kujue netted a fresh fish from the stream behind the village for soup, and everyone gathered around one table.
[ZH] 苦家的饭菜比起林府差得很远，胜在有股自然清香，千里寻认为口感倒也特别。
[EN] Simple compared to Lin Manor's banquets, the meal carried a crisp mountain freshness Qianli Xun loved.
[ZH] 吃过饭后，在苦家人的帮助下，把东西搬到新的竹屋。家人走后，苦荞开始收拾，按照小姐的喜好，一件一件安排。
[EN] After lunch, the Ku family helped haul her luggage into the new bamboo house. Once they left, Kuqiao got busy arranging everything to her mistress's taste.
[ZH] 新的竹屋里家具还未补全，好在有张用竹子打制的矮桌，苦荞将桌子对着门口放好，把笔墨纸砚摆放整齐，小姐坐在这里写字时，刚好能看到外面的竹林，实在惬意。目测桌子的高矮，只能席地而坐，苦荞从家里拿来一个方形的棉花垫子放在桌旁，小姐坐上刚刚好。
[EN] Furniture was sparse, yet a low bamboo table faced the open doorway—perfect for the Four Treasures of the Study so Miss could gaze straight into the bamboo grove while writing! Since it sat low for floor seating, Kuqiao fetched a square cotton cushion from home—just the right height.
[ZH] 还有一个低矮的竹柜，可以当茶桌使用。苦荞找到一块印花的粗布铺上，把制茶的工具一一摆好。
[EN] A low bamboo cabinet doubled as a tea table; draping patterned homespun cloth across it, Kuqiao lined up every tea tool.
[ZH] 卧室放着一张新打制的竹床，苦荞把家里为苦蕨新做的被褥铺好，再把小姐平日穿的衣裳挂在竹架上。
[EN] Inside the bedroom stood a fresh bamboo bed; Kuqiao spread Kujue's brand-new quilts over it and hung her mistress's garments along a bamboo rack.
[ZH] 一切收拾完毕后，千里寻十分满意，她瞧了瞧挂在竹架上的衣裳，再看看自己身上所穿，太过精美，显得与村民有距离感。
[EN] Thrilled with the cozy setup, Qianli Xun eyed the fine silks hanging on the rack—and her own fancy dress—feeling way too flashy for a rustic village.
[ZH] “苦荞，给我找几身你的衣裳。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, lend me a couple sets of your clothes."
[ZH] “小姐为何要我的衣裳？”
[EN] "Why wear my clothes, Miss?"
[ZH] “我这些衣裳在村里穿着显得跟大家格格不入，还是穿你的好。”
[EN] "Wearing these silks around the village makes me stick out like a sore thumb—yours fit right in."
[ZH] “既如此，委屈小姐，正好我多带了两身，小姐先试试。”
[EN] "If you don't mind rough cloth, Miss—happened to pack two spare sets. Try one on."
[ZH] 苦荞取来一身，给小姐换上。小姐穿上普通衣裳，依然难掩骨子里透出的高雅气质。
[EN] Changing into Kuqiao's simple dress, Qianli Xun still couldn't hide her poise beneath plain cotton.
[ZH] 千里寻又把头上的首饰去掉，让苦荞收起来。
[EN] She pulled off her hair ornaments too and had Kuqiao stow them away.
[ZH] 她来到竹屋门口，迎面是竹林，一股清风袭来，竹香沁人心脾。千里寻觉得在这么美的地方待着，一个月后，定会对她的命运产生助益。
[EN] Stepping to the doorway, she faced a lush wall of swaying green bamboo; a cool breeze carried crisp bamboo scent straight to her lungs. Living a month in a paradise like this was bound to turn her luck around.

#CH 30
[ZH] 乡下的第一个晚上，千里寻睡得相当踏实。
[EN] Her first night in the countryside, Qianli Xun slept like a rock.
[ZH] 当她睁开眼睛时，天刚刚放亮，太阳还未出山，干脆再睡一会儿。这一睡，反而做了一个梦，梦中她身着婚纱，正与申简辰举行婚礼，很多人前来为他们祝福。
[EN] Waking at first light before sunrise, she rolled over to catch a bit more sleep—and fell straight into a dream: wearing a wedding gown, standing beside Shen Jianchen at their wedding surrounded by cheering guests.
[ZH] 当申简辰将戒指戴在她的手上时，千里寻猛然醒来，她想让自己再次睡去，可怎么也睡不着，美好的梦境就这样没了。
[EN] Just as Shen Jianchen slipped the ring onto her finger, she jolted wide awake! Squeezing her eyes shut, she tried diving back in, yet sleep refused to return—the sweet dream gone like smoke.
[ZH] 她坐起来，怅然若失，自语道：“申简辰，你在哪里？我好想谈恋爱，为什么我不想谈恋爱时，你在我面前晃来晃去，我想时，却连个人影都没有？”
[EN] Sitting up hollow-hearted, she murmured aloud: "Shen Jianchen, where are you? I actually want to fall in love now—why'd you hover around nonstop back when I didn't care, and vanish without a trace the second I do?"
[ZH] 又转念一想，她已是朱淑真，即使见到申简辰，他也无法认出她是千里寻；如若申简辰也像她一样穿越而来，估计自己见了，也无法认出。原本相熟的两人，因一场地震于另一个时代丧生，穿越而来，又无法相见，人生的悲哀莫过于此。
[EN] Then sober reality crept in: wearing Zhu Shuzhen's face, even standing right in front of Shen Jianchen he'd never recognize her—and if he had transmigrated into another body too, she wouldn't recognize him either! Two souls who knew each other inside out, killed together in one quake, flung into the past unable to recognize each other—what crueler irony existed?
[ZH] 苦荞早早起来，先回家为小姐做好早饭，用竹篮提着走进竹屋。听到里面传出小姐的叹息声，便知小姐已醒，放下竹篮，进到里屋。
[EN] Up early, Kuqiao walked home to cook breakfast and returned carrying a bamboo basket. Hearing a sigh inside, she set the basket down and stepped into the bedroom.
[ZH] “小姐醒了，吃过早饭，我带您去各处逛逛，保准您心情越来越好。”
[EN] "Awake, Miss? After breakfast I'll show you around the area—guaranteed to lift your spirits!"
[ZH] 千里寻穿好衣裳，只让苦荞简单为她梳了发髻，没有簪任何发饰，也没有涂任何胭脂水粉，只有如此，才有入乡随俗之感。
[EN] Dressing in plain clothes, Qianli Xun had Kuqiao tie a simple bun with zero hairpins and zero makeup—blending right into village life.
[ZH] 收拾完毕后，来到桌前。苦荞已将笔墨纸砚收起，摆好早饭，一碗散发着竹香的软粥，一盘竹笋干炒肉。
[EN] Out front, Kuqiao cleared the writing tools and laid breakfast on the low table: steaming rice porridge scented with bamboo and a plate of stir-fried dried bamboo shoots with pork.
[ZH] 千里寻坐在软垫上，瞅着那盘菜，心想，苦荞的家人估计平日不舍得吃肉，这道肉菜应是专门为她而做。
[EN] Eyeing the pork, Qianli Xun knew Kuqiao's family rarely ate meat—cooked special just for her.
[ZH] “苦荞，记得把我的伙食费给伯父伯母，不能让你的家人为我破费。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, make sure you pay your parents board money—don't let them spend out of pocket for me."
[ZH] “小姐放心，伙食费我给着呢，乡下没什么好吃的，只怕小姐不习惯。”
[EN] "Already paying them, Miss! Just worried simple country fare won't suit you."
[ZH] “我口粗，吃什么都行。”
[EN] "I'm easy—eat anything."
[ZH] 千里寻抬头朝门外瞧去，眼前尽是一片绿色，一根根竹子直立挺拔，向上生长，无形中透出一股生命之力，梦境带来的失落感因这股力量去了大半。她嘴角露出笑容，拿起筷子开始享用早餐。
[EN] Looking out the doorway at towering emerald stalks thrusting skyward filled with raw vitality, she felt half the dream's ache dissolve. With a faint smile, she picked up her chopsticks and ate.
[ZH] 早餐刚享用到一半，传来唱曲儿的声音，时不时伴有打鼓的节奏。因传来的声音不是很清晰，千里寻听不清到底唱些什么内容。
[EN] Halfway through breakfast, singing drifted over on the breeze punctuated by rhythmic drumbeats—too far to catch the lyrics.
[ZH] “外面怎么有唱曲儿的？”
[EN] "Somebody singing outside?"
[ZH] “哦，是唱鼓词的，经常到各村表演，挣些糊口的小钱。”
[EN] "Oh, a drum-song performer! Travels village to village earning enough to get by."
[ZH] 千里寻听说过鼓子词，她来了兴致，迅速吃完剩下的饭菜，放下碗筷：“苦荞，先不忙着收拾，随我出去听曲儿。”
[EN] Having read about Song drum songs, Qianli Xun perked up, polished off her bowl fast, and set her chopsticks down. "Leave the dishes for now, Kuqiao—let's go listen!"
[ZH] 她们循着声音来到唱曲儿之地，在村口处，有一小片空地，已经有许多人围坐一旁认真听曲儿。苦荞找到一块石头，把丝帕铺上：“小姐，坐这儿。”
[EN] Following the rhythm to a small clearing at the village entrance, they found a crowd already gathered around. Kuqiao spread her silk handkerchief over a flat rock. "Sit right here, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻坐下，瞧着唱曲儿的老人，是位盲人，坐在一个木凳上，左手扶着一个鼓，右手拿着打鼓的木棒，说唱结合。说词说得行云流水，唱词唱得悦耳动听，既不像千里寻听过的戏曲，更不像流行歌曲，要说介于二者之间，好像也不大合适，反正是一种她从未听过的唱腔。
[EN] Sitting down, Qianli Xun watched the performer—a blind old man perched on a wooden stool, steadying a small drum in his left hand and tapping beats with a wooden stick in his right, weaving spoken narrative and song together. His patter flowed smooth as water, his melodies rang clear and moving—neither opera nor pop, an ancient vocal cadence she had never heard before.
[ZH] 最令千里寻感到神奇的是，乐器只有鼓，就那么简单敲一下，尽显节奏之美。认真听那唱词，原来负鼓盲翁唱的是汉代蔡邕蔡伯喈的故事，讲他考中状元，不认发妻，娶了丞相之女。
[EN] Most mesmerizing was how a single drum could carry such gripping rhythm. Listening closely to the lyrics, she realized the blind drummer was performing the tale of Han scholar Cai Yong—passing first in the imperial exams, disowning his loyal wife, and marrying the chancellor's daughter!
[ZH] 千里寻心里暗笑：“这故事简直是毁了蔡邕的美名，完全把他虚构成一个宋代的陈世美。”
[EN] Qianli Xun chuckled inside: Poor Cai Yong's name dragged straight through the mud—turned into a Song-dynasty Chen Shimei!
[ZH] 本想等负鼓盲翁唱完后，给蔡邕正名。又转念一想，鼓子词也是文学，既是文学，虚构一下前人的事迹倒也正常，她千里寻写网络小说时，不知道虚构过多少人的故事，鼓子词与她写的小说比起来真是小巫见大巫，便放弃了为蔡邕正名的想法。也许蔡邕在天有灵，知道用他杜撰的故事娱乐大众，说不定还甚感欣慰。
[EN] For a beat she considered clearing Cai Yong's historical name once the song ended—then thought better of it. Drum songs were entertainment, and fiction bent history all the time; how many plots had she invented herself as a web novelist? Small potatoes next to modern web fiction! Maybe Cai Yong's spirit up above didn't even mind giving common folk a good show.
[ZH] 再观察周围的村民，大家听得很认真，时不时发出笑声和慨叹。千里寻认识到诗词是宋代高雅文人喜欢的愉情方式，而普通百姓则更喜欢通俗易懂的鼓子词表演。
[EN] Watching the villagers laugh, gasp, and sigh along, Qianli Xun had a lightbulb moment: classical ci poetry belonged to refined Song literati, whereas everyday people craved lively, down-to-earth storytelling!
[ZH] 千里寻借着朱淑真的身份时不时写诗词高雅一回，心里却清楚这些诗词不属于她。
[EN] Borrowing Zhu Shuzhen's verse for salons was fine, yet deep down those poems didn't belong to her.
[ZH] 她突然想在这个时代写出属于自己的作品，负鼓盲翁的表演给她带来灵感，心里想道：“对啊，普通百姓如此喜欢通俗文学，我为何不在这个时代写写故事，供大家娱乐，也好发挥我的特长。”
[EN] An itch flared in her fingertips—she wanted to write her own stories in this era! Inspired by the blind drummer, she thought: Ordinary folks eat up popular tales—why not write stories for them here and put my own real talent to work?!
[ZH] 正想着，听到一个女子喊叫的声音：“爷爷！救救我！坏人要抓我！”
[EN] Mid-epiphany, a terrified girl's scream sliced the air: "Grandpa! Save me! Bad men are after me!"
[ZH] 盲翁停下表演，所有人都朝声音传来的方向看去。
[EN] The blind drummer froze; every head in the clearing whipped round.
[ZH] 千里寻也站起来，她看到一个如花似玉的姑娘朝这边跑来，身上虽穿着打补丁的衣裳，却难掩靓丽的容貌。
[EN] Qianli Xun sprang up as a breathtaking young girl sprinted toward them—clad in patched rags that couldn't dim her striking beauty.
[ZH] 姑娘跑得气喘吁吁，身后有四五个壮汉在追。她使出全身力气，冲过人群，扑到盲翁的怀里：“爷爷！那些坏人要抓我！”
[EN] Gasping for air with four or five burly thugs pounding on her heels, she burst through the crowd and threw herself into the blind old man's arms: "Grandpa! They're trying to drag me away!"
[ZH] 大家还没反应过来，四个壮汉已冲进人群。
[EN] Four thugs shoved straight into the ring.
[ZH] 其中一个说：“喂，瞎老头，赶紧劝说你孙女，让他从了陈公子，只要跟着陈公子，吃香的喝辣的，也省得你这么大年纪还到处卖唱为生。”
[EN] One barked: "Hey, blind old man! Talk sense into your granddaughter and hand her over to Young Master Chen! Go with him and she eats like royalty—beats dragging your old bones around singing for scraps!"
[ZH] 盲翁扶住孙女，气道：“我卖唱凭本事吃饭，总比让我孙女跟着你们挣昧良心的钱好，我就不信，你们敢光天化日之下抢人！小心我去官府告你们！”
[EN] Shielding his granddaughter, the blind man shook with rage: "I earn honest bread by my craft—better than selling my granddaughter for dirty coin! Dare snatch her in broad daylight? I'll drag you straight to the yamen!"
[ZH] 周围的人也开始斥责壮汉：“你们这些人真是无法无天！”
[EN] Villagers broke into angry murmurs: "Lawless bullies!"
[ZH] “爷孙两个那么可怜，你们就饶了他们吧。”
[EN] "Poor grandpa and girl—leave them alone!"
[ZH] “你们这些地痞流氓，就知道欺侮老百姓！”
[EN] "Nothing but local thugs preying on honest folk!"
[ZH] 四个壮汉露出臂膀上的肌肉，在众人面前展示拳头，露出凶狠的表情。围观的百姓都不敢再言语，有的不愿多管闲事，转身回家；有的则远远躲开，怕被伤到。
[EN] Flexing thick arms and shaking fists with ugly snarls, the four thugs shut the crowd right up; half the villagers hurried home wanting no trouble, the rest backed far off.
[ZH] 千里寻并不怕，继续站在原地，她想先看看这群人到底想干什么，该出手时，定将他们打得服服帖帖。有小姐撑腰，苦荞也是面不改色。
[EN] Qianli Xun stood her ground without blinking, sizing up the thugs ready to flatten them the second they moved—and backed by her mistress, Kuqiao didn't flinch either.
[ZH] 壮汉调戏道：“喂，你们两个，怎么不后退？你这姑娘长得不错，难道想代替她跟了陈公子？陈公子正在马车里等着，我这就带你过去。”
[EN] One thug leered straight at them: "Hey, you two—why aren't you backing off? Pretty little face there—volunteering to go with Young Master Chen in her place? His carriage is right down the road, let me escort you!"
[ZH] 千里十分不屑：“老娘本来还想忍忍，没想到你偏要送上门来激老娘，别怪老娘不客气！”
[EN] Qianli Xun snorted cold: "I was trying to hold back, and you had to walk right up asking for it—don't blame me for what happens next!"
[ZH] 趁壮汉没注意，千里寻猛地一拳打在壮汉的脸上，不仅把脸打得乌青，嘴角流出血，也因拳头的力度，壮汉退出两米多。
[EN] Before the thug blinked, she drove a lightning punch square into his jaw—bruising his cheek black, splitting his lip, and sending him staggering over two paces back!
[ZH] 壮汉稳住，用手擦擦嘴角的血迹，对其他人喊道：“一起上！”
[EN] Wiping blood off his mouth, he roared: "Jump her together!"
[ZH] 四个壮汉环绕在千里寻周围，想着聚集四人之力定能将眼前的女子打倒，让她知道多管闲事的坏处，他们一起用拳头向千里寻袭击而来。
[EN] All four surrounded Qianli Xun, swinging fists from every side to teach this meddling girl a lesson.
[ZH] 千里寻轻轻一跃，拳脚连绵，犹如一阵狂风暴雨，又用变幻莫测的拳法组合快速而又准确地将四个壮汉分别击倒在地。
[EN] Leaping light off the ground, Qianli Xun unleashed a storm of crisp punches and kicks, weaving unpredictable Qianli Fist combos that dropped all four men flat onto the dirt in seconds!
[ZH] 有一壮汉咬紧牙关爬起来，全力出击，千里寻冷静面对他的攻势，以快速的拳脚将自不量力的壮汉逼退，接着那壮汉又一次倒在地上。
[EN] Gritting his teeth, one thug scrambled back up charging full force; cool and steady, Qianli Xun met him with rapid strikes that battered him backward straight onto the ground again.
[ZH] 周围的百姓欢呼起来：“东家打得好！打得得劲儿！打得呱呱叫！”
[EN] Villagers erupted in cheers: "Nice punch, Proprietor! Knock 'em flat! Brilliant!"
[ZH] 千里寻看着四个壮汉从地上爬起，一个个捂着伤口，一脸疼痛的样子，训斥道：“这是老娘的地盘，敢在老娘的地盘上欺负人，就是找打！再告诉你们，这一老一少以后老娘罩着，你们胆敢再欺负那姑娘，我的拳头可不是吃素的！”
[EN] Glaring down at the four thugs groaning and clutching bruises as they crawled up, Qianli Xun snapped: "This is MY turf! Bully people on my turf and you're begging for a beating! Listen close—this old man and his granddaughter are under my protection now! Touch one hair on her head again, and see what my fists do!"
[ZH] 四个壮汉见打不过，只能互相说道：“我们去找陈公子，让陈公子来收拾她。”
[EN] Outmatched, the four thugs muttered among themselves: "Let's tell Young Master Chen—let him handle her!"
[ZH] “她那么厉害，陈公子肯定也打不过。”
[EN] "Strong as she is, Young Master Chen can't beat her either!"
[ZH] “打不过也得说，否则陈公子会怪罪我们。”
[EN] "Still gotta report, or he'll skin us alive!"
[ZH] “就是，让他来试试，知道那女的有多厉害，才不会责怪我们。”
[EN] "Right—let him see how fierce she is so he doesn't blame us!"
[ZH] 四人刚要退去，传来一个声音：“你们四个追个姑娘怎么这么久？让本公子好等！”
[EN] Just as they turned to flee, a familiar drawl floated over: "Took you four this long to grab one girl? Keeping me waiting!"
[ZH] 四人向身后看去，原来是陈公子亲自过来。
[EN] Glancing back, they saw Young Master Chen strolling up himself.
[ZH] 他们忍着痛，围上去：“陈公子，那女的半路杀出来，说这是她的地盘。她很厉害，您瞧，我们被她打成什么样？”
[EN] Limping over clutching aches, they whined: "Young Master Chen, that woman jumped out claiming this is her turf! She's a monster—look what she did to us!"
[ZH] 千里寻仔细瞧去，陈公子正是陈浩宇，真是冤家路窄。
[EN] One glance and Qianli Xun recognized Chen Haoyu—talk about bad pennies turning up!
[ZH] 陈浩宇开始没认出朱淑真，只因她穿着一身丫鬟衣裳，看到一旁的苦荞，才笑道：“怎么哪里都有你？一个大家闺秀跑到这种地方干什么？”
[EN] At first Chen Haoyu didn't recognize her in Kuqiao's plain cotton dress—until he spotted Kuqiao beside her and smirked: "How do you pop up everywhere? What's a noble lady doing slumming out here?"
[ZH] 千里寻很是不屑：“我还纳闷陈公子是谁，原来还是你这粒老鼠屎，到哪里都能搅坏一锅汤！”
[EN] Qianli Xun sneered right back: "Wondered which Young Master Chen—of course it's the one rat dropping ruining every pot of soup!"
[ZH] 被说成“老鼠屎”，陈浩宇很是气愤，他清楚打不过朱淑真，只能忍住怒气。
[EN] Burning at being called a rat dropping yet knowing full well he stood zero chance against her fists, Chen Haoyu swallowed his rage.
[ZH] “小爷我今儿看在你的面子上，放了那姑娘。姑娘，别以为我放了你是好事，跟着本公子，你的日子才好过。撤！”
[EN] "Out of respect for you, I'll spare the girl today. Hey, girl—don't think you got lucky! Stick with me and you'd live in luxury. Move out!"
[ZH] 刚走出几步，陈浩宇回过头：“朱淑真，两次了，别让我第三次再碰见你，你的地盘是吧？明儿就不知道是谁的喽。”说完转身带着四个打手吹着口哨离去。
[EN] A few steps away, he looked back with a venomous smirk: "Twice now, Zhu Shuzhen—pray we don't make it three! Your turf, huh? Wait till tomorrow and see whose turf it really is!" Turning on his heel, he whistled his way off with his four bruised thugs.
[ZH] 千里寻真想不通，世上竟有如此猖狂之人。
[EN] Hard to believe anyone walked around this arrogant!
[ZH] 经历此事，盲翁无法再表演下去，孙女搀扶他感谢千里寻：“谢谢姑娘相助，老朽无以为报，若喜欢听曲儿，改日老朽带孙女登门专为恩人表演。”
[EN] Shaken, the blind drummer could perform no more; leaning on his granddaughter, he bowed low. "Thank you for saving us, young lady! Nothing to repay you with—if you enjoy drum songs, let me bring my granddaughter to your door someday for a private performance."
[ZH] 盲翁的孙女跪在地上磕头：“谢谢姐姐帮我。”
[EN] The girl dropped to her knees kowtowing: "Thank you for saving me, Elder Sister!"
[ZH] 千里寻扶起女孩儿：“你叫什么名字？”
[EN] Pulling her up, Qianli Xun asked, "What's your name?"
[ZH] “我叫夏果。”
[EN] "Xiaguo."
[ZH] “名字不错。”千里寻瞅着夏果，真是个美人胚子，不由心生怜爱，“不如以后跟着我？”
[EN] "Pretty name!" Studying Xiaguo—a true natural beauty—pity stirred in Qianli Xun's chest. "How about coming to work for me?"
[ZH] 夏果感激不尽，又要跪下，被千里寻扶着：“别跪来跪去的，我不喜欢。不知家里还有什么人？”
[EN] Overwhelmed, Xiaguo started kneeling again; Qianli Xun caught her arms. "Quit kneeling—I hate kneeling! Who else is in your family?"
[ZH] “还有奶奶和爹娘。”
[EN] "Grandmother and my parents."
[ZH] “那便好，我把你要走，不至于没人照顾老人家。”
[EN] "Good—taking you won't leave your grandfather without help."
[ZH] 千里寻说着，给苦荞使了一个眼色。苦荞拿出一两银子塞到盲翁的怀里：“老人家，这是我家小姐给您打赏的。”
[EN] She shot Kuqiao a nod; Kuqiao slipped one tael of silver into the blind old man's hands. "A reward from my Miss, Grandpa."
[ZH] 盲翁摸着银两，激动道：“多了！要不了这么多！”
[EN] Feeling the heavy silver, the old man stammered: "Too much! Way too much!"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“我把您孙女从您身边要走，这些钱都少了，您老就拿着吧。”
[EN] "I'm taking your granddaughter away from your side—one tael's barely enough! Keep it!"
[ZH] 老人感激道：“小姐让夏果待在您身边，算是给这孩子找个去处，老朽感激不尽，怎能收您的银子？”
[EN] "Giving Xiaguo a safe place beside you is already a blessing beyond words, Miss—how could I take your silver?"
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁搭腔：“老人家，您别客气，再客气我家小姐可就不收夏果当丫鬟。”
[EN] Kuqiao jumped in playful: "Don't be polite, Grandpa—turn it down and Miss won't take Xiaguo as her maid!"
[ZH] 老人赶紧说：“好，好，不客气。”
[EN] "Alright, alright—no more refusing!" the old man hurried to accept.
[ZH] 千里寻让夏果先将盲翁送回家，安顿好后再来竹屋报道。
[EN] Qianli Xun told Xiaguo to escort her grandfather home first, settle things with her family, and report to the bamboo house afterward.
[ZH] 不能继续听曲儿，村民们都散去，村口恢复一片寂静，只剩下千里寻和苦荞。
[EN] With the performance cut short, the villagers dispersed, leaving quiet behind at the village edge—just Qianli Xun and Kuqiao.
[ZH] 越是由热闹转而寂静，越容易让人的内心出现些许空虚。对未曾恋爱过的千里寻来讲，此刻她想用爱情填补空虚，爱她的人，她想爱的人又在哪里？既然找不到，不如做些其他事情消磨空虚的感觉。
[EN] Silence rushing in right after excitement left a hollow ache inside. Never having loved once in her life, Qianli Xun felt an empty space craving warmth—where was the man meant to love her, the man she wanted to love? If she couldn't find him yet, better keep busy.
[ZH] “苦荞，给我找些话本来。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, get me some huaben storybooks."
[ZH] “小姐要它们做什么？”
[EN] "What do you want those for, Miss?"
[ZH] “在竹屋待着无聊，用来打发时间。”
[EN] "Bored sitting around the bamboo house—something to pass the time."
[ZH] “小姐以前从来不看这些下里巴人的故事。”
[EN] "You never read lowbrow tales like that before, Miss!"
[ZH] “你还知道下里巴人这个成语，不愧是我千……”本来要说千里寻，甚觉不合适，“不愧是我朱淑真的丫鬟，不过，苦荞你记着，不管是阳春白雪还是下里巴人，只要老百姓喜欢就是好东西。”
[EN] "Even you know 'lowbrow'! Truly a maid of Qian—uh, of Zhu Shuzhen! Remember this, Kuqiao: highbrow art or lowbrow tales, if ordinary folks love reading it, it's good stuff!"
[ZH] “好吧，我让苦蕨去城里给小姐买几本来。”
[EN] "Alright, I'll have Kujue buy a few in town for you."
[ZH] 千里寻叹口气：“唉，我现在不能离开竹林，否则亲自上街，要么去茶馆，或者去勾栏瓦肆逛逛，亲自听听说话这门艺术。现在只能麻烦你们兄妹两个。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed: "Pity I can't leave this bamboo grove for a month, or I'd hit town myself—visit teahouses and Goulan Wasi to hear live storytelling firsthand! Guess I'll have to trouble you and your brother."
[ZH] “小姐不用跟我客气。”
[EN] "No trouble at all, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻又到竹林周围转了一圈儿，才回到竹屋。
[EN] Taking another stroll around the bamboo grove, Qianli Xun returned to her cottage.
[ZH] 苦荞安顿好小姐，去找苦蕨安排买书之事。安排完后，半路上碰到夏果打听小姐的住处，顺道把她带回去，将平日如何照顾小姐之事说了一遍，夏果用心一一记下。
[EN] Once her mistress was settled, Kuqiao went to give Kujue the book errand. On her way back, she bumped into Xiaguo asking directions to Miss's cottage, brought her right over, and walked her through every daily routine—which Xiaguo soaked up word for word.

#CH 31
[ZH] 申简辰在仪王府的生活过得舒心自在，佳和郡主与邹嬷嬷把一切安排得妥妥当当，出行坐卧均有下人伺候，没有多少需要他费心之事。唯一美中不足的只有藏在他内心的陌生感。
[EN] Life in Prince Yi's Manor ran smooth and pampered for Shen Jianchen: Princess Jiahe and Matron Zou handled every detail, servants waited on his every breath, leaving nothing to fret over—nothing except the quiet alienation sitting in his chest.
[ZH] 对环境和人不熟悉，可以慢慢了解，哪怕是对宋代的文化不熟悉也可以慢慢学习。唯独面对乔梦容的感情，他总是有说不出距离感，因距离感搞得浑身不自在。
[EN] Unfamiliar halls and faces could grow familiar in time; Song customs could be learned. Only Qiao Mengrong's devotion left an awkward wall between them that made him squirm.
[ZH] 要说乔梦容长得也是花容月貌，可申简辰面对她多情的眼神就是不来电。说白了，乔梦容是赵朴的菜，不是他申简辰的菜。他喜欢千里寻那样的，地震时心里装着她，死后心里还装着她，穿越而来心里依然装着她。
[EN] Beautiful as Qiao Mengrong was, her adoring eyes sparked zero chemistry in him. Plain truth: Qiao Mengrong was Zhao Pu's type, not Shen Jianchen's. He loved Qianli Xun—loved her during the quake, after death, and right here in this new life.
[ZH] 神柳的力量既然能让他穿越而来，千里寻应该也与他一起穿越而来。她到底在哪里？在这个陌生的时代是否还能与她相见？
[EN] If the Divine Willow's force had carried him across time, surely Qianli Xun had crossed too. Where was she? Would he ever find her in this foreign era?
[ZH] 想起千里寻，申简辰再也睡不着，从床上坐起来，已穿越来几日，逐渐适应仪王的生活，很自然地说：“我要更衣。”
[EN] Thinking of her chased sleep away. Sitting up in bed—already sliding into princely habits after a few days—he called out natural as breathing: "Time to dress."
[ZH] 伺候的丫鬟赶紧将衣裳捧上。
[EN] Attendant maids hurried forward bearing robes.
[ZH] 申简辰坐在床边，一名丫鬟伺候他穿好鞋子，另一名丫鬟给她捧上水盆，让他清洁面部，待他清洗过，又递上柔软的面巾。申简辰接过，将面部的水擦干净，丫鬟又接过面巾。他站起来后，两名丫鬟上前为他整理好衣装。
[EN] Seated on the bedside, he let one maid slip on his shoes while another held out a washbasin, then handed him a soft towel to dry his face. Standing up, he let two more maids straighten his sash and robes.
[ZH] 完毕后，申简辰说：“我出去走会儿，你们不必跟着。”
[EN] Finished, he said, "Going out for a walk. Stay behind."
[ZH] 丫鬟们只好止步。
[EN] The maids halted at the doorway.
[ZH] 申简辰独自一人走出屋，天还未完全放亮，他边走边回忆与千里寻的过往。
[EN] Stepping out solo into pre-dawn twilight, Shen Jianchen drifted back over memories of Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 他们是在一次民间拳法切磋交流活动中认识，当时他代表申拳与千里拳切磋，而代表千里拳出战的正是千里寻。
[EN] They had met at a folk martial-arts exhibition where he represented Shen Fist against Qianli Fist—and Qianli Xun stepped into the ring for Qianli Fist.
[ZH] 一场切磋下来，千里寻便走进他的心里，为追求她，他经常以切磋拳技为借口去千里拳庄，可千里寻总是对他不冷不热。
[EN] One sparring match stole his heart. To chase her, he kept showing up at Qianli Fist Manor using "sparring" as an excuse, even when she met him with nonstop cold shoulders.
[ZH] 开始以为她有喜欢的人，后来发现她单身一枚，申简辰高兴得一晚上没睡着。本着精诚所至金石为开的原则，选择死缠烂打的方法，想着不久肯定会将她拿下，谁知发生了地震。要不是因为地震，说不定那晚，他已将美人拥入怀中。
[EN] At first he figured she had a boyfriend; discovering she was single kept him grinning awake all night! Banking on sheer persistence wearing down stone, he stuck to her like glue, convinced she'd crack soon—right up until the earthquake struck. Without that quake, maybe that very night he'd have held her in his arms.
[ZH] 申简辰望着东方逐渐亮起来的一片天空，以前这个时候，他总会起来练功，穿越而来，忙着适应新环境，练功之事暂时搁置，此时心血来潮，正好眼前有片空地，他走过去，试着用赵朴的身体打出申拳的一招一式，竟然无比自如。
[EN] Gazing at the pale eastern sky brightening overhead, he felt his muscles itch. Back home he trained every dawn; busy adjusting since crossing over, he hadn't thrown a single punch. Spotting an open yard ahead, he stepped out and ran through a sequence of Shen Fist using Zhao Pu's body—fluid, crisp, and effortless.
[ZH] 乔梦容的贴身丫鬟喜兰听下人说仪王已起床，正在外面练功，甚是惊讶。她便匆忙来到郡主的卧房，将一切说了。
[EN] Hearing from servants that Prince Yi was up at dawn practicing martial arts, Qiao Mengrong's personal maid Xilan hurried to wake her mistress with the astonishing news.
[ZH] 乔梦容迅速起床，整理好衣装，往哥哥练功之处走去。
[EN] Dressing in haste, Qiao Mengrong rushed straight toward the courtyard.
[ZH] 快到跟前时，发现许多丫鬟和下人躲在远处观看，还时不时互相交头接耳。
[EN] Nearing the yard, she spotted clusters of maids and servants peeking from afar and whispering.
[ZH] 乔梦容向喜兰示意。
[EN] She gave Xilan a sharp glance.
[ZH] 喜兰明白郡主的意思，走上前厉声训斥：“都围在这里干什么？还不赶紧去忙！一大早的王府很多活等着人干！”
[EN] Catching the signal, Xilan marched over and snapped: "What are you all loitering around for? Get back to work! Plenty of chores waiting across the manor this morning!"
[ZH] 丫鬟和下人立刻四散而去。
[EN] The servants scattered at once.
[ZH] 乔梦容走近看着正在打出一套精妙拳法的仪王，她感到很陌生。哥哥哪里会什么拳法？他真的不是哥哥。
[EN] Stepping closer to watch Prince Yi flow through crisp, powerful strikes, Qiao Mengrong felt a chill of strangeness. Since when did her brother know martial arts? Maybe he really wasn't her brother...
[ZH] 泪水在乔梦容眼中打转，难道哥哥真的已离开她？
[EN] Tears welled in her eyes—had her real brother truly left her forever?
[ZH] 很快，她又否认了自己的想法，他跟哥哥长得一模一样，不是哥哥又能是谁？况且她亲眼目睹哥哥醒来，当时并未发生异样，拳法定是上天垂怜，赐予哥哥的新本事。
[EN] Seconds later she pushed the dread down: identical face, identical frame—who else could he be? She had watched him wake with her own eyes; nothing uncanny had happened. This martial prowess had to be Heaven's blessing bestowed on his recovery!
[ZH] 自我安慰后，乔梦容不再难过，她用丝帕擦掉已流到眼角的泪水。
[EN] Comforted by her own logic, she blotted away the tear slipping down her cheek with a silk handkerchief.
[ZH] “哥哥！”
[EN] "Brother!"
[ZH] 听到乔梦容的声音，申简辰收起练拳的动作，回过身：“梦容，你怎么起这么早？”
[EN] At her voice, Shen Jianchen halted his form and turned. "Mengrong? Up this early?"
[ZH] “哥哥不是也早早起来？”
[EN] "Weren't you up early too, Brother?"
[ZH] “我睡不着。”
[EN] "Couldn't sleep."
[ZH] 乔梦容靠近哥哥身边：“哥哥这套拳法实在精妙，梦容虽不懂功夫，也能看出哥哥自从奇迹般活过来，跟以前很不同，都是上天垂怜，让哥哥有了这等本事，以后梦容有哥哥保护，就不怕被人欺负。”
[EN] Stepping right up beside him, Qiao Mengrong beamed: "What marvelous boxing, Brother! Even knowing nothing of martial arts, I see how much you've changed since waking up—Heaven took pity and gifted you such strength! Now with Brother protecting me, nobody will ever dare bully me!"
[ZH] 乔梦容说着，靠在仪王的胸前，双手环在他的腰间。她闭上眼睛，享受着哥哥像一棵大树一样站立着，给她遮风挡雨的感觉。
[EN] Saying so, she leaned into his chest and wrapped both arms around his waist, eyes shutting in bliss as she drank in the feeling of him standing tall like a sturdy tree shielding her from every storm.
[ZH] 以前她与赵朴也搂抱在一起过，可都是对方拖着病体坐在床上，从未有过像现在这般将整个身体贴进他怀里的感觉。以前怕影响哥哥身体，每次拥抱只是蜻蜓点水，也从来没有认真感受过他的心跳。
[EN] She and Zhao Pu had embraced before, always with him propped frail in bed—never pressing her whole body against a solid, upright chest like this, never daring hug tight lest she strain his weak lungs, never really hearing his pulse.
[ZH] 而此时，真真切切地感受到哥哥的心跳加快，她把他抱得更紧。
[EN] Now, feeling his heartbeat quicken beneath her ear, she tightened her arms.
[ZH] 一旁的丫鬟喜兰瞧见，又害羞又高兴。
[EN] Watching from nearby, Xilan blushed and smiled in delight.
[ZH] 猛然被乔梦容抱住，申简辰感觉整个人有些懵，心跳也不由加速。
[EN] Ambush-hugged out of nowhere, Shen Jianchen froze, his heart kicking faster by pure reflex.
[ZH] 很快他清醒许多，不忍心伤害赵朴所爱之人，又不愿像赵朴一样把这位妹妹拥入怀中。必须说些话，让她不好再抱下去：“梦容，我还没吃早饭，肚子很饿。”
[EN] Catching himself fast—hateful to wound the girl Zhao Pu loved, yet refusing to wrap arms around her like a lover—he searched for an exit line: "Mengrong, haven't had breakfast yet—I'm starving."
[ZH] 乔梦容没有迎来哥哥拥抱她的双臂，有些失望，但这是哥哥起死回生之后，她第一次与他亲密接触。
[EN] Though disappointed he hadn't closed his arms around her, this was their first real embrace since he came back from death.
[ZH] 他因失忆忘记这段感情，不习惯倒也正常，重要的是他没有一把将她推开，说明失忆的哥哥并不讨厌她，不讨厌就是良好的开端。
[EN] Forgetting their love through amnesia made shyness natural—what counted was he hadn't shoved her away! Not pushing her away meant he didn't dislike her, and that was a hopeful start.
[ZH] 想到这里，乔梦容放开哥哥，面带笑容：“这个时间，厨房正在做早膳，哥哥要是饿了，先回屋吃些糕点垫垫。”
[EN] Releasing him with a warm smile, Qiao Mengrong said, "The kitchen is still making breakfast, Brother. If you're hungry, come back inside for some pastries to tide you over."
[ZH] 两人并肩走着，一路上未说话。对乔梦容来讲，与哥哥走在一起，即使不说话，也很美好。
[EN] They walked back side by side in silence. For Qiao Mengrong, simply walking beside him without a single word felt like paradise.
[ZH] 而申简辰的心并不在妹妹身上，他想着千里寻的同时，纠结如何处理与乔梦容的感情。
[EN] Shen Jianchen's mind stayed miles away—missing Qianli Xun while agonizing over how to handle Qiao Mengrong's devotion.
[ZH] 他觉得这个世界真的很奇怪，还未将千里寻追到手，就来到一个陌生的时代，还遇到一段陌生的恋情。
[EN] What a crazy twist: hadn't even won Qianli Xun yet, got flung into another century, and landed straight inside someone else's romance!
[ZH] 接还是不接？不接吧，乔梦容每次面对他都含情脉脉，楚楚可怜，令他不忍伤害；接吧，又实在违心，也会对不起乔梦容，不爱她还要将她搂在怀里，甚至娶她，天理难容！关键是他心里装着千里寻。他有种强烈的预感，在这个陌生的时代，他依然能够遇见她。
[EN] Accept or reject? Reject her, and her melting, devoted gaze tore at his conscience; accept her, and living a lie—holding or marrying a girl he didn't love—betrayed them both! Worst of all, his heart still belonged to Qianli Xun, and a deep gut hunch told him they would cross paths again in this era.
[ZH] 两人走进屋，乔梦容吩咐丫鬟给仪王端上糕点。
[EN] Inside the hall, Qiao Mengrong had maids bring pastries.
[ZH] 看着诱人的糕点，申简辰在桌旁坐下，拿起一块，咬了一口，入口即化，甜而不腻，忍不住全部吃完。瞅瞅一旁的丫鬟，自己一个大男人身边老是些妙龄女子伺候实在不习惯。
[EN] Sitting at the table, Shen Jianchen bit into one—melting sweet on his tongue without being cloying—and polished off the whole piece. Glancing around at the ring of young maids hovering nearby, he frowned; being fussed over all day by delicate girls felt deeply awkward.
[ZH] 他作为申拳的传人，收徒弟时定下一个规矩，收男不收女。只因他不喜欢柔柔弱弱的女子，认为她们根本不适合走上练武这条道路。除了在公司，他无论走到哪里都是一群男弟子跟着，现在被一群女子环绕，实在感到不自在。便向坐在一旁的乔梦容问道：“仪王府近身伺候的怎么全是些丫鬟？难道没有侍卫什么的？”
[EN] As heir to Shen Fist, he had enforced one strict rule: male apprentices only—convinced delicate girls weren't cut out for hard boxing. Outside corporate meetings, a pack of male disciples followed him everywhere; surrounded by maids here made his skin itch. Turning to Qiao Mengrong, he asked, "Why are all my personal attendants maids? Don't we have guards or anything?"
[ZH] 乔梦容回道：“仪王府倒是有些侍卫，主要负责王府安全，伺候哥哥之事，他们并不适合。再说以前哥哥身体病弱，需要细心、手脚轻软之人伺候，丫鬟最合适，而且都是我精挑细选出来的。”
[EN] "Prince Yi's Manor keeps guards for security, Brother, yet they're clumsy for daily care," Qiao Mengrong replied. "When you were frail, you needed gentle, careful hands—maids suited best, and I handpicked every single one."
[ZH] 申简辰感动于乔梦容对赵朴的关怀和照料：“让你费心了。”
[EN] Touched by her endless care for Zhao Pu, Shen Jianchen murmured, "You went to a lot of trouble."
[ZH] 乔梦容强调道：“哥哥不必跟我客气，我们是一家人。”
[EN] "Never speak like an outsider with me, Brother—we're family."
[ZH] 申简辰对王府和这个时代很多事情还不大清楚，继续问：“我现在疾病全无，不需要丫鬟整日围在身边伺候，想选名近身侍卫，不知王府侍卫有没有会功夫的？或者府外有没有武林好汉之类的人物？”
[EN] Still piecing together both the manor and this era, he pressed on: "Now that I'm healthy, I don't need maids crowding around me all day. I want a personal guard—do any of the manor's guards know real martial arts? Or are there skilled martial artists out in the city?"
[ZH] 乔梦容心中叹道：“哥哥真的变了。”而后照实向哥哥说：“梦容平日不大关心这些，哥哥是王府的主人，有权召集侍卫，把他们都叫来，问问便知；至于武林好汉，等乔公子回来，哥哥可以向他打听。”
[EN] Brother really has changed, Qiao Mengrong thought with a quiet sigh, answering plain: "I don't pay much attention to such matters. You're master of this manor—summon the guards and see for yourself. As for outside martial heroes, ask Young Master Qiao once he gets back."
[ZH] “乔公子是谁？”
[EN] "Who's Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] “乔公子是乔贵妃娘家的弟弟，乔贵妃生下哥哥时，他才两岁，与哥哥年纪相仿。大半年前，他从临安来到汴梁，一直住在仪王府。一个月前有朋友约他去莱州玩儿，便跟着去了，错过哥哥离世又奇迹生还之事。算算时间，也该回来。乔公子虽是文人，却常年在外，结交甚广，肯定知道哪里有武林好汉。”
[EN] "Consort Qiao's younger brother—only two when she gave birth to you, nearly your own age! Over half a year ago he came up from Lin'an to Bianliang and stayed here at Prince Yi's Manor. A month back a friend invited him to visit Laizhou, so he missed your death and recovery—he should be back any day now. Scholar though he is, he travels wide and knows all sorts of people; he'll surely know where martial heroes gather."
[ZH] 多了一位差不多同龄的舅舅，申简辰苦笑一下：“好吧，等他回来，我问问。不过我有个疑问，不知当问不当问？”
[EN] An uncle practically his own age—Shen Jianchen smiled wryly. "Alright, I'll ask him when he returns. Wait—one question I've been meaning to ask..."
[ZH] “哥哥请说。”
[EN] "Go ahead, Brother."
[ZH] “你为何也姓乔？”
[EN] "Why is your surname Qiao too?"
[ZH] “我本不姓乔，而是姓木，叫木梦容，哥哥把我捡回来后，认我做妹妹，为纪念乔贵妃，加之哥哥喜欢《诗经》中的诗句：‘伐木丁丁，鸟鸣嘤嘤。出自幽谷，迁于乔木’，便让我改了乔姓。”
[EN] "My birth surname was Mu—Mu Mengrong. After you picked me up and took me in as your sister, to honor Consort Qiao—and because you loved the lines from the Book of Songs: 'Chop, chop go the axes on the trees; chirping, chirping sing the birds; leaving the dark valley, they rise onto the lofty Qiao tree' [TN: Translator's rendering]—you gave me the surname Qiao."
[ZH] “原来如此。”
[EN] "Got it."
[ZH] 两人正说着，一个丫鬟进来：“王爷，郡主，邹嬷嬷让奴婢来问，早膳是在用膳厅用还是在这里？”
[EN] Just then a maid entered: "Your Highness, Princess—Matron Zou asks whether you wish to take breakfast in the dining hall or right here?"
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“哥哥，你来定。”
[EN] "Your choice, Brother," Qiao Mengrong deferred.
[ZH] 申简辰随口说：“在这里吧。”
[EN] "Right here," Shen Jianchen tossed out.
[ZH] 丫鬟回道：“奴婢这就去安排。”接着几名丫鬟把放在一旁的两个小圆桌搬到合适的位置，也把椅子放好。
[EN] "Right away!" Stepping out, the maid sent others moving two small round tables and chairs into position.
[ZH] 不一会儿邹嬷嬷过来，身后跟着三名丫鬟，手中各端着盘子。其中一名把盘子中的早膳放在申简辰的桌子上，另外两名分别放在两个小圆桌上。邹嬷嬷挑了一个圆桌坐下，乔梦容起身在另一个圆桌旁坐下。丫鬟们则站在一旁伺候。
[EN] Moments later Matron Zou walked in followed by three maids bearing trays—one placed breakfast before Shen Jianchen, the other two set the small round tables where Matron Zou and Qiao Mengrong took their seats while maids stood ready.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“王爷请用，有什么不合胃口的，尽管说。以前王爷的用膳习惯我与郡主尽知，现在老天保佑，你身体健康，我们两个反而不知道该如何照顾你。”
[EN] "Please eat, Your Highness—tell us if anything doesn't suit your palate," Matron Zou beamed. "We knew your old sickbed diet by heart; now that Heaven restored your health, we almost don't know how to spoil you!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“以后嬷嬷和梦容爱吃什么，我就是吃什么，不用特别照顾，我天生口粗。”
[EN] "Whatever you and Mengrong eat works for me—don't make anything special. I'm not picky."
[ZH] 听到“天生口粗”，邹嬷嬷与乔梦容面面相觑，她们似乎用目光向对方说：“他不是以前的仪王。”
[EN] "Not picky" made Matron Zou and Qiao Mengrong trade startled glances—as if silently confirming: He isn't the old Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰看到，不希望她们失望，赶紧解释：“身体好了，自然什么都能吃，都爱吃，不是口粗又是什么？”
[EN] Catching their dismay, Shen Jianchen smoothed it over fast: "Healthy now means everything tastes good—of course I'm not picky!"
[ZH] 两人听后，终于放心，三人继续安心享用早膳。
[EN] Reassured, both smiled and tucked into breakfast.
[ZH] 申简辰边吃边想：“王府这么多人，一日三餐吃的也不错，开销应该挺大。自从我穿越而来，也没干什么正事，到底钱从哪里来？”
[EN] Chewing away, a practical question hit Shen Jianchen: Feeding this massive household three lavish meals a day must cost a fortune—and since arriving he hadn't worked a day! Where did the money come from?
[ZH] 他看向邹嬷嬷，问道：“嬷嬷，好多事情我不记得，想问问，这么大的仪王府每日的花销都从哪里来？”
[EN] Looking over at Matron Zou, he asked, "Matron, since I forgot everything—where does the money to run Prince Yi's Manor come from?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷正在吃粥，听到仪王发问，放下勺子：“王爷一直身体病弱，官家虽没安排任何职务，但体恤仪王，俸禄照给。您是官家的儿子，名下自然有许多良田铺子，都有专人负责管理，每年也有收益。加之乔贵妃生前从乔家带的嫁妆，以及陛下赐给的，也均是良田和铺子，有的在汴梁，有的在临安，还有分散在其他地方的。总之，收益虽比不上官家别的儿子，养活整个仪王府绰绰有余。”
[EN] Setting down her spoon, Matron Zou explained: "Though illness kept Your Highness from holding official posts, His Majesty pitied you and kept your princely stipend flowing in full. As an imperial son, you also own vast estates and storefronts managed by stewards bringing in yearly profits—plus Consort Qiao's dowry from the Qiao family and imperial gifts from His Majesty, all landed estates and shops spread across Bianliang, Lin'an, and beyond. Less than your imperial brothers hold, perhaps, yet more than enough to keep Prince Yi's Manor thriving!"
[ZH] 申简辰听后很开心，没想到穿越而来，竟然一下子变成一位有田产之人，这下他可放心，不用担忧怎么养活整个王府的人。
[EN] Delight lit Shen Jianchen's face—landed wealth handed straight to him! Zero stress over payrolls now!

#CH 32
[ZH] 仪王府的侍卫大约有五十来人，接到仪王的召唤，都聚集在正院，排着整齐的队伍，等待王爷发话。自从他们来到仪王府，还是头一遭被王爷聚集在一起，一个个充满好奇，也想亲眼目睹起死回生后健健康康的王爷。
[EN] Roughly fifty guards served Prince Yi's Manor. Summoned by the prince, they lined up crisp and orderly in the main courtyard awaiting orders—first time in memory His Highness had ever called roll, leaving every man eager to see their healthy, revived prince up close.
[ZH] 申简辰走到侍卫面前，这群充满阳刚之气的男子才让他找回点儿穿越之前的感觉，心情不由舒畅许多。
[EN] Stepping before the ranks, Shen Jianchen felt his spirits lift—surrounded by sturdy, masculine energy at last, bringing back a taste of his old world!
[ZH] “平时是谁负责管理你们？”
[EN] "Who commands you day to day?"
[ZH] 从侍卫中站出一位：“报告王爷，是我，我是侍卫总管，名叫段晖，平时负责安排侍卫轮流保卫王府的安全。”
[EN] One guard stepped forward crisp: "Reporting to Your Highness—I am Captain of the Guard Duan Hui, in charge of scheduling patrols to protect the manor."
[ZH] “你对侍卫们可否了解？”
[EN] "Know your men well?"
[ZH] “报告王爷，他们身上有多少颗痣我都一清二楚。”
[EN] "Reporting to Your Highness—I know every mole on every man's back!"
[ZH] “好，既如此，你给我说说他们中有没有人会功夫？”
[EN] "Good. Then tell me: do any of them know real martial arts?"
[ZH] 段晖想想说：“王爷放心，都会耍两下大刀，格斗的本领多少会些。”
[EN] Duan Hui weighed it a second. "Rest easy, Your Highness—every man can swing a saber and handle rough brawling."
[ZH] 听到这话，申简辰甚是开心：“很好，你先入列。”
[EN] Pleased, Shen Jianchen nodded: "Good. Step back in line."
[ZH] ﻿待段晖回到队伍中，申简辰大声说：“所有人！前后左右按照一臂距离散开！”
[EN] Once Duan Hui rejoined the ranks, Shen Jianchen barked: "Everyone—spread out one arm's length front, back, left, and right!"
[ZH] 侍卫们按照要求很快站好。
[EN] The guards shifted smartly into spacing.
[ZH] 申简辰又大声命令：“蹲马步！”
[EN] "Horse stance!"
[ZH] 侍卫们将马步扎好。
[EN] Fifty men sank into horse stances at once.
[ZH] 申简辰继续说：“握紧双拳！左右开弓！向前出拳！左！右！……”
[EN] "Clench fists! Alternate punches straight ahead! Left! Right!..."
[ZH] 申简辰一边喊，一边走动，仔细观察每个人，他要选一名贴身侍卫，最好有些武功根基，更重要的是根骨要好，以后还可以跟着他学习申拳。
[EN] Calling cadence as he paced down the rows, Shen Jianchen scrutinized every guard—hunting for a personal bodyguard with martial roots and, above all, prime natural aptitude he could train in Shen Fist.
[ZH] 不远处，乔梦容朝这边望来，哥哥训练侍卫，声如洪钟，仿佛他的身体里有使不完的劲儿。
[EN] Watching from afar, Qiao Mengrong listened to his booming voice drill the men, energy bursting from every stride.
[ZH] 她回忆起往事……
[EN] Memories surfaced unbidden...
[ZH] 有一天，卧在病榻上的哥哥想出去晒太阳。乔梦容吩咐侍卫搬来一个长长的坐榻，再让丫鬟铺上厚厚的软垫。她与一名丫鬟扶着哥哥来到太阳底下，在软榻上坐好后，哥哥已经开始轻微喘气，靠在她的身上，有气无力地说：“梦容，我如果不在，你一定要找个好人家嫁了。”
[EN] One sunny afternoon, bedridden Zhao Pu had wished to sit in the sun. Qiao Mengrong had guards haul out a long couch piled with thick cushions; assisted by a maid, she helped him sit outdoors. Barely seated, breathless already, he leaned weak against her shoulder and whispered: "Mengrong... once I'm gone, promise you'll marry a good man."
[ZH] “不！我要替哥哥守着王府。”
[EN] "No! I'll stay and guard Prince Yi's Manor for you!"
[ZH] “傻姑娘，我不在，守着王府有什么用？我已写下遗嘱，王府的财产一半儿归你，一半儿给邹嬷嬷……”
[EN] "Silly girl—what use is guarding an empty manor once I'm gone? I've already written my will: half the estate goes to you, half to Matron Zou..."
[ZH] 话说的有些多，赵朴已没力气继续说下去。那时乔梦容心想，如果可以把自己的力气分给哥哥一些，他就可以跟她多聊会儿。而她能做的只是伸出手臂，轻轻搂住哥哥，内心继续担忧他会随时离开。
[EN] Those few sentences drained his breath. Back then she had wished with all her soul she could pour her own strength into him just so he could talk longer—yet all she could do was slip her arms gentle around his frail frame, dreading the moment he stopped breathing.
[ZH] 乔梦容边回忆边瞧着眼前的哥哥，终于成为她期待的样子，真希望明年的中秋节来得快一些，她不想再当他的妹妹，她要成为他真正的女人。
[EN] Gazing now at the strapping man before her, transformed into everything she had prayed for, she longed for next Mid-Autumn Festival to race closer—done being his sister, eager to become his wife!
[ZH] 申简辰已将五十多名侍卫看了个遍，每个人身强力壮，加上格斗与耍大刀的本领，守护王府绰绰有余，却没一个根骨好的。
[EN] Having inspected all fifty-plus guards, Shen Jianchen found plenty of brawny men good with sabers and brawls—fine for guarding gates, yet not a single gem with top-tier martial bones.
[ZH] “今天就到这里，你们去忙吧。”
[EN] "Dismissed for today. Back to your posts."
[ZH] 听到王爷的命令，侍卫们又聚拢起来，队伍依然整齐。
[EN] Snapping back into tight formation at his command, the men stood ready.
[ZH] 段晖从队伍中出来：“所有人！按照今早安排的，各司其职，守卫好王府，散了！”
[EN] Duan Hui stepped out: "Carry on with morning patrols—guard the manor tight! Dismissed!"
[ZH] 侍卫们各自散去。
[EN] The guards dispersed.
[ZH] 段晖向王爷行礼：“王爷，以后有什么吩咐，您直接找我，属下定赴汤蹈火，在所不辞！”
[EN] Duan Hui bowed low: "Anything Your Highness needs, just give the word—through fire and boiling water, I won't blink!"
[ZH] 申简辰对他的忠心感到满意：“好！有事我定然找你，先去忙吧。”
[EN] Pleased by his loyalty, Shen Jianchen nodded. "I'll call when I need you. Go tend your duties."
[ZH] 段晖离开后，申简辰打算出府，整日憋在王府里，都不知道外面是什么样儿，不如出去逛逛。还没走到大门口，被乔梦容叫住：“哥哥！要去哪里？”
[EN] Once Duan Hui left, Shen Jianchen felt cabin fever creeping in—cooped inside the manor without seeing Bianliang outside? Time for a stroll! Barely halfway to the front gate, Qiao Mengrong called out: "Brother! Where are you headed?"
[ZH] 申简辰转过身：“是梦容啊，我打算出去逛逛。”
[EN] Turning, he smiled: "Ah, Mengrong—just heading out for a walk."
[ZH] “我陪哥哥一起去。”
[EN] "I'll come with you, Brother!"
[ZH] “我不打算坐马车，徒步行走，很累的，你还是别去了。”
[EN] "No carriage—walking on foot, real tiring. Better stay home."
[ZH] “我不怕累，从小到大，哥哥从未与我一起逛过街，我一直梦想着能有这么一天。”
[EN] "I don't mind getting tired! Growing up, you never once strolled the streets with me—I've dreamed of this day forever!"
[ZH] 乔梦容充满爱而又渴望的眼神令申简辰不忍拒绝：“好吧，一起逛街。”
[EN] Melting under her eager, adoring eyes, he couldn't say no. "Alright, let's go together."
[ZH] 得到哥哥肯定的答复，乔梦容脸上露出轻松的笑容，上前拉起哥哥：“我们走吧。”
[EN] Lighting up with a breezy smile, she stepped over and linked her hand in his. "Let's go!"
[ZH] 喜兰很快安排好马车，两位主子虽要步行，也得有马车跟着，以防走不动时随时有车可乘坐。
[EN] Xilan ordered a carriage right behind them—walking or not, royal habit kept a carriage trailing in case either tired.
[ZH] 被乔梦容拉着，申简辰很不习惯，又不忍拒绝，就这样被拉着走出王府。
[EN] Uncomfortable holding hands yet loath to hurt her feelings, Shen Jianchen let her pull him out past the manor gates.
[ZH] 离王府越来越远时，他们来到热闹的街市，申简辰才忍不住说：“梦容，你拉着我逛街很不方便，能不能放开？”
[EN] Once they left the quiet lanes and hit bustling market avenues, he spoke up: "Mengrong, hard to walk through crowds holding hands—mind letting go?"
[ZH] 乔梦容松开手：“哥哥既不习惯，我不拉了，只要哥哥开心就好。”
[EN] She slipped her hand free at once. "If it bothers you, Brother, I won't hold on—whatever makes you happy."
[ZH] 申简辰很自责，为何这么好的女孩子他就是喜欢不起来？如果喜欢上她，应该是件挺完美的事。
[EN] Guilt pricked Shen Jianchen—why couldn't he fall for a girl this sweet? Life would be so simple if he could!
[ZH] 街市的繁华令申简辰无法再自责下去，他被眼前的景象吸引。
[EN] Bianliang's dazzling bustle soon swept guilt aside, grabbing every ounce of his attention.
[ZH] 只见人流穿梭，商贩喧哗，一派生机和忙碌，热闹程度直逼申简辰经历过的现代大都市。街道两旁的摊位上摆着琳琅满目的商品：各种颜色鲜艳的丝绸、细腻的陶瓷器皿、香气扑鼻的茶叶、各色新鲜的水果蔬菜等等。
[EN] Crowds surged past clamoring vendors, pulsing with raw energy rivaling any modern metropolis he had known! Roadside stalls overflowed with vivid silks, delicate porcelain, fragrant teas, fresh fruits and vegetables.
[ZH] 他们的身旁，百姓络绎不绝地走过。士人们穿着青衫白带，挑着小篮子，在街上闲逛；有的品味着街边的小吃，有的观赏着街头艺人的表演；商贩们吆喝着自己的商品，争相吸引顾客的目光；婆婆们携着孩子逛街，一边讨价还价，一边与邻里交流。
[EN] Every walk of life streamed by: scholars in blue robes and white sashes strolling with small baskets; folks sampling street snacks or watching jugglers and buskers; peddlers shouting wares to catch eyes; grandmothers towing kids while haggling and gossiping with neighbors.
[ZH] 街市上的声音此起彼伏，有叫卖声、商贩的吆喝声、马车的嘶鸣声、人们的笑语声，汇聚在一起，形成了一曲繁华的乐章。街市上的繁忙和喧嚣，让申简辰感受到宋代汴梁城市生活的兴旺和活力。
[EN] Hawkers' cries, vendors' calls, whinnying carriage horses, and cheerful chatter blended into a lively chorus of city life, showing Shen Jianchen the vibrant heartbeat of Song-dynasty Bianliang.
[ZH] 他看到一家店铺门口挂着“脚店”的招牌，不由说道：“没想到宋代也有提供足浴的店铺。”
[EN] Spotting a shop sign reading "Foot Shop" (Jiaodian), he chuckled: "Whoa—even the Song dynasty had foot-massage parlors!"
[ZH] “足浴？”乔梦容很是不解。
[EN] "Foot massage?" Qiao Mengrong blinked blankly.
[ZH] 申简辰指着“脚店”的招牌：“你看，那里不是写着吗？”
[EN] Pointing at the signboard, he grinned: "Look right there—Foot Shop!"
[ZH] 乔梦容看后，笑道：“那种小店不提供足浴，只是供人短暂歇脚的地方，这么大的汴梁城一天也逛不完，逛累了可以到脚店歇歇。”
[EN] Glancing over, Qiao Mengrong burst into laughter. "That isn't a foot-massage parlor! It's a little tavern where travelers pause to rest their feet—Bianliang is too big to walk in a day, so tired shoppers drop into a Foot Shop for a breather!"
[ZH] 申简辰为自己的无知感到惭愧：“没想到宋代也是一个充满人性化的时代。”
[EN] Red-faced at his own blunder, Shen Jianchen muttered, "Turns out Song cities were pretty thoughtful!"
[ZH] 乔梦容听得不是很明白，正在思索哥哥的话语，又听他问：“写着‘天之’‘美禄’字样的店铺又是干什么的？”
[EN] Still puzzling over his phrasing, she heard him ask next: "And what do shops marked 'Heaven's' and 'Beautiful Boon' sell?"
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“是酒馆。”
[EN] "Wine taverns," Qiao Mengrong explained.
[ZH] “叫酒馆不就行了，偏偏用这样的词语，让人看不懂。”申简辰认为古人真是矫揉造作，他想不通“天之”“美禄”与酒有什么关系。
[EN] "Why not just write 'Tavern' instead of fancy riddles nobody gets?" Pretentious ancients—what did "Heaven's Beautiful Boon" have to do with booze?
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“哥哥以前告诉我，《汉书》有云：‘酒者，天之美禄’，此意乃赞美酒为上天所赐之美物。”
[EN] "You told me yourself before, Brother!" Qiao Mengrong smiled. "The Book of Han says, 'Wine is Heaven's beautiful boon'—praising fine liquor as a gift from Heaven."
[ZH] 穿越到古代后才真正意识到自己是文化浅薄之人，申简辰尴尬地说：“是我告诉你的？唉！失忆了什么都记不得，看来以后要把以前读过的书再拿出来读读。”
[EN] Hit again by how thin his classical education was, Shen Jianchen laughed awkwardly: "I told you that? Amnesia wiped everything clean—guess I ought to dust off my old books!"
[ZH] “是该拿出来读读，官家喜欢有才学之人，尤其是精通诗词歌赋以及书画技艺的文人才子，哥哥以前身体不好，官家不理会也就罢了，现在身体健康，必然有机会面见官家，多读些书，只有好处。”
[EN] "You really should, Brother! His Majesty prizes men of learning—especially masters of poetry, calligraphy, and painting. While you were sick, he left you be; now that you're healthy, you're bound to face him sooner or later. Reading more can only help."
[ZH] 申简辰听得出，在这位妹妹心里还是很希望他的哥哥受到官家重视。对申简辰来讲，见不见官家无所谓，有没有重视更无所谓，反正他有的是田产，在陌生的时代富足一生应该没有问题，他只关心还能不能再遇见千里寻。
[EN] Clearly his little sister still hoped he'd win imperial favor. Shen Jianchen couldn't care less about favor or court audiences—with estates paying his bills, he was set for life! Only one question mattered: could he find Qianli Xun?
[ZH] 申简辰边想边欣赏街市，不觉走到一棵大树底下。
[EN] Strolling along lost in thought, he wandered beneath a massive shade tree.
[ZH] 树下搭着一个棚子，棚子里面设有卦摊，桌子上方拴着绳子，绳子上挂着三张长形的纸张，上面写着“神课”“看命”“决疑”，服务倒挺周全。
[EN] Underneath stood a canvas awning sheltering a fortune-teller's stall, three paper banners hanging overhead: "Divine Divination," "Destiny Reading," and "Doubt Resolution"—full service!
[ZH] 申简辰想要知道还能不能找到千里寻，也许算卦先生能帮他解决心中疑惑。可等待占卜的百姓甚多，排着长长的队伍，也不知会等到什么时候，只好放弃。
[EN] Eager to ask whether he'd ever find Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen eyed the long line of townsfolk waiting—could take hours! He started turning away.
[ZH] 乔梦容看出哥哥的心思，给身边的喜兰使了个眼色。
[EN] Catching his look, Qiao Mengrong signaled Xilan with her eyes.
[ZH] 喜兰上前拿出二两银子放在算卦先生面前。
[EN] Stepping right up, Xilan laid two taels of silver smack in front of the old fortune-teller.
[ZH] 算卦的老先生见到银子，立刻拿在手中，笑眯眯地对面前正在看相的人说：“麻烦，过去等一会儿，让贵客先看。”
[EN] Scooping up the silver with a wide grin, the old man waved aside the customer mid-reading: "Step over there a moment, friend—honored guests first!"
[ZH] 那人很不高兴坐到一边。
[EN] Grumbling, the displaced man shuffled aside.
[ZH] “哥哥，你心中既有疑惑，不妨过去问问。”乔梦容把仪王拉到卦摊前坐下。
[EN] "Since something's weighing on your mind, Brother, go ask!" Qiao Mengrong drew him into the seat before the table.
[ZH] 算卦老先生说：“贵人想问什么？”
[EN] "What does the young lord wish to ask?" the fortune-teller beamed.
[ZH] 申简辰回道：“决疑。”
[EN] "Doubt Resolution."
[ZH] “请写一个字，我帮您看看。”
[EN] "Write down any single character for me to read."
[ZH] 申简辰想了一会儿，提起毛笔，在纸上写下一个“寻”字，因没练过书法，字写得实在难看。
[EN] Pausing a beat, Shen Jianchen picked up the brush and wrote "Xun" (寻)—having never practiced calligraphy, the strokes looked atrocious.
[ZH] 乔梦容看了一阵心酸，哥哥以前虽很少写字，偶尔写过几次，字迹也比这好看许多，如此字迹如何能入得了官家的法眼。
[EN] A pang twisted Qiao Mengrong's heart—seldom as her brother wrote before, his hand had been far cleaner than this! How could brushwork this rough ever impress His Majesty?
[ZH] 老先生将纸张转过来，看了半天，问道：“贵人写得可是一个寻字？”
[EN] Spinning the sheet around and squinting hard, the old man asked, "Did the young lord write the character 'Xun' (寻, meaning 'to seek')?"
[ZH] 申简辰才意识到他写的是简体字，尴尬地点点头：“是。”
[EN] Realizing he had written the modern simplified form, Shen Jianchen nodded sheepishly: "Yes."
[ZH] 老先生提笔重新写一遍：“应该这么写。”
[EN] Lifting the brush, the old man rewrote it in full traditional form (尋): "Ought to be written like this."
[ZH] 申简辰仔细瞧去，繁体的“尋”字他确实不熟悉。
[EN] Eyeing the dense traditional character, Shen Jianchen admitted he barely recognized it.
[ZH] “看来贵人是问寻人之事？”老先生撂下笔，一语中的。
[EN] "Asking about finding someone?" The old man set down his brush, hitting the bull's-eye first shot.
[ZH] 申简辰急切地回道：“是寻人，不知能否寻到？”
[EN] "Yes—finding someone! Can I find her?" Shen Jianchen leaned in eager.
[ZH] 老先生思索了一会儿说：“‘尋’字最上面的笔画为‘歸’字的一部分，‘歸’字其他笔画尽失，说明贵人您归来之地已无法再回去。……”
[EN] Studying the strokes a beat, the old man spoke: "The top radical of 'Xun' (尋) comes from 'Gui' (歸, 'return'), stripped of all its remaining strokes—meaning the place you came from is closed to you forever; you can never return..."
[ZH] 此话说到申简辰的心里，可不是吗？穿越而来，想要回去，已不可能。
[EN] Bull's-eye again! Having crossed centuries, going back was impossible.
[ZH] “至于寻人，中间的‘工’字说明需费一番工夫，‘口’字则说明贵人会在一个与口有关之地遇到所寻之人，比如吃饭的地方，饮酒作乐之地，抑或是吟诗作赋之处。……”
[EN] "As for your search: 'Gong' (工, 'effort') in the middle means it will take real work; 'Kou' (口, 'mouth') shows you will encounter the one you seek at a place linked to the mouth—a restaurant, a wine tavern, or a hall where verse is recited..."
[ZH] 申简辰一阵欣喜，听这话千里寻也应穿越而来，他们还能再次相见。
[EN] Joy surged in Shen Jianchen's chest—Qianli Xun really had crossed over too, and their paths would meet!
[ZH] “底下一个‘寸’字，说明您与所寻之人即使见到，方寸之间又无法相认……”
[EN] "At the bottom sits 'Cun' (寸, 'inch')—meaning even standing inches apart, you two won't recognize each other at first..."
[ZH] “没关系，能找到便好。不知可否看出我在哪里能找到她？”
[EN] "No problem—long as I find her! Can you tell which direction to look?"
[ZH] 一旁的乔梦容甚是疑惑，哥哥到底要寻找什么人？为何从来没在她面前提起过？
[EN] Listening beside him, Qiao Mengrong frowned in puzzlement—who on earth was Brother hunting for? Why had he never breathed a word of this person?
[ZH] “贵人写下的这个字，从卦摊所处方位来看，整体面朝南，如果我没猜错，您所找之人应该在南边。”
[EN] "Judging by the orientation of my stall, the character you wrote faces straight south. Unless I miss my mark, the one you seek lies to the south!"
[ZH] 算卦老先生一席话，申简辰豁然开朗，他起身谢过：“感谢老先生解惑，喜兰，再拿出一两银子给老先生，算我格外打赏。”
[EN] Light flooded Shen Jianchen's mind! Rising with cupped hands, he thanked him: "Much obliged for clearing my doubts, Old Sir! Xilan, hand him one more tael as an extra tip!"
[ZH] 喜兰又拿出一两，老先生更是喜出望外，站起来接过银子，感激不尽：“谢过贵人，谢过贵人，祝您早日找到所思之人。”算卦老先生继续坐下给其他客人占卜。
[EN] Xilan produced another tael; beaming wide, the old man rose to accept it with profuse bows: "Thank you, young lord! Thank you! May you soon reunite with the one you long for!" Sitting back down, he called the next patron.
[ZH] 乔梦容带着疑惑跟随哥哥离开卦摊，她仔细观察哥哥，占卜完后他一直面带笑容。
[EN] Walking away beside her smiling brother, curiosity gnawed at Qiao Mengrong.
[ZH] 乔梦容忍不住问：“哥哥要找的人是谁？”
[EN] Unable to hold back, she asked: "Brother, who are you looking for?"
[ZH] 申简辰被问住，该怎么告诉乔梦容？说了她也不信，况且说了，她便知道他心里装着别人，岂不是会难过？于是随口编道：“以前认识的一位朋友，好久没见，以为再也找不到，故而让老先生算算还能否遇见。”
[EN] Caught on the spot—tell her the truth and she'd dismiss it or ache realizing his heart belonged to another! Improvising fast, he shrugged: "An old friend I lost touch with ages ago—figured I'd never find them, so I asked the old man if our paths would cross."
[ZH] 哥哥一直病着，哪有人愿意与他做朋友？只有乔公子时常来做客。他的回答令乔梦容伤心，因为哥哥开始欺骗她。
[EN] Bedridden all his life, who did Zhao Pu ever call friend aside from Qiao Wenxian? Pain flickered in Qiao Mengrong's chest—her brother had started lying to her.
[ZH] “哥哥不是什么都不记得？为何还记得以前的朋友？”
[EN] "Didn't you say you forgot everything, Brother? How do you remember an old friend?"
[ZH] 申简辰继续编着：“哦，刚才看见卦摊，猛然起一人，很模糊，来了兴致，想过去算算，顺便检验一下算卦老先生的水平。”
[EN] Spinning faster, he said, "Just spotting that stall flashed a blurry face in my head—got curious and figured I'd test if the old fortune-teller was any good!"
[ZH] “检验的结果如何？他算得可灵验？”
[EN] "And? Was he accurate?"
[ZH] “还行还行，要不怎么又打赏了一两银子。”
[EN] "Pretty solid—why else tip an extra tael?"
[ZH] 正说着，人群中传来一阵喊叫声：“抓住那小贼！他偷了我的钱袋！”
[EN] Right then frantic shouts broke through the crowd: "Stop that little thief! He stole my money pouch!"
[ZH] 申简辰循声看去，一位中年男子在人群中穿梭着，边跑边喊：“抓小贼！我的钱袋！”
[EN] Turning toward the clamor, Shen Jianchen saw a middle-aged man pushing through the throng yelling, "Catch the thief! My money pouch!"
[ZH] 只见一个年轻的身影从眼前穿过，速度飞快。
[EN] A lithe young figure darted straight past them like a streak of wind.
[ZH] 申简辰心中赞道：“根骨不错！”然后对喜兰说：“你带郡主到脚店歇息，我去抓贼。”
[EN] Great martial bones! Shen Jianchen thought in a flash, barking to Xilan: "Take Princess Jiahe into a Foot Shop to rest—I'm catching that thief!"
[ZH] 说完申简辰“蹭”地消失在乔梦容面前。
[EN] Whoosh—he shot forward and vanished into the crowd before Qiao Mengrong's eyes.
[ZH] 乔梦容一脸失落：“我们去脚店等哥哥吧。”
[EN] Left standing with a hollow sigh, Qiao Mengrong murmured, "Let's wait for Brother at the Foot Shop."

#CH 33
[ZH] 长期练习申拳，除手上有功夫外，脚上的功力也比普通人厉害，申简辰奔跑的速度极快，迅速穿过人群，向小贼追去。
[ZH] 小贼拐进一条人少的小巷，奋力向前奔跑，接着又进入无人的巷子，跑得上气不接下气。回头见申简辰追得轻松，怕是跑不过对方，便停下来，弯着腰，口喘粗气：“别……别……别追了，我……我……我不……不跑了，钱袋……给……给你！”
[ZH] 申简辰也停下，面不改色，接过钱袋，仔细瞧眼前的小贼，大约十五六岁的模样，身上的衣裳很破烂，脸上脏兮兮的，五官倒十分清秀：“你是小偷？”
[ZH] “谁……谁是小偷？别污蔑人！”
[ZH] “你不是小偷，为何偷别人的钱袋？”
[ZH] “我不是偷，是借！”
[ZH] 申简辰觉得这个小贼倒挺能狡辩：“借？骗谁呢？刚才那人当着所有人喊你小贼，不是偷又是什么？”
[ZH] 小贼坐在地上，显得很坦然：“信不信随你，我坐在这里，等他来了，一问便知。”
[ZH] 丢钱的人还未到，无法辨别，反正有他在此，小贼根本逃脱不了。
[ZH] “你叫什么？”
[ZH] “凭什么告诉你？”
[ZH] “凭我能追上你。”
[ZH] “哼，你的脚力确实厉害，既比不过你，告诉你也无妨，我叫白风。”
[ZH] “白风，名字还不错，你能不能站起来，蹲个马步让我瞧瞧？”
[ZH] “你这人真奇怪，凭什么让我蹲马步？我才不听你的。”
[ZH] 申简辰伸出手，一把将白风拽起，用一只手把他的肩膀压下，用脚分别在两腿处踢两下，白风立刻呈马步状。接着又点了白风的穴位，站在一旁仔细瞧，心里赞道：“根骨确实不错！这趟街逛得值，碰到一个好徒弟。”
[ZH] 白风不满道：“你这人真没道理，我把钱袋已给你，凭什么这样对待我？快放开我！”
[ZH] “这是对你的惩罚，谁让你不干好事，偷别人的钱袋？等失主来，他让我放你，我再放。”
[ZH] 刚说完，中年男人才赶到，气喘吁吁地说：“谢谢……谢谢公子……帮我……帮我追到……他。”
[EN] Years of Shen Fist had honed both his hands and his footwork. Shen Jianchen tore through the crowded street at blazing speed, closing fast on the pickpocket. Ducking into a quiet lane and then a dead-end alley, the boy ran himself ragged; glancing back to see his pursuer cruising without even breaking a sweat, he gave up and doubled over gasping, "Stop... stop chasing... I quit... here, take the pouch!"
[ZH] 申简辰把钱袋给了中年男人，他拿过钱袋一屁股坐在地上，缓了一会儿说：“白风，你这小子，借钱就好好借，为何拿了我的钱袋就跑？要不是这位公子，我的钱都被你拿走了。”
[EN] Halting calm without a heavy breath, Shen Jianchen took the money pouch and sized up the lad: fifteen or sixteen, tattered garments, grimy face, yet crisp, handsome features underneath. "Pickpocket, huh?"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“你们认识？”
[EN] "Who's a thief?! Don't slander me!"
[ZH] 白风说：“听到没？我不是偷！是拿！更准确地说，是借！”
[EN] "Not a thief? Then why steal his money pouch?"
[ZH] 申简辰厉声训斥：“不经过他人允许，拿和借跟偷有什么区别？”
[EN] "Didn't steal—I borrowed!"
[ZH] 白风无言以对，满脸不服气。
[EN] Cheeky kid! Shen Jianchen smirked. "Borrowed? Tell that to the man screaming 'thief' down the whole street! If snatching and running isn't stealing, what is?"
[ZH] 中年男人基本缓过来，站起身：“公子，我认识这孩子，他本说借钱，我把钱袋拿出来给他取，结果他一把将钱袋抢走。”
[EN] Plopping straight onto the dirt unfazed, the boy shot back, "Believe me or not. I'll sit right here till he catches up and you can ask him yourself!"
[ZH] 白风不满道：“哼！你虽愿意借给我，可根本不够，我娘医病需五两银子，而你只答应借给我五百文铜钱，我虽抢你的钱袋，可我也答应以后挣钱还你！你就是个小气鬼！”
[EN] With the owner not here yet, and zero chance the lad could slip past him, Shen Jianchen asked, "What's your name?"
[ZH] 中年男人辩解道：“我的钱也不是大风刮来的，都是辛苦赚的，还要养家糊口，都借给你，我一家老小该怎么办？”
[EN] "Why should I tell you?"
[ZH] “可我娘还在赵太丞家病着呢！”说着白风哭起来，“呜呜……！我娘得重病，大夫说需花费五两银子，才给我娘医治，再不治，她会死掉！呜呜……！”
[EN] "Because I outran you."
[ZH] 中年男人产生怜悯之心，从钱袋里取出一两，递到白风面前：“我不能给你太多，希望你能理解。”
[EN] "Hmph, fast legs, I'll give you that. Fair enough: Bai Feng."
[ZH] 白风被点穴位，不能动弹，目睹银子被申简辰接到手中，又还给中年男人：“银子你还是拿回去，白风娘治病需要的银两我来出。”
[EN] "Bai Feng—good name. Stand up and drop into a horse stance so I can see."
[ZH] 中年男人接过银子高兴道：“那就好！那就好！白风啊，你遇到好人，大叔家里还有事，先走了。”
[EN] "Weird guy! Why should I squat in a horse stance for you? No way!"
[ZH] 男人装好银子走后，申简辰又点了白风的穴位：“我的银子不白给，以后你必须跟着我。”
[EN] Reaching down, Shen Jianchen yanked Bai Feng straight up, pressed one palm down on his shoulder, tapped both legs apart with his foot into a textbook horse stance, and struck an acupoint freezing him in place. Stepping back to inspect his build, delight lit his eyes: Top-grade martial bones! Worth every step of today's stroll—found myself a prime apprentice!
[ZH] 马步蹲得时间长，白风的腿有些麻，边活动边问：“我凭什么跟着你？”
[EN] Fuming, Bai Feng yelled, "What's wrong with you?! I already handed over the pouch! Why freeze me like a statue?! Let me go!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“凭你每月可以拿到一两银子。”
[EN] "Punishment for swiping someone's pouch. When the owner gets here, if he says let you go, I let you go."
[ZH] 白风仔细盘算，觉得很划算：“好！我答应你，现在把五两银子给我。”白风伸出手，一副要银子的姿态。
[EN] Right then the middle-aged man stumbled into the alley, wheezing hard. "Thank... thank you, young master... for catching him..."
[ZH] 申简辰身上没有带银两：“银子在丫鬟那里，你跟我去脚店拿。”
[EN] Handing over the pouch, Shen Jianchen watched the man collapse onto the ground catching his breath before scolding, "Bai Feng, you rascal! Want to borrow coin, ask proper! Why grab my whole pouch and run? If not for this gentleman, you'd have wiped me out!"
[ZH] 申简辰带着白风离开冷清的小巷，来到热闹的街市，穿过人群，找到离开之地，向周围仔细瞧了瞧，发现一家最近的脚店，旁边停着王府的马车，便朝脚店的方向走去。
[EN] "You two know each other?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 进入脚店后，果然是供人休息之地，还提供茶和水酒，当然不是免费。里面人不少，不时有伙计跑来跑去添茶倒水或上酒。
[EN] "Hear that?!" Bai Feng crowed. "Not stealing—taking! Better yet, borrowing!"
[ZH] 很顺利找到乔梦容，她正坐在角落的一个桌旁，面前放着一盏茶，旁边站着丫鬟喜兰。
[EN] Shen Jianchen snapped sharp, "Taking or borrowing without permission IS stealing!" Silenced, Bai Feng glared in stubborn defiance.
[ZH] 乔梦容看到哥哥，起身迎上去：“哥哥，你可来了，让我好等。”接着瞧见哥哥身后的小子，问道：“哥哥，他是谁？”
[EN] Catching his breath and climbing up, the man explained, "I know this boy, young master. He asked to borrow coin; when I opened my pouch to count some out, he snatched the whole bag and bolted."
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，直接对喜兰说道：“给他五两银子。”
[EN] Bai Feng shot back bitter, "Hmph! Five hundred wen wasn't near enough! My mother needs five taels of silver to save her life, and you only offered five hundred copper coins! Sure I grabbed your bag, and I swore I'd earn it back and repay you! Stingy miser!"
[ZH] 喜兰很不情愿，王爷已发话，不能不遵从，拿出一个装着五两银子的钱袋递给白风：“给你！拿着！”
[EN] "My coin doesn't blow in on the wind either!" the man argued. "I sweat for every copper to feed my whole family. Lend you all of it and what do my wife and kids eat?!"
[ZH] 白风接过，十分感激：“感谢公子，我娘有救了。”
[EN] "My mother's dying at Grand Physician Zhao's Clinic right now!" Tears burst down Bai Feng's grimy cheeks. "Sob...! She's gravely ill! The doctor wants five taels to treat her, or she'll die! Sob...!"
[ZH] 说着就要走，被申简辰一把拉住，在他耳边小声说：“记住，处理好你娘的事，来仪王府找我，不得食言，否则我挖地三尺也会把你找到。”
[EN] Softening with pity, the man fished one tael from his pouch and held it out. "This is all I can spare. Hope you understand."
[ZH] 既温暖又充满威胁的话语让白风很舒服，听到“仪王府”三个字，他快要傻眼，心里嘀咕：“妈呀！仪王府，这人该不会是仪王府的侍卫总管？我今儿可是遇到贵人，难道他想让我到仪王府当侍卫？”于是高兴地说：“不用挖地三尺，这么好的事，我怎么会不去？等治好我娘的病，一定去仪王府。”
[EN] Frozen by the acupoint, Bai Feng watched Shen Jianchen catch the silver and press it right back into the man's hand. "Keep your silver. I'll pay for Bai Feng's mother's treatment."
[ZH] 申简辰将手放开：“去吧!”
[EN] Relieved, the man beamed as he stowed his coin. "Bless you, bless you! You met a true savior today, Bai Feng! Got chores at home, I'm off!"
[ZH] 白风二话没说，撒腿跑出脚店，往赵太丞家而去。
[EN] Once the man left, Shen Jianchen tapped Bai Feng's acupoint free. "My silver isn't free. From now on, you follow me."
[ZH] 这趟逛街收获颇丰，既解决心中疑惑，又遇到一位根骨不错的小子，申简辰很满意，不想再逛下去。
[EN] Shaking out stiff legs from holding that horse stance, Bai Feng asked wary, "Why should I follow you?"
[ZH] 考虑到乔梦容逛了许久，腿脚肯定已累，加之仪王府在郊区，比较远，申简辰主动要求乘坐马车。
[EN] "For one tael of silver every month."
[ZH] 乔梦容很开心，哥哥看上去一点儿都不累，他若徒步回王府肯定没有任何问题，之所以乘坐马车，完全是关心她的缘故。
[EN] Doing quick math—a sweet deal!—Bai Feng grinned and thrust out an open palm. "Deal! Hand over the five taels now!"
[ZH] 登上马车，乔梦容有意坐在哥哥身边，很自然地靠在他的肩头。以前总是哥哥这样靠着她，现在换回来，她感到无比幸福，认真体会着哥哥突然之间变得十分有力的肩膀。
[EN] Cashless himself, Shen Jianchen said, "My maid holds the silver. Come back to the Foot Shop with me."
[ZH] 申简辰抬起手，想要扶起靠在肩膀上的脑袋，刚到半空又放下。心想，靠就靠吧，反正妹妹靠靠哥哥也没什么不妥。
[EN] Leaving the quiet alley for the noisy street, they threaded through crowds back toward where he had left Mengrong, spotted Prince Yi's carriage parked outside the nearest Foot Shop, and stepped inside. Sure enough, weary shoppers sat resting over tea and light wine—not free, of course—while waiters bustled around refilling cups. They spotted Qiao Mengrong seated at a corner table beside a tea bowl, with Xilan standing watch behind her.
[ZH] 他又陷入对千里寻的思念之中，回想着算卦老先生的话：“底下一个‘寸’字，说明您与所找之人即使见到，方寸之间又无法相认……”管不了那么多，先找到人再说。老先生说在南边，不妨去南边找找，可南边很大，地方很多，到底该去哪里？
[EN] Rising to greet him, Qiao Mengrong smiled. "Back at last, Brother! You kept me waiting!" Eyeing the scruffy boy behind him, she asked, "Who is he?"
[ZH] 带着一路上的疑问回到仪王府，一进门，丫鬟来报：“王爷，郡主，乔公子从莱州回来了。”
[EN] Without answering, Shen Jianchen told Xilan, "Give him five taels of silver."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“知道了，乔公子人在哪里？我带哥哥去见他。”
[EN] Reluctant yet bound by princely command, Xilan produced a pouch holding five taels. "Here! Take it!"
[ZH] 丫鬟回道：“正在会客厅等王爷。”
[EN] Clutching the pouch, Bai Feng beamed with gratitude. "Thank you, young master! My mother's saved!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“梦容，不知我以前如何称呼乔公子？”
[EN] He spun to dash off, only for Shen Jianchen to catch his collar and murmur low in his ear, "Remember: once your mother's settled, report to Prince Yi's Manor and ask for me. Break your word, and I'll dig three feet underground to drag you back!"
[ZH] 乔梦容回道：“乔公子名唤乔文仙，字文渊，哥哥平常称呼他文渊。”
[EN] Half warm, half menacing—and "Prince Yi's Manor" stopped Bai Feng cold! Holy cow, is this guy Captain of the Guard at Prince Yi's Manor?! Hit the jackpot today—wants to hire me as a manor guard! Grinning ear to ear, he swore, "No need to dig! Why pass up a gig like this? Soon as Mom's cured, I'm coming straight to Prince Yi's Manor!"
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“还好还好，只要不叫他舅舅，怎么称呼都无所谓。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let go. "Go on!" Bai Feng bolted straight out the door toward Grand Physician Zhao's Clinic.
[ZH] 申简辰与乔梦容来到会客厅，椅子上坐着一位身着白衣的男子，见他们进来，起身行礼：“仪王，佳和郡主。”
[EN] Doubts cleared and a prime apprentice recruited, today's stroll had paid off big. Ready to wrap up, and figuring Mengrong's feet must ache after hours walking—plus Prince Yi's Manor sat far out in the suburbs—Shen Jianchen suggested taking the carriage home. Delighted—healthy enough to walk back without breaking stride, he only rode for her sake!—Qiao Mengrong glowed inside.
[ZH] 申简辰快要看呆。
[EN] Aboard the carriage, she sat close beside him and rested her head against his shoulder. Before, frail Zhao Pu had always leaned on her; trading places now filled her with bliss as she felt the solid strength beneath her cheek. Half raising a hand to nudge her upright, Shen Jianchen paused midair and let his arm drop. Fine, let a little sister lean on her brother's shoulder; no harm done.
[ZH] 眼前的男子面庞洁白如玉，皮肤丰满而光滑，散发着健康的光泽；他的眼睛深邃明亮，像是藏着无尽智慧的星辰，又透露出内敛而深邃的气质；鼻子挺直修长，唇红齿白，微微上扬的嘴角勾勒出一抹自信而迷人的微笑；他的身材高挑，体魄健康，均匀挺拔的身姿不禁让人为之倾倒。
[EN] Thoughts drifted straight back to Qianli Xun and the fortune-teller's reading: "Even standing inches apart, you won't recognize each other..." Worry about recognition later; find her first! Head south, the old man said, yet the south stretched vast. Where on earth to start?
[ZH] 申简辰的脑中蹦出两个词语：“风度翩翩，貌若潘安。”
[EN] Turning that riddle over all the way back to Prince Yi's Manor, he stepped through the gate to a waiting maid: "Your Highness, Princess, Young Master Qiao has returned from Laizhou!"
[ZH] “我一进汴梁听人说起仪王之事，赶紧回来看个究竟，仪王果然已恢复健康，定是姐姐在天有灵。”乔文仙说着，上前抓住仪王的手，将他上下瞧了一遍，“仪王脱去病体，尽显男子英气，实在可喜可贺。”
[EN] "Understood," Qiao Mengrong said. "Where is he? I'll take Brother to meet him."
[ZH] 乔文仙又一把将仪王抱住：“身板也变得硬朗，稳如泰山，好，真是太好了！”
[EN] "Waiting in the reception hall, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰被乔文仙这么一抱，一股暖流涌入心头，来到这个陌生的时代，多了一位亲人和朋友，他感到很幸福，不由伸出双臂也将乔文仙抱住：“文渊，我虽疾病全无，但什么都不记得，要不是梦容告诉我，都不知道该如何称呼你。”
[EN] "Mengrong, what did I call Young Master Qiao before?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王：“不记得就不记得，身体恢复健康才最重要，再说以前的你生活在病榻上，难得走出府外，也没什么值得记住的事，只要记住你小时候出去过一趟，捡回来个妹妹这一件事即可，其他皆可忘记。”
[EN] "His name is Qiao Wenxian, courtesy name Wenyuan. You always called him Wenyuan."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“这件事哥哥也不记得，不过我与邹嬷嬷已把一切告诉哥哥。”
[EN] Whew, Shen Jianchen thought—long as I don't have to call a guy my own age Uncle!
[ZH] “那便好。”乔文仙拉起仪王，“我们别站门口叙话，还是坐下说。”
[EN] Entering the reception hall, they found a man in crisp white robes seated in a chair, who rose and bowed at once. "Prince Yi. Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 乔梦容赶快让喜兰吩咐丫鬟上茶。
[EN] Shen Jianchen almost stared slack-jawed. Face fair as carved jade, smooth skin glowing with health; deep, clear eyes shining like starlit pools of quiet intellect; straight nose, clean lips curving into a warm, confident smile; tall, balanced, upright carriage that turned heads on sight. Two phrases jumped straight into Shen Jianchen's head: elegant and refined—handsome as Pan An!
[ZH] 三人坐定后，乔文仙说：“仪王既然身体恢复健康，不如跟我去趟临安，见见临安的亲人，他们见到你现在这样，肯定会很开心。”
[EN] "Heard news of you the instant I entered Bianliang and raced straight back to see for myself! Truly healthy—Sister's spirit in Heaven must watch over you!" Clasping Prince Yi's hands to look him over, Qiao Wenxian beamed. "Casting off that frail frame brought out pure manly vigor—what a blessing!" Wrapping Prince Yi in a hearty embrace, he added, "Sturdy build too, steady as Mount Tai! Marvelous!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“临安？可在南边？”
[EN] Warmth surged in Shen Jianchen's chest; gaining a true friend and kinsman in this strange century felt wonderful. Returning the hug, he smiled, "Wenyuan, every illness is gone, yet my memory's wiped clean. If Mengrong hadn't told me, I wouldn't even know what to call you."
[ZH] 乔文仙回道：“正是，景色相当宜人，如若你去了，我带你去西湖走走，绝对不枉此行。”
[EN] Letting go, Qiao Wenxian waved it off. "Forget what's gone; health matters most! Stuck in bed all those years barely leaving the manor, nothing worth keeping anyway except that one childhood trip out when you picked up a little sister! Toss the rest!"
[ZH] “好！那我就跟你去趟临安。”申简辰正愁到南边什么地方去找千里寻，乔文仙就出现，还提到临安，他冥冥中认为定是上天的安排。
[EN] "He forgot that too, though Matron Zou and I told him everything," Qiao Mengrong smiled.
[ZH] 一旁的乔梦容想起算卦时的情景，再看哥哥渴望而又欢喜的表情，愁绪不由袭上心头，她继续在心中自问：“哥哥到底要找什么人？”
[EN] "Good, good! Quit standing by the door; let's sit and talk!" Qiao Wenxian guided Prince Yi inside while Qiao Mengrong sent Xilan to fetch tea.
[ZH] 丫鬟把泡好的茶端上来，给三人放好，退下后，乔梦容收拾好心情，问道：“乔公子打算回临安？”
[EN] Once all three sat down, Qiao Wenxian pitched an idea: "Now that you're healthy, Prince Yi, why not travel south to Lin'an with me to meet your mother's family? Seeing you hale like this will make them overjoyed!"
[ZH] “是该回去，离开家都快一年，再说那里有我喜欢的人，也该回去看看她。”
[EN] "Lin'an? Is that to the south?" Shen Jianchen sat straight up.
[ZH] “哦，乔公子已有喜欢之人，岂不是令喜欢乔公子的女子伤心？”
[EN] "Straight south! Stunning scenery—come along and I'll walk you around West Lake, guaranteed worth every mile!"
[ZH] “只可惜她已嫁人。”
[EN] "Deal! I'm going to Lin'an with you!" Just wondering where in the vast south to search for Qianli Xun, and Qiao Wenxian dropped Lin'an right into his lap! Had to be Heaven guiding his steps.
[ZH] “既已嫁人，乔公子还是放下为好。”
[EN] Watching her brother's eager grin, Qiao Mengrong recalled the fortune-teller's stall, and unease crept into her chest: Who on earth is Brother looking for?
[ZH] “佳和郡主有所不知，她嫁的丈夫与她志趣不相投，还经常打她，我虽未亲眼目睹她受伤的样子，可读了朋友信中所说，已揪心不已，决定此次回去，劝她与丈夫和离。”
[EN] A maid set fresh tea before the three and withdrew. Steading her mood, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Heading home to Lin'an for good, Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] “乔公子的想法真是大胆，令梦容佩服。”
[EN] "About time. Been away almost a year, plus someone I love lives there. High time I went back to see her."
[ZH] 申简辰只顾在一旁听两人聊天儿，只怪他不了解文渊，不知该与他聊些什么。
[EN] "Oh? Young Master Qiao already holds someone in his heart—how many fond ladies will weep at that!"
[ZH] 这时有侍卫前来报告：“王爷，刚收到两封从临安寄来的信，都是给乔公子的。”
[EN] "Only sorrow is, she's already married."
[ZH] 旁边伺候的丫鬟接过，送至乔文仙手中。
[EN] "If she's wed, better to let go, Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 乔文仙打开其中一封，看后先是气愤：“刚说到她被丈夫毒打，信中又提此事，我决不能让她在火炕再待下去！”
[EN] "You don't know, Princess Jiahe: her husband shares none of her soul and beats her all the time! Never saw her wounds with my own eyes, yet reading my friend's letters tears my heart out. I'm going back to urge her into mutual divorce."
[ZH] 乔梦容观察乔文仙的表情，想不通什么样的女子都已嫁人，还能让乔文仙有如此表现？
[EN] "Bold indeed, Young Master Qiao. I admire your spirit."
[ZH] 接着又看到乔文仙面露笑容，继续说：“信中说，她打算与丈夫和离，与我想到一处，明日一早我便回临安，早些回去，说不定还能帮到她。”
[EN] Knowing nothing of Wenyuan's past, Shen Jianchen sat quiet and listened.
[ZH] 乔文仙又拆开另一封书信，从中取出被叠得整整齐齐的一张纸，打开后，纸张挺大，上面写着一首词，“伊”字上滴着一滴墨痕。他将词在心里读了一遍，口中说道：“‘和别泪，看青山’，‘争奈醒来，愁恨又依然’，她心里该有多苦，才能写出这样的词，不行，我现在就回临安。”
[EN] Just then a guard stepped in to report: "Your Highness, two letters just arrived from Lin'an for Young Master Qiao." A maid took them and delivered them to Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] 申简辰立刻站起来：“我这就去收拾一番，跟文渊一起走。”
[EN] Breaking open the first, Qiao Wenxian flared in anger: "Just talking about her husband beating her, and here's another letter saying he did it again! No way I leave her suffering another day in that living hell!" Watching his fierce reaction, Qiao Mengrong wondered what sort of married woman held Qiao Wenxian this enthralled. Reading further, relief lit his face: "Says she's pushing for mutual divorce—right in line with my plan! I'm leaving for Lin'an first thing tomorrow morning; get back early and maybe I can help!"
[ZH] 乔梦容也站起来：“我陪哥哥一起去。”
[EN] Opening the second envelope, he unfolded a large sheet holding a handwritten lyric, a single drop of black ink staining the character "you" (伊). Reading silent, he murmured, "'Through farewell tears, I gaze at green hills... Yet waking finds sorrow and resentment waiting just as before...' How bitter must her heart be to write lines like these?! Forget tomorrow—I'm leaving for Lin'an right now!"
[ZH] 三人刚达成一致，侍卫前来报道：“王爷，宫中的内侍来了，在外面等候。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sprang to his feet too. "I'll pack right now and ride with you, Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 三人一起迎出去，只看到一名内侍前来，不是上次那位。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong rose beside him. "I'm coming with you, Brother!"
[ZH] 内侍上前拜见仪王：“仪王殿下果然疾病全无，看上去很精神。乔公子也在，真是太好了，我带来官家的请帖，邀请二位明日参加宫中举办的宴会。”
[EN] Just as all three sealed their plan, a guard stepped in to announce: "Your Highness, an Imperial Attendant from the palace awaits outside."
[ZH] 申简辰接过请帖，打开浏览一遍，心中埋怨官家举办宴会实在突然，搞得他无法今日出发去临安。
[EN] All three walked out to meet a lone eunuch, not the same one as before. Bowing low, the eunuch beamed, "Prince Yi truly looks the picture of health—and Young Master Qiao is here too, wonderful! I bring His Majesty's invitation summoning both of you to a palace banquet tomorrow."
[ZH] 申简辰还是有些历史常识，官家邀请，无法拒绝，只好说：“请内侍回禀父皇，明日我与文渊会按时参加。”
[EN] Opening the card for a quick glance, Shen Jianchen cursed the timing under his breath for derailing today's departure. Still, knowing enough court sense never to refuse the emperor, he inclined his head: "Tell Imperial Father that Wenyuan and I will attend on time tomorrow."

#CH 34
[ZH] 内侍走后，申简辰瞧着手中的请帖，只觉麻烦，官家既然不重视仪王，为何还要送来请帖？搞得他还得留在汴梁。
[EN] Once the eunuch left, Shen Jianchen stared at the invitation feeling annoyed—never favored Prince Yi before, why summon him now and stall the trip to Lin'an?
[ZH] 申简辰觉得自己变了，穿越前是公司CEO，最是喜欢跟身份尊贵，有钱之人打交道，现在竟然变得无所谓，甚至从心里觉得麻烦。也许人只有死过一回，才能明白金钱名利如过眼云烟。
[EN] Funny how he had changed: back as a CEO, he loved rubbing elbows with powerful, wealthy elites; now he couldn't care less and found court politics a headache. Guess dying once really showed how hollow cash and status were.
[ZH] 又一想，新觅的徒弟白风还未前来报到，不如趁宴会多留一日，等他亲自送上门来。
[EN] On the bright side, his new apprentice Bai Feng hadn't shown up yet—staying one extra day gave the kid time to report in.
[ZH] 乔梦容高兴道：“官家终于邀请哥哥！哥哥不必紧张，官家从未见过你，对以前的哥哥并不了解，你只需以真实的状态轻松应对即可。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong beamed with pride: "His Majesty finally invited you, Brother! Don't be nervous—he's never met you before and doesn't know your old self, so just be natural and relaxed!"
[ZH] 申简辰对乔梦容的体贴投以微笑。
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave her a warm smile for her thoughtfulness.
[ZH] 乔梦容又转向乔文仙：“乔公子是官家宴会中的常客，对官家的喜好多少了解一些，不如给哥哥讲讲，让他好提前有个心里准备。”
[EN] Turning to Qiao Wenxian, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Young Master Qiao attends palace banquets all the time and knows His Majesty's tastes—please brief Brother so he knows what to expect."
[ZH] “谨遵郡主吩咐。”乔文仙如数家珍般详细说明，“当今官家书法、绘画、诗词歌赋无一不通，极其推崇文人雅致，最喜举办宴会，不仅朝中大臣会参加，还会邀请有名的文人雅士。鄙人略懂书画歌赋，又能说上两句评论之语，也不知官家如何得知我的名头，在汴梁这段时日，只要宫中有活动，总会邀请我参加。”
[EN] "At your command, Princess," Qiao Wenxian smiled, rattling off details with ease. "His Majesty excels at calligraphy, painting, poetry, and lyrics alike—he adores scholarly elegance and throws banquets constantly, inviting both high ministers and noted literati. Dabbling a bit in art critique myself, somehow my modest name reached court; while living in Bianliang, I get invited to nearly every palace gathering."
[ZH] 乔梦容赞道：“乔公子过谦，你可是江南有名的才子，不仅在江南为人所知，到汴梁这段日子，也逐渐名声鹊起，足以证明你的才华。要说这江南真是人才辈出，我想起临安有位女词人，名叫朱淑真，她的诗词我读过一些，真是才华斐然，不知乔公子可见过她？”
[EN] "Too modest, Young Master Qiao!" Qiao Mengrong praised. "You're already celebrated across Jiangnan, and now your fame spreads through Bianliang too! Speaking of Jiangnan's rich talent, there's a female ci poet in Lin'an named Zhu Shuzhen—I've read several of her lyrics, truly brilliant! Have you ever met her?"
[ZH] 乔文仙叹道：“唉，她正是乔某喜欢之人。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian let out a soft sigh. "She is the very woman I love."
[ZH] 乔梦容也叹道：“原来乔公子喜欢的人是她。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed in echo. "So it's her..."
[ZH] 申简辰极少关注文学，他的文学知识仅限于上学期间所涉猎。上中学时，语文这门功课成绩还不错，但要站在专业角度鉴赏文学作品，他还是挺发憷的。
[EN] Literature had never been Shen Jianchen's strong suit—his exposure stopped at high-school Chinese class; respectable grades back then, sure, yet professional literary critique terrified him.
[ZH] 后来，因为千里寻写网络小说，他才会捧着手机读那些无聊的文字，只为追求美人时，能找到除拳法以外的共同话题。
[EN] Only because Qianli Xun wrote web novels had he forced himself to scroll through her books on his phone—just to find shared topics beyond boxing while wooing her!
[ZH] 在爱情力量的驱使下，他硬着头皮把千里寻写过的小说统统看了个遍，也仔细思索和体味过，对于网络小说这种文学形式还能略微谈些见解。
[EN] Driven by love, he had slogged through every single novel she published, digesting them enough to toss out decent comments on web fiction.
[ZH] 可对于那些没学过没见过的古诗词，他绝对是门外汉，更别说书画之类，更是一窍不通，参加官家的宴会只怕会出丑，给赵朴脸上抹黑。
[EN] Classical poetry and ink painting? Total blank! Showing up at an emperor's artsy banquet risked embarrassing Zhao Pu flat.
[ZH] 申简辰迅速打定主意，到时能躲多远便躲多远，那么多人，官家应该不会注意到他，毕竟赵朴不是官家在意的儿子。
[EN] Plan set fast: keep a low profile, blend into the crowd—with dozens of guests and zero imperial favor, His Majesty wouldn't spare him a glance.
[ZH] 乔文仙看出仪王面色中有些许担忧，宽慰道：“仪王不必担忧，到时你只需跟紧我，有什么不懂的，我悄悄告诉你。”
[EN] Spotting worry on his face, Qiao Wenxian reassured him: "Don't sweat it, Prince Yi—stick close to me, anything unfamiliar I'll whisper in your ear."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“有乔公子照顾，哥哥尽管放宽心。”
[EN] "With Young Master Qiao looking out for you, Brother, nothing to worry about," Qiao Mengrong added.
[ZH] 申简辰先谢道：“有劳文渊。”
[EN] "Much obliged, Wenyuan," Shen Jianchen thanked him.
[ZH] 第二天，申简辰与乔文仙乘坐马车应邀前往皇宫，他对即将谋面的父皇实在不熟悉，还想从乔文仙处多了解些信息：“文渊，我父皇叫什么名字？”
[EN] Next day, riding the carriage toward the palace with Qiao Wenxian, Shen Jianchen figured he ought to learn basic facts about the father he was about to meet: "Wenyuan, what's my father's name?"
[ZH] 乔文仙凑近，小声说：“官家的名讳本不应随便提起，仪王既问起，我就悄悄告诉你，当今官家大名赵佶。”
[EN] Leaning close, Qiao Wenxian whispered: "One shouldn't speak the sovereign's personal name aloud—since you asked, quietly: His Majesty is Zhao Ji."
[ZH] 穿越前申简辰虽不大关注过去的历史，可上学期间获得的历史知识还刻在脑中，宣和之类的年号他不甚熟悉，历代帝王的名讳还是有较为深刻的印象，赵佶不就是宋徽宗吗？
[EN] Rusty on reign titles like Xuanhe he might be, yet emperor names from school history still stuck—Zhao Ji was Emperor Huizong of Song!
[ZH] 他在历史上是位昏君，重用蔡京、王黼、童贯、高俅、朱勔、梁师成等人，这些人奸诈成性，横征暴敛，骄奢淫逸，要不怎么有会《水浒》一百零八个好汉被逼上梁山的故事？
