[EN] One of history's most notorious incompetent emperors—elevating treacherous scoundrels like Cai Jing, Wang Fu, Tong Guan, Gao Qiu, Zhu Mian, and Liang Shicheng to bleed the people dry in luxury, sparking the whole Water Margin saga of the hundred and eight heroes driven to Mount Liang!
[ZH] 想到这里，申简辰既失落又轻松，失落的是穿越而来，竟成为昏君的儿子；之所以轻松，是因为他从心里瞧不上昏君，既瞧不上便不会紧张，更不怕在昏君面前出丑。要知道昏君出的丑历史都记着呢！他申简辰是普通人物，出点儿丑最多被昏君和周围人笑笑，历史根本不会把他当回事。
[EN] Mixed feelings washed over Shen Jianchen: bummed to wake up son to an incompetent emperor, yet oddly relaxed—zero respect meant zero nerves! Who cared if he slipped up in front of Huizong? History remembered Huizong's blunders, not some obscure thirteenth prince's gaffes!
[ZH] 乔文仙奇怪道：“仪王听了官家的名讳，好像轻松许多，是何缘故？”
[EN] Puzzled, Qiao Wenxian asked, "You look way more relaxed hearing His Majesty's name—why?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，继续问：“不知文渊对我父皇，以及他管理的天下有何看法？”
[EN] Skipping the question, Shen Jianchen asked instead: "What do you honestly think of my father—and how he rules this empire?"
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“作为臣民，不应私下随便议论，不过仪王问起，我不妨说两句。”
[EN] "Subjects shouldn't gossip about court in private—yet since you ask, let me speak plain."
[ZH] 乔文仙又凑近仪王耳边：“当今官家是书画大家，用才华横溢四字形容绝不为过，大宋的天下表面一派繁荣祥和，老百姓的日子过得也算富足，不说别的，这汴梁街市的繁华便可见一斑，有时间我带你晚上出来逛逛，那才叫一个热闹……”
[EN] Lowering his voice further, Qiao Wenxian leaned close: "His Majesty is a towering master of calligraphy and painting—calling him gifted is no exaggeration. On the surface, the Great Song looks prosperous and peaceful, townsfolk live fairly well—just look at Bianliang's booming streets! Someday I'll take you out to see the night markets—dazzling..."
[ZH] 没等乔文仙说完，申简忍不住问：“你说表面一派繁荣祥和，实质上呢？”
[EN] Cutting right in, Shen Jianchen pressed: "On the surface—what about underneath?"
[ZH] 乔文仙声音压得更低：“实质上藏有隐患，外有北边金人虎视眈眈，内有起义军作乱，去年淮南盗生事，后被海州知州张叔夜打败，生擒义军副将，这股义军才不得不降。你可知淮南盗的首领是谁？”
[EN] Dropping to a bare murmur, Qiao Wenxian said, "Hidden perils everywhere. Up north the Jurchens of Jin eye us like tigers; inside, rebellions flare. Last year Huainan bandits rose up until Prefect Zhang Shuye of Haizhou defeated them and captured their deputy leader, forcing their surrender. Know who led those Huainan rebels?"
[ZH] 申简辰当然不知，摇摇头。
[EN] Shen Jianchen shook his head.
[ZH] “是位乡里小民，名叫宋江。”
[EN] "A rural clerk named Song Jiang."
[ZH] 申简辰立刻来了兴致：“宋江，我知道，他性情豪放，乐善好施，结识到不少英雄好汉，在百姓中有较高威望，是梁山起义的领袖，人称‘及时雨’……”
[EN] Eyes lighting up, Shen Jianchen burst out: "Song Jiang—I know him! Bold, generous, rallying heroes across the land, beloved by common folk, leader of the Mount Liang uprising—nicknamed Timely Rain!..."
[ZH] 正说着，见乔文仙用奇怪的目光看着他：“宋江反的可是你们赵家的天下，你怎么说起他如此兴奋？”
[EN] Mid-rave, he caught Qiao Wenxian staring at him wide-eyed: "Song Jiang rebelled against YOUR Zhao dynasty—why are you glowing about him?!"
[ZH] 申简辰赶紧解释：“我曾看过一本书，上面有位英雄豪杰，也叫宋江，没想到历史上真有宋江此人，不小心把两个人搞混了。”
[EN] Scrambling to cover, Shen Jianchen lied fast: "Read a book once with a hero named Song Jiang—didn't realize there was a real historical Song Jiang! Mixed the two up!"
[ZH] “真怀疑书上的宋江就是照着淮南盗首领宋江写的，要不你怎么说得那么像，是什么书？借我瞧瞧。”
[EN] "Sounds like that book copied the real Huainan bandit leader—how else match him so close? What book is it? Lend it to me!"
[ZH] 申简辰感到为难，又觉得好笑，宋代根本没有《水浒》，于是胡乱编道：“是我死后在另一个世界看的，看完又活过来，其他不记得，只记得这本书。”
[EN] Stuck—Water Margin wouldn't be written for centuries!—he spun on the fly: "Read it in the afterlife after I died! Woke back up remembering nothing else except that book."
[ZH] “仪王该不会是骗我吧？能有这等神奇之事？”
[EN] "Pulling my leg, Prince Yi? How could anything that wild happen?"
[ZH] “我可是起死回生之人，难道不能证明世上有神奇之事？”
[EN] "I literally came back from the dead—doesn't that prove wild things happen?"
[ZH] 乔文仙只能承认：“嗯……，也对，你以前病成那样，我就是长九颗脑袋，也不会想到有一天你能健康地坐在马车里和我聊天儿，好吧，信你一回。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had to concede: "Fair enough... frail as you were before, even with nine heads I never would've pictured you sitting healthy in a carriage bantering with me. Fine, I'll buy it."
[ZH] 申简辰又问：“也不知官家今日因何举行办宴会？”
[EN] Changing tracks, Shen Jianchen asked, "What's today's palace banquet even celebrating?"
[ZH] “官家举办宴会是常事，尤其在官家生日之际，场面更是宏大，而且要提前一个月准备。至于这次，如果我没猜错，应该是艮岳落成，庆祝庆祝。”
[EN] "His Majesty throws banquets nonstop—especially his birthday, planned a full month ahead! Today, if I guess right, celebrates completing Genyue."
[ZH] “艮岳是什么？”
[EN] "What's Genyue?"
[ZH] “看来你真的什么都不记得，艮岳是官家建的风景园林，里面有天下之灵石，南方艳美珍奇之花木，设有雕阑曲槛，修葺着亭台楼阁，历经数十年时间才建成。”
[EN] "Truly wiped your memory clean! Genyue is His Majesty's grand imperial landscape park—rare scholar rocks hauled from across the empire, exotic southern trees and blooms, carved railings, pavilions and towers—took decades to build!"
[ZH] 申简辰在心里批判道：“奢侈！不知搜刮了多少民脂民膏！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen cursed under his breath: Pure excess—how much of the people's blood and sweat did that suck dry?!
[ZH] 两人继续聊着，已到皇宫门外。下了马车，抬头看到“西华门”三个大字，仪王府离西华门较近，从这里进皇宫倒也方便。
[EN] Chatting on, they pulled up outside the palace walls beneath the sign "Xihua Gate"—closest gate to Prince Yi's Manor, nice and convenient.
[ZH] 申简辰向皇宫门口的侍卫出示了请帖，侍卫看后，恭敬地说：“仪王，乔公子，请。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen showed his invitation to the gate guards, who bowed deep: "Enter, Prince Yi, Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 他们走进皇宫，面前是一条直直的用石砖铺就的大道，大道旁整齐地摆放着轿子，每个轿子跟前站着四名侍卫。
[EN] Inside stretched a broad stone avenue lined with waiting sedan chairs, four guards stationed beside each.
[ZH] 一位内侍看到他们进来，迎上前：“乔公子请上轿。”
[EN] An attendant hurried forward: "Your sedan awaits, Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] “这位是仪王。”乔文仙见内侍未招呼仪王，猜他并不认识，故而介绍道。
[EN] Seeing the eunuch ignore his companion, Qiao Wenxian introduced him: "This is Prince Yi."
[ZH] 内侍连忙行礼：“奴才眼拙，没认出仪王，仪王这边请。”
[EN] The eunuch dropped into a hasty bow: "Blind eyes, forgive this humble servant—Prince Yi, right this way!"
[ZH] 申简辰与乔文仙分别上了轿子，每个轿子由四名侍卫抬着，走过一段路，穿过右承天门，又行了较长一段，在垂拱殿门外停下。
[EN] Each boarding a sedan chair hoisted by four guards, they passed through Youchengtian Gate and rode a long stretch before halting outside Chuigong Hall.
[ZH] 两人下了轿子后，侍卫很快将轿子抬走，毕竟垂拱殿之外庄严无比，不是放置轿子之处。
[EN] The instant they stepped out, guards whisked the chairs away—the sacred forecourt of Chuigong Hall tolerated no clutter.
[ZH] 迎面走来一位臣子，身着紫袍，头戴官帽，留着胡须，剑眉鼠目，表情严肃。
[EN] Approaching them came a purple-robed official wearing court headgear—bearded, sharp slanted brows, beady rat eyes, stern expression.
[ZH] 乔文仙上前行礼道：“高太尉，文渊这厢有礼了。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian bowed politely: "Grand Commandant Gao, Wenyuan pays respects."
[ZH] 高太尉瞧了瞧乔文仙：“原来是乔公子，最近你可是深得官家喜爱，身边这位是？”
[EN] Sizing up Qiao Wenxian, Grand Commandant Gao nodded: "Ah, Young Master Qiao—quite His Majesty's favorite of late! And your companion?"
[ZH] 乔文仙介绍道：“仪王。”
[EN] "Prince Yi," Qiao Wenxian announced.
[ZH] 高太尉严肃的表情立刻挤出笑容：“原来是仪王，听说您起死回生，疾病全消，真是可喜可贺！老臣今日有幸见到仪王真容，也是幸运。仪王若不嫌弃，与老臣一起进入垂拱殿，官家要先在这里接受朝臣以及文人才子跪拜，然后才带大家去艮岳欣赏美景。请！”
[EN] Instantly the official's stony face melted into a broad smile: "Prince Yi! Heard of your miraculous revival and full recovery—joyous news indeed! Meeting Your Highness in person honors this old minister! If you don't mind, enter Chuigong Hall with me—His Majesty receives the kowtows of court officials and scholars here first before leading everyone to tour Genyue. After you!"
[ZH] 申简辰在心里琢磨“高太尉”的称呼，难道此人正是《水浒》中被描写成无恶不作的高俅高太尉？因高太尉就在身旁，不好向文渊请教，只能将好奇心暂时搁置。
[EN] Grand Commandant Gao... could this be the infamous villain Gao Qiu from Water Margin?! With the man walking right beside him, Shen Jianchen held back from asking Wenyuan.
[ZH] 走进垂拱殿的大门，并未真正到殿内，而是一个很大的院落，里面聚集许多人，看衣着，有文武百官，也有像文渊一样的才子在其中。
[EN] Stepping through Chuigong Hall's outer gate opened into a vast courtyard teeming with officials in court robes and scholars like Wenyuan.
[ZH] 有官员过来与高太尉打招呼，高太尉向大家说：“你们猜猜这位是谁？”
[EN] Fellow ministers stepped up greeting Grand Commandant Gao, who beamed: "Guess who stands before you!"
[ZH] 大家都看向申简辰，一脸疑惑：“从未见过，还请太尉大人介绍。”
[EN] Every eye turned curious onto Shen Jianchen: "Never met him—please introduce us, Grand Commandant."
[ZH] 高太尉说：“官家的十三子仪王。”
[EN] "His Majesty's thirteenth son—Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 围过来的臣子都听说过仪王死而复生之事，纷纷说道：“我等终于有幸见到仪王真容。”“能看到仪王健康地站在我等面前，实乃荣幸。”
[EN] Having heard whispers of his resurrection, the ministers crowded in with smooth greetings: "At last we behold Prince Yi in person!" "An honor to see Your Highness standing hale before us!"
[ZH] 高太尉朝远处瞧了瞧，立刻说：“仪王，失陪，老臣去那边瞧瞧。”
[EN] Spotting someone across the yard, Grand Commandant Gao excused himself fast: "Pardon me, Prince Yi—let me step over there."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“高太尉尽管去忙。”
[EN] "Go right ahead, Grand Commandant."
[ZH] 高太尉离开后，围过来的臣子也都纷纷离开。申简辰才得空向乔文仙询问：“高太尉是否名叫高俅？”
[EN] The instant Gao left, the cluster of officials melted away too, finally leaving Shen Jianchen room to whisper: "Is Grand Commandant Gao's name Gao Qiu?"
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“没想到高太尉的大名你倒记得。”
[EN] "Well, well—you actually remember Grand Commandant Gao's name!" Qiao Wenxian marveled.
[ZH] 申简辰不由脱口而出：“大奸臣！”
[EN] "Major treacherous villain!" Shen Jianchen blurted out.
[ZH] 乔文仙赶紧拦道：“小声些，周围都是朝中大臣，被听去，传到高太尉耳朵里，对你不利。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hissed in panic: "Lower your voice! Court ministers everywhere—let word reach Grand Commandant Gao and you're in deep trouble!"
[ZH] “我是官家的儿子，我怕他？”
[EN] "I'm the emperor's son—scared of him?!"
[ZH] “你是个不受宠的皇子，有时还比不上朝中的大臣。难道你没看出来大家都不认识你？”
[EN] "Unfavored prince—sometimes lower in pull than senior ministers! Didn't you notice nobody here even recognized you?"
[ZH] 申简辰瞧向高俅：“高太尉正与何人说话？”
[EN] Glancing back toward Gao Qiu, Shen Jianchen asked, "Who's he talking to over there?"
[ZH] 乔文仙小声说：“是太子，你的大哥。”
[EN] "The Crown Prince—your eldest brother," Qiao Wenxian murmured.
[ZH] 申简辰又向另一边看去，也有一位被大臣们围着，看上去应该也是位皇子：“他又是谁？”
[EN] Looking the other way at another young man ringed by officials: "And him?"
[ZH] 乔文仙朝仪王说的方向瞧去，继续小声说：“是康王，你的九哥。”
[EN] "Prince of Kang—your ninth brother."
[ZH] 有一名内侍朝这边走来，正是申简辰在仪王府第一次见到的人。他悄悄问乔文仙：“向我们走过来的内侍叫什么？”
[EN] Just then an Imperial Attendant strode toward them—the very senior eunuch who had visited Prince Yi's Manor first! Shen Jianchen whispered: "Who's that eunuch walking over?"
[ZH] “梁师成，现任检校太傅，是个散官，但职位不低。”乔文仙尽量压低声音。
[EN] "Liang Shicheng—Honorary Grand Tutor! Sinecure title, yet massive clout," Qiao Wenxian breathed low.
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“又是一位奸臣！已经见到两位，接下来还不知会见到几位？”
[EN] Another treacherous villain! Two down already—how many more today?!
[ZH] 梁师成已走近，乔文仙有礼道：“内侍有何指教？”
[EN] As Liang Shicheng arrived, Qiao Wenxian bowed: "What instructions, Attendant?"
[ZH] 梁师成回礼：“官家一会儿就到，所有人即将进入大殿内，仪王第一次来，有些礼数不明白，下官前来说明。”
[EN] Returning the bow, Liang Shicheng spoke smoothly: "His Majesty arrives shortly and everyone enters the hall. Since this is Prince Yi's first attendance, allow me to brief you on protocol."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“内侍请讲。”
[EN] "Go ahead, Attendant."
[ZH] 梁师成便将规矩详细说明：“参加本次宴会的皇子加上仪王共二十六人，一会儿进入大殿内，仪王不能再与乔公子待在一处，要站到皇子处，与众位皇子一起拜见官家。还有，发生在仪王身上的事实属罕见，据下官猜测，官家定会问起，仪王要想好如何回答。就这些，下官已叮嘱完，还有其他事要安排，就不在此打扰。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng ticked off the rules: "Including Prince Yi, twenty-six imperial princes attend today. Inside the hall, Your Highness cannot stand with Young Master Qiao—you join the princes' ranks to kowtow before His Majesty together. Also, your revival is extraordinary; I expect His Majesty will ask about it, so have your answer ready. That is all—excuse me, other duties call."
[ZH] 梁师成走后，申简辰继续问：“难道进入大殿后我都没办法跟你待在一处？”
[EN] Once Liang Shicheng withdrew, Shen Jianchen asked, "Wait—do we have to split up inside the hall?"
[ZH] “放心，大殿内拜见官家，礼数还是要遵从的，离开大殿后比较自由，你我还能在一处。”
[EN] "Only for the formal kowtow inside—once we leave the hall, everyone mingles free and we stick together."
[ZH] “那便好。”
[EN] "Good."
[ZH] 只听传来梁师成的声音：“请各位进入大殿！”
[EN] Liang Shicheng's sharp cry echoed across the yard: "All assemble inside the hall!"

#CH 35
[ZH] 其他人向两边退去，皇子们则聚集到中间排好队。
[EN] Officials and scholars parted to both sides while imperial princes formed neat ranks down the center.
[ZH] 乔文仙推了仪王一把，申简辰只好向皇子的队伍走去。
[EN] Nudged forward by Qiao Wenxian, Shen Jianchen walked over to join his brothers.
[ZH] 官家的儿子的确不少，按照从大到小排列，太子站在最前面，他看上去大约二十多岁，最后一排大的也就十岁左右，最小的看上去只有三四岁。
[EN] Huizong sure had plenty of sons! Lined up oldest to youngest, the Crown Prince led the front row looking mid-twenties, while the back rows held ten-year-olds down to toddlers barely three or four.
[ZH] 申简辰心想，赵朴是官家十三子，应该站在中间一排，便迅速挤进去。
[EN] Thirteenth son—somewhere right in the middle! Shen Jianchen slipped straight into the center row.
[ZH] 所有皇子在太子带领下走进大殿内，接着是文武百官，最后是文人才子。
[EN] Led by the Crown Prince, the princes filed into the hall, followed by civil and military officials, and scholars last.
[ZH] 申简辰站在皇子中，向前瞅瞅，只看到前两排皇子的后脑勺；再用余光向两边瞧瞧，皇子们一个个面无表情。他甚觉无趣，干脆瞻仰起帝王的宝座。
[EN] Standing mid-pack, Shen Jianchen saw only the backs of older brothers' heads ahead and blank, expressionless faces on either side. Bored already, he studied the imperial throne instead.
[ZH] 宝座应是用珍贵的红木雕刻而成，底座宽阔而稳固，靠背上覆盖锦绣，扶手为龙形，雕刻得栩栩如生，烘托出皇帝的高贵身份。
[EN] Carved from prized red sandalwood on a wide, massive dais, draped in gold brocade with lifelike dragon armrests, the seat radiated imperial majesty.
[ZH] 传来梁师成的声音：“官家驾到！”
[EN] Liang Shicheng's cry rang out: "His Majesty arrives!"
[ZH] 申简辰的视线离开龙椅，刚好瞧见走向龙椅的赵佶。他身材高大，身着红色龙袍，头顶长翅帽，面色白皙，唇上两撇胡须，须眉长目，倒是一位帅哥。
[EN] Shen Jianchen shifted his eyes as Zhao Ji strode up to the throne—tall build, crimson dragon robes, long-winged court cap, pale refined features, neat mustache, fine brows and eyes—actually quite a handsome guy!
[ZH] 官家在龙椅上坐定后，双目环顾殿内所有人，慢条斯理地说：“大家不必紧张，放轻松些，今日又不是上朝。寡人还等着带你们欣赏艮岳美景，然后定要吃好喝好玩好，文人才子们也要尽情展示你们的才华。”
[EN] Settling onto the dragon throne, His Majesty swept a leisurely gaze across the hall. "Relax, everyone—no formal court session today! I'm eager to show you the wonders of Genyue, followed by fine food, fine wine, and merrymaking! And you scholars—be ready to shine!"
[ZH] 官家说完，梁师成大声宣道：“请大家依次跪拜官家！”
[EN] At his cue, Liang Shicheng announced: "Kneel and pay homage to His Majesty in order!"
[ZH] 先是皇子们走到大殿中间跪拜，都齐声称道：“父皇万岁！”申简辰只能跟着又跪又喊。
[EN] Princes stepped into the center first, dropping to their knees in unison: "Long live Imperial Father!" Shen Jianchen knelt and chanted right along.
[ZH] “平身吧！”当皇子们退到一边后，官家突然问，“仪王可来？”
[EN] "Rise!" As the princes stepped back into rank, His Majesty spoke up: "Did Prince Yi come?"
[ZH] 申简辰不得已从皇子中走出：“儿臣在。”
[EN] Stepping out from the middle row, Shen Jianchen bowed: "Your son is here."
[ZH] 赵佶将这位从未谋面的儿子仔细瞧了瞧：“倒是长得一表人才，因你从生下来就病着，寡人从未让你来过宫里，也因政务繁忙，没时间去看你，一晃都长这么大。听说你起死回生，不妨给寡人讲讲具体细节，毕竟这种事情难得一见。”
[EN] Zhao Ji looked over the son he had never seen face to face: "Fine bearing indeed! Sickly from birth, I never summoned you to court, and state affairs kept me from visiting—blink and you're grown! Heard you returned from the dead—tell me every detail! Rare marvel like that deserves hearing."
[ZH] 申简辰心里很不满，官家不关心国家政事，又是建造艮岳，又是对起死回生之说感兴趣，真是没得救！不如给他添油加醋瞎说一通，顺便映射一下腐败的朝廷。
[EN] Disgust stirred in Shen Jianchen—ignoring state crises to build pleasure parks and chase ghost stories! Hopeless ruler! Why not spin a spicy tall tale poking straight at his rotten court?
[ZH] ﻿“父皇在上，听儿臣细细道来。那日我只觉呼吸急促，最后一口气上不来，就闭了眼，感觉身体越来越轻，飘到空中，越飘越高，来到一个很奇怪的地方，也有官家和臣子，可那里的官家和臣子整日就知道吃喝玩乐，置百姓于不顾，儿臣看不惯打算离开。这时出现一位神人，教会我一套精妙的拳法，我很快练会，打了官家，杀了奸臣，无处可去又回到身体里，人就活过来，疾病也奇迹般消失。”
[EN] "Hear your son's account, Imperial Father. That day my breath grew short; gasping my last, I shut my eyes and felt light as air, floating higher and higher until I landed in a strange world! It had a sovereign and ministers too—only that ruler and his court did nothing all day but feast and play while ignoring the common people! Disgusted, I turned to leave—when a divine sage appeared and taught me a peerless boxing art! Mastering it in a flash, I beat that sovereign, slew his treacherous officials, and with nowhere left to go, dropped straight back into my body—waking alive with every illness gone!"
[ZH] 赵佶听了半天没言语，其他臣子也不敢说话。
[EN] Dead silence fell over the throne—not a minister dared breathe.
[ZH] 站在才子队伍中的乔文仙甚是担心，犹豫片刻走出来说：“官家，仪王的意思是他死后，看到一个不如大宋的地方，很不满，就回来了，足以说明仪王对大宋的天下十分眷恋，在草民看来仪王起死回生之事，正说明官家治理的天下深得人心。”
[EN] Breaking into cold sweat back in the scholars' rank, Qiao Wenxian hesitated one heartbeat before stepping out with cupped hands: "Your Majesty, Prince Yi means that after death he glimpsed a realm far inferior to our Great Song, felt repelled, and hurried back! Proof of his deep devotion to our Song empire—his miraculous revival confirms how deeply Your Majesty's reign wins all hearts!"
[ZH] 乔文仙的一番解释轻松化解尴尬，气氛变得缓和。
[EN] Smooth spin melted the freeze on the spot.
[ZH] 官家终于露出笑脸：“乔文仙的话令寡人满意，宴饮时，寡人定赏你一壶美酒。”
[EN] Zhao Ji broke into a pleased grin: "Well put, Qiao Wenxian! At the feast, I reward you a pot of imperial fine wine!"
[ZH] “谢官家！”乔文仙又退回到才子队伍中。
[EN] "Thank you, Your Majesty!" Qiao Wenxian backed smoothly into the scholars' line.
[ZH] 官家对仪王说：“你也退下吧。”
[EN] His Majesty waved at Prince Yi: "Step back too."
[ZH] 申简辰早已不想在众目睽睽之下待着，很快回到皇子的队伍中。
[EN] Glad to dodge the spotlight, Shen Jianchen slipped back among the princes.
[ZH] 接着文武百官和才子们也都跪拜官家。礼拜结束后，众人获得自由，赵佶也换上常服，带领众人坐上轿子，往艮岳而去。
[EN] Next civil officials, military brass, and scholars kowtowed in turn. Ceremonies done, everyone relaxed; changing into informal robes, Zhao Ji led the crowd out to sedan chairs bound for Genyue.
[ZH] 官家后宫之人，以及官宦家眷也被允许，纷纷赶来享受艮岳美景。
[EN] Palace consorts and officials' wives joined too, streaming toward the imperial gardens.
[ZH] 到达目的地，众人下了轿子，除了围在官家身边的要臣外，其他人则找趣味相投之人，三五人结伴而行。申简辰自然与乔文仙在一起，他们走在队伍的后面。
[EN] Arriving and dismounting, senior favorites clustered tight around the sovereign while everyone else split into congenial small groups. Naturally Shen Jianchen fell in beside Qiao Wenxian toward the rear.
[ZH] 乔文仙批评道：“刚才真是吓死我了，以后可不能再如此。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hissed in low rebuke: "Nearly scared the soul out of me back there! Never pull that again!"
[ZH] 申简辰不以为然：“我只说起死回生前去的地方，又没说官家和那些奸臣，你担心什么？”
[EN] Unbothered, Shen Jianchen shrugged: "Only talked about some place I saw while dead—never mentioned His Majesty or his cronies! What's there to panic over?"
[ZH] 乔文仙谨慎道：“小声些！傻子都能听出你的意思，再说官家与文武百官可都不是傻子，以后还需慎言！”
[EN] "Keep your voice down! Any fool caught your drift—and neither His Majesty nor these court foxes are fools! Watch your tongue from now on!"
[ZH] 申简辰只好说：“好吧，我以后注意。”
[EN] "Fine, fine—I'll watch it."
[ZH] 正说着，抬头见一个大石上写着“艮岳”两字。穿过大石，一波一波的美景映入眼帘：有高峰耸立，梅花万数；有瀑布飞流直下，池水碧青见底；亦有凫雁浮泳水面，栖息石间……，说也说不完，道也道不尽，真是奇花异草，奇珍异兽，极尽奢华。
[EN] Ahead loomed a monumental boulder inscribed "Genyue." Stepping past it revealed marvel after marvel: craggy peaks bristling with ten thousand plum trees, waterfalls plunging into emerald pools clear to the bottom, wild ducks swimming and perching over sculpted stones—rare blooms, exotic beasts, luxury pushed beyond imagination.
[ZH] 申简辰边欣赏边在心里慨叹，这位官家是他见过的最会享受之人，恨不得把天底下所有美的事物统统搬到艮岳之内。
[EN] Touring along, Shen Jianchen marveled silently: Nobody on earth knew how to splurge like Huizong—practically hauled every wonder under Heaven inside one garden!
[ZH] 正想着，听到一个女声：“仪王，乔公子，可否与你们结伴而行？”
[EN] Just then a poised woman's voice hailed them: "Prince Yi, Young Master Qiao—mind if I walk with you?"
[ZH] 申简辰站住，眼前出现一位妇人，看上去四十多岁，端庄秀丽，年轻时定是位亭亭玉立的女子。
[EN] Stopping, Shen Jianchen met a woman in her forties—dignified, graceful, clearly a stunner in her younger days.
[ZH] 乔文仙回道：“能与魏夫人同行，文渊不胜荣幸。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian bowed with warm pleasure: "Walking alongside Lady Wei honors Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 魏夫人又问：“仪王呢？可愿与我结伴而行？”
[EN] Turning to Shen Jianchen, Lady Wei asked, "And Prince Yi? Mind my company?"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“文渊乐意，我当然不介意。”
[EN] "If Wenyuan welcomes you, count me in."
[ZH] 乔文仙介绍道：“这位是魏夫人，乃曾丞相的家眷，也是我朝有名的女词人，北可与易安居士齐名，南可与幽栖居士媲美，是闺阁中的大家。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian introduced her: "This is Lady Wei, wife of Chancellor Zeng—one of our dynasty's foremost female ci poets! Up north she rivals Yi'an Jushi, down south she matches Youqi Jushi—a towering master of the inner chambers!"
[ZH] 申简辰开始在心里埋怨神柳，为何不让他穿越到一个重武轻文全是英雄豪杰的时代？这样他还会觉得自己有用武之地。偏偏来到重文轻武的宋朝，不是遇到大奸臣，就是遇到会写诗词之人，搞得他在这些人面前都不敢轻易张嘴。
[EN] Silent curses flew toward the Divine Willow again—why drop him into this bookish Song dynasty crawling with treacherous court officials and world-class poets instead of a martial era full of warriors?! Barely dared open his mouth around people like this!
[ZH] 面对文渊的介绍，又不能不回应，客气地说：“久闻魏夫人大名，今日得以相见，不胜荣幸。”
[EN] Still, manners first—he bowed smooth: "Long admired Lady Wei's great name; meeting you today is an honor."
[ZH] 魏夫人笑道：“仪王久居王府，我也只知你的大名，没见过人，今日终于见到庐山真面目，先祝贺仪王得以新生，仪王若不嫌弃，往后多到我府上走动走动。”
[EN] Lady Wei laughed warm: "With Your Highness tucked away in Prince Yi's Manor, I only knew your name until today—glad to behold the real person! Congratulations on your new lease on life! Drop by our manor anytime you're free."
[ZH] 申简辰客气道：“如若有机会定去。”
[EN] "Whenever I have the chance, certainly."
[ZH] 三人并肩走着，边走边欣赏美景。走了一会儿，魏夫人问：“乔公子什么时候回临安？”
[EN] Strolling three abreast taking in the vistas, Lady Wei asked after a while: "When are you heading back to Lin'an, Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] “本应今日已在回临安的路上，怎奈官家宴请，耽搁下来。”
[EN] "Meant to be on the road today—delayed by His Majesty's banquet."
[ZH] “如若回到临安，定要帮我邀请幽栖居士，让她抽时间来趟汴梁，顺便告诉她汴梁有位魏夫人想与她结交。”
[EN] "Once you return to Lin'an, invite Youqi Jushi on my behalf! Tell her to find time to visit Bianliang—Lady Wei longs to make her acquaintance."
[ZH] “放心，我定帮魏夫人转达。”
[EN] "Count on it—I'll pass word straight to her."
[ZH] 大家在艮岳足足逛了一上午，终于逛完，出了艮岳又乘坐轿子来到集英殿。集英殿是宫中宴请群臣的大殿，最尊贵的位置摆着一张大大的方桌，上面放满了珍馐佳肴，周围则摆着鼓墩。只见官家、太子与重要的臣子都走到方桌旁坐下。
[EN] Touring Genyue took the entire morning; exiting by sedan chair, everyone reconvened at Jiying Hall, the grand palace banquet hall. At the seat of honor stood a huge square table laden with delicacies encircled by drum stools where His Majesty, the Crown Prince, and top ministers sat.
[ZH] 离方桌不远处，则安置着各色小桌，依次供后宫妃嫔、皇子、其他大臣、才子，以及官眷就坐。申简辰不愿与皇子们一道，依然跟着乔文仙。他们两个刚坐下，魏夫人过来，坐在他们旁边。
[EN] Grouped nearby sat smaller tables for imperial consorts, princes, lesser ministers, scholars, and officials' wives. Steering clear of his royal brothers, Shen Jianchen stuck with Qiao Wenxian—and moments later Lady Wei walked right over to share their table.
[ZH] 经过艮岳一路，申简辰与魏夫人已熟，主动开口问：“魏夫人为何不与各位官眷同桌？偏偏喜欢与我和文渊挤在一起？”
[EN] Warm after their walk through Genyue, Shen Jianchen teased, "Why sit squeezed in with Wenyuan and me instead of the other court ladies, Lady Wei?"
[ZH] 魏夫人笑道：“仪王为何不与皇子们同桌？偏偏与乔公子挤在一处？”
[EN] Lady Wei smiled back: "Why sit squeezed in with Young Master Qiao instead of your royal brothers, Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 这话倒把申简辰问住，照实说道：“你也知道，我从小病着，很少出王府，故而与皇兄皇弟们不熟，没有共同语言。”
[EN] Touché! Answering plain, he said, "Sick all my life, barely left the manor—I'm a stranger to my brothers with nothing in common."
[ZH] 魏夫人说：“这便是我的答案，那些官眷坐在一起，只懂得讨论谁的首饰贵重，谁的衣裳锦缎珍贵，还有谁家女儿嫁给谁，谁家儿子又娶了谁，跟我实在不合，只能跑到你们这里来。不过，为何这一桌只有你们两个？”
[EN] "Exact same answer! All those ladies gab about is whose jewels cost more, whose silk is rarer, whose daughter married whom and whose son took which bride—drives me crazy! So I fled to your table! Wait—why are there only you two sitting here?"
[ZH] 乔文仙叹道：“唉！还不都怪仪王，当着众人的面说出那些话，让官家不高兴，大家都避之不及，害得我也交不到朋友。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian sighed wryly: "Blame Prince Yi! After his speech in the hall displeased His Majesty, everyone steers ten paces clear—costing me company too!"
[ZH] 魏夫人笑道：“仪王得敬我一杯。”
[EN] Lady Wei laughed: "Then Prince Yi owes me a toast!"
[ZH] 申简辰不大明白：“为何？”
[EN] "Why's that?" Shen Jianchen blinked.
[ZH] 魏夫人说：“只因我不畏权贵，在众人远离你时，还与你说话。”
[EN] "For defying power and sitting with you when everyone else ran clear!"
[ZH] 申简辰立刻端起酒壶，给魏夫人杯中斟满酒，也给自己倒满，举起酒杯：“魏夫人乃女中豪杰，必须敬！我先干了，你随意！”申简辰一饮而尽。
[EN] Snatching the wine pot at once, Shen Jianchen filled both her cup and his to the brim and raised his glass: "A true heroine deserves a toast! Bottoms up for me—drink at your pace!" He tossed his straight back.
[ZH] 魏夫人也一口喝下。
[EN] Lady Wei drained hers clean in one shot too!
[ZH] 乔文仙不满道：“你们两个只顾喝酒，把我晾到一边，实在不公平，我可是对仪王不离不弃，关键时候还替你说话，你是不是得多敬我几杯？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian protested: "Drinking up a storm while leaving me high and dry? Unfair! I stuck by Prince Yi through thick and thin and saved his neck back there—don't you owe me several cups?!"
[ZH] 申简辰二话不说，向旁边伺候的宫女挥挥手，等宫女过来，吩咐她再拿三个酒杯。宫女很快拿来，申简辰将四个酒杯倒满。
[EN] Without a word, Shen Jianchen waved a palace maid over and called for three extra cups, filling all four brimful.
[ZH] “文渊对我情深义重，一杯怎么能够？我喝四杯，你随意！”
[EN] "For loyalty deep as Wenyuan's, one cup won't cut it! Four for me—sip as you please!"
[ZH] 说着将四杯酒一一喝下，竟然面不改色。
[EN] Downing all four cups straight without flushing a shade, he set them dry.
[ZH] 乔文仙赞道：“好酒量！”也拿起自己的酒杯喝了。
[EN] "Iron liver!" Qiao Wenxian cheered, emptying his own cup.
[ZH] 这时一位内侍端着酒壶过来：“乔公子，官家赏你的美酒。”
[EN] Right then an eunuch approached carrying a wine pot: "Young Master Qiao, His Majesty's promised gift of fine wine."
[ZH] 乔文仙起身接过：“感谢官家恩赐，文渊不胜荣幸。”
[EN] Rising with cupped hands, Qiao Wenxian accepted: "Wenyuan bows in gratitude for His Majesty's favor!"
[ZH] 内侍离开后，魏夫人说：“仪王可还喝得下这壶美酒？”
[EN] Once the eunuch left, Lady Wei teased: "Got room left for this whole pot, Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“绝不在话下！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen grinned: "Piece of cake!"
[ZH] 三人吃着、喝着、聊着。
[EN] Food, wine, and lively talk flowed on.
[ZH] 整个宴会在丰盛的珍馐美味中度过，不时有舞伎表演跳舞，也有唱曲儿的女子手握琵琶弹唱，真是一派歌舞升平。
[EN] Course after sumptuous course graced the tables while dancing girls twirled and singing girls plucked Chinese lutes—a dazzling vision of gilded peace.
[ZH] 接着梁师成传达官家的意思，让文人才子各写一首赞美艮岳的诗词。很快宫女们拿来笔墨，在不远处整齐的书桌上摆好。文人才子个个摩拳擦掌提笔写下，再由宫女呈给官家阅览，最终魏夫人的诗词取得头筹，得到官家赞赏。
[EN] Next Liang Shicheng relayed His Majesty's call for every scholar to compose a poem praising Genyue. Palace maids set writing gear along rows of desks; scholars dipped brushes eagerly and submitted their pieces—Lady Wei winning top prize and glowing praise from the throne!
[ZH] 官家也来了兴致，要当场泼墨，宫女们纷纷撤去方桌上的碗碟和吃剩的杂物，拿来一大块干净的布子铺上，迅速摆好笔墨纸砚，老臣蔡京研磨，高俅拿起笔双手捧到官家面前。官家喝完内侍捧的茶后，接过笔，迅速在纸上挥毫起来。
[EN] Caught up in the mood, His Majesty called for brush and ink himself. Maids swept the great square table clean, spread fresh cloth, and laid out the Four Treasures; senior minister Cai Jing ground ink while Gao Qiu presented the brush with both hands. Taking a sip of tea, Zhao Ji took the brush and swept bold strokes across the paper.
[ZH] 没多时，一幅《腊梅山禽图》已成，并在上面题下一首诗：“山禽矜逸态，梅粉弄轻柔。已有丹青约，千秋指白头。”写了落款。宫女拿来数方大印，一一盖了。
[EN] In minutes "Winter Plum and Mountain Birds" stood complete, crowned with an inscribed quatrain: "The mountain bird boasts a leisurely grace, / The plum blossoms display their gentle softness. / An eternal pact in ink and color we embrace, / For a thousand autumns, till our heads turn white." [TN: Translator's rendering] Signing his cipher, he had maids stamp several vermilion imperial seals.
[ZH] 周围的臣子无不赞叹有加。
[EN] Ministers erupted in ecstatic applause.
[ZH] 官家撂下笔：“你们这些人对寡人的诗画极其熟悉，溢美之词寡人不感兴趣，还是让寡人挑两人前来品鉴一番。”
[EN] Setting down his brush, His Majesty spoke: "You lot know my work too well—spare me empty flattery! Let me pick two fresh eyes to critique it."
[ZH] 赵佶瞧向文人才子处：“不如让乔文仙品品寡人的诗，至于画，仪王不妨品鉴一番。”
[EN] His gaze swept over to the scholars' side: "Qiao Wenxian—critique my poem! As for the painting—Prince Yi, step up and critique it!"
[ZH] 众人都看向乔文仙和仪王……
[EN] Every head in Jiying Hall swiveled toward Qiao Wenxian and Prince Yi...
[ZH] 乔文仙的水平大家领教过，品诗绝不在话下；至于仪王，是否能品得了帝王之画，真不好说。
[EN] Everyone knew Qiao Wenxian's brilliance with verse—could Prince Yi actually critique an imperial masterpiece?
[ZH] 申简辰猜测定是自己在大殿之上映射之词引起官家不满，这位父皇是要以此让他当众丢脸，众目睽睽之下不能拒绝，只能硬着头皮上。
[EN] Betting His Majesty remembered those barbs in Chuigong Hall and meant to humiliate him in public, Shen Jianchen knew refusing wasn't an option—time to bite the bullet and step up.

#CH 36
[ZH] 被众人盯着，乔文仙不敢妄动，压低嗓音，小声提醒：“别担心，诗题在画上，内容与画有关，我解完诗，画你自然会品，尽快随我上前感谢官家选中我们。”
[ZH] 两人在众目睽睽之下走到方桌跟前，乔文仙说：“感谢官家赏识。”
[ZH] 申简辰接着说：“感谢父皇赏识。”
[ZH] 赵佶说：“很好！你们两个谁先来？”
[EN] Under hundreds of eyes, Qiao Wenxian stayed still and murmured low, "Don't panic. The poem sits right on the painting and mirrors its scenes; once I unpack the verse, you'll have plenty to say about the painting. Hurry up and thank His Majesty with me."
[ZH] 乔文仙回道：“草民最喜第一个鉴赏，只因怕别人说了我想说的，还请仪王不要跟我抢。”
[EN] Walking together up to the great table beneath every stare, Qiao Wenxian bowed: "Thank you for your grace, Your Majesty."
[ZH] 申简辰瞅着桌上的画，毫无头绪，又怎会第一个往上冲？再说文渊也是为照顾他。他稳住心绪，装作镇定的样子：“我怎会与文渊抢？请。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen echoed, "Thank you for your grace, Imperial Father."
[ZH] 官家与重臣让开位置，乔文仙行礼后，走到《腊梅山禽图》前，将画作瞧了一遍，目光放在诗句上，用心品了一会儿，说道：“官家的字越发劲健挺拔，又不失妩媚，一笔一划风骨中尽显婀娜，每个字都透出帝王之气，令草民佩服……”
[EN] "Good! Which of you goes first?" Zhao Ji asked.
[ZH] 赵佶满意道：“别光顾着品寡人的字，解解诗句。”
[EN] "This humble subject always loves going first lest someone steal my words. Don't compete with me, Prince Yi!" Qiao Wenxian volunteered smooth.
[ZH] “是。”乔文仙行礼后，继续说，“官家的诗写了山禽与腊梅，山禽安逸轻松，腊梅花开轻柔，都极富美态。最妙的莫过于‘矜’与‘弄’二字，“矜”字写出山禽动中之静，安逸中尽显高贵；“弄”字写出腊梅静中之动富于生气。一动一静使整首诗充满意境之美，让人流连忘返，沉浸在诗中。再看下句才明白原来是丹青相约，顷刻间意趣横生。最后一句‘千秋指白头’升华了整首诗的意蕴，真是一语三关：一写世世代代白首不相离的忠贞爱情；二写官家愿为书画穷白首之力，喜爱之情溢于字里行间；三则蕴含着官家对千秋基业的忠贞追求，尽显帝王之气，令文渊倾佩。”
[EN] Staring blank at the scroll with zero ideas yet—and grateful for Wenyuan covering him—Shen Jianchen kept a calm face. "Wouldn't dream of stealing your turn, Wenyuan. Go ahead."
[ZH] 赵佶忍不住赞道：“还是文渊最了解寡人，你的品诗才华难有人及，赏赐纯金打造的金碗三个！”
[EN] As His Majesty and senior ministers stepped back to clear the view, Qiao Wenxian bowed, took in "Winter Plum and Mountain Birds," studied the inscribed verse a beat, and began: "Your Majesty's calligraphy grows ever more vigorous and crisp without losing supple beauty; every stroke balances iron bone with graceful flow, radiating imperial grandeur that leaves this subject in awe..."
[ZH] 梁师成捧来一个锦盒，送到乔文仙手中：“乔公子，官家赏赐的金碗，可要收好了。”
[EN] Pleased, Zhao Ji nudged him, "Enough praising my brushwork; unpack the poem."
[ZH] 乔文仙谢过官家后，收起锦盒。
[EN] "Yes, Your Majesty!" Bowing again, Qiao Wenxian went on, "Your Majesty's verse pairs mountain birds with winter plum—birds poised in ease, blossoms unfolding in soft grace, both rich in charm. Purest genius lies in the two words 'boasts' and 'displays': 'boasts' captures stillness inside motion, dignity within leisure; 'displays' captures vibrant life stirring within stillness! Motion and stillness weave together into boundless poetic mood. Moving to the third line reveals a pact sealed in ink and color, bursting with wit. And the closing line, 'For a thousand autumns, till our heads turn white,' lifts the whole poem on three layered meanings: first, faithful love between partners growing old together; second, Your Majesty's lifelong devotion poured into calligraphy and painting; third, Your Majesty's steadfast pursuit of a thousand-year imperial legacy—sheer sovereign majesty that leaves Wenyuan humbled!"
[ZH] 申简辰在一旁听着看着，着实佩服文渊能把短短的四句诗解出这么多内容来。等文渊说出最后的话时，他在心里批判道：“你就好好替官家吹吧，北宋很快会败落在昏君手中，还什么千秋基业，忠贞追求，他要真这样，也不会重用奸臣。”
[EN] Delighted, Zhao Ji praised him aloud: "Nobody understands me like Wenyuan! Matchless gift for verse—reward him three pure gold bowls!"
[ZH] 听到赏赐金碗，申简辰又十分不满，心想：“文渊吹嘘一下你的诗，就赏赐金碗，真是昏庸到家！金碗应该赏赐给那些为国家出生入死，建功立业之人！”
[EN] Liang Shicheng stepped up presenting a brocade box: "Guard His Majesty's golden bowls well, Young Master Qiao." Bowing thanks, Qiao Wenxian accepted the box.
[ZH] 正想着，传来官家的声音：“仪王对寡人的画有何见解？”
[EN] Listening beside him, Shen Jianchen marveled that Wenyuan squeezed an essay out of twenty characters, while scoffing in silence at that closing flattery: Keep blowing smoke! Northern Song is about to fall on this fool's watch—some "thousand-year legacy" while propping up crooks! Hearing gold bowls handed out for kissing up annoyed him even more: Three pure gold bowls just for praising a poem?! Gold ought to reward soldiers bleeding for the empire!
[ZH] 申简辰依然没有任何思路，但他已不再紧张，面对昏君实在不值得绷紧神经，轻松说道：“父皇，儿臣一直病着，书读得少，更不懂诗词书画，怕说辞玷污了父皇的丹青，还是请其他懂的人鉴赏，才不辜负父皇的一番心血。”
[EN] Right then His Majesty turned to him: "Now, Prince Yi—what do you make of my painting?"
[ZH] 周围臣子听了，均露出不屑的笑容。
[EN] Still drawing a blank yet calm as water—not worth stressing over an incompetent emperor—Shen Jianchen answered light: "Imperial Father, illness kept me from books; I know nothing of poetry or painting and fear clumsy words would stain your masterpiece. Better invite someone learned so your painstaking art gets its due."
[ZH] 赵佶板着脸：“你要当众拒绝寡人？”
[EN] Snickers rippled among the surrounding ministers.
[ZH] “儿臣不敢。”
[EN] Zhao Ji's face darkened. "Refusing my command before the whole court?"
[ZH] “既不敢，就老老实实鉴赏一番，说的好，寡人自然给你赏赐，说的不好以后就不要再进宫。”
[EN] "Your son wouldn't dare."
[ZH] 申简辰对进不进宫并不在意，干脆随便说两句应付应付，回了声：“是。”
[EN] "Then critique it right now! Impress me and rewards await; disappoint me and never set foot in this palace again!"
[ZH] 他走近《腊梅山禽图》，还是看不出所以然，即使随便说说也得知道如何开口。
[EN] Couldn't care less about palace invites—might as well wing a few lines and wrap up! "Yes, Imperial Father."
[ZH] 忽然想起有一次千里寻去看画展，他得知后悄悄跟了去，装作偶遇的样子，获得与美人相伴的机会。在展厅里，他只顾欣赏千里寻，根本没有关注任何画作。当千里寻问他对其中一幅画的见解时，他只说：“你的看法就是我的看法。”
[EN] Stepping closer to "Winter Plum and Mountain Birds," he stared, still blank; even winging it needed an opening hook.
[ZH] 千里寻批评他根本不懂画，还装作高雅的样子跑来欣赏，然后像老师一样对他讲：“想要知道一幅绘画作品说什么，必须抓住艺术形象，艺术形象的每个细微之处都能体现出作画者的心思。还有，欣赏画作千万不能人云亦云，而要把自己看到的和想到的说出来，才有意思。”
[EN] Just then a memory surfaced: once Qianli Xun went to an art gallery, and he trailed behind faking a chance encounter to spend the afternoon with her. Strolling through the rooms, he only watched Qianli Xun and ignored every canvas—until she asked his take on one painting and he quipped, "Whatever you think, I think!" She had scolded him for pretending to care about art, then lectured him like a teacher: "To read a painting, latch onto the visual subjects; every small detail reflects the painter's mind. And never parrot others—say what you see and think yourself; that's what makes it interesting!"
[ZH] 回忆到这里，申简辰猛然有了思路。他抬头瞧一眼有权利或是有机会靠近方桌的众人，除了文渊发自内心替他担忧外，其他人都等着看他的笑话，包括官家在内。
[EN] Light burst across Shen Jianchen's mind! Glancing around the circle of officials pressing near, aside from Wenyuan's genuine worry, every single face—His Majesty included—smirked waiting to watch him make a fool of himself!
[ZH] 他们等着看笑话的表情使得申简辰放弃了随便应付的想法。他搜刮肚肠，启动知识库，又将目光放在画作上，终于有所得，嘴角露出一丝笑容，开口说：“儿臣首先看到兰花，兰花幽居空谷，不染尘俗。父皇画兰花，定是想表达对像兰花一样品性高洁之人的喜爱之情，一个人喜欢什么，他便是什么样的人，像父皇这样品性高洁的帝王定不会重用奸佞之臣，我大宋何愁不会兴旺昌盛！”
[EN] Those smug grins killed any plan to half-ass it! Pulling every ounce of wit from memory and locking his gaze onto the painting, a faint grin touched his mouth as he began: "First, your son sees orchids blooming pure in deep valleys, untainted by worldly dust. Painting orchids expresses Imperial Father's love for noble souls pure as orchids. A man is what he loves; a sovereign pure as orchids will never keep treacherous flatterers close—how could our Great Song fail to prosper?!"
[ZH] 赵佶脱口而出：“说得好！还有呢？”
[EN] "Well said!" Zhao Ji blurted out. "Keep going!"
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“真不愧是昏君，就喜欢别人拍你马屁，既如此好好夸夸你，我申简辰最会拍马屁，要不怎能年纪轻轻当上公司CEO？哼！除个人能力外，拍马屁的本事也少不了。”
[EN] Typical incompetent emperor, eats flattery right up! Shen Jianchen smirked to himself. Fine, I'll lay it on thick! Think I made CEO young on talent alone? Polishing egos is a core skill!
[ZH] “顺着兰花向上看去，是一株腊梅，叶子还未长出，有些花骨朵含苞待放，有些则已开放，娇艳无比。腊梅在腊月开花，而腊月十分寒冷……”正说着猛然想起上学时背过毛主席的一首词，“我不由想到一首词：‘风雨送春归，飞雪迎春到。已是悬崖百丈冰，犹有花枝俏。俏也不争春，只把春来报。待到山花烂漫时，她在丛中笑。’这首词告诉我们，在恶劣的环境中人应该像梅花一样保持威武不能屈的精神和乐观态度。父皇笔下的梅花亦然。”
[ZH] 赵佶忍不住上前抓住仪王的手：“没想到仪王会看出这等内容，真不愧是寡人的儿子，你的词也写得好，‘已是悬崖百丈冰，犹有花枝俏’道出寡人笔下腊梅的真意。还看出什么？继续给寡人道来。”
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“毛爷爷，对不住，借用您的词，昏君还以为是我写的，您老人家在天有灵，千万不要怪罪。”
[ZH] 乔文仙听了仪王的鉴赏之语，小声嘀咕：“仪王真是深藏不露啊！”
[ZH] 赵佶放开仪王的手，继续认真聆听。
[ZH] 申简辰仔细研究画面：“腊梅的枝头站着两只白头翁，它们背对着，看向不同方向，神态安详自若。在儿臣看来，前面这只象征父皇，后面那只则象征江山和百姓，一代帝王应背靠江山和百姓，基业方可稳固，父皇便是背靠大宋江山，心有万千百姓的帝王……”
[ZH] 赵佶脱口而出：“说得好！”
[ZH] 申简辰继续讲：“儿臣不懂作画，更不懂如何构图，却也能感受到父皇的画整体上给人一种舒适之感，不仅安排得错落有致，上下呼应，而且字与画之间关系密切，少了哪一样都会让人觉得不够完美。就连方印亦不可缺，朱红之色高高在上，既让整个画面变得生动，更显示出帝王虽高高在上，心却永远与臣民在一起。”
[ZH] 赵佶最后赞道：“仪王虽不懂作画，却懂得如何鉴赏画作，你的才华可与文渊媲美，寡人赏赐你十个金碗！”
[ZH] 一番吹嘘夸耀竟然成了才华，甚至被认为可比肩文渊。申简辰如何都没想到，更觉得昏君不可救药。他懒得理会昏君，向文渊挤挤眼睛。
[ZH] 乔文仙则向他竖起大拇指。
[EN] "Rising past the orchids stands a winter plum, bare of leaves yet, half buds tight, half petals open bright and proud. Winter plum blooms in the twelfth lunar month, deepest bitter frost..." Suddenly a poem memorized in school popped straight into his head: "It brings to mind a lyric: 'Wind and rain escorted Spring's departure, / Flying snow welcomes Spring's return. / On the ice-clad rock rising high and sheer / A flower blooms sweet and fair. / Sweet and fair, she craves not Spring for herself alone, / To be the harbinger of Spring she is content. / When the mountain flowers are in full bloom / She will smile mingling in their midst.' [TN: Official Foreign Languages Press translation] That lyric reminds us to hold unbroken courage and bright hope amid harsh winter—just like the winter plum under Imperial Father's brush!"
[ZH] 梁师成提着一个更大的锦盒过来：“仪王，十个金碗可要收好了。”
[EN] Overwhelmed, Zhao Ji stepped forward and gripped Prince Yi's hand. "Never dreamed you'd see such depth—truly my own son! And what a lyric you composed! 'On the ice-clad rock rising high and sheer, a flower blooms sweet and fair' captures the very heart of my winter plum! What else do you see? Tell me!"
[ZH] 申简辰接过，拜谢了昏君，退回到乔文仙身边，小声说：“我的金碗可比你的多，难道接下来要一直拎着这玩意儿？”
[EN] Sorry, Grandpa Mao, borrowing your masterpiece! This clueless emperor thinks I wrote it—please don't blame me from above! Beside them, Qiao Wenxian murmured under his breath, "Prince Yi sure hides his talents deep!"
[ZH] “不用，看我的。”乔文仙招了招手，过来一名宫女，吩咐道，“你把我和仪王的金碗给侍卫，让他拿到西华门去，放在我和仪王的马车里。”
[EN] Letting go of his hand, Zhao Ji listened rapt.
[ZH] “是。”宫女接过一大一小的锦盒离开。
[EN] Studying the scroll closer, Shen Jianchen went on, "Perched atop the plum bough sit two white-headed bulbuls facing opposite ways, calm and composed. To your son, the front bird represents Imperial Father, while the rear bird represents the empire and its common people. A sovereign rests his back upon land and subjects so his rule stands rock-solid; Imperial Father is just such a ruler, backed by Song hills and rivers, holding millions of people in his heart..."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“放在马车上会不会丢？”
[EN] "Magnificent!" Zhao Ji exclaimed again!
[ZH] “看你那财迷样！十个金碗就把你收买。放心，马车旁有侍卫守着，没人敢偷。”
[EN] "Knowing nothing of painting or layout, your son still senses complete harmony across the entire scroll—every branch staggered in balance, upper and lower echoing each other, verse and painting intertwined so removing either would mar perfection. Even the square vermilion seals belong: crimson stamps poised high above bring the composition alive, showing a sovereign exalted on high whose heart stays forever bound to his subjects!"
[ZH] 乔文仙刚说完，梁师成又对众人大声说：“为庆祝艮岳落成，宫中组织了蹴鞠表演，现在请大家前往蹴鞠场！”
[EN] Zhao Ji beamed wide. "Unschooled in painting yet gifted with keen eyes—your brilliance rivals Wenyuan's! Reward Prince Yi ten pure gold bowls!"
[ZH] 皇宫的蹴鞠场离宴饮之处不远，除了官家乘坐轿撵，其他人均步行前往。蹴鞠场为官家设了主位，既搭有凉棚，又放着靠背屏风，上画祥云仙鹤。屏风前面放着一把红木做的椅子，外形普普通通。蹴鞠场周围其他方位设有许多座椅，座椅和座椅之间放置着小小木桌，上面摆满茶点。
[EN] Pure hot air hailed as brilliance matching Wenyuan?! Hopeless sovereign! Ignoring Huizong's glow, Shen Jianchen shot Wenyuan a cheeky wink, earning a discreet thumbs-up in return.
[ZH] 申简辰与乔文仙到达后，挑位置坐下。他们看到官家坐好后，梁师成站立一旁伺候，太子、高太尉、蔡京、曾丞相等重要官员坐在离官家较近的地方，大家都满面春风；宫中妃嫔、其他皇子、官眷等也都找地方坐下。
[EN] Liang Shicheng hurried over bearing an even larger brocade box: "Ten gold bowls for Prince Yi—keep them safe!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“高太尉是不是很会蹴鞠？”
[EN] Accepting the box, bowing thanks to the fool on the throne, and backing up beside Qiao Wenxian, Shen Jianchen whispered, "More gold bowls than you—do I have to lug this thing around all day?"
[ZH] 乔文仙回道：“那是当然，蹴鞠水平可是一流。”
[EN] "Watch a pro," Qiao Wenxian grinned, waving a palace maid over. "Pass both my box and Prince Yi's to our guards and have them placed inside our carriage at Xihua Gate."
[ZH] “他现在年纪不小，会不会不如从前？”
[EN] "Yes, Young Master." Taking both boxes, the maid withdrew.
[ZH] “老当益壮，再说也没多老。”
[EN] "Won't somebody swipe them out of the carriage?"
[ZH] 二人正说着，魏夫人过来，坐在仪王身边：“仪王是我见过的最会隐藏个人实力的人。”
[EN] "Look at you turning miser—ten gold bowls bought you off fast! Relax, guards stand watch beside the carriage; nobody dares touch them."
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“此话怎讲？”
[EN] Just then Liang Shicheng called aloud across the hall: "To celebrate Genyue's completion, the palace has arranged a cuju match! Please proceed to the cuju field!"
[ZH] 魏夫人说：“仪王表面上让人觉得他根本不懂诗词歌赋书画之类，还故作谦虚，说自己没读过多少书，什么都不懂，结果品鉴画作被官家不断赞赏，还赏赐他十个金碗，比乔公子都厉害，不是会隐藏实力又是什么？”
[EN] Set just a short walk from the banquet hall, the cuju field drew everyone on foot except His Majesty in his sedan. A shaded pavilion held the sovereign's seat before a folding screen painted with auspicious clouds and cranes, a plain rosewood chair centered in front; rows of guest chairs paired with small tea-and-snack tables ringed the field.
[ZH] 申简辰低声道：“魏夫人有所不知，只要学会拍马屁的本事，不仅脑子会变得灵光，口齿也会变得无比流利。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen and Qiao Wenxian chose seats as His Majesty settled in with Liang Shicheng standing beside him. The Crown Prince, Grand Commandant Gao, Cai Jing, and Chancellor Zeng sat close by beaming, while imperial consorts, princes, and court ladies filled the surrounding tiers.
[ZH] “我不信你只会拍马屁。”
[EN] "Is Grand Commandant Gao actually good at cuju?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] “魏夫人不信，我也没办法，事实如此，当然还得益于一位朋友的指点。”
[EN] "Top-tier—master level!" Qiao Wenxian replied.
[ZH] “指点你如何拍马屁？”
[EN] "Getting on in years though. Hasn't he lost a step?"
[ZH] “非也，指点我如何欣赏画作。”
[EN] "Sharp as ever—not that old yet!"
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“你一直病着，才刚好，除了我，哪里有其他朋友？难道是得到我的指点？可我并不记得有此事。”
[EN] Mid-chat, Lady Wei walked over and took the seat beside Prince Yi. "Prince Yi is the craftiest dark horse I've ever met!"
[ZH] “错！”魏夫人说，“谁说仪王只有你一位朋友？还有我这位新朋友，可我也没指点过你，乔公子可以作证。”
[EN] "How so?" Qiao Wenxian asked.
[ZH] 申简辰失落地说：“这位朋友是我在另外一个世界认识的，现在根本找不到她。”
[EN] "Acting clueless about poetry and painting, claiming he'd barely read a book, then winning praise after praise from His Majesty and walking off with ten gold bowls—outdoing even Young Master Qiao! If that isn't hiding your strength, what is?"
[ZH] “另外一个世界？”魏夫人与乔文仙互相对视，两人根本不相信，认为是仪王瞎说，都不想再继续这个话题。
[EN] Lowering his voice with a smirk, Shen Jianchen said, "Truth be told, Lady Wei, master the art of flattery, and both your brain and your tongue run like greased lightning!"
[ZH] 鼓声想起，蹴鞠表演开始，大家认真观赏起来。
[EN] "Don't buy for one second you only know flattery!"
[ZH] 球场中央立着一个球门，被称为风 流眼。两队表演者开始上场，一队穿着蓝色球服，一队穿着红色球服，每队有五位球员，各位于球门两边，互相行礼后，当鼓声再次响起时，两队开始蹴鞠，场面异常激烈。
[EN] "Believe what you want, facts are facts—plus I had pointers from a friend."
[ZH] 申简辰第一次欣赏蹴鞠，看了一会儿，觉得很像他会踢的足球。不同的是，蹴鞠的球不能落地，由其他队员不断传递，最终传给球头，再由球头一脚踢进球门，就算成功。
[EN] "Pointers on flattery?"
[ZH] 申简辰心里乐道：“我可是踢球高手，足球不落地也曾练过，加上申拳的功力，玩儿这种球不在话下。”
[EN] "Nope. Pointers on how to look at paintings."
[ZH] 申简辰向高俅望去，见他正沉浸在比赛中，想起《水浒》中对高俅的描写，英雄主义情结再次生起：“不遇到你也罢了，既然遇到，我必须替一百零八个好汉教训教训你!”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian frowned. "Sick in bed till days ago—aside from me, what other friend do you even have? Don't recall teaching you art critique!"
[ZH] 他看到一队人员正要把球传给球头，起身用轻功跃过去，球头还没反应过来，球已被仪王一脚踢进风 流眼。
[EN] "Wrong!" Lady Wei chimed in. "Who says you're Prince Yi's only friend? There's his new friend right here—though I didn't coach him either, Young Master Qiao can vouch!"
[ZH] 在场的人被仪王的举动惊住，很快反应过来。
[EN] A shadow crossed Shen Jianchen's eyes. "A friend from another world—someone I can't find now."
[ZH] 乔文仙赞道：“仪王果然本事见长！”
[EN] "Another world?" Trading amused glances, Lady Wei and Qiao Wenxian wrote it off as princely nonsense and let the subject drop.
[ZH] 魏夫人说：“我听大家议论仪王死后跟着神人学了功夫，看来是真的。”
[EN] Drums boomed—the cuju exhibition began, drawing every eye onto the pitch. Dead center stood the tall netted goal ring called the Fengliu Yan. Two teams of five marched out, one squad in blue uniforms, one in red, taking positions on either side of the goal. Bowing to each other, at the next drumbeat they plunged into fierce action.
[ZH] 传来官家的声音：“仪王！你这是做什么？”
[EN] Watching cuju live for the first time, Shen Jianchen saw instant kinship with soccer—except the ball could never touch the ground, juggled teammate to teammate until passed to the Lead Striker to volley clean through the elevated ring! Grinning inside, he thought: I'm a soccer beast—keepie-uppies were child's play, and add Shen Fist power on top? Easy!
[ZH] 发生意外，两队球员都停下来。
[EN] Glancing toward Gao Qiu watching rapt, his Water Margin hero complex flared straight up: Might have let you slide if we'd never met—now that we have, time to teach you a lesson for the hundred and eight heroes!
[ZH] 申简辰行礼道：“父皇，儿臣也想蹴鞠。”
[EN] Spotting a player poised to loft a pass to his Lead Striker, Shen Jianchen kicked off with light-foot skill, vaulted straight onto the field, intercepted midair before the striker blinked, and blasted the ball dead-center through the goal ring!
[ZH] 官家笑道：“呵呵，既然想蹴鞠应早些说，让你蹴就是，何必来这么一出？”
[EN] Gasps froze the crowd before cheers rippled out. "Prince Yi's skills keep getting wilder!" Qiao Wenxian applauded.
[ZH] 申简辰继续说：“儿臣听说高太尉蹴鞠技术高超，想与他比试比试，还请父皇允许。”
[EN] "Heard court whispers a divine sage taught Prince Yi martial arts after death—looks true!" Lady Wei marveled.
[ZH] 官家看向高俅：“高太尉，仪王刚刚起死回生，不妨满足他，与他比试比试？”
[EN] His Majesty's voice boomed down: "Prince Yi! What is the meaning of this?!"
[ZH] 官家都已发话，高俅怎能不从？他站起来行礼：“臣遵旨。”
[EN] Every player froze at the interruption. Shen Jianchen bowed: "Imperial Father, your son itches to play cuju too!"
[ZH] 申简辰换上红色球服，高俅则是蓝色，两人分别顶了两队的球头，一声鼓响，两队开始比赛。
[EN] Zhao Ji laughed. "Haha, wanted to play? Should've said so—plenty of room! Why pull a stunt like that?"
[ZH] 红队使出流星赶月，申简辰手扶在一位球员的身上直接踢进一球，赢了一分；接着蓝队使出落花流水，高太尉也踢进一球。
[EN] "Your son heard Grand Commandant Gao's cuju skills are unmatched and longs to test himself against him—begging Imperial Father's permission!"
[ZH] 一连比了七八场，两队均处于平手。
[EN] Zhao Ji looked to Gao Qiu. "Grand Commandant Gao, Prince Yi just came back from death—indulge him with a friendly match!"
[ZH] 申简辰心想，高俅年龄虽比他大，球技确实了得，想打败他实在不易，就目前情形而言，只能以速度和力度取胜，于是提出中场休息，官家无不同意。
[EN] With His Majesty speaking, Gao Qiu could not refuse; rising with a bow, he replied, "Your minister obeys."
[ZH] 申简辰利用休息时间告诉队友：“无论怎么传球，我都能接住，你们无需过多颠球，要抓紧时间快速将球传给我。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen slipped on a red jersey, Gao Qiu donned blue, each taking Lead Striker for his squad. Drums sounded—game on! Red launched a "Meteor Chasing the Moon" combo; resting one hand on a teammate's shoulder, Shen Jianchen vaulted and drilled home the opening goal! Blue countered with "Fallen Flowers and Flowing Water," Gao Qiu answering right back with a crisp score. Seven or eight rounds later, both teams stood dead even.
[ZH] 球员们从未遇到过这样的球头，惊讶之余，只能同意，谁让人家是仪王，再说也省了他们不少事。
[EN] Old or not, Gao Qiu's footwork was wicked; beating him on pure finesse looked tough. Speed and raw force would crack him! Shen Jianchen called a brief halftime breather, His Majesty nodding assent.
[ZH] 鼓声又响起，新一轮比赛开始，两队开始激烈进球。只见红队的球员不论如何将脚上的球传来，申简辰都能迅速准确地接住，丝毫不差踢进风 流眼，一下子先于对方两球。
[EN] Huddling his squad during the break, Shen Jianchen issued crisp orders: "No matter how wild the pass, I'll catch it. Quit wasting time juggling; ping the ball straight to me fast as you can!" Never having played under a striker like this, the teammates gaped yet complied—Prince Yi called the shots, plus it saved them tons of work!
[ZH] 高俅急了，使出流星赶月，将队员传来的球踢向球门，刚到风 流眼跟前，却被申简辰踢来的球撞了回去，落在地上。
[EN] Drums rolled again and the second half exploded! Every rapid pass from red jerseys met Shen Jianchen's foot on the volley, rockets drilling clean through the goal ring until red jumped two goals ahead! Flustered, Gao Qiu fired a "Meteor Chasing the Moon" strike straight at the ring—only for Shen Jianchen's counter-kick to slam the ball dead in midair right outside the net, knocking it down to the turf! Three straight shots blocked cold!
[ZH] 一连几个球都是如此，高太尉无奈，拱手认输：“仪王踢球又快又狠，臣老了，力不从心，认输认输。”
[EN] Out of breath, Gao Qiu cupped his hands in defeat. "Prince Yi strikes too fast and fierce! This old minister's strength fails him—I concede, I concede!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“高太尉可听说过梁山一百零八个好汉？”
[EN] Stepping close, Shen Jianchen asked cool, "Grand Commandant Gao, ever heard of the hundred and eight heroes of Mount Liang?"
[ZH] 高俅疑惑：“仪王说的可是水泊梁山，那里长年贼寇流窜，没听说有什么好汉。”
[EN] Blinking baffled, Gao Qiu replied, "Your Highness means the Marshes of Mount Liang? Bandits swarm there year round—never heard of any 'heroes.'"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“可能我们说的不是一回事，反正好汉们嘱咐我要替他们会会高太尉。”
[EN] "Guess we mean different folks. Either way, those heroes asked me to test your mettle for them."
[ZH] 高俅不明所以，起死回生的仪王总是说些奇怪的话：“仪王，老臣告退。”
[EN] Totally perplexed—this revived prince spoke in riddles nonstop!—Gao Qiu bowed: "Your minister takes his leave, Prince Yi."
[ZH] 申简辰面向官家：“父皇对儿臣的球技可满意？”
[EN] Turning toward the throne pavilion, Shen Jianchen bowed. "Did your son's footwork please Imperial Father?"
[ZH] 赵佶笑道：“没想到你蹴鞠的本事不小，寡人算是看出来，神人教你功夫不假，既然你功夫了得，寡人封你为临安节度使，在汴梁待几日，就去临安任职吧。”
[EN] Beaming wide, Zhao Ji laughed. "Never knew your cuju ran this sharp! Proof that divine sage truly trained you! Since your martial prowess shines so bright, I hereby appoint you Military Commissioner of Lin'an! Stay a few days in Bianliang, then head south to take office!"
[ZH] 申简辰愕然，一场蹴鞠赢得一个官职，难怪高俅因蹴鞠节节高升。官家已开金口，只能接着：“谢父皇隆恩。”
[EN] Floor-dropped—one game of cuju just landed him a regional military post! No wonder Gao Qiu rose through the ranks kicking balls! With His Majesty's decree spoken, he bowed low: "Your son thanks Imperial Father for this boundless grace!"
[ZH] 乔文仙和魏夫人都替仪王感到高兴，这场宫廷大宴他总算没有白来一趟。
[EN] Both Qiao Wenxian and Lady Wei beamed with joy for him; what a jackpot trip to the palace!

#CH 37
[ZH] 皇宫的宴会终于结束，申简辰与乔文仙乘坐马车回仪王府时，天色已渐渐暗下。
[EN] By the time the palace banquet wrapped up and Shen Jianchen and Qiao Wenxian rolled back to Prince Yi's Manor, twilight had settled over the city.
[ZH] 一天的皇宫经历，申简辰无心回味，只玩味着临安节度使的职位。
[EN] Skipping over every pompous palace detail, Shen Jianchen rolled one gleaming prize around in his head: Military Commissioner of Lin'an!
[ZH] 临安，他本就要去，现在更有正当理由去，岂不是天遂人愿？足以说明他与临安有缘，难道千里寻真的在那里？想到这些申简辰对临安更加充满向往。
[EN] He was already heading to Lin'an—now he held an official imperial mandate to live there! Talk about fate opening every door! Did that mean Qianli Xun truly waited in Lin'an? Anticipation tugged him south harder than ever.
[ZH] 当他与文渊踏进王府，乔梦容第一个迎上来：“你们终于回来，我在家里替哥哥担心一整天。”
[EN] Stepping through the manor gate alongside Wenyuan, Qiao Mengrong hurried straight up to meet them: "Back at last! I worried sick at home all day, Brother!"
[ZH] 乔文仙笑道：“佳和郡主多虑，仪王不仅一点儿事没有，还被官家亲口封为临安节度使。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian grinned: "Worried for nothing, Princess Jiahe! Not only did Prince Yi walk out without a scratch, His Majesty named him Military Commissioner of Lin'an on the spot!"
[ZH] 乔梦容喜出望外：“哥哥被陛下赏识，往后仪王府终于不会再被人轻视。”
[EN] Pure joy lit Qiao Mengrong's face: "His Majesty finally recognizes Brother's worth! Nobody will ever look down on Prince Yi's Manor again!"
[ZH] 乔梦容既兴奋又幸福的表情令申简辰产生莫名的内疚：“我与文渊口渴，大家不要站在这里，进屋喝口茶再说。”
[EN] Her radiant happiness stirred another pang of guilt in Shen Jianchen. "Wenyuan and I are parched—let's not stand in the yard, head inside for tea."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“都怪我太担心哥哥，才把你们堵在院子里。快进屋，我让丫鬟泡壶散茶。喜兰，快去安排。”
[EN] "Silly me blocking you in the courtyard out of worry! Come inside—I'll have maids steep loose-leaf tea! Xilan, hurry!"
[ZH] “是，郡主。”喜兰笑着离开。
[EN] "Yes, Princess!" Xilan dashed off grinning.
[ZH] 三人进入屋内，坐了没多时，喜兰端着茶壶和两个茶盏进来，给仪王与乔公子斟好茶，站在郡主身边，听他们聊天儿。
[EN] Moments after the three settled inside, Xilan returned with a teapot and two tea bowls, poured for Prince Yi and Young Master Qiao, and stood beside her mistress listening.
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“哥哥，刚泡的散茶，有些烫，你等会儿再喝。”
[EN] "Freshly steeped loose-leaf tea, Brother—still scalding, let it cool a bit," Qiao Mengrong cautioned.
[ZH] 申简辰点点头。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded.
[ZH] 乔文仙看着乔梦容充满爱意的眼神调侃道：“我看我还是走吧，继续待下去，怕自己显得多余。”
[EN] Catching Qiao Mengrong's melting gaze locked onto her brother, Qiao Wenxian teased: "Think I'd better bow out before I feel like a third wheel!"
[ZH] 乔梦容因为太高兴，一直盯着哥哥，自觉失态。她把目光从仪王身上移开，害羞道：“乔公子向来喜欢开玩笑，你怎么会显得多余？在宫中乔公子肯定帮过哥哥，梦容在此替哥哥谢过。”乔梦容说着起身行谢礼。
[EN] Flushing hot as she realized she had been staring nonstop, Qiao Mengrong lowered her eyes shyly: "Always teasing, Young Master Qiao—how could you ever be in the way? You surely looked out for Brother at the palace today; allow Mengrong to thank you on his behalf!" Rising, she offered a formal curtsy.
[ZH] 乔文轩赶快站起来回礼：“佳和郡主折煞乔某，仪王哪里需要我帮忙？他文武双全，靠自己的本事赢得官家赞赏，与我毫无关系。”
[EN] Jumping up to return the bow, Qiao Wenxian protested: "You overwhelm me, Princess Jiahe! Prince Yi needed zero help from me—skilled in letters and arms alike, he won His Majesty's favor entirely on his own!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“你们两个能不能别这样，太见外。”
[EN] "Quit being so stiff, you two—acts like strangers," Shen Jianchen cut in.
[ZH] “哥哥说的对，我听你的。”
[EN] "Right as always, Brother—I'll listen to you."
[ZH] 乔梦容不再客气，继续坐下，思忖乔文仙刚才说的话。他说哥哥文武双全，武，她已见识过，至于文，哥哥曾说，写诗作词太费心力，他身体虚弱，不能在诗词方面费心思，故而从未涉猎。
[EN] Sitting back down, Qiao Mengrong turned over Wenyuan's phrase: skilled in letters and arms alike! She had witnessed his martial skill—yet in letters, Zhao Pu used to say poetry drained his energy too much for his frail frame and never wrote a line.
[ZH] 不由问道：“乔公子，能否给我说说哥哥今日的表现，也让我高兴高兴。”
[EN] Curious, she asked: "Tell me what Brother did today, Young Master Qiao—let me celebrate too!"
[ZH] 申简辰阻止道：“没什么好说的，都是些奉承巴结之语。”
[EN] "Nothing worth telling—just a pile of bootlicking flattery," Shen Jianchen tried shutting it down.
[ZH] 乔文仙也已坐下，反对道：“仪王此言差矣，奉承巴结之语你确实说过不少，可也有真才实学，就拿你做的词来讲，真是不简单，我给佳和郡主念念……”
[EN] Seated again, Qiao Wenxian countered: "Don't sell yourself short, Prince Yi! Plenty of flattery, sure—yet real talent too! Take that ci lyric you recited—utterly extraordinary! Let me recite it for Princess Jiahe..."
[ZH] 词并非申简辰所作，他必须阻止：“作词的人不是我，另有其人。”
[EN] Not his poem—Shen Jianchen moved to stop him: "I didn't write that lyric—someone else did!"
[ZH] 乔文轩质问道：“不是你还能是谁？我可是第一次从你口中听到这样的词句？”
[EN] "Who else could've written it?" Qiao Wenxian demanded. "First time in my life I ever heard those lines!"
[ZH] 申简辰只能说：“我死后在另一个世界学的，是一位伟人所作。”
[EN] Cornered, Shen Jianchen shrugged: "Learned it in the other world after I died—written by a great man."
[ZH] 乔文仙根本不认可仪王所说：“就知道你会这么说，另一个世界我没见过，既然第一次从你口中听说，只会认为是你所作。”
[EN] Disbelieving as ever, Qiao Wenxian smirked: "Knew you'd say that! Never seen this 'other world'—first heard it from your lips, so as far as I'm concerned you wrote it!"
[ZH] 乔梦容已按耐不住：“乔公子，快念念，我一定要认真记下。”
[EN] Bursting with curiosity, Qiao Mengrong pleaded: "Recite it, Young Master Qiao—I want to memorize every word!"
[ZH] 申简辰拦不住文渊，只能由着他。毛爷爷的词借助他来宋代走一遭，想着也挺好玩儿。
[EN] Unable to muzzle Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen gave up—letting Grandpa Mao's masterpiece visit the Song dynasty sounded kinda fun anyway!
[ZH] 估摸着茶水温度已合适，乔文仙端起来喝了几口，放下茶盏，起身，酝酿好情绪，念道：“风雨送春归，飞雪迎春到。已是悬崖百丈冰，犹有花枝俏。俏也不争春，只把春来报。待到山花烂漫时，她在丛中笑。”
[EN] Checking the tea temperature, Qiao Wenxian took a few sips, set down his bowl, rose, gathered feeling, and recited aloud: "'Wind and rain escorted Spring's departure, / Flying snow welcomes Spring's return. / On the ice-clad rock rising high and sheer / A flower blooms sweet and fair. / Sweet and fair, she craves not Spring for herself alone, / To be the harbinger of Spring she is content. / When the mountain flowers are in full bloom / She will smile mingling in their midst.' [TN: Official Foreign Languages Press translation]"
[ZH] 乔梦容认真品味句子，不由陷入回忆……
[EN] Savoring every word, Qiao Mengrong drifted straight back into memory...
[ZH] 有一年冬季，天气寒冷，哥哥病情加重，厚厚的被子和一个火盆根本不管用。乔梦容吩咐侍卫再给屋子里搬进一个火盆，她亲自抱来一床被子给哥哥盖上，他才感觉好些，渐渐睡去。
[EN] One brutal winter, her brother's illness worsened; thick quilts and one brazier couldn't warm him. Ordering guards to haul in a second brazier, she fetched another quilt herself until he drifted off to sleep.
[ZH] 外面正下着鹅毛大雪，乔梦容披上红色斗篷，带着喜兰走出屋外，踩着刚积了一层的冬雪来到园子里，一株梅花正迎雪开放。
[EN] Outside, heavy snow drifted down like goose feathers. Throwing on a red cloak, she stepped into the garden with Xilan through ankle-deep snow—where one winter plum tree bloomed brave against the frost.
[ZH] 冬天哥哥根本不出来，他很少看到冬梅开放的景象，乔梦容吩咐喜兰折几枝梅花。
[EN] Unable to step outdoors in winter, her brother rarely beheld winter plum blossoms; Qiao Mengrong had Xilan snap several branches.
[ZH] 回到屋里，给花瓶中放些水，她把花枝亲手插入后坐在床边欣赏熟睡中的哥哥。
[EN] Back inside, she filled a vase with water, arranged the plum twigs by hand, and sat beside his bed watching him sleep.
[ZH] 哥哥睁开眼时，闻到一股清幽之香：“怎么会有股梅花的香气？”
[EN] Waking to a crisp, delicate fragrance, Zhao Pu murmured: "Where is plum blossom scent coming from?"
[ZH] “是我让喜兰折了几枝梅花插在瓶中。”
[EN] "I had Xilan break a few winter plum branches for a vase."
[ZH] “快扶我起来瞧瞧。”
[EN] "Help me sit up to see!"
[ZH] 乔梦容将哥哥扶起，再让喜兰搬个凳子放在床边，把花瓶放在凳子上，哥哥便可以尽情欣赏。
[EN] Propping him up while Xilan set a stool by the bedside, she placed the vase right before his eyes.
[ZH] 神奇的是，当梅花见到哥哥时，竟然有一朵即刻开放。乔梦容认为定是喜兆，预示着哥哥一定会好起来。
[EN] Like magic, the instant the branches met his gaze, one tight bud unfurled open before them! Joyful, Qiao Mengrong took it for a heavenly omen predicting his recovery.
[ZH] 哥哥却说：“梦容，在我眼中，你就是那朵开放的梅花，是我心中的梅花，让我拥有活下去的勇气，要不是因为你，哥哥早已坚持不下去……”
[EN] Yet Zhao Pu whispered: "In my eyes, Mengrong, you are that blooming plum—the plum flower in my heart giving me courage to hold on. Without you, Brother would have given up long ago..."
[ZH] 想到这里，乔梦容眼中含着泪，透过泪光，瞧着眼前已脱离病体的哥哥，他写这首词时会不会是因为想起了过往？
[EN] Tears misted Qiao Mengrong's eyes; gazing through shimmering blur at the healthy man seated across from her, she wondered: had memories stirred when he recited that poem?
[ZH] 申简辰注意到乔梦容眼睛发红，问道：“梦容，你怎么了？”
[EN] Spotting her reddened eyes, Shen Jianchen asked: "What's wrong, Mengrong?"
[ZH] 乔文仙也向佳和郡主瞧去，意识到他不能再待下去：“二位，乔某还有事，失陪。”说完迅速离开。
[EN] Glancing at the princess, Qiao Wenxian read the room fast: "Business calls—excuse me, you two!" Stepping briskly out, he left them alone.
[ZH] 乔梦容才说道：“哥哥，你写的词我很喜欢，不管你写的时候心里想些什么，在我看来这首词是哥哥写给我的。”
[EN] Only then did Qiao Mengrong speak: "I adore your lyric, Brother. Whatever was in your heart when you spoke it, to me you wrote it for me."
[ZH] 申简辰一阵无语，既不能过多解释，又不忍伤害乔梦容，他保持沉默，端起茶盏，用喝光茶水的动作掩饰无语的表情。
[EN] Struck speechless—unable to shatter her dream yet refusing to lie—Shen Jianchen kept silent, lifting his tea bowl to drain the rest and hide his awkwardness.
[ZH] 趁喝茶时，迅速调整好情绪，放下茶盏，笑道：“梦容，今日参加宴会我有些累，想回屋早些睡，你不困吗？”
[EN] Collecting himself behind the cup, he set it down with an easy smile: "Exhausted from the palace banquet, Mengrong—heading to bed early. Aren't you sleepy?"
[ZH] 乔梦容很失望，要搁以前，哥哥绝对会做出肯定的回应，而现在的哥哥只想回避她的感情。
[EN] A sharp sting of letdown pierced her—the old Zhao Pu would have answered with tender devotion, whereas this brother dodged her heart every single time.
[ZH] “我送哥哥回屋休息。”
[EN] "Let me walk you back to your room."
[ZH] 申简辰站起来：“不用，我自己可以。”
[EN] Rising, Shen Jianchen waved it off: "No need—I'll head back myself."
[ZH] 乔梦容也站起来，目送哥哥往出走的背影，更加失落，病着时希望他好起来，可好起来后，哥哥却不再依赖她。
[EN] Standing to watch his retreating back, ache deepened in her chest: sick, she had prayed for his health; healthy, he no longer leaned on her.
[ZH] “哥哥！”
[EN] "Brother!"
[ZH] 申简辰站住，转过身：“还有事？”
[EN] Halting, he turned back: "Something else?"
[ZH] “什么时候去临安任职？”
[EN] "When do you leave for Lin'an to take office?"
[ZH] “明日还要到宫中领取任职的文书和大印，需耽搁一日，计划后天动身。”
[EN] "Have to pick up my imperial commission and seal at the palace tomorrow—one day delay, then we set out the day after."
[ZH] “哥哥可愿意带上我？”
[EN] "Will you take me along, Brother?"
[ZH] “你想一起去临安，哥哥没有拒绝的理由，跟着便是。”
[EN] "If you want to see Lin'an, how could Brother say no? Come right along."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出笑容：“多谢哥哥！”
[EN] Brightness bloomed back on her face: "Thank you, Brother!"
[ZH] “我们是一家人，不用对我客气。”申简辰说完回屋去了。
[EN] "We're family—no need to thank me." With that, he headed off to his quarters.
[ZH] “一家人”从哥哥的口中说出，乔梦容感到无比温馨，说明在哥哥心里她依然很重要。
[EN] "We're family"—hearing those words from his lips warmed Qiao Mengrong straight through; clearly she still held a vital place in his heart!
[ZH] 申简辰跟乔文仙已商量好，到宫中办完事，收拾好行囊启程南下。
[EN] Plans set with Qiao Wenxian, they would handle palace paperwork first, pack up, and head south.
[ZH] 天亮后，他来到宫中，梁师成将盖有官家大印的文书和临安节度使的大印给了。
[EN] Next morning at court, Liang Shicheng handed over the imperial commission stamped with the sovereign's seal alongside the official seal of Military Commissioner of Lin'an.
[ZH] “临安是个好地方，仪王到那里想要了解什么，尽管找临安知府朱晞颜。去年朝廷虽派人打败方腊，招安了淮南盗，可往南去的路上流寇不断，仪王定要一路谨慎。”
[EN] "Lin'an is a fine post—need local guidance, turn straight to Prefect Zhu Xiyan," Liang Shicheng advised. "Though imperial armies crushed Fang La last year and granted amnesty to the Huainan bandits, straggler outlaws still roam southern roads—travel with care, Prince Yi."
[ZH] “不知淮南盗被招安后现状如何？”
[EN] "How did the Huainan bandits fare after amnesty?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] “淮南盗被招安后朝廷妥善安置，可这些盗贼贼心不死，又聚集作乱，原来投降并非出于本意，一周前已被大将折可存镇压，首领宋江也被杀死，其他要犯都一一被斩杀。”
[EN] "The court settled them properly, yet rebel hearts never die—they rose up again! Clearly their surrender was fake from the start. A week ago General Zhe Kecun crushed them, killing their leader Song Jiang and executing every ringleader."
[ZH] “死了！”申简辰本想有机会见见他曾在书中看到的人物，竟如此无缘。
[EN] "Dead?!" Hoping to meet one legendary character from the novels he knew, Shen Jianchen felt a dull thud of loss.
[ZH] 梁师成无法理解仪王的失落：“当然死了，跟朝廷作对没有什么好结果。”
[EN] Baffled by the prince's gloom, Liang Shicheng scoffed: "Of course dead! Defying the throne brings only one end."
[ZH] 离开皇宫，申简辰坐在马车上，沉浸在无法见到宋江的失落中。
[EN] Leaving the palace inside his carriage, Shen Jianchen brooded over missing Song Jiang.
[ZH] 马车经过闹市，汴梁街市的喧闹声将他从失落中唤醒。他向车窗外瞧去，街市繁华热闹，人影穿梭，有贵家妇女，有行脚僧人，还有说话艺人，有抬着轿子从身边经过的，有骆驼拖着人或物品慢悠悠走过大街的……
[EN] Soon the lively roar of Bianliang's market avenues pulled him from his gloom. Peering out the window, he took in the teeming streetscape: noble ladies, wandering monks, storytellers, sedan chairs threading through pedestrians, camels lumbering down the boulevard loaded with goods and riders...
[ZH] 申简辰看着看着，眼前出现了一家店铺，写着“赵太丞家”，原来是家医铺。不由想起白风，这家伙还没主动去仪王府找他，不如进赵太丞家打听打听。
[EN] Then a familiar shop sign caught his eye: "Grand Physician Zhao's Clinic"—an apothecary! Wait—that reminded him of Bai Feng! The kid still hadn't shown up at Prince Yi's Manor; why not check inside?
[ZH] 刚有此想法，听到一阵哭闹声从医铺传出：“呜呜……！我娘被你们治死了！还我娘！还我五两银子！呜呜……！”
[EN] Just as the thought struck, loud wails burst right out of the clinic door: "Sob...! You killed my mom! Give back my mom! Give back my five taels of silver! Sob...!"
[ZH] 申简辰让车夫把马车停下，他走下马车，进入赵太丞家。
[EN] Halting the carriage, Shen Jianchen hopped down and stepped inside Grand Physician Zhao's Clinic.
[ZH] 白风正坐在地上又哭又闹，身边站着医铺的伙计和一些前来诊治的病人。
[EN] Sprawled on the floor howling sat Bai Feng, ringed by clinic clerks and waiting patients.
[ZH] 申简辰上前问：“白风，你不去找我，怎么在这里胡闹？”
[EN] Stepping right up, Shen Jianchen asked: "Bai Feng! Why are you making a scene here instead of reporting to me?"
[ZH] 白风像见到救星，立刻爬起来，拽住他：“呜呜！哥哥，你是仪王府的侍卫总管，帮帮我，赵太丞家的大夫把我娘治死了，还不给我还钱，呜呜……！”
[EN] Spotting his savior, Bai Feng scrambled up and latched onto his sleeve: "Sob! Big Brother—you're Captain of the Guard at Prince Yi's Manor, help me! This doctor killed my mom and won't give back my silver! Sob...!"
[ZH] 听到是仪王府的人，大夫从里面出来，拱手道：“不知阁下是仪王府的什么人？”
[EN] Hearing "Prince Yi's Manor," the physician stepped out from the back bowing low: "May I ask your honored position in Prince Yi's Manor, sir?"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“如果我说我就是仪王，你可相信？”
[EN] "What if I told you I AM Prince Yi?" Shen Jianchen replied calm.
[ZH] 大夫立刻拜道：“仪王殿下，这孩子把他娘带来时人已病入膏肓，根本治不好……”
[EN] The doctor dropped into a deep bow at once: "Prince Yi! When this lad brought his mother in, she was already beyond saving..."
[ZH] 白风哭着强辩：“既然治不好，为何还要我五两银子？”
[EN] Bai Feng cried back: "If you couldn't save her, why take my five taels?!"
[ZH] 大夫继续解释：“我看你一片孝心，想着也许能救回人，就帮你娘治治，治她这病的药材不便宜，五两银子都是少的，诊金我还没问你要。既诊了脉，也开了药，回到家里，药还没吃到嘴里，人就没了，怎能怪我？”
[EN] "Moved by your filial devotion, I still tried my utmost!" the doctor defended himself. "Her condition needed costly herbs—five taels barely covered ingredients, I didn't even charge a consultation fee! Checked her pulse, weighed the herbs—she passed away at home before taking a single dose! How is that my fault?!"
[ZH] “我娘死了虽不能怪你，但你开的药我娘都没吃上，凭什么不把银子还给我？”白风不依不饶。
[EN] "Her dying isn't your fault—yet she never swallowed a drop of your medicine! Why keep all the silver?!" Bai Feng refused to budge.
[ZH] 申简辰算是听明白，白风是想要回五两银子：“大夫，这孩子抓的药还能不能再退回去？”
[EN] Catching the gist—Bai Feng just wanted his five taels back—Shen Jianchen asked: "Doctor, can you take back the herbs he filled?"
[ZH] 大夫为难道：“都是按照药方抓的，不同药材按量混在一起，退是不能再退。不过看在仪王的面子上，药退不退无所谓，我退给这孩子二两银子，让他回去好安葬他娘，您看如何？”
[EN] The doctor sighed awkwardly: "Mixed together by prescription weight—impossible to separate and resell. Still, out of respect for Prince Yi, herbs or no herbs, I'll refund the boy two taels to pay for his mother's burial—fair enough?"
[ZH] 白风依然不满意，还想强辩，被申简辰拦住：“如此甚好。”
[EN] Still bristling to argue more, Bai Feng got cut off by Shen Jianchen: "More than fair."
[ZH] 医铺把二两银子退给白风后，申简辰一把拽起白风，出了赵太丞家，上了马车。
[EN] The moment the clinic handed over two taels of silver, Shen Jianchen hauled Bai Feng by the arm out the door and straight into his carriage.
[ZH] 白风揣好二两银子问：“你果真是仪王？”
[EN] Stashing the two taels safe, Bai Feng squinted up at him: "You're REALLY Prince Yi?"
[ZH] “当然。”
[EN] "Of course."
[ZH] 白风凑近，瞧了又瞧：“原来王爷长这样，跟我没什么区别，就是穿戴好些。”
[EN] Leaning close for a long look, Bai Feng muttered: "Huh—princes look just like regular folks, just fancier threads!"
[ZH] “你家住哪里？我送你回去。”
[EN] "Where do you live? I'll drop you home."
[ZH] “娘都没了，哪里还有家？不回也罢。”
[EN] "Mom's gone—what home? No point going back."
[ZH] “难道你不打算回去安葬好你娘？”
[EN] "Don't you need to go bury your mother?"
[ZH] “昨日已安葬。”
[EN] "Buried her yesterday."
[ZH] “家里可有其他人？”
[EN] "Any other family left?"
[ZH] “我爹在我出生时就死了，剩我和娘，她现在也走了，我已无家可归。本想今日要到五两银子去仪王府找你，没想到又碰上，看来我与王爷十分有缘。”
[EN] "Dad died when I was born, just Mom and me—now she's gone too, I've got nowhere left. Planned to get my five taels back today and head straight to Prince Yi's Manor—never figured I'd bump right into you! Guess fate really links me to Your Highness!"
[ZH] “既如此，现在就去仪王府，以后跟着我。”
[EN] "Then straight to Prince Yi's Manor right now—from today on, you follow me."
[ZH] “好的，王爷。”白风立刻转悲为喜，他又有了新家。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness!" Grief flipping to pure grin, Bai Feng had a home again.

#CH 38
[ZH] 千里寻已在竹林度过七八日，每日不是欣赏竹林美景，就是阅读苦蕨找来的话本。
[ZH] 她越读越兴奋，终于在这个时代找到用武之地，她可以把曾经利用网络写过的故事改编成话本，供百姓娱乐。
[ZH] 说写就写，千里寻提笔在纸上书写起来……
[ZH] 一直写了几十张纸，终于撂下笔。
[ZH] 夏果帮小姐整理纸张，边整理，边瞅着纸张上的字看。
[ZH] “小姐真厉害，能写这么多字。”
[ZH] “你可认得？”
[ZH] “只认得几个。”
[ZH] 千里寻甚觉可惜，夏果长得如此貌美，如若生在富贵人家，定是琴棋书画样样精通。
[ZH] “以后我让苦荞教你识字。”
[ZH] 夏果开心道：“谢谢小姐！”
[ZH] “苦荞呢？”
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐回旧屋给小姐做午饭。”
[ZH] 千里寻透过门望向外面的天空，慨叹道：“时间过得真快，都晌午了。”
[ZH] 苦荞做好午饭，用竹篮提回竹屋，伺候小姐吃过饭后，又开始收拾，这是她每日的生活主题。
[ZH] 千里寻吩咐她把写好的话本交给苦蕨，麻烦苦蕨拿到集市，随便找位说话艺人给了。
[ZH] 苦荞收拾好后，提上竹篮，拿着写好的话本往旧屋而去。
[ZH] 等苦荞再回来时，身边多一位妇人，两个丫鬟和两个小厮。
[ZH] 苦荞先进竹屋禀报：“小姐，您猜我把谁带来？”
[ZH] 千里寻继续翻着已看完的话本：“谁？”
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷，其他地方的租子已收完，今日来到竹林，听说小姐在，赶来拜见。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷掌握着朱淑真的经济大权，又是她的奶娘，千里寻早想见她，没成想在竹林给碰上。
[ZH] 千里寻放下书：“快！有请！”
[EN] Seven or eight peaceful days flew by in the bamboo grove, Qianli Xun splitting her hours between admiring emerald bamboo vistas and devouring the huaben storybooks Kujue brought back. Excitement climbed with every page: she had found her true craft in this era! She could adapt her old modern web novels into Song-dynasty huaben scripts for common folk to enjoy!
[ZH] 苦荞出去说道：“韦嬷嬷，小姐让您进去。”
[EN] No sooner thought than done—she picked up her brush and began writing nonstop. Dozens of pages later, she set down the brush at last.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷是位五十岁左右的妇人，人看上去慈祥中不失威严，威严中藏着精明。她进入竹屋，先行礼。苦荞拿个垫子放在小姐对面，韦嬷嬷很习惯地坐下。
[EN] Stacking the sheets neat, Xiaguo stole admiring looks at the crisp lines of ink. "You're amazing, Miss, writing so many characters!"
[ZH] “没想到在这里遇见姑娘，苦荞在路上已将姑娘的情况告诉我，早知道我晚些出来收租，也不至于你被姑爷打得失忆。”
[EN] "Can you read them?"
[ZH] 说着韦嬷嬷流出两滴眼泪，她用巾帕沾去泪水，继续说：“和离之事没有成功，姑娘不要灰心，等回到林府，我再劝说姑爷，他若还不同意，我们搬出林府，临安城空着的宅子多的是，姑娘买一处就是，何必在林府受姑爷的气。”
[EN] "Only a few."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷一番话，千里寻听着舒畅无比，这才是家人该说的话，亲切感油然而生，也理解为何朱淑真愿意把财政大权交给她。
[EN] What a pity, Qianli Xun thought: blessed with looks like Xiaguo's, born into a wealthy home she'd have mastered zither, chess, calligraphy, and painting alike! "I'll have Kuqiao teach you to read from now on."
[ZH] “嬷嬷不必担心，我和离之心坚决，也正为此努力，如若林觉还不同意，就听你的，搬出林府。”
[EN] Xiaguo lit up. "Thank you, Miss!"
[ZH] 姑娘说话比以前底气更足，也更有力度。韦嬷嬷意识到，她真的变了。变了也好，省得像以前一样任人欺负。
[EN] "Where's Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “姑娘如此说，我深感欣慰。”
[EN] "Sister Kuqiao went back to the old cottage to cook your lunch."
[ZH] “嬷嬷前来收租可有地方住？”
[EN] Glancing out the doorway at the high sun, Qianli Xun sighed, "Time flies—already noon!"
[ZH] “村长已安排好竹屋，离姑娘不远，每年来都住那里。”
[EN] Soon Kuqiao returned carrying a bamboo basket of lunch, waited on Miss as she ate, and cleared up—her steady daily rhythm. Qianli Xun told her to pass the finished huaben script to Kujue and have him hand it to any storyteller at the market. Packing the basket and clutching the manuscript, Kuqiao headed back to her family's old cottage.
[ZH] “有村长安排，我就放心。”
[EN] When Kuqiao returned, an older woman walked beside her flanked by two maids and two page boys. Slipping indoors first, Kuqiao grinned, "Guess who I brought, Miss!"
[ZH] 夏果泡好茶，给韦嬷嬷端来，放在桌上。
[EN] Still flipping through a finished storybook, Qianli Xun looked up. "Who?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷抬头看一眼夏果：“这丫鬟我怎么没见过？”
[EN] "Matron Wei! Done collecting rents elsewhere, she came here for the bamboo grove, heard you were staying here, and hurried right over!"
[ZH] 夏果赶紧说：“嬷嬷好，我是小姐新收的丫鬟，名叫夏果。”
[EN] Holder of Zhu Shuzhen's purse strings and her loyal wet nurse—Qianli Xun had been eager to meet her, never expecting to cross paths right here in the grove! Tossing the book aside, she called, "Quick—invite her in!"
[ZH] “夏果，你到竹屋外等着，我与姑娘说些事。”
[EN] Stepping back out, Kuqiao announced, "Miss invites you inside, Matron Wei!"
[ZH] 夏果很听话，到竹屋外站在韦嬷嬷带来的丫鬟和小厮身旁。
[EN] Around fifty, carrying a blend of warmth, authority, and sharp judgment, Matron Wei stepped inside and bowed low. Kuqiao set a cushion across from her mistress, and Matron Wei settled down at ease. "Never dreamed I'd meet you out here, young lady! Kuqiao caught me up on the walk over; had I known, I would've put off rent collection instead of leaving you to get beaten into amnesia by Son-in-law!"
[ZH] 竹屋内韦嬷嬷说：“姑娘怎么能给身边收这么漂亮的丫鬟？”
[EN] Two tears slipped down her cheeks; dabbing them with a handkerchief, she pressed on crisp, "Don't lose heart over the failed divorce, young lady. Once back in Lin Manor, I'll talk to Son-in-law myself—and if he still refuses, we move right out! Plenty of vacant houses across Lin'an; buy one of our own instead of swallowing his garbage!"
[ZH] 千里寻不解：“嬷嬷为何如此说？”
[EN] Music to Qianli Xun's ears! Now this was real family! Instant warmth surged through her chest, showing at once why Zhu Shuzhen trusted this woman with her whole dowry. "Don't worry, Matron. My resolve to divorce hasn't budged an inch, and if Lin Jue drags his feet, we'll do just what you said and move out!"
[ZH] “大家闺秀身边的丫鬟就应该像苦荞这样，长相不差但也不出挑，夏果长相太过出众，恐带出去，大家只顾看她，而不顾姑娘，岂不会强压姑娘一头？”
[EN] Hearing steel and grit in her voice, Matron Wei saw the change plain—good! Better strong than pushed around like before. "Hearing you speak like that puts my heart at ease."
[ZH] “嬷嬷多虑，我不在乎这些，再说若把她带出去，有好人家的公子看上，也算给她寻个好去处。”
[EN] "Do you have a place to stay while collecting rent, Matron?"
[ZH] “姑娘既这样想，我也不多说什么，就是担心夏果的长相会给姑娘带来麻烦。”
[EN] "Village head set up a bamboo cottage close by, same place I stay every year."
[ZH] 苦荞插嘴道：“嬷嬷说我长得不好看，我不怪您。不过您说得没错，夏果一出现，小姐确实遇到麻烦，多亏小姐拳头厉害，才不怕地痞流氓。”
[EN] "Good, glad you're settled."
[ZH] “在我眼里，你长得刚刚合适，比那夏果强多了！呵呵……”韦嬷嬷笑完继续说，“我听苦荞说，姑娘被姑爷打晕后醒来，竟然神奇般地会拳法，真替姑娘高兴，以后看姑爷还敢打你!”
[EN] Just then Xiaguo brought fresh tea over and set it on the table. Glancing up at the striking girl, Matron Wei paused. "Don't recognize this maid."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷是第一个知道朱淑真醒来后会拳法没有惊讶甚至高兴的人，更让千里寻感到亲切：“我有事与嬷嬷商量。”
[EN] Xiaguo curtsied quick. "Good day, Matron. I'm Xiaguo, Miss's newly hired maid."
[ZH] “姑娘请说。”
[EN] "Wait outside the cottage a moment, Xiaguo. Private business with my young lady."
[ZH] “在竹林生活这段时日，我发现竹林的百姓生活挺不容易，想着今年的租子能不能减免些？”
[EN] Obedient, Xiaguo stepped out beside Matron Wei's own servants.
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁听了，甚是高兴。
[EN] Once alone indoors, Matron Wei spoke blunt: "Why hire a maid THIS gorgeous, young lady?"
[ZH] “姑娘与我想到一处去，可如今这竹林很快就不是姑娘的，今年的租子没有办法再收。”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked. "What's wrong with that, Matron?"
[ZH] “我因失忆，名下到底有多少田产，根本不记得。一到竹林，苦荞说我是竹林的主人，才知道我名下有这份田产，竹民们见我也是东家长东家短的，嬷嬷为何要说很快竹林不是我的？”
[EN] "A noble lady's personal maids ought to look neat yet modest like Kuqiao! Xiaguo is way too eye-catching; bring her out in public and folks stare at the maid instead of the mistress! Won't she steal your thunder?"
[ZH] “姑娘不是因为失忆不记得名下有多少田产，而是姑娘根本不关心田产之事，都交由我负责，等回城里，我定向姑娘一一细说。至于竹林，本就是姑娘的。我前来收租，找到村长，他告诉我竹林的田根在陈家公子手中，他要按照原价收回竹林，也会帮村民把今年的租子一并给了。”
[EN] "Overthinking it, Matron. I don't care about stuff like that! Besides, if some good young gentleman spots her and takes a shine to her, all the better for her future."
[ZH] 千里寻搞不清田根是什么，听到韦嬷嬷提起陈家公子，猜想定是陈浩宇搞鬼：“为何我是竹林的东家，陈家又能插一杠子进来？”
[EN] "If that's your view, I won't argue—just worried her face will invite trouble for you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷解释道：“当年朱家买竹林时，与售卖之人签的是活卖契约，朱家可以自由使用和收租，但田根还在以前的主人手中，他有随时按照原价收回的权利。”
[EN] Kuqiao piped up, "Calling me plain, Matron, I'll forgive you! Still, you hit the nail on the head: the second Xiaguo showed up, trouble hit—lucky Miss's fists sent those local thugs packing!"
[ZH] 千里寻总算听明白：“难道出售竹林的是陈家？”
[EN] "To my eyes you look just right, beats Xiaguo by a mile! Haha..." Laughing warm, Matron Wei went on, "Kuqiao told me you woke up knowing martial arts—thrilled for you! Let's see Son-in-law dare lay a finger on you now!"
[ZH] “不是，另有其人，我估计是陈家看上这片竹林，从卖主手中出高价买了田根，陈家是临安首富，有的是钱。想不通的是，竹林收成一般，朱家和陈家还是亲家，陈家怎么会这么干？”
[EN] First person in this whole dynasty to cheer rather than freak out over her fighting skills—Qianli Xun warmed to Matron Wei even faster! "Got something to discuss with you, Matron."
[ZH] 千里寻气道：“肯定是陈浩宇想要报复我。”
[EN] "Go ahead, young lady."
[ZH] “报复？”韦嬷嬷不解。
[EN] "Living here these days, I see how tough these bamboo villagers have it. Can we cut their rent this year?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“嬷嬷一直在乡下收租，很多事情不清楚，小姐两次阻止陈公子欺负良家妇女，定是他怀恨在心，才想出这招对付小姐。”
[EN] Beside them, Kuqiao beamed in delight.
[ZH] “原来如此，姑娘做得没错，既然陈公子想要竹林，给他便是，其他田产够姑娘用，少个竹林不算什么。”
[EN] "Great minds think alike—except this bamboo grove won't belong to you much longer, so we can't collect rent this year anyway."
[ZH] 千里寻更加喜欢韦嬷嬷，她的心胸和想法与自己实在相投。可她不想轻易饶了陈浩宇，必须给他些颜色瞧瞧，再让他吐出些东西来。
[EN] "Wait—with amnesia I didn't even know what estates I own! Only found out this grove was mine when Kuqiao told me and everyone started calling me Proprietor! Why won't it be mine anymore?"
[ZH] “他想用手中的田根收回竹林，那我就会会他。”
[EN] "Not amnesia; you never bothered tracking estates before and left everything to me. Once back in town I'll walk you through every single ledger. As for this grove, it was yours—until I went to collect rent and the village head told me Young Master Chen now holds the land root! He plans to redeem the grove at original price and cover this year's village rent out of pocket too!"
[ZH] 聊完后，韦嬷嬷在竹屋里转了一圈儿，十分满意，将苦荞夸赞一番。
[EN] Unsure what "land root" meant yet catching "Young Master Chen," Qianli Xun saw red—Chen Haoyu's dirty trick! "Wait—if I'm the owner, how does the Chen family butt in?"
[ZH] 拜别姑娘，出了竹屋，见夏果恭敬地站在外面，上前仔细瞧了瞧，用温和而有力度的语气说：“以后跟着姑娘出去，心里要明白自己是伺候姑娘的人，不能喧宾夺主，要守好丫鬟的本分，记住没？”
[EN] Matron Wei explained, "When the Zhu family bought this grove, they signed a conditional sale contract with the original owner, giving the Zhus use and rent rights while the land root stayed with the seller, letting him buy it back at original cost anytime."
[ZH] 夏果老实答道：“嬷嬷放心，我会牢记在心。”
[EN] Light dawned. "Was the seller the Chen family?"
[ZH] “回去吧，好生伺候姑娘。”韦嬷嬷带着丫鬟和小厮离开。
[EN] "No, someone else. My guess is the Chens paid top silver to buy the land root off the seller! Richest house in Lin'an, swimming in cash! Weird part is, this grove yields next to nothing, plus Zhus and Chens are in-laws—why pull a stunt like this?"
[ZH] 夏果回到屋内，见小姐又在看书，不敢打扰，进里面去找苦荞。
[EN] Qianli Xun fumed, "Because Chen Haoyu wants revenge on me!"
[ZH] 苦荞忙着绣花，她蹭到苦荞身边：“苦荞姐姐，小姐让你教我识字，你教教我呗？”
[EN] "Revenge?" Matron Wei frowned.
[ZH] 苦荞边绣花边说：“其实啊，我们做丫鬟的，识不识字并不重要，重要的是把主子伺候好。”
[EN] Kuqiao filled her in: "Out collecting rents, you missed it, Matron—Miss busted up Young Master Chen harassing innocent girls twice! He's nursing a grudge and pulled this to spite her!"
[ZH] “可小姐让你教我，关键是我也想学，再说苦荞姐姐是丫鬟不也会识字？”
[EN] "Aha! Good on you for stepping in, young lady! If Young Master Chen wants the bamboo grove, let him have it—your other estates leave you rolling in silver anyway; losing one grove is pocket change."
[ZH] “好吧，既然小姐吩咐，我教你便是，你每日把小姐写废的纸拿来，我按照上面的字教你。”
[EN] Matron Wei's cool head matched Qianli Xun's own vibe, yet no way was she letting Chen Haoyu walk off smug without bleeding a little! "Wants to use the land root to reclaim the grove? Fine—let me meet him face to face!"
[ZH] “谢谢苦荞姐姐。”
[EN] Chatting done, Matron Wei toured the cozy cottage, praised Kuqiao's tidy work, and bade farewell. Stepping outside, she looked Xiaguo over standing dutiful by the doorway and delivered a mild yet steel-edged warning: "When you go out serving our young lady, remember your place: never outshine your mistress, and keep a maid's proper bounds. Clear?"
[ZH] 千里寻看话本一直保持一个动作，很不舒服，她放下书，起身走出竹屋，打算活动活动。
[EN] Xiaguo bowed low. "Yes, Matron. I'll engrave it on my heart."
[ZH] 苦蕨正站在外面犹豫要不要进去。
[EN] "Go back in and wait on her well." Gathering her attendants, Matron Wei departed.
[ZH] 千里寻走下竹屋的台阶，来到苦蕨跟前：“你怎么站在这里？”
[EN] Stepping inside to find Miss reading again, Xiaguo slipped to the inner room where Kuqiao sat embroidering and sidled close. "Sister Kuqiao, Miss asked you to teach me to read—will you teach me?"
[ZH] 别看苦蕨长得健壮，却是个害羞之人，一开口说话就紧张，尤其是面对像朱淑真这等大家闺秀。
[EN] Keeping her needle moving, Kuqiao remarked, "Truth is, reading or not doesn't matter for maids; serving our mistress well is what counts."
[ZH] “东……东家，你写的……我给了说话艺人，他喜欢，直说好，让……继续，继续写。”
[EN] "Still, Miss told you to, and I really want to learn! Besides, you're a maid and you know how to read!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“紧张什么？我又不是老虎！”
[EN] "Fine, since Miss ordered it. Every day collect Miss's discarded practice sheets, and I'll teach you from those characters."
[ZH] 苦蕨用手挠挠头，害羞道：“东家是贵人，我见贵人……紧张。”
[EN] "Thank you, Sister Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “我在竹林这段时间，送手稿的事就麻烦你，放心，不会白跑，我会让苦荞给你跑路费。”
[EN] Stiff from sitting over storybooks, Qianli Xun set her volume down and stepped outdoors to stretch. Right outside hovered Kujue, hesitating whether to knock. Walking down the bamboo steps, Qianli Xun smiled, "What are you standing out here for?"
[ZH] “白跑我乐意。”
[EN] Sturdy as Kujue was, shyness tied his tongue around refined ladies like Zhu Shuzhen. "Pr... Proprietor, gave your pages to a storyteller... he loved it, praised it nonstop, begs you to keep... keep writing!"
[ZH] “你可真老实，回去忙吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "Why so jumpy? I'm not a tiger!"
[ZH] 苦蕨得到允许扭头跑走。
[EN] Scratching his head sheepish, Kujue mumbled, "Proprietor's a grand lady—I get nervous around bigwigs..."
[ZH] 千里寻正想再回竹屋，余光瞥到陈浩宇带着人朝竹屋方向走来，心想：“我不找你，你倒送上门来，老娘正好与你聊聊竹林之事。”
[EN] "While I'm staying here, I'll trouble you to deliver scripts—not for free, Kuqiao will pay your errand fee."
[ZH] 陈浩宇身边除四个地痞流氓外，还多出一位，从步履来看，身手不错，看来他今日有备而来。
[EN] "Happy to run 'em free!"
[ZH] 千里寻仔细瞧那位高手，长得虎背熊腰，袒胸露背，身上的肌肉十分发达，让人不由产生畏惧。她能否打得过对方，还不好判断，只有交过手之后才能得知。
[EN] "Too honest for your own good! Go on back to work." Excused, Kujue spun around and trotted off.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走近，冷笑道：“喂！朱淑真，竹林已经是我的，你赶紧离开！”
[EN] Turning back toward her cottage, Qianli Xun caught movement out of the corner of her eye: Chen Haoyu swaggering straight toward her with an entourage! Didn't even have to hunt him down—he marched right to her door! Beside his usual four street thugs strode a newcomer whose steady gait screamed martial master; Chen Haoyu had brought heavy muscle today. Sizing up the giant—broad shoulders, bare brawny chest rippling with thick muscle, intimidating build—Qianli Xun couldn't tell who'd win until fists met.
[ZH] 千里寻不屑道：“陈浩宇，田根虽在你手中，可你实在缺乏契约精神。当初朱家与竹林主人签过活卖契约，需用原价把竹林赎回，钱还没给老娘，就说竹林是你的，实在可笑！也有失富家公子的身份！”
[EN] Strutting close, Chen Haoyu sneered, "Hey, Zhu Shuzhen! This bamboo grove belongs to me now—pack up and get out!"
[ZH] “银子很快就到，银子一到，马上给本公子搬出竹林，顺便把你刚收的丫鬟给本公子留下！”
[EN] Qianli Xun scoffed right back. "Holding the land root and still zero clue how contracts work, Chen Haoyu! The Zhu family signed a conditional sale contract—you have to redeem it at original price first! Haven't paid me a single coin and you're claiming the land? Hilarious—some rich young master!"
