[ZH] 一个月实在太快，千里寻在竹林还未待够，本想处理完和离之事，再来竹林住些时日。可一想竹林已是陈浩宇的田产，只能放弃再来暂住的想法。
[EN] One month flew by in a blink! Qianli Xun hated leaving already—almost planned to come back once her divorce settled, until remembering Chen Haoyu owned the grove now! Oh well, scratch that plan.
[ZH] 在竹林这段时日，千里寻没有感觉到竹林为她加持了什么神奇的力量，只觉远离林府，内心越来越平静，思考问题也越发理智。她打算回去后不再用武力逼迫林觉，与他像朋友一样好好聊聊，心平气和地解决和离之事。
[EN] No magical thunderbolt had struck her destiny in this bamboo grove—yet stepping away from Lin Manor had cooled her head and sharpened her thinking. Once back, she planned to ditch fists and sit down with Lin Jue like calm adults to talk through mutual divorce.
[ZH] 千里寻也理性地思考过她想要谈场恋爱的心理。
[EN] She had also looked squarely at her sudden longing for love.
[ZH] 穿越前，之所以不涉足爱情，除了有理想和事业要追求外，还因她不想涉足婚姻。尽管很多人说只谈恋爱不结婚，可千里寻认为不以结婚为目的恋爱是耍流氓，她不想成为女流氓。因而，对于摆在面前的恋爱机会只有一种态度：拒绝。
[EN] Back in her modern life, she had steered clear of romance—chasing career goals and loathing casual flings, convinced dating without marriage in mind was pure player nonsense she refused to touch, so she turned down every suitor cold.
[ZH] 死过一回，曾追求的理想和事业在地震那一刻被震得粉碎。借助朱淑真的身体于另是一个时代活过来，逐渐意识到人生的功名利禄如过眼烟云，对于像她这样的普通人来讲，最大的幸福莫过于与另一个人相伴到老。加之穿越而来，一场不属于她婚姻摆在面前，让从未涉足恋爱的她有种强烈的失落感。
[EN] Dying once shattered every ambition and business dream into rubble beneath that quake. Waking up in another era inside Zhu Shuzhen's body drove home how hollow fame and fortune really were—real happiness meant walking through life beside one cherished partner! Worse, waking trapped inside an unwanted marriage sharpened the ache of never having tasted real love once.
[ZH] 所以，千里寻想要谈一场恋爱，想要找一位能与她在陌生的时代共度余生之人。
[EN] So yes—she wanted to fall in love, to find someone worth sharing the rest of this strange new life with.
[ZH] 千里寻想过，要与谁来一场甜蜜的恋爱之旅？每一次申简辰的身影会浮现在她的脑海，她会问自己：“不知他有没有穿越而来？如果也穿越而来，该到哪里找他？”
[EN] Who? Every time she pictured romance, Shen Jianchen's face surfaced unbidden: Did he cross over too? Where on earth would I even find him?
[ZH] 她无法给出答案，也许再也遇不到申简辰。她决定将自己的爱情交给命运，若遇到心仪之人，哪怕不是申简辰，也要与那人开始一段刻骨铭心的恋情。
[EN] No answers—maybe their paths would never cross again. Leaving love to fate, she decided: if the right man appeared—Shen Jianchen or not—she'd dive in with an open heart!
[ZH] 她到底喜欢什么样的男子？也许是像申简辰那样，毕竟曾经追求她的异性中，申简辰是最优秀的一位。也许不是， 只有遇到后才知道。
[EN] What sort of man would win her? Probably someone like Shen Jianchen—head and shoulders above every suitor she'd ever met back home—or maybe someone totally unexpected; she'd know when she met him.
[ZH] 她幻想过申简辰穿越而来成为他喜欢的类型，神奇般地出现在她面前：“千里寻，嫁给我吧。”她一定会答应，像梦中梦到的那样。
[EN] Sometimes she daydreamed Shen Jianchen crossing into a dashing Song gentleman, popping out of nowhere proposing: "Qianli Xun, marry me!"—and just like her dream, she'd say yes in a heartbeat!
[ZH] 千里寻回到竹屋，吩咐苦荞和夏果把能收拾的东西先整理好，甚至让她们把书桌也收了，剩下的两三日，她不想再动笔墨，打算除吃饭睡觉外，其他时间去竹林转转。
[EN] Stepping inside, she told Kuqiao and Xiaguo to start packing up—even clearing the study desk; done writing for the final two or three days, she planned to soak in pure bamboo serenity till departure.
[ZH] 要离开，真有些舍不得，她甚至想过再从陈浩宇手中买回竹林，又怕对方趁机抬高价格，故意讹人，便暂时放弃买回竹林的想法。
[EN] Part of her hated parting with this grove so much she almost considered buying it back from Chen Haoyu—only to drop the thought fast, knowing that petty jerk would jack the price to the sky!
[ZH] 第二天，千里寻用过早饭，不想任何人陪伴，一个人顺着竹林的小路缓缓前行。脚下铺着干枯的竹叶，被她踩得沙沙作响。
[EN] Next morning after breakfast, craving solo quiet, Qianli Xun wandered down a narrow bamboo trail alone, crisp dry bamboo leaves rustling underfoot.
[ZH] 穿过小路，来到竹林深处的溪水旁，溪水清澈见底，从一块一块的顽石上流过，神态悠然自得。千里寻在旁边找块石头坐下，用心欣赏山泉美景。
[EN] Deep inside the grove she emerged beside a crystal mountain stream rippling carefree over smooth boulders. Settling onto a flat rock, she drank in the tranquil scene.
[ZH] 正当她欣赏得入神，发现两名男子踩踏着溪水中的石头，从对岸走来，看那衣着并不像居住在竹林里的村民。前面的是位公子，后面跟着的应是随身小厮。
[EN] Mid-reverie, two men stepped across stepping-stones from the opposite bank—finely dressed, clearly outsiders, not villagers: a tall gentleman in front, a sharp-looking attendant behind.
[ZH] ﻿他们正是申简辰与白风，二人穿越溪流，抬头看到千里寻。
[EN] Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng—crossing the stream and looking up straight at Qianli Xun!
[ZH] 白风上前问道：“姑娘，请问竹林里的村庄怎么走？”
[EN] Stepping forward polite, Bai Feng asked: "Miss, which way to the bamboo village?"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得纳闷，这二人真奇怪，放着大道不走，偏偏走小路，说不定居心不良。
[EN] Suspicion flickered in Qianli Xun—ignoring the main road to sneak through back paths? Up to no good?
[ZH] “你们从哪里来？为何不走大道直接从村口进？”
[EN] "Where'd you two come from? Why skip the main road into the village?"
[ZH] 白风回道：“哦，我们坐马车来的，碰到一大片竹林，我家公子没见识过，很好奇，又见竹林里有小路，想走走试试，可走着走着迷了路，幸好遇见姑娘。”
[EN] "Rode out by carriage," Bai Feng explained smooth. "Spotted this huge bamboo forest—my young master had never seen one, got curious walking a side trail, and got turned around! Lucky we bumped into you!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞅瞅站在远处的公子，长得倒是高大挺拔，五官俊朗，面前的小厮也是一脸真诚，看上去不像骗人：“你们到村子里做什么？”
[EN] Sizing up the tall, upright young gentleman standing back—handsome features, earnest attendant, zero shady vibe—Qianli Xun asked: "What brings you to the village?"
[ZH] 白风回道：“找人。”
[EN] "Looking for someone," Bai Feng replied.
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“找什么人？”
[EN] "Who?"
[ZH] 白风不知如何回答：“姑娘怎么像审讯犯人一样？”
[EN] Stuck—Bai Feng frowned: "Talking like a magistrate interrogating suspects, miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻不屑道：“谁知道你们是好人还是坏人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sniffed: "How do I know if you're honest folks or crooks?"
[ZH] 申简辰喊道：“白风！不用向她打听，既然她能出现在这里，说明离村子不远，前面有条路，顺着那条路走，肯定能找到村子！”
[EN] Calling from behind, Shen Jianchen cut in: "Drop it, Bai Feng! If she's sitting here, the village is right nearby—there's a clear path right ahead, follow it and we hit the village!"
[ZH] 白风回到王爷身边，小声说：“王爷，竹林里难道也住着有钱人家？那姑娘看着像有钱人家的丫鬟。”
[EN] Jogging back beside his master, Bai Feng whispered: "Think rich folks live in this forest too, Your Highness? Looks like a fancy household's maid!"
[ZH] 申简辰瞧向那姑娘：“也许是城里的丫鬟回乡下探亲。”
[EN] Glancing back at her plain cotton clothes, Shen Jianchen guessed: "Probably a city maid home visiting family."
[ZH] “也对。”二人顺着千里寻走来的路向村子而去。
[EN] "Makes sense!" Side by side, they turned straight onto the trail Qianli Xun had just walked.
[ZH] 千里寻自语道：“倒挺聪明。”
[EN] "Pretty sharp," Qianli Xun murmured after them.
[ZH] 她继续欣赏山泉，努力向山泉流来的方向望去，远处有座山，泉水应该是那座山上流下来的。
[EN] Returning her gaze to the clear stream, she traced its sparkling path back toward the distant green hills feeding it.
[ZH] 申简辰与白风顺着竹林的小路一直往前走，终于看到各色竹屋。
[EN] Following the woodland track straight ahead, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng soon emerged among clustered bamboo houses.
[ZH] 白风喜道：“王爷，应该是这里。”
[EN] "Found it, Your Highness!" Bai Feng cheered.
[ZH] 申简辰批评道：“都给你叮嘱过不要称呼王爷，又忘记。”
[EN] "Told you to stop calling me Your Highness—forgot again!" Shen Jianchen chided.
[ZH] 白风不好意思：“一高兴给忘了。”
[EN] "Oops—got excited!" Bai Feng grinned sheepish.
[ZH] “你找人打听一下送书稿的下人住哪里？”
[EN] "Ask around where that script courier lives."
[ZH] “是，公子。”
[EN] "On it, Young Master!"
[ZH] 白风看到一位竹民扛着几根竹子经过，上前行礼：“大叔，向你打听个人。”
[EN] Spotting a villager hauling cut bamboo poles, Bai Feng stepped up bowing: "Uncle, hoping to ask after someone."
[ZH] 竹民站住：“你想打听谁？”
[EN] Halting, the villager asked: "Who?"
[ZH] 白风编道：“我曾向一人借过钱，还未还，却找不到人，只知他住在这村子，跟我年纪差不多，个头没我高，长得又黑又壮，人看上去挺老实。”
[EN] Spinning smooth on the spot, Bai Feng said: "Borrowed money from a guy and came to pay him back—lost his address! Know he lives here: about my age, shorter, tanned, stocky, honest look."
[ZH] 竹民问道：“你来还钱？”
[EN] "Came all this way to repay a debt?"
[ZH] 白风笑道：“是。”
[EN] "Yep!" Bai Feng grinned.
[ZH] “现在的世道，像你这样的人不多，看在你懂得还钱的份上，我帮你想想。”竹民想了一会儿说，“你说的人很像苦蕨。”
[EN] "Rare to see honest folks these days—for that, let me think!" Pausing a beat: "Sounds dead-on like Kujue!"
[ZH] 白风兴奋道：“不知他住在哪个竹屋？”
[EN] "Which bamboo house is his?!" Bai Feng lit up.
[ZH] 竹民指着村里的小路：“顺着这条路，一直向里走，看到第一个最破烂的竹屋就是苦家，苦蕨住那里。”
[EN] Pointing down the lane: "Straight down this path—very first super rundown bamboo shack is the Ku house! Kujue lives there!"
[ZH] 白风谢道：“多谢大叔！”
[EN] "Thanks a million, Uncle!"
[ZH] 申简辰与白风顺着村里曲里拐弯的路向里走，边走边瞧哪个竹屋比较破烂。
[EN] Winding down the narrow lanes, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng scanned left and right for the shabbiest bamboo shack.
[ZH] 苦蕨在屋里忙完，刚走到竹屋门口，一眼瞧见白风，立刻缩了回去，拉住苦荞：“姐，昨日向我打听写手稿的人竟然找来，就在外面，我躲一下，他们要是问起，就说没我这个人。”
[EN] Inside, finishing chores and stepping to his doorway, Kujue spotted Bai Feng instantly—and jerked straight back inside grabbing Kuqiao: "Sis! That kidnapper hunting the writer tracked me right here! I'm hiding—if they ask, swear you never heard of me!"
[ZH] 苦荞还没反应过来，苦蕨已溜出后门。
[EN] Before Kuqiao even blinked, Kujue bolted straight out the back door!
[ZH] 申简辰与白风终于瞧到第一个最破烂的竹屋，白风在外面大声问道：“请问苦蕨是不是住这里？”
[EN] Reaching the first weathered bamboo shack, Bai Feng hollered outside: "Excuse me—does Kujue live here?!"
[ZH] 没有任何回应，白风又喊一遍：“请问苦蕨是不是住这里？”
[EN] Dead silence. He called again: "Does Kujue live here?!"
[ZH] 苦荞走出来，看向两人：“你们找谁？”
[EN] Kuqiao stepped out onto the stoop eyeing both strangers: "Looking for who?"
[ZH] 白风又重复一遍：“请问苦蕨是不是住这里？”
[EN] "Does Kujue live here?" Bai Feng repeated.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“没听说过此人，你找错地方。”
[EN] "Never heard of him—wrong house!" Kuqiao lied flat.
[ZH] 正好有竹民从苦荞家经过，打招呼：“苦荞，你家来客人了？”
[EN] Right on cue, a neighbor strolled past waving cheerful: "Hey Kuqiao—got visitors?!"
[ZH] 直接将苦荞出卖，苦荞气得没理会那人，竹民不知所以，疑惑着离开。
[EN] Sold out on the spot! Shooting the clueless neighbor a death glare as he wandered off baffled, Kuqiao braced herself.
[ZH] 白风笑道：“苦荞？你姓苦，还说没听过苦蕨？快说，苦蕨到底跟你什么关系？”
[EN] Bai Feng smirked wide: "Kuqiao?! Same surname 'Ku' and never heard of Kujue?! Spill it—what's Kujue to you?!"
[ZH] 苦荞问：“你们是什么人？为什么找我弟弟？”
[EN] "Who ARE you two, hunting my little brother?!" Kuqiao bristled.
[ZH] 申简辰解释道：“姑娘放心，我不会伤害你弟弟，只想问问他给说话艺人送的书稿是谁所写？”
[EN] Stepping forward gentle, Shen Jianchen reassured her: "Don't panic, miss—mean your brother zero harm! Just want to know who wrote the huaben scripts he drops off for the storyteller."
[ZH] 小姐警告过，她写话本之事不能向外人提起，苦荞虽不懂小姐的用意，但只要是小姐说的话，她必然遵从。再说也不知道眼前这位公子为何打听此事，说不定对小姐不利。
[EN] Remembering Miss's strict warning never to expose her pen name—and unsure if these strangers meant trouble—Kuqiao played stone wall:
[ZH] “你问这干什么？”
[EN] "Why do you care?"
[ZH] 申简辰问道：“写书稿的人可叫千里寻？”
[EN] "Is the author's name Qianli Xun?" Shen Jianchen pressed eager.
[ZH] 眼前的公子不回答问题反而向她发问，苦荞又怎能直接回答？骗道：“这位公子，我弟弟送书稿没错，也告诉说话艺人是位叫千里寻的才子所写，只可惜你们晚来一步，他已离开，不知所踪。”
[EN] Dodging straight answers, Kuqiao improvised smooth: "Sure, my brother ran scripts and gave the name Scholar Qianli Xun—too bad you're a step late! He already packed up and left, no idea where!"
[ZH] 申简辰失望道：“什么？他走了？去了哪里？”
[EN] Every spark crashed out of Shen Jianchen's eyes: "What?! Left?! Where did he go?!"
[ZH] 苦荞强调道：“都说了，不知所踪。”
[EN] "Told you—no idea where!"
[ZH] “什么时候走的？”
[EN] "When did he leave?!"
[ZH] “三天前离开。”
[EN] "Three days ago!"
[ZH] “朝哪个方向走的？”
[EN] "Which direction?!"
[ZH] “你这公子，怎么这么多问题？千里寻来得突然，走得也突然，我们跟他不熟，我弟弟帮他送书稿也是收钱办事，其他一概不知。”
[EN] "So many questions, young master! Qianli Xun drifted in out of nowhere and drifted out just as fast! Barely knew him—my brother just took coin to run errands, know nothing else!"
[ZH] 苦荞装得坦然，瞎话编得也算圆满，申简辰不得不信：“打扰姑娘。”然后垂头丧气地离开。
[EN] Calm face, airtight story—deflated to the core, Shen Jianchen bowed heavy: "Forgive the intrusion, miss." Turning, he walked off shoulders slumped.
[ZH] 王爷要走，白风再留着没有任何意义，紧跟上王爷，边走边扭过头对苦荞“哼”了一声。
[EN] No point lingering; shooting Kuqiao a parting "Hmph!" over his shoulder, Bai Feng hurried after his master.
[ZH] 苦荞以白眼回应，庆幸自己够聪明，很快将两个陌生人打发走。
[EN] Rolling her eyes right back, Kuqiao beamed proud of herself for brushing off two strangers clean!
[ZH] 苦蕨从后门进屋，来到前门：“姐，人走了？”
[EN] Slipping back in through the back door, Kujue peeked out front: "Gone, Sis?"
[ZH] “有你姐姐我出马，问题立刻解决。”苦荞很骄傲，叮嘱道，“爹娘近几日走亲戚不在家，我过两日也要离开，你要照顾好自己。”
[EN] "Your big sister handled it in two seconds!" puffing up proud before reminding him: "Parents are away visiting kin, and I leave in two days too—take good care of yourself!"
[ZH] 苦蕨憨憨地说：“不用替我担心，我都快成亲，已是大人，会照顾好自己。”
[EN] Grinning sheepish: "Almost a married man—I can handle myself!"
[ZH] “有你这话，姐就放心。”苦荞说完，回屋去忙活，小姐在竹林吃不了几顿饭，临走前更要给她做几顿好吃的，今日她打算做最拿手的竹筒饭。
[EN] "Good to hear!" Heading back to the kitchen, Kuqiao set to cooking her signature bamboo tube rice—only a few country meals left for Miss!
[ZH] 申简辰带着白风向村口走去，一路上沉默不语，唯一的希望就此破灭，他又该到何处去找千里寻？
[EN] Walking glum toward the village exit beside silent Bai Feng, Shen Jianchen felt his sole lead crumble to ash—where on earth to hunt Qianli Xun now?
[ZH] 二人走到村口附近，正好遇见千里寻从竹林回来。
[EN] Right near the village edge, they crossed paths once more with Qianli Xun strolling back from the bamboo stream.
[ZH] 毕竟刚刚有过一面之缘，申简辰向对方微笑点头，千里寻则礼貌回应。两人就这么完美地错过第一次相认的机会。
[EN] Recognizing each other from minutes earlier, Shen Jianchen offered a polite nod and faint smile; Qianli Xun smiled politely back—walking clean past each other, missing their very first chance at reunion by inches!

#CH 50
[ZH] 苦荞做好竹筒饭，放进竹篮，提着回到竹屋，把饭摆好。桌上的笔墨纸砚已收拾，连做茶的工具也收了，临时布置的书桌彻底沦为饭桌。
[EN] Steaming hot bamboo tube rice packed in her basket, Kuqiao carried lunch back to the cottage and set it out—writing gear and tea utensils all packed away now, leaving the low study table pure dining table.
[ZH] 当小姐坐下享用美味的竹筒饭时，苦荞向周围瞅瞅，没见夏果的身影：“小姐，夏果怎么没在？”
[EN] Settling down to enjoy her meal, Kuqiao noticed Xiaguo missing: "Where's Xiaguo, Miss?"
[ZH] “要回城里，我让夏果回去跟家人道别。”
[EN] "Heading back to town soon—sent her home to say goodbye to her family."
[ZH] “小姐对夏果真好。”
[EN] "You spoil Xiaguo, Miss."
[ZH] “我对你一样好。”
[EN] "Spoil you just as much!"
[ZH] “我和夏果能跟着小姐，是几辈子修来的福气。”苦荞想起陌生公子打听千里寻之事，“对了，今日来了两个人，找苦蕨打听千里寻。”
[EN] "Serving you is lifetimes of luck for both of us!" Remembering the morning's surprise visitors, Kuqiao added: "Oh—two strangers came hunting Kujue today asking after Qianli Xun!"
[ZH] 千里寻停下手中的竹勺，怎么会有人知道她在另一个时代的名字？难道是申简辰？她激动地问：“问的人有没有说他是谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun froze mid-scoop with her bamboo spoon! Who in this dynasty knew her real name?! Could it be Shen Jianchen?! Heart leaping, she blurted: "Did they say who they were?!"
[ZH] 苦荞摇摇头：“他们没说，我一心想着尽快打发他们离开，忘记问。”
[EN] Kuqiao shook her head: "Never said—too busy shooing them off, forgot to ask."
[ZH] “他们为什么要找千里寻？”
[EN] "Why were they hunting Qianli Xun?"
[ZH] “小姐给说话艺人提供书稿留的名字是千里寻，我猜那公子喜欢听说书，认为话本写得好，想要结识，才从说话艺人那里打听而来。我当然什么都没说，编个瞎话，告诉他们千里寻已离开竹林不知所踪，那位公子看上去很失落，然后就离开了。”
[EN] "You left the pen name Qianli Xun with the storyteller—bet that gentleman loved hearing the story and tracked the courier hoping to meet the author! Kept my mouth locked tight, told them Qianli Xun left the grove days ago to parts unknown! Poor guy looked crushed and walked right off."
[ZH] 千里寻想起在溪水旁遇到的两人：“那位公子是不是长得高高大大？身边的小厮也长得五官俊俏？”
[EN] Wait—connecting the dots to the two strangers by the mountain stream: "Tall, handsome gentleman with a sharp-looking attendant?"
[ZH] “是，小姐难道见过他们？”
[EN] "Yep! Ran into them, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞬间接受苦荞的解释，怎么可能是申简辰？哪有这么巧的事？她又开始吃起竹筒饭：“见过，还向我问过路。”
[EN] Instantly Qianli Xun bought Kuqiao's theory—no way could that be Shen Jianchen, coincidence wasn't THAT wild! Resuming her bamboo tube rice: "Yeah—even asked me directions."
[ZH] 苦荞赞道：“我家小姐就是有才华，有人守在门口捡拾您写废的手稿也就罢了，才写起话本就有人亲自找上门来结识，您要是男子，肯定结交甚广，绝不比乔公子差。”
[EN] "My Miss is unstoppable!" Kuqiao beamed. "Folks camp at the gate stealing your poetry scraps, and one week writing huaben brings fans chasing you to the woods! Were you a man, you'd have friends across the whole empire—beat Young Master Qiao hands down!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有搭话，用心享受乡间美食，也不知结束竹林的生活回到城里等待她的又是什么。
[EN] Smiling silent, Qianli Xun savored her country meal, wondering what awaited her back in Lin'an once this peaceful month closed.
[ZH] 两天很快结束，林府的车夫按照约定准时在村口等候。
[EN] Two days flew by; right on schedule, Lin Manor's coachman waited at the village entrance.
[ZH] 苦蕨、村长，还有一些竹民前来相送。
[EN] Kujue, the village head, and a warm knot of villagers gathered to see her off.
[ZH] 千里寻向大家告别，走上马车，踏上回城之路。
[EN] Waving goodbye to all, Qianli Xun boarded the carriage for the road back to town.
[ZH] 竹林离城里不算太远，乘坐马车半个时辰即到，苦蕨步行也就一个多时辰。可对千里寻来讲，林府与竹林之间在她心里产生的距离根本无法用时间衡量。
[EN] Only an hour back to Lin'an by carriage—barely two hours on foot for Kujue—yet in Qianli Xun's heart, Lin Manor sat light-years apart from her bamboo haven.
[ZH] 马车到达林府，韦嬷嬷带着几个小厮已经在门口候着。等千里寻与两个丫鬟从马车上下来后，小厮们忙着上前搬东西。
[EN] Pulling up outside Lin Manor, Matron Wei stood waiting at the gate with several page boys who hustled forward to unload luggage the moment Qianli Xun and her maids stepped down.
[ZH] “我已吩咐人把竹兰居打扫干净，姑娘刚回来，路途劳顿，先歇歇脚，再忙笔墨之事。”
[EN] "Had Zhulan Residence aired and scrubbed spotless—rest from the ride before touching brush or ink," Matron Wei welcomed warm.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话令千里寻感到温馨，身边有位替她操心之人，也算在陌生的时代有所依靠。她点点头：“林觉可在？”
[EN] Having someone genuinely look out for her wrapped Qianli Xun in quiet comfort. Nodding thanks, she asked: "Is Lin Jue home?"
[ZH] “姑爷还未回来，姑娘可是头一次一进门问姑爷在不在。”韦嬷嬷开着玩笑。
[EN] "Not back yet—first time in history you walked through the gate asking after Son-in-law!" Matron Wei teased.
[ZH] 千里寻凑近韦嬷嬷耳边，小声说：“我想快些与他谈和离之事。”
[EN] Leaning near Matron Wei's ear, Qianli Xun whispered: "Eager to bring up mutual divorce right away."
[ZH] “姑娘别急，据我观察和离之事已有眉目，回屋我详细说给姑娘听。”韦嬷嬷拉起姑娘向府内走去。
[EN] "No rush—from what I hear, mutual divorce is already warming up! Come inside and I'll tell you everything." Linking arms, Matron Wei walked her in.
[ZH] 进入竹兰居，千里寻换件居家的衣裳，苦荞很自觉地坐在茶桌旁做茶，夏果待在旁边学习。
[EN] Inside Zhulan Residence, Qianli Xun slipped into comfortable robes while Kuqiao naturally set to whisking tea with Xiaguo watching beside her to learn.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“夏果，你出去一下，我有事与姑娘讲。”
[EN] "Step outside a moment, Xiaguo—need a word with our young lady," Matron Wei directed.
[ZH] 夏果心中不满，她也是小姐的贴身丫鬟，凭什么让她出去？苦荞就可以心安理得在里面做茶？她不敢反抗，乖乖回声：“是。”
[EN] Resentment pricked Xiaguo—personal maid too, why always boot HER out while Kuqiao stayed pouring tea?! Swallowing her pride, she murmured: "Yes, Matron."
[ZH] 待夏果出去后，韦嬷嬷在桌旁坐下：“我回到林府，听下人说林家大爷和二爷跟姑爷在一起喝酒，酒桌上苦口婆心劝姑爷同意和离，姑爷的态度没有以前坚决，开始动心，告诉两位哥哥，打算考虑一段时日。”
[EN] Once Xiaguo stepped out, Matron Wei sat down: "Getting back to Lin Manor, I heard from servants that Eldest Master Lin and Second Master Lin sat Son-in-law down over wine and talked hard sense into him about mutual divorce! He isn't digging his heels in like before—actually swayed, told his brothers he'd think it over for a few days!"
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻认为定是竹林给她带来好运，更在心里感激净光法师。
[EN] Bamboo luck REALLY worked! Qianli Xun silently sent a thousand thanks up the hill to Master Jingguang!
[ZH] “希望林觉考虑的时间不要太久。”
[EN] "Hope he doesn't 'think it over' forever!"
[ZH] “我还听说姑爷打算纳妾。”
[EN] "Even better—word is Son-in-law plans on taking a concubine!"
[ZH] “他纳他的妾，跟我没有任何关系。”
[EN] "He can take ten concubines for all I care!"
[ZH] “怎能没关系？姑爷纳妾说明他已打算放下姑娘。”
[EN] "You SHOULD care—taking a concubine proves he's finally letting you go!"
[ZH] “此话怎讲？”
[EN] "How so?"
[ZH] “我虽没瞧上姑爷，可也看得出他真心喜欢姑娘，宁可把商妓带到家里伤害你，也不愿意纳一房妾室。可惜你们两个不相称，在一起日子不会好过。他现在想要纳妾室，足以说明他打算放下姑娘。”
[EN] "Never cared for Son-in-law myself, yet I saw plain he truly loved you—even hauling courtesans home to spite you, he refused to take a proper concubine! Pity you two didn't fit. Now choosing to take a concubine proves he's ready to move on from you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的解释更令千里寻高兴：“嬷嬷一会儿吩咐后厨做些好菜，再备壶好酒，我要在竹兰居宴请林觉，与他平心静气谈谈和离之事。”
[EN] Thrilled by Matron Wei's insight, Qianli Xun jumped on the opening: "Matron, order the kitchen to whip up fine dishes and good wine—I'm hosting Lin Jue right here in Zhulan Residence tonight to talk mutual divorce calm and friendly!"
[ZH] “姑娘既然这么着急，我听你的，这就去安排。”
[EN] "Eager as you are, say no more—I'll arrange it at once!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷起身去忙活，走到院内，见夏果在院子里无所事事的样子，心里很不爽。
[EN] Rising to head out, Matron Wei spotted Xiaguo idling aimless in the courtyard and frowned hard.
[ZH] 夏果见到韦嬷嬷赶紧拜见：“嬷嬷好。”
[EN] Xiaguo bobbed a quick curtsy: "Good day, Matron."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷平静而又严厉地叮嘱：“姑娘好心收你当丫鬟，你要记住这份恩情，用心伺候姑娘。现在来到城里不比乡下，说话做事要讲究分寸，不该说的一个字都不要说，不该做的一件都不要做。”
[EN] Calm yet razor-sharp, Matron Wei warned: "Remember Miss's kindness taking you in and serve with your whole heart. City life isn't a country village—watch every word and every step; never speak out of turn, never cross a line."
[ZH] 夏果回道：“是。”
[EN] "Yes, Matron."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷又将她瞧一番，才离开竹兰居。
[EN] Giving her one last piercing look, Matron Wei strode out of Zhulan Residence.
[ZH] 夏果瞧着韦嬷嬷的背影，着实想不通，韦嬷嬷为何见自己总是如此严厉？她又没做错任何事，难道只因她长相出众些，就该遭受如此待遇？越想心中越不满，担心小姐与苦荞看出不满，决定到处走走散散心，等情绪疏散后再回屋伺候小姐。
[EN] Staring after Matron Wei's back, bitter resentment festered inside Xiaguo—why did that old woman treat her like dirt every single day?! Done zero wrong—just punished for having a prettier face?! Worried Miss or Kuqiao might read resentment on her face, she stepped out for a walk to cool off.
[ZH] 夏果出了竹兰居，走出西院，顺着林府的小径瞎逛。林府的路旁除种些地桃花外，也没种什么，景致很一般，还不如乡下。
[EN] Slipping past Zhulan Residence and out of the West Courtyard, Xiaguo drifted down Lin Manor's plain lanes—nothing but Caesarweed along the dirt paths, duller than the countryside!
[ZH] 夏果想着自己的容貌并不比小姐差，为何命运如此不同？她出生的家庭已不好，还被陈公子调戏，好不容易成为小姐的贴身丫鬟，又被韦嬷嬷嫌弃，真是越想越伤心，不由立住脚步，盯着地桃花悲伤起来。
[EN] Comparing herself to Miss—every bit as pretty, why was fate so wildly lopsided?! Born poor, chased by Chen Haoyu, rescued as a maid only to get bullied by Matron Wei! Eyes misting with self-pity, she paused staring down at pink Caesarweed blooms.
[ZH] 林觉从练兵署回来，无意中瞧见夏果的背影，以为是朱淑真，心想她终于从乡下回来。仔细一瞧，那背影身着丫鬟服，并不是朱淑真，没想到林府还有这等身姿的下人，他怎么从来没见过。上前问道：“你是新来的？”
[EN] Returning from the Military Training Office, Lin Jue caught sight of a slender silhouette from behind—thinking Zhu Shuzhen had come home at last! One step closer he saw maid's garb—who knew Lin Manor had a maid with a figure this graceful?! Stepping up, he asked: "New around here?"
[ZH] 夏果听到男子的声音，赶紧转过身，拜道：“我是小姐在乡下新收的丫鬟，名叫夏果。”
[EN] Spinning round at a man's voice, Xiaguo curtsied low: "Miss's new maid hired in the countryside—my name is Xiaguo."
[ZH] 夏果转过身的一刻，林觉看呆，这丫鬟的容貌绝不比朱淑真差，有过之而无不及，他竟然忘记说话，想起第一次见朱淑真的情景，心中也生起同样的情感。
[EN] The instant she lifted her face, Lin Jue went breathless—every ounce as breathtaking as Zhu Shuzhen, maybe even softer! Tongue-tied, that exact bolt of lightning from his very first glimpse of Zhu Shuzhen hit his chest all over again!
[ZH] 林觉不说话，夏果低着头不知该如何是好，看他身上的穿着，应该是林府的主人之一。沉默一会儿，夏果壮起胆子叫道：“老爷……”
[EN] Silence stretched; head bowed low, spotting fine robes telling her he was a master of the house, Xiaguo risked a timid murmur: "Master..."
[ZH] “不要称呼我老爷，叫我主君。”林觉终于说话，说完转身向西院而去，
[EN] "Don't call me Master—call me Lord," Lin Jue spoke at last, pivoting brisk toward the West Courtyard.
[ZH] 夏果才敢抬头瞧林觉的背影，自语道：“主君？他难道是小姐的夫君？”
[EN] Daring a peek at his receding back, Xiaguo whispered: "Lord? That's Miss's husband?!"
[ZH] 等林觉走进西院后，夏果才回到西院的竹兰居。
[EN] Waiting till he entered the West Courtyard, Xiaguo slipped back into Zhulan Residence.
[ZH] 苦荞从屋里出来，见夏果走进院中，大声说：“我到处找你找不到，你去了哪里？”
[EN] Stepping out of the room, Kuqiao spotted her and called sharp: "Hunted everywhere for you—where'd you vanish to?!"
[ZH] “我刚给一位洒扫的丫鬟帮忙，耽搁了一会儿。”
[EN] "Helped a sweeping maid a moment, got held up."
[ZH] “你是小姐的贴身丫鬟，只需伺候小姐，洒扫的事以后不用理会。”
[EN] "You're Miss's personal maid—tend Miss alone, leave sweeping to sweeping maids!"
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] 又被苦荞嘱咐，夏果心里更不满。当着苦荞的面不敢流露太多，还是快些进屋伺候小姐。
[EN] Scolded by Kuqiao too—stinging resentment dug deeper inside Xiaguo, hidden fast behind an obedient mask as she stepped indoors.
[ZH] 苦荞则来到林觉屋外，向内说：“姑爷，小姐请您一会儿到竹兰居用饭。”
[EN] Meanwhile Kuqiao marched over to Lin Jue's quarters and called in: "Son-in-law, Miss invites you to Zhulan Residence for dinner shortly."
[ZH] 出来一位小厮：“主君让你进去回话。”
[EN] A page boy stepped out: "Lord says come inside."
[ZH] 林觉正坐在桌边喝茶，见苦荞进来，问道：“你们今日几时回来的？”
[EN] Sipping tea at his table, Lin Jue asked as Kuqiao entered: "What time did you get back today?"
[ZH] “回姑爷，未时。”
[EN] "The Hour of the Sheep, Son-in-law."
[ZH] “你家小姐身边什么时候多出一名丫鬟？”
[EN] "When did your Miss pick up a new maid?"
[ZH] “回姑爷，是小姐在乡下新收的。”
[EN] "Hired her out in the countryside, Son-in-law."
[ZH] “好吧，告诉朱淑真，待会儿我自会过去。”
[EN] "Fine. Tell Zhu Shuzhen I'll be over in a bit."
[ZH] 苦荞走后，林觉估摸着时间差不多，换过衣裳，来到竹兰居，苦荞与夏果正站在屋外伺候。
[EN] Once Kuqiao left, checking the hour, Lin Jue changed into fresh robes and walked over to Zhulan Residence, where Kuqiao and Xiaguo stood waiting outside the door.
[ZH] ﻿林觉瞧一眼夏果，甚觉满意，跨进屋内，一桌子菜出现在眼前，还有美酒。朱淑真坐在桌旁，面带微笑。林觉感到恍惚，难道她出去一趟想通了，想与他和好？
[EN] Casting a pleased glance at Xiaguo before stepping inside, he found a lavish table laden with dishes and fine wine—and Zhu Shuzhen seated smiling! A dizzy flash hit him: had a month away changed her mind about staying together?!
[ZH] “你既不允许我进竹兰居，为何又请我前来用饭？”
[EN] "Barred me from setting foot in Zhulan Residence—why invite me to dine now?"
[ZH] “此一时彼一时，很多事情会随着时间而变化，我今日请你前来，是想与你像朋友一样聊聊天儿。”
[EN] "Times change—people think clearer with space. Invited you tonight to talk things over calm, like friends."
[ZH] 林觉坐下，主动拿起酒壶将两人面前的酒盅斟满：“既如此，你我先饮酒，再聊。”
[EN] Taking a seat, Lin Jue lifted the wine pot and poured both cups brimful: "Friends it is—drink first, talk after!"
[ZH] 千里寻举起酒盅：“干！”
[EN] Raising her cup crisp: "Cheers!"
[ZH] 喝完酒，千里寻放下酒盅：“我在乡下一个月，认真思考你我之间的关系，你没错，我也没错，错只错在两个不合适的人硬被凑到一起……”
[EN] Setting down her empty cup, Qianli Xun spoke level and sincere: "Spent a whole month in the country thinking through us. Neither of us is evil—just two totally mismatched people shoved under one roof..."
[ZH] 原来她并不想与他和好，林觉在心里叹口气，继续认真听朱淑真说些什么。
[EN] Not making up after all! Suppressing a silent sigh, Lin Jue kept listening.
[ZH] “如果你能娶到一位喜欢你，认可你的娘子，相信你也不会采取家庭暴力；如果我嫁给心仪之人，也应该会过得圆满。不如你放过我，不为别的，只为你自己。常言道，人不为己天诛地灭，你耗着我，对你没有任何好处。”
[EN] "Marry a wife who adores and respects you, and I bet you'd never raise a fist; marry a man who fits my heart, and I'd find peace too. Let me go—not for me, for YOUR own sake! Folks say look out for yourself first—trapping me here brings you zero joy!"
[ZH] 林觉“哼”了一声：“我耗着你，大不了再纳一房妾室，什么都不影响，而你到死只能待在林府。”
[EN] Lin Jue snorted: "Holding you here? Easy—I just take a concubine and live fine, while you stay stuck in Lin Manor till you die!"
[ZH] “错！如果你耗着我，我会在外重新置办一处宅子，搬出林府，过自己想过的日子。到时在外面做出什么出格的事来，丢了林府的面子，可别怪我。”
[EN] "Wrong! Keep dragging your feet, and I buy my own house outside and move straight out of Lin Manor to live how I please—and whatever wild headlines I make outside shaming the Lin name will be on YOUR head!"
[ZH] 千里寻发现自己竟然一点儿都不生气，而对方看上去虽冷淡，却已没有以前那种令她讨厌的表情。
[EN] Funny enough, not a drop of anger heated her blood—and though Lin Jue sounded cool, that nasty bully glare had vanished too.
[ZH] 林觉已有和离的想法，朱淑真的话并未激起他的怒火，可总不能白白放朱淑真离开。
[EN] Already warming toward mutual divorce anyway, her blunt words didn't spark rage—yet no way was he letting her walk off empty-handed!
[ZH] 他将自己的酒盅斟满，又喝下一盅，爽快地说：“我放你走……”
[EN] Refilling his cup and tossing it back clean, he spoke smooth: "Fine—I'll let you go..."
[ZH] 千里寻开心道：“君子一言驷马难追！”
[EN] Qianli Xun lit up: "A gentleman's word stands solid!"
[ZH] “听我把话说完，放你走可以，必须答应我一件事。”
[EN] "Hold on—not finished! I grant mutual divorce on ONE condition."
[ZH] “尽管说。”
[EN] "Name it!"
[ZH] “你新得的丫鬟不错，把她送给我，我就答应和离。”
[EN] "That new maid of yours caught my eye—hand her over to me, and I sign the mutual divorce!"
[ZH] 千里寻为难道：“此事需问问夏果，如若她同意，当然好说，如若她不同意，我也没办法。”
[EN] Qianli Xun hesitated: "Have to ask Xiaguo first—if she agrees, great; if she says no, my hands are tied."
[ZH] “她是你的丫鬟，只要你想送给我，她不敢说一个不字。”林觉看着一桌子菜，“这么好的菜，可惜我没胃口，就不在此陪你享用。”
[EN] "She's YOUR maid—if you decide to give her to me, she can't say no!" Eyeing the untouched feast, he rose: "Fine dishes—shame I lost my appetite. Enjoy without me."
[ZH] 林觉起身走到门口，又将夏果瞧了一眼：“你叫夏果？”
[EN] Pausing at the threshold, he eyed Xiaguo again: "Your name is Xiaguo?"
[ZH] “是，主君。”
[EN] "Yes, Lord."
[ZH] “长得不错！呵呵呵！”林觉笑了一声离开竹兰居。
[EN] "Real looker! Hehehe!" Chuckling low, Lin Jue strolled out of Zhulan Residence.
